 kauśīdyavīryotsāhanāvadāna

++ oṃ namaḥ śrīsarvabuddhabodhisatvebhyaḥ ++

yaḥ śrīmāñchrīghano loke saddharmaṃ samupādiśat |
śāsanāni trilokeṣu jayantu tasya sarvadā || 1.1{1} ||
athāśoko mahārājaḥ kṛtāñjaliḥ pramoditaḥ |
upaguptaṃ yatiṃ natvā punarevamabhāṣata || 1.2{2} ||
bhadanta śrotumichāmi punaranyatsubhāṣitam |
tadyathā guruṇādiṣṭaṃ tathā deṣṭuṃ ca me'rhati || 1.3{3} ||
iti saṃprārthitaṃ rājñā śrutvā so'rhan yatiḥ sudhīḥ |
upagupto narendraṃ taṃ samālokyaivamādiśat || 1.4{3 } ||
sādhu śṛṇu mahārāja yathā me guruṇoditam |
tathāhaṃ te pravakṣyāmi śrutvā cāpyanumodaya || 1.5{4} ||
purāsau bhagavān buddhaḥ śākyasiṃho dayānidhiḥ |
dharmarājo jagacchāstā sarvajñaḥ sugato jinaḥ || 1.6{5} ||
(rm 2)
sarvavidyākalābhijñaḥ ṣaḍabhijño munīśvaraḥ |
mārajillokavinnātho vināyakastathāgataḥ || 1.7{6} ||
śravastyāṃ jetakāraṇye mahodyāne manorame |
anāthapiṇḍadākhyasya gṛhasthasya mahātmanaḥ || 1.8{7} ||
nānāvṛkṣaiḥ samāpanne nānāpuṣpapraśobhitaiḥ |
nānāphalabharānamraiḥ kalpapādapasannibhaiḥ || 1.9{8} ||
aṣṭāṅgaguṇasaṃpannajalaiḥ padmotpalādibhiḥ |
puṣpaiśca paripūrṇābhiḥ puṣkariṇībhirāśrite || 1.10{9} ||
nānājantugaṇaiścāpi mithasnehanivandhitaiḥ |
nānāpakṣigaṇaiścāpi saṃrāvairupasevite || 1.11{10} ||
tasmin divyamanoramya ārāme pariśobhite |
puṇyakṣetre jināvāse vihāre maṇimaṇḍite || 1.12{11} ||
śrāvakairbhikṣubhiḥ sārddhaṃ bodhisatvagaṇairapi |
sarvasatvahitārthena tasthau dharmaṃ prakāśitum || 1.13{12} ||
evaṃ tatra samāsīnaṃ saṃbuddhaṃ dharmadeśinam |
dṛṣṭvā dharmottamaṃ śrotuṃ sarve lokāḥ samāyayuḥ || 1.14{13} ||
devā daityāśca siddhāśca yakṣagaṃdharvakinnarāḥ |
grahā vidyādharāścāpi nāgāśca garuḍā api || 1.15{14} ||
sarve'pi lokapālāśca munayaśca maharṣayaḥ |
brāhmaṇāḥ kṣatriyāścāpi vaiśyāśca maṃtriṇo janāḥ || 1.16{15} ||
amātyāḥ sādhavaḥ śūdrāḥ sārthavāhā mahājanāḥ |
dhanino vaṇijaścāpi gṛhasthāḥ paurikā api || 1.17{16} ||
tathā jānapadāścāpi grāmyāḥ kārpaṭikā api |
evamanye'pi satvāśca saddharmaśravaṇārthinaḥ || 1.18{17} ||
pūjāpaṃcopahārāṇi gṛhītvā bhaktisaṃyutāḥ |
saddharmaṃ saugataṃ śrotumanumodya samāyayuḥ || 1.19{18} ||
sarve tatra praviṣṭāste dṛṣṭvā taṃ sugataṃ jinam |
vismitānaṃditāḥ pūjāṃ kṛtvā natvā yathākramam || 1.20{19} ||
sarve tatra samāsīnāḥ parivṛtāḥ puraskṛtāḥ |
taṃ dṛṣṭvā sadguruṃ dharmaṃ śrotuṃ tasthuḥ samāhitāḥ || 1.21{20} ||
athāsau bhagavān dṛṣṭvā lokān sarvān samāhitān |
ādimadhyāntakalyāṇaṃ dideśa dharmamuttamam || 1.22{21} ||
evaṃ nityamasau nāthaḥ sarvasatvahitārthikaḥ |
dharmaṃ prakāśayan loke tasthau bhānurivoditaḥ || 1.23{22} ||
(rm 3)
tasmiṃśca samaye tatra śrāvastyāṃ sanmahājanaḥ |
āḍhyaḥ śreṣṭhī mahābhogo vistīrṇasuparigrahaḥ || 1.24{23} ||
śrāddho bhadrāśayo dhīmān sarvabandhusuhṛtpriyaḥ |
tīrthikaśrāvako mānī babhūva dhanagarvitaḥ || 1.25{24} ||
tadāsau sundarīṃ bhāryyāṃ kuladharmasamānikām |
nītvā kāmaguṇāsakto reme nityaṃ tayā saha || 1.26{25} ||
tasyaivaṃ ramatastasyāṃ bhāryāyāṃ sucirādapi |
putro na duhitā vāpi nodbabhūva kathaṃ cana || 1.27{26} ||
tadaputro gṛhastho'sau putrābhidarśanotsukaḥ |
kapolaṃ svakare dhṛtvā vyacintayadraho gataḥ || 1.28{27} ||
aho daivānna me putraḥ putrī vādyāpi nāsti yat |
tanme syuḥ saṃpado vyarthāyāsāṃ bhoktā na vidyate || 1.29{29!} ||
kiṃ mamaitairdhanai ratnaiḥ kevalaṃ duhkhasādhanaiḥ |
yeṣāṃ bhoktā na vidyeta vyarthaṃ mayā hyupārjitam || 1.30{30} ||
hyaputrasya jagacchūnyaṃ saṃsāre niḥsukhaṃ mama |
yato na varddhate dharmastajjanmāpi ca nisphalam || 1.31{31} ||
kimupāyaṃ kariṣyāmi yato bhāgyaṃ na vidyate |
daivo hi valavāṃlloke kimupāyairguṇairvalaiḥ || 1.32{32} ||
kutrātra śaraṇaṃ yāsye hyaputro'haṃ kulāntakaḥ |
tanme syātko'tra sanmitraṃ saṃkaṭe yaḥ samuddharet || 1.33{33} ||
nūnaṃ me pitaraḥ sarve piṇḍavichedadarśinaḥ |
matparaṃ durlabhaṃ matvā bhaviṣyanti nirāśitāḥ || 1.34{34} ||
sarvathāhaṃ vinaṣṭo'smi kuladharmavināśakaḥ |
kiṃ paratra prabhokṣyāmi ko me piṇḍaṃ pradāsyati || 1.35{35} ||
iti cintāviṣaṇṇaṃ taṃ dṛṣṭvā sarve ca bāndhavāḥ |
(rm 4)
samupetya samāmaṃtrya duhkhahetumapṛchan || 1.36{36} ||
kiṃ duḥsthito'si sādho'tra kiṃ cāpi duḥkhacintayā |
vaktavyaṃ cettadasmākaṃ sarvathā vaktumarhasi || 1.37{37} ||
iti pṛṣṭe gṛhastho'sau dīrghaniśvāsamutsṛjan |
sarvāṃstān bāndhavān dṛṣṭvā duḥkhahetumudaharat || 1.38{38} ||
bhavantaḥ śrūyatāṃ sarvairmama duḥkhasya kāraṇam |
yadadyāpi na me putraḥ putrī vāpi na vidyate || 1.39{39} ||
saṃpado me gṛhe santi sarvaratnasamanvitāḥ |
sarvadravyasamṛddhāśca tāsāṃ bhoktā na vidyate || 1.40{40} ||
tatsarvaṃ nakṣyate vyarthaṃ mayā yātnairupārjitam |
aputrasya hi sarvasvaṃ nūnaṃ rājā grahīṣyti || 1.41{[41]} ||
aputro'haṃ hyapuṇyātmā gantavyaṃ maraṇaṃ dhruvam |
pretībhūtasya me ko'nyaḥ piṇḍadānaṃ pradāsyati || 1.42{[42]} ||
mayā na prakṛtaṃ puṇyaṃ saṃpadarthaprasaṃginā |
tanme sarvaṃ nirarthaṃ syātko māṃ pāpātsamuddharet || 1.43{43} ||
iha bhogyaṃ prabhuktvāpi kiṃ me sāramavasthitam |
kevalaṃ dhanasaktasya paratra sadgatiḥ katham || 1.44{44} ||
iti cintā hṛdi sthitvā cittaṃ me tudate'niśam |
evaṃ cittavyathākrāntaḥ śalyaviddha ivāsmyaham || 1.45{45} ||
tadvyathāharaṇopāyaṃ dātavyaṃ mama taddhitam |
anyathā cedihāmutra vinaṣṭaḥ syāṃ nirāśitaḥ || 1.46{46} ||
iti tenoditaṃ śrutvā sarve te bāndhavā janāḥ |
karuṇāviṣṭacittāstaṃ bodhayantaḥ samabruvan || 1.47{47} ||
mā mā bhaiṣṭhā mahābhāga tyaja cintāṃ vacaḥ śṛṇu |
tadupāyaṃ vayaṃ brūmastatkuruṣva samāhitaḥ || 1.48{48} ||
devatārādhanāṃ kṛtvā kuruṣva vaṃśayācanām |
nūnaṃ vaṃśaṃ pradāsyanti devatāste prasāditāḥ || 1.49{49} ||
(rm 5)
eṣa loke pravādo'sti yadāyācanahetutaḥ |
putrā duhitaro vāpi jātā devaprasādataḥ || 1.50{50} ||
tathā naiva pramāṇaṃ syācchāstre karmapramāṇataḥ |
tattu devaprasādena karmaśśīghraṃ prasiddhyate || 1.51{51} ||
tadeva devavairuddhe karma vighnairnihanyate |
tasmāddaivaprasiddhyarthaṃ śraddhābhaktisamāhitaḥ || 1.52{52} ||
devatārādhanāṃ kṛtvā sarvakāryyaṃ prasādhayet |
iti matvā bhavāṃścāpi satputrapratipattaye || 1.53{53} ||
bhāryyayā saha bhaktyā ca devatāyācanāṃ kuru |
tato raktaiḥ priyāṃ bhāryāmṛtusnātāṃ prakāmataḥ || 1.54{54} ||
gāḍhamāliṃgya saṃrakto dharmavījaṃ praropaya |
tato devaprabhāvena suniṣpannaḥ prasiddhitaḥ || 1.55{55} ||
tvatputraḥ saṃprajāyeta satyamevaṃ pramāṇaya |
iti teṣāṃ vacaḥ śrutvā gṛhastho'sau vinoditaḥ || 1.56{56} ||
tatheti ca pratiśrutya bhāryyayā saha moditaḥ |
śivabrahmādideveśāniṃdrādidigadhīśvarān || 1.57{57} ||
jalāśrayavanārāmacatvaramaṇḍapālayān |
sūryyādīṃśca grahān sarvā bhairavādyāśca mātṛkāḥ || 1.58{58} ||
svakulādhipatīṃ devānanyāṃśca tribhavasthitān |
sarvānevaṃ samārādhya pūjayitvā vidhānataḥ || 1.59{59} ||
saṃtānaṃ prārthayitvāsau patnyā saha tathāramat |
tadā kaścinmahāsatvaḥ svargāccyutvā bhuvaṃ gataḥ || 1.60{60} ||
tasya gṛhapateḥ patnyā garbhe sa samupāviśat |
tataḥ sā śreṣṭhino bhāryyāpannasatvāśu gurviṇī || 1.61{61} ||
garbhe satvaṃ samutpannaṃ matvā bharttāramabravīt |
svāmin prasīda mā bhūstvaṃ viṣaṇno'tra sukhī bhava || 1.62{62} ||
tava bhāgyāddhi me garbhe nūnaṃ satvaḥ pravarttate |
(rm 6)
yadgarbhe dakṣiṇe bhāge sthitaḥ saṃparivarttate || 1.63{63} ||
tannūnaṃ dārako'yaṃ syātsatyameva na saṃśayaḥ |
iti bhāryāvacaḥ śrutvā gṛhastho'sau pramoditaḥ || 1.64{64} ||
bhāryāyā garbhamālokya satyamityanvamanyata || 1.65{56!} ||
tato'sau sahasā vandhusuhṛnmitrasahāyakān |
prāhūya puratasteṣāṃ mudaivaṃ samudānayat || 1.66{66} ||
aho bhāgyānmamotpannaṃ yadabhivāṃchitaṃ mayā |
dhanyo'smi nāvagītaḥ syāddevatānuprasādataḥ || 1.67{67} ||
cirābhilaṣitaṃ putramukhaṃ paśyeya sāṃpratam |
kṛtyā me pratikurvīta bhṛtaśca prativibhṛyāt || 1.68{68} ||
dāyādyaṃ pratipadyeta vaṃśo me sthitiko bhavet |
suhṛdo me prasannāḥ syurdviṣantaśca viṣāditāḥ || 1.69{69} ||
mayā yāvanti dānāni puṇyāni prakṛtāni ca |
etatpuṇyaphalairnityaṃ maṃgalaṃ bhavatu dvayoḥ || 1.70{70} ||
etatpuṇyānubhāvena bhūyānmātā nirāturā |
sukhaṃ sunotu satputraṃ mā pāpaṃ samudāgamat || 1.71{71} ||
iti tenoditaṃ śrutvā sarve bandhusuhṛtsakhāḥ |
tathāstvityanumodantaḥ svasvālayaṃ samāyayuḥ || 1.72{72} ||
tato'sau garbhiṇī kāntā kramādgarbhapravarddhitā |
vaidyopadiṣṭamāhāraṃ bhuktvācaradyathā sukham || 1.73{73} ||
tataśca samaye'sūta putraṃ kāntaṃ manoharam |
sarvalakṣaṇasaṃpannaṃ bhadrāṃśaṃ maṃgalānvitam || 1.74{74} ||
tacchrutvaiva gṛhastho'sau suprasannaḥ pramoditaḥ |
dṛṣṭvā taṃ dārakaṃ saumyaṃ naiva tṛptiṃ samāyayau || 1.75{75} ||
tato jñātīn samāhūya kṛtvā jātimahaṃ mudā |
putranāmābhisaṃskartuṃ bhūya etān samabravīt || 1.76{76} ||
bhavanto dṛśyatāṃ siddhaṃ mamabhilaṣitaṃ cirāt |
(rm 7)
tannāmāsya yathāyogyaṃ prasiddhaṃ kriyatāṃ śubham || 1.77{76!} ||
iti tasya vacaḥ śrutvā sarve te jñātayo mudā |
dārakaṃ taṃ samālokya naṃditāḥ samabhāṣata || 1.78{78} ||
yasmin dine hyayaṃ jātastadā sarve'pi paurikāḥ |
nanditāstadbhavatvayaṃ nāmnā nanda iti śrutaḥ || 1.79{79} ||
athāsau dārako nandaḥ samyagdehopacāraṇe |
pitrāṣṭāsu ca dhātrīṣu paritoṣya samarpitaḥ || 1.80{80} ||
tato'sau kramaśastāsāṃ dhātriṇāmupacārataḥ |
dine dine pravṛddho'bhūddhradasthaṃ paṃkajaṃ yathā || 1.81{81} ||
evaṃ pravarddhamāno'sau ṣaḍvarṣiko yadābhavat |
tadā kuśīdasaṃvṛtto babhūva viratotsavaḥ || 1.82{82} ||
sadā śayyāsanasthastatsamutthātuṃ na caichata |
śayyāsanasthitaścaiva bhuktvā naivācaratkvacit || 1.83{83} ||
sadā svāntargṛhastho'pi sutīkṣṇabuddhimān sudhīḥ |
adhītya sarvaśāstrāṇi satyadharmarato'bhavat || 1.84{84} ||
atha śreṣṭhī pitā dṛṣṭvā putramevaṃ kuśīditam |
sarvavidyāguṇādhāraṃ dṛṣṭvā caivaṃ vyacintayat || 1.85{85} ||
kathaṃ cidyo mayā prāpto devatāsaṃprasādataḥ |
so'pi putro mamābhāgyādbhavatyevaṃ kuśīditaḥ || 1.86{86} ||
sarvavidyāguṇādhāraṃ sarvaśāstravicakṣaṇaḥ |
kiṃ mamānena putreṇa dusthiteneva roginā |
yataḥ svasthaśarīro'pi bhuktvaivaṃ paśuvatsthitaḥ || 1.87{87} ||
kimatrāhaṃ kariṣyāmi yatputro'yaṃ ni rudyamaḥ |
kimīdrigjantunā kāryyaṃ kuladharmopahāriṇā || 1.88{68!} ||
dhigme daivaṃ prayatnaṃ ca sarvaṃ tatsyānnirarthakam |
kimupāyaṃ kariṣyāmi sarvathāhaṃ vibhagnitaḥ || 1.89{78} ||
evaṃ cintāparīto'sau gṛhasthaśca vyacintayat |
pūraṇo me'sti śāstā yaḥ pṛcheyaṃ tadupāyakam || 1.90{79} ||
iti matvā gṛhastho'sau tadāśramaṃ samāgataḥ |
pūraṇaṃ taṃ guruṃ natvā papraccha tadupāyatām || 1.91{80} ||
(rm 8)
bhagavan bho mahābhijña śāstāsi me prasīda tat |
yanmayā pṛchyate kāryyaṃ tatsamādeṣṭumarhasi || 1.92{81} ||
iti tenārthitaṃ śrutvā tīrthiko mānagarvitaḥ |
āśu siddhaṃ kariṣyāmo yatkāryyaṃ te'tra tadvada || 1.93{82} ||
iti proktvaivamāśvāsya pūraṇo'tyabhimānikaḥ |
pravadaṃstaṃ gṛhasthaṃ vai sarvathaivamabodhayat || 1.94{83} ||
iti tena samādiṣṭaṃ śrutvā gṛhapatirmudā |
kṛtāñjalipuṭo natvā taṃ guruṃ samabhāṣata || 1.95{84} ||
bhagavan bho vijānīyā yo me putraḥ prajāyate |
sa pravṛddho nirutsāhī kuśīdo varttate'dhunā || 1.96{85} ||
sadā śayyāsthito bhuṅkte bhuktvā śete nirudyamaḥ |
notsahati kvacidgaṃtuṃ paśuvattiṣṭhate gṛhe || 1.97{86} ||
na śayanātsamutthāya śrotuṃ draṣṭuṃ samicchati |
gṛhe śayyāsanārūḍho rogīva vasate sadā || 1.98{87} ||
tadupāyaṃ kathaṃ kuryyāṃ yenāyaṃ śayanotthitaḥ |
śrotuṃ draṣṭuṃ ca gaṃtuṃ ca karttuṃ kāryyaṃ yathotsahet || 1.99{88} ||
tathā me bhagavan putraṃ kuladharme niyojaya |
bhavāneva hi me śāstā taddhitaṃ kartumarhasi || 1.100{89} ||
ityevaṃ prārthitaṃ tena śrutvāsau pūraṇo yatiḥ |
tasya gṛhapateścittaṃ praharṣayan samabravīt || 1.101{90} ||
are mā gā viṣādaṃ tvaṃ cintayā kiṃ pratapyase |
asmāsu vidyamāneṣu ṣaṭsu vijñeṣu śāstṛṣu || 1.102{91} ||
tadahaṃ te mahābhāga sārddhaṃ sarvaiśca tīrthikaiḥ |
ṛddhyā gatvā kariṣyāmi dārakaṃ vistayotthitam || 1.103{92} ||
yadāsau dārako hyasmān sarvān dṛṣṭvā samutthitaḥ |
kṛtvā praṇāmamasmākaṃ dharmmaṃ śrotuṃ samutsahet || 1.104{93} ||
śrutvā dharmasamutsāhātkuśalāni samācaret |
tatkuśalaiḥ samāpanno vīryavān sa bhavetsudhīḥ || 1.105{94} ||
(rm 9)
tataste sarvakāryāṇi sādhayetsa samudyataḥ |
svakuladharmakarmāṇi kuśalī saṃcariṣyate || 1.106{95} ||
evaṃ me vacanaṃ satyaṃ dṛṣṭvā samyakpramāṇaya |
tadviṣādaṃ parityajya karttavyaṃ hi tathā kuru || 1.107{96} ||
iti tena samādiṣṭaṃ śrutvā sa gṛhabhṛnmudā |
sahasā svagṛhaṃ gatvā tadbhojyaṃ samasādhayat || 1.108{97} ||
athāsau pūraṇo'nyaiśca tīrthikaiḥ sārddhamāgataḥ |
gṛhe gatvā purastasya paryyaṅke samupāśrayat || 1.109{98} ||
tathāsau tān samāgatvā kramaśa āsane sthitān |
dṛṣṭvā saṃharṣitaḥ śreṣṭhī sahasā putramabravīt || 1.110{99} ||
putra paśya gṛhe'smākaṃ śāstāraḥ samupasthitāḥ |
tatsamutthāya natvaitāñchāstṝn bhojyaiḥ pravāraya || 1.111{100} ||
ityuktvā janakenāsau dārako na samutthitaḥ |
draṣṭumapi na cotsehe kiṃ namecca pravārayet || 1.112{1} ||
bahuśaḥ preryyamāṇo'pi pitrā sa bandhubhiḥ saha |
dārakastān samāsīnān draṣṭumapi na caichata || 1.113{2} ||
kiṃ punarutthito natvā bhojaṇaiḥ saṃpravārayet |
tathā śayyāsanārūḍha evaṃ tasthau nirutsavaḥ || 1.114{3} ||
tataste tīrthikāḥ sarve dṛṣṭvaivaṃ nirutsavam |
nānāvidhiprayatnaistamutthāpayitumārabhan || 1.115{4} ||
tathāpi naiva śayyāyā udatiṣṭhatsa dārakaḥ |
svadharmadeśakāṃścāpi tān draṣṭumapi naihata || 1.116{5} ||
evaṃ cānutthitaṃ dṛṣṭvā taṃ te sarve ca tīrthikāḥ |
haṭhāccainaṃ samutthāpya vaśīkarttuṃ parākraman || 1.117{6} ||
tathaitāṃstīrthikān sarvāneva garvābhivāditān |
(rm 10)
dṛṣṭvābhisaṃmukhāyātān bhīto'rautsītsa dārakaḥ || 1.118{7} ||
tathā taṃ ruditaṃ dṛṣṭvā sarve te lajjayā bhiyā |
pratijagmurvibhinnāsyāḥ svasvālayāni bheditāḥ || 1.119{8} ||
tathā gṛhapatirdṛṣṭvā sarvānetān parāyitān |
pratibhinnaviṣaṇnātmā tasthau cintāvimohitaḥ || 1.120{9} ||
atha śrībhagavān buddhaḥ sarvadarśī vināyakaḥ |
paśyan satvān samuddhartuṃ dadarśa taṃ kuśīditam || 1.121{10} ||
dṛṣṭvā tasya kuśīdasya karmavṛttiparaṃparām |
tatkuśīdaṃ samuddhartumaryānaṃdaṃ samabravīt || 1.122{11} ||
paśyānaṃda gṛhasthasya śrāvastīpuravāsinaḥ |
putro naṃda iti khyātaḥ sāmprataṃ saṃkuśīdikaḥ || 1.123{12} ||
asau me darśanādeva nūnaṃ vīryyaṃ samārabhan |
sahasotthāya sa natvā dharmaṃ praśṛṇuyānmudā || 1.124{13} ||
tato me śāsane dharmaṃ pracaretsa sudhīḥ sadā |
kramādbodhicarīṃ pūryya saṃbodhiṃ cāpi lapsyati || 1.125{14} ||
tasmāttasya gṛhe gatvā tīrthikadarppachittaye |
saddharme saṃpratiṣṭhāpya sarvān bodhau niyojaye || 1.126{15} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa śrutvānandaḥ sa harṣitaḥ |
karttavyaṃ bhagavannevamityanumodito'bhavat || 1.127{16} ||
tato bhagavatā tena suvarṇābhā marīcayaḥ |
utsṛṣṭāstaiśca tadgehamavabhāsya vyaśodhayat || 1.128{17} ||
yadā tairdārakaḥ spṛṣṭo buddhamaitrīmarīcibhiḥ |
tadā prahlāditaścāsāvityevaṃ samacintayat || 1.129{18} ||
aho kasya prabhāvo'yaṃ yenaivamavabhāsitam |
prahlāditaśca me kāyastanna jāne kutaḥ katham || 1.130{19} ||
iti cintāṃ samālambya kautukākrāntamānasaḥ |
samantato nirīkṣyaivaṃ tasthau gehe suvismitaḥ || 1.131{20} ||
(rm 11)
tathā taṃ vismitaṃ dṛṣṭvā bhagavān bhikṣubhiḥ saha |
sahasā tadgṛhaṃ gatvā prādurāsītprabhāsayan || 1.132{21} ||
tatra sa dārako'paśyadbhagavantaṃ tamāsthitam |
kāntaṃ divyātiriktābhaṃ samantabhadrarūpiṇam || 1.133{22} ||
dṛṣṭvaiva sahasotthāya natvā pādau pramoditaḥ |
svayamevāsanaṃ tasmai prajñapyaivamabhāṣata || 1.134{23} ||
svāgataṃ bhagavannātha vijayasva munīśvara |
prasīdānugrahārthena niṣīdātra śubhāsane || 1.135{24} ||
iti tenodite tatra bhagavān bhikṣubhiḥ saha |
tatprajñaptāsane sthitvā dharmaṃ deṣṭuṃ samārabhat || 1.136{25} ||
taddṛṣṭvā dārakasyaivaṃ dharmotsāhaṃ samutthitam |
pitarau jñātivargāśca sarve'pyāsan suvismitāḥ || 1.137{26} ||
tatrāsau dārako harṣādutphullanayano mudā |
natvā pādau muneścāgre dharmaṃ śrotumupāśrayat || 1.138{27} ||
athāsau bhagavāṃstasya jñātvāśayaviśuddhatām |
ādimadhyāntakalyāṇaṃ dharmaṃ deṣṭuṃ tamabravīt || 1.139{27} ||
śṛṇu vatsa mahābhāga pāpapuṇyārthasādhane |
hetuṃ vakṣye śubhārthe te tadatra tvaṃ samācara || 1.140{29} ||
mānuṣyaṃ durlabhaṃ prāpya vidyuttaraṃgacaṃcalam |
pāpakṣaye matiḥ kāryā puṇyārthasādhaneṣu ca || 1.141{30} ||
pāpena durgatiṃ yāyātpuṇyena sadgatiṃ vrajet |
pāpamitrānurāgeṇa matiḥ pāpe pravarttate || 1.142{31} ||
tataḥ pāparataḥ kuryātpāpāni dāruṇānyapi |
tato duṣṭaḥ supāpiṣṭhaḥ saddharmāṇi vinindayet || 1.143{32} ||
tato dharmaviruddhatvātkuśaktiḥ kutsito bhavet |
tataḥ sa kutsitātmā hi sarvasatvairvinindyate || 1.144{33} ||
paribhūto viṣādena bhavetmūḍho nirutsahaḥ |
utsāhavarjite citte ālasyaṃ samupāśrayet || 1.145{34} ||
ālasvī sādhayennaiva kiṃciddharmaṃ guṇāni vā |
(rm 12)
dharmakarmaviraktātmā nirguṇī kiṃ kariṣyati |
bhogya eva sadāsaktaḥ paśorapyadhamo naraḥ |
kiṃ tena puruṣeṇāpi nirguṇena sukhāśinā |
yasya puṇye guṇe vāpi kadāpi notsahenmanaḥ |
puṇyotsāhavipannātmā duṣṭakleśairvihanyate |
kleśito mārasaṃghaiśca vaśīkṛtya nivaddhyate || 1.146{36} ||
mārasya vaśagaḥ strīṇāṃ dāsatvaṃ samupāśrayet |
sa dāsanirato bhuktvā paśuvattiṣṭhate gṛhe || 1.147{36} ||
naiva dharmaṃ kvacicchrotuṃ draṣṭuṃ vāpi na cotsahet |
dharmaṃ vinā vrajennaiva sadgatiṃ hi kadā cana || 1.148{37} ||
tasmāddharme samācartuṃ puṇyotsāhaṃ pravarddhayan |
trimaṇḍalaviśuddhena dānaṃ dadyācchubhāptaye || 1.149{39} ||
dānena siddhyate dharmaṃ dharmāccittaṃ viśuddhyate |
śuddhacittaścarecchīle śīlavān hi bhavetsudhīḥ || 1.150{40} ||
sudhīro bhāvayetkṣāṃtiṃ kṣāntimān duṣṭajitkṛtī |
kuśalī prārabhedvīryyaṃ vīryyavān puruṣottamaḥ || 1.151{41} ||
mahāsatvo labheddhyānaṃ dhyātā brahmavihārikaḥ |
saṃsmṛtaḥ sādhayetprajñāṃ prajñāvān hi guṇālayaḥ || 1.152{42} ||
guṇajñaḥ samupāyaiśca satvān dharme niyojayet |
etatpuṇyānubhāvena bodhau praṇidhimācaret || 1.153{43} ||
bodhipraṇidhicittena balāni sādhayeddaśa |
tadbalaiśca mahāghorāṃ mārasaṃghān parājayet || 1.154{44} ||
jitvā māragaṇān sarvān saṃbodhijñānamāpnuyāt |
tataḥ sarvatra lokeṣu dharmacakraṃ pravarttayet || 1.155{45} ||
evaṃ pravarttayan dharmaṃ śāstā lokādhipo bhavet |
sarvalokahitaṃ kurvanmahatpuṇyaiḥ samṛddhitaḥ || 1.156{46} ||
sadaivaṃ sadgatau sthitvā saṃbuddhapadamāpnuyāt |
evaṃ matvā tvayā vatsa caritavyaṃ tathā śubhe || 1.157{47} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ jinendreṇa śrutvā sa dārako mudā |
(rm 13)
kṛtāṃjaliḥ punarnatvā bhagavantamabhāṣata || 1.158{48} ||
adyārabhya sadā śāstarbhavataḥ śaraṇaṃ vraje |
tadyathā bhavatājñaptaṃ tathā satyaṃ carāmyaham || 1.159{49} ||
kṛpādṛṣṭimupasthāpya samanvāhara māṃ sadā |
bhavāneva hi sarvajñastraidhātukavināyakaḥ || 1.160{50} ||
yadaivaṃ te dayā nāsti mayyudyamavivarjite |
ka evaṃ samupāgatya proddharenmāṃ kuśīdikam || 1.161{51} ||
pavitrībhūtamātmānaṃ manye'haṃ te prasādataḥ |
dhanyo'smi tanmahābhadro yacchāstraivaṃ pradarśitaḥ || 1.162{52} ||
iti tenodite bhūyaḥ sarvajñaḥ sa munīśvaraḥ |
sucaṃdanamayīṃ yaṣṭiṃ datvainaṃ samabhāṣata || 1.163{53} ||
vatsemāṃ yaṣṭimādhāya prakoṭaya samāhitaḥ |
tatastvaṃ sarvadāvaśyaṃ labheḥ puṇyamahotsavam || 1.164{54} ||
iti śāstrā pradattāṃ tāṃ natvāsau dārako'grahīt |
yathādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa tathā kartumudācarat || 1.165{55} ||
ityevaṃ taṃ samādiśya sa saṃbuddhaḥ sasāṃghikaḥ |
svaṃ vihāramupāśritya dharmaṃ diśan samāvasat || 1.166{56} ||
athāsau dārako harṣāttāṃ yaṣṭiṃ svayamādadhat |
yathādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa prākoṭayatsamāhitaḥ || 1.167{57} ||
tasyāmākoṭyamānāyāṃ puṇyaśabda udācarat |
gṛhe ratnanidhānāni prādurāsan bahūni ca || 1.168{57} ||
tacchabdaśravaṇaṃ kṛtvā dārako'sau pramoditaḥ |
gṛhe ratnanidhānāni dṛṣṭvāścaryyaṃ samāyayau || 1.169{59} ||
tataśca vismito harṣātpunarevaṃ vyacintayat |
batedaṃ sumahāśauryyaṃ vīryyaṃ prārambhaye tataḥ || 1.170{60} ||
tathā ratnākaraṃ gatvā ratnāni samupārjayan |
sasaṃghaṃ sugataṃ nityamupatiṣṭheya saṃbhajan || 1.171{61} ||
(rm 14)
iti dhyātvā samutsāhaṃ saṃnibadhya sa dārakaḥ |
ratnākaraṃ samāgantuṃ prārebhe saha vāṇijaiḥ || 1.172{62} ||
tato'sau dārako vīrastasyāṃ pūryyāṃ samantataḥ |
sārthavāhaṃ svamātmānaṃ kṛtvā ghoṣamakārayat || 1.173{63} ||
tathā tadghoṣaṇāṃ śrutvā ye vīrāste vaṇigjanāḥ |
tena sārddhaṃ mahotsāhairmahābdhiṃ gantumaichan || 1.174{64} ||
tato'sau puruṣo vīraḥ sarvaistaiśca vaṇigjanaiḥ |
saha ratnākaraṃ gatvā bahuratnānyasādhayat || 1.175{65} ||
tataḥ sārthapatiḥ sarvaiḥ sārthaiḥ sārddhaṃ mahodadheḥ |
kṣemena sahasottīryya svadeśaṃ samupāyayau || 1.176{66} ||
atha sārthapatistatra yātrāsiddhipramoditaḥ |
sarvān sārthān samāmaṃtrya dṛṣṭvaivaṃ samabhāṣata || 1.177{67} ||
bhavantaḥ śrūyatāṃ sarvairyatsvasti prāgatā vayam |
tatprabhāvaṃ vijānīdhvaṃ jinendrasya prasādatāḥ || 1.178{68} ||
tasmādasya munīndrasya kṛtvā darśanamādarāt |
pūjayitvā praṇatvā ca paścādgehaṃ vrajema hi |
iti tasya vacaḥ śrutvā sarvve'pi te vaṇigjanāḥ |
dṛṣṭasatyānumodantaḥ tatheti pratimenire |
iti saṃbhāṣaṇāṃ kṛtvā sarvvasārthasamanvitaḥ |
tataḥ sārthapatiḥ śīghraṃ vihāraṃ samupāyayau |
tatra dṛṣṭvā jinendraṃ taṃ sarvve te saṃpraharṣitāḥ |
pādau natvā munestasya parivṛtyopatasthire || 1.179{69} ||
athāsau bhagavān dṛṣṭvā sarvān sārthagaṇānapi |
dharmotsāhaprāvṛddhyarthaiḥ samāmaṃtrya samabravīt || 1.180{70} ||
āgatāḥ stha samāyāta mā stha śrāntāśca kheditāḥ |
yātrāsāphalyasiddhiśca kaccidvaḥ kuśalāni ca || 1.181{71} ||
evaṃ pṛṣṭe jinendreṇa sarve te saṃpramoditāḥ |
kṛtāñjalipuṭo natvā saṃbuddhaṃ procurādarāt || 1.182{72} ||
(rm 15)
bhagavan bhavatāmevaṃ kṛpādṛṣṭiprasādataḥ |
yātrāsiddhiḥ kathaṃ na syātsarvatra kuśalaṃ hi naḥ || 1.183{73} ||
yadvayaṃ śāstarāyātāḥ śubharatnasamanvitāḥ |
tatsarvaṃ tvatprabhāvena satyametatpramāṇyate || 1.184{74} ||
tadbhavantaṃ suśāstāraṃ draṣṭuṃ sarve samāgatāḥ |
pūjayituṃ samichāmastatprasīda jagadguro || 1.185{75} ||
iti vijñāpanāṃ kṛtvā saha sārthaiḥ sa sārthabhṛt |
sasaṃghaṃ pūjayitvā ca ratnāni samaḍhaukata || 1.186{76} ||
namaste bhagavannātha vrajāmaḥ śaraṇaṃ tava |
pāhi naḥ sarvadāpyevaṃ tvameva jagadīśvaraḥ || 1.187{77} ||
evaṃ stutvā munīndraṃ taṃ sarve te saṃprasāditāḥ |
kṛtāñjalipuṭā natvā svagṛhaṃ gantumīhire || 1.188{78} ||
tataśca bhagavān dṛṣṭvā tān sarvān svagṛhotsukān |
datvāśīrvacanaṃ spṛṣṭvā vrajateti vyasarjayat || 1.189{79} ||
tataḥ sarve'pi te sārthāḥ kṛtvā pradakṣiṇatrayam |
śāstāraṃ sugataṃ natvā svaṃ svaṃ gehaṃ samāyayuḥ || 1.190{80} ||
tato bhūyastathānyaiśca prārthyamānaḥ sa vīryyavān |
ṣaḍḍhā ratnākaraṃ gatvā bahuratnānyasādhayat || 1.191{81} ||
tataśca gṛhapatiḥ śreṣṭhī sārthavāhaḥ suvīryyavān |
sasaṅghaṃ sugataṃ gehe praṇītvārcitumārabhat || 1.192{[82]} ||
tato bhojanasāmagrīṃ sādhayitvā niveśane |
sasaṃghaiḥ buddhamāmaṃtrya nyaveśayacchubhāsane || 1.193{[83]} ||
tataḥ pūjopahāraiśca pūjayitvā vidhānataḥ |
pratyekaṃ cīvaraṃ datvā bhojanaiḥ samatoṣayat || 1.194{84!} ||
tataśca bhojanānte'sau sārthavāhaḥ kṛtāñjaliḥ |
sasaṃghaṃ sugataṃ natvā praṇidhānaṃ tathākarot || 1.195{85} ||
yatkiñcitprakṛtaṃ dānaṃ saṃbuddhaśāsane mayā |
etatpuṇyaphalena syāṃ saṃbuddho'yaṃ jino yathā || 1.196{86} ||
iti tasya gṛhasthasya cittaṃ saṃbodhivāṃchitam |
jñātvāsau bhagavān buddhaḥ smitaṃ kṛtvābhyanaṃdata || 1.197{87} ||
tadā bhagavato vaktrānniśceruḥ paṃcavarṇikāḥ |
(rm 16)
raśmayastāḥ samantācca trailokyaṃ śāmabhāsayan || 1.198{88} ||
yāḥ kāścidraśmayo yātā adhastānnarakeṣvapi |
tāśca niścaritāstāvatsaṃjīvaṃ kālasūtrakam || 1.199{[89]} ||
saṃghātaṃ rauravaṃ cāpi mahārauravaviśrutam |
tapanākhyaṃ tathā caivaṃ pratāpanāhvayaṃ tataḥ || 1.200{90} ||
avīcimarbudaṃ caivaṃ nirarbudaṃ tathāṭaṭam |
hahavaṃ huhuvaṃ caivamutpalaṃ padmakaṃ tataḥ || 1.201{91} ||
mahāpadmākhyametāni ṣoḍaśa nilayāni hi |
tatroṣṇanarakā ye tu teṣu sarveṣu niḥsṛtāḥ || 1.202{92} ||
śītībhūtā viniḥpatya prabhāsayan samaṃtataḥ |
ye śītanarakāsteṣu tūṣṇībhūtāpyabhāsayan || 1.203{93} ||
teṣu ye nārakāḥ satvā nānāduḥkhābhivedinaḥ |
te tābhī raśmibhiḥ spṛṣṭā mahatsaukhyaṃ prarebhire || 1.204{94} ||
atha sarve'pi te satvā mahatsaukhyasamanvitāḥ |
vismitāstatra saṃmīlya mithaścaivaṃ vabhāṣire || 1.205{95} ||
aho bhavanta āścaryyaṃ kiṃ cāsmākaṃ bhavetkhalu |
yadvayaṃ sarvadāpītthaṃ nānāduḥkhaiḥ prapīḍitāḥ |
tanmuktāḥ sāṃprataṃ sarve vayaṃ saukhyasamanvitāḥ || 1.206{96} ||
kiṃ tato hi vayaṃ muktā anyatra caritā nanu |
iti vismitacittānāṃ sarveṣāṃ cittabodhane || 1.207{97} ||
bhagavānnirmitaṃ teṣāṃ purastātsamadarśayat || 1.208{98} ||
tatra taṃ nirmitaṃ dṛṣṭvā sarve'pi te'tivismitāḥ |
parasparaṃ mukhaṃ dṛṣṭvā tathaivaṃ saṃvabhāṣire || 1.209{99} ||
bho bhavanta itaścyutvā nānyatra gamitā vayam |
kiṃ tvayaṃ sugatākāra āyāto'pūrvadarśanaḥ || 1.210{100} ||
nūnamasyānubhāvena vayamitthaṃ sukhānvitāḥ || 1.211{1} ||
iti saṃbhāṣya te sarve tasmiṃ saugatanirmite |
cittaṃ prasādya buddhāya nama iti praṇemire || 1.212{2} ||
tataste nirmitaṃ dṛṣṭvā natva cittaprasāditaḥ |
(rm 17)
sarvapāpavinirmuktāḥ sarve'pi sugatiṃ yayuḥ || 1.213{3} ||
yāśca kāścidgatā ūrddhvaṃ tā mahārājikān gatāḥ |
trayastriṃśāṃśca yāmāṃśca tuṣitāṃśca gatāstataḥ || 1.214{4} ||
nirmāṇaratimābhāsya nirmitavaśavarttikān |
brahmakāyikalokāṃśca gatā brahmapurohitān || 1.215{5} ||
mahābrahmālayaṃ cāpi parītābhāṃstathā gatāḥ |
apramāṇābhalokāṃśca tata ābhāsvarān gatāḥ || 1.216{6} ||
parīttaśubhalokāṃstadāpramāṇaśubhān gatāḥ |
śubhakṛtsnānanabhrāṃśca puṇyaprasavakāṃstataḥ || 1.217{7} ||
bṛhatphalāṃstataścaivamavṛhānatapān gatāḥ |
ḥsudṛśāṃśca tato yātā tathā caivaṃ sudarśanān || 1.218{8} ||
akaniṣṭhaṃ tathā gatvā sarvānetān vyabhāsayan |
teṣu ye saṃsthitā lokāḥ sarve tābhiśca raśmibhiḥ || 1.219{9} ||
parispṛṣṭāḥ sukhaprāptā dṛṣṭvā tāṃ caivamūcire |
aho citraṃ śubhā kāmyā kasya puṇyaprabhā imāḥ || 1.220{10} ||
yābhirvayaṃ parispṛṣṭā mahatsaukhyaṃ labhāmahe |
iti vismitacittānāṃ teṣāṃ cittavinodane || 1.221{11} ||
tathā tā raśmayaścaināṃ gāthābhiḥ samacodayan |
anityaṃ khalu saṃsāraṃ duḥkhaṃ śūnyaṃ hyanātmakam || 1.222{12} ||
ataḥ kleśagaṇāṃstyaktvā bhajadhvaṃ sugataṃ sadā |
niṣkrāmatārabhadhvaṃ kaṃ yujyadhvaṃ buddhaśāsane || 1.223{13} ||
dhunīta mārasainyāṃśca naḍāgāramiva dvipaḥ |
(rm 18)
yo hyasmiṃ dharmavaineye'tyapramattaścaretsudhīḥ || 1.224{14} ||
prahāya sa bhave kleśān duḥkhasyāntaṃ kariṣyati |
iti tā raśmayaḥ sarvā avabhāsya samantataḥ || 1.225{15} ||
prerayitvā śubhe satvān punarmuneḥ purogatāḥ |
tato bhagavatastasya kṛtvā trayapradakṣiṇām || 1.226{16} ||
tathā tā raśmayaḥ sarvā uṣṇīṣe'ntarhitaṃ yayuḥ |
athānandaḥ samutthāya kṛtāñjalipuṭo mudā || 1.227{17} ||
bhagavantaṃ praṇamyaivaṃ papraccha smitakāraṇam |
bhagavaṃstava śubhā raśmiḥ smitodbhavā vinirgatā || 1.228{18} ||
yayāvabhāsitā lokāḥ sūryyeṇodayatā yathā |
nākasmāddarśayantyevaṃ smitaṃ buddhā jināḥ kvacit || 1.229{19} ||
tatkasmādbhagavān smitaṃ saṃdarśayati sāṃpratam |
yasyārthe bhavatāpyevaṃ smitaṃ saṃdarśitaṃ tathā || 1.230{20} ||
taṃ dṛṣṭvā vismitāḥ sarve śrotumichanti sajjanāḥ |
tadatra bhagavāñchāstā hyesāṃ dharmābhikāṃkṣiṇām || 1.231{21} ||
sarveṣāṃ tatsamādiśya saṃdehaṃ chettumarhati |
ityānaṃdoditaṃ śrutvā bhagavāṃśca tamabravīt || 1.232{22} ||
evametattathānaṃda yathā tvaṃ bhāṣase kila |
nākāraṇe jinaṃ sarvaṃ smitaṃ saṃdarśitaṃ kvacit || 1.233{23} ||
tadarthaṃ śṛṇu vakṣye'haṃ yadarthaṃ saṃsmitaṃ mama |
paśyānanda gṛhastho'yaṃ dārako yaḥ kuśīditaḥ || 1.234{24} ||
mama saṃdarśanādevaṃ viryyotsāhaṃ samāpnuvan |
itthaṃ ratnāni saṃsādhya buddhimāṃśca samṛddhitaḥ || 1.235{25} ||
evaṃ śrāddhaḥ prasannātmā śāsane me'numoditaḥ |
satkṛtya śaraṇaṃ kṛtvā bhajati māṃ sasāṃghikam || 1.236{26} ||
yadayaṃ sārthabhṛnnando mama dharmābhisaṃrataḥ |
(rm 19)
asya puṇyavipākena bodhicittaṃ samāpnuyāt || 1.237{27} ||
tataḥ cikṣāḥ samāsādya pūryya pāramitā daśa |
kramānmāragaṇāñjitvā saṃbodhiṃ samavāpnuyāt || 1.238{28} ||
tato'rhannabhivandyo'tibalavīryyaparākramaḥ |
iti nāmnā prasiddho'yaṃ saṃbuddhaḥ sugato jinaḥ || 1.239{29} ||
sarvadharmādhipaḥ śāstā sarvavidyāguṇākaraḥ |
sarvajñastrijagadbharttā tathāgato bhaviṣyati || 1.240{30} ||
tasmādevaṃ sadānaṃda karttavyaṃ buddhadarśanam |
buddhadarśanapuṇyena dharmotsāhaṃ samāpuṇyāt || 1.241{31} ||
tato vīryyasamārūḍhaḥ saddharmaṃ sādhayenmudā |
tato dharmabalairmārāñjitvā saṃbodhimāpnuyāt || 1.242{32} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa śrutvānaṃdaḥ sasāṃghikaḥ |
anumodya guruṃ natvā tatheti prābhyanaṃdata || 1.243{33} ||
evaṃ me guruṇādiṣṭaṃ dharmotsāhapravṛddhaye |
tathā tava mayā khyātaṃ satyametatpradhāryyatām || 1.244{34} ||
evaṃ rājaṃstvayāpyevaṃ kṛtvānumodanāṃ tathā |
dharmaprotsāhanāṃ kṛtvā karttavyaṃ buddhadarśanam || 1.245{35} ||
saddharmacaraṇotsāhe cāraṇīyāḥ prajāḥ sadā |
ityetadguruṇādiṣṭaṃ śrutvāśokaḥ sa bhūmipaḥ || 1.246{36} ||
satyamevaṃ pratiśrutya prābhyanandatsapārṣadaḥ || 1.247{37!} ||
tatkauśīdyāvadānaṃ munivarakathitaṃ śraddhayā yaḥ śṛṇoti |
śrutvā yaḥ śrāvayedyaḥ pratidinamaniśaṃ buddhasevānuraktaḥ |
hitvā kleśān praduṣṭān sakalakalivalānmārapakṣāṃśca jitvā |
pāraṃ gatvā guṇābdhervrajati sa subhagaṃ śrīghanasya caritram |

++ iti ratnamālāyāṃ kauśīdyavīryyotsāhanāvadānaṃ ++


(rm 20)
ii snātāvadāna
athāśoko nṛpendro'sau saddharmacaraṇotsavaḥ |
upaguptaṃ guruṃ natvā kṛtāñjalipuṭo'vadat || 2.1{1} ||
bhadanta śrotumichāmi punaranyatsubhāṣitam |
yathā te guruṇā khyātaṃ tathā me khyātumarhasi || 2.2{2} ||
iti tena nṛpeśena prārthyamāno jināṃśajaḥ |
upaguptaḥ samāmaṃtrya taṃ kṣitīśaṃ samabravīt || 2.3{3} ||
sādhu suṣṭhu mahāraja śṛṇu dharmaṃ samāhitaḥ |
yathā me guruṇādiṣṭaṃ tathātra te pracakṣyate || 2.4{5!} ||
puraikasamaye yo'bhūcchākyasiṃho dayākaraḥ |
dharmarājo jagacchāstā sarvajñaḥ sugato jinaḥ || 2.5{6} ||
sarvavidyākalāvijñaḥ ṣaḍabhijño munīśvaraḥ |
samantabhadrakṛnnātho vināyakastathāgataḥ || 2.6{7} ||
sa śrāvastyāṃ mahodyāne jetārāme manohare |
anāthapiṇḍadasyaiva vihāre maṇimaṇḍite || 2.7{8} ||
śrāvakairbhikṣubhiḥ sārddhaṃ bodhisatvairupāsakaiḥ |
devāsuramahārājairmanujendraṃ sapārṣadaiḥ || 2.8{9} ||
sarvalokādhipaiścāpi traidhātukanivāsitaiḥ |
caturvarṇaiśca pauraiśca tathānyaiśca śubhārthibhiḥ || 2.9{10} ||
satkṛto mānitaścābhivanditaḥ saṃstuto'rcitaḥ |
gurukṛtaḥ puraskṛtya parivṛto'valokitaḥ || 2.10{11} ||
ādimadhyāntakalyāṇaṃ dharmaṃ loke prakāśayan |
sarvasatvahitārthena vijahāra samāhitaḥ || 2.11{12} ||
tasmiṃśca samaye tatra śrāvastyāṃ ye vaṇigjanāḥ |
te paṃcaśatamātrāṇi ratnākaraṃ samāgatāḥ || 2.12{13} ||
tataḥ pratyāgatāḥ sarve kāntāraṃ samupāviśan |
tatra te bhramitā mārgātparibhraṣṭā vicerire || 2.13{14} ||
vimārgeṇa carantaste vālukāsthalamupāviśan |
tatra praptāśca te sarve madhyāhne dyotitātape || 2.14{15} ||
bhānostīkṣṇakaraistaptā gharmatāpābhikheditāḥ |
(rm 21)
kṣīṇapathyādanāścāpi kṣuttṛṣṇā paripīḍitāḥ || 2.15{16} ||
tīvraduḥkhābhisaṃtaptāḥ paryāvarttanta tatra ca |
sthalaprāptā yathā mīnā mṛtyubhayavikaṃpitāḥ || 2.16{17} ||
tadā sarve'pi te sārthāḥ śraddhayā śaraṇaṃ gatāḥ |
svasvakuleśasvasveṣṭadevatāḥ saṃyayācire || 2.17{18} ||
kecitsvayaṃbhuvaṃ smṛtvā kecinnārāyaṇaṃ tathā |
kecicchivaṃ mahāraudraṃ kecitsūryyagrahādhipam || 2.18{19} ||
keciccaṃdraṃ ca tāreśaṃ kecidiṃdraṃ surādhipam |
kecidagniṃ yamaṃ caiva naiṛtyaṃ ca tathāpare || 2.19{20} ||
vāruṇaṃ ca tathā vāyukuveraṃ ca maheśvaraiḥ |
bhairavaṃ mātṛkāścaivaṃ kecitskandhaṃ vināyakam || 2.20{21} ||
mahākālaṃ tathā caivaṃ kecidṛṣīn yatīśvarān |
keciddaityādhipān vīrān kecinnāgādhipāṃstathā || 2.21{22} ||
keciccāturmahārājān kecidyakṣādhipān tathā |
evamanyāṃstridhātusthāṃssarvān smṛtvā yayācire || 2.22{23} ||
tairevaṃ yācyamānānāṃ devatānaṃ na kaścana |
eko'pyāsīttadā trātā vāṇijāṃ duḥkhabhāginām || 2.23{24} ||
tataste vaṇijaḥ sarve mṛtyubhayavikaṃpitāḥ |
nirāśayā viṣīdantastasthurniśceṣṭamānasāḥ || 2.24{25} ||
tatra yaḥ sugatopāsī dṛṣṭasatyaḥ sa dhīravān |
tān sārthān vipannāśāṃ dṛṣṭvāmaṃtryaivamabravīt || 2.25{26} ||
bhavanto mā viṣīdadhvaṃ kiṃ viṣādena setsyate |
tasmāttyaktvā viṣādatvaṃ dhīryyamālambya tiṣṭhata || 2.26{27} ||
dhairyyeṇa tārayedduḥkhaṃ mahākṛcchraṃ vipatsvapi |
tasmāddhairyyaṃ samādhāya smaradhvaṃ ca jinaṃ drutam || 2.27{28} ||
jina eva jagannāthastrātā sarvānukaṃpakaḥ |
tadasmadvacanaṃ śrutvā saṃbuddhaṃ smaratādarāt || 2.28{29} ||
(rm 22)
tatrāpyevaṃ bhaye'smākaṃ trātā ko'nyo bhaviṣyati |
buddha eva jagadbaṃdhustadvrajadhvaṃ jinaṃ drutam || 2.29{30} ||
sarvathā no jinendro'sau sarvasatvānukanipakaḥ |
ḍṛṣṭvāsmān karuṇādṛṣṭyā saṃrakṣetsahasā khalu || 2.30{31} ||
iti tenoditaṃ śrutva sarve te vaṇijastathā |
saṃbuddhaṃ śaraṇaṃ kṛtvā smṛtvābhayaṃ yayācire || 2.31{32} ||
namaste bhagavannātha sarvajño'si jagatprabhuḥ |
bhavataiva jagatsarvaṃ pālitaṃ putravatsadā || 2.32{33} ||
adyāgreṇa vayaṃ sarve yāvajjīvaṃ sadāpi hi |
bhavatāṃ śaraṇaṃ yāmastadrakṣāsmāñjagadguro || 2.33{34} ||
bhavāneva jagaddharmaṃ saṃsāre pālitaṃ sadā |
bhagavan sarvavinnātha trātumarhasi sarvathā || 2.34{35} ||
paṃcaśatasarvāṇi vāṇijāśca samāgatāḥ |
vimārgeṇa carantaste madhyāhne dyotitātape || 2.35{36} ||
tatra prāptāśca te sarve vālukāsthale saṃsthitāḥ |
bhāno tīkṣṇakare taptā kṣuttṛṣārttā paripīḍitāḥ || 2.36{37} ||
tīvrātivedanākrāntā mṛtyubhayaṃ samāgatāḥ |
namo buddhāya dharmāya saṃghāyeti namo namaḥ || 2.37{38} ||
dṛṣṭvāsmān karuṇā sarvān paṃcaśatāni vāṇijān |
bhavāneva jagatsvāmiṃ tathā buddhānubhāvatām || 2.38{39} ||
tadā sa bhagavān tebhya māhendravṛṣṭimācaran |
śītalā vāyavo yāti tena śītatvamāgatāḥ || 2.39{40} ||
taddṛṣṭvā sārthapatinā sarvān sārthān samabravīt |
bhavantaḥ śrūyatāṃ sarve yanmayā procyate hitam || 2.40{41} ||
tadbhavadbhiḥ samādhṛtvā karttavyaṃ kṛtavedibhiḥ |
yadvayaṃ mṛtyusaṃprāptā paralokādivāgatāḥ || 2.41{42} ||
tadjñeyaṃ jinendrasya kṛpādṛṣṭiprasādataḥ |
tasmātsarve vayaṃ tasya buddhasya śaraṇaṃ gatāḥ || 2.42{43} ||
saddharmmaṃ samupāśritya caremahi sadāpi hi |
anityaṃ khalu saṃsāramanityaṃ jīvitaṃ sukham || 2.43{44} ||
kṣaṇadhvaṃsi śarīraṃ ca bhave nityaṃ na kiṃ cana |
dharma eva mahāsāraṃ yenaivaṃ rakṣyate jagat || 2.44{45} ||
tasmāddharmaṃ samādhartuṃ gantavyaṃ śaraṇaṃ muneḥ |
iti tenoditaṃ śrutvā sarve te vaṇijo mudā || 2.45{46} ||
tathetyabhyanumodantastato gaṃtumupākramat |
atha sārthapatiścaivaṃ kṛtvā taṃ saha saṃmatam || 2.46{47} ||
(rm 23)
vihāre sugataṃ draṣṭuṃ dharmaṃ cāptumagānmudā |
tatropetya praviṣṭo'sau sarvasārthagaṇānvitaḥ || 2.47{48} ||
dṛṣṭvā taṃ sugataṃ natvā viṣasāda puro muneḥ |
athāsau bhagavāṃsteṣāṃ dṛṣṭvāśayaviśuddhatām |
āryyasatyāryyamārgāṅgaṃ dideśa dharmamuttamam || 2.48{49} ||
tadā sarve'pi te satvāḥ śrutvā taṃ dharmamādarāt |
satkāyadṛṣṭinirmuktā bodhicittaṃ pralebhire || 2.49{50} ||
tatra kecinmahāśrāddhā dharmānusāriṇo'bhavan |
śrotāpannāstathā kecitsakṛdāgāmino pare || 2.50{51} ||
tathānāgāminaḥ kecitkecidbhavaparāṅmukhāḥ |
saṃsārakleśasaṃghāṃśca hitvā suvimalāśayāḥ || 2.51{52} ||
sākṣātsaddharmmamāsādya babhūvurdharmacāriṇaḥ |
kecicchrāvakabodhau ca pratyekāyāṃ tathāpare || 2.52{53} ||
kecidanuttarāyāṃ ca saṃbodhau praṇidhiṃ dadhuḥ |
evaṃ tāḥ parṣadaḥ sarvāḥ saṃbuddhaśaraṇe sthitāḥ || 2.53{54} ||
saddharmmācaraṇe raktāḥ saṃghabhaktā babhūvire |
atha te bhikṣavo dṛṣṭvā tān sarvān bodhicāriṇaḥ || 2.54{55} ||
saṃbuddhaṃ prayatā natvā paprachurvismayānvitāḥ |
āścaryyaṃ bhagavan yāvadyadime vaṇijastathā || 2.55{56} ||
kāṃtārapatitāḥ sarve bhavatā paripālitāḥ |
bhavaccintitamātre'pi māhendravṛṣṭimācaran || 2.56{57} ||
śītalā vāyavo vātāstatkathaṃ vaktumarhasi |
iti tairbhikṣubhiḥ pṛṣṭo bhagavāṃstānabhāṣata || 2.57{58} ||
śṛṇudhvaṃ bhikṣavaḥ karmma yanmayā kṛta kṛtaṃ kathyate |
yanmayā prakṛtaṃ karma ko'nyo bhuṃjyāddhi tatphalam || 2.58{59} ||
yena yatprakṛtaṃ karma sa evādyāddhi tatphalam |
abhuktaṃ kṣīyate karma naiva kvāpi kathaṃ cana || 2.59{60} ||
(rm 24)
agnibhirdahyate naiva vāyubhirnāpi śuṣyate |
udakaiḥ klidyate nāpi bhūmiṣu || 2.60{61} ||
api tu pacyate skandhaṣaḍdhātvāyataneṣu hi |
anyathā cāpi karmāṇi na dadanti phalāni ca || 2.61{62} ||
yathaiva yatkṛtaṃ karma tathaiva tatphalaṃ matam |
śubhakarma śubhaṃ dadyātpāpakarmāśubhaṃ khalu || 2.62{63} ||
miśritamubhayaṃ dadyāditi matvā śubhe caret |
na praṇaśyaṃti karmāṇi kalpakoṭiśatairapi || 2.63{64} ||
sāmagrīṃ prāpya kālaṃ ca phalanti khalu dehinām |
yanmayā prakṛtaṃ pūrvaṃ tatphalaṃ prāpyate'dhunā || 2.64{65} ||
kathyate tatpurāvṛttaṃ sādhu śṛṇuta sādaram |
puraikasamaye buddhaścaṃdano nāma dharmarāṭ || 2.65{66} ||
sarvajño'rhañjagannāthaḥ śāstā lokādhipeśvaraḥ |
vidyācaraṇasaṃpannaḥ sugato lokavijjinaḥ || 2.66{67} ||
tathāgato mahābhijñaḥ saṃbuddho'bhūdvināyakaḥ |
tadaikasamaye tatra caṃdanaḥ sa munīśvaraḥ || 2.67{38!} ||
bhikṣubhiḥ saha saddharmmaṃ prakāśayan samaṃtataḥ |
hitāya sarvasatvānāṃ jānapadeṣu saṃcaran || 2.68{69} ||
anyatamāṃ purīṃ ramyāṃ rājadhānīmupāśrayat |
tatra satvahitārthena bodhicaryāṃ prakāśayan || 2.69{70} ||
śrāvakairbhikṣubhiḥ sārddhaṃ vijahāra jināśrame |
atha tadādhipo rājā śrutvā taṃ buddhamāgatam || 2.70{71} ||
sa maṃtrijanapauraiśca saha pratyudyayau mudā |
tatrāsau sahasopetya dṛṣṭvā taṃ sugataṃ munim || 2.71{72} ||
kṛtāṃjalipuṭo natvā tasthāvekāṃtike mudā |
athāsau bhagavān dṛṣṭvā taṃ nṛpaṃ samupasthitam || 2.72{73} ||
ādimadhyāntakalyāṇaṃ dideśa dharmamuttamam |
śṛṇu rājan bhave sāraṃ dharmaṃ nityamataṃdritam || 2.73{74} ||
dharmeṇa jāyate loko dharmeṇa svargamāruhet |
tasmāddharmaṃ puraskṛtya saṃpālayan prajā sadā || 2.74{75} ||
prajārakṣāvrataṃ dharmaṃ mūlaṃ rājñāṃ vidurbudhāḥ |
prajārakṣāvrataṃ tyaktvā kimanyairbahubhirvrataiḥ || 2.75{76} ||
(rm 25)
tena rājan tvayā dharme pālanīyāḥ prajāḥ śubhe |
taddaśākuśalebhyaśca vinivārya tvayā sadā || 2.76{77} ||
yojanīyāḥ prajā dharme tatpuṇyaistvaṃ pramokṣyase |
ādau dāne prajān yojyāḥ saṃbodhipadakāraṇe || 2.77{78} ||
tatpuṇyaiḥ pracariṣyanti prajāḥ saṃvarddhitāḥ śubhe |
śubhakarmarate loke tatpuṇyaiḥ varddhyate nṛpaḥ || 2.78{79} ||
tasmāddānaṃ svayaṃ datvā prajā śīle niyojaya |
śīlena śuddhyate kāyaḥ śuddhakāyo bhavetsudhīḥ || 2.79{80} ||
subuddhiḥ kṣamayā sarvācchatrūṃ jitvā virājate |
tato vīryyabalaiḥ sarvān satvān saukhye niyojayet || 2.80{81} ||
tataḥ kleśān vinirjitya dhyātvā bodhiṃ prasādhayet |
bodhipraṇidhicittena kṛtī prajñāṃ prasādhayet || 2.81{82} ||
tataḥ prājña upāyaiśca jagadbodhau niyojayet |
tato mārān vinirjitya saṃbodhijñānamāpnuyāt || 2.82{83} ||
tatra prāpya rasābhijñā daśabhūmīśvaro bhavet |
tataḥ kṛtvā vaśe sarvāṃstraidhātukanivāśinaḥ || 2.83{84} ||
saṃsthāpya saugate dharme svayaṃ nirvṛtimācaret || 2.84{85} ||
evaṃ jñātvā mahārāja nirvāṇagatisādhanam |
yadi te nirvṛtau vāṃchā tadaitāsu samācara || 2.85{86} ||
tataste maṃgalaṃ nityaṃ saṃvṛttisukhamāpnuyāḥ |
kramātpāramitāḥ pūryya nirvṛtiṃ samavāpnuyāḥ || 2.86{87} ||
nirvāṇaṃ paramaṃ jñānaṃ nirvāṇaṃ paramaṃ sukham |
nirvāṇaṃ paramaṃ puṇyaṃ nirvāṇaṃ paramāṃ gatim || 2.87{88} ||
evaṃ hi sukṛtaṃ sarvaṃ jñātvā nirvāṇakāraṇam |
tathā dharme svayaṃ sthitvā preraṇīyāḥ prajā api || 2.88{89} ||
evaṃ sa caṃdano buddho bodhayitvā ca taṃ nṛpam |
bodhimārge pratisthāpya virarāma kṣaṇaṃ tataḥ || 2.89{90} ||
atha sa nṛpatī rājā śrutvā taddharmamādarāt |
pramoditaḥ samutthāya uttarāsaṃgamudvahan || 2.90{91} ||
jānubhyāṃ bhuvi saṃsthitvā kṛtāṃjalipuṭo nataḥ |
(rm 26)
suprasannamukhaḥ pādau natvā taṃ prārthayanmunim || 2.91{92} ||
namaste bhagavannātha bhavatāṃ śaraṇaṃ vraje |
saddharmamādiśannatra vijayasva gaṇaiḥ saha || 2.92{93} ||
iti tena nṛpendreṇa prārthite sa munīśvaraḥ |
tatheti pratisaṃśrutya traimāsyamadhyavāsayat || 2.93{94} ||
tataḥ sāntaḥpurā rājā taṃ munīndraṃ sasāṃghikam |
sadā satkārapūjāṅgairbhojyaiḥ prārāgayanmudā || 2.94{95} ||
tasmiṃśca samaye tatra lokānācarakopitāḥ |
varṣādhidevatā meghāvṛṣṭiṃ na mumucurbhuvi || 2.95{96} ||
tatastasyāmanāvṛṣṭyāṃ taḍāgādi jalāśrayāḥ |
dine dine prahīnāṃbhaḥ paṃkaśeṣaviśoṣitāḥ || 2.96{97} ||
niḥpuṣpā niṣphalā vṛkṣāḥ śākhāpattraviśīrṇṇitāḥ || 2.97{97!} ||
sthāṇukalpā nirābhogyāḥ pakṣisaṃpātavarjitāḥ |
mahīśasyavipannāśca bījāṃkulaviśoṣitāḥ || 2.98{98} ||
nistṛṇā prakhalībhūtā marukalpābhavattadā |
annapānaviyogena mṛtā anekajantavaḥ || 2.99{99} ||
kṣutpipāsahatāḥ kecinnyaṣīdanta mṛtā iva |
kecidgehe viṣaṇṇāsyā mṛtyuśaṃkāviṣāditāḥ || 2.100{100} ||
parasparaṃ mukhaṃ dṛṣṭvā niḥśvasanto niṣedire |
kecinmeghaṃ ca khe dṛṣṭvā prāyācanta divāniśam || 2.101{1} ||
hā hatāsma iti proktvā kecittasthurnirāśitāḥ |
evaṃ nānāprakāreṇa vilapanto vikheditāḥ || 2.102{2} ||
sarve'pi paurikāstatra cikhedurmṛtyuśaṃkitāḥ |
taddṛṣṭvā nṛpatiścāpi mṛtyutrastānukaṃpitaḥ || 2.103{3} ||
maṃtriṇaḥ sacivān sarvān samāmaṃtryābravīditi |
hā kaṣṭaṃ kathamatraivaṃ durbhikṣaṃ varttate'dhunā |
kiṃ karomi na manye'haṃ vadadhvaṃ tatpratikriyām || 2.104{4} ||
yena tadā mahāpāpadurbhikṣaṃ śāmyate drutam |
tadupāyaṃ prakuryāma tatkurudhvaṃ yathāvidhim || 2.105{5} ||
(rm 27)
iti śrutvā nṛpājñāṃ te samaṃtrijanapaurikāḥ |
devatā vidhinārādhya tacchāntiṃ saṃyayācire || 2.106{6} ||
bho devatāḥ prasīdadhvaṃ namo vaḥ praṇatā vayam |
aparādhaṃ yadasmāsu kṣantavyaṃ sarvathāpi tat || 2.107{7} ||
imaṃ durbhikṣanirvṛṣṭiṃ prāptaṃ śamayata drutam |
suvṛṣṭiṃ kārayitvātra subhikṣaṃ kuruta dhruvam || 2.108{8} ||
evaṃ kecidvidhātāraṃ kecidviṣṇuṃ janārdanam |
kecicchivaṃ maheśānamiṃdraṃ kecitsurādhipam || 2.109{9} ||
kecidgrahādhipaṃ kecillokapālānmaharddhikān |
kecicca mātṛkādevīṃ skaṃdavighneśabhairavān || 2.110{10} ||
mahākālagaṇāṃścāpi kecinnāgāñjalādhipān |
kecinmeghān samālokya natvā vṛṣṭiṃ yayācire || 2.111{11} ||
evaṃ sarve'pi lokāśca svasveṣṭakuladevatāḥ |
vidhinārādhya saṃprārthya suvṛṣṭiṃ prayayācire || 2.112{12} ||
evaṃ taiḥ prārthyamānāstāḥ sarvāśca devatā api |
tatra vṛṣṭiṃ samutsraṣṭuṃ naiva śekurvighātitāḥ || 2.113{13} ||
tataḥ sarve ca te lokā dṛṣṭvā tadvṛṣṭighātitām |
hāhākāravirāvanto vicikhedurnirāśitāḥ || 2.114{14} ||
atha rājā sa tān dṛṣṭvā durbhikṣākṣobhitāśāyān |
tathā ca grahāmālokya visaṇṇāsyo vyacintayat || 2.115{15} ||
aho kaṣṭaṃ mahotpātaṃ mama rājye bhaviṣyati |
kathamatra pratīkāraṃ tatpraśāntyai kariṣyate || 2.116{16} ||
kiṃ mayā nṛpabhūtena yaśodharmavighātinā |
na śakyaṃte prajā yena rakṣitumītipīḍite |
dhigmāṃ nṛpādhamībhūtaṃ kiṃ pāpaṃ prakṛtaṃ mayā |
yena yasya prajā eva kṣutpipāsahatā mṛtāḥ |
kimeva mama rājye na bhogyaiścāpi dhanairapi |
kiṃ tena jīvitenāpi yaśodharmavināśinā || 2.117{18} ||
yena rājñā na rakṣyante prajāḥ svaviṣayāśritāḥ |
sa kiṃ rājā baliṣṭho'pi kṛtānta iva manyate || 2.118{19} ||
(rm 28)
yaśca rājā svabhogyārthaṃ kevalaṃ daṇḍamāharan |
na vipatsu prajāḥ pāti sa kiṃ rājāpi cauravat || 2.119{20} ||
yeṣāmeva hitārthāya yaśca rājābhiṣicyate |
sa teṣāṃ na hitaṃ kuryyātsa kiṃ rājā kṛtārthahā || 2.120{21} ||
dhikpravādahataḥ so'tra yaśodharmasukhārthahā |
svayaṃ naṣṭaḥ parān hatvā narakeṣu vrajeddhruvam || 2.121{21!} ||
kiṃ cāhamapi mānuṣyaḥ samāna iṃdriyairapi |
yato me kriyate sarvaiḥ sevo nityaṃ samāhitaiḥ || 2.122{22} ||
yadi na kriyate rakṣā mayāpyeṣāṃ vipatsvapi |
kathametadṛśairmuktaḥ kadā yāsyāmi sadgatim || 2.123{23} ||
sarvadaitadṛṇairbaddhaḥ sarvathā pratibaṃdhitaḥ |
karmabhiḥ preryyamāṇo'haṃ yāsyāmi narakān dhruvam || 2.124{24} ||
tadāhaṃ kiṃ kariṣyāmi yamadūtairadhiṣṭhite |
dṛṣṭvā tāṃ bhīṣaṇāṃ krūrān patiṣyāmi vimohitaḥ || 2.125{25} ||
tadā tatra na vidyeta kaścittrātā dayānvitaḥ |
dharma eva tadā rakṣettaddharmaṃ sādhayecchubham || 2.126{26} ||
dharmāśca bahudhā proktāsteṣāṃ prāhurmunīśvarāḥ |
ādimadhyāntakalyāṇaṃ saugataṃ dharmamuttamam || 2.127{27} ||
yenaiva śubhatāṃ kṛtvā sarvatra bhuvaneṣvapi |
sarvasatvāśca pālyante sarvadeha paratra ca || 2.128{28} ||
tathātrāpi śubhaṃ kartuṃ saṃbuddhaṃ śaraṇaṃ vrajan |
saddharmaṃ samavāptuṃ ca sevitavyaṃ sasāṃghike || 2.129{29} ||
tenātra sahasā kṛtvā puṣkaraṇīmanoramām |
saṃbuddhasugataṃ tatra snāpayitvā sasāṃghikam || 2.130{30} ||
mahatsatkārapūjābhirbhojanaiśca pratoṣayan |
tatastaṃ sugataṃ natvā taddurbhikṣaṃ praśāṃtaye || 2.131{31} ||
suvṛṣṭicāraṇe loke prārthayiṣyāmi sādaram |
tadā sa bhagavānatra suvṛṣṭiṃ cārayeddhruvam || 2.132{32} ||
sarvatra śubhatāṃ kṛtvā subhikṣaṃ kārayetsadā |
tato'tra sarvadāpyevaṃ durbhikṣaṃ na kadā cana || 2.133{33} ||
subhikṣaṃ sarvato bhadraṃ bhavennūnaṃ samaṃtataḥ |
(rm 29)
iti dhyātvā viniścitya manasā sa narādhipaḥ || 2.134{34} ||
sahasā sacivān sarvān samāmaṃtryābravīttathā |
śṛṇudhvaṃ maṃtriṇo yanme manasā saṃviciṃtitam || 2.135{35} ||
tadanumodya yuṣmābhiḥ prakarttavyaṃ samāhitaiḥ |
yadadya jāyate'vṛṣṭiṃ durbhikṣaṃ copavarttate || 2.136{36} ||
tadasmatpāpato nūnaṃ tatpāpaparihāṇaye |
saṃbuddhaśaraṇaṃ kṛtvā saddharmasamavāptaye || 2.137{37} ||
sasaṃghaṃ sevituṃ nityamiche tadanumodyatām |
sa eva bhagavānnāthaḥ kṛtvātra śubhatāṃ sadā || 2.138{38} ||
suvṛṣṭiṃ cārayitvā ca subhikṣaṃ kārayeddhruvam |
tato'smākaṃ tadāpyatra maṃgalaṃ syātsamaṃtataḥ || 2.139{39} ||
sadā saddharmamādhāya saukhyaṃ bhuktvā bhajemahi |
dharmeṇa śubhatā nityaṃ dharmeṇa jīyate vipat || 2.140{40} ||
dharmeṇa pālyate sarvaṃ dharmeṇa sadgatiṃ vrajet |
dharmaṃ tu saugataṃ bhadraṃ sarve prāhurmunīśvarāḥ || 2.141{41} ||
saddharmaṃ samavāptuṃ taṃ saṃbuddhaṃ bhajatādarāt |
buddha eva jagadbaṃdhuḥ sarvasatvahitaṃkaraḥ || 2.142{42} ||
buddha eva jaganmitraṃ sarvasatvasukhapradaḥ |
buddha eva jagattrātā sarvasatvānukaṃpakaḥ || 2.143{43} ||
buddha eva jagadbhartā dharmārthasukhasaṃpradaḥ |
buddha eva jagacchāstā sarvasattvānubodhakaḥ |
buddha eva jagannātho bodhisaṃbhārapūrakaḥ |
buddha eva jagatsvāmī saṃbodhimārgadeśakaḥ |
buddha eva mahāvīraḥ sarvamāraprabhaṃjakaḥ || 2.144{44} ||
buddha eva mahāsatvaḥ ṣaḍabhijño jagatprabhuḥ |
buddha eva trilokeṣu dharmarūjā munīśvaraḥ |
yenaiva trijagallokaṃ pālitaṃ putravatsadā || 2.145{47!} ||
sa eva bhagavān buddhaḥ sevanīyaḥ śubhārthibhiḥ |
yenaivaṃ triṣu lokeṣu sarvalokādhipeśvaraḥ || 2.146{48} ||
rājate jayate tena siddho'yaṃ dharmmarājjinaḥ |
yathā ca triṣu lokeṣu bodhicaryāḥ prakāśayan |
(rm 30)
karoti bhadratāṃ nityaṃ tathātrāpi kariṣyati || 2.147{19} ||
saṃsthāpayati saddharme yathā traidhātuvāsinaḥ |
satvān prabodhayannasmānniveśayettathā khalu || 2.148{20} ||
tataḥ sarve vayaṃ dhṛtvā saddharmaṃ śubhadaṃ śivam |
yaśopuṇyasukhaṃ bhuktvā yāsyāmo'nte sukhāvatīm || 2.149{21} ||
iti matvā tathāsmābhiścandano'yaṃ jineśvaraḥ |
mahatsatkārapūjābhiḥ sevitavyo mudā sadā || 2.150{22} ||
iti saṃbhāṣya taiḥ sarvaistathetyabhyanumoditaiḥ |
maṃtribhiḥ saha rājā sa prayayau tajjināśramam || 2.151{23} ||
tatra prāptaḥ praviṣṭaśca dṛṣṭvā taṃ caṃdanaṃ munim |
kṛtāṃjalipuṭo natvā cakāra prārthanāmiti || 2.152{24} ||
namaste bhagavacchāstaḥ vayaṃ te śaraṇaṃ gatāḥ |
tatprasanno bhavāsmākaṃ kṣantavyā cāparādhatā || 2.153{24} ||
yadavṛṣṭiḥ pravṛttātra tadasmatpāpataḥ khalu |
tatpāpaparihāṇāya saddharmaṃ deṣṭumarhasi || 2.154{26} ||
tadbhavantaṃ vayaṃ sarve snāpayitvā sasāṃghikān |
yathāśaktyupacāraiśca samichāmo'rcituṃ pure || 2.155{27} ||
tatprasīda munīndro'si yathāsmākaṃ hitaṃ bhavet |
tathāsmatpuramāgatvā śubhaṃ kartuṃ samarhasi || 2.156{28} ||
iti tenārthitaṃ rājñā śrutvā sa caṃdano muniḥ |
tūṣṇībhūtvā tathetyevamadhyuvāsa sa sāṃghikaḥ || 2.157{29} ||
tataḥ sa nṛpatirjñātvā tadadhivāsitaṃ muneḥ |
pādau natvā punardṛṣṭvā sahasā svaṃ puraṃ yayau || 2.158{60!} ||
tataḥ pure ca mārgeṣu śodhayitvā samaṃtataḥ |
sugaṃdhijalasaṃsiktamakārayanmahītalam || 2.159{61} ||
tataḥ puṣpāvakīrṇṇaṃ ca kārayitvā samaṃtataḥ |
dhvajacchatravitānaiśca tatpuraṃ paryyabhūṣayat || 2.160{62} ||
tatastannagare madhye kārayitvā saro varam |
tadgaṃdhodakasaṃpūrṇamakārayatpraśobhitam || 2.161{63} ||
tatra puṣpābhiḥ saṃyuktaṃ kṛtvā tīre samaṃtataḥ |
chatradhvajapatākāni vitatya paryyamaṇḍayat || 2.162{64} ||
(rm 31)
āsanāni ca prajñapya sasaṃghasya kramānmuneḥ |
svarṇakuṃbhāṃśca saṃsthāpya śucigaṃdhāmbupūritān || 2.163{65} ||
tato pūjopacārāṃ bhojyopakaraṇāni ca |
sādhayitvopasaṃsthāpya saṃgītisahitāni ca || 2.164{66} ||
tato rājā sa sāmātyaiḥ paurikaiśca samanvitaḥ |
taṃ buddhaṃ puramānetuṃ jināśramamagānmudā || 2.165{67} ||
tatra sa sahasā prāpto dṛṣṭvā taṃ candanaṃ munim |
kṛtāṃjalipuṭo natvā gamanaṃ prārthayaditi || 2.166{68} ||
sarvajña bhagavaṃ gantuṃ samayo varttate'dhunā |
sarvaṃ ca sādhitaṃ siddhaṃ tadāśvāgantumarhasi || 2.167{69} ||
evaṃ tenārthite rājñā sa saṃbuddhaḥ sasāṃghikaḥ |
pātracīvaramādāya prāgāttatpurasaṃmukhaḥ || 2.168{70} ||
yadāsau bhagavān buddhāḥ prātihāryyaṃ pradarśayan |
lokān bhāsāvasaṃbhāsya prācaran prāptagopuram || 2.169{71} ||
tadākampadrasā sābdhirvavau vāyuḥ sumaṃgalam |
kṣutpipāsā hatā ye ca te sarve paritṛptitāḥ || 2.170{72} ||
ye ca hīnendriyāḥ satvāste ca pūrṇendriyā babhuḥ |
nirdhanā dhanināścāsan rogino nirujo'bhavan || 2.171{73} ||
baṃdhanasthā vimuktāśca duḥkhitāḥ sukhino'bhavan |
unmattāḥ susmṛtiṃ prāptā nagnā āsan suvastrikāḥ || 2.172{74} ||
gurviṇyā yāśca tāḥ sarvāḥ pramadāḥ sukhasūtikāḥ |
ciravairānusaṃbaddhā maitrībhūtāḥ samācaran || 2.173{75} ||
ye ca pāparatāḥ satvā daśākuśalacārikāḥ |
te sarve parisaṃtṛptāḥ puṇyakāmāḥ pracerire || 2.174{76} ||
evamanye ca ye tatra pāpakā upasargikāḥ |
te sarve vilayaṃ yātāḥ te sarve saṃśubhāṅkitāḥ || 2.175{77} ||
evaṃ sarvaprakāreṇa hatvā sarvamamaṅgalam |
kṛtvā bhadraṃ ca sarvatra sasaṃghaḥ sa pure'viśat || 2.176{78} ||
kramātpradakṣiṇenaivaṃ pracaritvā mahotsavaiḥ || 2.177{79} ||
(rm 32)
vaṃdyamānastu taiḥ sarvaiḥ sadevāsuramānavaiḥ || 2.178{80} ||
taṃ nṛpaṃ purataḥ kṛtvā rājamārgamupāsaran |
tatsarovarasaṃprāpto dṛṣṭvā tīre samāśrayat || 2.179{81} ||
tataḥ sa bhagavān buddho yathā prajñapta āsane |
ekacīvarakastasthau snātuṃ tatra sarovare || 2.180{82} ||
tato nṛpādayaḥ sarve lokāstaṃ caṃdanaṃ munim |
sasaṃghaṃ snāpayāmāsuḥ svarṇakumbhāmbubhiḥ kramāt || 2.181{83} ||
kṛtvā snānaṃ tato'nyatra yathāprajñapta āsane |
sasaṃgho bhagavān tasthau svaprabhāvaṃ pradarśayan || 2.182{84} ||
atha rājā sa taṃ buddhaṃ samāsīnaṃ sasāṃghikam |
cīvaraprāvṛtaṃ kṛtvā pūjāṅgaiḥ samapūjayat || 2.183{85} ||
tataśca bhojanaiḥ śuddhaiḥ praṇītairvyañjanairapi |
paṃcāmṛtaiśca pānaiśca sutṛptiṃ samatoṣayat || 2.184{86} ||
tataśca bhojanāṃte saṃśodhayitvā mukhādikam |
nīcāsane samāsīnaḥ kṛtāṃjalipuṭo nṛpaḥ || 2.185{87} ||
suprasannamukhāmbhojaḥ sa maṃtrījanapaurikaiḥ |
natvā kṣamāpayitvā taṃ sasaṃghaṃ prārthayattathā || 2.186{88} ||
sarvajña bhagavannātha kṣamasva no'parādhatām |
prasīda sarvathā rakṣa dṛṣṭvāsmān pāpacāriṇaḥ || 2.187{89} ||
taddurbhikṣavināśāya suvṛṣṭiṃ cāraya drutam |
sarvasatvahitārthāya saddharmaṃ ca samādiśa || 2.188{90} ||
ityevaṃ prārthite tena rājñā lokahitārthinā |
saṃbuddho bhagavāṃścaivaṃ svāśīrvacanamabravīt || 2.189{91} ||
ārogyamastu te rājan sarvatrāpi ca maṃgalam |
subhikṣaṃ sarvadāpyatra nirvighnaṃ bodhimāpnuhi || 2.190{92} ||
iti svāśīrvaco datvā saṃbuddhaḥ sa sasāṃghikaḥ |
tataśca svāśramaṃ prāgātsa nṛpajanamanvitaḥ || 2.191{93} ||
evamasya munīndrasya traimāsyaṃ ca niraṃtaram |
tenākāri nṛpendreṇa sevā satkārapūjanaiḥ || 2.192{94} ||
tataḥ śakro mahendro'sau dṛṣṭvā tatsugatārcitām |
taddurbhikṣapraśāṃtyarthaṃ suvṛṣṭiṃ samacārayat || 2.193{95} ||
(rm 33)
tato jalāśrayānyambupūritāni samaṃtataḥ |
padmotpalādipuṣpaiśca channāni rurucustadā || 2.194{96} ||
tṛṇairnavāṅkurodbhūtaṃ sādvalaiśca prarūḍhitaiḥ |
pratichannā haridvastraiḥ prāvṛtaiva rarāja bhūḥ || 2.195{97} ||
vṛkṣāścāpi haritpatraiḥ samāchannāḥ praśobhitāḥ |
puṣpaiḥ prasphullitairamyaiḥ phalaiśca saṃcakāsire || 2.196{98} ||
kramāccaivaṃ prarūḍhāni śasyāni varddhitāni ca |
pariniṣpannasiddhāni pravipecuḥ samaṃtataḥ |
tataḥ sarve janāścaivaṃ saṃprayātāṃ subhikṣatām |
dṛṣṭvānaṃdaṃ samuccāryya pracerire praharṣitāḥ || 2.197{99} ||
atha rājā sa tān sarvānmahāharṣapramoditān |
dṛṣṭvā bhūyaḥ samāmaṃtrya vabhāṣa saṃprabodhayan || 2.198{100} ||
bhavanto dṛśyatāmasya saṃbuddhasyānubhāvatā |
yasya puṇyānubhāvena prajātaiva subhikṣatā || 2.199{1} ||
tasmānnityaṃ tathaivāsya saṃbuddhasya jagatprabhoḥ |
mahatsatkārapūjābhiḥ karttavyaṃ bhajanaṃ sadā || 2.200{2} ||
evaṃ yadi sadā cāsya saṃbuddhasya jagatprabhoḥ |
pūjābhiḥ satkariṣyanti sadātra maṃgalaṃ bhavet || 2.201{3} ||
tasmādasya munīndrasya nakhakeśavidarbhitaḥ |
gandhastūpaḥ pratisthāpya sevitavyaḥ sadārcanaiḥ || 2.202{4} ||
iti saṃbhāṣya rājāsau taistathetyanumoditaiḥ |
saha bauddhāśramaṃ gatvā natvā taṃ prārthayanmunim || 2.203{5} ||
namaste bhagavaṃ chāstarvayaṃ te śaraṇasthitāḥ |
sadā caivaṃ kariṣyāmo bhavatsevāmupāsthitāḥ || 2.204{6} ||
tatprasīda sadā pātra vihṛtya dharmamādiśan |
vijayasva na cānyatra mā vraja no'nukaṃpayā || 2.205{7} ||
tenaivaṃ prārthite rājñā bhagavāṃśca sa caṃdanaḥ |
(rm 34)
taṃ nṛpaṃ sajanaṃ dṛṣṭvā prasannāsyo'bravīttathā || 2.206{8} ||
naiva rājanmayā bodhirekasyārthe prasādhitā |
api ca sarvasatvānāṃ hitārthe hyanukaṃpinā || 2.207{9} ||
tasmādatra sadā naiva sthāsyāmyahaṃ sasāṃghikaḥ |
sarvatrāpi careyāhaṃ saddharmaṃ saṃprakāśitum || 2.208{10} ||
yadi me śraddhayā nityaṃ sevāṃ kartuṃ samichatha |
keśastūpaṃ pratisthāpya sevyata sarvadārcanaiḥ || 2.209{11} ||
tataste'tra sadā rājye subhikṣaṃ maṃgalaṃ bhavet |
durbhikṣatā ca kutrāpi nākramiṣyati kadā cana || 2.210{12} ||
iti matvā tvayā gatvā triratnaśaraṇaṃ svayam |
sarvasatvāśca saddharme preraṇīyāḥ prayatnataḥ || 2.211{13} ||
ityādiśya munīṃdro'sau chitvā svānnakharāṃstathā |
keśāṃścāpyavatāryyaivaṃ tasmai rājñe svayaṃ dadau || 2.212{14} ||
tato rājā sa taṃ buddhaṃ natvā keśāṃ nakhāṃśca tān |
samādāya praharṣeṇa pratyāyayau svamālayam || 2.213{15} ||
tatra prāptaḥ pure madhye puṣkariṇyāstaṭāntike |
tatkeśanakharān ropya gaṃdhastūpamakārayat || 2.214{16} ||
tasmiṃ siddhe pratisthāpya vidhinā ca mahotsavaiḥ |
nityaṃ satkārapūjābhiḥ sevāṃ cakre prasannadhīḥ || 2.215{17} ||
etatpuṇyānubhāvena loke'ṃdhe'pariṇāyake |
jagalloke hitaṃ kartuṃ bhaveyaṃ sugatastathā || 2.216{18} ||
iti dhṛtvā pracitte'sau praṇidhānaṃ narādhipaḥ |
gaṃdhajalaiśca taṃ stūpaṃ snāpayitvā sadārcayat || 2.217{19} ||
pratisthāpya mahaṃ cāpi gatvā ca śaraṇe mudā |
aṣṭāṃgaiśca praṇatvaivaṃ mahotsāhairasevayat || 2.218{20} ||
tathā sarve janāścāpi śraddhayā taṃ prabhejire |
ye gatāḥ śaraṇaṃ tatra te sarve nirvṛtiṃ yayuḥ || 2.219{21} ||
ahamevāvasiṣṭaḥ sa teṣāṃ yo'bhūnnarādhipaḥ |
saṃbodhipraṇidhiṃ dhṛtvā naivaṃ nirvāṇamāyayau || 2.220{22} ||
(rm 35)
etatpuṇyānubhāvaiśca suvṛṣṭiḥ saṃpracāritā |
sarvadāpyabhavattatra subhikṣaṃ nirupadravam || 2.221{23} ||
etaddharmānubhāvaiśca sarvatra sāṃprataṃ labhe |
pūjāsatkāramānyāni yaśodharmasukhāni ca || 2.222{24} ||
yatra yatra pragachāmi tatra tatra samaṃtataḥ |
itthaṃ satkārapūjābhirmānito'haṃ sasāṃghikaḥ || 2.223{25} ||
tacchāstāraṃ samārādhya yaśodharmasukhepsubhiḥ |
śubheṣu sarvadā nityaṃ caritavyaṃ prayatnataḥ || 2.224{26} ||
śubhena sukhatā nityaṃ bhavelloke samaṃtataḥ |
pāpena duḥkhatā caivaṃ miśritenāpi miśritā || 2.225{27} ||
tasmātpāpaṃ viramyaivaṃ miśritāni vihāya ca |
śubheṣveva prayatnena caritavyaṃ sukhārthibhiḥ || 2.226{28} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa sarve te sāṃghikāstathā |
śrutvā pramenire satyaṃ karmabhāvaṃ bhavālaye || 2.227{29} ||
pāpebhyo viratā nityaṃ miśritebhyo'pi te sadā |
sadā śubheṣu niratāḥ sarve saṃcerire samāhitāḥ || 2.228{30} ||
evaṃ rājaṃstvayā nityaṃ pāpācca miśritādapi |
viramya sarvathā dharme caritavyaṃ sukhāptaye || 2.229{31} ||
prajāścāpi vinirvāryya sarvathā pāpacāraṇāt |
śrāvayitvā ca saddharmaṃ yojanīyāḥ śubhe sadā || 2.230{32} ||
tathā te sarvadā nityaṃ sarvatra viṣayeṣvapi |
suvṛṣṭiḥ pracareccaivaṃ maṃgalaṃ nirupadravam || 2.231{23!} ||
iti tena samādiṣṭamupaguptena śāsinā |
śrutvāśoko nṛpo'stvevamityanandatsapārṣadaḥ || 2.232{24} ||
snātāvadānaṃ tadidaṃ niśamya ye śrāvayanti pratibodhya lokān |
te pāpanirmuktaviśuddhadehāḥ prayānti nūnaṃ sugatālayeṣu || 2.233{25} ||

++ iti ratnāvadānamālāyāṃ snātāvadānaṃ samāptam ++


(rm 36)
iii cakrāvadāna
athāśoko narendro'sau bhūyo natvā kṛtāṃjaliḥ |
upaguptaṃ guruṃ prāha śrotumanyatsubhāṣitam || 3.1{1} ||
bhadanta śrotumichāmi bhūyo'pyanyatsubhāṣitam |
yathā te guruṇā khyātaṃ tathā me vaktumarhasi || 3.2{2} ||
iti tenārthite rājñā bhūyo'pyasau jināṃśajaḥ |
upagupto yatirbhikṣustaṃ nṛpamevamabravīt || 3.3{3} ||
yathā me guruṇādiṣṭaṃ vakṣye'haṃ te subhāṣitam |
sādhu cittaṃ samādhāya śṛṇu rājan samāhitaḥ || 3.4{4} ||
yaḥ purāsīnmahābuddhaḥ sarvajñaḥ śrīghano jinaḥ |
sarvavidyākalābhijño dharmarājastathāgataḥ || 3.5{5} ||
śāstārhan sugato nāthastraidhātukavināyakaḥ |
sa śākyādhipatirloke hitāya ca samaṃtataḥ || 3.6{6} ||
saddharmadeśanāṃ kṛtvā bodhimārgaṃ prakāśayan |
bhikṣuśrāvakasaṃghaiśca bodhisatvaiśca sāṃghikaiḥ || 3.7{7} ||
upāsikāgaṇaiścaivamupāsakagaṇaṃ saha |
rājagṛhamupāśritya veṇuvane jināśrame || 3.8{8} ||
kalaṃdake nivāpākhye mahodyāne samāśritaḥ |
ādimadhyāṃtakatyāṇaṃ dideśa dharmamuttamam || 3.9{9} ||
taddharmadeśanāṃ śrotuṃ devāsuramanuṣyakāḥ |
yakṣakinnaragaṃdharvāḥ siddhavidyādharādayaḥ || 3.10{10} ||
garuḍā nāgarājāśca mahāharṣasamanvitāḥ |
brāhmaṇā kṣatriyāścaiva rājāno rājaputrakāḥ || 3.11{11} ||
vaiśyāśca śreṣṭhinaḥ śūdrā maṃtriṇo mātṛkājanāḥ |
sārthavāhāśca paurāśca dhaninaśca vaṇigjanāḥ || 3.12{12} ||
evamanye'pi lokāśca sarve te saṃpraharṣitāḥ |
mahatsatkārapūjābhiḥ sarvopakaraṇaistathā || 3.13{13} ||
(rm 37)
pūjayitvā pravaṃditvā saṃbuddhaṃ sugataṃ munim |
parivṛtya puraskṛtya tasthau sarvānya tasthire || 3.14{14} ||
saddharmavāṃchitāḥ sarve śrotuṃ tatra samāgatāḥ || 3.15{15} ||
tasmiṃśca samaye pūryyāṃ śrāvastyāṃ subhago vaṇik |
anyattamaḥ sārthavāha āsīnmahādhanaḥ sudhīḥ || 3.16{16} ||
tena bhāryyā subhadrāṃgī jātidharmasamānikā |
pariṇītā kulasthityai kuladharmānusārataḥ || 3.17{17} ||
tatastau daṃpatī raktau snehānubandhitau mudā |
yathākāmaṃ prabhuṃjānau sanramāte yathechayā || 3.18{18} ||
tatastasya gṛhasthasya bhogyeṣvevābhirāgiṇaḥ |
svakulavṛttidharmeṣu pramādādanapekṣiṇaḥ || 3.19{19} ||
vyavahāre viraktasya prodyamarahitasya ca |
arjanopāyamaṃdasya kevalaṃ vyayakurvataḥ || 3.20{20} ||
dine dine kraṃānnityamativyayānnirāgamat |
saṃpadaḥ kṣīṇatāṃ yātā griṣmakāla iva hradāḥ || 3.21{21} ||
tatastasya gṛhasthasya bhāryā yā kuladharmikā |
saṃpadaḥ kṣīnatāṃ dṛṣṭvā gṛhe caivaṃ vyacintayat || 3.22{22} ||
ayaṃ me puruṣo bharttā sārthavāho vaṇikdhanī |
bhāgyameva gṛhe bhuktvā nivasati nirudyamaḥ || 3.23{23} ||
atyarthavyayatā yatra vidyate na tathāgamaḥ |
sā ca saṃpatkiyatkālaṃ tiṣṭhetkṣīṇaṃ vrajetkramāt || 3.24{24} ||
tadayaṃ puruṣaścāpi kathaṃ sustho bhaviṣyati |
kena saṃdhāryyate lokairvihīne bhogyasaṃcaye || 3.25{25} ||
vṛddho'pyayaṃ bhavennūnaṃ daridro'pi bhavettathā |
tadā kiṃ paribhuktvaivaṃ gṛhe tisthetkathaṃ sukhī || 3.26{26} ||
yasya saṃpadgṛhe nāsti kathaṃ sa puruṣaḥ sukhī |
(rm 38)
svayaṃ bhoktuṃ na śaktaḥ syātkiṃ punardānamācaret || 3.27{27} ||
dānaṃ vinā na saṃsāre kiṃ sārasukhamāpsyate |
dānenaiva hi labhyante yaśodharmasukhāni ca || 3.28{28} ||
dānena śuddhyate cittaṃ śuddhacitto bhavetsudhiḥ |
sudhiyā sādhyate śīlaṃ śīlavān syācchubhaṃkarī || 3.29{29} ||
śubhakarmā ca saddharmaṃ kṛtvā yāsyati sadgatim |
tasmādahamimaṃ nāthaṃ bharttāraṃ puruṣaṃ prabhum || 3.30{30} ||
saddharmasukhasaṃprāptyai prerayeyaṃ dhanārjane |
dhanavān puruṣo loke sarvārthasādhane kṛtī || 3.31{31} ||
dharmārthakāmamokṣāṇāṃ mūlaṃ prāhurdhanaṃ budhāḥ |
bharturhi puruṣasyāpi trivargakāryyasādhane || 3.32{32} ||
sahāyaḥ kathyate prājñaiḥ satī bhāryyānucāriṇī |
asya bharturhyahaṃ bharyyā satī dharmārthasādhane || 3.33{33} ||
manaḥ protsāhitaṃ kṛtvā prerayeyaṃ dhanārjane || 3.34{34!} ||
iti niścitya sā sādhvī dṛṣṭvā bharttāramādarāt |
dharmārthasādhane caivaṃ protsāhayitumabravīt || 3.35{34!} ||
svāmi bharttāsi me nātho devatāpi tvameva hi |
tanmayā vakṣyate tathyaṃ paratreha sukhāya te || 3.36{35} ||
tathyaṃ ca tacchṛṇuṣvaivamanyathā cettu mā kuru || 3.37{36} ||
yadbhavān purusaḥ prāḍhyaḥ sārthavāho vaṇikprabhuḥ |
sadā bhogyāni bhuktvaiva sthitvā naivaṃ praśobhati || 3.38{37} ||
tatpramādaṃ parityaktvā yaśodharmasukhāptaye |
satyadharmānusāreṇa kuruṣva dhanasādhanam || 3.39{38} ||
dhanena sādhayedbhogyaṃ bhogyena pālayettanum |
dehena sādhayeddharmaṃ dharmeṇa satsukhaṃ labhet || 3.40{39} ||
kiṃ ca yatra gṛhe svāmī vyavahāranirudyamaḥ |
tatra sarve janāścāpi niṣatsyaṃtyavasādinaḥ || 3.41{40} ||
api yatra gṛhe svāmī vyavahārasamudyataḥ |
tatra sarve janāścaiva pracareyuḥ samudyamāḥ || 3.42{41} ||
yacca dānamahotsāhaṃ yatra gehe na vidyate |
sa kiṃ geho na ramyaḥ syācchūnyāraṇye śmaśānavat || 3.43{41!} ||
(rm 39)
yasmin gṛhe sadā nityaṃ dānapuṇyamahotsavam |
sa eva mandiro ramyasvargavatpratibhāsyate || 3.44{42} ||
yasmiṃścāpi gṛhastho hi dānapuṇyanirutsahaḥ |
svayameva prabhuktvaivaṃ vasate paśuvatsa kim || 3.45{43} ||
dhanyāste puruṣā vīrā yaśodharmasukhānvitāḥ |
kṛtvā dānavṛṣotsāhaṃ sukhaṃ bhuktvā vasaṃti ye || 3.46{44} ||
teṣāmeva hi saṃsāre susāraṃ jīvitaṃ khalu |
ye kṛtvā dānapuṇyāni svayaṃ saukhyaṃ ca bhuṃjate || 3.47{45} ||
dānenaiva hi lobhyaṃte yaśodharmasukhāni ca |
anyathā naiva lapsyante tataḥ saṃpannirarthitā || 3.48{46} ||
kiyatkālaṃ ca jīveyurye bhuktvaiva sukhaṃ sthitāḥ |
avaśyaṃ sarvaśo hitvā mṛtā yāyuryamālayam || 3.49{47} ||
tatra karmasahāyāṃstān dṛṣṭvā dharmādhipo yamaḥ |
nirṇayitvā ca tatkarma prerayetkarmabhuktaye || 3.50{48} ||
ye ca pāpakarā duṣṭā prerayettān tu durgatau |
ye tu puṇyakarā bhadrāḥ prerayettāṃśca sadgatau || 3.51{49} ||
tatra karmaphalānyevaṃ duḥkhāni ca |
sukhāni ca sarvatheha paratrāpi bhuṃjate sarvajāṃtavaḥ || 3.52{50} ||
śubhena sadgatiṃ yayādyaśodharmasukhānvitāḥ |
pāpena durgatiṃ yāyāddhikpravādāsukhānvitāḥ || 3.53{51} ||
evaṃ matvā prabho svāmin yaśodharmasukhāptaye |
svakulavṛttimādhāya prodyamasva dhanārjane || 3.54{52} ||
sadā dānamahotsāhaṃ kṛtvā sadgatilabdhaye |
yathechayā sukhaṃ bhuktvā pālayasva sadā janān || 3.55{53} ||
tataste maṃgalaṃ nityaṃ bhavediha paratra ca |
saphalaṃ mānuṣaṃ janma nūnaṃ sadgatimāpnuyāt || 3.56{54} ||
iti bhāryāvacaḥ śrutvā sa gṛhastho vaṇiksudhīḥ |
bhāryāṃ tāṃ vallabhāṃ kāntāṃ dṛṣṭvā caivaṃ samabravīt || 3.57{55} ||
ayi priye subhadrāsi mama bhāryānucāriṇī |
yattvayā bhāṣitaṃ satyaṃ tatkariṣyāmi sarvathā || 3.58{56} ||
kiṃ tu tvaṃ me priyā bhāryā vallabhātimanoharā |
tatkathaṃ tvāṃ priyāṃ tyaktvā kutra yāsye dhanārthataḥ || 3.59{57} ||
(rm 40)
sārthavāho hyahaṃ cāpi tatkathaṃ nīcavṛttibhiḥ |
ratnārjanaṃ kariṣyāmi tato ratnākaraṃ vraje || 3.60{58} ||
tasmātpriye subhadrāṃgī sati dharmānucāriṇī |
gṛhakāryyaṃ samādhāryya vasa bhuktvā yathāsukham || 3.61{59} ||
yāvannāhaṃ gṛhe prāptastāvaddhairyyaṃ samāśraya |
śīghramevāgamiṣyāmi maṃ smṛtvā mānvatapyathāḥ || 3.62{60} ||
iti bharttoditaṃ śrutvā sā ca bhāryānutāpitā |
galadaśrumukhī natvā svāminaṃ tamabhāṣata || 3.63{61} ||
he nātha supriya svāminmāṃ vihāya kathaṃ vrajeḥ |
tvayā vinā kathaṃ gehe tiṣṭheyāhaṃ yathā vane || 3.64{62} ||
darśane śravaṇe vāpi gaṃdhatailānulepane |
sparśane bhojane pāne suvastraparibhūṣaṇe || 3.65{63} ||
saurabhyā'ṅgane cāpi bhāṣane gamane kvacit |
nidrābhoge tathā naiva vidyeta me'bhilāṣatā || 3.66{64} ||
tavaiva smaraṇaṃ kṛtvā niḥśvasantī muhurmuhuḥ |
virahānalasaṃtaptā vrajeyaṃ maraṇaṃ dhruvam || 3.67{65} ||
mṛtāyāṃ mayi te gehe ko'nurakṣennu saṃpadaḥ |
śrutvā me maraṇaṃ nūnaṃ dhakṣyase virahānalaiḥ || 3.68{66} ||
tadaitābhiśca saṃpattiratnaiśca kiṃ kariṣyasi |
aputrasya hi sarvasvaṃ nūnaṃ rājā grahīṣyati || 3.69{67} ||
tadātmanā vikhidyaivaṃ dūraṃ mā gā vihāyatām |
gṛhe sthitvā mayā sārddhāṃ sukhaṃ bhuktvodyamaṃ kuru || 3.70{68} ||
atrāpi bahavaḥ santi vaṇiksaṃghā mahājanāḥ |
paṇyaṃ prasāryya taṃ sārddhaṃ sādhayasva dhanaṃ sukham || 3.71{69} ||
kiyatkālaṃ ca jīveva santatiśca na vidyate |
tatkiṃ nau bahubhī ratnairmā gā ratnākaraṃ hyataḥ || 3.72{70} ||
yāvajjīvaṃ sukhaṃ bhuktvā dānaṃ kṛtvā sadārthine |
saṃvṛtyā prārjaya dravyaṃ svagehe saṃsthito rama || 3.73{71} ||
mā dhāva priya kutrāpi kiṃ vā ratnākare labhet |
svagṛhe'pi labhejjantuḥ svakarmanirmitaṃ dhanam || 3.74{72} ||
svakṛtaṃ karma tadbhāvi nirmāṇasadṛśaṃ phalam |
(rm 41)
sarvatrāpi labhantyeva mahāntaḥ kṣudrikā api || 3.75{73} ||
tathā ye sujanā loke sadhanā sadguṇāśrayāḥ |
te'pi kṣaṇādbhavantyeva nirdhanā bhikṣutāśritāḥ || 3.76{74} ||
ye cāpi durjanā loke nirdhanā nirguṇāśca ye |
te'pi labhanti mānyārhaṃ sevyante cāpi sajjanaiḥ || 3.77{75} ||
tathā ye vaṇijo dhīrāḥ sārthavāhā maṇīchayā |
ratnākaraṃ gatāsteṣāṃ ke citsvastisamāgatāḥ || 3.78{76} ||
ke cittatra samudre vā bhagnanaukā dhanaiḥ saha |
patitāḥ sannimajjantā mṛtāśca vilayaṃ gatāḥ || 3.79{77} ||
ke cidvīryabalenaivaiḥ samuttīrya prayatnataḥ |
akiṃcanyāḥ paribhraṣṭā riktahastā gṛhāgatāḥ |
evaṃ sarve'pi satvāśca svakṛtakarmanirmitam |
phalaṃ bhuktvā bhramantyevaṃ saṃsāre ṣaḍgatiṣvapi || 3.80{78} ||
iti karmapramāṇatvaṃ jñātvā saṃpadvipattitā |
mā khedaya svamātmānaṃ dharmaṃ kṛtvā sukhaṃ vasa || 3.81{79} ||
tathā nau maṃgalaṃ nityaṃ sarvatrāpi bhavetkhalu |
yāvajjīvaṃ sukhaṃ bhuktvā sadgatiṃ ca vrajemahi || 3.82{80} ||
iti bhāryoditaṃ śrutvā sārthavāhātmajaḥ sa ca |
taṃ priyāṃ ramaṇīṃ bhāryyāṃ dṛṣṭvaivaṃ samabhāṣata || 3.83{81} ||
bhadre satyaṃ tvaṃ yā proktaṃ yatsvakarmapramānatā |
iti matvāhamichāmi gantuṃ ratnākare'pi hi || 3.84{82} ||
yadabhāvi na tadbhāvi bhāvi cenna tadanyathā |
sarvatrāpi bhavennūnaṃ tenābdhiṃ gantumīhyate || 3.85{83} ||
yadi bhāgyaṃ mamāstyevaṃ svasti syādgamane khalu |
ratnāni bahuśo labdhā pratyāgacheya siddhitāḥ || 3.86{84} ||
atha me bhāgyatā naiva nūnaṃ tatra vipattitā |
mahattīrthe nimagno'haṃ mṛto yāsye surālayam || 3.87{85} ||
yathā bhaṭṭā mahāvīrā yaśodharmasukhāptaye |
śatruṃ jetuṃ mahotsāhairviśanti raṇamaṇḍale || 3.88{86} ||
tatra ke ciddhatā vīrā yaśodharmānvitā mṛtāḥ |
sahasā te prayāntyevaṃ svargasaukhyānubhoginaḥ || 3.89{87} ||
ahamapi vaṇikputraḥ sārthavāhātmajo hyaham |
(rm 42)
tattathāhaṃ gaṃiṣyāmi ratnākaraṃ na cānyataḥ || 3.90{88} ||
yadi svasti gṛhaṃ prāpsye yaśoratnasukhānvitaḥ |
mṛto'tha nirmalībhūtvā svarge saukhyamavāpnuyām || 3.91{89} ||
iti matvā priye bhadre mā viṣīda sukhāṃ vasa |
yāvannāhaṃ gṛhaṃ prāptastāvadbhajeṣṭadevatām || 3.92{90} ||
sarvathāhaṃ gamiṣyāmi svakulakīrtilabdhaye |
anivarttī hyahaṃ kāryye tanmāṃ tvaṃ mā nivāraya || 3.93{91} ||
iti bhartturvacaḥ śrutvā nirbandhaṃ gamanaṃ prati |
sā satī taṃ svabharttāraṃ natvā prāha kṛtāṃjaliḥ || 3.94{92} ||
svāmiṃstvaṃ sarvathā gantuṃ yadīchasi mahāmbudhim |
dhairyyamālamvya saṃpaśyanmārge vraja samāhitaḥ || 3.95{93} ||
svasti te vrajato mārge sarvatrāpi divāniśam |
svasti pratyāgate caivaṃ bhūyānnityaṃ śubhaṃ sadā || 3.96{94} ||
yātrāsiddhimahāsaṃpadyaśodharmasukhānvitaḥ |
svasti śīghraṃ samāgacha vrajā te'stu sadā śivam || 3.97{95} ||
iti bhāryoditaṃ śrutvā sārthavāhastatheti saḥ |
pratijñāya vaṃksaṃghān sarvānāhūya cābravīt || 3.98{96} ||
bhavanto'haṃ samichāmi yośodharmasukhāptaye |
svakulavṛttimādhāya gantuṃ ratnākarāṃbudhau || 3.99{97} ||
tatra gantuṃ mayā sārddhaṃ yadi yūyaṃ samichatha |
tatpaṇyaṃ sahasādāya samāyāta caremahi || 3.100{98} ||
iti tenoditaṃ śrutvā sarve te vaṇijo mudā |
sahasā paṇyamādāya tatra gantumupākraman || 3.101{99} ||
atha sārthapatiḥ śīghraṃ taiśca paṃcaśataiḥ saha |
svastyayanavidhiṃ kṛtvā pratasthe saṃpramoditaḥ || 3.102{100} ||
tataḥ kramādatikramya grāmajanapadāni te |
vilaṃghyāraṇyadeśāṃśca tīraṃ prāpurmahodadheḥ || 3.103{1} ||
tato nāvaṃ samāruhya tāni paṃcaśatāni ca |
pravitatya dhvajaṃ śubhraṃ jagāhire'mbudhau kramāt || 3.104{2} ||
tathā te vahanārūḍhā vātānukūlato dhruvam |
kramāddvīpāṃśca laṃghitvā ratnākaraṃ samāyayuḥ || 3.105{3} ||
tatra ratnākaraṃ prāptā devatā supramodataḥ |
te mithaḥ kalahaṃ kṛtvā pratyāgaṃtuṃ na śekire || 3.106{4} ||
(rm 43)
ciramevaṃ virodhitvādratnāni ca na lebhire |
bāṃdhavāṃśca muhuḥ smṛtvā tasthuḥ sarve vilaṃvitāḥ || 3.107{5} ||
atrāṃtare ca sā nārī sārthavāhapriyā satī |
bharttrā vinā gṛhaṃ śūnyamamanyata nirutsavāt || 3.108{6} ||
yaddine prasthito bhartā sārthavāhaḥ purādvahiḥ |
taddinārabhya sā nārī gaṇayantī dinaṃ prati || 3.109{7} ||
bharttureva muhuḥ smṛtvā virahavedanāturā |
niḥśvasantī muhuścaivamadhairyyā paritāpinī || 3.110{8} ||
kṛśāṅgā pāṇḍuvarṇā ca virukṣākīrṇakeśinī |
prasādhanānabhīchantī malinavasanāvṛtā || 3.111{9} ||
darśanaśravaṇe cāpi gaṃdhānvāharaṇe'pi ca |
bhojaneṣvapi pāne ca sparśane gamaneṣvapi || 3.112{10} ||
kutūhare tathānyatra manorame'pi cādbhute |
sarvatra viṣayeṣvevaṃ na protsahe yathāturā || 3.113{11} ||
bhartturviyogaduḥkhārttā roginīva viṣādinī |
asahyamadanākrāntā dhairyyopāyavicetanā || 3.114{12} ||
bharturāgatimālambya kṣaṇaṃ dhairyyasamāśritā |
bhartārameva saṃsmṛtvā gṛha eva nyaṣīdata || 3.115{13} ||
na nidrāṃ śayane bheje nānyasmṛtau mano'nudat |
prabhumevaṃ samādhyātvā tasthau dhyāyīva yogavit || 3.116{14} ||
athaivaṃ vikalībhūtāṃ yauvanīṃ tāṃ pativratām |
kācidekā sakhī bhadrā saṃnirīkṣyābravīdrahaḥ || 3.117{15} ||
bhadre kin te viṣāda tvaṃ vibhāsi tvaiḥ yathāturā |
mā viṣīda yadarthan te tadvadasva mayi priye || 3.118{16} ||
kathaṃ notsahase bhadre svakuladharmacāriṇi |
kautuke cādbhute cāpi sarvatra viṣayeṣvapi || 3.119{17} ||
bhojane bhūṣaṇe cāpi draṣṭuṃ śrotuṃ na vāṃchasi |
kiṃ viṣādena siddhaṃ te tatprabhukṣva yathāsukham || 3.120{18} ||
(rm 44)
iti tayodite sakhyā sārthavāhapriyāpi sa |
tāṃ sakhīṃ pramadāṃ kāntāṃ dṛṣṭvā caivamabhāṣata || 3.121{19} ||
nāhaṃ bhogārthinī bhadre nāpi kāmātirāgiṇī |
kiṃ tu dharmānuraktāsmi tatkathaṃ svāminaṃ vinā || 3.122{20} ||
tenāhaṃ viṣayāṃ tyaktvā smṛtvā bharttārameva tam |
svāgāra eva tiṣṭhāmi bhogyeṣvapi ca nispṛhā || 3.123{21} ||
kadā sa me priyo bharttā gṛhe pratyāsariṣyati |
iti ciṃtāṃ samādhāya tiṣṭhāmi yoginī yathā || 3.124{22} ||
yāvacca sa priyaḥ svāmī nāyāti svagṛhaṃ prati |
tāvadevaṃ na bhokṣye'haṃ bhogyāni surasānyapi || 3.125{23} ||
viṣayeṣvapi sarveṣu na me vāṃchā priyaṃ vinā |
nānyacciṃtā ca me citte tasmādevaṃ sthitā sadā || 3.126{24} ||
yāvatsvāmī na me dṛṣṭastāvatsthāsyāmyahaṃ tathā |
api me jivitaṃ yātu na ca yāyāṃ gṛhādbahiḥ || 3.127{25} ||
strīṇāṃ hi devatā svāmī dharmo'pi bhartṛsevayā |
tasmātsmṛtvaiva bharttāraṃ bhājāmyahaṃ pativratā || 3.128{26} ||
kutra dharmaṃ cariṣyāmi yasyā nāsti gṛhe pumān |
tatmanasaiva bharttāraṃ dhyātvā bhaje divāniśam || 3.129{27} ||
dhigme janma nirarthaṃ syādyasyā na yauvane patiḥ |
vṛddhatve kiṃ kariṣyāmi svāminā ca dhanairapi || 3.130{28} ||
varamevādya me mṛtyurna mithyā cirajīvitam |
yasmācciramapi sthitvā mṛtyuduḥkhaṃ tadeva hi || 3.131{29} ||
yamevālaṃvya jīvāmi sa yadi nāgamiṣyati |
kimeva jīvitenāpi kevaladuḥkhabhāvinā || 3.132{30} ||
dhanyāstā hi striyo bhadrāḥ svasvāmisahacārikāḥ |
kṛtvā dharmaṃ sadā gehe saukhyaṃ bhuktvā vasanti yāḥ || 3.133{31} ||
mā bhūttu mādṛśi kācidabhadrā duḥkhabhāginī |
samyagbhogyaiśca kiṃ tasyā yasyā bharttā na yauvane || 3.134{32} ||
tathāpi kiṃ kariṣyāmi nāryyahaṃ hi pativratā |
tasmāttameva bharttāraṃ smṛtvā sthāsyāmi sarvadā || 3.135{33} ||
(rm 45)
iti tayoditaṃ śrutvā sā sakhī hitakāriṇī |
tadupāyahitaṃ kartuṃ tāṃ sakhīṃ ca samabravīt || 3.136{34} ||
bhadre satyaṃ tvayā proktaṃ strīṇāṃ hi devatā patiḥ |
tathāpi vakṣyate bhadre tava prītyā ca tacchṛṇu || 3.137{35} ||
yāvannivasate gehe bhartā kāmarasārataḥ |
tāvatstrī sevayennityaṃ dharmaṃ bharturanujñayā || 3.138{36} ||
yadā bharttā gṛhe na syāddūradeśaṃ gatastathā |
yāvadgṛhaṃ na cāyātastāvaddevaṃ bhajetpatiḥ || 3.139{37} ||
pateste devatāsmākaṃ viṣṇurnārāyaṇo hariḥ |
tasmādenaṃ prabhornāmnā smṛtvā nityaṃ bhajārcaya || 3.140{38} ||
sa eva kāmado devaḥ kāmadhātvīśvarādhipaḥ |
saṃsārasthitidharmastho ratibhogyasukhapradaḥ || 3.141{39} ||
tasyaiva hyanubhāvena preryyamāno mano bhuvāḥ |
bharttā te sahasā prāyātsvasti ratnaiśca saṃyutaḥ || 3.142{40} ||
tataste saphalaṃ janma saṃsāre prabhunā saha |
dānaṃ kṛtvā sukhaṃ bhuktvā yāvajjīvaṃ sukhaṃ vasa || 3.143{41} ||
evaṃ sakhyoditaṃ śrutvā ramaṇī sā pativratā |
tatheti pratisaṃśrutya punastāmavadatsakhīm || 3.144{42} ||
satyaṃ bhadre yathā proktaṃ tvayā me hitakāraṇam |
tathāhaṃ saṃbhajiṣyāmi smṛtvā nāmnā prabhorharim || 3.145{43} ||
yathā svasti samāyātaḥ svāmī ratnasamṛddhibhṛt |
tadā hyekaṃ suvarṇasya cakraṃ dāsyāmi viṣṇave || 3.146{44} ||
ityevaṃ sā pratijñāya viṣṇumevaṃ samasmarat || 3.147{45} ||
namaste bhagavan viṣṇo hare nārāyaṇa prabho |
prasīda paśya māṃ nārīṃ prabhuṃ ca prāpayāśu me || 3.148{46} ||
yadā me puruṣo bharttā svasti gehasamāgataḥ |
tadopaḍhaukayiṣyāmi svarṇacakraṃ hare tava || 3.149{47} ||
iti sakhyāḥ puraḥ proktvā smṛtvā nityaṃ hariṃ mudā |
svarṇacakraṃ ca saṃkalpya tasthau bhartuḥ smaranti sa || 3.150{48} ||
tadaiva sārthavāho'sau sarvasārthagaṇaiḥ saha |
bahuratnāni saṃgṛhya pratasthe sahasā tataḥ || 3.151{49} ||
tataḥ svasti samuttīryya mahodadhestadāgataḥ |
sarvasārthagaṇaiḥ sārddhaṃ sahasā gṛhamāyayau || 3.152{50} ||
(rm 46)
tatastaṃ gṛhasaṃprāptaṃ dṛṣṭvā sā naṃditā satī |
pādau natvā puraḥ sthitvā svaṃ pravṛttamabhāṣata || 3.153{51} ||
svāmin yadā bhavān gehātprasthito māṃ vihāya ca |
tadārabhya sadā smṛtvā tvāmevāsmi sthitā gṛhe || 3.154{52} ||
tadāhamanayā sakhyā dṛṣṭvā tvadvirahāturā |
tadduḥkhaṃ me vinodārthaṃ preritā smaraṇe hareḥ || 3.155{53} ||
tadārabhya sadā nityaṃ smṛtvā taveṣṭadevatām |
bhajāmi satataṃ bhaktyā tavāśu gamanārthinī || 3.156{54} ||
cakramekaṃ suvarṇasya saṃkalpya śraddhayā mayā |
prārthanāpi kṛtā nityaṃ tava yātrāprasiddhaye || 3.157{55} ||
yathā me prārthitaṃ sarvaṃ tathā siddhaṃ hi sāṃpratam |
tanme saṃkalpitaṃ deva pūrayeyaṃ tathā prabho || 3.158{56} ||
tatpūjāṃgaṃ samādāya śakraṃ samupaḍhokitum |
devakulaṃ gamiṣyāmi tadanujñāṃ pradehi me || 3.159{57} ||
iti bhāryoditaṃ śrutvā sārthavāho'numoditaḥ |
pūraya tvaṃ priye satyaṃ gacchetyevamabhāṣata || 3.160{58} ||
tataḥ sānujñayā bhartuḥ satī sārddhaṃ sakhījanaiḥ |
pūjāṃgāni ca taṃ cakraṃ dhṛtvā devakulaṃ yayau || 3.161{59} ||
tasmiṃ kṣaṇe sa saṃbuddho bhagavān karuṇāmayaḥ |
loke satvān samuddhartumapaśyadbuddhacakṣuṣā || 3.162{60} ||
tāmevaṃ prasthitāṃ dṛṣṭvā bhadrāṃ saddharmacāriṇīm |
praikṣacca bhagavānenāṃ pratyekabodhilābhinīm || 3.163{61} ||
tato'sau bhagavān buddhaḥ saṃnirīkṣya ca tāṃ satīm |
sarvān bhikṣugaṇāṃ chiṣyān samāmaṃtryābravīttathā || 3.164{62} ||
paśyadhvaṃ bhikṣavo yūyaṃ dārikaṃ tāṃ subhadrikām |
nūnaṃ maddarśanādeṣā bodhicittamavāpnuyāt || 3.165{63} ||
samyakkuśalamūlāni samavaropayiṣyati |
dhruvaṃ pratyekabodhau ca praṇidhānaṃ kariṣyati || 3.166{64} ||
tasmātsaṃdarśanaṃ datvā dārikāṃ tāṃ subhāvinīm |
pratyekabodhicaryāyāṃ supratisthāpayāmyaham || 3.167{65} ||
tatra ca gantumichanti ye mayā saha bhikṣavaḥ |
(rm 47)
pātracīvaramādāya te prāyātaṃ caremahi || 3.168{66} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ jinendreṇa śrutvā sarve'pi sāṃghikāḥ |
pātracīvarasaṃyuktāstatra gaṃtumupācaran || 3.169{67} ||
tato'sau bhagavān buddhaḥ svaprabhāvaṃ pradarśayan |
sarvaiśca sāṃghikaiḥ sārddhaṃ rājagṛhamupāsarat || 3.170{68} ||
tatra mārge samāyātā dārikā sasakhīvṛtā |
taṃ buddhaṃ śrīghanaṃ dūrāddadarśa saṃprabhāsvaram || 3.171{69} ||
dvātriṃśallakṣaṇopetamaśītivyaṃjanānvitam |
divyātikrāntasaundaryyaṃ samantabhadrarūpakam || 3.172{70} ||
sahasreṇātiriktābhaṃ saumyaṃ kāntaṃ manoharam |
puṇḍarīkamivodbhāsaṃ ratnāṅgamiva jaṃgamam || 3.173{71} ||
sākṣātpuṇyāvatāraṃ ca dṛṣṭvā cātyanumoditā |
sahasopetya taccakramupaḍhaukitumaichata || 3.174{72} ||
evaṃ tāṃ cakramādāya saṃbuddhamupaḍhaukitum |
prayātaṃ tāṃ sakhī dṛṣṭvā nivārayitumabravīt || 3.175{73} ||
nāyaṃ nārāyaṇo bhadre sugato'yaṃ jitendriyaḥ |
tadasmai mā pradehīdaṃ cakraṃ saṃkalpitaṃ hareḥ || 3.176{74} ||
evaṃ nivāryyamānāpi sakhībhiḥ sā ca bhadrikā |
saddharmaguṇavāṃchantī tāḥ sakhīḥ punarabravīt || 3.177{175} ||
sakhyo bhāgyānmayāpyevaṃ saṃbuddho dṛśyate'dhunā |
tadadya saphalaṃ janma saṃsāre jīvitaṃ ca me || 3.178{76} ||
yadābhāsparśitā cāpi satvāḥ kleśavimocitāḥ |
saṃvṛttisukhasaṃprāptā nirvṛttimapi cāpnuyuḥ || 3.179{77} ||
yannāmoccāraṇādvāpi saṃbhāvyasmaraṇādapi |
mārapāśavinirmuktāḥ saṃbodhipadamāpnuyuḥ || 3.180{78} ||
yaddharmaśravaṇādevaṃ nityānumodanādapi |
sarvapāpavinirmuktāḥ sadgatiṃ samavāpnuyuḥ || 3.181{79} ||
yatsaṃgheṣu supātreṣu kiṃcinmātrārppanādapi |
saṃsāre'nantasaukhyāni bhuktvā yāyuḥ sukhāvatīm || 3.182{80} ||
yenaivaṃ pālitāḥ satvāḥ ṣaḍgaticaritā api |
(rm 48)
bodhayitvā ca saddharme sthāpitā nirvṛtāvapi || 3.183{81} ||
yasya dharmānubhāvācca maṃgalaṃ bhuvanatraye |
sarve satvā sukhāḍhyāśca saṃbodhipadacāriṇaḥ || 3.184{82} ||
so'pyayaṃ bhagavānnāthaḥ śāstā trailokyādhipaḥ |
nūnaṃ me puṇyabhāgyena dṛśyate'yatnato'dhunā || 3.185{83} ||
tadahaṃ kuśalinī syāṃ hi saṃbuddhena darśitā |
mahābhāgyavatī cāsmi yadayaṃ sadguruḥ sudhīḥ || 3.186{84} ||
amṛgyamāna evetthaṃ labhyate purato mayā |
tadahaṃ pūjayitvemaṃ cakraṃ ca samupaḍhaukitum || 3.187{85} ||
ichāmyasmai munīṃdrāya nirvṛtipadalabdhaye |
api cātra sadā naiva saṃbuddho lapsyate tathā || 3.188{86} ||
kadā ciddhi jinotpattiḥ puṣpamaudunvaraṃ yathā |
mānuṣye'pi sadā naiva janma bhavānucāriṇām |
puṇyaireva hi mānuṣyaṃ lapṣyate janma dullabham || 3.189{87} ||
mānuṣye'labhyamāne tu kathaṃ buddhānusevanā |
buddhasevāṃ vinā kena bodhicittamavāpnuyāt || 3.190{88} ||
bodhicittaṃ vinā kasya saddharmeṣu matiścaret |
vinā dharmamatiṃ ko hi bodhicaryyāṃ samācaret || 3.191{89} ||
bodhicaryāṃ vinā loke hitaṃ kartuṃ kathaṃ caret |
loke hitamakṛtvaiva kutaḥ puṇyamavāpnuyāt || 3.192{90} ||
apuṇyena kathaṃ kleśān hitvā nirvṛtimāpnuyāt |
tasmātkleśavimuktyarthaṃ nirvṛttipadalabdhaye || 3.193{91} ||
imaṃ buddhaṃ jagannāthamichāmyabhyarcituṃ mudā |
buddha eva jagannāthaḥ sarvasatvahitārthadaḥ || 3.194{92} ||
saddharmadeśakaḥ śāstā saṃbodhijñānanāyakaḥ |
tasmādenaṃ samutsṛjya nānyamichāmi cārcitum || 3.195{93} ||
asyaiva śaraṇaṃ gatvā bhajiṣyāmi sadāpyaham || 3.196{94} ||
iti sā dārikā proktvā cakramādāya moditā |
saṃbuddhaṃ cārppituṃ tasmai pūjāṃ kartumupāyayau || 3.197{95} ||
atha sā taṃ muniṃ natvā pūjābhiśca samarcayat |
taṃ cakraṃ samupasthāpya natvaivaṃ praṇidhiṃ vyadhāt || 3.198{96} ||
(rm 49)
yathāyaṃ sugato buddho niḥkleśo vijitendriyaḥ |
tathāhamapi śuddhātmā nirvṛtipadamāpnuyām || 3.199{97} ||
tathāsau bhagavān buddho dṛṣṭvā tatpraṇidhiṃ tataḥ |
suprasannamukhāmbhojādvyasṛjatsaṃsmitāmṛtam || 3.200{98} ||
tatsmitasahoccāryamāṇā paṃcavidhāṃśavaḥ |
ke cidūrddhvaṃ gatāḥ ke cidadho yātāśca te'ṃśavaḥ || 3.201{99} ||
ye ca yātā adholoke te saṃjīvanarake gatāḥ |
kālasūtre ca saṃghāte tathā ca raurave tataḥ || 3.202{100} ||
mahārauravake cāpi tapane ca pratāpane |
avīcau cārbude cāpi nirarbude tathāṭaṭe || 3.203{1} ||
hahave huhuve caivamutpale padmake tathā |
mahāpadme'pi caiteṣu ṣoḍaśeṣu samaṃtataḥ || 3.204{2} ||
tathopanārakeṣvevaṃ sarveṣvapi ca te'ṃśavaḥ |
prasṛtā bhāsayitvaivaṃ prākāśayan samaṃtataḥ || 3.205{3} ||
ye śītanarakāsteṣu tūṣṇībhūtā nipātitāḥ |
ye uṣṇanarakāsteṣu śītībhūtā vyabhāsayan || 3.206{4} ||
teṣu ye pāpinaḥ satvā duḥkhātivedanāturāḥ |
te sarve taddyutispṛṣṭāstadduḥkhavedanojjhitāḥ || 3.207{5} ||
śāṃtasaukhyānubhūtāśca vismayoddhatamānasāḥ |
dṛṣṭvaiva saha saṃmīlya mithaścaivamabhāṣata || 3.208{6} ||
aho citraṃ bhavanto'dya kiṃ nu vayamitaścyutāḥ |
anyatra caritāḥ smaḥ kiṃ vatāhosvinna manyate || 3.209{7} ||
iti saṃdigdhacittānāṃ teṣāṃ cittaprabodhane |
bhagavānnirmitaṃ tatra praiṣayacca samaṃtataḥ || 3.210{8} ||
atha taṃ nirmitaṃ saumyaṃ dṛṣṭvā te saṃprasāditāḥ |
bhūyo dṛṣṭvābhimodanto mithaścaivaṃ vabhāṣire || 3.211{9} ||
bhavanto nu vayaṃ sarve nānyatra caritā itaḥ |
api tvayamihāyātaḥ satvo hyapūrvadarśanaḥ || 3.212{10} ||
(rm 50)
nūnamasyānubhāvena nirduḥkhā susukhā vayam |
iti te nirmite tasmin bauddhe cittaṃ prasādya ca || 3.213{11} ||
namo buddhāya te tāyinnityuktvā śaraṇaṃ yayuḥ |
tataḥ sarve ca te satvāstadbuddhasmṛtidhāriṇaḥ || 3.214{12} ||
niḥpāpāḥ kuśalādhyāśca sadgatiṃ sahasā yayuḥ |
ye tathorddhaṃ gatāste ca buddhasmitotsṛtāḥ karāḥ || 3.215{13} ||
gatvā cāturmahārājabhuvaneṣu tato gatāḥ |
trayastriṃśāśca yāmāśca tathā ca tuṣitāṃ gatāḥ || 3.216{14} ||
nirmāṇaratikāṃ cānyanirmitavaśavarttinaḥ |
brahmakāyikalokaṃ ca tathā brahmaprohitam || 3.217{15} ||
mahābrāhmaṇalokāṃśca parīttābhaṃ tathā gatāḥ |
apramāṇaṃ tato yātā ābhāsvarāṃ tathā gatāḥ || 3.218{16} ||
parīttaśubhamevaṃ ca hyapramāṇaśubhaṃ tathā |
śubhakṛtsnamanabhraṃ ca puṇyaprasavasaṃjñikam || 3.219{17} ||
bṛhatphalaṃ tathā caivamabṛhamatapaṃ tathā |
sudṛśaṃ bhuvanaṃ caiva sudarśanaṃ tathā gatāḥ || 3.220{18} ||
akaniṣṭhaṃ tathā gatvā proccairevamaghoṣayan || 3.221{19} ||
anityaṃ khalu saṃsāraṃ duḥkhaṃ śūnyaṃ hyanātmakam |
iti matvā triratnāni saṃbhajadhvaṃ samāhitāḥ || 3.222{20} ||
niṣkrāmatārabhadhvaṃ ca yujyadhvaṃ buddhaśāsane |
dhunīta mṛtyusainyāśca naḍāgāraṃ yathā karī || 3.223{21} ||
yo'pyasmin dharmavaineye hyapramattaścariṣyati |
sa hitvā jātisaṃsāraṃ duḥkhasyāntaṃ kariṣyati || 3.224{22} ||
evaṃ tā arciṣaḥ sarvāṃllokadhātūn samaṃtataḥ |
avabhāsya śubhe satvān vinīya punarāgatāḥ || 3.225{23} ||
bhagavato'grataḥ sthitvā kṛtvā pradakṣiṇatrayam |
ūrṇāyāṃ ca munīndrasya sarvā antardadhustviṣaḥ || 3.226{24} ||
tadānandaḥ samutthāya kṛtāṃjalipuṭo nataḥ |
(rm 51)
jānubhyāṃ bhuvi saṃsthitvā bhagavantaṃ samabravīt || 3.227{25} ||
nānāraṅgakalāpaste vaktrānniṣkasito muneḥ |
yenāvabhāsitāḥ sarvā sūryeṇodayatā yathā || 3.228{26} ||
vigatoddhavā dainyamadaprahīṇā buddhā jagatyuttamahetubhūtāḥ |
nākāraṇaṃ śaṃkhamṛṇālagauraṃ smitamupadarśayanti jinā jitārayaḥ || 3.229{27} ||
tatkālaṃ svayamadhigamya dhīrabuddhyā śrotṝṇāṃ śramaṇajinendra kāṃkṣitānām |
dhīrābhirmunivṛṣa vāgbhiruttamābhirutpannaṃ vyapanaya saṃśayaṃ śubhābhiḥ || 3.230{28} ||
nākasmāllavaṇajalādrirājadhairyyāḥ saṃbuddhāḥ smitamupadarśayanti nāthāḥ |
yasyārthe smitamupadarśayanti dhīrāstaṃ śrotuṃ samabhilaṣanti janaughāḥ || 3.231{29} ||
ityānaṃdavacaḥ śrutvā saṃbuddho bhagavānasau |
tatsmitahetumādeṣṭuṃ tamānandaṃ samabravīt || 3.232{30} ||
evametattathānanda nāhetvapratyayaṃ smitam |
prāviṣkurvanti saṃbuddhā arhantaḥ sugatā jināḥ || 3.233{31} ||
paśyānandānayā nāryyā cakraṃ me samupārppitam |
pūjayitvā praṇatvā ca bodhau ca praṇidhiḥ kṛtā || 3.234{32} ||
anena dānadharmeṇa kuśalaiśca samanvitāḥ |
dārikeyaṃ subhadrāṃśā divyabhogyaṃ samāpnuyāt || 3.235{33} ||
na paṃcadaśakalpāni vinipātaṃ gamiṣyati |
tataścakrāntaro nāma pratyekasugato sudhīḥ || 3.236{34} ||
arhaṃ kleśavimuktātmā paṃcābhijño jitendriyaḥ |
sarvasatvānukaṃpī ca brahmacārī bhaviṣyati || 3.237{35} ||
yanmayi sugate buddhe cittamasyāḥ prasīdati |
tena puṇyabaleneyaṃ pratyekabodhimāpsyati || 3.238{36} ||
sugateṣu kṛtaṃ puṇyaṃ na kṣiṇoti kadā cana |
kramāttena viśuddhātmā saṃbodhimapi cāpnuyāt || 3.239{37} ||
iti matvā triratneṣu cittamevaṃ prasādya ca |
śraddhayā saṃprakarttavyaṃ satkāraṃ bodhivāṃchibhiḥ || 3.240{38} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa śrutvā sarve janāśca te |
(rm 52)
tathetyabhyanumodantastriratnasevino'bhavan || 3.241{39} ||
tato buddhānubhāvena taccakraṃ khe samudgatam |
