kṣaṇaṃ buddhopari sthitvā chatramivojjvalan babhau || 3.242{40} ||
tataśca viyato gatvā hariharmyopari sthitam |
sudarśanamivoddīptaṃ babhau rasmisamutkiran || 3.243{41} ||
tato'sau bhagavān buddho dṛṣṭvā tāṃ dārikāṃ svayam |
śirasi pāṇinā spṛṣṭvā svāśīrvacanamādiśat || 3.244{42} ||
bhadre te maṃgalaṃ bhūyā vinipātaṃ na cāpnuhi |
kramātpāramitāḥ pūryya pratyekaṃ bodhimāpnuhi || 3.245{43} ||
ityāśīrvacanaṃ datvā saṃbuddho'sau munīśvaraḥ |
tato rājagṛhaṃ gaṃtumupācaratsasāṃghikaḥ || 3.246{44} ||
tato'sau dārikā cāpi natvā taṃ sugataṃ punaḥ |
dṛṣṭvaivaṃ muhuḥ smṛtvā svagehaṃ samupāyayau || 3.247{45} ||
tatra gehe praviṣṭvāsau dārikā saṃpramoditā |
bhartturagre ca tadvṛttamācakhye sarvamādarāt || 3.248{46} ||
bharttāpyasau tathā śrutvā buddhadharmānumoditaḥ |
tayaivaṃ bhāryayā sārddhaṃ bheje ratnatrayaṃ sadā || 3.249{47} ||
evaṃ me guruṇā khyātaṃ tathā te kathyate mayā |
tvamapyevaṃ mahārāja triratnāni sadā bhaja || 3.250{48} ||
prajāścāpi triratnānāṃ bhajane preraya prabho |
tathā te maṃgalaṃ nityaṃ bodhiṃ cāpi samāpnuyāḥ || 3.251{49} ||
iti tenopaguptena bhāṣitaṃ tatsubhāṣitam |
śrutvāśoko narendro'sau mumoda saha pārṣadaiḥ || 3.252{50} ||
etaccakrāvadānaṃ munivaraguṇadaṃ yaḥ śṛṇoti prasādya |
śrutvā yaścānumodya pramuditamanasā śrāvayatyevamanyān |
sa jitvā kleśasaṃghān vigatakalimalo buddhadharmānucārī |
pūtātmā sadguṇāḍhyaḥ munivaranilayaiḥ saṃprayāti pramodāt || 3.253{51} ||
++ iti ratnāvadānamālāyaṃ cakrāvadāno nāma samāptam ++


(rm 53)
ivpretikāvadāna
athāśoko mahīpālaḥ saddharmacaraṇotsavaḥ |
upaguptaṃ guruṃ natvā kṛtāñjalipuṭo'vadat || 4.1{1} ||
bhadanta śrotumichāmi punaranyatsubhāṣitam |
yathā me guruṇā proktaṃ tathā vaktuṃ samarhasi || 4.2{2} ||
iti tena narendreṇa prārthite'sau jināṃśajaḥ |
upagupto mahābhijñastaṃ nareśamabhāṣata || 4.3{3} ||
yathā me guruṇādiṣṭaṃ tathā vakṣyāmi te'dhunā |
tathāpi śrūyatāṃ rājan tava dharmapravṛddhaye || 4.4{4} ||
puraikasamaye cāsau saṃbuddho bhagavāñjinaḥ |
cailakaiśca tathānyaistatsaddharmaguṇavāṃchibhiḥ || 4.5{5} ||
rājagṛhamupāśritya veṇuvane jināśrame |
karaṇḍakanivāpākhye tasthau dharmaṃ samādiśat || 4.6{6} ||
taddharmmadeśanāṃ śrotuṃ sadevāsuramānuṣāḥ |
siddhavidyādharāścāpi yakṣagaṃdharvakinnarāḥ || 4.7{7} ||
garuḍā nāgarājāśca rākṣasāśca mahoragāḥ |
sarve lokādhipāścāpi sasainyaparivārakāḥ || 4.8{8} ||
rājāno rājamātrāśca brāhmaṇāśca mahājanāḥ |
maṃtriṇo śreṣṭhinaścāpi sārthavāhāśca paurikāḥ || 4.9{9} ||
ṛṣayo yoginaścāpi yatayo brahmacāriṇaḥ |
evamanye'pi lokāśca parivṛtya samaṃtataḥ || 4.10{10} ||
satkṛtya svaryya pūjāṅgaiḥ sarvopakaraṇairapi |
mānayitvā ca taṃ nāthaṃ dṛṣṭvā tasthuḥ samāhitāḥ || 4.11{11} ||
tasmiṃśca samaye tatra maudgalyāyana ātmavit |
sa pretabhuvane gatvā caran pretīmapaśyata || 4.12{12} ||
svakeśaromasaṃchannāṃ dagdhasthūnāmahākṛtim |
sūcīmukhīṃ mahatkāyīṃ parvatasaṃnibhodarām || 4.13{13} ||
piṅgalakeśinīṃ raudrīṃ pradīptāgniśikhāmiva |
(rm 54)
tīvrātivedanākrāntāṃ tṛṣārttaṃ parimūrchitām || 4.14{14} ||
tatrāyuṣmān sa maudgalyo dṛṣṭvā tāṃ pretikāṃ ciram |
kaiṣeti sahasopetya papracha samupāgatām || 4.15{15} ||
kiṃ tvayā prakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ yenaivaṃ duḥkhabhāginī |
tṛṣārttā jalamārgantī bhramitvā carase'dhunā || 4.16{16} ||
iti tena yatīśena pṛṣṭvā sā pretikā tataḥ |
taṃ yatiṃ saugataṃ natvā praruditvāvadattathā || 4.17{17} ||
bhadanta tadvijānīyāḥ pāpakāriṇyahaṃ yataḥ |
evaṃ tṛṣṇāprataptāṅgā bhramāmi jalalālasā || 4.18{18} ||
yatrāhamudakaṃ pātuṃ gachāmi tṛṣṇayānvitā |
atra paśyāmi nadyādijalādhārān viśoṣitān || 4.19{19} ||
yadā varṣati deve ca dṛṣṭvā tadambupātitam |
pātumichantltṛṣṇārttā sahasāhaṃ pradhāvitā || 4.20{20} ||
tadā paśyāmi tadvinduvisphuliṅganibhojvalān |
dṛṣṭvaivaṃ nirāśāndhā bhramāmi dikṣu sarvataḥ || 4.21{21} ||
kiṃ mayā prakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ yenāsmyevaṃ supāpinī |
tanme pāpaṃ jagacchāstā pṛchyatāṃ bhavatādarāt || 4.22{22} ||
nūnaṃ so bhagavāñchāstā yanme karmapurākṛtam |
vyākariṣyati tatsarvaṃ tathā lokaprabodhane || 4.23{23} ||
yacchrutvānye'pi satvāśca saṃvignamanaso drutam |
viramya pāpamārgebhyaścariṣyanti śubhe sadā || 4.24{24} ||
tasmādbhadanta sarvajñastava śāstā sa pṛchyatām |
vyākuryānmama sarvatra yatkarma prakṛtaṃ purā || 4.25{25} ||
asmannāmnā sa saṃbuddho vaṃdyatāṃ bhavatā yathā |
ityuktvā sā tamātmajaṃ natvā pretā gṛhaṃ yayau || 4.26{26} ||
sa maudgalyastathetyuktvā tatsaṃdeśamanusmaran |
sahasaitatparipraṣṭuṃ veṇuvanamupāyayau || 4.27{27} ||
tadādau bhagavān dṛṣṭvā tanmaudgalyamupāgatam |
prahasan suprasannāsyaḥ samāmaṃtryaitadabravīt |
kaṃcitte kuśalaṃ vatsa kuto'trāsi samāgataḥ |
lokeṣu kiṃ pravṛttāṃtaṃ tatpracakṣva mahāmate |
(rm 55)
iti pṛṣṭe jinendreṇa sa maudgalyaḥ purogataḥ || 4.28{28} ||
ādau tasya munernatvā tatpravṛttamudāharat |
āgato'smi bhadanto'haṃ caritvā pretabhūmiṣu || 4.29{29} ||
tatra sthito'hamīkṣan tāṃ pretīmekāṃ bhayānakīm |
dagdhasthūṇānibhāṃ nagnāṃ parvatasannibhodarām || 4.30{30} ||
sūcīchidramukhacchidrāṃ kaṃkārayantrikāmiva |
svakeśaromasaṃchannāṃ jvalitāgniśikhāmiva || 4.31{31} ||
tivrātivedanākrāntāmārttasvarānurodinām |
durgandhāmaśucivyāptāṃ svakeśaparitāpitām || 4.32{32} ||
viṇmūtrānubhoktuṃ ca sākṣātpāpāvatārikām |
bhagavan sā mayā dṛṣṭvā tṛṣṇārttā jalalālasā || 4.33{33} ||
bhramantī jalamārgantī jalāśrayeṣu sarvataḥ |
nadīkūpataḍāgādinudapānajalāśrayān || 4.34{34} ||
svechāmbupūritān dṛṣṭvā prādhāvatsahasā yadā |
tasyā darśaṇamātreṇa te sarve'pi jalāśrayāḥ || 4.35{35} ||
nirjalāḥ paṃkaśeṣeṇa śuśuṣuśca samaṃtataḥ |
tathā viśoṣitāṃ dṛṣṭvā nadyādīṃstāñjalāśrayān || 4.36{36} ||
nirāśā paritaptāṅgā sā pretī paryamūrchata |
tataśca punarutthāya kṣuttṛṣṇonmādacāriṇī || 4.37{37} ||
jalaṃ cānnaṃ samanveṣṭuṃ babhrāma dikṣu sarvataḥ |
tathāpi sā jalaṃ nāsādayatkuhāpi vā || 4.38{38} ||
tato'titṛṣṇayā taptā viṇmūtrabhoktumaichata |
tadapi kṛcchrato'nviṣya bhuktvā tṛptiṃ na sā yayau || 4.39{38} ||
tathāmedhyāni bhuktvāpi kadācinnaiva tṛptitā |
kṣuttṛṣṇāhatāklāntā patitā kheditāvasat || 4.40{40} ||
māṃ dṛṣṭvā sahasotthāya śanairmandasvarāvadat |
svāgataṃ bho bhavānaitu rakṣa māṃ pāpinīṃ laghu || 4.41{41} ||
pānīyaṃ dehi me bhadra pānīyaṃ dātumarhasi |
iti proktābhidhāvanti pānīyaṃ māmayācata || 4.42{42} ||
tato'hamavadaṃ caināṃ pānīyaṃ nātra vidyate |
kātvamevaṃ nu duḥkhāni bhuktvātra vasase katham || 4.43{43} ||
kiṃ tvayā hi kṛtaṃ pāpaṃ yenaivaṃ duḥkhatādhunā |
(rm 56)
vaktavyaṃ cecca tatpāpaṃ sarvaṃ me vaktumarhasi || 4.44{44} ||
iti me gaditaṃ śrutvā sa pretī punarabravīt |
kiṃ mayā vakṣyate pāpaṃ yatpurā kudhiyā kṛtam || 4.45{45} ||
sarvajño hi bhavacchāstā sa evaṃ paripṛchyatām |
tatsarvāṇi ca me pāpaṃ sa vyākuryātsa sarvavit || 4.46{46} ||
asmannāmnā ca saṃbuddho vaṃdyatāṃ bhavatādarāt |
iti tayoditaṃ śrutvā pratijñāya tatheti ca || 4.47{47} ||
bhavantaṃ praṣṭumāyāmi tatsamādeṣṭumarhasi |
ārttasvarā rudantī sā pretī vindati vedanām || 4.48{48} ||
yena viṇmūtradhānāni tena dhāvati duḥkhitā |
tadapi kṛcchrato labdhvā bhuṃkte kiṃ citkadā cana || 4.49{49} ||
evaṃ sā pāpinī pretī dṛṣṭātiduḥkhitā mayā |
kiṃ tayā prakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ martyaloke sudāruṇam || 4.50{50} ||
yenaivaṃvidhaduḥkhaṃ sā hyanubhavati sāṃpratam |
kadā sā pāpato muktā kathaṃ ca sugatiṃ vrajet || 4.51{51} ||
tatsarvaṃ vadatāṃ śāsta sarvasatvaprabodhane |
iti tenārthite cāsau bhagavān punarabravīt || 4.52{52} ||
maudgalyāyana sā pretī pāpinī matsarī kudhīḥ |
tayā yatprakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ yena sā duḥkhabhāginī || 4.53{53} ||
tatsarvaṃ te pravakṣyāmi śṛṇu śrotuṃ yadīchasi || 4.54{54} ||
purāsīdbhagavān buddhaḥ kāśyapo nāma sarvavit |
sarvavidyādhipo nātho dharmarājastathāgataḥ || 4.55{55} ||
samantabhadrakṛcchāstā traidhātukavināyakaḥ |
jino'rhan sugatastrāyī saṃbodhimārgadeśakaḥ || 4.56{56} ||
sa satvānāṃ hitārthena bodhicaryāḥ prakāśayan |
vārāṇasīmupāśritya mṛgadāve jināśrame || 4.57{57} ||
śrāvakairbhikṣubhiḥ sārddhaṃ bodhisatvagaṇairapi |
tasminneva parṣatmadhye tasthau dharmaṃ samādiśat || 4.58{58} ||
tasmiṃśca samaye tasya bhikṣuranyatamo yatiḥ |
anyatranagare gatvā pratyāgachatsvamāśramam || 4.59{59} ||
(rm 57)
tatra mārge tṛṣārtto'sau dūrātkūpaṃ vilokayan |
sahasā samupāgatya kūpāntikamupāyayau || 4.60{60} ||
tadaikā dārikā tatra pūrayitvā ghaṭaṃ jalaiḥ |
sahasā śirasohitvā gṛhaṃ gantumupācarat || 4.61{61} ||
sa tṛṣārtto yatirdṛṣṭvā tāmambughaṭavāhikām |
samupetya jalaṃ pātuṃ prārthayan samupācarat || 4.62{62} ||
bhaginyo'haṃ tṛṣārtto'smi dūradeśādupāgataḥ |
tvāṃ dṛṣṭvā sahasopetya jalaṃ pātumihāgataḥ || 4.63{63} ||
tadbhaginī jalaṃ dehi jīvaya māṃ tṛṣāturam |
datvā me jīvanaṃ tvaṃ tacciraṃ jīvyā niroginī || 4.64{64} ||
iti tenārthitaṃ dṛṣṭvā dārikāsau durāśayā |
mātsaryyaprahatatvātā taṃ bhikṣuṃ paryyabhāṣata || 4.65{65} ||
are bhikṣo'tra māyāhi mriyase yadi vā khalu |
na te dadāmi pānīyaṃ ghaṭo pūrṇo bhavenmama || 4.66{66} ||
kūpe yatnātsamuddhṛtya pūrayitvāmbunā ghaṭe |
prohitvāyaṃ mayānītastvadarthe kiṃ na manyate || 4.67{67} ||
nirlajja kva nu te lajjā parakīyāmbumichasi |
rājā kiṃ tvaṃ dhanī vāsi gururvā kiṃ na manyate || 4.68{68} ||
ityākṣepaṃ pratikṣipya taṃ bhikṣuṃ saugataṃ yatim |
adatvaivāmṛtaṃ kiṃ citsatvarā sā gṛhaṃ yayau || 4.69{69} ||
tato bhikṣustṛṣārtto'pi lajjayāsau nyavarttate |
anyatraivaṃ samāyācya pītvā yayau svamāśramam || 4.70{70} ||
tatrāśrame tarormūle paryaṇkamupabhujya saḥ |
anuśocaya tāṃ nārīṃ manasaivaṃ vyaciṃtayat || 4.71{71} ||
aho sā durmatī nārī mātsaryyaprahatāśayā |
kathaṃ na manyate hyevaṃ mānuṣyajanma durllabham || 4.72{72} ||
mānuṣyaṃ puṇyato labdhvā nisphalaṃ kriyate tayā |
dhiktāṃ durāśayāṃ mūḍhāṃ yasyā janma nirarthakam || 4.73{73} ||
kadaiṣā pāpato muktvā sadgatiṃ samavāpsyati |
mā gātsā narake duḥkhaṃ bhūyāddharmānumodinī || 4.74{74} ||
śīghraṃ lokeśvarasmārttaṃ labdhvā vrajatu sanmatim |
(rm 58)
ityevaṃmanasā bhāṣya sa bhikṣurvijitendriyaḥ || 4.75{75} ||
triratnasmaraṇaṃ kṛtvā tasthau dhyānasamāhitaḥ |
tato'sau dārikā gehe prāptā bhartuḥ purogatā || 4.76{76} ||
etatsarvaṃ pravṛttāṃtaṃ vistareṇābravīnmudā |
svāminnadyāmbusaṃpūrṇaṃ ghaṭaṃ dhṛtvāgatā pathi || 4.77{77} ||
tato bhikṣurupāgatya pānīyaṃ māṃ prayācati |
adatvaivaṃ jalaṃ tasmai bhikṣave satvarāgatā || 4.78{71} ||
vahuśo yācyamānāpi kiṃ cinnaiva dadāmyaham |
iti tayoktamātre'sau bharttā sādhurapi krudhā |
tāṃ bhāryāṃ rukṣaṇetreṇa dṛṣṭvā caivamabhāṣata || 4.79{72} ||
are pāpini durbuddhe kathaṃ na dīyate tvayā |
kiyadvyeti jalaṃ datvā bhikṣave tṛṣitāya te || 4.80{71!} ||
jalamātrapradāne'pi yasyāḥ śraddhā na vidyate |
sā kimannaṃ dhanaṃ ratnaṃ kathāṃ dātuṃ samutsahet || 4.81{72} ||
dhiktvāṃ pāpinīṃ duṣṭāṃ yasyā nāsti dayārthine |
kiṃ tvayā mama satyāpi yaśodharmapraghātayā || 4.82{73} ||
yadyevaṃ na pradadyāstvaṃ kasmai cidarthine jalam |
preyasīmapi bhārye tvāṃ parityakṣyāmyahaṃ khalu || 4.83{74} ||
yadi me priyatāṃ kartumichasi snehacetasā |
arthino mānayitvā taddānaṃ kartuṃ samutsaheḥ || 4.84{75} ||
no cedevaṃ mayā tyaktā vraja mā tiṣṭha me gṛhe |
avaśyaṃ nau viyogaḥ syādekānte maraṇaṃ dhruvam || 4.85{76} ||
kiṃ tvādṛśyā striyā puṃsaḥ kevalaṃ dharmahānaye |
dharmaṃ vinā na me kāryyaṃ dākṣiṇyāpi ca bhāryayā || 4.86{77} ||
kiṃ tvayā kāṃtayāpyevaṃ mātsaryyakuladurdhiyā |
dharma eva vinaśyeta tadvraja mā gṛhe vasa || 4.87{78} ||
dharmeṇa rakṣyate lokaḥ kāntayā kiṃ hi rakṣyate |
tatkāntāpi parityājyā yadi pāpanuraginī || 4.88{79} ||
(rm 59)
bhāryā hi pālanīyā sā yadi dharmārthinī satī |
kiṃ tvaṃ pāpārthinī duṣṭā yaśodharmavināśinī || 4.89{80} ||
naivāhaṃ ca tvayā sārddhaṃ bhoktuṃ rantuṃ samutsahe |
kriyate cettvayā dharmastathā vasa mayā saha || 4.90{81} ||
no cedevaṃ mayā tyaktā tvaṃ mā tiṣṭha gṛhe vraja |
evaṃ bharttrocyamānāpi pramadā sā na bodhitā || 4.91{82} ||
mātsaryyālīḍhacittatvādroṣitā caivamabravīt |
naiva sthāsyāmyahaṃ yāsye kiṃ sthitvaivaṃ vininditā || 4.92{83} ||
kiyattvayyaparādhaṃ me yattathāhaṃ vimocitā |
kiṃ sa bhikṣurgururrājā mitraṃ vā te pitā suhṛt || 4.93{84} ||
jñātirvā sa sakhā vandhuḥ kiṃ ca bhrātā prabhurdhanī |
pradattaṃ kimadattaṃ ca kiyannaṣṭaṃ bhavetyataḥ || 4.94{85} ||
naiva kiṃcicca dāsyāmi kasmai cidbhikṣave'rthitam |
adatvā me bhavetkiṃ hi dāsyāmyahaṃ yathechayā || 4.95{86} ||
dāsyāmi vā na dāsyāmi kā carcātra tathā tava |
pāpaścenmayi pacyeta dharmo vāpi tathā mama || 4.96{87} ||
tvamatra mā kṛthāścintāṃ kiṃ tvayātra vicāryyate |
śubhaṃ vāpyaśubhaṃ vā me tava kiṃ tadvicāratā || 4.97{88} ||
tathāpi mama doṣaṃ cedyāsyāmi na vasāmyaham |
varaṃ prāṇaparityājyaṃ na tu dānaṃ dadāmyaham || 4.98{89} ||
ityasau pramadākruṣya bharturākṣipya garvitā |
kvacitkiṃcicca kasmaiciddadau naiva kadācana || 4.99{90} ||
gṛhe tithīnupāyātān dṛṣṭvaivātipraroṣitā |
vahudhā kruṣya tān gehānnyakāsayadbalādapi || 4.100{91} ||
tadāśīrvacanānyevaṃ śrutvāpi parikopitā |
hasati nindayitvaināṃ paribhāṣya nyakāsayat || 4.101{92} ||
sadā badhvā gṛhadvāraṃ yācakāgatiśaṃkayā |
koṣṭha eva samāsīnā kadācinna viniryayau || 4.102{93} ||
(rm 60)
sarvadā svāminā sārddhaṃ kṛtvaivaṃ kalahaṃ muhuḥ |
svayameva prabhuktvāsau kālamevamayāpayat || 4.103{94} ||
tataḥ kāla upāyāte pramadā sā rujānvitā |
tadāpyevaṃ na cotsehe kiṃciddātuṃ samarthine || 4.104{95} ||
tataḥ pravṛddharogārttā tṛṣṇārttā pariśoṣitā |
mṛtā sā narake yātā pretī bhavati sāṃpratam || 4.105{96} ||
yā cābhūddārikā duṣṭā mātsaryyaprahatāśayā |
saivaiṣā hi tvayā dṛṣṭā pretī narakavāsinī || 4.106{97} ||
iti maudgalya dṛṣṭvaivaṃ pratyakṣaṃ pāpaduḥkhinīm |
sarvādā śraddhayārthibhyo dātavyaṃ jalamādarāt || 4.107{98} ||
sarvavastupradānānāṃ jaladānaṃ mahattaram |
sarveṣāṃ jīvanaṃ yasmādamṛtaṃ saṃvaraṃ jalam || 4.108{99} ||
jalapānaṃ vinā sarve na jīvanti hi jantavaḥ |
tadamṛtaṃ jalaṃ śuddhaṃ dātavyamarthine sadā || 4.109{100} ||
jalameva hi pītvā te nirāhārāstapasvinaḥ |
yogino'nekavarṣāni jīvanti prāṇino yathā || 4.110{1} ||
sarvāhārābhisaṃtyaktā jivaṃti prāṇino jalaiḥ |
sarvānna paribhuktvāpi na jīvanti jalairvinā || 4.111{2} ||
tadamṛtapradānaṃ tajjīvadānaṃ pracakṣyate |
sarvavastupradānebhyo jaladānaṃ praśasyate || 4.112{3} ||
viśeṣādgrīṣmakāle tu tṛṣārttāya ca rogine |
bālavṛddhāturebhyaśca dātavyaṃ jalamāditaḥ || 4.113{4} ||
jaladānaṃ mahādānaṃ jaladānasamaṃ na hi |
yenābhidhāryyate prāṇaṃ tatphalaṃ saṃpraśasyate || 4.114{5} ||
sarvadhātupradānebhyaḥ sarvaratnapradānataḥ |
sarvānnabhogyadānebhyo jaladānaṃ mahīyate || 4.115{6} ||
kanyākoṭipradānācca gajakoṭipradānataḥ |
gavāṃ koṭipradānācca jaladānaṃ mahīyate || 4.116{7} ||
saptaratnamayībhūmipradānācca mahīyate |
evaṃ sarvapradānebhyo jaladānaṃ mahattaram || 4.117{8} ||
tṛpyante devatāḥ sarve pitṛlokāstathāpare |
sarve bhūtāśca satvāśca jalenaiva sadāpi hi || 4.118{9} ||
tathā dravyāni sarvāṇi bhūtāni hyaśucinyapi |
jalābhiṣiktamātreṇa śuddhyante sarvadā khalu || 4.119{10} ||
jalaṃ vinā na tṛpyante bhuktvāpi divyabhojanam |
(rm 61)
tasmātsarvapradānānāṃ jaladānaṃ mahīyate || 4.120{11} ||
tathā ye ca sudhāṃ pītvā divyakāntā samarpitā |
ramanti tridaśāḥ svarge te hi sarve jalapradāḥ || 4.121{12} ||
cakravarttī nṛpendro yo ramaṇībhiḥ sahāmṛtam |
bhuktvā ramati saṃmodī so'pi cāmbupradānataḥ || 4.122{13} ||
evaṃ cānye'pi ye lokā yathechā sukhabhoginaḥ |
niḥkleśā nirujā bhadrāste hi sarve jalapradāḥ || 4.123{14} ||
ye tu nīcāśayādīnāḥ kṣutpipāsāturāḥ kṛśāḥ |
yācakāḥ preṣyakāścāpi sarve te'mbho'pradānikāḥ || 4.124{15} ||
jaladānodbhavaṃ puṇyaṃ saṃkhyātuṃ naiva śakyate |
sarvairapi munīṃdraiśca kimanyairvibuddhairjanaiḥ || 4.125{16} ||
matveti śraddhayā nityaṃ divyāmṛtābhivāṃchibhiḥ |
arthibhyaḥ sarvasatvebhyo dātavyaṃ jalamādarāt || 4.126{17} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa śrutvā sarve ca bhikṣavaḥ |
sarvebhyaḥ sarvadā kartuṃ jaladānaṃ samaichan || 4.127{18} ||
athānandaḥ samutthāya natvā taṃ śrīghanaṃ gurum |
kṛtāṃjalipuṭo pṛchan tatpāpaparimocanam || 4.128{19} ||
kadā sā bhagavan pretī tatpāpātparimokṣyate |
sarvasatvābhibodhārthaṃ tatsamādeṣṭumarhasi || 4.129{20} ||
ityānandena pṛṣṭe'sau sarvajño bhagavāṃstadā |
tamānaṃdaṃ samārabhya prāha pāpavimocanam || 4.130{21} ||
śṛṇvānaṃda samādhāya yena sā parimokṣyate |
sarvasatvaprabodhāya tadupāyaiḥ pracakṣyate || 4.131{22} ||
sā pretī narakeṣvevaṃ sarveṣu bhramitā sadā |
duḥkhābhivedanākhinnā paritaptānutāpitā || 4.132{23} ||
tataḥ kālāntare saivaṃ smṛtvā ratnatrayaṃ yadi |
(rm 62)
namo buddhāya dharmāya saṃghāyeti vadedyadā || 4.133{24} ||
tadā lokeśvaro sarvalokanātho jinātmajaḥ |
dṛṣṭvā tāṃ sahasopetya svaprabhābhiḥ parispṛśet || 4.134{25} ||
yadā lokeśvarābhābhiḥ sparśitā sā sudhī satī |
tadātisaukhyatāṃ labdhvā vismitaivaṃ vyacintayet || 4.135{26} ||
aho saukhyaṃ mayā labdhaṃ kasyānubhāvato'dhunā |
kimāhosviditaścyutvāgato'nyatra vasāmyaham || 4.136{27} ||
iti cintāri tiṣṭhantī vismayoddhatamānasīm |
dṛṣṭvā lokeśvaraścaināṃ bodhayitumupācaret || 4.137{28} ||
upetya tarpayitvā ca svatanuniḥsṛtairjalaiḥ |
vividhairbhojanaiścāpi pratarppayetsa bodhirāṭ || 4.138{29} ||
tato'sau pāpinī pretī pītvā tattanujāmṛtam |
paritṛptā viśuddhāṅgā natvā taṃ prārthayettathā || 4.139{30} ||
namaste bhagavannātha kṛpayā māṃ samuddhara |
darśaya dharmamārgaṃ me sadā te śaraṇaṃ vraje || 4.140{31} ||
tayaivaṃ prārthite cāsau lokanātho dayānidhiḥ |
darśayitvāryamārgaṃ ca śrāvayeddharmadeśanām || 4.141{32} ||
yāṃ śrutvaiva ca sā pretī sarvapāpavimocitā |
tataścyutvā viśuddhātmā janma bodhikule labhet || 4.142{33} ||
tatra bodhicarīḥ prāptuṃ caredvratamupoṣadham |
tadvratapuṇyabhāvaiśca bodhicaryyāḥ samācaret || 4.143{34} ||
kramādbodhicarīḥ pūryya bodhipraṇidhimānasaḥ |
bodhisatvo mahāsatvo bhaviṣyati na saṃśayaḥ || 4.144{35} ||
tato lokahitaṃ kṛtvā saṃbodhipadamāśrayan |
sarvadā ca sukhaṃ bhuktvā sukhāvatīṃ gamiṣyati |
tatrāmitābhasaṃbuddhaṃ sevitvā samupāśrayan |
saṃbodhijñānamāsādya nirvṛtiṃ cāpyavāpsyati || 4.145{36} ||
yadyasau smarate pretī triratnānāṃ samāhitā |
tadā caivaṃ tataścyutvā saukhāvatīṃ gamiṣyati || 4.146{37} ||
no cedevaṃ hi sā pretī sadāpi narake vaset |
yāvanna smarate buddhaṃ tāvanna mokṣyate tataḥ || 4.147{38} ||
(rm 63)
evaṃ sarve'pi ye satvā yāvanto narakāsthitāḥ |
tāvantaste'pi sarve ca duṣṭāḥ pāpānucārikāḥ || 4.148{39} ||
yadaiṣāṃ ye triratnānāṃ smarantyanutāpitāḥ |
namo buddhāya dharmāya saṃghāyeti vadaṃti ca || 4.149{40} ||
tadā tatpāpanirmuktā viśuddhāṅgāḥ śubhāśayāḥ |
saddharmācaraṇaṃ kartumutsaheyuḥ samāhitāḥ || 4.150{41} ||
tato lokeśvaro dṛṣṭvā tān satvān dharmabhājanān |
bodhayitvā prayatnena bodhimārge niyojayet || 4.151{41!} ||
tataḥ sarve ca te satvā bodhipraṇidhimānasāḥ |
sarvasatvahitārthena samicheyuḥ śivāṃ carīm || 4.152{42} ||
tataste narakāccyutvā saṃprayāyuḥ sukhāvatīm |
tatrāmitābhanāthasya dharmaṃ śrutvānumoditāḥ || 4.153{33!} ||
bodhicaryyāḥ samāśritya kuryurlokahitāni te |
kramādbodhicarīḥ pūryya sarvakleśavimuktigāḥ || 4.154{34} ||
saṃbodhijñānamāsādya nirvṛtiṃ samavāpnuyuḥ |
evamanye'pi ye satvā lokeśasaraṇaṃ gatāḥ || 4.155{35} ||
te āśu pāpanirmuktāḥ sukhāvatīṃ vrajanti hi |
ye ye lokeśvaraṃ smṛtvā bhajanti satataṃ mudā |
te te sarve ca niḥpāpā bhavanti bodhibhāginaḥ || 4.156{36} ||
tasmāllokeśvaraṃ smṛtvā gatvā ca śaraṇaṃ sadā |
trimaṇḍalaviśuddhena caritavyaṃ vratottamam || 4.157{37} ||
vratottamaṃ samākhyātamāryāṣṭāṃgikapoṣādham |
sarveṣāmapi puṇyānāṃ tadvratapuṇyamuttamam || 4.158{38} ||
yenaivāśu viśuddhyaṃte ghorapātakināpi ca |
tasmādetatmahatpuṇyaṃ vratarājaṃ vidurjināḥ || 4.159{39} ||
yasya puṇyānubhāvena pūryya pāramitāgaṇāḥ |
sarvakleśān vinirjitya śīghraṃ bodhimavāpnuyuḥ || 4.160{40} ||
ye'pyatītāśca saṃbuddhāḥ sarvajñāḥ sugatā jināḥ |
te'pi tadvratapuṇyaistu śīghraṃ bodhimavāpnuvan || 4.161{41} ||
ye'pyetarhi ca saṃbuddhāḥ saṃbodhidharmadeśakāḥ |
(rm 64)
te'pyetadvratapuṇyaiśca bhavanti bodhipāragāḥ || 4.162{42} ||
ye cāpyanāgatā buddhā bhaviṣyanti tathāgatāḥ |
te'pyetadvratapuṇyaistu lapsyante bodhisaṃpadam || 4.163{43} ||
evamanye'pi ye prājñāḥ saṃbodhipadavāṃchitāḥ |
te'pyetadvratamādhāya labhanti bodhisaṃpadam || 4.164{44} ||
tasmādetadvrataṃ dhṛtvā dātavyaṃ dānamadarāt |
etatphalaṃ sadākṣīṇaṃ sūte kalpataruryathā || 4.165{45} ||
etadvratadharāḥ satvāḥ saddharmācaraṇodyatāḥ |
sarvathā na vrajaṃtyeva durgatiṣu kadācana || 4.166{46} ||
sadaiva sugatiṃ prāptāḥ sarvasatvānukaṃpinaḥ |
saddharmācaraṇaṃ dhṛtvā bodhisatvā bhavanti te || 4.167{47} ||
ye ca lokeśvaraṃ smṛtvā carantyetadvrataṃ sadā |
te paśyanti sadāpyenaṃ lokanāthaṃ jagatprabhum || 4.168{48} ||
tena lokeśvareṇāpi dṛśyaṃte te'pi sarvadā |
ye ca lokeśasaṃdṛṣṭāste sarve bodhibhāginaḥ || 4.169{49} ||
bodhisatvā mahāsatvāḥ saṃbodhiṃ samavāpnuyuḥ |
ye smaranti sadā nityaṃ lokeśaṃ karuṇāmayam |
sahasā durgatiṃ tyaktvā muktvā yāyuḥ sukhāvatīm || 4.170{50} ||
ye ca taccharaṇaṃ gatvā bhajaṃti taṃ jinātmajam |
sarvapāpavinirmuktāste'pi yāyuḥ sukhāvatim || 4.171{51} ||
ye sugandhairjalaiḥ śuddhaiḥ snāpayanti jagadgurum |
mandākinyāṃ ca te snātvā śuddhā yāṃti sukhāvatim || 4.172{52} ||
paṃcagaṃdhādisaurabhyairlokeśaṃ lepayaṃti ye |
cakravarttipadaṃ bhuktvā te'pi yāṃti sukhāvatīm || 4.173{53} ||
cīvaraṃ ye dadaṃtyasmai te divyavastrasaṃyutāḥ |
sarvadharmādhināthāśca yāṃti cāṃte sukhāvatīm || 4.174{53*} ||
satkṛtya śraddhayā ye'pi puṣpaistamarcayanti ye |
(rm 65)
te surendrapadaṃ prāpya yānti cānte sukhāvatīm || 4.175{54} ||
saurabhyayojitairdhūpairye bhajanti tamīśvaram |
te narendrapadaṃ bhuktvā yāṃti cānte sukhāvatim || 4.176{55} ||
ye dadanti ca bhaiṣajyaṃ tasmai lokādhipāya ca |
cakravarttipadaṃ prāpya yāṃti te ca sukhāvatīm || 4.177{56} ||
ye dadanti pradīpāni tasmai lokeśvarāya ca |
te mahendrapadaṃ bhuktvā yānti cānte sukhāvatīm || 4.178{57} ||
naivedyaṃ ye dadantyasmai te bhavanti nareśvarāḥ |
divyabhogyāni bhuktvā ca yānti cānte sukhāvatīm || 4.179{58} ||
ye ca pānaṃ dadantyasmai te pītvā satataṃ sudhām |
svarge ramanti devendrā yānti cānte sukhāvatīm || 4.180{59} ||
phalamūlādikaṃ dattaṃ yaiśca tasmai sutāyine |
yathepsitasukhaṃ bhuktvāṃte'pi yāṃti sukhāvatīm || 4.181{60} ||
tāmbūlaṃ pūgasaṃyuktaṃ lokeśāya dadanti ye |
surūpāḥ satsukhaṃ bhuktvāṃte'pi yānti sukhāvatīm || 4.182{61} ||
śayyāsanaṃ pradattaṃ yaistasmai satvānukaṃpine |
te kṣitīśaśriyaṃ bhuktvā yāntyaṃte ca sukhāvatīm || 4.183{62} ||
siṃhāsanaṃ pradattaṃ yaistasmai dharmānuśāsine |
te vaṃdyāḥ sarvalokaiśca yāntyante ca sukhāvatīm || 4.184{63} ||
chatraṃ tasmai pradattaṃ yaiste nṛpāścakravarttinaḥ |
yaśodharmasukhaṃ bhuktvā saṃprayānti sukhāvatīm || 4.185{64} ||
patākāṃ ye prayachanti tasmai lokādhipāya cā |
te'pi sarvāṃ mahīṃ bhuktvā yānti cānte sukhāvatīm || 4.186{65} ||
tasmai ye ca prayachanti dhāturatnagaṇāni ca |
te narendrāścaturdvīpāṃ bhuktvā yānti sukhāvatīm || 4.187{66} ||
ye vitānaṃ vitanvaṃti gehe tasya kṛpānidheḥ |
te narendrāḥ surendrāśca bhūtvā yāṃti sukhāvatīm || 4.188{67} ||
trailokyādhipatestasya ye kurvanti pradakṣiṇām |
te narendraśriyaṃ bhuktvā yānti cānte sukhāvatīm || 4.189{68} ||
saṃgītairye prakurvanti pūjāṃ tasya jagatprabhoḥ |
sadā subhāṣitānyeva śṛṇvanti te guṇākarāḥ || 4.190{69} ||
cāmarairvyajanairvāpi taṃ nāthaṃ vījayanti ye |
(rm 66)
te nṛpāḥ suralakṣmīṃ ca bhuktvā yāṃti jinālaye || 4.191{70} ||
śodhayanti ca ye nityaṃ vihāre kāruṇānidheḥ |
divyanetrāśca te saukhyaṃ bhuktvā yāṃti surālayam || 4.192{71} ||
kṛtvā ramyaṃ vihāraṃ ca lokeśāya dadanti ye |
te mahendraśriyaṃ bhuktvā saṃprayānti sukhāvatīm || 4.193{72} ||
vihāre tatra saṃsiddhe yaṣṭimāropya ye narāḥ |
suvarṇakalaśāṃśchatradhvajaghaṇṭāvalīyutān || 4.194{73} ||
paṃcavarṇapatākābhiḥ puṣpasragbhiśca saṃyutān |
saṃpratisthāṃ prakurvati mahotsāhairvidhānataḥ || 4.195{74} ||
te narendrāḥ surendrāśca yaśodharmasukhānvitāḥ |
māheśvaryyaśriyaṃ bhuktva yānti cānte sukhāvatīm || 4.196{75} ||
padyagadyamayaiḥ stotrairye stuvaṃti tamīśvaram |
niḥpāpāḥ sukhinaḥ prājñāste prayānti sukhāvatīm || 4.197{76} ||
ye cāṣṭāṃgapraṇāmāni asmai śāstre prakurvaṃte |
cakravartīpadaṃ bhuktvā te prayānti jinālayam || 4.198{77} ||
ye smaranti sadā nityaṃ tamīśaṃ sugatātmajam |
te sadyaḥ pāpanirmuktā ante yānti sukhāvatīm || 4.199{78} ||
ye ye satvāśca taṃ nāthaṃ smṛtvā bhajanti sarvadā |
te te sarve na yāntyeva durgatiṣu kadācana || 4.200{80} ||
sadāpi sadgatiṣveva yātāḥ saukhyasamanvitāḥ |
saddharmācaraṇaṃ kṛtvā saṃprayānti sukhāvatīm || 4.201{81} ||
evaṃ ye pāpinaścāpi lokeśaṃ śaraṇaṃ gatāḥ |
te'pi pāpavinirmuktāḥ śuddhā yāṃti jinālayam || 4.202{82} ||
evaṃ lokeśvaro nāthaḥ puṇyakṣetraṃ śubhapradaḥ |
ciṃtāmaṇiḥ kalpavṛkṣo yathepsitaphalapradaḥ || 4.203{83} ||
(rm 67)
na hi tena samaḥ kaścinmahatpuṇyaphalapradaḥ |
kuto'dhikastu vidyeta traidhātubhuvaneṣvapi || 4.204{84} ||
tenaiva sakalāḥ satvā bodhayitvā prayatnataḥ |
āryyamārge pratiṣṭhāpya preritā nirvṛtāvapi || 4.205{85} ||
pāpiṣṭho api satvāśca tenopetya svayaṃ tathā |
narakebhyaḥ samuddhṛtya saṃpreritāḥ sukhāvatīm || 4.206{86} ||
evaṃ sarve'pi satvāśca nānopāyavidhānataḥ |
darśayitvāryyamārgāṇi tena bodhau niveśitāḥ || 4.207{87} ||
tasmātsarvādhikeśaḥ sa lokeśvaro maheśvaraḥ |
sarvadharmānubhartā sa lokanāthaḥ pratīyate |
kenāpi gaṃtuṃ tasya puṇyaskaṃdhaṃ na śakyate |
mayāpi śakyate naivaṃ sarvaiścāpi munīśvaraiḥ |
evaṃ matvā tvamānaṃdaḥ lokanathaṃ dayodadhim || 4.208{88} ||
sarvadevādhideveśaṃ smṛtvaiva satataṃ bhaja |
tataste maṃgalaṃ nityaṃ sarvadāpi samaṃtataḥ || 4.209{89} ||
kutaścinnaiva vidyeran bhayāni vividhānyapi |
yatra lokeśvarasmṛtistatrotpātabhayaṃ na hi || 4.210{90} ||
agnerapi bhayaṃ naiva nāpyutpātamarudbhayam |
na codakabhayaṃ tatra na ca bhūmibhayaṃ khalu || 4.211{91} ||
vidyutpātabhayaṃ nāpi na cāśanibhayaṃ sadā |
na ca śatrubhayaṃ kvāpi nāpi rājabhayaṃ tathā || 4.212{92} ||
na ca caurabhayānyevaṃ na ca rogabhayāni ca |
sarvabhayaḥ praśāmyante lokeśasmṛtibhāvataḥ || 4.213{93} ||
sarve'pyupadravāścaivaṃ prayāṃti vilayaṃ tataḥ |
evaṃ lokeśvaro nātho mahatpuṇyānubhāvavān || 4.214{94} ||
yenaivaṃ trijagallokaṃ pālitaṃ putravatsadā |
tasmāllokeśvaro nityaṃ sevitavyaḥ śubhārthibhiḥ || 4.215{95} ||
yasya dharmānubhāvena sarvatrāpi śivaṃ sadā |
iti matvā tvayānaṃda taddharmāñchrāvayan sadā || 4.216{97} ||
prerayitvā ca tadbhaktau saṃbodhau saṃpraveśaya |
(rm 68)
etatpuṇyānubhāvaistvamapyevaṃ kuśalī bhavan || 4.217{98} ||
bodhisatvo mahāsatvo bhavennūnaṃ jinātmajaḥ || 4.218{99} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa śrutvānaṃdaḥ sasāṃghikaḥ |
tatheti pratimoditvā sadā lokeśamasmarat || 4.219{100} ||
evaṃ rājaṃstvayā cāpi smṛtvā lokeśvaraḥ sadā |
sevitavyaḥ prayatnena bodhipraṇidhicetasā || 4.220{1} ||
tatastvaṃ sahasā sarvapāpebhyaḥ parimocitaḥ |
bodhisatvo viśuddhātmā prayāsyasi sukhāvatīm || 4.221{2} ||
ityevaṃ guruṇādiṣṭamupaguptena dhīmatā |
śrutvā rājānumoditvā so'bhyanandatsapārṣadaḥ || 4.222{3} ||
idaṃ śrutvāvadānaṃ ye śrāvayitvā parānapi |
bhajaṃti lokanāthaṃ te saṃprayāyuḥ sukhāvatīm || 4.223{4} ||

++ iti ratnāvadānamālāyaṃ pretikāvadānaṃ samāptam ++


(rm 69)
vśālapuṣpāvadāna
athāśoko mahīpālo bhūyaḥ śrotuṃ subhāsitaiḥ |
upaguptaṃ guruṃ natvā kṛtāṃjaliravocat || 5.1{1} ||
bhadanta śrotumichāmi punaranyatsubhāṣitam |
tadarhasi samādeṣṭuṃ lokānāṃ puṇyavṛddhaye || 5.2{2} ||
iti vijñāpite tena nṛpendreṇa prasādinā |
upagupto yatiścāpi taṃ nṛpaṃ pratyabhāṣata || 5.3{3} ||
śṛṇu rājanmahābhāga sarvalokahitārthabhṛt |
yathā me guruṇādiṣṭaṃ vakṣye'haṃ te tathā khalu || 5.4{4} ||
puraikasamaye buddho bhagavāñchrīghano jinaḥ |
śrāvastyāṃ jetakāraṇye vihāre maṇimaṇḍite || 5.5{5} ||
śrāvakairbhikṣubhiḥ saṃghairbodhisatvagaṇaiḥ saha |
saddharmadeśanāṃ kartuṃ vyaharallokaśāsitum || 5.6{6} ||
taddharmadeśanāṃ śrotuṃ sarve satvāḥ samāgatāḥ |
brāhmaṇāḥ kṣatriyāścāpi rājāno rājamātrikāḥ || 5.7{7} ||
vaiśyāḥ śūdrā amātyāśca maṃtriṇaḥ sādhavo janāḥ |
śreṣṭhinaḥ sārthavāhāśca dhaninaśca vaṇiggaṇāḥ || 5.8{8} ||
paurājānapadāścāpi nānādeśasamāgatāḥ |
devā daityāśca gandharvā yakṣakinnararākṣasāḥ || 5.9{9} ||
siddhāvidyādharāḥ sādhyā vasavaśca grahādhipāḥ |
nāgeṃdrā garuḍāścaivamṛṣayo brahmacāriṇāḥ || 5.10{10} ||
yogino yatayaścāpi lokapālāḥ sasainyakāḥ |
sarve taṃ sugataṃ natvā dharmaṃ śrotumupāgatāḥ || 5.11{11} ||
satkṛtya pūjayitvā ca kṛtāṃjalipuṭo mudā |
parivṛtya puraskṛtya dṛṣṭvā tasthuḥ samāhitāḥ || 5.12{12} ||
athāsau bhagavān dṛṣṭvā tān sarvān dharmavāṃchinaḥ |
ādimadhyāntakalyāṇaṃ dideśa dharmamuttamam || 5.13{13} ||
tatra devādayaḥ sarve tatsaddharmāmṛtaṃ mudā |
pītvā cābhyanumodantaḥ prāpurjñānaviśeṣatām || 5.14{14} ||
(rm 70)
śrāvasteyāttadāneke dārikā dārakāśca te |
kusumān yācitaṃ śālabhaṃjikākhyaṃ giriṃ yayuḥ || 5.15{15} ||
tatra te sahasā gatvā dṛṣṭvā puṣpāṇyanekaśaḥ |
prādāya samupāgatvā śrāvastyāmacaranmudā || 5.16{16} ||
tatraikā dārikā vālā śreṣṭhiputrī pramoditā |
śanaiḥ sā śālapuṣpāṇi prādāya pṛṣṭhato'carat || 5.17{17} ||
tadā ca bhagāvān buddho bhikṣusaṃghasamanvitaḥ |
śrāvastyāṃ bhojanaṃ bhuktvā tato bahirviniryayau || 5.18{18} ||
tatra sā dārikādrākṣīdbhagavantaṃ tamāgatam |
dvātriṃśallakṣaṇāśītivyaṃjanaṃ samalaṃkṛtam || 5.19{19} ||
vyāmaprabhāvirājantaṃ sahasreṇādhikaprabham |
jaṃgamamiva ratnāṅgaṃ samantabhadrarūpikam || 5.20{20} ||
dṛṣṭvaivaṃ śrīghanaṃ nāthaṃ dārikā sā pramoditā |
śraddhayārcayituṃ puṣpairmanasaivaṃ vyaciṃtayat || 5.21{21} ||
aho puṇyaṃ mayā labdhaṃ yatsaṃbuddho dṛśyate'dhunā |
evaṃ kadā kathaṃ kutra drakṣyate'yaṃ munīśvaraḥ || 5.22{22} ||
bhāgyenaivaṃ mayā labdhaṃ saṃbuddhadarśanaṃ tathā |
nūnaṃ bhāgyavatī cāsmi yatpuṇyanidhirāpyate || 5.23{23} ||
tasmātpuṇyaṃ mahāratnaṃ prāptumupetya sādaram |
etaiśca kusumaiḥ sarvairarhāmyetaṃ samarcitum || 5.24{24} ||
kadāyaṃ lapsyate bhūyaḥ paravaśyā striyā mayā |
tadahaṃ sahasopetya puṣpāṇyasmāyupākire || 5.25{25} ||
sadā notpadyate nātra saṃbuddho hi kadā cana |
atisudurllabhaṃ hyasya darśanaṃ ca sadā na hi || 5.26{26} ||
mānuṣyaṃ durllabhaṃ janma vinā puṇyairna labhyate |
mānuṣye'labhyamāne'pi saṃbuddhe sevyate katham || 5.27{27} ||
mānuṣye labhyamāne'pi kṣaṇaṃ saṃpatsudurllabhā |
narairapyakṣaṇaprāptaiḥ kathaṃ buddhe'nusevyate || 5.28{28} ||
tadadya mānuṣī bhūtvā prāpyaivaṃ sukṛtau kṣaṇam |
yadyatra kusumairebhiḥ saṃbuddhaṃ nārcayāmyaham || 5.29{29} ||
(rm 71)
tato me niṣphalaṃ janma mānuṣye vyarthameva hi |
kiṃ mamānena dehena sarvathaikāntanāśinā || 5.30{30} ||
kimanenāpi jīvena mṛtyau nityaṃ puraḥsthite |
kiyatkālaṃ ca jīveyaṃ gacheyaṃ maraṇaṃ dhruvam || 5.31{31} ||
ciraṃ sthitvāpi kiṃ sāraṃ saṃbuddhabhajanaṃ vinā |
varaṃ prāṇaparityāgaṃ kṛtvā saṃbuddhadarśanam || 5.32{32} ||
anityaṃ khalu saṃsāraṃ sarvadehādikaṃ dhanam |
etadeva tu satsāraṃ yadbuddhe bhajanaṃ sadā || 5.33{33} ||
tadaitaiḥ kusumaiḥ sarvaiḥ pūjayāmi munīśvaram |
kathaṃ na pūjayisyāmi saṃbuddhaṃ trijagadgurum || 5.34{34} ||
iti niścitya sā vālā dṛṣṭvā taṃ sugataṃ mudā |
sahasopetya taiḥ sarvaiḥ sālapuṣpairavākiran || 5.35{35} ||
tataḥ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya praṇamya taṃ praharṣitā |
kiṃ ciddūre parāvṛtya tasthau dṛṣṭvā muniṃ mudā || 5.36{36} ||
tato'sau bhagavān buddhastasyā āyurvihīnatām |
matvā tāṃ saṃnrīksyaivaṃ svaṃ vihāraṃ samāyayau || 5.37{37} ||
atha sā dārikā bhūyastathānyatkusumāni ca |
gṛhasyārthe samādātuṃ tataḥ śālavanaṃ yayau || 5.38{38} ||
tatra śālataruṃ dṛṣṭvā praphullakusumānvitam |
tatpuṣpāṇi samācetuṃ sahasā sā ruroha tam || 5.39{39} ||
tatrāvaruhya puṣpāṇi cinvantī sā pramodataḥ |
agraśākhāsamārūḍhā papāta sahasā tataḥ || 5.40{40} ||
patitā sā virugnāṅgā tīvrātivedanāturā |
hā hatāsmīti niścitya saṃbuddhasmaraṇaṃ vyadhāt || 5.41{41} ||
namo buddhāya dharmāya saṃghāya sugatāya ca |
iti proktvaiva saṃbuddhaṃ smaranti maraṇaṃ yayau || 5.42{42} ||
tataḥ svargopapannā sā vismitaivaṃ vyacintayat |
kutaścyutvā kuhotpannā kena vā kuśalena ca || 5.43{43} ||
iti cintāparītā sā sahasaivaṃ vyabuddhyata |
martyāccyutvāgatā svarge buddhe puṣpapradānataḥ || 5.44{44} ||
iti buddhvā ca sā bhadrā suprasāditamānasī |
tameva sugataṃ nāthaṃ smṛtvā natvābhyanandata || 5.45{45} ||
(rm 72)
tato devairupanītaṃ divyapuṣpasvalaṃkṛtam |
vimānaṃ sā samāruhya devasabhāmupāyayau || 5.46{46} ||
tasmiṃśca samaye śakraḥ sudharmāyāṃ suraiḥ saha |
saṃbuddhavarṇanāṃ kṛtvā tasthau devān pramodayan || 5.47{47} ||
tathā tāṃ samupāyātāṃ śālapuṣpābhyalaṃkṛtām |
dṛṣṭvā divyātivarṇābhāṃ papracha vismito hariḥ || 5.48{48} ||
gātraṃ kena vimṛṣṭakāṃcananibhaṃ padmotpalābhaṃ tava |
divyaśrīratulākṛteyamiha te kena prabhā niḥsṛtā |
vaktraṃ kena vibuddhapadmasadṛśaṃ cāmīkarābhaṃ tava |
kasya tvaṃ varadevate phalamidaṃ yatkarmajaṃ bhujyate || 5.49{49} ||
iti prokte surendreṇa sā bhadrā devakanyakā |
taṃ devādhipatiṃ natvā kṛtāṃjalipuṭo'bravīt || 5.50{50} ||
saśrāvakā narāditya ākīrṇaśālapuṣpakaiḥ |
tatkarmakuśalaṃ kṛtvā rājate me'dhikaṃ tanuḥ || 5.51{51} ||
jalajenduviśuddhābhaṃ vadanaṃ me'bhirocate |
etatkarmānubhāvena vijānīyāḥ sureśvara || 5.52{52} ||
tayeti proditaṃ śrutvā devendro'sau suvismitaḥ |
dṛṣṭvā caināṃ subhadrāṅgīṃ praharṣayannabhāṣata || 5.53{53} ||
aho guṇamayaṃ kṣetraṃ sarvadoṣavivarjitam |
yatra nyastaṃ tvayā vījamitthaṃ svargopapattaye || 5.54{54} ||
ko nārcayetpravarakāṃcanarāśigauraṃ buddhaṃ vibuddhakamalāyatapatranetram |
yatrādhikārajanitāni viśobhitāni kāṃtāmukhāni kamalāyatalocanāni || 5.55{55} ||
dhanyo'si tvaṃ subhadrāṃgi saṃbuddhaguṇabhāvini |
sarvathā hi sa saṃbuddhaḥ sevitavyaḥ sadāpi ca || 5.56{56} ||
yasya puṇyaprasādāttvaṃ sadyaḥ svarge samāgatā |
sa eva bhagavānnāthaḥ saṃsmṛtvā sevyatāṃ sadā || 5.57{57} ||
tataste sarvadā nityaṃ maṃgalaṃ syātsamaṃtataḥ |
ciraṃ divyasukhaṃ bhuktvā yāyāścānte sukhāvatīm || 5.58{158} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ surendreṇa sā devakanyakā sudhīḥ |
anumodya ca taṃ śakraṃ natvā svamālayaṃ yayau || 5.59{59} ||
(rm 73)
tataḥ sā saṃsthitā devīprāsāde divyamaṇḍite |
divyabhogyasukhaprāptā moditvaivaṃ vyacintayet || 5.60{60} ||
yaccyutvāhaṃ manuṣyebhyo'dhunā svargasamāgatā |
tatsaṃbuddhaprasādena jānāmi nānyathā khalu || 5.61{61} ||
yanmayā pūjito buddhaḥ śālapuṣpairvanodbhāvaiḥ |
tatpuṇyaphalabhogyārthe āgatāhaṃ surālayam || 5.62{62} ||
yaddivyasukhasaṃprāptaṃ tatsaṃbuddhaprasādataḥ |
tena tamevaṃ saṃbuddhaṃ punargacheya vaṃditum || 5.63{63} ||
na yuktaṃ saugate gantuṃ paryyuṣitena vāsasā |
yuktaṃ tu saugate gaṃtumaparyuṣitavāsasā || 5.64{64} ||
iti sā manasā matvā devakanyā subhadrikā |
snātvā saurabhyaliptāṃgā divyaśuddhāmvarāvṛtā || 5.65{65} ||
divyālaṃkāramālādimaulīkuṇḍalamaṃḍitā |
anekadevakanyābhirapsarobhiḥ samanvitā || 5.66{66} ||
divyapūjopacārāṇi gṛhītvā sahasā yayau |
tasyāmeva niśi svarddhyā bhāsayantī samaṃtataḥ || 5.67{67} ||
jetavanamupetyāsau praviveśa jinālayam |
tataḥ pradakṣiṇaṃ kṛtvā praṇamya caraṇau muneḥ || 5.68{68} ||
paṃcopahārapūjābhiḥ pūjayitvā kṛtāṃjaliḥ |
natvā ca purataḥ tasthau saddharmaśravaṇotsukā || 5.69{69} ||
athāsau bhagavāṃstasyā jñātvāśayaviśuddhatām |
ādimadhyāntakalyāṇaṃ dideśa dharmamuttamam || 5.70{70} ||
catvāri cāryyasatyāni niḥkleśapuṇyalabdhaye |
daśakuśalamūlāni samyaksatvaviśuddhaye || 5.71{71} ||
āryyāṣṭāṅgikamārgāṃśca saṃbodhimārgalabdhaye |
bodhipākṣikadharmāṃśca saṃbuddhaguṇalabdhaye || 5.72{72} ||
athāsau devakanyā tāṃ saddharmadeśaṇaṃ muneḥ |
śrutvānumoditā cāpi prāpya dharmaviśeṣatām || 5.73{73} ||
jñānavajreṇa satkāyadṛṣṭiśailaṃ vibheda sā |
śrotāpattiphalaṃ prāptā dṛṣṭasatyābhavattadā || 5.74{74} ||
tato bhūyaḥ praṇamyaivaṃ bhagavantaṃ kṛtāṃjaliḥ |
suprasannamukhāmbhojo trirudānamudāharat || 5.75{75} ||
(rm 74)
bhagavannidamasmākaṃ mahāpuṇyasukhodayam |
jananyāpi kṛtaṃ nāpi na pitrā na ca vaṃdhubhiḥ || 5.76{76} ||
na jñātibhirna mitraiśca na sahāyasuhṛjjanaiḥ |
nāpi sālohitaiścāpi pitṛlokairna cāparaiḥ || 5.77{77} ||
na rājñā na cāmātyairna maṃtribhirna ca paurikaiḥ |
na gurubhirna vipraiśca na yatibhirna tāpasaiḥ || 5.78{78} ||
na devairnāpi daityaiśca nāpi lokādhipairapi |
tathānyaiśca kṛtaṃ naiva yadidaṃ bhavatā kṛtam || 5.79{79} ||
uchoṣito yadasmākaṃ rudhirāśrusaritpatiḥ |
asthimayo mahāchailo yadasmābhiśca laṃghitaḥ || 5.80{80} ||
tavānubhāvātpihitaḥ sughoro hyapāyamārgo bahudoṣayuktaḥ |
apāvṛtā svargagatiḥ supuṇyā nirvāṇamārgaśca mayopalabdhaḥ || 5.81{81} ||
bhavatprasādānnirupetadoṣaṃ mayādya labdhaṃ suviśuddhacakṣuḥ |
prāptaṃ ca śāntaṃ padamāryyakāntaṃ tīrṇaśca duḥkhārṇavapāramasmi || 5.82{82} ||
naravarendrasurāsurapūjite vigatajanmajarāmaraṇādayaḥ |
bhavasahasrasudullabhadarśane saphalamadya mune tava darśanam || 5.83{83} ||
tataḥ praṇatvā caraṇāraviṃdau muneḥ prakṛtvā tṛpradakṣiṇāṃ ca |
prajātaharṣā sugataṃ jineṃdraṃ smṛtvā ca bhūyo divamāyayau sā || 5.84{84} ||
tathā svargasamāyātā devakanyā mumoda sā |
yathā vaṇiglabdhalābho rogamukto yathāturaḥ || 5.85{85} ||
samyagniṣpannaśasyāpto yathā ca kṛṣikārmikaḥ |
siddhayātrāpralabdhārthaḥ sārthavāho vaṇigyathā || 5.86{86} ||
atha te bhikṣavaḥ sarve dṛṣṭvaivamavabhāsitam |
saṃdigdhavismitāścaivaṃ paprachustaṃ munīśvarām || 5.87{87} ||
bhagavannadya rātrau ko bhavato'ntikamāgataḥ |
brahmā vā surarājo vā lokapālagaṇā api || 5.88{88} ||
asmābhirjñāyate nāyaṃ yo'dya rātrāvihāgataḥ |
(rm 75)
tadasmān vismitān sarvān bodhayituṃ samarhasi || 5.89{89} ||
iti tairbhikṣubhiḥ pṛṣṭo bhagavān sa munīśvaraḥ |
tāṃśca bhikṣugaṇān sarvān samāmaṃtryābravīttathā || 5.90{90} ||
na hi sa bhikṣavo brahmā nāpi vāsau surādhipaḥ |
naiva lokādhipālāśca yādya rātrāvihāgatā || 5.91{91} ||
api tu dārikā dṛṣṭā yuṣmābhiḥ sagatā pathi |
yayāhaṃ sālapuṣpaiśca prāvakīryyābhitoṣitaḥ || 5.92{92} ||
sā māṃ puṣpairavākīryya gṛhasyārthe punarvanam |
gatā puṣpāṇyupāhartuṃ vṛkṣārūḍhāpatattaroḥ || 5.93{93} ||
tato'sau patanodvignā vedanākrāṃtacetasā |
triratnāni smarantyeva dehaṃ tyaktvā divaṃ yayau || 5.94{94} ||
tatra svarge samāsādya smṛtva māṃ sugataṃ punaḥ |
draṣṭuṃ dharmaṃ ca me śrotumihāsau samupāgatā || 5.95{95} ||
tanmayā deśitaṃ tasyai saṃbodhidharmamuttamam |
śrutvā sā cānumodyaivaṃ saṃharṣitā divaṃ yayau || 5.96{96} ||
ityevaṃ bhikṣavo matvā triratnāni śubhārthibhiḥ |
sevitavyāni saṃsmṛtvā gatvā ca śaraṇaṃ sadā || 5.97{97} ||
ye bhajanti triratnāni saṃsmṛtvā śaraṇaṃ gatāḥ |
durgatiṃ te na gachaṃti vrajanti sadgatiṃ sadā || 5.98{98} ||
ye nindanti triratnāni pratikṣipya pramādataḥ |
sadgatiṃ te na gachanti vasanti narake sadā || 5.99{99} ||
tasmānnityāṃ triratnāni saṃsmṛtvā śaraṇaṃ gatāḥ |
satkṛtya bhāvayitvā ca bhajadhvaṃ bodhivāṃchayā || 5.100{100} ||
tathā vo maṅgalaṃ nityaṃ sarvatra sarvadā bhavet |
kramādbodhicarīṃ pūrya saṃbodhimapi lapsyatha || 5.101{1} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa śrutvā te bhikṣavo mudā |
tathetyabhyanumodantastriratnāni sadābhajan || 5.102{2} ||
ityevaṃ guruṇādiṣṭaṃ mayaivaṃ te pracakṣyate |
śrutvaitacchrāvayitvā ca triratnāni sadā bhaja || 5.103{3} ||
tatastvaṃ kramaśaścaiva bodhicaryāṃ samācaran |
sarvapāpavinirmuktaḥ sukhāvatīṃ samāvrajeḥ || 5.104{3*} ||
ityetadguruṇādiṣṭamupaguptena bhikṣuṇā |
(rm 76)
śrutvā rājā tathetyevamabhyanandatsapārṣadaḥ || 5.105{4} ||
tatsaṃbuddhānubhāvaṃ munivarakathitaṃ śālapuṣpāvadānam |
ye śrutvā śrāvayanti pramuditamanasaḥ śraddhayā bodhikāmāḥ |
te sarvakleśamuktāḥ sakalaguṇadharā bodhicaryyānuraktāḥ |
kṛtvā loke hitārthaṃ jinavaravasatiṃ saṃprayaṃti praharṣaiḥ || 5.106{5} ||

++ iti śālapuṣpāvadānaṃ samāptaṃ ++


(rm 77)
vi sūkaryavadāna
punarevaṃ yatīśo'sāvupagupto jināṃśajaḥ |
triratnasmṛtijaṃ puṇyaṃ khyātuṃ nṛpamabravīt || 6.1{1} ||
mahatpuṇyānubhāvatvaṃ triratnasmṛtisaṃbhavam |
tadahaṃ te pravakṣyāmi śṛṇu rājan samāhitaḥ || 6.2{2} ||
dharmatā khalu devānāṃ svargacyavanadharmmiṇām |
paṃcapūrvanimittāni prādurbhaveyurātmani || 6.3{3} ||
akliṣṭāni hi vāsāṃsi kliśyeyuścyavanāntike |
amlānāni ca mālyāni mlāyeran sahasā khalu || 6.4{4} ||
daurgandhaṃ niḥsareddehātkakṣābhyāṃ sveda udbhavet |
pracyutisamaye prāpte svāsane na dhṛtiṃ labhet || 6.5{5} ||
etatpaṃcanimittāni svargāccyavanadharmiṇām |
sarveṣāṃ prodbhaveyuśca pracyutisamayāgame || 6.6{6} ||
puraikadevaputrasya tathā cyavanadharmiṇaḥ |
etatpaṃcanimittāni prādurāsaṃścyuteḥ puraḥ || 6.7{7} ||
atha cyavanadharmāsau devaputro viṣāditaḥ |
avaśyaṃ cyavanaṃ svargānmatvā rautsīdadhīritaḥ || 6.8{8} ||
āvartya svavimānasthaḥ parivarttya vimūrchitaḥ |
punaścaitanyamāsādya vilapaṃ paryyadevataḥ || 6.9{9} ||
hā mandākini hemāvjavyāpte puṇyāmbuvāhini |
tvayi snātvapyahaṃ cyutvā yāsyāmi durgatiṃ katham || 6.10{10} ||
hā caitrarathamutsṛjya kva krīḍeyaṃ yathechayā |
hā nandanavanaṃ ramyaṃ tyaktvāhaṃ kva rameya ca || 6.11{11} ||
hā miśrikāvanaṃ divyaṃ tyaktvā ramye kuhādhunā |
hā pāruṣyavanaṃ tyaktvā kva ramyāmyapsarogaṇaiḥ || 6.12{12} ||
kva sukhaṃ lapsyate pāṇḍukaṃvalakaśilāṃ vinā |
(rm 78)
hā devasamitiṃ tyaktvā kva śroṣyāmi subhāṣitām || 6.13{13} ||
hā hā sudarśanaṃ ramyaṃ puraṃ tyaktvā vraje cyutaḥ |
prāsādaṃ vaijayaṃtañca kadā drakṣyāmyahaṃ punaḥ || 6.14{14} ||
hā śakrapramukhairdevaiḥ sārddhaṃ kṛdāpramoditaḥ |
adhunā me kva tatsaukhyaṃ śrotuṃ cāpi na lapsyate || 6.15{15} ||
hā devyamamṛtaṃ tyaktvā kiṃ prabhokṣyāmi sāṃpratam |
divyakāmasukhaṃ bhuktvā punardurgatimāśrayan || 6.16{16} ||
kiyadduḥkhaṃ ca saṃbhoktuṃ yāsyāmi narakeṣvaham |
etaddivyamahotsāhādbhraṣṭo'haṃ narake gataḥ || 6.17{17} ||
mahadduḥkhābhisaṃtaptaḥ sahiṣyāmi kathaṃ tadā |
hā hato'smi svapāpena kiṃ pāpaṃ prakṛtaṃ mayā || 6.18{18} ||
yenetaḥ svargataścyutvā punaryāsyāmi nārakān kim |
upāyaṃ kariṣyāmi na yāyāṃ yena durgatim || 6.19{19} ||
ko me śāstātra vidyeta yo māṃ dharme niyojayet |
dharmeṇa rakṣyate prāṇī narake saṃsthito'pi ca || 6.20{20} ||
dharmaṃ vinātra devo'pi svargātprabhransyate punaḥ |
kiṃ karomi kva yāsyāmi nātropāyaṃ tu manyate || 6.21{21} ||
sarvathāhamitaścyutvā gacheyaṃ durgatiṃ khalu |
itaścyutasya me janma labdhavyaṃ bhuvane kuha || 6.22{22} ||
iti dhairyaṃ samādhāya manasaivaṃ vyalokayat |
cyutvetaḥ saukaraṃ janma prāptavyaṃ saptame dine || 6.23{23} ||
evaṃ svāptagatiṃ dṛṣṭvā papāta savimūrchitaḥ |
athādrākṣīnmahendrastaṃ patitaṃ bhuvi mūrchitam || 6.24{24} ||
dṛṣṭvā ca sahasopetya sudhāsekairasiṃcayat |
tato'sau cetanāṃ labdhvā śanairutthāya taṃ prabhum || 6.25{25} ||
dṛṣṭvā pādau pariṣvajya natvaivaṃ prārthayadrudan |
hā hā nātha mahendro'si sarvalokādhipaḥ prabhuḥ || 6.26{26} ||
māmadhaḥ patitaṃ dṛṣṭvā nopekṣāṃ kartumarhasi |
(rm 79)
bhavān hi jagatāṃ rājā svāmī bharttā patiḥ prabhuḥ || 6.27{27} ||
nātho dharmānuśāstā ca tanmāṃ rakṣatumarhasi |
tvadṛte'nyo na me kaścidrakṣako vidyate'tra hi || 6.28{28} ||
tadanukaṃpayā dṛṣṭyā dhṛtvā māmuddhara prabho |
yadi māṃ rakṣituṃ nātha na śaknoti bhavānapi || 6.29{29} ||
ko māṃ samuddharedanyo hā hato narake vraje |
maṃḍākinyāṃ tvayā sārddhaṃ snātvā pūtātmako'pyaham || 6.30{30} ||
kathaṃ ca narake gatvā vaseyamaśuciṃ caran |
sahāpsarogaṇairnityaṃ caritvā naṃdane vane || 6.31{31} ||
divyasaṃgītivādyaiśca ramāmi saṃpramoditaḥ |
adyetaḥ patito pṛthvyāṃ rājagṛhopajāṃgale || 6.32{32} ||
saukaraṃ janma lapsyāmi saptame divase khalu |
tatra mūtrapuriṣādīnamedhyānapi kṛcchrataḥ || 6.33{33} ||
labdhvā bhuktvā cariṣyāmi vyādhasaṃghairupadrutaḥ |
bahuvarṣasahasrāṇi bhūtvaivaṃ sūkarātmajaḥ || 6.34{34} ||
amedhyakardame nityaṃ vatsyāmyāvartya saṃcaran |
evaṃ divyāmṛtaṃ bhuktvā ramitvā bhavatā saha || 6.35{35} ||
kathaṃ hi nārake gatvā bhokṣye'medhyāni saṃcaraḥ |
imaṃ kalpataruṃ tyaktvā yathepsitaphalapradam || 6.36{36} ||
jāṃgalaṃ vṛkṣamāśritya sthito bhokṣyāmi kiṃ tadā |
sarvathāhaṃ cyuto'smītaḥ prerito hi svakarmaṇā || 6.37{37} ||
vividhāni ca duḥkhāni bhoktuṃ yāsyāmi nārake |
tadupāyaṃ pradātavyaṃ bhavatā me'nukaṃpinā || 6.38{38} ||
yenāśu durgatermuktaḥ prāgacheyaṃ ca svargatim |
sarvathā rakṣa māṃ nātha yadyasti te kṛpā mayi || 6.39{39} ||
bhavānmāṃ patitaṃ dṛṣṭvā nopekṣāṃ kartumarhati |
bhavāneva jagacchāstā sarvalokādhipaḥ prabhuḥ || 6.40{40} ||
tadāśu māṃ prayatnena samuddhartuṃ samarhati |
idaṃ te darśanaṃ nātha paścimaṃ me bhavetkhalu || 6.41{41} ||
(rm 80)
janmaśatasahasraiśca durlabhaṃ syāddhi saṃgamam |
ityevaṃ vilapaṃ devaputrassa taṃ surādhipam || 6.42{42} ||
natvā pādau nirīkṣyaiva tasthāvaśrugalanmukhaḥ |
atha śakraḥ surendro'sau dṛṣṭvaivaṃ pravilāpitam || 6.43{43} ||
kāruṇyā krāntacittastamāśvāsayannabhāṣata |
mā kṛthā rodanaṃ bhadra samāśvasi hi dhīraya || 6.44{44} ||
kriyante kiṃ prayatnāni sarvathā maraṇaṃ dhruvam |
mṛtyurmahāvaliṣṭho hi sarvasatvāntakārakaḥ || 6.45{45} ||
hrīyante mṛtyunā sarve mṛtyuḥ kena nihanyate |
anirvāyo hi sarvatra sarvatraidhātukeṣvapi || 6.46{46} ||
sarveṣāmapi jantūnāmekānte maraṇaṃ dhruvam |
tasmānmā bhestathā mṛtyoḥ kiṃ viṣādena sidhyate || 6.47{47} ||
taddhairyyataiḥ samālaṃvya triratnaṃ śaraṇaṃ vraja |
gatvā ca śaraṇaṃ teṣāṃ smara nityaṃ samāhitaḥ || 6.48{48} ||
tatastvaṃ durgatiṃ muktvā sadgatiṃ samavāpnuyāḥ |
etadeva hi saṃsāre mahopāyaṃ śubhāptaye || 6.49{49} ||
nānyaddhi vidyate kiṃcinmṛtyau sādhāraṇe dhruve |
tadanyaccetanāṃ hitvā triratnānyabhisaṃsmaran || 6.50{50} ||
namo buddhāya dharmāya saṃghāyeti vadan bhaja |
ye triratnasmṛtiṃ dhṛtvā mṛtāste puṇyabhāginaḥ || 6.51{51} ||
kramādbodhicarīṃ prāpya yāyuścānte sukhavatīm |
tvaṃ cāpi triratnānaṃ smṛtvā mṛtaḥ supuṇyadhṛk || 6.52{52} ||
kramādbodhicarīṃ prāpya saṃprayāyāḥ sukhāvatīm |
itīndreṇa samādiṣṭaṃ śrutvā devasuto'tha saḥ || 6.53{53} ||
tathetyanusaṃmodya punarnatvaivamabravīt |
adyārabhya mahendrāhaṃ triratnānaṃ sadā smaran || 6.54{54} ||
gatvā ca śaraṇaṃ teṣāmupāsako bhave khalu |
ityuktvā sa mahendrasya puraḥ sthitvā kṛtāñjaliḥ || 6.55{55} ||
anusmṛtya triratnānāṃ namaskṛtimudāharat |
namo buddhāya bhadrāya namo dharmāya tāyine || 6.56{56} ||
(rm 81)
namaḥ saṃghāya nāthāya sarvadāpi name bhaje |
adyārabhya triratnānāṃ sadāhaṃ śaraṇaṃ vraje || 6.57{57} ||
upāsako bhavennityaṃ namāmi satataṃ smaret |
ityuktvā devaputro'sau kāladharmayutaścyutaḥ || 6.58{58} ||
tuṣite devaloke'bhūdupapannaḥ supuṇyataḥ |
dharmatā khalu devānāṃ ye devāḥ svargataścyutāḥ || 6.59{59} ||
te'dholokeṣu gacheyurna tūrddhvabhuvaneṣu hi |
atha śakro narendro'sau dṛṣṭvā taṃ svargataścyutam || 6.60{60} ||
kutrāsau labhate janma ityadhastādvyalokayat |
kimasau sūkarīgarbha utpanno vā na veti ca || 6.61{61} ||
apaśyatsarvataścainaṃ na dadarśa kuhāpi sa |
tato'sau vismitaḥ śakro divyena cakṣuṣā punaḥ || 6.62{62} ||
kutrāsau labhate janma ityevaṃ samalokayat |
vata tiryyakṣu jāto'sau preteṣu ca prajāyate || 6.63{63} ||
atha narakeṣu vā jāta ityevaṃ samalokayat |
teṣu triṣvapyapāyeṣu nādrākṣīttaṃ samaṃtataḥ || 6.64{64} ||
tato manuṣyalokeṣu jāto veti dadarśa ca |
tatrāpi ca sa sarvatra nādrākṣīttaṃ sureśvaraḥ || 6.65{65} ||
tataścaturmahārājabhuvaneṣu prajāyate |
iti teṣu ca sarvatra vilokayan dadarśa na || 6.66{66} ||
tataśca vismitaḥ śakrastrayastriṃśe vyalokayan |
tatrāpi taṃ na cādrākṣītkutrāpi ca samaṃtataḥ |
evaṃ sarvatra lokeṣu vilokayaṃstadudbhavam |
adṛṣṭvā vismitaḥ śakraḥ punarevaṃ vyaciṃtayat |
aho vata mahāścaryyaṃ yanmayāpi na dṛśyate || 6.67{67} ||
kutrāsau jāyate nūnaṃ na manye tatpravṛttitām |
ko nu jñāsyati tadvṛttiṃ buddhādanyo na kaścana || 6.68{68} ||
buddha eva jagaddarśī ṣaḍabhijñastrikālavit |
tasmādahamito gatvā saṃbuddhe samupācaran || 6.69{69} ||
(rm 82)
vijñapyaivaṃ ca tadvṛttiṃ praṣṭumarhāmi sāṃprātam |
ityevaṃ manasā dhyātvā devendro'sau savismayaḥ || 6.70{70} ||
sahasā jetakāraṇye jināśramamavātarat |
tatrāsau sugataṃ dṛṣṭvā saharṣavismayānvitaḥ || 6.71{71} ||
praṇamya purataḥ sthitvā kṛtāṃjalipuṭo'vadat |
bhagavaṃstadvijānīyā yadihāhamupāgataḥ || 6.72{72} ||
tathāpi praṣṭumichāmi tatsamādeṣṭumarhasi |
ekaḥ kālagato devaputraḥ svargāccyuto gataḥ || 6.73{73} ||
ṣaṭsu lokeṣu sarvatra dṛśyate na mayā hyasau |
kutrāsau jāyate śāstarmayā na dṛśyate kvacit || 6.74{74} ||
tasyotpattiḥ kuha sthāne tatsamādeṣṭumarhasi |
itīndravacanaiḥ śrutvā sa saṃbuddho munīśvaraḥ || 6.75{75} ||
tasya puṇyātmanaḥ sthānamiṃdrāya samacakṣatac |
kauśika tuṣito nāma lokadhātuḥ prasiddhitaḥ || 6.76{76} ||
tatrāsau modate devo gatvā triśaraṇaṃ cyutaḥ |
ityādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa śrutvā sa tridaśādhipaḥ || 6.77{77} ||
vismitaḥ suprasannātmā praṇatvaivaṃmudānayat |
aho buddhamaho dharmamaho saṃghaṃ śubhaṃkaram || 6.78{78} ||
yatsaṃśaraṇamātre'pi satvā yānti śubhālayam |
ye buddhaṃ śaraṇaṃ yānti na te gachaṃti durgatim || 6.79{79} ||
prahāya pātakān sarvān divyalokamupāsate |
ye dharmaṃ śaraṇaṃ yānti na te gachaṃti durgatim |
prahāya mānuṣān kāyān divyān kāyānupāsate || 6.80{80} ||
ye saṃghaṃ śaraṇaṃ yānti na te gachanti durgatim |
prahāya sarvaduḥkhāni divyasaukhyānyupāsate || 6.81{81} ||
dhanyāste puruṣā bhadrāḥ saṃbodhipadagāminaḥ |
ye saṃsmṛtvā triratnāni vrajanti maraṇaṃ dhruvam || 6.82{82} ||
athāsau bhagavāñchrutvā śakreṇaivaṃ subhāṣitam |
tathānuvarṇayan prāha kauśikaṃ taṃ surādhipam || 6.83{83} ||
evametaddhi devendra yathaivaṃ tvaṃ prabhāṣase |
(rm 83)
dhanyāste puruṣā bhadrā triratnaṃ bhajaṃti ye || 6.84{84} ||
ye buddhaṃ śaraṇaṃ yānti na te gachanti durgatim |
kleśavairīṃ vinirjitya saṃbodhiṃ samavāpnuyuḥ || 6.85{85} ||
ye dharmaṃ śaraṇaṃ yānti na te gachanti durgatim |
māradharmavinirmuktā bodhidharmānavāpnuyuḥ |
ye saṃghaṃ śaraṇaṃ yāṃti na te gachaṃti durgatim |
pātakebhyo vinirmuktāḥ saṃprayānti sukhāvatīm || 6.86{86} ||
na bhajanti munīndraṃ ye te bhramaṃti bhavārṇave |
saṃbodhimārgato bhraṣṭā naṣṭā gachanti durgatim || 6.87{87} ||
ye śṛṇvaṃti na saddharmaṃ te kleśaparidāhitāḥ |
māradharmaratā duṣṭāḥ praṇaṣṭā yāṃti durgatim |
na mānayaṃti ye saṃghaṃ na te jānaṃti sadgatim |
sadā duḥkhābhisaṃtaptā bhraṣṭā gachaṃti durgatim |
nindanti ye triratnāni te duṣṭā mārapākṣikāḥ |
naiva caraṃti sanmārge vinaṣṭā yaṃti durgatim |
tasmādye mānavāḥ santo vāṃchanti sadgatiṃ sadā |
taiḥ saṃsmṛtvā triratnānāṃ karttavyaṃ śaraṇaṃ mudā || 6.88{88} ||
ye smaranti triratnānāṃ na te gachaṃti durgatim |
prahāya pātakān sarvāñśīghraṃ yānti sukhāvatīm || 6.89{89} ||
sukhāvatyāṃ jinendrasya dharmaṃ śrutvānumoditāḥ |
bodhicittaṃ samāsādya caranti bodhicārikām || 6.90{90} ||
tatastāḥ kramataḥ pūryya saṃvṛttipāramāgatāḥ |
paramārthacarīṃ prāpya nirvṛttiṃ samavāpnuyuḥ || 6.91{91} ||
idaṃ hi bodhimārgānāṃ vījaṃ vijñāya kauśika |
triratnasmaraṇaṃ nityaṃ karttavyaṃ bodhivāṃchibhiḥ || 6.92{92} ||
kadāpi naiva moktavyaṃ triratnasmaraṇaṃ sadā |
gatvā ca śaraṇaṃ teṣāṃ triratnānāṃ samācara || 6.93{93} ||
evaṃ tena munīndreṇa samādiṣṭaṃ sa kauśikaḥ |
śrutvaivamityanujñāya prābhyanandatprasāditaḥ || 6.94{94} ||
tato'sau bhagavantaṃ taṃ natvā sa racitāṃjaliḥ |
tridhā pradakṣiṇīkṛtya punarnatvā divaṃ yayau || 6.95{95} ||
(rm 84)
tato devapure gatvā sudharmamaṃdire sthitaḥ |
devalokān samāmaṃtrya tatpravṛttaṃ samabravīt || 6.96{96} ||
bho bho devagaṇāḥ sarve śṛṇudhvaṃ tatsamāhitaḥ |
yadadbhutaṃ mahāpuṇyaṃ saṃbodhihetumucyate || 6.97{97} ||
dharmatā khalu devānāṃ ye ye devā hi nākataḥ |
cyutāste te prayāntyadho na tūrddhvaṃ yānti ke cana || 6.98{98} ||
idānīṃ tu vrajeyuste ye devāḥ svargataścyutāḥ |
triratnaśaraṇaṃ kṛtvorddhvagatiṃ na tvadhogatim || 6.99{99} ||
dṛśyatāmamuko devaputrastriśaraṇaṃ gataḥ |
svargāccyutvā samutpannastuṣite'sau pramodate || 6.100{100} ||
yūyamapi ca taddṛṣṭvā triratnānāṃ samāhitāḥ |
gatvā ca śaraṇaṃ nityaṃ smṛtvā bhajadhvamādarāt || 6.101{1} ||
tato yūyaṃ na yāyāta durgatiṣu kadā cana |
sadā divyasukhaṃ bhuktvā prayāsyatha sukhāvatīm || 6.102{2} ||
ye buddhe śaraṇaṃ yāṃti na te gachanti durgatim |
kleśavairān vinirjitya saṃbodhipadamāpnuyuḥ || 6.103{3} ||
ye dharme śaraṇaṃ yānti na te gachanti durgatim |
māradharmavinirmuktā buddhadharmāṇyavāpnuiyuḥ || 6.104{4} ||
ye saṃghe śaraṇaṃ gatvā na te gachanti durgatim |
sarvapātakanirmuktā prayāsyaṃti sukhāvatīm || 6.105{5} ||
na bhajanti triratnaṃ ye te bhramanti bhavārṇave |
saddharmanindakā bhraṣṭā naṣṭā gachanti durgatim || 6.106{6} ||
durgatiṣu bhramaṃtaste duḥkhāni vividhāni ca |
bhuktvā caranti duṣṭāṃśā naiva gachaṃti sadgatim || 6.107{7} ||
tadduḥkhaṃ hātumichaṃti sukhaṃ prāptuṃ ca ye sadā |
te triratnaṃ sadā smṛtvā bhajaṃtāṃ śaraṇaṃ gatāḥ || 6.108{8} ||
tato vo maṃgalaṃ nityaṃ naiva yāyāta durgatim |
sadgatimeva yāyāta saṃbodhimapi lapsyatha || 6.109{9} ||
iti tena surendreṇa samādiṣṭaṃ hitārthinā |
śru<vā sarve'pi te devāstathetyuktvā nanandire || 6.110{10} ||
tadā sarve'pi devāśca triratnaṃ śaraṇaṃ gatāḥ |
(rm 85)
sarvadānusmarantaśca satkṛtyaivaṃ prabhejire || 6.111{11} ||
evaṃ devāstadā ye ye triratnaśaraṇaṃ gatāḥ |
te te svargādapi cyutvā naivāgachannadhogatim || 6.112{12} ||
tata ūrddhvaṃ samudgamya prāgachaṃstuṣitālaye |
triratnaśaraṇaṃ yātā maitreyaṃ samupāśrayan || 6.113{13} ||
tathā śakraśca tān sarvānmaitreyasamupāśritān |
dṛṣṭvānumodanāṃ kurvan prābhyanandatsuraiḥ saha || 6.114{14} ||
iti me guruṇā khyātaṃ tathā te vakṣyate mayā |
evaṃ matvā mahārāja triratnaśaraṇaṃ vraja || 6.115{15} ||
tataste maṃgalaṃ nityaṃ sarvatrāpi bhaveddhruvam |
kramādbodhicarīḥ prāpya saṃbodhipadamāpnuyāḥ || 6.116{16} ||
prajāścāpi mahārāja bodhayitvā prayatnataḥ |
triratnaśaraṇe sthāpya tvayā dharmapravṛddhaye || 6.117{17} ||
ityevamupaguptena samādiṣṭaṃ subhāṣitam |
śrutvā rājā tathetyevaṃ prānvamodatsapārṣadaḥ || 6.118{18} ||
ye puṇyaprāptukāmāstadidamavirataṃ sūkarīkāvadānam |
śṛṇvanti śravayanti pramuditamanasā durgatiṃ te na yānti |
nirjitya kleśasaṃghāṃstriguṇahitakarā bodhicaryyānuraktā |
maitreyaṃ bodhisatvaṃ tuṣitabhuvanāgāḥ saṃbhajanti prakāmam || 6.119{19} ||

++ iti ratnāvadānamālāyāṃ sūkaryyavadānaṃ samāptaṃ ++


(rm 86)
vii vapuṣmatkumārāvadāna
athaśoko mahīpālaḥ kṛtāṃjalipuṭo mudā |
upaguptaṃ guruṃ natvā punarevamabhāṣata || 7.1{1} ||
bhadanta śrotumichāmi punaranyatsubhāṣitam |
sarvasatvahitārthāya tatsamādeṣṭumarhasi || 7.2{2} ||
iti saṃprārthite tena rājñāśokena bhūbhujā |
upagupto yatiścāsau dṛṣṭvā taṃ nṛpamabravīt || 7.3{3} ||
sādhu rājan samādhāya śrūyatāṃ puṇyavṛddhaye |
yathā me guruṇā khyātaṃ tathā te vakṣyate mayā || 7.4{4} ||
purāsīdbhagavān buddhaḥ śrīghanaḥ śakyakeśarī |
sarvavidyāguṇācāryyastraidhātukavināyakaḥ || 7.5{5} ||
sugato'rhanmahābhijño dharmarājastathāgataḥ |
jinaḥ samantabhadrāṃśaścaturbrahmavihārakaḥ || 7.6{6} ||
sa ekasamaye tatra kapilākhye purottame |
nyagrodhāramake ramye vyaharacchrāvakaiḥ saha || 7.7{7} ||
tasmiṃśca samaye tatra dharmmaṃ śrotuṃ samāgatāḥ |
devāsuramahārājā yakṣagaṃdharvakinnarāḥ || 7.8{8} ||
garuḍā nāgarājāśca siddhavidyādharā grahāḥ |
yogino yatayaścāpi munayo brahmacāriṇaḥ || 7.9{9} ||
rājānaḥ kṣatriyāścāpi vaiśyāmātyāśca maṃtriṇaḥ |
śreṣṭhinaḥ sārthavāhāśca paurā jānapadā api || 7.10{10} ||
evamanye'pi satvāśca ye saddharmaguṇavāṃchinaḥ |
te sarve samupāgatya vaṃditvā taṃ munīśvaram || 7.11{11} ||
pūjayitvā puraskṛtya parivṛtya samantataḥ |
kṛtāṃjalipuṭo dharmaṃ śrotuṃ samupatasthire || 7.12{12} ||
athāsau bhagavān dṛṣṭvā tān sarvān dharmakāṃkṣiṇaḥ |
ādimadhyāṃtakalyāṇaṃ dideśa dharmamuttamam || 7.13{13} ||
taṃ ca dharmāmṛtaṃ pītvā sarve te saṃpramoditāḥ |
(rm 87)
satkṛtya śraddhayā nityaṃ bhajanta upatasthire || 7.14{14} ||
bhagavāṃśca tathā nityaṃ sarvasatvahitārthataḥ |
saddharmadeśanāṃ kṛtvā vyaharatsāṃghikaiḥ saha || 7.15{15} ||
tasmiṃśca samaye tatra pure kapilavastuni |
āsīcchākyo mahāsādhurmahābhogo mahādhanaḥ || 7.16{16} ||
tena kalatramānītaṃ svakulasadṛśātkulāt |
tayā sa kāntayā sārddhaṃ rarāma paricārayan || 7.17{17} ||
tasyaivaṃ ramamāṇasya sā bhāryāsītsugarbhiṇī |
tato'sau samaye'sūta dārakaṃ divyasuṃdaram || 7.18{18} ||
martyātikrāntavarṇābhaṃ divyakalpasuvarṇitam |
sūkṣmachaviṃ subhadrāṃgaṃ gauravarṇaṃ manoharam || 7.19{19} ||
maheśākhyaṃ manāpaṃ ca darśanīyaṃ prasādikam |
abhirūpaṃ supuṣṭāṃgaṃ sarvalakṣaṇamaṇḍitam || 7.20{20} ||
tatrāsau janako dṛṣṭvā dārakaṃ taṃ subhadrikam |
moditaḥ sahasā jñātiṃ samāhūyābravīttathā || 7.21{21} ||
bhavantaḥ paśyatāṃ putro mamāyaṃ divyasuṃdaraḥ |
asya jātimahaṃ kṛtvā nāmadheyaṃ pracakṣyatām || 7.22{22} ||
iti tenoditaṃ śrutvā jñātayaste sasaṃmatāḥ |
dṛṣṭvā taṃ vālakaṃ divyaṃ kalyavarṇaṃ susuṃdaram || 7.23{23} ||
saṃmīlya saṃmataṃ kṛtvā dṛṣṭvā tasyāṅgalakṣaṇam |
kṛtvā jātimahaṃ nāma sthāpituṃ saṃvabhāṣire |
yadayaṃ dārako divyavapuḥ kalyātisuṃdaraḥ |
tasmādbhavatu nāmnāyaṃ vapusmāniti viśrutaḥ |
iti saṃbhāṣaṇāṃ kṛtvā sarve te jñātivargikāḥ || 7.24{24} ||
purastāttasya śākyasya gatvā caivaṃ vabhāṣire |
yadayaṃ bhavataḥ putro divyavapurguṇānvitaḥ || 7.25{25} ||
tasmādbhavatu nāmnāyaṃ vapuṣmāniti viśrutaḥ |
tatheti samabhikhyāya pitrā sa suta ātmajaḥ || 7.26{26} ||
aṣṭābhya upamātṛbhya upanyasto'bhipālane |
tato'sau dārakastābhirdhātrībhirupacāraṇaiḥ || 7.27{27} ||
(rm 88)
pālito varddhitaḥ puṣṭa āsītpadmaṃ yathā hrade |
kramātpravarddhitaścāsau kumāro'bhūdyadā tataḥ || 7.28{28} ||
tadā suhṛtsahāyaiśca sārddhaṃ krīḍitumācarat |
yatra yatra vapuṣmān sa cakrāma bhuvi saṃcaram || 7.29{29} ||
tatra tatrābhavanmedhyā bhūpradeśāḥ samaṃtataḥ |
evaṃvidhamahāpuṇyamaheśākhyaḥ śivāśayaḥ || 7.30{30} ||
kāruṇiko mahāśrāddhaḥ svaparātmahitārthabhṛt |
dharmakāmaḥ subhadrātmā pūjyo mānyo jitendriyaḥ || 7.31{31} ||
abhivādyaḥ kumāro'pi vapuṣmān sa vyarājata |
tataḥ karmavipākena preryyamānaḥ kumārakaḥ || 7.32{32} ||
sa vapuṣmān suhṛtsaṃghaiḥ sārddhaṃ puro viniryayau |
tato'sau jetakāraṇyaṃ dūrāddṛṣṭvā pramoditaḥ || 7.33{33} ||
ramituṃ tatra mahodyāne sahasā samupācarat |
tataḥ samupaviṣṭo'sau dṛṣṭvodyānaṃ pramoditaḥ || 7.34{34} ||
śāntarūpān yatīn paśyan vihāre harṣito'viśat |
tatrāsau bhagavantaṃ taṃ dadarśa samitisthitam || 7.35{35} ||
dvātriṃśallakṣaṇāśītivyañjanaparimaṇḍitam |
vyāmābhālaṃkṛtaṃ kāmyaṃ śatasūryyādhikaprabham || 7.36{36} ||
samaṃtabhadrarūpaṃ ca ratnāṃgamiva jaṃgamam |
dṛṣṭvāsau suprasannātmā samupetya purogataḥ || 7.37{37} ||
pādau tasya munernatvā dharmaṃ śrotumupāviśat |
athāsau bhagavān dṛṣṭvā tasya cittaṃ viśodhitam || 7.38{38} ||
aryyasatyāni mārgaṃ ca dideśa bodhisādhanam |
tacchrutvā sugatoditaṃ vapuṣmān sa praharṣitaḥ || 7.39{39} ||
bhavābhisaraṇodvignaḥ saddharmaguṇamaichata |
bhitvā sa jñānavajreṇa satkāyadṛṣṭiparvatam || 7.40{40} ||
śrotāpattiphalaṃ labdhvā dṛṣṭasatyo'bhavatsudhīḥ |
saṃsāraṃ cāsthiraṃ matvā bhavabhogyasunispṛhaḥ || 7.41{41} ||
pravrajyāṃ caritumaichatsvākhyāte sugatāśrame |
tato'sau purato gatvā munīndrasya kṛtāṃjaliḥ || 7.42{42} ||
pādau natvā prasannāsyaḥ pravrajyāṃ samayācata || 7.43{43} ||
namaste bhagavañchāstaḥ śaraṇaṃ bhavatāṃ vraje |
(rm 89)
tatpravrajyāvrataṃ dehi cariṣye brahmacārikām || 7.44{44} ||
iti tenārthitaṃ dṛṣṭvā bhagavāṃstaṃ kumārakam |
pravrajyācaraṇāraktaṃ matvā caivaṃ samabravīt || 7.45{45} ||
kumāra yadi te vāṃchā pravrajyācaraṇe'sti hi |
pitrorādeśamāsādya prāgacha dāsyate tadā || 7.46{46} ||
iti śrutvā munīndrasya nirdeśaṃ tattatheti saḥ |
naṃditaścaraṇau natvā svagṛhaṃ sahasāgataḥ || 7.47{47} ||
tato gṛhe samāsādya pitrośca purataḥ sthitaḥ |
kṛtāṃjalipuṭo natvā vṛttāṃtaṃ samabhāṣata || 7.48{48} ||
adyāmba tāta saṃbuddho dṛśyate'pūrvadarśanaḥ |
sarvalakṣaṇasaṃpūrṇo divyātikāntasuṃdaraḥ || 7.49{49} ||
saṃsthitaḥ parṣadāṃ madhye bhāṣati dharmamuttamam |
apūrvaṃ śrūyate tasya subhāṣitavaco'mṛtam || 7.50{50} ||
tatsubhāṣitasudhāṃ pītvā cittaṃ me tṛpyate na hi |
bhūyastadamṛtaṃ pātuṃ vratamichāmi sāṃpratam || 7.51{51} ||
anye'pi bahavo dṛṣṭvā bhikṣavo brahmacāriṇaḥ || 7.52{52} ||
śāntaśīlāḥ śubhātmāno dayākāruṇyamānasāḥ |
tatsabhāgavrataṃ cartumichāmi khalu sāṃpratam || 7.53{53} ||
tadanujñāṃ pradattaṃ me kṛpāsti yadi vāṃ mayi |
evaṃ tasya svaputrasya vacanaṃ vajrasannibham || 7.54{54} ||
tau mātāpitarau śrutvā mūrchitau petaturbhuvi |
tataḥ putraprayatnena cetanāṃ prāpya tau punaḥ || 7.55{55} ||
pariṣvajya svakaṃ putraṃ tamevaṃ paryyadevatām |
hā putra kathamajño'si hātmaja vallabho'si naḥ || 7.56{56} ||
hā suta vaṃcyase kena hā tāta kiṃ nu vāṃchasi |
hā jīva kathamekānte tyaktvāsmān kva nu yāsyase || 7.57{57} ||
nandanaḥ supriyāścāsi kena duḥkhena yāsyase |
kiṃcidduḥkhaṃ na te dattamasmābhirvā kadā cana || 7.58{58} ||
kutaḥ kiṃ jāyate duḥkhaṃ tadvadasva yadīhasi |
ityevaṃ paridevitvā pitāsau cetasātmajam || 7.59{59} ||
(rm 90)
nivārayan pravaṃdhena bodhayituṃ samabravīt |
tvaṃ kumāra vayastho'si sukomalaḥ sukhaidhitaḥ || 7.60{60} ||
śītatāpāsahiṣṇuśca yathechābhogyasaṃrataḥ |
kiṃcidduḥkhānabhijñastvaṃ tatkathaṃ pravrajeḥ suta || 7.61{61} ||
pravrajito gṛhaṃ tyaktvā bahirdeśe vasessadā |
cīvaraprāvṛtaḥ piṇḍaṃ yācitvā svayamāhareḥ || 7.62{62} ||
anekajantusaṃcāre vane'raṇye kathaṃ vaseḥ |
jaṃgale durgame naikacaurākīrṇe kathaṃ careḥ || 7.63{63} ||
ekākī ca kathaṃ tiṣṭhervṛkṣamūle ca nirjane |
śmaśāne saṃsthitaḥ paśyanmṛtakānto traseḥ katham || 7.64{64} ||
śayithāśca kathaṃ śūnye gṛhe bhūtanivāsite |
evaṃ suduṣkarīṃ caryyāṃ carituṃ kathamichasi || 7.65{65} ||
tatpravrajyāvrate cittaṃ mā kṛthāstvaṃ nivarttaya |
iti madvacanaṃ śrutvā gṛhe sthitvā sukhaṃ rama || 7.66{66} ||
nityaṃ pravrajatāṃ duḥkhaṃ kiṃ siddhaṃ duḥkhacaryyayā |
dharme vaṃchāsti cetputra gṛhe sthitvā cara vratam || 7.67{67} ||
śṛṇvaṃśca dharmaśāstrāṇi śrāvayaṃśca bahūñjanān |
dānaṃ kṛtvā viśuddhātmā triratnaṃ satataṃ bhaja || 7.68{68} ||
yasya kiṃcidgṛhe nāsti jñātirvaṃdhuśca kaścana |
nāpi mitragatiścāpi pravrajyā tasya yujyate |
kiṃ tava setsyate putra pravrajyāṃ pākuniṣṭhayā |
sarvathā tyaja tadvāṃchā gṛhe dharmaṃ sukhaṃ cara |
iti pitroditaṃ śrutvā vapuṣmān sa viśaṃkitaḥ || 7.69{69} ||
pitroḥ pādān punarnatvā pratyuvāca prabodhayan |
satyaṃ tāta gṛhe saukhyaṃ dharmāṇāṃ caramuttamam || 7.70{70} ||
saṃpado'pi samṛddhāste kāmabhogyaṃ mahatsukham |
gṛhe parigrahāḥ poṣyāstadarthaṃ dhanamarjayet || 7.71{71} ||
(rm 91)
arjane hi mahatkaṣṭaṃ rakṣaṇe'pi prayatnatā |
aneke saṃpado'pāyā rājāgnyudakataskarāḥ || 7.72{72} ||
kṣaṇāddhareyurākṛṣya tadduḥkhaṃ sahituṃ katham |
saṃpado'nte vipattiḥ syātkasya saṃpadviniścalā || 7.73{73} ||
vipattau hīyate dhairyyamadhairyyātkleśito bhavet |
kleśito doṣamāpnoti tadā puṇye matiḥ kutaḥ || 7.74{74} ||
apuṇye kupitā lakṣmirnaivāśraye gamiṣyati |
lakṣmīvirahite gehe kuto dharmasya gocaram || 7.75{75} ||
saṃpattau tu labhenmānaṃ mānāddhi jāyate madaḥ |
madādpramādito dharme tadā dharmo gṛhe kutaḥ || 7.76{76} ||
adharmātkāmabhogyaṃ tu kevalaṃ pāpasādhanam |
kāmārthe kleśitā lokāḥ kāmārthe duḥkhabhāginaḥ || 7.77{77} ||
kāmārthe pratihanyante tena kāme sukhaṃ katham |
saṃtyevaṃ tāta sarvatra bhayāni vividhāni hi || 7.78{78} ||
traidhātukabhavasthānāṃ kasya nāsti bhayaṃ kuha |
daivātsarvatra jāyante duḥkhāni ca sukhāni ca || 7.79{79} ||
kleśacittasamutthāni duḥkhāni ca bhayāni ca |
yatra yatrāpi saṃyāti kleśitātmā sukhechayā || 7.80{80} ||
tatra tatrāpi karmotthairduḥkhairbhayaiśca hanyate |
yatra yatrāpi saṃyāti dharmacitto hitechayā |
tatra tatrāpi sarvatra nirbhayaścarate sukham |
yallokānāṃ bhayasthānaṃ tatraiva ramate yatiḥ |
vivikte nirjane'raṇye vṛkṣamūle guhāsu ca || 7.81{81} ||
śūnyagehe śmaśāne'pi sukhaṃ dhyātvā yatirvaset |
svadharmai rakṣito yogī saṃyatātmā kuto bhayaḥ || 7.82{82} ||
kimeva vahuvādena nirvāṇārthimano mama |
tannirvātuṃ svadehe'pi nirapekṣaḥ parivraje || 7.83{83} ||
tadanujñāṃ pradattaṃ me pravrajyāvratasādhane |
yadi na dīyate'nujñā mariṣye'haṃ na saṃśayaḥ || 7.84{84} ||
iti tasyātmajasyaivaṃ nirvandhavacanaṃ pitā |
(rm 92)
śrutvā viyogaduḥkhārttaḥ punastaṃ sutamabravīt || 7.85{85} ||
mā putra sahasā kārṣī maraṇābhilāṣitaṃ manaḥ |
sarvathāpyanivarttī tvamavaśyaṃ cedgamiṣyasi || 7.86{86} ||
yadichasi vrataṃ kartuṃ saddharmārthimanastava |
pravraja śraddhayā putra svākhyāte buddhaśāsane || 7.87{87} ||
evaṃ bharttroditaṃ śrutvā tanmātā mūrchitāpatat |
cirātsā cetanāṃ prāptā rudantī cāvadatsutam || 7.88{88} ||
hā putra kathamekānte māṃ vihāya kva yāsyasi |
hā me jīvo'si kiṃ paśyaṃ pravrajituṃ samīhasi || 7.89{89} ||
kiṃcidduḥkhaṃ na te dattaṃ mayāmvāyā kadā cana |
kena duḥkhena te cittaṃ pravrajituṃ samichati || 7.90{90} ||
pravrajyā śobhate putra vṛddhatve vā daridrite |
tvaṃ tu dahara ityaśca tatpravrajyāṃ careḥ katham || 7.91{91} ||
yāvacca jivitaṃ me'sti tāvanmā gāḥ suta kvacit |
yāvaccāsti gṛhe saṃpattāvadbhuktvā sukhaṃ vasa || 7.92{92} ||
yadāhaṃ maraṇaṃ yātā yadā cāpattitā gṛhe |
jñātimitraparityaktastadā vrajasva vāṃchayā || 7.93{93} ||
ityukte'pi tayoḥ pitrorudatorvighnaśaṃkitaḥ |
pādāṃ natvā vapuṣmān sa sahasā niryayau gṛhāt || 7.94{94} ||
tato veṇuvanaṃ gatvā praṇamya munimādarāt |
kṛtāṃjalipuṭo bhūtvā pravrajyāṃ samayācata || 7.95{95} ||
pitṛprāptābhyanujño'haṃ nātha sāṃpratamāgataḥ |
tatpravrajyāṃ vrataṃ dehi paṃcaśikṣāpadāni ca || 7.96{96} ||
upasampacchubhācāramanuśikṣāpadaṃ ca me |
brahmacaryyaṃ cariṣyāmi saṃbodhau kṛtaniścayaḥ || 7.97{97} ||
adyāgreṇa mahābuddha bhavatāṃ śaraṇaṃ vraje |
sadharmasāṃghikānāṃ cṃ kāyavākcittabhaktitaḥ || 7.98{98} ||
ityukte bhagavān dṛṣṭvā pṛṣṭvā hastena tacchiraḥ |
ehi bhikṣo kumāreti saṃvadaṃstaṃ samagrahīt || 7.99{99} ||
ehīti prokte sugatena muṇḍaḥ pātrī vapuṣmān sakaṣāyavāsāḥ |
(rm 93)
sadyapraśāntendriya eva tasthau svarṇṇaprabho buddhamanorathena || 7.100{100} ||
saccittalabdhaḥ sa muneḥ prasādātprayujyamāno vyaharatsamādhau |
vyāyachamānaḥ khalu bodhimārge saṃbuddhadharme ghaṭamāna evam || 7.101{1} ||
sarvaṃ ca saṃsāramanityatāhataṃ matvā ca traidhātugatiṃ vighātinīm |
kleśāṃśca sarvān pravihāya saṃyataḥ sākṣādvaro'rhaṃ sa babhūva sāṃghikaḥ || 7.102{2} ||
suvītarāgaḥ samaloṣṭahema ākāśacittaśubhagaṃdhivāsī |
bhindannavidyāṃgamivāṇḍakośaṃ prāptādabhijñāḥ pratisaṃvidaśca || 7.103{3} ||
satkāralobheṣu parāṃmukhatvātsa śakradevāsuramānuṣāṇām |
pūjyaśca mānyo'pyabhivandanīyo babhūva sa brahmavihāracārī || 7.104{4} ||
atha te bhikṣavaḥ sarve taṃ dṛṣṭvā vismayānvitāḥ |
bhagavantaṃ praṇamyaivaṃ paprachustasya karmatām || 7.105{5} ||
kenāyaṃ karmaṇā nātha vapuṣmānatisuṃdaraḥ |
pravrajya sahasārhatvaṃ prāpto bhavati cātmavit || 7.106{6} ||
iti pṛṣṭe ca tairnātho bhagavāṃstān samabravīt |
bhikṣavaḥ śrūyatāṃ vakṣye tasya ca karma yatkṛtam || 7.107{7} ||
etena yatkṛtaṃ karma tatkenānyena bhokṣyate |
yenaiva yatkṛtaṃ karma tenaiva taddhi bhujyate || 7.108{8} ||
puraikanavate kalpe vipaśvī nāma sarvavit |
samudapādi loke'rhan dharmarājastathāgataḥ || 7.109{9} ||
vidyācaraṇasaṃpannaḥ sugato bhadrakṛjjinaḥ |
sarvajño'nuttaro nāthaḥ puruṣadamyasārathiḥ || 7.110{10} ||
śāstā traidhātukasthānāṃ vināyako guṇākaraḥ |
saddharmaratnadāta sa bhagavān puṇyabhāskaraḥ || 7.111{11} ||
purīṃ vaṃdhumato rājño rājadhānīmupāśrayan |
sārddhaṃ bhikṣugaṇaiḥ śiṣyai bodhisatvagaṇairapi || 7.112{12} ||
hitāya sarvasatvānāṃ dideśa dharmamuttamam |
ādimadhyāṃtakalyāṇaṃ bodhicaryyānusārikam || 7.113{13} ||
tataḥ sa buddhakāryyāṇi kṛtvā sarvāṇyaśeṣataḥ |
nirvāṇamāyayau śānto vahnirivendhanakṣayāt || 7.114{14} ||
tato vandhumatā rājñā tattanuḥ saṃskṛto'gninā |
(rm 94)
taddhātūśca samāropya stūpa uccairmahān kṛtaḥ || 7.115{15} ||
tatra ratnamaye stūpe sadvidhāne pratiṣṭhite |
nityasatkārapūjābhiḥ kāritaṃ ca mahotsavam || 7.116{16} ||
saṃgītivādyanṛtyaiśca mahānaṃdapracāribhiḥ |
kārayitvā mahotsāhaṃ sthāpitaṃ sumahanmahaḥ || 7.117{17} ||
ekasmin divase tatra pumāneko daridritaḥ |
tanmahāṃte dine'nyasmiṃ stāpāṃgaṇamupāviśat || 7.118{18} ||
tatra stūpāṅgaṇe cāsau praviṣṭaḥ samalokayat |
mlānībhūtāni puṣpāṇi prakīrṇṇāni samaṃtataḥ || 7.119{19} ||
tāni nirmālyapuṣpāṇi vikīrṇṇāni samaṃtataḥ |
glānībhūtānyaśuddhāni dṛṣṭvā caivaṃ vyacintayat |
ayaṃ caityo munīndrasya vipaścino guṇāṃbudheḥ || 7.120{20} ||
dhāturatnamayastūpaḥ sarvalokābhiḥ pūjitaḥ |
etannirmālyapuṣpaistu mlānitaiḥ pavaneritaiḥ || 7.121{21} ||
ākīrṇo malinībhūto hyaśuddheḥ syādaśobhitaḥ |
tasmādetāni puṣpāṇi nirmālyāni samaṃtataḥ || 7.122{22} ||
apanīya viśuddhāya mārjayeyamiha drutam || 7.123{23} ||
iti dhyātvā daridro'sau nirmālyānyapanīya vai || 7.124{25} ||
saṃmārja nyāsamamārkṣī stūpāṃgaṇaṃ samaṃtataḥ |
tataḥ stūpāṃgaṇaṃ sarvaṃ virajaṃ pariśodhitam |
dṛṣṭvā prasannacitto'sau natvaivaṃ praṇidhiṃ dadhe |
yanmayātra jinastūpe nirmālyānyapanīya vai |
saṃmṛjya śobhanaṃ kṛtvā kuśalaṃ samupārjitam |
anena kuśalenāhaṃ sadā durgatimocitaḥ || 7.125{26} ||
evaṃvidhaguṇajñānalābhi bhūyāsamātmavit |
evaṃvidhaṃ ca śāstāraṃ saṃbuddhaṃ jñānabhāskaram || 7.126{27} ||
ārāgayaṃ samāśritya labheyaṃ saugatiṃ gatim |
praṇidhānaṃ tathā dhṛtvā nityamevamupāśrayan || 7.127{28} ||
tatstūpe śaraṇaṃ gatvā bhaje ratnatrayaṃ sadā |
bhikṣavaḥ kiṃ nu manyadhve yo puruṣaḥ daridrikaḥ || 7.128{29} ||
(rm 95)
ayameva vapuṣmān sa jñeyo hi nānyathā khalu |
yatstūpāṃgaṇaṃ tena saṃbuddhaguṇabhāvinā || 7.129{30} ||
samantataḥ susaṃmṛjya pariśuddhīkṛtaṃ tadā |
tena puṇyavipākena jāta āḍhyakule'dhunā || 7.130{31} ||
divyābhisundaraḥ kāntaḥ sa vapuṣmān bhavatyayam |
yatpraṇidhiḥ kṛtaḥ stūpe natvā tena sucetasā || 7.131{32} ||
tena me śāsane'pyadya pravrajyārhan bhavatyayam |
ārāgitastathārhañca saṃbuddhaḥ sugato jinaḥ || 7.132{33} ||
anena bhikṣuṇā bodhiprāptena brahmacāriṇā |
ityevaṃ karma jānīta sukṛtaṃ duṣkṛtaṃ bhave || 7.133{34} ||
yenaiva yatkṛtaṃ karma tenaiva tatphalaṃ gatam |
na naśyanti hi karmāṇi kalpakoṭiśatairapi || 7.134{35} ||
sāmagrīṃ prāpya kālaṃ ca phalaṃti khalu dehinām |
abhuktaṃ kṣīyate naiva karma kvāpi kathaṃ cana || 7.135{36} ||
nāgnibhirdahyate karma vāyubhiśca na śuṣyate |
jalaiśca klidyate nāpi kṣīyate nāpi bhūmiṣu || 7.136{37} ||
anyathāpi bhavennaiva karmaṇāṃ gatirābhavam |
śuklānāṃ śubhataiva syātkṛṣṇānāṃ durgatissadā || 7.137{38} ||
miśritā miśritānaṃ tu bhujyaṃte sarvajantubhiḥ |
tasmādapāsya kṛṣṇāni karmāṇi miśritāni ca || 7.138{39} ||
caraṇīyāṃ śubheṣveva karmasu sukhavāṃchibhiḥ |
tatheti bhikṣavaḥ sarve te ca lokāḥ sabhāśritāḥ || 7.139{40} ||
buddhavāco'mṛtaṃ pītvā nananduḥ śubhakāminaḥ |
evametanmahārāja śrutaṃ me gurubhāṣitam || 7.140{41} ||
evaṃ śrutvā tvayāpyevaṃ karttavyaṃ puṇyameva hi |
puṇyameva bhavettrātā suhṛnmitraṃ hitaṃkaraḥ || 7.141{42} ||
nānyaḥ kaścidihāmutra vidyate bhavacāriṇām |
tasmātpuṇyaṃ svayaṃ śrutvā karttavyamādarātsadā || 7.142{43} ||
prajāśca śrāvayitvaivaṃ puṇye saṃsthāpaya sadā |
ityevaṃ guruṇādiṣṭamupaguptena bhikṣuṇā || 7.143{44} ||
śrutvā rājā janaiḥ sārddhaṃ tatheti prābhyanaṃdata |
(rm 96)
śṛṇvanti ye śrāvayantīha yaśca vapuṣmato'daḥ prathitāvadānam |
te kleśanirmuktaviśuddhacittāḥ prayānti nūnaṃ sugatālayeṣu || 7.144{45} ||

++ iti ratnāvadānamālāyāṃ vapuṣmatāvadānaṃ nāma samāptam ++


(rm 97)
viii praśnottarāvadāna
athāśoko mahīpālaḥ saṃbuddhaguṇalālasaḥ |
upaguptaṃ guruṃ natvā prāṃjaliścaivamabravīt || 8.1{1} ||
bhadanta śrotumichāmi punaranyatsubhāṣitam |
yathā te guruṇādiṣṭaṃ tathā me khyātumarhasi || 8.2{12} ||
iti tena narendreṇa pṛṣṭe'sau ca jināṃśajaḥ |
upagupto yatirbhūyastaṃ nṛpeśamabhāṣata || 8.3{31} ||
śṛṇu rājaṃstathā vakṣye yathā me guruṇoditam |
śrutvā ca bhavatāpyevaṃ karttavyaṃ dharmasādhanam || 8.4{41} ||
puraikasamaye cāsau śākyasiṃhosdayākaraḥ |
sarvajñaḥ sugato nāthaḥ śāstā traidhātukādhipaḥ || 8.5{5} ||
sarvavidyādhipo buddhaḥ ṣaḍabhijño munīśvaraḥ |
mārajiṣṇurvinetārhaṃ dharmarājastathāgataḥ || 8.6{6} ||
sarvasatvahitārthāya śrāvastyāṃ vahirāśrame |
jetavane vihārasthe vyaharacchrāvakaiḥ saha || 8.7{7} ||
bodhisatvagaṇaiścāpi sūpāsakaiśca cailakaiḥ |
devāsuragaṇaiścāpi caturvarṇṇādimānavaiḥ || 8.8{8} ||
caturbhiśca mahārājaiḥ sasainyaparivārakaiḥ |
sarvasatvaiśca pauraiśca saddharmaguṇavāṃchibhiḥ || 8.9{9} ||
satkṛtya pūjayitvā ca parivṛtya puraskṛtaḥ |
sa bhagavān sabhāmadhye siṃhāsane sthito vabhau || 8.10{10} ||
tadā sa bhagavān dṛṣṭvā tāṃ sabhāṃ samupasthitām |
ādimadhyāntakalyāṇaṃ dideśa dharmamuttamam || 8.11{11} ||
te taddharmāmṛtaṃ pītvā sarve lokāḥ pramoditāḥ |
bhagavantaṃ punarnatvā svasvasthānaṃ pratīyire || 8.12{12} ||
tato'sau bhagavān rātrau dhyānāgāre samāviśan |
paryyaṅkaṃ samupāśritya tasthau dhyānasamāhitaḥ || 8.13{13} ||
tadaikā devatā tasyāṃ rātrau svargātsamāgatā |
svakāntyā bhāsayaṃtyevaṃ jetavanamupāviśat || 8.14{14} ||
(rm 98)
tatastāṃ bhagavān dṛṣṭvā devatāṃ samupāgatām |
saṃnirīkṣya samāmaṃtrya tathaināṃ samabodhayat || 8.15{15} ||
devate svāgataṃ kacci kuśalaṃ te samehi bho |
prāyātāsi yadarthena tadvadasva prapūraye || 8.16{16} ||
ityādiṣṭe munīndreṇa tenāsau devatā mudā |
saṃpṛchāvasaraṃ labdhvā harṣitā samupākramat || 8.17{17} ||
tatrāsau purato gatvā natvā pādau kṛtāṃjaliḥ |
pradakṣiṇatrayaṃ kṛtvā tasthāvekāntike muneḥ || 8.18{18} ||
sarvajetavanaṃ tasyā devatāyāḥ prabhāvataḥ |
udāreṇāvabhāsena pravisphuṭaṃ vabhau tadā || 8.19{19} ||
tathāvabhāṣitaṃ dṛṣṭvā sarve te bhikṣusāṃghikāḥ |
vismitāḥ sahasotthāya dṛṣṭvā tamupatasthire || 8.20{20} ||
atha sā devatotthāya kṛtāṃjalipuṭo'gragā |
bhagavantaṃ munīndraṃ taṃ praṇatvaivamapṛchata || 8.21{21} ||
sarvajña bhagavaṃ chāstaḥ yenāhaṃ samupāgatā |
tadarthaṃ tvaṃ samādiśya cittaṃ me paribodhaya || 8.22{22} ||
ke narāḥ sugatiṃ yānti ke narāḥ svargagāminaḥ |
keṣāṃ cāpi divārātrau sadā puṇyaṃ pravarddhate || 8.23{23} ||
ke cāpi durgatiṃ yāṃti ke cādhaḥ patitā narāḥ |
keṣāṃ cāpi divārātrau sadā pāpaṃ pravarddhate || 8.24{24} ||
kiṃdado valavāṃ syācca kiṃdadaśca praśobhitaḥ |
kiṃdadaḥ sukhito lokaḥ cakṣuṣmānapi kiṃpradaḥ || 8.25{25} ||
kiṃ nu vā niśitaṃ śastraṃ kiṃ vā hālāhalaṃ viṣam |
kiṃ ca prajvalito vahniḥ kiṃ mahaddāruṇaṃ tamaḥ || 8.26{26} ||
gṛhītaṃ kiṃ nu martyena kin teneha samujjhitam |
abhedyaṃ kavacaṃ kiṃ ca kiṃ vā tīkṣṇamihāyudham || 8.27{27} ||
ko nvasau procyate cauro dhanaṃ kiṃ vā satāṃ matam |
ke vā traidhātuke loke procyante muṣitaiti || 8.28{28} ||
kaśceha satsukhī loke ko vā ca parameśvaraḥ |
ko vibhūṣito nityaṃ kaścāpyatra vidaṃvitaḥ || 8.29{29} ||
vatsalo vāṃdhavo ko nu ko vā duṣṭāśayo ripuḥ |
kiṃ mahaddāruṇaṃ duḥkhaṃ kiṃ mahatparamaṃ sukham || 8.30{30} ||
(rm 99)
kiṃ ca loke priyo pathyaḥ kiṃ vāpathyaṃ na cāpriyam |
ko nu pīḍākaro vyādhiḥ ko nu vai ko bhiṣagvaraḥ || 8.31{31} ||
kenāyamāvṛto lokaḥ kena loko vaśīkṛtaḥ |
kena tyajati mitrāṇi kena svargaṃ na gachati || 8.32{32} ||
kena mitrāṇi varddhyaṃte kena śāmyaṃti śatravaḥ |
kena svargamavāpnoti kena mokṣaṃ ca gachati || 8.33{33} ||
kenāyaṃ badhyate lokaḥ kena loko vimucyate |
kasyeha viprahāṇena nirvāṇamiti kathyate || 8.34{34} ||
kiṃ nu rājā ca caurāśca syaṃdamānāḥ samudyatāḥ |
no śaknuvantyapāhartuṃ striyo vā puruṣasya ca || 8.35{35} ||
kiṃ na dahati saptārciḥ kiṃ na bhinatti mārutaḥ |
kiṃ vā na kledayaṃtyāpaḥ kiṃ na kṣīṇaṃ ca bhūmiṣu || 8.36{36} ||
imaṃ saṃśayamadyāpi mama tacchetumarhasi |
asmāllokātparaṃ lokaṃ ko gato'tyaṃtavaṃcitaḥ || 8.37{37} ||
kiṃ nu hatvā sukhaṃ śete kiṃ ca hatvā na śocati |
kasya caikasya dharmasya vadhaṃ saṃśasi gautama || 8.38{38} ||
mayaitadbhagavan pṛṣṭaḥ sarvajño'si yato sudhīḥ |
tanmama saṃśayaṃ chetuṃ samyagādeṣṭumarhasi || 8.39{39} ||
tayaivaṃ pṛchyamāno'sau bhagavān sarvavijjinaḥ |
devatāṃ tāṃ samālokya tatpraśnottaramabravīt || 8.40{40} ||
śṛṇu tvaṃ devate samyakkṛtvā cittaṃ samāhitam |
tavaitatparipṛchāyāḥ pratyuttaramudāhare || 8.41{41} ||
ārāmāropakā ye'tra ye ca vā setukārakāḥ |
prapātodakayānaṃ ca pradadanti pratiśrayam || 8.42{42} ||
śraddhāśīlena satyena kṣamayā vītamatsarāḥ |
te narāḥ sugatiṃ yānti te narāḥ svargagāminaḥ || 8.43{43} ||
teṣāmeva hi martyānāṃ divārātrau niraṃtaram |
avichinnāḥ puṇyadhārāḥ pravarddhyante sadā khalu || 8.44{44} ||
daśākuśalakarttāro yena saddharmaniṃdakāḥ |
te narā durgatiṃ yānti bhramanti pāpacāriṇaḥ || 8.45{45} ||
ye cātatāyino martyāḥ svakuladharmanaṣṭakāḥ |
te narāḥ patitā yanti narake duḥkhabhāginaḥ || 8.46{46} ||
(rm 100)
teṣāṃ pāpiṣṭhasatvānāṃ divārātrau niraṃtaram |
avichinnāḥ pāpadhārāḥ pravahante sadā khalu || 8.47{47} ||
annado valavān bhogī vastradaḥ śobhito bhavet |
pānadaḥ sukhitaḥ tṛptaścakṣuṣmān bhavati dīpadaḥ || 8.48{48} ||
duṣṭavāgniśitaṃ śastraṃ rāgo hālahalaṃ viṣam |
dveṣaḥ prajvalito vahniravidyā dāruṇaṃ tamaḥ || 8.49{49} ||
gṛhītaṃ yatsvayaṃ dattaṃ yadgṛhe tadihojjhitam |
abhedyaṃ kavacaṃ kṣāntiḥ prajñā tīkṣṇaṃ mahāyudham || 8.50{50} ||
vitarko'kuśalaścauraḥ śilaṃ dhanaṃ satāṃ matam |
te eva muṣitā loke yaiḥ śīlaṃ vinipātitam || 8.51{51} ||
alpechaḥ satsukhī loke saṃtuṣṭaḥ parameśvaraḥ |
śīlavān bhūṣito nityaṃ naṣṭaśīlo vidaṃvitaḥ || 8.52{52} ||
vatsalo vāndhavaḥ puṇyaṃ pāpaṃ duṣṭāśayo ripuḥ |
nārakaṃ dāruṇaṃ duḥkhaṃ skaṃdhābhāvaparaṃ sukham || 8.53{53} ||
kāmāḥ priyā apathyā hi mokṣaḥ pathyo priyaḥ satām |
dveṣaḥ pīḍākaro vyādhiḥ buddha eko bhiṣagvaraḥ || 8.54{54} ||
ajñānenāvṛto loko mohanaḥ pravaśīkṛtaḥ |
lobhāttyajati mitrāṇi saṃgātsvargaṃ na gachati || 8.55{55} ||
tyāgānmitrāṇi varddhante maitryā śāmyanti śatravaḥ |
śīlātsvargamavāpnoti jñānānmokṣaṃ su gachati || 8.56{56} ||
ichayā badhyate loko nīchayā ca vimucyate |
tṛṣṇāyā viprahāṇena nirvāṇamiti kathyate || 8.57{57} ||
puṇyaṃ rājā ca caurāśca syaṃdamānāḥ samudyatāḥ |
na śaknuvaṃtyapāhartuṃ striyo vā puruṣasya ca || 8.58{58} ||
puṇyaṃ na dahate vahnirbhinatti ca na mārutaḥ |
puṇyaṃ na kledayaṃtyāpo naiva kṣīṇvaṃti bhūmayaḥ || 8.59{59} ||
vidyamāneṣu bhogyeṣu puṇyaṃ yena na saṃcitam |
asmāllokātparaṃ lokaṃ sa gato'tyaṃtavaṃcitaḥ || 8.60{60} ||
krodhaṃ hatvā sukhaṃ śete krodhaṃ hatvā na śocati |
(rm 101)
krodhasyaikasya dharmasya vadhaṃ saṃśāmi sarvadā || 8.61{61} ||
etatpraśnottaraṃ śrutvā devatāsau pramoditā |
bhagavaṃtaṃ punarnatvā prasaṃśyaivamavocata || 8.62{62} ||
dhanyo'si bhagavan buddha sarvapraśnottarapradaḥ |
sarvajño jagatāṃ śāstā ṣaḍabhijño munīśvaraḥ || 8.63{63} ||
vīrasya ca na paśyāmi brāhmaṇyaṃ parinirvṛtim |
sarvavīro bhayātītastrātuṃ loke'bhiśaktibhāg || 8.64{64} ||
ityuktvā devatā bhūyaḥ kṛtvā pradakṣiṇatrayam |
natvā pādau munīndrasya tata eva divaṃ yayau || 8.65{6} ||
atha te bhikṣavaḥ sarve śrutvā śāstrānudeśitam |
satyamevaṃ parijñāya samyakkarmasu cerire || 8.66{66} ||
evametanmahārāja guruṇā me prabhāṣitam |
śrutvā caivaṃ parijñāya saddharme nirato bhava || 8.67{67} ||
dharmeṇa sugatiṃ yāyātpāpena durgatiṃ vrajet |
tasmāddharmaṃ puraskṛtya prajāḥ samyakprapālaya || 8.68{68} ||
daśākuśalakarmāṇi tyaktvā saṃvṛtimācara |
sarvopakaraṇaṃ dānaṃ dātavyaṃ svargavāṃchinā || 8.69{69} ||
duṣṭasaṃgaṃ parityajya hatvā rāgaviṣaṃ muhuḥ |
dveṣāgniśamanaṃ kṛtvā hyavidyā saṃpraghātyatām || 8.70{70} ||
kāmavitarkatāṃ tyaktvā bodhipraṇidhicetasā |
trikāyaśodhanaṃ kṛtvā śīladhanamupārjyatām || 8.71{71} ||
skaṃdhābhāvaṃ parijñāya matvā duṣṭāśayaṃ ripum |
kṣamāsvāvaraṇaṃ dhṛtvā duṣṭasaṃghān vijīyatām || 8.72{72} ||
māradharmān vinirjitya saṃbodhidharmasādhane |
dhairyavīryamahotsāhairudyamaṃ kriyatāṃ sadā || 8.73{(73)} ||
kleśamānamadāñjitvā kṛtvā cittaṃ samāhitam |
sarvasatvahitārtheṣu dhyātāṃ bodhisādhanam || 8.74{(74)} ||
lokaṃ māyāsamudbhūtamajñānatamasāvṛtam |
dṛṣṭvā prajñāpradīpena preryatāṃ sugateḥ pathi || 8.75{(75)} ||
evaṃ rājan bhavānmatvā duṣṭasaṃgān vivarjayet |
(rm 102)
satsaṃgaṃ nirato nityaṃ triratnasevako bhava || 8.76{(76)} ||
sarvā api prajāścaivaṃ paribodhya prayatnataḥ |
preraṇīyā tvayā rājan saṃvṛtticaraṇe sadā || 8.77{(77)} ||
evaṃ matvā bhavāṃ rājā saṃsārasāramāpnuvan |
kramādbodhicarī pūrya saṃbuddhapadamāpnuyāt || 8.78{(78)} ||
ityetadguruṇādiṣṭamupaguptena bhikṣuṇā |
śrutvā tatheti rājāsāvabhyanaṃdatsaparṣikāḥ || 8.79{(79)} ||
devatāparipṛchākhyasūtraṃ śṛṇvaṃti ye mudā |
śrāvayaṃti ca te saukhyaṃ bhuktvā yāṃti sukhāvatīm || 8.80{(80)} ||

++ iti devatāparipṛchāsūtraṃ samāptaṃ ++


(rm 103)
ix śuklāvadāna
athāśoko nareṃdraśca kṛtāṃjalipuṭo mudā |
upaguptaṃ guruṃ natvā punaḥ śrotuṃ samarthayat || 9.1{(1)} ||
bhadaṃta śrotumichāmi punaranyatsubhāṣitam |
yathā te guruṇādiṣṭaṃ tanmamākhyātumarhasi || 9.2{(2)} ||
iti pṛṣṭe nṛpeṇāsauvupagupto jināṃśajaḥ |
tamaśokaṃ mahīnāthaṃ saṃnirīkṣya samabravīt || 9.3{(3)} ||
śṛṇu vakṣyāmi te rājaṃ yathā me guruṇoditam |
śrutvaitadbhavatāpyevaṃ karttavyaṃ dharmamādarāt || 9.4{(4)} ||
puraikasamaye rājaṃ śākyasiṃho dayāṃbudhiḥ |
sarvajño dharmarāṇnāthaḥ śāstā traidhātukādhipaḥ || 9.5{(5)} ||
sarvavidyādhipo buddhaḥ ṣaḍabhijño munīśvaraḥ |
jino'rhan sugato buddhastathāgato vināyakaḥ || 9.6{(6)} ||
bhagavān satvahitārthena pure kapilavastuni |
nyagrodhamaṇḍitodyāne karaṇḍakanivāpake || 9.7{(7)} ||
śrāvakabhikṣusaṃghaiśca bodhisatvagaṇaiḥ saha |
saddharmadeśanāṃ kartuṃ vijahāra jināśrame || 9.8{(8)} ||
taddharmaṃ śrotumāyātairupāsakaiśca cailakaiḥ |
devāsuragaṇaiścāpi caturbhi lokapālakaiḥ || 9.9{(9)} ||
cāturvarṇṇyairmanuṣyaiśca paurajānapadādibhiḥ |
saṃbodhicaraṇotsāhaiḥ saddharmaguṇalālasaiḥ || 9.10{(10)} ||
satkṛto pūjitaścaivaṃ parivṛtya puraskṛtaḥ |
bhagavān sa sabhāmadhye siṃhāsanasthito vabhau || 9.11{(11)} ||
tatra sa bhagavān dṛṣṭvā sabhāṃ tāṃ samupasthitām |
ādimadhyāṃtakalyāṇaṃ dideśa dharmamuttamam || 9.12{(12)} ||
tatsaddharmāmṛtaṃ pītvā sarve satvāśca moditāḥ |
tathainaṃ śrīghanaṃ nityaṃ bhejire dharmalālasāḥ || 9.13{(13)} ||
tasmiṃśca samaye tatra pure kapilavastuni |
rohiṇākhyo mahāchākyo dhanāḍhyo dhanadopamaḥ || 9.14{(14)} ||
mahābhogo mahāśrāddhaḥ suviśālaparigrahaḥ |
dātā sādhurmahā dhīraḥ pratyuvāsa sukhānvitaḥ || 9.15{15} ||
samaye tena śākyena svakulasadṛśātkulāt |
bhāryā nītā subhadrāṅgī svakuladharmacāriṇī || 9.16{16} ||
tato'sau rohiṇaḥ śākyastayaiva kāntayā saha |
(rm 104)
mahāragānurāgeṇa rarāma paricārayan || 9.17{17} ||
tasyaivaṃ ramamāṇasya kṛīḍato'pyanurāgataḥ |
na putro na sutā vāpi sucireṇāpyajāyata || 9.18{18} ||
tato'sau rohiṇaḥ putrīputralābhe samutsukaḥ |
putracintāparaḥ koṣṭhe sthitvaivaṃ ca vyacintayat || 9.19{19} ||
īyatsaṃpadgṛhe me'sti na ca putrasutāpi na |
etā me saṃpado rājā mamātyayādgrahīṣyati || 9.20{20} ||
hā me saṃpadvinaṣṭā syātparatrāpi gatirna hi |
vyarthasmajjanma saṃsāre yato me santatirna hi || 9.21{21} ||
ko me kuryācca saṃskāraṃ kaśca piṇḍaṃ pradāsyati |
kutrāhaṃ śaraṇaṃ yāyāṃ hā daivanihato'smyaham || 9.22{22} ||
iti cintāviṣaṇṇo'sau śokāgāre vyavasthitaḥ || 9.23{23} ||
kapolaṃ svakare dhṛtvā tasthau bhogyanirutsukaḥ |
evaṃ cintāsamākrāntaṃ rohiṇaṃ taṃ nirutsukam || 9.24{24} ||
dṛṣṭvā sarve suhṛnmitrā vāndhavāśca vyabodhayan |
kimevaṃ tiṣṭhase bhadra kiṃ ca duḥkhaṃ prajāyate || 9.25{25} ||
vaktavyaṃ cettathāsmākaṃ vaktumarhasi sarvathā |
iti pṛṣṭe ca taiḥ sarvairvandhumitrasuhṛjjanaiḥ || 9.26{26} ||
rohiṇo'sau purasteṣāṃ sarvamevamabhāṣata |
bhavantaḥ śṛṇuta sarve kathyate duḥkhatā mayā || 9.27{27} ||
mahatī me gṛhe saṃpadasyā bhoktā na vidyate |
kathaṃ me vidyate vaṃśastatprakāraṃ na manyate || 9.28{28} ||
yadi vo'sti mayi snehastadupāyaṃ pracakṣyatām |
evaṃ tenārthitaṃ śrutvā sarve te snehavaṃdhitāḥ || 9.29{29} ||
tasya vaṃśasamutpatterupāyaṃ samupādiśan |
mā kārṣīḥ śokatāṃ bhadra tadupāyaṃ nu vidyate || 9.30{30} ||
devatārādhanāṃ kṛtvā yācasva putramātmanā |
