avaśyaṃ devatānāṃ te prasannānāṃ prasādataḥ || 9.31{31} ||
putro vā duhitā vāpi bhaviṣyati na saṃśayaḥ |
ityetattaiḥ samādiṣṭaṃ śrutvāsau rohiṇo mudā || 9.32{32} ||
devatārādhanāṃ kartuṃ prayatātmā samārabhat |
pūjāṅgaiḥ pūjāyitvā ca brāhmaṇaṃ harimīśvaram || 9.33{33} ||
(rm 105)
iṃdrādilokapālāñca saṃtatiṃ samayācata |
evaṃ putrābhinandī sa sadāraḥ vaṃśakāmyayā || 9.34{34} ||
devatārādhanaṃ kṛtvā tasthau nityaṃ samutsukaḥ |
tathāpi tasya śākyasya svadaivaparimāṇataḥ || 9.35{35} ||
na putro na ca putrī vā suciramapyajāyata |
tato'sau rohiṇaḥ śākyo devatāsu nirāśayā || 9.36{36} ||
putravāṃchāviraktātmā tasthau gehe nirutsavaḥ |
asti caiṣa mahārāja pravādo laukikodbhavaḥ || 9.37{37} ||
yaddevatāprasādena jāyate saṃtatiḥ khalu |
evaṃ cedvā tathā rājannekaikasya bhavetkhalu || 9.38{38} ||
yathā putrasahasraṃ vā nṛpasya cakravarttinaḥ |
api tu saṃmukhībhāvāttristhānānāṃ nṛpeśvara || 9.39{39} ||
putrā vāpi sutāścāpi jāyaṃte prāṇināṃ khalu |
tadyathā pitarau raktau kalyā cartumatī prasūḥ |
gaṃdharvaḥ prasthitaḥ kāle tadā garbhe samudbhavet || 9.40{40} ||
tataśca samaye tasya rohiṇasyānurāgiṇaḥ |
bhāryyā kalyāvatībhūtā snātvā ratiṃ samārabhat || 9.41{41} ||
tadaikā devatā svargāccyutvā karmānubhoginī |
tasya śākyasya bhāryyāyā garbhe sā samupāviśat || 9.42{42} ||
tataḥ svāpannasatvā sā matvā garbhasamudbhavam |
svāmino'gre rahaḥ sthitvā harṣitaivaṃ nyavedayat || 9.43{43} ||
svāminnāpannasatvāsmi vaṃśa āvāṃ samudbhavet |
tadbhavān sarvakṛyāni kārayatvanumoditaḥ || 9.44{44} ||
iti bhāryoditaṃ śrutvā rohiṇo'sau pramoditaḥ |
bhāryāṃ tāṃ garbhiṇīṃ dṛṣṭvā saṃsmitaḥ pratyabhāṣata || 9.45{45} ||
satyaṃ me bhāṣitaṃ bhadre matvā bhava samāhitā || 9.46{45!} ||
pramādamatra mā kārṣī yadi snehāsti te mayi |
evaṃ te kuśalaṃ bhadre yadi putraṃ janiṣyasi || 9.47{46} ||
tadā niṣkāśayāmi tvāṃ yadi putrīṃ janiṣyasi |
iti bharturvacaḥ śrutvā sā strī vivṛddhadohadā |
(rm 106)
bharturviyogaśaṃkārttā babhūva parikheṭitā || 9.48{47} ||
yāvatsā samaye sūta dārikāṃ divyasuṃdarīm |
śuklavastraparītāṅgāṃ garbhamalāviliptitām || 9.49{48} ||
tatastāṃ dārikāṃ jātāṃ śrutvāsau rohiṇo ruṣā |
pratyākhyātuṃ svayaṃ bhāryāṃ viveśa sūtikāgṛhe || 9.50{49} ||
prajāpatyā tayā bharturupanītā puraḥ sutā |
śuklavastraparītāṅgā bhadrāṅgī divyasuṃdarī || 9.51{50} ||
tataḥ sa rohiṇo dṛṣṭvā tāṃ sutāṃ divyasuṃdarīm |
śuklavastraparītāṅgāṃ paravismayamāyayau || 9.52{51} ||
aho bhāgyānmayā labdhā suteyaṃ bhadrarūpiṇī |
yadeṣā sahajaiḥ śuklavastraiḥ prāvṛtya jāyate || 9.53{52} ||
tasmānnūnamiyaṃ sākṣāddevakanyāvatāritā |
saddharmacāriṇī bhadrā bhaviṣyati na saṃśayaḥ || 9.54{53} ||
ityuktvāsau pitā śākyaḥ paśyaṃstāṃ dārikāṃ cirāt |
atṛpto niścalākṣaśca tasthau saṃmūrchito yathā || 9.55{54} ||
tato jñātīn samāhūya kṛtvā jātimahaṃ mudā |
tannāmakaraṇaṃ tasyāstān punaḥ sa samabravīt || 9.56{55} ||
bhavanto'syāḥ sutāyā me bhadrāṅgyā anurūpataḥ |
nāmadheyaṃ prasiddhena karttavyaṃ kriyatāṃ śubham || 9.57{56} ||
iti tenārthite tasyāḥ sarve te jātayastathā |
sahajaśuklavastrāṅgaṃ dṛṣṭvaivaṃ saṃvabhāṣire || 9.58{57} ||
yadiyaṃ dārikā saumyā bhadrāṃgī divyasuṃdarī |
śuklavastrāvṛtā jātā tacchukleti prasidhyatu || 9.59{58} ||
iti saṃbhāṣaṇāṃ kṛtvā sarve te jñātayastathā |
rohiṇaṃ taṃ samāmaṃtrya tathaivaṃ samudāharat || 9.60{59} ||
yadiyaṃ te sutā bhadra bhadrāṅgī divyasuṃdarī |
śuklavastrāvṛtā jātā tacchukleti pracakṣyatām || 9.61{60} ||
tathetyanumataṃ kṛtvā tena pitrā prasāditā |
śuklā sā duhitāṣṭāsu dhātrīṣu samupārpitā || 9.62{61} ||
tatastāsāṃ ca dhātrīṇāmupacārapracārataḥ |
varddhitābhūtsuramyāṅgī hradasthamiva paṃkajam || 9.63{62} ||
yathā yathā ca sā śuklā parivṛddhābhavatkramāt |
(rm 107)
tathā tathā ca tadvastraṃ vavṛddhe nirmalī'bhavat || 9.64{63} ||
na ca kāyo'pyabhūttasmātmalābhiliptadurbhagaḥ |
vabhau tu nirmalaḥ śuddhaḥ sugaṃdhiparivāhakaḥ || 9.65{64} ||
yadā sā dārikā śuklā parivṛddhā kramādabhūt |
kumārī sarvalokānāṃ manonetrābhihāriṇī || 9.66{65} ||
tataḥ sā śikṣituṃ vidyā guruṇāṃ samupāśritā |
śikṣitvā kramaśo vidyāṃ pāramāśu yayau sudhīḥ || 9.67{66} ||
tadā ye ye ca tāṃ śuklāmapaśyaddivyasuṃdarīm |
te te sarve'pi paśyanto vismitā mohamāyayuḥ || 9.68{67} ||
tataste brāhmaṇādyāśca tadguṇākṛṣṭamānasāḥ |
svasvadūtena tāṃ śuklāṃ rohiṇaṃ prārthayanmuhuḥ || 9.69{68} ||
jātiśreṣṭho hyahaṃ viprastacchuklāṃ me dātumarhasi |
iti tāṃ brāhmaṇaputrā rohiṇaṃ pratyayācayan || 9.70{[69]} ||
rājaputro'smyahaṃ vīrastatsutā me pradīyatām |
iti rājakumārāstaṃ rohiṇaṃ tāṃ samarthayan || 9.71{[70]} ||
ahaṃ vaiśyaḥ prajābhartā tacchuklāṃ me dātumarhasi |
iti vaiśyakumārāstāṃ rohiṇaṃ prārthayari muhuḥ || 9.72{[71]} ||
ahaṃ maṃtrisuto vijñastacchuklā me pradīyatām |
iti maṃtrisutāḥ sarve rohiṇaṃ pratyayācayan || 9.73{71!} ||
mahāmātyasuto'haṃ hi tacchuklāṃ me prayachatām |
ityāmātyakumārāstāṃ rohiṇaṃ pratyayācayan || 9.74{72} ||
evamanye'pi lokāśca rūpadravyābhimāninaḥ |
svasvadūtena tāṃ śuklāṃ rohiṇaṃ pratyayācayan || 9.75{73} ||
evaṃ sarvaiśca tairnityaiḥ prārthyamāno mahājanaiḥ |
rohiṇaḥ sa viṣaṇṇātmā sthitvaivaṃ ca vyacintayat || 9.76{74} ||
hā me duḥkhāni jātāni kathamatra pratikriyā |
sarvathāhaṃ praṇaṣṭaḥ syāṃ sutayāḥ kāraṇe'dhunā || 9.77{75} ||
yadyekasmai pradāsyāmi sutāmetāṃ manoharām |
anye sarve'pi me vairā bhaviṣyaṃti tadā khalu || 9.78{76} ||
avaśyaṃ me sutā hīyaṃ vairiṇī māṃ haniṣyati |
evaṃ duḥkhānudāyinyā putryāpi kiṃ mamaitayā || 9.79{77} ||
iti cintāviṣaṇṇo'sau taccintākulamānasaḥ |
(rm 108)
kapolaṃ svakare sthāpya tasthau koṣṭhe nirutsavaḥ || 9.80{78} ||
evaṃ cintāvibhinnāsyaṃ pitaraṃ taṃ viṣādinam |
sā śuklā duhitā dṛṣṭvā praṇatvaivamabhāṣata || 9.81{79} ||
kimevaṃ tiṣṭhase tāta kiṃ te duḥkhaṃ nu jāyate |
kathyatāṃ tadyadi sneho mayi te varttate pitaḥ || 9.82{80} ||
iti putryā vacaḥ śrutvā pitā sa ruṣitāśayaḥ |
niḥsnehastāṃ sutāṃ śuklāṃ dṛṣṭvaivaṃ paryyabhāṣata || 9.83{81} ||
tvannimitte mahadduḥkhaṃ jāyate me kuputrike |
kiṃ tavāgre kathitvaitaṃ yato naiva pratikriyā || 9.84{82} ||
avaśyaṃ tvaṃ sutā śatruḥ sarvathā māṃ haniṣyasi |
evaṃ duḥkhābhidāyinyā putryāpi kiṃ mama tvayā || 9.85{83} ||
iti pitroditaṃ śrutvā sā śuklā prahatāśayā |
sahasā pitaraṃ natvā rudaṃtyaivamabhāṣata |
tāta kiṃ me nimittena duḥkhaṃ te jāyate katham |
sarvathaitatpravaktavyaṃ yadi te'haṃ sutātmajā |
tadupāyaṃ kariṣyāmi yena duḥkhaṃ nihanyate || 9.86{84} ||
tadetatsarvathā tāta mamāgre vaktumarhasi |
iti putryodite bhūyaḥ pitāsau rohiṇo'vadat || 9.87{85} ||
tatkin te kathyate putri yato nāsti pratikriyā |
bahavaḥ prārthayitārastava rājasutādayaḥ || 9.88{86} ||
kumārā brāhmaṇā vaiśyāḥ śūdrāśca maṃtrinaṃdanāḥ |
amātyanaṃdanāścāpi sārthavāhasutādayaḥ || 9.89{87} ||
evamanye'pi ye cānyā guṇadravyābhimānitāḥ |
paurikā grāmikāścāpi kularūpābhimānikāḥ || 9.90{88} ||
etaiḥ sarvairahaṃ nityaṃ sumudritastvadarthibhiḥ |
kathamatra kariṣyāmi cetanā me na vidyate || 9.91{89} ||
tvamevaikā hi me putrī bahavo yācakāśca te |
ekasmai yadi dāsyāmi sarve'nye syurmamāhitāḥ || 9.92{90} ||
atastvāṃ khaṇḍaśaḥ kṛtvā sarvebhyo'pyarpaye ca vai |
evaṃ tu kriyamāṇe me kuśalatvaṃ bhaviṣyati || 9.93{91} ||
anyathā cedbhavennaiva kuśalaṃ me sadāpi hi |
(rm 109)
iti pitroditaṃ śrutvā śuklā sā mṛtyuśaṃkita || 9.94{92} ||
moditeva pituḥ pādau praṇatvaivamabhāṣata |
prasīda mā kṛthāḥ khedaṃ mamaitatkāraṇe pitaḥ || 9.95{93} ||
tathāhaṃ te kariṣyāmi yathā doṣo na vidyate |
nāhaṃ kāmārthinī tāta tadanujñāṃ pradehi me || 9.96{94} ||
pravrajitvā cariṣyāmi bhagavacchāsane vratam |
ityetadbhāṣitaṃ putryā śrutvāsau rohiṇaḥ pitā || 9.97{95} ||
putrīviyogamāśaṃkya punarevamabhāṣata |
mā kṛthāḥ sarvathā putri pravrajyācaraṇe manaḥ || 9.98{96} ||
tvaṃ strī vālā na śaknuyāḥ pravrajyāṃ carituṃ sute |
pravrajyāyāṃ vrataṃ kaṣṭaṃ puruṣairapi duṣkaram || 9.99{97} ||
tvaṃ tu vālā subhadrāṃgī komalā divyasuṃdarī |
kathaṃ tadduṣkarasthāne vrataṃ kartuṃ saheḥ sute || 9.100{98} ||
tanmā kṛthāstathā vāle pravrajyāyāṃ manastyaja |
yadi kāmārthinī nāsi kasmai ciddāsyate na hi || 9.101{99} ||
tatputri svagṛhe sthitvā vrataṃ dhṛtvā sukhaṃ vasa |
yadi kāmārthinī vāsi putri teṣāṃ tvadarthinām || 9.102{100} ||
svayaṃvaravidhiṃ kṛtvā svayaṃ vara yamichasi |
tadā me dūṣaṇaṃ nāsti tavāpi dūṣaṇaṃ na hi || 9.103{1} ||
sarvatrāpi hi lokeṣu vidhireṣa pravarttate |
tasmātteṣāṃ kumārāṇāṃ patiṃ bhadraṃ svayaṃ vara || 9.104{2} ||
tenaiva patinā sārddhaṃ vrataṃ kṛtvā sukhaṃ cara |
tadā santatimutpādya bhuktvā bhogyaṃ yathā sukham || 9.105{3} ||
saṃvṛtticaraṇaṃ kṛtvā tathānte svargamāpnuyāḥ |
tenaivaṃ mā kṛthāḥ putri pravrajyācaraṇe manaḥ || 9.106{4} ||
kiṃ te pravrajyayā siddhyenmahadduṣkaracaryyayā |
ityukte janakenāsau śuklā kāmaparāṅmukhā || 9.107{5} ||
saddharmacaraṇaprepsuḥ pitaramevamabravīt |
nāhaṃ kāmārthinī tāta saṃbodhipadavāṃchinī || 9.108{6} ||
tasmādbhogyānyapi tyaktvā caritumichāmi sadvratam |
sabhayāḥ saraṇāḥ kāmāḥ sakleśāśca viṣopamāḥ || 9.109{7} ||
tasmātkāme na me vāṃchā nirvāṇe eva me manaḥ |
(rm 110)
sadevāsuramānuṣyā ye satvāḥ kāmarāgiṇaḥ || 9.110{8} ||
sarve te kleśitāścāndhāścaranti pātakeṣvapi |
kṛtvā te pātakānyevaṃ gachanti durgatiṣvapi || 9.111{9} ||
vividhāni ca duḥkhāni bhuṃjate narakāsthitāḥ |
brāhmaṇā api dhīrāśca vaidikā brahmacāriṇaḥ || 9.112{10} ||
te'pi kāmānurāgena bhraṣṭā gacchaṃti durgatim |
tapasvino mahābhijñāḥ śuddhātmānā jitendriyāḥ |
te'pi kāmānurāgena bhraṣṭā gacchaṃti durgatim |
yatayo yoginaścāpi nirgraṃthāśca digaṃvarāḥ || 9.113{11} ||
te'pi kāmānurāgena bhraṣṭā gacchanti durgatim |
rājānaḥ kṣatriyāścāpi kṛtvā yuddhaṃ parasparam || 9.114{12} ||
āhave nidhanaṃ yānti tadapi kāmakāraṇe |
amātyā maṃtriṇaścāpi mārayanti parārdhinaḥ || 9.115{13} ||
tatpāpairdurgatiṃ yānti tadapi kāmahetunā |
yodhāraśca mahāvīrā yuddhaṃ kṛtvā parasparam || 9.116{14} ||
saṃgrāme nidhanaṃ yānti tadapi kāmakāraṇe |
vaṇijaḥ sārthavāhāśca ratnadravyātilālasāḥ || 9.117{15} ||
samudre patitāḥ kecinmṛtāḥ kecicca jaṃgale |
kecinmṛtā mahāraṇye cauraiḥ kecidupadrutāḥ || 9.118{16} ||
kṣutpipāsāhatāḥ kecinmṛtā mārge rujānvitāḥ |
śītavātātapākrāntā mṛtā kecicchramāturāḥ || 9.119{17} ||
kecidgṛhagatā eva mṛtāḥ kecicca roginaḥ |
kecidbhāraṃ vahantaśca mṛtāḥ kecidbhayānvitāḥ || 9.120{18} ||
jantubhiḥ khāditāḥ kecittatsarvaṃ kāmahetunā |
kṛṣiṇaśca dinaṃ yāvatkṣatrakarmasamudyatāḥ || 9.121{19} ||
dinānte gṛhamagatya śerate te mṛtā iva |
evamanye'pi lokāśca śilpipreṣyajanādayaḥ || 9.122{20} ||
dāsadāsījanāścāpi svasvakarmasamudyatāḥ |
evaṃ nityamaviśrāntaṃ kuṭuṃvadhanasādhane || 9.123{21} ||
vahaṃti kheditāḥ kleśaṃ tatsarvaṃ kāmahetunā |
evaṃ caurādiduṣṭāśca paradravyāpahāriṇaḥ || 9.124{22} ||
pātakānyapi kurvanti tadapi kāmakāraṇe |
kecicca paradravyāṇi haranti hārayanti ca || 9.125{23} ||
keciddhatvāpi sarvaṃ haranti krūramānasāḥ |
dhanārthe kiṃ na kurvanti duṣṭā lubdhāśca nirdayāḥ || 9.126{24} ||
(rm 111)
praghnanti pitarau kecitte'pi ghnanti gurūnapi |
kecidvighnanti mitrāṇi jñātivandhujanānapi || 9.127{[125]} ||
patnīputraduhitṝśca bhrātrīṃśca bhāginīrapi |
kecidghnanti sahāyāṃśca dāsadāsījanānyapi || 9.128{26} ||
bālān vṛddhāṃśca nārīṃśca tatsarvaṃ kāmakāraṇe |
kecicca prāṇinaḥ pakṣimṛgamatsyādijantukān || 9.129{27} ||
hatvā khādanti pāpiṣṭhāstadapi kāmakāraṇe |
evaṃ tiryyaggaṇāścāpi pakṣisiṃhādijantavaḥ || 9.130{28} ||
parasparaṃ nihatyaivaṃ khādanti kāmahetunā |
evaṃ śathāvaṃcayantastrāsayanto'pi vālakān || 9.131{29} ||
adattaṃ pratigṛhnanti tadapi kāmakāraṇāt |
mithyākāmaratāḥ kecitparadārāṃśca kāminīḥ || 9.132{30} ||
haranti chadmanā cāpi tadapi kāmakāraṇe |
yacca mṛṣāvadaṃto'pi parakāryyābhighāṭakāḥ || 9.133{31} ||
sādhayanti svakāryāṇi tadapi kāmahetunā |
yacca piśunavādena suhṛnmitrānucāriṇām |
virodhaṃ kārayantyevaṃ tadapi kāmahetunā || 9.134{[132]} ||
yacca pāruṣyavādena gurusādhujanānapi |
paribhāṣya vinindanti tadapi kāmakāraṇe || 9.135{[133]} ||
yatsaṃbhinnapralāpena suhṛnmitrānuyāyinām |
cittāni bhedayantyevaṃ tadapi kāmakāraṇe || 9.136{[134]} ||
yacca māyānurāgeṇa sādhūnāṃ dharmacāriṇām |
prabhedayanti cittāni tadapi kāmahetunā || 9.137{[135]} ||
yadīrṣyāmatsarālīḍhā dṛṣṭvā paraguṇarddhitām |
vyāpādaṃ cintayantyevaṃ tadapi kāmakāraṇe || 9.138{[136]} ||
yanmithyādṛṣṭikā loke sarvadharmaprahāsikāḥ |
bhogyāni bhuṃjate hṛtvā tadapi kāmakāraṇe || 9.139{[137]} ||
kecijjātiparibhraṣṭā nīcajātiprasaṃgatāḥ |
svakuladharmabhraṣṭāśca tadapi kāmakāraṇe || 9.140{[138]} ||
evaṃ sarve'pi satvāśca sadgatiparivartinaḥ |
kāmarthe kleśitātmāno bhramanti durgatau sadā || 9.141{[139]} ||
(rm 112)
evaṃ yāvanti duḥkhāni saṃsāre ṣaḍgatiṣvapi |
tāni sarvāṇi satvānāṃ jāyante kāmahetunā || 9.142{[130]} ||
evaṃ matvā tu ye vijñāḥ kāmaṃ tyaktvā virāgiṇaḥ |
pravrajitvā suniḥkleśā brahmacaryyaṃ caranti te || 9.143{[131]} ||
tato me sabhaye kāme saraṇe ca viṣopame |
vidvadbhirgarhite tāta manovāṃchā na varttate || 9.144{32} ||
tatpravrajyāṃ samāśritya bhagavaccharaṇaṃ gatā |
brahmacaryyaṃ caritvāhaṃ prāptumichāmi nirvṛtim || 9.145{33} ||
buddho hi jagatāṃ śāstā dharmarājo vināyakaḥ |
tattasya śaraṇe gatvā caritumichāmi saccarīm || 9.146{34} ||
yadyasti te mayi snehaḥ saddharme cānurāgatā |
tadanumodanāṃ kṛtvānujñāṃ me dātumarhasi || 9.147{35} ||
yadi tu saugate dharme'pyanujñāṃ me dadāsi na |
tīrthayātrāṃ bhramiṣyāmi sarvadāhaṃ virāginī || 9.148{36} ||
iti putrīvacaḥ śrutya śākyo'sau rohiṇaḥ pitā |
sutādharmaviyogatvaṃ vijñāyaivamacintayat || 9.149{37} ||
yadiyaṃ me sutā bhadrā nirāśravā virāginī |
avaśyaṃ svagṛhaṃ tyaktvā pravrajetsarvathā khalu || 9.150{38} ||
avaśyaṃ me'nayā putryā viyogaṃ sarvathā bhavet |
sarvathā hyanivāryyeyaṃ vrajatu saugatāśrame || 9.151{39} ||
yadāyaṃ saugate dharme pravrajitvā nirāśravā |
brahmacaryyaṃ caratyevaṃ tato nirvṛtimāpnuyāt || 9.152{40} ||
yadiyaṃ me sutā kāntā subhadrā divyasuṃdarī |
yadi gehaṃ parityaktvā tīrthayātrāṃ kariṣyati || 9.153{41} ||
tadā nūnamiyaṃ bhrāṃtā tīrthikadharmasaṃratā |
kuladharmaparibhraṣṭā vinaṣṭā syānna saṃśayaḥ || 9.154{42} ||
tato'smākaṃ yaśodharmavinaṣṭo'pi bhavetkhalu |
tasyāśca dūragāminyā darśanaṃ syātsudullabham || 9.155{43} ||
(rm 113)
tasmādasyā na karttavyā vāraṇā dharmasādhane |
caratu saugatīṃ caryyāmiyaṃ hi nirvṛtaye || 9.156{44} ||
tathā cetsaphalaṃ janma mānuṣye syānmamāpi ca |
teneyaṃ jagatāṃ bhartuḥ śāsane caratu vratam || 9.157{45} ||
ityevaṃ manasā dhyātvā śākyo'sau rohiṇaḥ pitā |
śuklāṃ tāṃ svātmajāṃ bhadrāṃ saṃnirīkṣyaivamabravīt || 9.158{46} ||
satyamevaṃ sute bhadre yathoddiṣṭaṃ tvayā khalu |
kiṃ tu tvaṃ dārikā nārī pravrajyāṃ tatkathaṃ careḥ || 9.159{47} ||
pravrajyā duṣkarasthānaṃ nārīṇāṃ tu viśeṣataḥ |
tatpravrajyāvrate sthitvā mā paścātparitapyathāḥ || 9.160{48} ||
yadi tvaṃ niścayenaiva pravrajyāvratamichasi |
taccarasva munīndrasya śāsane vratamādarāt || 9.161{49} ||
yathā ca guruṇādiṣṭaṃ śrutvā tathā samāhitaḥ |
samyakchikṣāḥ samādhāya cara nityamatandritā || 9.162{50} ||
saṃbuddhaśāsanaṃ dhṛtvā śraddhābhaktisamanvitā |
triratnaśaraṇaṃ gatvā bodhivrataṃ samācara || 9.163{51} ||
iti pitrā samādiṣṭe śuklā sā pratimoditā |
natvā pādau pituścaivaṃ jananyāścaraṇe'namat || 9.164{52} ||
tataḥ sā jananī dṛṣṭvā tāṃ śuklāṃ svātmajāṃ priyām |
pariṣvajya rudantyevaṃ pravrajyāyāṃ nyavārayat || 9.165{53} ||
hā putri kathamekānte māṃ tyaktvā kva vrajiṣyase |
hā jīva kiṃ nu paśyantī pravrajituṃ tvamīhase || 9.166{54} ||
kiṃ cidduḥkhaṃ na te dattaṃ mayāmvayā kadā cana |
kenaivaṃ hetunā putrī pravrajituṃ tvamichasi || 9.167{55} ||
tvaṃ tu nārī subhādrāṅgī kumārī divyasuṃdarī |
kiṃcidduḥkhānabhijñā ca tatpravrajyāṃ kathaṃ care || 9.168{56} ||
yāvajjīvāmyahaṃ caiva tāvatmā gāḥ kuhāpi hi |
yāvaccāsti gṛhe saṃpattāvadbhuktvā sukhaṃ vasa || 9.169{57} ||
yadāhaṃ maraṇaṃ yātā yadā cāpi gṛhe vipat |
jñātimitrajanaistyaktvā tadā vrajasva vāṃchayā || 9.170{58} ||
iti mātrā niṣiddhe'pi śuklā sā vighnaśaṃkitās|
mātuḥ pādau praṇatvaiva sahasā niryayau gṛhāt || 9.171{59} ||
(rm 114)
tataḥ sā satvarā gatvā nyagrodhārama āśrame |
dadarśa bhikṣuṇībhikṣūn śantaśīlāñjiteṃdriyān || 9.172{60} ||
tān sarvān kramato natvā vihāre samupāviśat |
tatrasthaṃ sugataṃ nāthamapaśyacchrighanaṃ jinam || 9.173{61} ||
dvātriṃśallakṣaṇāśītivyaṃjanaiḥ parimaṃḍitam |
sahasreṇādhikābhāsaṃ vyāmābhaparirocitam || 9.174{62} ||
śāntātmānaṃ subhadrāṃśaṃ samantabhadrarūpiṇam |
ratnarāśimivojjvālaṃ sumerumiva jaṃgamam || 9.175{63} ||
dṛṣṭvaiva niścalā tasthau harṣotphullatanūruhā |
dṛṣṭvaivaṃ sucirāttasya śrīghanasyāntike yayau || 9.176{64} ||
tathaivaṃ samupāyātāṃ dṛṣṭvā tāṃ bhagavānmuniḥ |
svāgataṃ dārike bhadre ehītyuktvā samagrahīt || 9.177{65} ||
tato'sau dārikā śuklā svāgatāsmi munīśvara |
ityuktvā prāṃjaliṃ kṛtvā nanāma caraṇau muneḥ || 9.178{66} ||
tato bhagavatā tena preṣitā māturantike |
sā śuklā sahasā gatvā gautamyāccaraṇe'namat || 9.179{67} ||
tathā tāṃ samupāyātāṃ śuklāṃ sadvratavāṃchinīm |
dṛṣṭvā sā gautamī spṛṣṭvā hastenaiva samagrahīt || 9.180{68} ||
tataḥ pravrājayitvā sā gautamyā bhikṣuṇī kṛtā |
khikkhirīpātrahastāṅkā saṃgatābhūjjitendriyā || 9.181{69} ||
yenaiva śuklavastreṇa prāvṛtā samajāyata |
tataḥ paṃcasuraktaṃ jātāni cīvarāṇyapi || 9.182{70} ||
taireva civarai raktaiḥ prāvṛtā pariśobhitā |
sā śuklā suprasannāsyā muṇḍitāpyabhyarocata || 9.183{71} ||
tato buddhānubhāvena supraśāntasubhāvinī |
jitvā kleśārivargāṃśca śikṣāpāraṃ samāyayau || 9.184{72} ||
matvānityaṃ ca saṃsāraṃ bhavagatiṃ vighātinīm |
nihatya sarvasaṃskāragatīsatanadharmiṇī || 9.185{73} ||
māracaryyāvinirmuktā sākṣādarhatvamāyayau |
avidyāṅgaṃ vinirbhidya prāptavidyā śubhāśayā || 9.186{74} ||
abhijñā guṇasaṃpannā pratisaṃvidvatī sudhīḥ |
suvītarāgiṇī dhīrā loṣṭhahemasamānikā || 9.187{75} ||
(rm 115)
ākāśasamacittā ca pāṇītalasamāśayā |
niraṃjanāvikalpā ca śrīkhaṇḍatulyavāsinī || 9.188{76} ||
svātmasatkāralābheṣu parāṇmukhī sunispṛhā |
sarvasatvahitādhāni saṃbuddhadharmacāriṇī || 9.189{77} ||
sadevāsuralokānāṃ pūjyā mānyā samantataḥ |
vaṃdanīyā cābhitoṣyā ca vabhūva brahmacāriṇī || 9.190{78} ||
atha te bhikṣavaḥ sarve dṛṣṭvā tāṃ brahmacāriṇīm |
vismitāḥ sugataṃ natvā paprachuriti sādaram || 9.191{79} ||
bhagavan kimiyaṃ śuklā purā karmākarocchubham |
yena vastrāvṛtā jātā bhavatyevaṃ ca bhikṣuṇī || 9.192{80} ||
tatsarvaṃ naḥ samādeṣṭumarhasi sarvavidguro |
yadanayā kṛtaṃ karma bhikṣuṇyā śuklayā purā || 9.193{81} ||
iti tairbhikṣubhiḥ pṛṣṭe bhagavān sa munīśvaraḥ |
tān bhikṣusāṃghikān sarvān saṃnirīkṣyaivamabravīt || 9.194{82} ||
śṛṇudhvaṃ bhikṣavaḥ sarve yadanayā kṛtaṃ purā |
tatsarvaṃ kathayiṣyāmi sarvalokābhibodhane || 9.195{83} ||
purāsminneva bhadrākhye kalpe'bhūtkāśyapo jinaḥ |
saṃbuddhaḥ sugato nātho dharmarājastathāgataḥ || 9.196{84} ||
sarvavidyādhipaḥ śāstā traidhātukavināyakaḥ |
sarvajño bhagavānarhan brahmacārī hitaṃkaraḥ || 9.197{85} ||
sa munīndro jagannātho vārāṇasīmupāśrayan |
mṛgadāve vane ramye vyaharacchrāvakaiḥ saha || 9.198{86} ||
bhikṣubhirbodhisatvaiśca bhikṣuṇībhirupāsakaiḥ |
sārddhamupāsikābhiśca yatibhiśca maharṣibhiḥ || 9.199{87} ||
tatrastho bhagavān buddho sarvasatvahitāya saḥ |
ādimadhyāntakalyāṇaṃ dideśa dharmamuttamam || 9.200{88} ||
tadaikānyatarā kācicchreṣṭhibhāryā śubhāśayā |
sukalyāṇārthinī śrāddhā saddharmamānasī sudhīḥ || 9.201{89} ||
kena citkaraṇiyena mṛgadāvamupācarat |
tatra bhikṣūn kramāddṛṣṭvā saṃbuddhāntikamupāyayau || 9.202{90} ||
tatra sā samupāgatya dadarśa taṃ munīśvaram |
dvātriṃśallakṣaṇāśītivyaṃjanairupalakṣitam || 9.203{91} ||
vyāmābhālaṃkṛtaṃ saumyaṃ śatasūryyādhikaprabham |
supraśāntendriyāṃ kāmyaṃ samaṃtabhadrarūpakam || 9.204{92} ||
(rm 116)
ratnarāśimivojjvālaṃ sumerumiva jaṃgamam |
tathā dṛṣṭvaiva prāmodātsāṃjaliṃ samupāgatā || 9.205{93} ||
natvā pādau munestasthāvekāntike kṛtāṃjaliḥ |
tataḥ sa bhagavān dṛṣṭvā tasyāścittaṃ viśodhitam || 9.206{94} ||
ādimadhyāntakalyāṇaṃ dideśa dharmamuttamam |
tatsaddharmāmṛtaṃ pītvā sā śūdrī pariharṣitā || 9.207{95} ||
sasaṃghaṃ sugataṃ nāthaṃ bhojayituṃ samaichata |
tataḥ sā svagṛhe gatvā sādhayitvā subhojanam || 9.208{96} ||
saṃbuddhapramukhaṃ saṃghaṃ svāṃtargehe nyamaṃtrayat |
tatra svasvāsanāsīnaṃ saṃbuddhaṃ taṃ sasāṃghikam || 9.209{97} ||
pūjayitvā tadarhaiśca bhojanaiḥ samatoṣayat |
tataḥ sā śraddhayā tebhyaḥ sarvebhyo'pi prasannitā || 9.210{98} ||
buddhapramukhasaṃghebhyaḥ pratyekaṃ cīvaraṃ dadau |
tato nityaṃ ca sā bhaktyā cakre saṃbuddhasevanām || 9.211{99} ||
tatpuṇyaiścāpi saddharme pravrajituṃ samaichata |
tato'sau pitarau natvābhyanujñāpya prasādinī || 9.212{100} ||
bhagavacchāsane gatvā pravrajyāvratamācarat |
tasmātkiṃ bhikṣavo yūyaṃ manyadhve yābhavattadā || 9.213{1} ||
śreṣṭhibhāryā na cānyā sā śuklaivaiṣeti manyata |
yadanayā prasādinyā buddhapramukhasāṃghike || 9.214{2} ||
pratyekaṃ cīvaraṃ dattaṃ pravrajyā cāpi sādhitā |
etatpuṇyavipākena prāvṛtya śuklavāsasā || 9.215{3} ||
jātā pravrajitā caiṣā bhavati brahmacāriṇī |
ityevaṃ karmajaṃ bhogyaṃ prāṇino bhuṃjate bhave || 9.216{4} ||
yenaiva yatkṛtaṃ karma tenaiva bhujyate phalam |
na naśyaṃti hi karmāṇi kalpakoṭiśatairapi || 9.217{5} ||
sāmagrīṃ prāpya kālaṃ ca phalaṃti khalu dehinām |
abhuktaṃ kṣīyate naiva karma kvāpi kathaṃ cana || 9.218{6} ||
nāgnibhirdahyate karma vāyubhiśca na śuṣyate |
jalaiśca klidyate nāpi kṣīyate nāpi bhūmiṣu || 9.219{7} ||
anyathā ca bhavennaiva karmagatiḥ kathaṃ cana |
śuklānāṃ śubhatā nityaṃ kṛṣṇānāṃ duḥkhatā sadā || 9.220{8} ||
miśritā miśritānāṃ ca jaṃtubhirbhujyate gatiḥ |
tasmādapāsya kṛṣṇāni karmāṇi miśritāni ca || 9.221{9} ||
caritavyaṃ śubheṣveva karmasu sukhavāṃchibhiḥ |
(rm 117)
tatheti bhikṣavaḥ sarve te ca lokaḥ sabhāsthitāḥ || 9.222{10} ||
śrutvā tatsugatādiṣṭamabhyanandan prasāditāḥ |
evametanmahārāja guruṇā me prabhāṣitam || 9.223{11} ||
evaṃ rājaṃstvayāpyevaṃ karttavyaṃ puṇyamādarāt |
puṇyameva bhave vandhubhrātā mitraṃ suhṛtsakhā || 9.224{12} ||
nānyaḥ puṇyātparaḥ kaścidvidyate bhavacāriṇām |
tasmātpuṇyaṃ svayaṃ kṛtvā lokeṣvapi pracāraya || 9.225{13} ||
prajāśca bodhayitvaivaṃ puṇyamārge pracāraya |
ityevaṃ guruṇādiṣṭamupaguptena bhikṣuṇā || 9.226{14} ||
śrutvā tatheti rājāsau prābhyanandajjanaiḥ saha || 9.227{15} ||
ya idaṃ śuklāvadānaṃ jinavarakathitaṃ puṇyahetornarāṇāṃ
śṛṇvanti śravayanti pramuditamanasaḥ sadguṇaprāptukāmāḥ |
te sarve kleśamuktāḥ suvimalamanaso bodhicaryā carantaḥ
satsaukhyānyeva bhuktvā munivaranilaye saṃprayātā vasanti || 9.228{16} ||

++ iti ratnamālāyāṃ śuklāvadānaṃ nāma samāptam ++


(rm 118)
x hiraṇyapāṇyavadāna
athāśoko mahīpāla upaguptaṃ jināṃśajam |
kṛtāṃjalipuṭo natvā punarevamabhāṣata || 10.1{1} ||
bhadanta śrotumichāmi punaranyatsubhāṣitam |
yathā te guruṇā khyātaṃ tathā vaktuṃ samarhasi || 10.2{2} ||
evaṃ tena nṛpendreṇa prārthite sa jinātmajaḥ |
upagupto mahābhijñastaṃ nṛpaṃ caivamabravīt || 10.3{3} ||
śṛṇu rājaṃstathā vakṣye yathā me guruṇoditam |
śrutvā caivaṃ sadā bodhicaryāṃ cara samāhitaḥ || 10.4{4} ||
puraikasamaye'sau ca śākyamunistathāgataḥ |
sarvajñaḥ sugato nāthaḥ śāstā traidhātukādhipaḥ || 10.5{5} ||
sarvavidyādhipastāyī ṣaḍabhijño munīśvaraḥ |
jino'rhaṃ bhagavān buddho dharmarājo vināyakaḥ || 10.6{6} ||
śrāvastyāṃ vahirudyāne jetavane jināśrame |
anāthapiṇḍadārāme vihāre maṇimaṇḍite || 10.7{7} ||
bhikṣubhiḥ śrāvakaiḥ sārddhaṃ bhikṣuṇībhirupāsakaiḥ |
triratnopāsikābhiśca cailakaiśca śubhāśayaiḥ || 10.8{8} ||
bodhisatvagaṇaiścāpi sahānyaiśca maharṣibhiḥ |
osaddharmadeśanāṃ datvā tasthau lokahitārthataḥ || 10.9{9} ||
tatsaddharmāmṛtaṃ pātuṃ prājagmuḥ sadguṇārthinaḥ |
devā daityāśca nāgāśca yakṣagaṃdharvakinnarāḥ || 10.10{10} ||
siddhavidyādharādyāśca lokapālagaṇādayaḥ |
brāhmaṇāḥ kṣatriyāścāpi vaiśyāḥ śūdrāśca maṃtriṇaḥ || 10.11{11} ||
amātyāḥ śreṣṭhinaścāpi dhaninaśca mahājanāḥ |
vaṇijaḥ sārthavāhāśca tadanye'pi ca sajjanāḥ || 10.12{12} ||
paurā jānapadāścāpi sarve te samupāgatāḥ |
taṃ buddhaṃ sugataṃ natvā pūjayitvā samaṃtataḥ || 10.13{13} ||
parivṛtya paraskṛtya tasthuḥ sāṃjalayo mudā |
athāsau bhagavān dṛṣṭvā tān sarvān samupāsthitān |
ādimadhyāntakalyāṇaṃ dideśa dharmamuttamam || 10.14{14} ||
tatsaddharmāmṛtaṃ pītvā sarve satvāḥ surādayaḥ |
harṣitā anumodanto vabhūvurbodhicāriṇaḥ || 10.15{15} ||
(rm 119)
evaṃ nityaṃ ca te satvāḥ pītvā dharmāmṛtaṃ muneḥ |
triratnaśaraṇaṃ gatvā bhejire samupāśritāḥ || 10.16{16} ||
tadaiva samaye tasyāṃ śrāvastyāṃ puri saṃsthitaḥ |
āsīdgṛhapatiḥ prāḍhyo mahābhogyasamṛddhimān || 10.17{17} ||
kuvelavanmahāśrīmān suvistīrṇaparigrahaḥ || 10.18{18} ||
śrāddho dātā mahābhogī sarvalokahitārthabhṛt |
evaṃ maharddhikasyāpi tasya gṛhapateściram |
na putro na ca putrī vā samudapādi daivataḥ || 10.19{19} ||
tato'putro gṛhastho'sau vaṃśavichinnakheṭitaḥ |
kapolaṃ svakare sthāpya viṣādyaivaṃ vyacintayat || 10.20{20} ||
anekadhanaratnādyāḥ saṃpado me maharddhikāḥ |
viśālaparivārāśca vidyante suparigrahāḥ || 10.21{21} ||
putro me duhitā vāpi vidyate na kathaṃ cana |
saṃpado'pi nirarthāḥ syuryato bhoktā na vidyate || 10.22{22} ||
hā hā sarvaṃ vinaṣṭaṃ syādyato me santatirna hi |
kiṃ me'nayā samṛddhyāpi yatra vaṃśo na vidyate || 10.23{23} ||
vyarthameva mayā dravyaṃ kaṣṭena samupārjitam |
kiṃ kariṣyāmi taddravyaṃ sarvathā maraṇe sati || 10.24{24} ||
ke hi grastā na saṃsāre mṛtyusarppeṇa jaṃtavaḥ |
mayi mṛtyuprayāte'tra saṃskāraṃ kaḥ kariṣyati || 10.25{25} ||
durgatiśodhanaṃ kaśca piṇḍamekaṃ pradāsyati |
nūnaṃ madatyayādrājā sarvasvamāhariṣyati || 10.26{26} ||
tadā nāmāpi me loke na kaścana grahīṣyati |
yadarthameva saṃpattīḥ sādhayāmi prayatnataḥ || 10.27{27} ||
tadeva yadi nāstyevaṃ tatkiṃ me syātsujīvite || 10.28{28!} ||
varamevādya mṛtyurna mithyācirajīvanam |
yasmācciramapi sthitvā mṛtyuduḥkhaṃ tadaiva hi || 10.29{29} ||
tanme bhogye'pi vāṃchā na bhuktvāpi kiṃ sukhaṃ vinā |
aputrasya sukhaṃ naiva paratreha niranvayāt || 10.30{30} ||
tanme janmāpi vyarthaṃ syāddhyaputro'smi niranvayāt |
hā hato'haṃ svadaivena kiṃ mayātra kariṣyate || 10.31{31} ||
kasyāgre saṃvadiṣyāmi ko me buddhiṃ pradāsyati |
(rm 120)
kasyāhaṃ śaraṇaṃ yāsye ko māṃ samuddhariṣyati || 10.32{32} ||
iti cintāviṣaṇno'sau tasthau koṣṭhe yathāturaḥ |
nirutsāhī gṛhānnaiva nirapāyātkadācana || 10.33{33} ||
etaccintāviṣaṇṇābhaṃ taṃ gṛhasthaṃ suhṛjjanāḥ |
dṛṣṭvaiva sahasopetya bodhayituṃ samabruvan || 10.34{34} ||
kiṃ te duḥkhaṃ samutpannaṃ kenaivaṃ tiṣṭhase sakhe |
vaktavyaṃ cettadā āśu sarvathā vaktumarhasi || 10.35{31} ||
iti pṛṣṭe suhṛdbhistairgṛhasthaḥ sa viniśvasan |
tān sarvān suhṛdo dṛṣṭvā tadduḥkhaṃ samudāharat || 10.36{36} ||
suhṛdo me bhavanto hi tanmayātra pracakṣyate |
saṃpattirmahatī me'sti putro naiva sutāpi naḥ || 10.37{37} ||
tena me saṃpadaḥ sarvā vinakṣyanti nirarthitāḥ |
etaccintāviṣārttāhaṃ tiṣṭhāmi putravāṃchitaḥ || 10.38{38} ||
aputrasya sukhaṃ nāsti paratreha samaṃtataḥ |
tanme janmāpi vyarthaṃ syādaputrasya niranvayāt || 10.39{39} ||
tathā hato'haṃ svadaivena kiṃ mayātra cariṣyate |
iti tenodite sarve jñātivaṃdhusuhṛjjanāḥ || 10.40{40} ||
tasya grīhapateścaivāṃ tadupāyaṃ samādiśat |
kiṃ bhadrātra viṣādena yatra devādhikāratā || 10.41{41} ||
tathāpi kriyatāṃ yatnaṃ klaibyaṃ tyaktvā tvayādhunā |
yadaśakyaṃ valairdravyaistadupāyena śakyate || 10.42{42} ||
tasmātklaibyamiha tyaktvā kuru yatnaṃ samāhitaḥ |
devatārādhanāṃ kṛtvā sarvakāryyaṃ hi sādhayet || 10.43{43} ||
tadādau devatāḥ pūjya yācasva putramātmanā |
tato devaprasādena tavadharmānubhāvataḥ |
putro vā duhitā vāpi samupapatsyate dhruvam || 10.44{44} ||
tataste saṃtatiścaivaṃ varddhiṣyati kramāttathā |
tadāpi bhṛśato muktaḥ sadgatiṃ samavāpnuyāt || 10.45{45} ||
iti taiḥ samupādiṣṭaṃ śrutvāsāvanumoditāḥ |
devatārādhanaṃ kartuṃ prayatātmā samārabhat || 10.46{46} ||
pūjāṃgaiḥ pūjayitvaivaṃ brahmāṇaṃ harimīśvaram |
iṃdrādilokapālānāṃ saṃtatiṃ samayācata || 10.47{47} ||
(rm 121)
evaṃ putrābhilāsī sa sadā ca santatīchayā |
devatārādhanaṃ kṛtvā tasthau nityaṃ samutsukaḥ || 10.48{48} ||
nityamevaṃ kṛtasyāpi tasya daivapraṇāmataḥ |
na putro duhitā vāpi nodapādi cirādapi || 10.49{49} ||
tathā cāpi gṛhastho'sau devatāsu nirāśayā |
santatyucchinnacintārttastasthau bhūyo viṣāditaḥ || 10.50{50} ||
tataḥ kaścitsuhṛtsādhuḥ saṃbuddhopāsakaḥ sudhīḥ |
taṃ gṛhasthamupāgatya tathaivaṃ paryyabodhayat || 10.51{51} ||
sādho kin te viṣādena taddhīraṃ samupāśraya |
yadi te santatau vāṃchā tanme śrutvā tataścara || 10.52{52} ||
kimevaṃ devatāḥ pūjya yācase putramādarāt |
yatra daivādhikāratvaṃ tatra kiṃ devatā valam || 10.53{53} ||
vaṃśārthe yadi te bhaktirastyevaṃ devatāsu tat |
saṃbuddhaśaraṇaṃ gatvā saddharmaṃ samupāśraya || 10.54{54} ||
tato dharmānubhāvena buddhasyāpi prasādataḥ |
nūnaṃ te satsutaḥ śīghramutpatsyati na saṃśayaḥ || 10.55{55} ||
yadi notpatsyate putrastava devavirodhataḥ |
puṇyamutpatsyate nūnaṃ tena sadgatimāpnuyāt || 10.56{56} ||
ye buddhaśaraṇaṃ yānti te labhanti śubhāśayam |
ye viśuddhāśayā loke saddharmaṃ te śṛṇvanti hi || 10.57{57} ||
ye saddharmānuśṛṇvan to bodhicittaṃ labhanti te |
pralabdhabodhicittā ye te caranti guṇārthinaḥ || 10.58{58} ||
guṇakāmāśca ye loke te bhajanti jinātmajān |
bodhisatvānubhāvena bodhicaryāṃ samāpnuyuḥ || 10.59{59} ||
bodhicaryāratā ye ca na te gachaṃti durgatim |
sarvakleśagaṇāñjitvā saukhyāvatīmavāpnuyuḥ || 10.60{60} ||
evaṃ matvā jinaṃ smṛtvā saddharmaṃ samupāśrayeḥ |
tataste saṃbhavedbhadramihāmutrāpi sarvataḥ || 10.61{61} ||
iti tenoditaṃ śrutvā gṛhastho'sau'numoditaḥ |
tatheti pratisaṃśrutya saṃbuddhaṃ smartumaichata || 10.62{62} ||
tato gṛhapatirnityaṃ sadāraḥ śraddhayānvitaḥ |
saṃbuddhaṃ sugataṃ smṛtvā vaṃśamevamayācata || 10.63{63} ||
namaste bhagavannātha bhavatāṃ śaraṇaṃ vraje |
(rm 122)
tanmāṃ karuṇāya dṛṣṭyā draṣṭumarhasi sarvathā || 10.64{64} ||
bhavāneva hi jagannāthaḥ sarvasatvahitārthadaḥ |
tvayā mahyaṃ hitārthāya satputraṃ dātumarhasi || 10.65{65} ||
ityevaṃ sa gṛhī sādhuḥ putrābhilaṣitaḥ sadā |
sabhāryyaḥ sugataṃ smṛtvā tathā nityaṃ samutsukaḥ || 10.66{66} ||
tadaiva samaye kaściddevaputro divaścyutaḥ |
sa saṃbuddhānubhāvena tadbhāryāgarbhamāviśat || 10.67{67} ||
tataḥ svāpannasatvābhūtsā bhāryā gṛhiṇaḥ satī |
dohadalakṣaṇopetā kṛśāṅgī bhāvinī kramāt || 10.68{68} ||
pravṛddhagarbhā sā dṛṣṭvā svayaṃ ca garbhamādarāt |
dakṣapārśvagataṃ satvaṃ matvā caivaṃ vyaciṃtayat || 10.69{69} ||
nūnaṃ me jāyate garbhe satvo buddhānubhāvataḥ |
yadayaṃ dakṣiṇe vartti tena putro bhavetkhalu || 10.70{70} ||
iti niścitya sā nārī putrotpatyāśayā mudā |
svasvāmino pure sthitvā rahasyaivaṃ nyavedayat || 10.71{71} ||
dṛṣṭyāryyaputra varddhasva yadahaṃ garbhiṇī bhave |
nūnamayaṃ bhavetputro yatsthito dakṣiṇodare || 10.72{72} ||
iti bhāryoditaṃ śrutvā sa gṛhasthaḥ pramoditaḥ |
daksiṇaṃ vāhumunnamya nandannevamudānayat || 10.73{73} ||
cirābhilaṣitaṃ putramukhaṃ paśyeyaṃ sāṃpratam |
jātaḥ syānnāmajātā me kulavaṃśasthitirbhavet || 10.74{74} ||
kṛtyāni ca prakurvīta bibhṛyādbharaṇaṃ prati |
nūnaṃ buddhānubhāvena satputro'pi bhaviṣyati || 10.75{75} ||
yadasmākaṃ kṛtaṃ pūrvaṃ karma dānādikaṃ śubham |
etatpuṇyānubhāvena bhavetkuśalametayoḥ || 10.76{76} ||
tatastena sadā bharttā saha saṃmodacāriṇī |
pathyāhāropacāraiśca varddhito sukhacāriṇī || 10.77{77} ||
tataḥ sā samaye sūta putraṃ divyātisuṃdaram |
darśanīyaṃ susaumyāṅgaṃ prāsādikaṃ manoharam || 10.78{78} ||
sarvāṅgalakṣaṇopetaṃ sadīnārakaradvayam |
sarvasatvamanāpīśamabhirūpaṃ subhadrikam || 10.79{79} ||
tataḥ sā jananī tasya vālakasya karadvayam |
(rm 123)
dīnārāṃkitamālokya saharṣaṃ vismitābhavat || 10.80{80} ||
tato'sau janakaścāpi jātaṃ putramahādbhutam |
sakhībhirveditaṃ śrutvā vismito draṣṭumāyayau || 10.81{81} ||
tataḥ prajāpatiḥ sāpi vismito svāmino'grataḥ |
darśayitvā sutaṃ putraṃ śaṃkitaivaṃ nyavedayat || 10.82{82} ||
svāmiṃ paśya mahāścaryyamasya hemānvitau karau |
tacchubhaṃ vāpyaśubhaṃ vā darśayitvā vicāraya || 10.83{83} ||
iti bhāryyoditaṃ śrutvā dṛṣṭvā taṃ vālakaṃ ciram |
dīnāraṃ bhujayoḥ paśyaṃ tasthau savismayānvitaḥ || 10.84{84} ||
tatastasya gṛhastho'sau nimittaṃ jñātumādarāt |
naimittikaṃ samāhūya dārakaṃ tamadarśayat || 10.85{86} ||
bhavanto dṛśyatāmasya dārakasya karadvayam |
tannimittaṃ parijñāya tatphalaṃ vaktumarhatha || 10.86{86} ||
ityukte tena te sarve dṛṣṭvā taṃ dārakaṃ śubham |
dīnāre bhujayoścāpi parijñāyaivamabravīt || 10.87{87} ||
sādho gṛhapate hyasya dārakasya karadvayam |
suvarṇalakṣaṇopetaṃ tatte bhadraṃ bhavetkhalu || 10.88{88} ||
tavāyaṃ dārako dātā maheśākhyo bhavetkila |
svahastaṃ saṃprasāryyaiva hemavṛṣṭiṃ kariṣyati || 10.89{89} ||
apanīya svayaṃ hastādidaṃ dīnāramarpayet |
apanīte punaranyatprādurbhavettato'dhikam || 10.90{90} ||
ityevaṃ taiḥ samādiṣṭaṃ śrutvā saṃharṣito gṛhī |
tato jātimahaṃ kṛtvā jñātīnevamabhāṣata || 10.91{91} ||
bhavanto dārakasyāsya śubhākṣarasamanvitam |
nāmadheyaṃ yathāyogyaṃ kriyatāṃ saṃprasiddhitam || 10.92{92} ||
iti tenārthitaṃ śrutvā sarve te jñātayastathā |
dṛṣṭvā tasya nimittāni saṃmatenaivamabruvan || 10.93{93} ||
yasmāddhastadvayo'pyasya hiraṇyenopalakṣitam |
tannāmnā prasiddho'stu hiraṇyapāṇirityayam || 10.94{94} ||
evaṃ jñātisamudiṣṭaṃ śrutvāsau sa gṛhādhipaḥ |
tatheti tasya putrasya cakre nāmavyavasthitam || 10.95{95} ||
tato hiraṇyapāṇiḥ sa dārakaḥ pratipālane |
aṣṭāsu yo'pi dhātrīṣu tena pitrā samarpitaḥ || 10.96{96} ||
tābhiḥ prapālyamāno'sau pathyapuṣṭopacāraṇaiḥ |
varddhito'bhūtkramādāśu hradasthamiva paṃkajam || 10.97{97} ||
(rm 124)
tathā kramātpravṛddho'sau hiraṇyapāṇirātmavit |
śrāddho bhadrāśayo dhīmān svaparārthahitārthikaḥ || 10.98{98} ||
tataḥ sa śikṣituṃ vidyāḥ sadguruṃ samupāśrayat |
kramātteṣāṃ prasādācca vidyāpāraṃ yayau laghuḥ || 10.99{99} ||
tato'sau dārako vidvān dātā śraddhāsamanvitaḥ |
svahastaṃ saṃprasāryyaiva dadau dānaṃ yathepsitam || 10.100{100} ||
gurubhyo brāhmaṇebhyaśca yācakebhyaḥ sa dārakaḥ |
svahastaṃ saṃprasāryyaiva dadau hemaṃ yathepsitam || 10.101{1} ||
tathā taddānamākarṇya śravaṇabrāhmaṇādayaḥ |
arthino dhanalubdhāśca saṃpratīyuḥ samaṃtataḥ || 10.102{2} ||
tathā taṃ samupetyaivaṃ sarvadigbhyaḥ samāgatāḥ |
vālāvṛddhādayo dīnā yayācire yathepsitam || 10.103{3} ||
tadā hiraṇyapāṇiḥ sa dṛṣṭvaitānarthino mudā |
sarvopakaraṇaiścāpi sarvāṃstān samatoṣayat || 10.104{4} ||
tataste yācakāḥ sarve tenaiva paritarpitāḥ |
yathechayā sukhaṃ bhuktvā pracerurdharmamānasā || 10.105{5} ||
tathā vīthigataścaivaṃ hastadvayaṃ prasārya saḥ |
hemarāśiṃ samutthāpya sarvebhyaḥ pradadau mudā || 10.106{6} ||
tathā sarve'pi te lokā dhanāḍhyāḥ sukhino'bhavan |
naiko'bhūtkṛpano duḥkhī no'pi kaściddaridritaḥ || 10.107{7} ||
sarve'pi dhaninaḥ prāḍhyā dātāro bhogino'bhavan |
satyadharmānuraktāśca saddharmaśravaṇārthinaḥ || 10.108{8} ||
tadā tasyaiva tatkīrttiḥ sarvatra bhuvaneṣvapi |
lokairudgīryamāno'sau prasasāra samaṃtataḥ || 10.109{9} ||
taddṛṣṭvā brāhmaṇo lobhī mātsaryyaprahatāśayaḥ |
tadyaśaḥ pratilabdhaṃ sa manasaivaṃ vyacintayat || 10.110{10} ||
yadasya bhujayorhema cintāmaṇisamaṃ khalu |
tenāyaṃ bhavate śrīmān prārthitārthādhikapradaḥ || 10.111{11} ||
yaccāyaṃ dārakaḥ śrāddhaḥ prārthanāsaṃpradāyakaḥ |
tadahaṃ prārthanāṃ kṛtvā pratigṛhṇīya tatmaṇim || 10.112{12} ||
ityasau manasā dhyatvā lobhākṛṣṭo tvarānvitaḥ |
(rm 125)
taṃ hiraṇyabhujaṃ pretya taddīnāraṃ samayācata || 10.113{13} ||
jayo'stu te sadā sādho dīrghāyuśca sumaṃgalam |
tathaiva tvaṃ sadā bhūyā arthināṃ kalpapādapaḥ || 10.114{14} ||
tvatkīrttyā samāhūtaḥ prāgato'smi yadāśayā |
tadarthaṃ pūrayitvā me cittaṃ harṣaya sarvataḥ || 10.115{15} ||
ityevaṃ prārthite tena vipreṇābhipragalbhinā |
dṛṣṭvā hiraṇyapāṇiḥ sa taṃ vipraṃ pratyabhāṣata || 10.116{16} ||
sādhu vipra prasīda tvaṃ yadarthaṃ samupāgataḥ |
tadarthaṃ saṃpradatvādya harṣayiṣyāmi te manaḥ || 10.117{17} ||
tacchaṃkā mā kṛthā hyatra yadyatte hṛdi rocate |
tattannūnaṃ pradāsye te tadvadasva yadīhitam || 10.118{18} ||
iti tenoditaṃ śrutvā vipro'sau saṃpraharṣitaḥ |
svastītyāśīrvaco datvā taddīnāramayācata || 10.119{19} ||
sādho hiraṇyapāṇe yattava hastasamudbhavam |
taddīnāraṃ pradatvā me cittaṃ saṃharṣayārthadaḥ || 10.120{20} ||
iti tenoditaṃ śrutvā tatpitāsau suvismitaḥ |
savandhujñātimitrastaṃ putramevamabhāṣata || 10.121{21} ||
śṛṇu putra mayā proktaṃ tavaiva hitakāraṇam |
yadi te svāminī prītistatkarttavyaṃ hi sarvathā || 10.122{22} ||
tvaṃ hi putra mayā labdhaḥ saṃbuddhasyānubhāvataḥ |
tatsaṃbuddhaśāsane kāryyameva dātu samarhasi || 10.123{23} ||
yaccedan bhujayorjātaṃ dīnāraṃ maṇisaṃnibham |
tadapi sugatasyaiva prabhāvānmanyatāṃ khalu || 10.124{24} ||
yaccedaṃ te karodbhūtaṃ jīvitaṃ sahajendriyam |
tatsvakarātsamuddhṛtya kathaṃ dadyā dvijātaye || 10.125{25} ||
yadīdaṃ dāsyate hastātsamuddhṛtya valādapi |
tadā te jīvitaṃ vāpi sahānena vrajaṃ khalu || 10.126{26} ||
tasmādevaṃ parijñāya yadi te jīvita priyam |
tadevaṃ sahajaṃ hema mā pradāhi dvijātaye || 10.127{27} ||
tadanyaddīyatāṃ tasmai yadyadvastu samīchitam |
idaṃ tu sarvathānyasmai dātavyaṃ naiva yatnataḥ || 10.128{28} ||
yenaiva tvaṃ maheśākhyo sarvārthiprārthitārthadaḥ |
tena yadā paribhraṣṭastadā kiṃ tena manyase || 10.129{29} ||
yenāpi ca tvayā sarve yācakāḥ saṃpratarpitāḥ |
(rm 126)
taccintāmaṇisāmānyaṃ kathaṃ dātuṃ samichasi || 10.130{30} ||
yattvayā sukṛtaṃ karma prakṛtvā puṇyamācitam |
nūnaṃ tasya vipākatvaṃ bhuṃjayoste pratiṣṭhitam || 10.131{31} ||
tasmādidaṃ mahāratnaṃ kasmiñcinmā pradīyatām |
datvā tvidaṃ mahāratnaṃ kimanyallabdhamichasi || 10.132{32} ||
yadi tvidaṃ mahāratnaṃ dātumevaṃ pratiṣṭhitam |
saṃpradehi jinendrāya tatpuṇyamakṣayaṃ vahum || 10.133{33} ||
puṇyena labhyate janma śuddhavamṣe mahatkule |
puṇyena dīyate kīrttiṃ nirvṛtiṃ ca samāpnuyāt || 10.134{34} ||
yadi puṇye'sti te vāṃchā tadbuddhaśaraṇaṃ gataḥ |
dehi buddhānuśāsinyā taddānaṃ hi mahatphalam || 10.135{35} ||
tato nityaṃ sukhaṃ bhuktvā kṛtvā saṃbodhisādhanam |
kramādbodhicariṃ prāpte nirvṛtiṃ vā samāpnuyāḥ || 10.136{36} ||
iti pitrā samādiṣṭaṃ śrutvāsau hemabhṛtsudhīḥ |
tatheti niścayaṃ kṛtvā punarevamabhāṣata || 10.137{37} ||
satyaṃ tāta tvayādiṣṭaṃ tatsarvaṃ hitameva me |
tasmāttathā cariṣyāmi tvadājñāṃ śirasā vahan || 10.138{38} ||
kiṃ tu satyaṃ mayā proktāmidaṃ dātuṃ dvijātaye |
tatkathamanyathā kṛtvā puṇyameva samāpnuyāt || 10.139{39} ||
tatsatyaṃ pūraṇe tāta pradadyāmi mamātmanaḥ |
atha na dāsyate hyatra kiyatkālaṃ hi jīvitam || 10.140{40} ||
yadidaṃ bhujayorhemaṃ mamātmasahasaṃbhavam |
tathaivaṃ sahajīvena vinakṣyati na saṃśayaḥ || 10.141{41} ||
yathedaṃ tu mahāratnaṃ mama devasamudbhavam |
tathaivaṃ saṃpradatvāsmai punaḥ prāpsyāmi te'dhikam || 10.142{42} ||
yathā buddhānubhāyena saṃjātaṃ karayoridam |
tathā satyānubhāvena punaranyajjaniṣyate || 10.143{43} ||
dharmeṇa tuṣyate buddho mūlaṃ dharmasya satyavāk |
tatsatyapāraṇārthena dāsyāmyahamidaṃ maṇim || 10.144{44} ||
satyaṃ vinā na phullante puṇyāni prakṛtānyapi |
mlānibhūtāni naśyante śasyānīvāmṛtaṃ vinā || 10.145{45} ||
satyena varddhate dharmaṃ dharmeṇa pālyate naraḥ |
dharmiṣṭhaḥ sadgatiṃ yāti tasmātsatyaṃ mahadvalī || 10.146{46} ||
tasmātsatyavalenātra yaśodharmārthalabdhaye |
sarvathāsmai pradāsyāmi mā kṛthāstaṃ nivāraṇam || 10.147{47} ||
(rm 127)
iti putravacaḥ śrutvā pitā sa vismayānvitaḥ |
tathetyabhyanumoditvā tasthau tūṣṇī vilokayan || 10.148{48} ||
tato hiraṇyapāṇiḥ sa matvā pitrādhivāsitam |
taddīnāraṃ sahasotkṛtya viprāya pradadau mudā || 10.149{49} ||
tatpradattamasau vipraḥ pratigṛhya pramoditaḥ |
datvāśīrvacanaṃ tasmai svagṛhaṃ sahasā yayau || 10.150{50} ||
tataścānantaraṃ tasya bhuje'nyattadguṇādhikam |
dīnāraṃ divyaratnābhaiḥ prādurāsīnmahādbhutam || 10.151{51} ||
taddattānaṃtarodbhūtaṃ dīnāraṃ tadguṇādhikam |
dṛṣṭvā śrutvā ca sarve te lokā āśu suvismitāḥ || 10.152{52} ||
tato hiraṇyapāṇiḥ sa evaṃ lokān praharṣayan |
saṃbuddhaśāsane dātuṃ jetavanamupācarat || 10.153{53} ||
tatra bhikṣūn yatīn dṛṣṭvā saddharmacaraṇotsukaḥ |
saṃbuddhaṃ sugataṃ draṣṭuṃ vihāraṃ samupāviśat || 10.154{54} ||
tatrāsau bhagavantaṃ tamadrākṣītsamitisthitam |
dvātriṃśallakṣaṇāśītivyaṃjanaiḥ parimaṃḍitam || 10.155{55} ||
vyāmaprabhāsamujvālaṃ sahasreṇādhikaprabham |
śānteṃdriyaṃ subhadrāṃgaṃ saumyarūpaṃ manoharam || 10.156{56} ||
dṛṣṭvāsau suprasannātmā sahasā samupāgataḥ |
natvā pādau munestasya purastātsamupāśrayan || 10.157{57} ||
tatrāsau bhagavān tasya dṛṣṭvāśayaviśuddhatām |
ādimadhyāntakalyāṇaṃ dideśa dharmamuttamam || 10.158{58} ||
taṃ dharmaṃ sugatādiṣṭaṃ śrutvāsau saṃpramoditaḥ |
ānaṃdaṃ samupāśritya praṇamyaivaṃ vyajijñapat || 10.159{59} ||
bhadanta dātumichāmi saṃghebhyo bhojanaṃ khalu |
tadājñāṃ dehi me śāsta yadi te'sti kṛpā mayi || 10.160{60} ||
iti vijñāpite tena hiraṇyapāṇinā mudā |
sa ānaṃdaḥ samālokya dārakaṃ taṃ samādiśat || 10.161{61} ||
sādhu vatsa mahābhāgyādbhadramālocitaṃ tvayā |
yadyatra dāsyate bhojyaṃ kārṣāpaṇaṃ pradīyatām || 10.162{62} ||
evaṃkṛte tu te vatsa maṃgalaṃ sarvadā bhavet |
ataśca durgatiṃ hitvā sadgatiṃ samavāpnuyāḥ || 10.163{63} ||
ityānaṃdasamādiṣṭe dārako'sau pramoditaḥ |
svayaṃ hastau prasāryyaiva hemarāśiṃ vyadhātpunaḥ || 10.164{64} ||
tāṃ dṛṣṭvā bhikṣavaḥ sarve vismitāḥ saṃpraharṣitāḥ |
(rm 128)
taddravyaiḥ sādhayāmāsa bhojyāni surasāni ca || 10.165{65} ||
tataḥ sa dārako bhojyaistaiḥ praṇītasusādhitaiḥ |
saṃbuddhapramukhaṃ sarvasāṃghikaṃ samatoṣayat || 10.166{66} ||
tato'sau bhojanānte ca saṃbuddhapramukhaṃ kramāt |
sarvasaṃghaṃ praṇamyaivaṃ punastasthau kṛtāṃjaliḥ || 10.167{67} ||
tataḥ śrībhagavān dṛṣṭvā tasyāśayaviśuddhatām |
āryyasatyaṃ samāyuktaṃ dideśa dharmamuttamam || 10.168{68} ||
tacchrutvā hemapāṇiḥ sa satkāyadṛṣṭiparvatam |
prabhitvā jñānavajreṇa viṃśatiśikharodgatam || 10.169{69} ||
śrotāpattiphalaṃ prāpto dṛṣṭasatyo'bhavattataḥ |
saṃbuddhasagaṇaṃ natvā svagehaṃ harṣito yayau || 10.170{70} ||
tatra sarvaṃ sa tadvṛttaṃ pitrorākhyāya harṣitaḥ |
pravrajyācaraṇānujñāṃ pitarau pratyayācata || 10.171{71} ||
cittaṃ me rocate tāta pravrajyāvratasādhane |
tadanujñāṃ pradattaṃ me hitaṃ mayi yadīcchatha || 10.172{72} ||
iti putroditaṃ śrutvā svātmajaṃ sa pitāvadat |
mā kṛthā sahasā putra pravrajyācaraṇe manaḥ || 10.173{73} ||
pravrajyācaraṇe bhaikṣyaṃ duḥkhināmeva tadvratam |
tvaṃ vṛddhimānmahādātā tatpravrajyāṃ kutaścareḥ || 10.174{74} ||
kiṃ vāpyate vivāhastvaṃ daharaśca kumārikaḥ |
pravrajyā duṣkaraṃ sthānaṃ tatkathaṃ pracaretsutaḥ || 10.175{75} ||
tattāvattvaṃ yuvā putra vivāhitvā kulastriyam |
bhuktvā saukhyaṃ yathākāmaṃ gṛhe dharmaṃ samācara || 10.176{76} ||
tato vaṃśaṃ samutpādya kulasthitiprasiddhaye |
kuladharmaṃ pratisthāpya tataḥ pravraja vārddhikaḥ || 10.177{77} ||
yadā vṛddho daridraśca pravrajyāṃ hi tadā caret |
yāvadyuvā samṛddhaśca tāvattvaṃ pravraja katham || 10.178{78} ||
saṃpattiḥ kṣaṇayauvanyametanmāravaladhruvam |
tadetadā kathaṃ putra pravrajyāvratamācareḥ || 10.179{79} ||
ityetadduṣkaraṃ matvā pravrajyācaraṇe manaḥ |
sarvathā tvaṃ vinirvārya kuladharmaṃ samācara || 10.180{80} ||
tataste maṃgalaṃ nityamimu putra bhaveddhruvam |
(rm 129)
yaśodharmasukhānvetya sadgatiṃ cāpyavāpnuyāḥ || 10.181{81} ||
iti pitroditaṃ śrutvā dārako'sau viśaṃkitaḥ |
bodhayan pitaraṃ dharme punarevamabhāṣata || 10.182{82} ||
satyameva tathā tāta yadetadbhāṣyate tvayā |
tathāpi śrūyatāṃ tāta yadabhiprāyakaṃ mama || 10.183{83} ||
anityaṃ khalu saṃsāraṃ jīvitaṃ cāpi bhaṃguram |
kṣaṇadhvaṃsi śarīraṃ ca kṣaṇasaṃpadghanopamam || 10.184{84} ||
evaṃ paśyanahaṃ tāta saṃsārabhoganispṛhaḥ |
pravrajyāvratamāśritya prāptumichāmi nirvṛtim || 10.185{85} ||
api ca dullabhaṃ janma mānuṣaṃ bhavacāriṇām |
sarveṣāmapi jantūnāṃ maraṇaṃ sarvathā dhruvam || 10.186{86} ||
mānuṣye labhyamāne tu kuto dharmānubhāvanā |
vinā dharmānubhāvena kathaṃ sadgatimāpnuyāt || 10.187{87} ||
saṃbuddho'pi sasaṃgho'yaṃ sthāsyati na sadeha ca |
anyatrāpi vrajennūnaṃ nirvṛtiṃ vā gamiṣyati || 10.188{88} ||
saṃbuddhe nirvṛtiṃ yāte saddharmo'pi vinakṣyate |
saddharmāntargate loke mlechadharmavalī bhavet || 10.189{89} ||
mlechadharmavalīkrānte sarvatra bhūmimaṃḍale |
sarvalokāśca kāmāṃdhā bhaveyuḥ kleśamāninaḥ || 10.190{90} ||
tada dharmānusaṃvṛttau matiḥ kasyāpi no caret |
pravrajyāsaṃvaraṃ dhartuṃ kaḥ samartho bhaviṣyati || 10.191{91} ||
naiva saṃyujyate cittaṃ pravrajyāsaṃvaraṃ vinā |
aviśodhitacittātmā na yāyātparamāṃ gatim || 10.192{92} ||
taccittaye viśuddhyarthaṃ nirvāṇapadalabdhaye |
pravrajyāsaṃvaraṃ dharttumichāmi tāta sāṃpratam || 10.193{93} ||
tadyadi me hitaṃ draṣṭumichasi tāta sarvadā |
tadanujñāṃ samādātumarhasi mātra cāraye || 10.194{94} ||
iti puroditaṃ śrutvā pitā sa paribodhitaḥ |
tathetyanumataṃ kṛtvābhavattūṣṇīvyavasthitaḥ || 10.195{95} ||
tadā tajjananī cāsau dṛṣṭvā taṃ sutamātmajam |
snehādaśru vimocantī nivārayitumabravīt || 10.196{96} ||
hā putra kathamekānte māṃ vihāya kva yāsyase |
mā putra sahasā gacha kiṃcitkālaṃ vinodaya || 10.197{97} ||
yāvajjīvāmyahaṃ tāta tāvanmā gāḥ kuhāpi ca |
(rm 130)
mṛtāyāṃ mayi bho putra tadā vrajasva vāṃchite || 10.198{98} ||
acireṇaiva māṃ mṛtyurgrahīṣyati na saṃśayaḥ |
tāvadeva gṛhe sthitvā dānaṃ kurvan sukhaṃ vasa || 10.199{99} ||
iti mātroditaṃ śrutvā putro'sau parimoditaḥ |
natvā tāṃ jananīmevaṃ bodhayitumabhāṣata || 10.200{100} ||
yattvayāmve rudantyevaṃ mṛtyunāma prakīrtitam |
tena me coditaṃ cittaṃ pravrajyāvratasādhane || 10.201{1} ||
dhruvaṃ janmavatāṃ mṛtyurmṛtānāṃ janma ca dhruvam |
parivarttini saṃsāre mṛtaḥ ko hi na jāyate || 10.202{2} ||
dharmiṣṭho vīryāvāṃścāpi maraṇaṃ yāti sarvataḥ |
pāpiṣṭho nighnitaścāpi maraṇaṃ yāti sarvataḥ || 10.203{3} ||
dharmiṣṭhaḥ satsukhaṃ bhuktvā mṛto yātyeva sadgatim |
pāpiṣṭho duḥkhatāṃ bhuktvā mṛto gachati durgatim || 10.204{4} ||
yadā saṃtyajate jantuḥ saṃsāraprāṇavāyunā |
tadā saṃtyajyate sarvairvaṃdhumitrajanairapi || 10.205{5} ||
tadā na vidyate ko'pi hitakārī sahānugaḥ |
dharma ekaḥ sahāyaḥ syātsarvatrāpi hitārthakṛt || 10.206{6} ||
tasmātmātaḥ prayatnena karttavyaṃ dharmamādarāt |
dharmeṇa rakṣyate sarva ihāmutra sahāpi hi || 10.207{7} ||
dharmeṇaivābhipālyante vālavṛddhāturādayaḥ |
dharmeṇa sudhṛtāḥ satvā nimagnā duḥkhasāgare || 10.208{8} ||
dharmeṇa jīyate duṣṭo dharmeṇa jīyate kaliḥ |
dharmeṇa jīyate rāgo mṛtyurdharmeṇa jīyate || 10.209{9} ||
dharmeṇa sukule janma lakṣmī dharmeṇa sthīyate |
vidyāyaśo'rthamoksāśca prāpyante dharmataḥ khalu || 10.210{10} ||
dharmaṃ tu saugataṃ kāryaṃ sarvalokahitaṃkaram |
sugatasyāpi dharmeṣu pravrajyādharmamuttamam || 10.211{11} ||
yamāśritya mahāsatvā jitvā kleśagaṇān laghuḥ |
sākṣādarhatpadaṃ prāpya vrajanti nirvṛtiṃ parām || 10.212{12} ||
iti matvā hi te sarve bhikṣavo bhavanispṛhāḥ |
pravrajyāvratamāśritya bhavanti brahmacāriṇaḥ || 10.213{13} ||
tathātrāhaṃ samichāmi pravrajyāvratamuttamam |
tadanujñāṃ pradatvā me nātra kāryo viṣāditaḥ || 10.214{14} ||
(rm 131)
yadi na dīyate'nujñā tadapi no viyogataḥ |
akasmānmṛtyunā ghrāya grasiṣyati puro hi naḥ || 10.215{15} ||
tadā kiṃ te kariṣyāmi mamāpi kiṃ kariṣyasi |
kṛtvānuśocanāṃ caiva yāsyāmo maraṇaṃ dhruvam || 10.216{16} ||
iti mātaḥ parijñāya tadanujñāṃ pradehi me |
viṣādaṃ mā kṛthā atra prasādīnī bhavetprabhu || 10.217{17} ||
tathā te maṃgalaṃ nityamihāmutrāpi saṃbhavet |
ahaṃ ca saugataṃ dharmaṃ dhṛtvā nirvṛtimāpnuyām || 10.218{18} ||
iti putroditaṃ śrutvā mātāsau paribodhitā |
tathānumodanāṃ kṛtvā tasthau tūṣṇīvyavasthitān || 10.219{19} ||
atha hiraṇyapāṇiḥ saḥ pitrormatvābhisaṃmatam |
tayośca caraṇe natvā sahasā niryayau gṛhāt || 10.220{20} ||
tatra jñātigaṇāṃścāsau vandhumitrasuhṛjjanān |
kṛpaṇātithisaṃghādiṃ vāṃchitārthairatoṣayat || 10.221{21} ||
tato'sau vāṃdhavān sarvān saṃtarpya paribodhayan |
āśu jetavanaṃ gatvā vihāraṃ samupāviśat || 10.222{22} ||
tatrāsau sugataṃ dṛṣṭvā kṛtāṃjalipuṭo mudā |
bhagavaṃtaṃ tamānamya pravrajyāṃ samayācata || 10.223{23} ||
namaste bhagavacchāsta bhavatāṃ caraṇaṃ vraje |
tatpravrajyāvrataṃ datvā brahmacaryaṃ pradehi me || 10.224{24} ||
iti tenārthite'sau śrībhagavān tamabhāṣata |
ehi bhikṣo kumāra tvaṃ brahmacaryaṃ carehi sa || 10.225{25} ||
iti prokto munīndro'sau pāṇinā tacchiraḥ spṛśan |
samyakpravājayitvāśu svasaṃghe taṃ samagrahīt || 10.226{26} ||
ehīti proktamātre'pi jinendreṇa sa muṇḍitaḥ |
khikkhirīpātrahastābhūclcīvaraprāvṛtaḥ sudhīḥ || 10.227{27} ||
tataḥ śikṣāsu śikṣitvā prāpyopasaṃpado'pi ca |
vyāyacchadghaṭamānaśca samādhiṣu samāhitaḥ || 10.228{28} ||
tadidaṃ paṃcagaṃdaṃ ca bhavacakraṃ calācalam |
viditvā sarvasaṃskāragatiścāpi vighāṭinī || 10.229{29} ||
bhiṃdan sarvamavidyāṅgaṃ prāpya vidyāḥ śubhaṃkarāḥ |
pratisaṃvidguṇaprāpto jitakleśagaṇo yatiḥ || 10.230{30} ||
sākṣādarhatpadaṃ prāpya vītarāgo jitendriyaḥ |
samaloṣṭasuvarṇaśca vāsīcaṃdanasaṃnibhaḥ || 10.231{31} ||
(rm 132)
ākāśasamacittaśca nirvikalpo niraṃjanaḥ |
saṃsārabhogyasatkāralābhalobhasunispṛhaḥ || 10.232{32} ||
paṃcābhijñapadaprāpto caturbrahmavihārikam |
sadevāsuramartyānāṃ traidhātukanivāsinām || 10.233{33} ||
pūjyo mānyo'bhivaṃdyaśca babhūva sa hiraṇyabhṛt |
tataste bhikṣavaḥ sarve dṛṣṭvā taṃ dārakaṃ yatim || 10.234{34} ||
vismitāḥ śrīghanaṃ natvā paprachustasya karmatām |
bhagavan kena dharmeṇa hiraṇyapāṇirayaṃ sudhīḥ || 10.235{35} ||
sarvārthino hi saṃtarpya sākṣādarhan bhavatyapi |
iti tairbhikṣubhiḥ pṛṣṭe bhagavān tān tathābravīt || 10.236{36} ||
śrūyatāṃ bhikṣavaḥ sarvairyuṣmābhirvismitāśayaiḥ |
anena yatkṛtaṃ karma tanmayā kathyate'dhunā || 10.237{37} ||
purāsīdbhagavān buddhaḥ kāśyapo'rhanmunīśvaraḥ |
sarvavidyādhipaḥ śāstā tathāgato vināyakaḥ || 10.238{38} ||
sasāṃghiko jinendro'sau vārāṇasīmupāśritaḥ |
bodhicaryāṃ samādiśya kṛtvā dharmamayaṃ jagat || 10.239{39} ||
sarvāṇi buddhakāryāṇi samāpya nirvṛtiṃ yayau |
tadā rājā kṛkī nāma matvā taṃ nirvṛtiṃ gatam || 10.240{40} ||
vidhivadagnisaṃskāraṃ kārayitvā mahārcanaiḥ |
tatastaddhāturatnāni garbha āropya sādarāt || 10.241{41} ||
hemaratnamayaṃ stūpaṃ kārayāmāsa sa prabhuḥ |
tatsusiddhaṃ pratiṣṭhāpya sadvidhānairmahotsavaiḥ || 10.242{42} ||
mahaścāpi tadā tatra sthāpayitvā bhajannṛpaḥ |
tasminnāropyamānāyāṃ yaṣṭyāṃ pūjāparigrahī || 10.243{43} ||
kintavo kaṃdaro nāma nidadhe rūpakadvayam |
tataḥ sa pādayornatvā praṇidhānaṃ vyadhāttathā || 10.244{44} ||
yanmayā śraddhayā bhaktyāropitaṃ rūpakadvayam |
yatpuṇyaṃ ca vipākena jāyeyaṃ sukule sadā || 10.245{45} ||
karodbhavasuvarṇaiḥ saṃtarpayitvārthino janān |
evaṃvidhaṃ ca śāstāramārogya samupāśritaḥ || 10.246{46} ||
etaddharmaṃ samāsādya prāpnuyāṃ paramāṃ gatim |
tena puṇyavipākena praṇidhānena bhūyasā || 10.247{47} ||
hiraṇyapāṇirayaṃ dātā bhavatyarhaṃ sa sāṃpratam |
evaṃ sarve'pi saṃsāre bhuṃjate karmajaṃ phalam || 10.248{48} ||
(rm 133)
yenaivaṃ yatkṛtaṃ karma tenaiva bhuṃjyate phalam |
abhuktaṃ kṣīyate naiva karma kvāpi kadā cana || 10.249{49} ||
śubhaṃ vāpyaśubhaṃ vāpi bhuktaṃ tu kṣīyate khalu |
nāgnibhirdahyate karma klidyate nodakairapi || 10.250{50} ||
vāyubhiḥ śuṣyate nāpi bhūmiṣu kṣīyate na hi |
anyathāpi bhavennaiva saṃsāre karmaṇo gatiḥ || 10.251{51} ||
śubhasya śubhataiva syātkṛṣṇasya kṛṣṇataiva hi |
miśrasya miśritaiva syādanyathā na sadā bhavet || 10.252{52} ||
evaṃ matvātha saṃsāre caritavyaṃ sadā śubham |
na praṇasyaṃti karmāṇi kalpakoṭiśatairapi || 10.253{53} ||
sāmagrīṃ prāpya kālaṃ ca phalanti prāṇināṃ khalu |
ityasau bhagavān buddhaḥ satkarmadeśanāmataiḥ || 10.254{54} ||
tarpayan sa kalāṃllokān tato viśrāntamādadhau |
iti śāstroditaṃ śrutvā sarve te bhikṣusāṃghikāḥ || 10.255{55} ||
śubheṣveva sadā nityaṃ saṃpraceruḥ samāhitaḥ |
mahārāja tvayāpyevaṃ caritavyaṃ śubhe sadā || 10.256{56} ||
bodhayitvā prajāñcaivaṃ sthāpanīyāḥ śubhe sadā |
tataste maṃgalaṃ nityaṃ sarvatrāpi bhavetkhalu |
kramādbodhicarīṃ prāpya saṃbodhipadamāpuṇyāḥ || 10.257{57} ||
iti tena samādiṣṭaṃ śrutvā sa nṛpatīśvaraḥ |
tathetyabhyanumoditvā prābhyanaṃdatsapārṣadaḥ || 10.258{58} ||
śṛṇvanti ye śrāvayantīha yaśca hiraṇyapāṇeravadānametat |
te kleśamuktāḥ sugatānubhaktāḥ prayānti nūnaṃ sugatālayeṣu || 10.259{59} ||

++ iti śrīratnamālayāṃ hiraṇyapāṇyavadānaṃ nāma samāptaṃ ++


(rm 134)
xi hastakāvadāna
athāśoko mahīpālaḥ kṛtāṃjalipuṭo mudā |
upaguptaṃ guruṃ natvā bhūyo'pyevamabhāṣata || 11.1{1} ||
bhadanta śrotumichāmi punaranyatsubhāṣitam |
yathā te guruṇākhyātaṃ tathā me vaktumarhasi || 11.2{2} ||
iti tena narendreṇa pṛṣṭo'sau sugatāṃśajaḥ |
upagupto mahābhijñastaṃ nṛpaṃ pratyabhāṣata || 11.3{3} ||
śṛṇu rājanmahābhāga yathā me guruṇoditam |
tathāhaṃ te pravakṣyāmi tadanumodanāṃ kuru || 11.4{4} ||
puraikasamaye caivaṃ śrīghano'sau tathāgataḥ |
saṃbuddhaḥ sugataḥ śāstā sarvadharmānudeśakaḥ || 11.5{5} ||
śrāvastyāṃ vahirudyāne jetavane manorame |
anāthapiṇḍadodyāne vihāre sugatāśrame || 11.6{6} ||
bhikṣubhiḥ śrāvakaiḥ sārddhamupāsakaiśca cailakaiḥ |
bodhisatvairmahāsatvaistadanyaiśca maharṣibhiḥ || 11.7{7} ||
vyaharatsarvahitārthāya dharmāmṛtaṃ pravarṣayan |
tatsaddharmāmṛtaṃ pātuṃ prayayurbodhikāminaḥ || 11.8{8} ||
lokapālā mahārājā svasvasainyajanaiḥ saha |
brāhmaṇāḥ kṣatriyā vaiśyāḥ śūdrāśca maṃtriṇo janāḥ || 11.9{9} ||
amātyāḥ sādhavaḥ paurā dhaninaśca mahājanāḥ |
vaṇijaḥ sārthavāhāśca tathānye sarvajātikāḥ || 11.10{10} ||
sarvadigbhyaḥ samāgatya sarve te samupāgatāḥ |
taṃ buddhaṃ sugataṃ nāthaṃ pūjayitvā samaṃtataḥ || 11.11{11} ||
parivṛtya purodhāya śakhyāviguṇakāmitāḥ |
kṛtāṃjalipuṭo tasthuḥ saddharmacaraṇotsukāḥ || 11.12{12} ||
athāsau bhagavān dṛṣṭvā tān sarvān samupasthitān |
ādimadhyāntakalyāṇaṃ dideśa dharmamuttamam || 11.13{13} ||
tatsaddharmāmṛtaṃ pītvā sarve lokādhipādayaḥ |
harṣitāste'numodanto vabhūvuḥ paribodhitāḥ || 11.14{14} ||
tadaiva samaye tasyāṃ śrāvastyāṃ purisaṃbhavaḥ |
āsīdgṛhapatiḥ prāḍhyaḥ kuveravatsamṛddhimān || 11.15{15} ||
śrāddho dātā mahābhogī sarvahitārthabhṛt |
(rm 135)
triratnasevako dhīmān suviśālaparigrahaḥ || 11.16{16} ||
saṃgṛhī vidhivadbhāryāṃ svakuladharmacāriṇī |
pariṇītā yathākāmaṃ saṃrame paricārayan || 11.17{17} ||
tasyāsau ramaṇī bhāryā saṃvṛttā garbhiṇī satī |
asūta samaye putraṃ prāsādikaṃ manoharam || 11.18{18} ||
sa ca jātismaro vālo jātamātraḥ svakau bhujau |
saṃdṛṣṭvāliṃgya cuṃvitvā pariṣvajyaivamabravīt || 11.19{19} ||
aho vatādya me hastau sucireṇa samudbhavau |
aho labdhāvimau hastau sucireṇa mayādhunā || 11.20{20} ||
tenaivaṃ jātamātreṇa dṛṣṭaṃ kṛtaṃ prabhāṣitam |
dṛṣṭvā śrutvā ca sā mātā trāsitā vismitābhavat || 11.21{21} ||
kimetaditi saṃtrastā dṛṣṭvā taṃ dārakaṃ mudā |
bharttāraṃ sahasāhūya agramenamadarśayat || 11.22{22} ||
tadāsau dārakaścāpi dṛṣṭvā taṃ janakaṃ mudā |
hastau dṛṣṭvā tathāliṃgya cuṃvayitvā caivamabravīt || 11.23{23} ||
aho vatādya me hastau sucireṇa samudbhavau |
aho labdhāvimau hastau sucireṇa mayādhunā || 11.24{24} ||
iti saṃdarśayaṃ hastāvevaṃ cāpyavadacchiśuḥ |
dārako'sau punaḥ supto nidrāṃ bheje yadichayā || 11.25{25} ||
tato'sau janako'pyetaddṛṣṭvā śrutvā pravismitaḥ |
kimetaditi sāśaṃkā jñātināhūya cābravīt |
bhavanto'dya prajātaṃ me putraratnaṃ manoharam |
tannimittaṃ nirīkṣyaitatsatyaṃ me vaktumarhatha |
iti tenoditaṃ śrutvā sarve te jñātayo mudā |
tatputraṃ dārakaṃ draṣṭuṃ tatkoṣṭhaṃ samupādiśat |
tathāsau dārakaścaivaṃ tān dṛṣṭvā samupasthitān |
bhujau paśyan pariṣvajya cuṃvitvaivamabhāṣata |
aho vatādya me hastau sucireṇa mayādhunā |
bhavanto'rhatsu mā cittaṃ dūṣayata kadā cana |
kharāṃ vācaṃ ca mārhatsu niścārayata sarvadā |
evaṃ saṃdarśayaṃ hastau vacanaṃ saṃvadaṃ śiśuḥ |
dārako'sau punaḥ supto nidrāṃ bheje yadichayā |
etatte jñātayo dṛṣṭvā śrutvā sarve'pi vismitāḥ |
kimetaditi bhāṣanto vabhūvuḥ parisaṃkitāḥ || 11.26{26} ||
tataste jñātayaḥ sarve vijñāya tasya lakṣaṇam |
saṃmīlya tamupāmaṃtrya gṛhasthaḥ pratiharṣitaḥ || 11.27{27} ||
tasya jātimahaṃ kṛtvā punarevamavocata |
bhadanto dārakasyāsya nāmadheyaṃ pramāṇataḥ || 11.28{28} ||
(rm 136)
yathāyogyaprasiddhena karttumarhatha sāṃpratam |
iti tenoditaṃ śrutvā sarve te jñātayastataḥ || 11.29{29} ||
saṃmīlya saṃmataṃ kṛtvā taṃ gṛhasthaṃ samabruvan |
yadayaṃ te suto vālo jātamātraḥ svakau bhujau || 11.30{30} ||
paśyanāliṃgyā cuṃvitva mudā caivaṃ prabhāṣate |
tadayaṃ dārako nāmnā hastaka iti viśrutaḥ || 11.31{31} ||
bhavatu sarvalokeṣu suciraṃ cāpi jīvatu |
tatheti pratinaṃditvā gṛhastho'sau pramoditaḥ || 11.32{32} ||
tena nāmnā prasiddhena prathayāmāsa taṃ sutam |
tato'sau dārakastena pitrā putrābhinaṃditaḥ |
upanyastopadhātrīṣu dhāraṇapratipālane || 11.33{33} ||
tatastāsāṃ prayatnaiḥ sa pathyopacāraṇaiḥ kramāt |
dine dine pravṛddho'bhūdhradasthamiva paṃkajam || 11.34{34} ||
evaṃ sa hastakastābhiḥ pālyamānaḥ pravarddhitaḥ |
kumāraḥ sakhibhiḥ sārddhaṃ krīḍāsthānaṃ gato'ramat || 11.35{35} ||
tatra teṣāṃ sakhīnāṃ ca hastako'sau puraḥsthitaḥ |
hastāvāliṃgya saṃrakṣya cuṃvitvaivamabhāṣata || 11.36{36} ||
aho vatādya me hastau sucireṇa samudbhavau |
aho labdhāvimau hastau sucireṇa mayādhunā || 11.37{37} ||
bhavanto'rhatsu mā cittaṃ dūṣayata kadācana |
kharāṃ vācaṃ ca mārhatsu niścārayata keṣu cit || 11.38{38} ||
iti saṃrakṣitau hastau tenaivaṃ ca prabhāṣitam |
dṛṣṭvā śrutvābhavan sarve sakhāyātīvavismitāḥ || 11.39{39} ||
tataśca janakenāsau lipiśālāniveśitaḥ |
guruṃ samyagupāśritya vidyāpāraṃ yayau laghu || 11.40{40} ||
tatra teṣāṃ guruṇāṃ ca hastako'sau puraḥsthitaḥ |
hastau dṛṣṭvā samāliṃgya cuṃvitvā caivamabravīt || 11.41{41} ||
aho vatādya me hastau sucireṇa samudbhavau |
aho labdhavimau hastau sucireṇa mayādhunā || 11.42{42} ||
bhavanto'rhatsu mā cittaṃ dūṣayata kadā cana |
kharāṃ vācaṃ ca mārhatsu niścārayata keṣu cit || 11.43{43} ||
iti dṛṣṭvā ca saṃśrutya sarve te guravo'pi ca |
sarve śiṣya gaṇāścāpi vabhūvurvismayānvitāḥ |
yasmiṃśca samaye tatra deśe bhayaupakrame |
(rm 137)
janakāyaḥ samudbhrānto'gopāyatsvasvabhāṇḍakam |
tadāsau hastako'pyevaṃ samudbhrāntaḥ svakau bhujau |
saṃbhujya gopayitvaivaṃ janakāyamabhāṣata |
aho vatādya me hastau sucireṇa samudbhavau |
aho labdhāvimau hastau sucireṇa mayādhunā |
bhavanto'rhatsu mā cittaṃ dūṣayata kadā cana |
kharāṃ vācaiva mārhatsu niścārayata keṣu cit |
evaṃ hastau susaṃrakṣya tenaivaṃ ca prabhāṣitam |
dṛṣṭvā śrutvā ca te sarve lokā āsan suvismitāḥ || 11.44{44} ||
tato'sau hastako'nyasmiṃ divase sakhibhiḥ saha |
jetavanaṃ mahodyānaṃ jināśrame upāyayau || 11.45{45} ||
tatrādrākṣītmahābuddhaṃ bhagavantaṃ sabhāsthitam |
dvātriṃśallakṣaṇāśītivyaṃjanaiḥ parimaṃḍitam || 11.46{46} ||
vyāmaprabhāsamuddīptaṃ śatasūryādhikaprabham |
śāntendriyaṃ subhadrāṃgaṃ saumyakāntaṃ manoharam || 11.47{47} ||
dṛṣṭvāsau suprasannātmā sahasā samupāgataḥ |
pādau tasya munernatvā dharmaṃ śrotuṃ puro'viśat || 11.48{48} ||
tato'sau bhagavān tasya dṛṣṭvāśayaṃ viśodhitam |
ādimadhyāntakalyāṇaṃ dideśa dharmamuttamam || 11.49{49} ||
taṃ sugatoditaṃ dharmamāryyasatyasamanvitam |
śrutvāsau hastako vijñaḥ satkāyadṛṣṭiparvatam || 11.50{50} ||
saṃbhidya jñānavajreṇa viṃśatiśikharodgatam |
śrotāpattiphalaṃ prāpto dṛṣṭasatyo'bhavattataḥ || 11.51{51} ||
saṃbuddhasugataṃ natvā svagehaṃ sahasā yayau || 11.52{52} ||
tatraitatsarvavṛttāṃtaṃ pitrorājñāya harṣitaḥ |
pravrajyāsādhane'nujñāṃ pitarau pratyayācata || 11.53{53} ||
cittaṃ me rocate tāta pravrajyāvratasādhane |
tadanujñāṃ pradattaṃ me hitaṃ mayi yadīchatha || 11.54{54} ||
iti putroditaṃ śrutvātmajaṃ taṃ pitāvadat |
mā kṛthāḥ sahasā putra pravrajyāsādhane manaḥ || 11.55{55} ||
pravrajyā duṣkaraṃ sthānaṃ tadanyadvratamācara |
pravrajyāgrahaṇaṃ vṛddhe daridrite suśobhite || 11.56{56} ||
tvaṃ ca putra kumāro'si daharo'tisukomalaḥ |
kiṃcidduḥkhe'nabhijñāya tatpravrajyāṃ careḥ katham || 11.57{57} ||
tadyāvadyauvanaṃ dehaṃ yāvatsaṃpacca te gṛhe |
tāvadgehe sukhaṃ bhuktvā gṛhī dharmaṃ samācara || 11.58{58} ||
yadā vṛddho daridro ca pravrajyāyāṃ tadā cara |
(rm 138)
yāvadyuvā samṛddhaśca tāvattvaṃ pravrajeḥ katham || 11.59{59} ||
saṃpattikṣaṇayauvanyametanmārasya gocaram |
tadetasmiṃ kathaṃ putra pravrajyāvratamācareḥ || 11.60{60} ||
ityetadduṣkaraṃ matvā pravrajyācaraṇe manaḥ |
yatnenāpi vinivārya kuladharme samāśraya || 11.61{61} ||
tataste maṃgalaṃ nityamihāmutrāpi sarvataḥ |
yaśodharmasukhānyevaṃ sadgatiṃ samavāpnuyāḥ || 11.62{62} ||
iti vākyaṃ pituḥ śrutvā hastako'sau viśaṃkitaḥ |
pitarau bodhayan dharme punarevamabhāṣata || 11.63{63} ||
satyametattathā tāta tvayaivaṃ satyamucyate |
atrāpi śrūyatāṃ tāta mamābhiprāyamucyatām || 11.64{64} ||
anityaṃ khalu saṃsāraṃ jīvitaṃ cāpi caṃcaram |
kṣaṇadhvaṃsi śarīraṃ ca saṃpaccāpi ghanopamā || 11.65{65} ||
evaṃ dṛṣṭvā ha tātāhaṃ saṃsārasukhaniḥspṛhaḥ |
pravrajyāvratamādhāya prāptumichāmi sadgatim || 11.66{66} ||
api ca janma mānuṣyaṃ dullabhaṃ bhavacāriṇām |
tatrāpi maraṇaṃ nityaṃ purataḥ sarvasaṃmukham || 11.67{67} ||
mānuṣyaṃ labhyamāne hi kuto dharmānucāritam |
vinā dharmānucāreṇa sadgatiṃ kathamāpnuyām || 11.68{68} ||
saṃbuddho'pi sadā nātra saṃsthāpyati sasāṃghikaḥ |
ato'nyatrāpi yāyādvā nirvṛtiṃ cāvrajetkhalu || 11.69{69} ||
saṃbuddhe nirvṛtiṃ yāte saddharma lapsyate kutaḥ |
saddharmāntargate loke māracaryyā samācaret || 11.70{70} ||
māracaryyāsamākrānte sarvatra bhuvaneṣvapi |
sarve satvāśca kāmārttā bhaveyuḥ kleśabhāginaḥ || 11.71{71} ||
tadā dharmānucāreṣu buddhiḥ kasya kathaṃ caret |
pravrajyāsaṃvaraṃ sthātuṃ kaḥ samartho bhavettadā || 11.72{72} ||
na ca saṃśuddhyate cittaṃ pravrajyāsaṃvaraṃ vinā |
aviśodhitacitte hi naiva yāyācchivāṃ gatim || 11.73{73} ||
taccittapariśuddhyarthaṃ nirvṛte ca yadāptaye |
pravrajyāvratamādātumichāmi tāta sāṃpratam || 11.74{74} ||
yadatra yadi me tāta hitaṃ kartuṃ samichasi |
pravrajyāsādhane'nujñāṃ dātuṃ me prārhasi drutam || 11.75{75} ||
iti putravacaḥ śrutva sa gṛhastho'nubodhitaḥ |
(rm 139)
tathetyabhyanumoditvā tadanujñāṃ vyasṛjata || 11.76{76} ||
tacchrutvā jananī cāsau dṛṣṭvā taṃ putramātmajam |
viyogaduḥkhasaṃkārtā rundantyevaṃ nyavārayat || 11.77{77} ||
hā putra kathamekānte māṃ vihāya kva yāsyasi |
sahasā putra mā kārṣī pravrajyācaritaṃ manaḥ || 11.78{78} ||
yāvajjīvāmyahaṃ jīva tāvanmā pravrajātmaje |
mṛtāyāṃ mayi putra tvaṃ pravrajasva yathechayā || 11.79{79} ||
acireṇa hi me mṛtyurbhaviṣyati na saṃśayaḥ |
tāvadeva gṛhe dharmaṃ kurvan bhuktvā sukhaṃ vasa || 11.80{80} ||
iti māturvacaḥ śrutvā putro'sau paricoditaḥ |
mātaraṃ tāṃ praṇatvaivaṃ bodhayitumabhāṣata || 11.81{81} ||
yattvayāmve rudantyevaṃ mṛtyunāmātra kīrttyate |
tena me preritaṃ cittaṃ pravrajyāvratadhāraṇe || 11.82{82} ||
dhruvaṃ janmavatāṃ mṛtyuḥ saṃsāre parivarttinaḥ |
jāto na mriyate ko hi mṛtaḥ kaśca na jāyate || 11.83{83} ||
gṛhastho dharmakṛccāpi yātyevaṃ maraṇaṃ dhruvam |
brahmacārī vanastho'pi yātyevaṃ maraṇaṃ khalu || 11.84{84} ||
gṛhastho bahubhiryatnaiḥ prakṛtvā dravyasādhanam |
kṛcchreṇa sādhayan dharmaṃ mṛto yāti surālayam || 11.85{85} ||
yāvaddharmaphalaṃ svarge tāvadbhuktvā sukhaṃ vaset |
tato dharmaphale hīne svargāccyuto yatedadhaḥ || 11.86{86} ||
adholokeṣvavīcyādau narakeṣu bhramecciram |
vividhāni ca duḥkhāni bhuktvā jīvaṃ ciraṃ vaset || 11.87{87} ||
yāvanna jāyate tasya puṇyānumodanā matau |
tāvatkarmajaduḥkhāni prabhuktvā narake caret || 11.88{88} ||
yadā tu jāyate tasya triratnasmaraṇe matiḥ |
tadā tatsmṛtipuṇyena dharme'numodyate matiḥ || 11.89{89} ||
yadā puṇyānurāgeṇa saṃbuddhaśaraṇaṃ vrajet |
tadā taṃ sugataṃ dṛṣṭvā dharmākaraiḥ parispṛśet || 11.90{90} ||
sugate dṛkprabhāspṛṣṭe pāpaduḥkhairvimocitaḥ |
narakebhyaḥ samuttīrya prayāyātsugatālayam || 11.91{91} ||
tatrāpi yadi dharmeṣu carannityamataṃdritaḥ |
kramādbodhicarīṃ prāpya prayāyātparamāṃ gatim || 11.92{92} ||
evaṃ māta gṛhastho hi kṛtvāpi dharmamādarāt |
(rm 140)
sukhaduḥkhāni saṃbhuktvā saṃsāre carate bhraman || 11.93{93} ||
kathaṃ citsucireṇaivaṃ sugatasya prasādataḥ |
bhavacārakanirmuktaṃ prayāyātparamāṃ gatim || 11.94{94} ||
pravrajyārhatpadaṃ prāpya prāptumichāmi nirvṛtim |
tanmamātra tvayā māta naiva kāryā nivāraṇā || 11.95{95} ||
yadi puṇye'sti te vāṃchā tadanujñāśu dīyatām |
yadi na dīyate'nujñā tadapi nau viyogatā || 11.96{96} ||
akasmātmṛtyurāgatya bhaviṣyati purāpi nau |
tadā te kiṃ kariṣyāmi kiṃ mamāpi kariṣyasi || 11.97{97} ||
sadānuśocanāmeva kṛtvā jīvaṃ tyajevahi |
iti mātarviditvā tvaṃ tadanujñāṃ pradehi me || 11.98{98} ||
vighnatāṃ mā kṛthā hyatra dharme bhavānumodinī |
tathā cecchubhatā nityamihāmutrāpi te bhavet || 11.99{99} ||
ahaṃ ca saugatiṃ caryāṃ kṛtvā yāsyāmi nirvṛtīm |
iti putroditaṃ śrutvā matāsau paribodhitā || 11.100{100} ||
tathānumodanāṃ kṛtvābhavattūṣṇīvyavasthitā || 11.101{1} ||
athāsau hastako vijñaḥ pitrormatvābhisaṃmatam |
tayoḥ pādān praṇatvaivaṃ sahasā niryayau gṛhāt || 11.102{2} ||
tato jñātigaṇān sarvān vandhumitrasuhṛjjanān |
sarvānatithivargāṃśca vāṃchitārthaiḥ pratoṣayan || 11.103{3} ||
santarpya bodhayitvā ca sahasā saṃpraharṣitaḥ |
jetavanaṃ pragatvā sa vihāraṃ samupāviśat || 11.104{4} ||
tatrasthaṃ sugataṃ dṛṣṭvā sahasā samupāyataḥ |
kṛtāṃjalipuṭo natvā pravrajyāṃ samayācata || 11.105{5} ||
namaste bhagavannātha bhavatāṃ śaraṇaṃ vraje |
tatpravrajyāṃ vrataṃ dehi cariṣye brahmasaṃvaram || 11.106{6} ||
iti tenārthite buddho bhagavān tamabhāṣata |
ehi bhikṣo kumārātra cara brahmavrataṃ varam || 11.107{7} ||
ityādiśya munīndro'sau pāṇinā tacchiraḥ spṛśan |
samyakpravrājayitvāśu svasaṃghe taṃ samagrahīt || 11.108{8} ||
ehītyādiṣṭamātre'pi munīndreṇa sa hastakaḥ |
muṇḍitaḥ khiṣkhirīpātrahasto'bhūccīvarāvṛtaḥ || 11.109{9} ||
kramācchikṣā samāsādya prāpyopasaṃpado'pi ca |
vyāyaccha ghaṭamānaśca samādhiṣu samāhitaḥ || 11.110{10} ||
tadidaṃ paṃcagaṃḍaṃ ca bhavacakraṃ calācalam |
viditvā sarvasaṃskāragatiścāpi vighātinī || 11.111{11} ||
bhindan sarvamavidyāṅgaṃ prāpya vidyāgaṇāḥ śivāḥ |
(rm 141)
pratisaṃvidguṇāḥ prāpya jitvā kleśagaṇāṃ yatiḥ || 11.112{12} ||
sākṣādarhatpadaṃ prāptaḥ vītarāgo jiteṃdriyaḥ |
samaloṣṭahiraṇyaśca vāsīcaṃdanasaṃnibhaḥ || 11.113{13} ||
ākāśasamacittāṃgo nirvikalpo niraṃjanaḥ |
saṃsārabhogyasatkāralābhalobhaparāṅmukhaḥ || 11.114{14} ||
paṃcābhijñāpadaprāptaścaturbrahmavihārakaḥ |
sadevāsuramartyānāṃ traidhātukanivāsinām || 11.115{15} ||
pūjyo mānyo'bhivaṃdyo'bhūddhastako'rhaṃ yatiḥ sudhīḥ |
sahasā kleśagaṇāñjitvā sākṣādarhan bhavatyapi || 11.116{16} ||
tataste bhikṣavaḥ sarve dṛṣṭvā taṃ dārakaṃ yatim |
vismitāḥ śrīghanaṃ natvā paprachustasya karmatām || 11.117{17} ||
tadadya bhagavacchāstaḥ sarvasatvānubodhaye |
yadanena kṛtaṃ karma tatsamādeṣṭumarhati || 11.118{18} ||
iti te bhikṣubhiḥ pṛṣṭe bhagavān sa munīśvaraḥ |
tān svaśiṣyān yatīn bhikṣūn samāmaṃtryābravīttathā || 11.119{19} ||
śṛṇudhvaṃ bhikṣavaḥ sarve yūyaṃ hi vismayānvitāḥ |
yadanena kṛtaṃ karma tanmayā vaḥ pracakṣyatām || 11.120{20} ||
purāsīdbhagavān buddhaḥ kāśyapo nāma sarvavit |
ssarvatraidhātukādhīśaḥ sarvavidyādhipo jinaḥ || 11.121{21} ||
dharmarājo jagacchāstā tathāgato vināyakaḥ |
sarvadharmānusaṃbharttā saṃbodhisaṃprakāśitaḥ || 11.122{22} ||
sasāṃghiko munīndro'sau vārāṇasīmupāśritaḥ |
sarvasatvahitārthena tasthau dharmaṃ samādiśat || 11.123{23} ||
tasya saddharmarājasya kāśyapasya jagadguroḥ |
śāsane dvau yatī bhikṣū saṃśītikau vabhūvatuḥ || 11.124{24} ||
tayoreko mahādhīro vinīto'rhaṃ vahuśrutaḥ |
dvitīyo'lpaśrutaḥ bhikṣuḥ pṛthagjanasamānikaḥ || 11.125{25} ||
yo'sāvarhanmahāvijño vahuśruto vicakṣaṇaḥ |
sa jñāto'rhanmahāpuṇyaḥ sarvalokaiḥ prapūjitaḥ || 11.126{26} ||
yo'bhūccālpaśruto bhikṣuḥ saṃśītikaḥ pṛthagjanaḥ |
sa kena citpūjito naivaṃ yathā so'rhaṃ yatiḥ sudhīḥ || 11.127{27} ||
yadā so'rhanmahābhijño yatra yatra nimaṃtritaḥ |
(rm 142)
tenaiva sarvataḥ paścācchramanena sahācarat || 11.128{28} ||
tathānyasmin dine so'rhaṃ dātrānyena nimaṃtritaḥ |
gantukāmastadā tatra paścācchramaṇamaicchata || 11.129{29} ||
tadāgamanamanviṣya naiva pratyalabhatkvacit |
tatastaddarśanābhāvādanyamāhūya bhikṣukam || 11.130{30} ||
taṃ paścācchramaṇaṃ kṛtvā dāturgṛhamupācarat |
tadanantaramevāsau tatrāśrame upāgataḥ || 11.131{31} ||
tamarhantamapaśyan sa tasthau tūṣṇī nirāśitaḥ |
tathaivaṃ taṃ niṣīdantaṃ dṛṣṭvā te bhikṣavo narāḥ || 11.132{32} ||
tasyauddhatyamanaḥ kartumevamūcuḥ parasparam |
bhadanta paśyatādyāyaṃ tenāhato na nīyate || 11.133{33} ||
anyo'dya nīyate paścācchramaṇaḥ kena hetunā |
iti tairbhikṣubhiḥ proktaṃ śrutvāsau pratibheditaḥ |
tasyārhato'ntike cittaṃ praduṣyābhyaśapattathā || 11.134{34} ||
yadadya māṃ parityajya samāhūyānyato harāt |
gachata bhojanaṃ bhoktuṃ tadahasto bhavatvasau || 11.135{35} ||
tadāsau bhojanaṃ bhuktvā tenaivaṃ bhikṣuṇā saha |
svāśramaṃ samupāyātastamadrākṣīdruṣākulam || 11.136{36} ||
ityevaṃ śapitaṃ vāpi matvā so'rhaṃ mahāmatiḥ |
dṛṣṭvā taṃ kṛpayā dṛṣṭyā tūṣṇībhūtvā vyaciṃtayat || 11.137{37} ||
aho ayaṃ mahāmūḍha īdṛgmayi prabhāṣate |
etatkarmaphalaṃ ghoraṃ kathaṃ bhokṣyati nārake || 11.138{38} ||
yadayaṃ mama doṣeṇa krodhenaivaṃ khalīkṛtaḥ |
tadetatkarmavaipākyaṃ mayaiva paripācyatām || 11.139{39} ||
ityevaṃ manasā dhyātvā sa mahārhaṃ kṛpānidhiḥ |
tatpāpapariśuddhyarthaṃ samādhiṃ vidadhe ciram || 11.140{40} ||
tataḥ so'pyayatirbhikṣurdṛṣṭvā taṃ dhyānasaṃsthitam |
paścāttāpāgnisaṃtaptestatpādapraṇato'vadat || 11.141{41} ||
bhadanta yanmayā rauṣyātkrodhābhibhūtacetasā |
procitaṃ pātakaṃ ghoraṃ tadbhavān kṣantumarhati || 11.142{42} ||
yanmayā duṣṭacittena bhavato'pyupabhāṣyate |
(rm 143)
tadatyayaṃ bhavāñchāstaḥ pratihartuṃ samarhati || 11.143{43} ||
adyāgreṇa sadā cāpi bhavatāṃ śaraṇaṃ vraje |
tanme yadaparādhatvaṃ tatsarvaṃ kṣantumarhati || 11.144{43!} ||
punarnaitatkariṣye'hamakarttavyaṃ yadyatra hi |
avaktavyaṃ ca yatkiṃ cinna tadvakṣyāmi sarvathā || 11.145{44} ||
abhāvyaṃ cāpi yatkiṃcidbhāvayiṣye na tatpunaḥ |
yadeva taṃ prakarttavyaṃ tadeva hi karomyaham || 11.146{45} ||
vaktavyameva vakṣye'haṃ bhāvyameva prabhāvaye |
etatsarvaṃ samāsena samādeṣṭuṃ samarhati || 11.147{46} ||
bhavadājñāṃ śirodhṛtvā satyamevaṃ caretsadā || 11.148{47!} ||
iti tenārthitaṃ śrutvā sa samādheḥ samutthitaḥ |
taṃ bhikṣuṃ kṛpayā dṛṣṭvā paśyannevaṃ samabravīt || 11.149{48} ||
śṛṇu vatsa samādhāya tvāmatrāhaṃ prabodhaye |
yathā me gaditaṃ śrutvā tathā kuru samāhitaḥ || 11.150{49} ||
prāṇihiṃsā na karttavyā tathādattapratigraham |
strīṇāṃ saṃgānurāgaṃ ca akarttavyaṃ kadā cana || 11.151{50} ||
mṛṣāvādaṃ na vaktavyaṃ paiśūnyavacanaṃ tathā |
saṃbhinnapralāpaṃ ca pāruṣyaṃ ca kadā cana || 11.152{51} ||
mithyādṛṣṭirabhidhyā ca vyāpādaṃ ca tathā sadā |
bhikṣubhirmanasā naiva ciṃtanīyā kadā cana || 11.153{52} ||
etāni daśapāpānāṃ mūlāni duḥkhadāni hi |
tasmādevaṃ parijñāya tyaktavyāni sukhārthibhiḥ || 11.154{53} ||
punaranyacchṛṇuṣvātra vakṣye dāruṇapātakam |
saddharmāṇāṃ pratikṣepaṃ kartavyaṃ na kadā cana || 11.155{54} ||
tathā vāryāpavādatvaṃ vaktavyaṃ na kadā cana |
paṃcānaṃtaryapāpāni naiva kāryāṇi kena cit || 11.156{55} ||
yāni śrutvāpi dṛṣṭvāpi bhāvayitvānumodya ca |
patanto narakeṣveva kleśāndhāni ca santi hi || 11.157{56} ||
tasmādetāni karmāṇi mānasāpi ca mā kṛthā |
varjanīyāni yatnena saddharmasukhavāṃchibhiḥ || 11.158{57} ||
karttavyaṃ tu sadā nityamāryasatyānubhāvitam |
āryāṣṭāṃgikamārge ca caritvā vratamādarāt || 11.159{58} ||
vratānāmapi sarveṣāmāryāṣṭāṃgamupoṣadham |
(rm 144)
pravaraṃ vratamākhyātaṃ sarvairapi munīśvaraiḥ || 11.160{59} ||
yasya puṇyānubhāvena pariśuddhatrimaṃḍalāḥ |
niḥśeṣapāpanirmuktāḥ prayāntyarhatpadaṃ drutam || 11.161{60} ||
evametatparijñāya vrataṃ kṛtvā samācaran |
tataste kramataḥ pāpaṃ niḥśeṣaṃ kṣiṇuyāddrutam || 11.162{61} ||
tataśca kramaśaḥ śikṣāḥ śikṣitvātra samāhitaḥ |
triratnaśaraṇaṃ kṛtvā brahmacaryyaṃ samācara || 11.163{62} ||
tatastvaṃ kramaśaścaivaṃ śodhayitvā trimaṃḍalam |
sarvakleśavinirmuktaḥ sākṣādarhan bhaviṣyasi || 11.164{63} ||
iti tenārhatādiṣṭaṃ śrutvāsau paribodhitaḥ |
tatheti ca parijñāya tadvrataṃ kartumaichata || 11.165{64} ||
tathā tasyārhataḥ śāstuḥ sadaivaṃ śaraṇaṃ gataḥ |
kramācchikṣāśca śikṣitvā tadupoṣadhamācarat || 11.166{65} ||
adhītya sakalā vidyā triratnasamupāśrayan |
sarvakleśān vinirjitya brahmacaryaṃ samācarat || 11.167{66} ||
bhikṣavo jñāyatāmeṣa yo'sau bhikṣuḥ pṛthagjanaḥ |
yadanenārhate śāstre śapitaṃ ruṣṭacetasā || 11.168{67} ||
tenāhasto vabhūvāyaṃ paṃcajanmaśatānyapi |
yaccānenārhataḥ śasturgatvā ca śaraṇaṃ sadā || 11.169{68} ||
adhītya sakalā vidyā brahmacaryaṃ samāśritam |
tenāyaṃ śāsanenātra pravrajyārhaṃ bhavatyapi || 11.170{69} ||
iti hi bhikṣavo matvā caritavyaṃ śubhe sadā |
śubhena sadgatiṃ yāṃti kṛṣṇena durgatiṃ sadā || 11.171{70} ||
miśritenāpi miśratvaṃ bhuṃjate sarvajaṃtavaḥ |
yenaiva yatkṛtaṃ karma tasyaiva karmaṇaḥ phalam || 11.172{71} ||
tenaiva bhujyate loke nānyena spṛśyate kvacit |
abhuktaṃ kṣīyate naiva karma kvāpi kadā cana || 11.173{72} ||
bhuktaṃ tu kṣīyate karma śubhaṃ vāpyaśubhaṃ tathā |
nāgnibhirdahyate kena vāyubhiśca na śuṣyate || 11.174{73} ||
klidyate nodakaiścāpi kṣīyate naiva bhūmiṣu |
na praṇaśyanti karmāṇi kalpakoṭiśatairapi || 11.175{74} ||
sāmagrīṃ prāpya kālaṃ ca phalanti prāṇināṃ khalu |
evametatprabhaṣitvā jinendro'sau mahāmuniḥ || 11.176{75} ||
bodhayan sakalāṃllokān samādhiṃ vidadhe tataḥ |
iti śāstrā samādiṣṭaṃ śrutvā sarve ca sāṃghikāḥ || 11.177{76} ||
śubheṣveva sadā raktāḥ pracerire samāhitāḥ || 11.178{77} ||
ityevaṃ me samākhyātaṃ guruṇā śāṇavāsinā |
(rm 145)
tathaivaṃ te mayā khyātaṃ śrutvā caivaṃ śubhe cara || 11.179{78} ||
prajāśca śrāvayitvaivaṃ bodhayitvā prayatnataḥ |
saddharmācaraṇeṣvevaṃ preraṇīyā narādhipa || 11.180{79} ||
tataste maṃgalaṃ nityaṃ sarvatrāpi bhaveddhruvam |
kramādbodhipathaḥ prāpya saṃbodhimāpnuyāḥ khalu || 11.181{80} ||
iti tenopaguptena bhāṣitaṃ sa nareśvaraḥ |
śrutvā tatheti vaṃditvā prābhyanandatsapārṣadaḥ || 11.182{81} ||
ye śṛṇvantīdamevaṃ pramuditamanaso hastakākhyāvadānaṃ
ye cāpi śrāvayanti pratidinamaniśaṃ bodhicaryābhiraktāḥ |
te sarve kleśamuktāḥ suvimalamanaso bodhisatvā guṇāḍhyā
bhuktvā saukhyaṃ prakāmaṃ munivaranilayaṃ saṃprayāṃti pramodāḥ || 11.183{82} ||

++ iti śriratnamālāyāṃ hastakāvadānaṃ nāma samāptam ++


(rm 146)
xii sārthavāhāvadāna
athāśoko mahīpālaḥ kṛtāṃjalipuṭo mudā |
upaguptaṃ guruṃ natvā punarevamabhāṣata || 12.1{1} ||
bhadanta śrotumichāmi punaranyatsubhāṣitam |
yathā te guruṇādiṣṭaṃ tathā me khyātumarhasi || 12.2{2} ||
iti tena narendreṇa pṛṣṭe'sau sugatātmajaḥ |
upagupto yatiścaivaṃ taṃ nṛpaṃ pratyabhāṣata || 12.3{3} ||
śṛṇu rājan samādhāya yathā me guruṇoditam |
tathāhaṃ te pravakṣyāmi tacchrutvā mudito bhava || 12.4{4} ||
puraikasamaye caivaṃ śākyasiṃho jagadguruḥ |
sarvajñaḥ sugataḥ śāstā sarvadharmānudeśakaḥ || 12.5{5} ||
sarvavidyādhipo nāthastraidhātukavināyakaḥ |
dharmarājo munīndro'sau sarvasatvahitaṃkaraḥ || 12.6{6} ||
śrāvastyāṃ vahirudyāne jetāhvaye mahāvane |
anāthapiṇḍadārāme vihāre saugatāśrame || 12.7{7} ||
śrāvakairbhikṣubhiḥ sārddhamupāsakaiśca cailakaiḥ |
bodhisatvagaṇaiścāpi tathānyaiśca maharṣibhiḥ || 12.8{8} ||
vyaharatsatvahitārthena dharmāmṛtaiḥ pravarṣayan |
tatsaddharmāmṛtaṃ pātuṃ prāyayurbodhivāṃchinaḥ || 12.9{9} ||
lokapālagaṇāḥ sarve svasvasainyagaṇaiḥ saha |
brāhmaṇāḥ kṣatriyāścāpi vaiśyāḥ śūdrāśca maṃtriṇaḥ || 12.10{10} ||
amātyā dhaninaḥ paurāḥ sādhavaśca mahājanāḥ |
vaṇijaḥ sārthavāhāśca tathānye sarvajātikāḥ || 12.11{11} ||
sarvadigbhyaḥ samāgatya sarve te samupāgatāḥ |
saṃbuddhaṃ śrīghanaṃ natvā pūjayitvā samaṃtataḥ || 12.12{12} ||
parivṛtya puraskṛtya saddharmaśravaṇārthinaḥ |
kṛtāṃjalipuṭo dṛṣṭvā saṃtasthuḥ saṃmukhānatāḥ || 12.13{13} ||
athāsau bhagavān dṛṣṭvā tān sarvān samupāśritān |
ādimadhyāṃtakalyāṇaṃ dideśa dharmamuttamam || 12.14{14} ||
tatsaddharmāmṛtaṃ pītvā sarve lokādhipādayaḥ |
harṣitāste'numodanto nananduḥ paribodhitāḥ || 12.15{15} ||
tadaiva samaye tasyāṃ śrāvastyāṃ puri saṃsthitaḥ |
(rm 147)
āsītsāṃyātrikotsāhī sārthavāho mahodyamī || 12.16{16} ||
svakuladharmabhṛdvīraḥ svakulavṛttimānitaḥ |
yaśodharmārthakāmāptyai samudraṃ gantumaichata || 12.17{17} ||
tato'sau svāṃ priyāṃ bhāryāṃ bodhayitvā prayatnataḥ |
svasahāyāśca tāṃ sarvāṃ samāhūya samabravīt || 12.18{18} ||
bhavaṃto śrūyatāṃ sarve yadabhilaṣitaṃ mama |
atrāsti yadi vo vāṃchā tanme svāgantumarhatha || 12.19{19} ||
ahaṃ ratnākaraṃ gantumiche ratnasamṛddhaye |
tatprāyāta mayā sārddhaṃ yadi saṃpattimichatha || 12.20{20} ||
iti tenoditaṃ śrutvā sarve te ratnalobhitāḥ |
sahasā gaṃtumichante ratnākare mahodadhau || 12.21{21} ||
tato'sau sārthabhṛcchīghraṃ sarve sārthagaṇaiḥ saha |
svastyayanavidhiṃ kṛtvā pratasthe saṃpramoditaḥ || 12.22{22} ||
tataḥ kramādvyatikramya grāmāraṇyavanāni ca |
sarve te pracaranto vai tīraṃ prāpurmahodadheḥ || 12.23{23} ||
tato bhāṃḍāni naukāyāmāropyāruhya ca svayam |
karṇadhāraprayatnaiste jagāhire'mbudhiṃ kramāt || 12.24{24} ||
tataḥ kramātsamullaṃghya bahudvīpāntarāṇi te |
ratnākaraṃ samāsādya ratnāni samasādhayan || 12.25{25} ||
tataste bahuratnāni samāsādya pramoditāḥ |
tataḥ pratyāgatā abdhestīrāntikaṃ samāyayuḥ || 12.26{26} ||
tatra tatkarmadoṣeṇa kālavātairvighaṭṭitam |
bhidyate vahanaṃ teṣāṃ hṛdayaiḥ sā sahāśu tat || 12.27{27} ||
tadā te vaṇijaḥ sarve niḥprayatnapratikriyā |
sarvabhāṇḍaiḥ sahāgāḍhe nipeturduṣkare'mbudhau || 12.28{28} ||
kecitphalakamālamvya samuttīrṇāḥ svavīryataḥ |
kecidvāhuvalādhānaṃ samuttīrya sthalaṃ yayuḥ || 12.29{29} ||
kecicca vilayaṃ prāptā nirālamvyanirāśrayāḥ |
utpravanto nimajjantaḥ kheditā nidhanaṃ yayuḥ || 12.30{30} ||
tataste vaṇijaḥ sarve ye viśiṣṭā viṣāditāḥ |
te yaśodhanavibhraṣṭāḥ śūnyahastā gṛhaṃ yayuḥ || 12.31{31} ||
tathāsau sārthavāho'pi gṛhaṃ prāpto viṣāditaḥ |
etadvṛttaṃ svabhāryāyāḥ kathitvaivaṃ vyacintayat || 12.32{32} ||
hā hā me mandabhāgyatvājjāyate mīdṛśī vipat |
(rm 148)
atrāhaṃ kiṃ kariṣyāmi yato bhāgyaṃ na vidyate || 12.33{33} ||
bhāgyavān puruṣo dhīro bhāgyavān kuśalī sudhīḥ |
bhāgyena labhyate saṃpadyaśaḥ saukhyaguṇānvitaḥ || 12.34{34} ||
dhīḥ śrīlakṣmīyaśoratnaṃ valaṃ saṃpatkṣaṇādayaḥ |
etadādyā guṇāḥ sarve śobhanti bhāgyamāśritāḥ || 12.35{35} ||
nihīnabhāgyavṛttestu puruṣasyāpi saṃmate |
etadādyādiguṇāḥ sarve na prasiddhyaṃti sarvataḥ || 12.36{36} ||
tadatra kiṃ kariṣye'haṃ kva yāsyāmi dhanāptaye |
dhanahīnā na śobhante gṛhasthāḥ sādhavo'pi hi || 12.37{37} ||
varaṃ prāṇaparityāgaṃ na tu dravyairvinā gṛhe |
paśuvannirasaṃ bhuktvā sthāsyāmyevaṃ nirutsavam || 12.38{38} ||
bhāgyena labhyate dravyaṃ bhāgyaṃ dharmeṇa labhyate |
dharme hi jāyate sveṣṭadevatāyāḥ prasādataḥ || 12.39{39} ||
taddharmabhāgyalabdhārthaṃ svakuladevatāṃ prati |
samyagārādhayitvaivaṃ yāyāṃ ratnākaraṃ punaḥ || 12.40{40} ||
manasīti viniścitya sa sārthavāhakaḥ punaḥ |
svakuladevatāṃ samyagārādhya samayācata || 12.41{41} ||
namaste devate nityaṃ bhaje'haṃ śaraṇaṃ gataḥ |
kṣaṃtavyaṃ me'parādhatvaṃ prasīda parameśvara || 12.42{42} ||
itthaṃ sā devatā samyagārādhya saṃpramoditā |
sarvasārthān samāhūya punarevamabhāṣata || 12.43{43} ||
bho bhavanto vijānīdhvaṃ vayaṃ sarve vaṇigjanāḥ |
tadatra bhavatāmagre kathyate me samīhitam || 12.44{44} ||
yadbhavanto vayaṃ sarve sārthavāhakulodbhavāḥ |
tattathaiva gṛhe sthitvā naiva śobhāṃ vrajemahi || 12.45{45} ||
tasmātsvakulavṛttisthaiḥ sarvairasmābhirudyataiḥ |
yaśodharmasukhāptyarthaṃ karttavyaṃ dhanasādhanam || 12.46{46} ||
dhanahīnā na śobhante gṛhasthā hi kuṭuṃvinaḥ |
sārthavāho'stu śobhante vahuratnārjanodyatāḥ || 12.47{47} ||
tadviṣādaṃ parityaktvā yaśoratnasukhāptaye |
dhairyyamālamvya sarvaiśca karttavyaṃ dhanasādhanam || 12.48{48} ||
dhanavān puruṣo lokaiḥ sarvatrāpi pramānyate |
dhanena sādhayeddharmaṃ dharmeṇa sadgatiṃ vrajet || 12.49{49} ||
tasmānnityaṃ mahotsāhaisñltyaktvālasyakuśīdatā |
arjanīyaṃ prayatnena dhanaṃ dānāya bhuktaye || 12.50{50} ||
dānaṃ vibhūṣaṇaṃ loke dānaṃ durgatinivāraṇam |
(rm 149)
dānaṃ svargasya sopānaṃ dānaṃ śāntikaraṃ sadā || 12.51{51} ||
asmāddānaṃ sadā kartuṃ bhoktuṃ saukhyaṃ yathechayā |
bhūyo ratnākaraṃ gaṃtumarhāmahe dhanārjane || 12.52{52} ||
tatra ratnāni saṃgṛhya svastipratyāgatā vayam |
yāvajjīvaṃ sukhaṃ bhuktvā dānaṃ kṛtvā vasemahi || 12.53{53} ||
yadi daivo vipattiḥ syātsamudre patitā vayam |
tīrtharāje mṛtāḥ sarve yāsyāmaḥ svargatiṃ dhruvam || 12.54{54} ||
yadi bhāgyādathāsmākaṃ devānāṃ ca prasādataḥ |
yātrāsiddhirbhavennūnaṃ yāvajjīvaṃ sukhaṃ tadā || 12.55{55} ||
asmādratnākare gantumiche ratnapralabdhaye |
tatprāyāta mayā sārddhaṃ yadi saṃpattimichatha || 12.56{56} ||
iti tasyoditaṃ śrutvā te sarve vaṇijo mudā |
tathā tena sahaichānta gantuṃ ratnākaraiḥ punaḥ || 12.57{57} ||
tato'sau sārthabhṛcchīghraṃ dhṛtvā svastyayanaṃ mudā |
sarve sārthagaṇaiḥ sārddhaṃ pratasthe svapurādvahi || 12.58{58} ||
tataḥ kramādvyatikramya grāmāraṇyavanāni ca |
pracarantaśca te sarve tīraṃ prāpurmahodadheḥ || 12.59{59} ||
tatra sarve'pi te sārthā bhāṇḍāni vahane kramāt |
āropya sahasārūḍhā jagāhire mahāmbudhau || 12.60{60} ||
tataḥ kramātsamullaṃghya vahudvīpāntarāṇi te |
ratnākaraṃ samāsādya ratnāni samasādhayan || 12.61{61} ||
tatra te vahuratnāni samāgṛhya praharṣitāḥ |
tataḥ pratyāgatāścābdhestīrāntikamupāyayuḥ || 12.62{62} ||
tatastaddaivayogena kālikāvātaghaṭṭitā |
sā naukā bhidyate caivaṃ teṣāṃ vai hṛdayaiḥ saha || 12.63{63} ||
tadā te vaṇijaḥ sarve niḥprayatnapratikriyā |
sarvabhāṇḍaiḥ sahāgāḍhe nyapatan duṣṭare'mbudhau || 12.64{64} ||
tatra te vilayaṃ prāptā nirālaṃvā nirāśrayāḥ |
unmajjanto nimajjantaḥ preritāstīramūrmibhiḥ || 12.65{65} ||
kecitphalakamāśritya samuttīryya svavīryyataḥ |
kecidvāhuvalādhānaiḥ samuttīryya sthalaṃ yayuḥ || 12.66{66} ||
kecinnihīnavīryyāṅgā hanyamānā mahormibhiḥ |
utplavantā nimajjantaḥ kheditā nidhanaṃ yayuḥ || 12.67{67} ||
tatasteṣāṃ ca sārthānāṃ ye'vaśiṣṭā sujīvinaḥ |
(rm 150)
yaśoratnaviyuktāste śūnyahastā gṛhaṃ yayuḥ || 12.68{68} ||
tathāsau sārthavāho'pi gṛhaṃ gatvā viṣāditaḥ |
etadvṛttaṃ svavaṃdhūnāṃ kathitvaivamabhāṣata || 12.69{69} ||
hā mayā kiṃ kṛtaṃ pāpaṃ yenedrigjāyate vipat |
dvidhāpi siddhyate naiva yato bhāgyaṃ na vidyate || 12.70{70} ||
pāpairna siddhyate karma atra yatnasya kiṃ valaiḥ |
dharmeṇa siddhyate karma tasmāddharmo valottamaḥ || 12.71{71} ||
tasmāddharmaṃ puraskṛtya karttavyaṃ dravyasādhanam |
dravyena sādhayetkarma sadgatiṃ samavāpnuyāḥ || 12.72{72} ||
karma tu siddhyate samyakkulavṛttiṃ ca cāriṇām |
kulavṛttiṃ parityajya yo'nyavṛttau caretkudhīḥ || 12.73{73} ||
sa ihāpi paribhraṣṭaḥ paratra durgatiṃ vrajet |
yenaivaṃ sadgatiprāptistadaiva karmma sādhayet || 12.74{74} ||
yato'pi durgatiṃ prāptistatkarma tarhitaṃ budhaiḥ |
tadatra kiṃ kariṣye'haṃ kva yāsyāmyarthamarjitum |
arthaṃ vinā kathaṃ dharmaṃ karttavyaṃ hi kuṭuṃvibhiḥ || 12.75{75} ||
vinā dharmaṃ hi saṃsāre janma jīvaṃ ca nisphalam |
kiṃ te na pauruṣeṇāpi yena dharmaṃ na sādhyate || 12.76{76} ||
dhigdhigme janma saṃsāre jīvituṃ na tathotsahe |
