varaṃ prāṇaparityāgaṃ na tvevaṃ cirajīvitam || 12.77{77} ||
avaśyaṃ jantubhirmṛtyurgantavyamekadhā dhruvam |
tato'tra martumichāmi na tvevamuṣituṃ gṛhe || 12.78{(78)} ||
evaṃjīvo na śobhe'haṃ dhigpravādāgnidagdhitaḥ |
kathaṃ me śāmyate duḥkhaṃ yenāhaṃ parikheditaḥ || 12.79{(78)} ||
kiṃ karttavyaṃ kva gantavyaṃ yatnaṃ cātra na vidyate |
sarvathāhaṃ vinaṣṭo'smi ko me'sti rakṣako janaḥ || 12.80{79} ||
yaśodharmaparibhraṣṭo vrajeyaṃ durgatiṃ khalu |
tadāhaṃ kiṃ kariṣyāmi dharmayaśaḥsukhānvitaḥ || 12.81{80} ||
mānuṣye labhyamāne hi kuto dharmānusādhanam |
vinā dharmmaṃ yaśodravyaḥ kiṃ saukhyaṃ gṛhavāsinām || 12.82{81} ||
vinā dharmayaśodravyairyo gṛhasthaḥ paśuḥ khalu |
dharmeṇa sādhayeddravyaṃ yaśobhāgyasukhāptaye || 12.83{82} ||
dharmāddhi jāyate puṇyaṃ dānaśīlakṣamādibhiḥ |
puṇyenaiva pralabhyante caturvargā api dhruvam || 12.84{83} ||
(rm 151)
tadeteṣāṃ mamaiko'pi na vidyate kathaṃ cana |
hā hato'smi svadaivena kiṃ karomi vikheditaḥ || 12.85{84} ||
sarvathāhaṃ vinaṣṭo'smi namo daivāya karmaṇe |
evaṃ teṣāṃ svavaṃdhūnāṃ vilapītvā sa sārthapaḥ || 12.86{85} ||
tasthau cintākulībhūto dhanārjananirāśrayā |
evaṃ vyavasthitaṃ dṛṣṭvā taṃ te sarve'pi vāṃdhavāḥ || 12.87{86} ||
datvāśvāsaṃ vinodāya bodhayante dhruvaṃ tathā |
sādho sārthapate satyaṃ tatsarvaṃ yattvayocyate || 12.88{87} ||
tadevaṃ dhairyyamālaṃvya prodyamasva gṛhe vasan |
sādho kutrāpi mā dhāva nirmāṇasadṛśaṃ phalam || 12.89{88} ||
yāvadevāmbhasā kumbhaḥ siṃdhāvandhau'vapūryate |
yadabhāvi na tadbhāvi bhāvi naiva tadanyathā || 12.90{89} ||
avaśyaṃ bhāvino bhāvā bhavaṃti sarvadehinām |
avaśyaṃ bhāvino bhāvā na bhaveyustathā yadi || 12.91{90} ||
pitrā tyaktaḥ kathaṃ rāmo rājyabhraṣṭo vanaṃ yayau |
tatrāpi vasatastasya bhāryā śītā pativratā || 12.92{91} ||
āhṛtya rakṣasā nītā kathaṃ laṃkāpure'vasat |
tato rāmeṇa vīryeṇa hatvāsau rāvaṇastataḥ || 12.93{92} ||
pratyānīya punaḥ śītā tyaktā bharttrā svayaṃ katham |
punarṛṣeḥ samānīya sabhāmadhye parikṣitā || 12.94{93} ||
tataḥ pṛthyā svayaṃ gṛhya sītānītā rasātalam |
tatastadvirahārttāsau rāmaḥ saṃsāranispṛhaḥ || 12.95{94} ||
nadyāṃ prāṇasvayaṃ tyaktvā janaṃ sārddhaṃ divaṃ yayau |
evamanye'pi rājāno yudhiṣṭhirā nṛpādayaḥ |
rājyabhraṣṭā vane gatvā vibhramuste'pi daivataḥ || 12.96{95} ||
evamanye'pi lokāśca sarvatraidhātukabhavāḥ |
uttamā adhamā madhyā saṃti te'pi svadaivataḥ || 12.97{96} ||
kecidgṛhe mṛtāḥ kecijjāyamānā mṛtā api |
kecidvālye mṛtāḥ kecitkaumāratve mṛtāstathā || 12.98{97} ||
yauvane'pi mṛtāḥ kecinmṛtā vṛddhe vadanti me |
sarve'pyevaṃ mṛtā lokāḥ svasvadaivapramāṇataḥ || 12.99{98} ||
evaṃ devādayo lokāḥ sukhaduḥkhānuyoginaḥ |
svasvadaivānubhāvena ṣaḍgatiṣu caranti te || 12.100{99} ||
kecidvālye sukhaṃ bhuktvā yauvane duḥkhabhāginaḥ |
kecicca yauvane saukhyaṃ bhuktvā vṛddhe suduḥkhitāḥ || 12.101{100} ||
(rm 152)
kecicca duḥkhitā vālye yauvane saukhyabhāginaḥ |
vṛddhatvasya sukhībhūtā bhavaṃti te'pi hi daivataḥ || 12.102{1} ||
sarve'pi sukhamichanti duḥkhaṃ nechati kaścana |
tathā sukhāni saṃprāptuṃ yatnaṃ kurvantyanekaśaḥ || 12.103{2} ||
keṣāṃ citsiddhyate yatnaṃ keṣāṃ cidvā na siddhyati |
keṣāṃ cidvighnatāṃ yāti tatsarvaṃ daivayogataḥ || 12.104{3} ||
tathā ca rogiṇāṃ rogā vaidyopacāraṇairapi |
varddhyante naiva śāmyante tadapi daivayogataḥ || 12.105{4} ||
keṣāṃ cidrogiṇāṃ rogā vinā vaidyopacāraṇe |
śāmyaṃte yatnato cāpi tadapi daivayogataḥ || 12.106{5} ||
tathā caldmaṃtriṇāṃ maṃtrāḥ siddhyante yatnato laghuḥ |
keṣāṃ cidvighnatāṃ yānti tadapi daivakāraṇāt || 12.107{6} ||
tathaivaṃ vaṇijāṃ lābhoḥ siddhyante yatnato'pi ca |
keṣāṃ cinnaiva yatnena tadapi daivayogataḥ || 12.108{7} ||
tathā vīrā raṇe śatruṃ vinā yatnaṃ jayaṃtyapi |
kecittu ripunā yuddhe hanyamānā mṛtāḥ pare || 12.109{8} ||
kecitkṛtvā mahāyuddhaṃ hanyamānāḥ parasparam |
tyaktvā dehaṃ gatāḥ svargaṃ tadapi daivayogataḥ || 12.110{9} ||
evaṃ sāṃyātrikā ratnaṃ prāptuṃ ratnākaraṃ gatāḥ |
kecidratnāni saṃgṛhya svasti pratyāgatā gṛham || 12.111{10} ||
kecidratnamalabdhaṃ vai śūnyahastā gṛhāgatāḥ |
kecicca nidhanaṃ yātāstatraiva makarālaye || 12.112{11} ||
kecidyatnātsamuttīrya jīvanto gṛhamāgatāḥ |
kecicca jāṃgale caurairnihatā maraṇaṃ gatāḥ || 12.113{12} ||
kecicca jaṃtubhirmārge hiṃsyamāne mṛtā vane |
keṣāṃ citsiddhyate yātrā mahallābhasamanvitāḥ || 12.114{13} ||
keṣāṃ cidvighnatāṃ yānti tathāpi daivayogataḥ |
kaiścidgṛhe nidhiḥ prāptaḥ kaiściddeśāntare vane || 12.115{14} ||
kaiściddūre bhramadbhiśca naiva kiṃ citkva ciddhanam |
keṣāṃ cidvarddhate saṃpadyatnāyāsaṃ vinā laghu || 12.116{15} ||
keṣāṃ ciddhīyate saṃpadrakṣitāpi prayatnataḥ |
keṣāṃ citsusthirā saṃpatkeṣāṃ cidvala asthirā || 12.117{16} ||
keṣāṃ cijjāyate naiva nānopāyārjanairapi |
evaṃ nānāprakāraiśca satvāstraidhātukodbhavāḥ || 12.118{17} ||
(rm 153)
bhuṃjate sukhaduḥkhāni svasvadaivānusāriṇāḥ |
sukhasyānte hi duḥkhāni duḥkhasyānte sukhāni ca || 12.119{18} ||
bhuṃjante jaṃtavaḥ sarve bhramante ṣaḍgatiṣvapi |
tathā bhavanti mitrāṇi dviṣo'pi hitakāriṇaḥ || 12.120{19} ||
mitrāṇyapi dviṣo duṣṭā bhavaṃti daivayogataḥ |
tathāmṛtaṃ viṣībhūtaṃ viṣaṃ cāpyamṛtī bhavet || 12.121{20} ||
tadapi daivayogena tasmāddaivaṃ mahadvaram || 12.122{21} ||
tathā ca brahmaṇaḥ śīrṣaṃ nikṛttaṃ śūlapāṇinā |
tadapi daivayogena tasmāddaivaṃ mahāvalī || 12.123{22} ||
tathā cakrī suvīro'pi vyādheṣu nihato mṛtaḥ |
tadapi daivayogena tasmāddaivaṃ mahadvalam || 12.124{23} ||
yaśca bhūtādhipaśceśaḥ so'pyumatto digaṃvaraḥ |
tadapi daivayogena tasmāddaivaṃ maheśvaraḥ || 12.125{24} ||
caṃdro'pi ca kalaṃkāṅko māse māse'pi hīyate |
tadapi daivayogena tasmāddaivaṃ mahadvalam || 12.126{25} ||
evaṃ candradineśau ca graheṇāpi nipīḍitau |
tadapi daivayogena tasmāddaivaṃ mahāvalī || 12.127{26} ||
rāmo rakṣovijetāpi putreṇa nihato raṇe |
tadapi daivayogena tasmāddaivaṃ mahāvalī || 12.128{27} ||
tathārjjunaḥ sudhīro'pi putreṇāpi hato raṇe |
tadapi daivayogena tasmāddaivaṃ mahāvalam || 12.129{28} ||
tathā ca parśurāmeṇa mātāpi nihatā krudhā |
tadapi daivayogena tasmāddaivaṃ mahāvalaḥ || 12.130{29} ||
biṃbisāro nṛpendro'pi putreṇājātaśatruṇā |
vaṃdhane māritaṃ caivaṃ tadapi daivayogataḥ || 12.131{30} ||
tasmācca brahmadattena dharmapālo sa nandanaḥ |
māritaḥ pramadātuṣṭyai tadapi daivayogataḥ || 12.132{31} ||
rājñī ca durpatī mātā svātmajasyāpi rohitam |
pītvaiva nirdayā tuṣṭā tadapi daivayogataḥ || 12.133{32} ||
kṣāntivādī muniścāpi rājñā nirdayacetasā |
asinā ghātito'raṇye tadapi daivayogataḥ || 12.134{33} ||
prasenajinnṛpeśo'pi putreṇāpi svarājyataḥ |
nirvāsito vane bhrāntastadapi daivayogataḥ || 12.135{34} ||
evamanye'pi rājāno hatvā jñātī svavāṃdhavān |
bhuṃjate prāpya rājyāni tadapi daivayogataḥ || 12.136{35} ||
evaṃ pitrā sutāstyaktvā mātrā ca svātmajā api |
vaṃdhumitrādibhistyaktvā keciddaivānuyogataḥ || 12.137{36} ||
bhāryāpi svāminā tyaktvā bharttāpi bhāryyayā tathā |
(rm 154)
tadapi daivayogena tasmāddaivaṃ mahattaram || 12.138{37} ||
putreṇāpi pitā tyaktaṃ mātāpi jananī tathā |
dhātrī cāpi parityaktāstathā pautrapitāmahaḥ || 12.139{38} ||
tathā pitāmahastyaktaḥ putraiścāpyatinirdayaiḥ |
tadapi daivayogena tasmāddaivaṃ mahattaram || 12.140{39} ||
tathā rājñā svabhṛtyāśca sevakā api sajjanāḥ |
aparādhaṃ vinā tyaktāstadapi daivayogataḥ || 12.141{40} ||
tathā sakhisahāyāṃ ca vaṃdhumitrasuhṛjjanān |
parityaktvā nihaṃtyante tadapi daivayogataḥ || 12.142{40*} ||
tathā śiṣyān gururhanti śiṣyo hanti gurumapi |
tadapi daivayogena tasmāddaivaṃ mahattaram || 12.143{41} ||
tathāpi ghnanti bharttāraṃ svāminaṃ sevakānnapi |
tadapi daivayogena tasmāddaivaṃ mahattaram || 12.144{42} ||
tathā ca svayamātmānaṃ ghnanti kecidruṣānvitāḥ |
tadapi daivayogena tasmāddaivaṃ mahattamaḥ || 12.145{43} ||
kecicchastraiḥ svayaṃ hatvā kecidbhuktvā viṣādikam |
kecidagnau jale svabhre prapātātpatitā mṛtāḥ || 12.146{44} ||
kecitpāśaiḥ svayaṃ badhya mṛtāḥ kecicca vaṃdhane |
kecitpānāśanādīṃśca tyaktvā yāṃti yamālayam || 12.147{45} ||
evaṃ kṛtvāpi yāntyeva kecidvāpi surālayam |
kecicca narake yānti tatsarvaṃ daivayogataḥ || 12.148{46} ||
tathā mṛddārupāṣāṇe nānādhātupratiṣṭhitāḥ |
devatāśca dadantyevaṃ phalaṃ daivaniyogataḥ || 12.149{47} ||
pare'pi svajanā iṣṭāḥ sahajā api ca dviṣaḥ |
bhavanti tatkṣaṇādeva tadapi daivayogataḥ || 12.150{48} ||
evaṃ traidhātukotpannāḥ sarve satvāśca jaṃtavaḥ |
ṣaḍgatiṣu bhramantaste caranti daivayogataḥ || 12.151{49} ||
kecidvīrā guṇāḍhyāśca suṃdarā bhāgyamāninaḥ |
sarvasaṃpatsamṛddhāśca cakravarttinṛpādayaḥ || 12.152{50} ||
kecicca nirguṇāḥ prāḍhyāḥ kecinnirguṇinaḥ śaṭhāḥ |
kecicca vikalāṃgāśca guṇasaṃpattibhāginaḥ || 12.153{51} ||
kecicca suṃdarā mūrkhā nirdhanā nirguṇāḥ śaṭhāḥ |
kecicca guṇinaḥ prāḍhyā nīcakarmānucāriṇaḥ || 12.154{52} ||
kecinnīcakulotpannāḥ guṇāḍhyāḥ sādhuvṛttayaḥ |
(rm 155)
dātāro dhanino vīrāḥ sarvasatvānupālakāḥ || 12.155{53} ||
keciduccakulotpannā nirguṇā duṣṭavṛttayaḥ |
kṛpaṇā vikalāṃgāśca nirdayāśca durāśayāḥ || 12.156{54} ||
kecidvīrā hyalābhijñāḥ kulīnāḥ sādhavo'pi ye |
te'pi jātinihīne'pi bhajaṃti śaraṇaṃ gatāḥ || 12.157{55} ||
kecicca guṇavanto'pi kucelāḥ kṛśagātrikāḥ |
pretavadbhujyamānāśca caraṃti bhikṣukā bhuvi || 12.158{56} ||
kecicca nirguṇā vāpi vaṃcakā duṣṭamānasāḥ |
mānyante sādhuvalloke tadapi daivayogataḥ || 12.159{57} ||
evaṃ nānāvidhā lokāḥ ṣaḍgatiṣu samudbhavāḥ |
sukhaduḥkhāni bhuṃjante bhramaṃti daivayogataḥ || 12.160{58} ||
tasmāddaivaṃ mahāvīraṃ sarvatraidhātukeṣvapi |
kenāpi śakyate naiva vaśe netuṃ kathaṃ cana || 12.161{59} ||
tena daivaṃ mahānāthaḥ sarvakarmādhipaḥ prabhuḥ |
sraṣṭā bharttā ca harttā ca traidhātuvāsināmapi || 12.162{60} ||
tasyāpyadhipatibrahmā sarvalokeśvaro vidhiḥ |
tattasya śaraṇaṃ gatvā bhaktyā nityaṃ samarcaya || 12.163{61} ||
tasmiṃ tuṣṭe vidhau devaiḥ parituṣṭaṃ bhaveddhruvam |
tato daivaprasādena sarvatra maṃgalaṃ bhavet || 12.164{62} ||
daive tuṣṭe susiddhyante sarvakarmāṇi yatnataḥ |
viruddhe tu na siddhyante yatnaiḥ saṃsādhitānyapi || 12.165{63} ||
tasmāddaivādhipaṃ devaṃ brahmāṇaṃ caturānanam |
bhaja daivaprasannāya sarvakāryaprasiddhaye || 12.166{64} ||
daivaṃ brahmaprasādena śubhadaṃ saṃmukhī bhavet |
tataste maṃgalaṃ nityaṃ sarvakāryyeṣu saṃbhavet || 12.167{65} ||
evaṃ sārthapate natvā vidhānaṃ kāryyasādhanam |
vidhinārādhya sadbhaktyā kuruṣva karmasādhanam || 12.168{66} ||
tathā cetsarvathā nityaṃ yātrāyaṃ maṃgalaṃ bhavet |
anyadvā cintitaṃ kāryaṃ sarvāpyevaṃ prasatsyate || 12.169{67} ||
yadi vāṃchāsti te bhūyo gantuṃ ratnākaraṃ prati |
tathābhyarcya vidhātāraṃ gachetsvasti bhavetkhalu || 12.170{68} ||
iti tenoditaṃ śrutvā sārthavāhastatheti saḥ |
pratijñāya samabhyarcya brahmāṇaṃ prārthayattadā || 12.171{69} ||
namaste bhagavan brahmaṃ yātrāsiddhiṃ kuruṣva me |
evaṃ vidhiṃ samārādhya sa sārthādhipatistataḥ || 12.172{70} ||
ratnaṃ prāptyai punaḥ sārthān samāhūyābravīttathā |
(rm 156)
bhavantaḥ śrūyatāṃ vākyaṃ yanmayā samudīritam || 12.173{71} ||
atrāsti yadi vo vāṃchā tathā kartuṃ samudyatām |
ahaṃ ratnākaraṃ bhūyo gantumichāmi sāṃpratam || 12.174{72} ||
tadyadīchatha ratnāni tathā yāta mayā saha |
kiṃ cāpyatra vayaṃ sarve sārthavāhakulodbhavāḥ || 12.175{73} ||
tadgṛhe paśuvadbhuktvā kathaṃ śobhī vrajemahi |
saṃsāre sarvajaṃtūnāmekadhā maraṇaṃ dhruvam || 12.176{74} ||
tatkiṃ no maraṇe bhītyā mṛtyuṃ kena nivāryyate |
yadi daivādvipattiḥ syādasmākaṃ karmmadoṣataḥ || 12.177{75} ||
sarvatīrthajalādhāre mṛtāḥ svargaṃ vrajemahi |
yadi bhāgyācca no yātrā siddhā svastimatī bhavet |
tadā jīvaṃ sukhaṃ dānaṃ kṛtvā svargaṃ vrajemahi |
tadviṣādaṃ parityajya yaśodharmmasukhāptaye || 12.178{76} ||
vīryotsāhaṃ samādhāya samāyāta mayā saha |
iti tasya vacaḥ śrutvā sarve te vaṇijo mudā || 12.179{77} ||
tatheti ca samutsāhaṃ kṛtvā gaṃtuṃ samudyatāḥ |
tataḥ sarve'pi te sārthā dhṛtvā svastyayanaṃ vidhim || 12.180{78} ||
paṇyamādāya tenaiva saha sārthabhṛtā yayuḥ |
tataste prasthitāḥ sarve grāmāraṇyavanasthalīḥ || 12.181{79} ||
samuttīryya kramādyātāstīraṃ prāpurmahodadheḥ |
tatra nāvi samārūḍhā karṇadhāraprayatnataḥ || 12.182{80} ||
marutāṃte'nukūlena jagāhire mahodadhim |
tatasteṣāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ daivayogānuyogataḥ || 12.183{81} ||
sā naukā kālikāvātasaṃkṣubdhābhūdvibheditā |
tadā sarve'pi te sārthā dṛṣṭvā naukāṃ vibheditām || 12.184{82} ||
trasitāḥ sahasā tasmātpratyāyayuḥ prayatnataḥ |
tataste vaṇijaḥ sarve vipaṃnāśā viṣāditāḥ || 12.185{83} ||
rātrau svasvagṛhe gatvā vibhagnāsā viṣedire |
tathāsau sārthavāho'pi lajjitaḥ khaṇḍitāśayaḥ || 12.186{84} ||
kheditātmā gṛhe tasthau mūkavaddhi natotsavaḥ |
tato bhāryyā satī bhadrā patikhedavibheditam |
dṛṣṭvā taṃ vikalībhūtaṃ samupetyābravītpunaḥ || 12.187{85} ||
mā viṣādaṃ kṛthāḥ svāminnatra dhairyyaṃ samāśrayaḥ |
dharmiṣṭhe śaraṇaṃ gatvā kuruṣva dharmasādhanam || 12.188{86} ||
(rm 157)
dharmmeṇa jīyate pāpaṃ niṣpāpo nirmalāśayaḥ |
śuddhacitto viśuddhātmā puṇyātmā bhāgyamāpnuyāt || 12.189{87} ||
tato bhāgyavataḥ puṃso yadyatkarmaṇi sādhitam |
tattatsarvaṃ prasiddhyeta tasmāddharmaṃ carottamam || 12.190{88} ||
dharmameva suhṛdmitramihāmutra sahānugaḥ |
dharmmeṇa pālyate sarvaṃ tasmāddharmo jagatsuhṛt || 12.191{89} ||
dharmā nānāvidhā loke kulacaryyānusarataḥ |
sarveṣāmapi dharmāṇāṃ dayā dharme viśiṣyate || 12.192{90} ||
dayāluḥ sugato buddho dharmarājastathāgataḥ |
samantabhadra ātmajñaḥ sarvasatvahitaṃkaraḥ || 12.193{91} ||
sarvajño mārajicchāstā jagannāthaḥ śubhārthadaḥ |
ṣaḍabhijño jagadbharttā sarvalokādhipeśvaraḥ || 12.194{92} ||
tasmāttasya munīndrasya sadbhaktyā śaraṇaṃ vrajan |
saddharmaśaraṇaṃ kṛtvā bhaja nityamupāsakaḥ || 12.195{93} ||
tataste naśyate pāpaṃ maṃgalaṃ ca bhaveddhruvam |
tadaiva sarvakāryāṇi sametsyaṃte na saṃśayaḥ || 12.196{94} ||
iti bhāryoditaṃ śrutvā sa sārthādhipatirmudā |
tatheti saṃpratijñāya saṃbuddhaśaraṇaṃ yayau || 12.197{95} ||
namaste bhagavannātha pāpistho'haṃ sudurbhagaḥ |
bhavatāṃ śaraṇaṃ gache tanmāṃ dṛṣṭvāśu rakṣatām || 12.198{96} ||
yanmayā prakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ kāritaṃ vānumoditaḥ |
tatsarvaṃ deśayiṣyāmi tanme dehi śubhāṃ gatim || 12.199{97} ||
bhavatāṃ sarvadā nātha śaraṇaṃ yāmyupāsakaḥ |
anyo me śaraṇaṃ nāsti tanmāṃ rakṣa sadā bhaje || 12.200{98} ||
tridhāpi me jagannātha yātrā bhagnā svadaivataḥ |
gṛhasthaḥ sārthavāho'haṃ tanme dehi susaṃpadaḥ || 12.201{99} ||
yanvahaṃ sāṃprataṃ nātha bhavataṃ śaraṇaṃ vrajan |
bhūyo ratnākaraṃ gantumichāmi ratnalabdhaye || 12.202{100} ||
yadi yātrā bhavetsiddhi yāvallābhaṃ ca lapsyate |
tadupāyena saṃbuddhaṃ pūjayeyaṃ sasāṃghikam || 12.203{1} ||
ityevaṃ niścayaṃ kṛtvā saṃbuddhaṃ saṃsmaranmuhuḥ |
tāṃ bhāryyāṃ bhadrikāṃ dṛṣṭvā punarevamabhāṣata || 12.204{2} ||
bhadre tvayā yadādiṣṭaṃ tathā kartuṃ samutsahe |
adyāgreṇa jagannāthaṃ śaraṇaṃ yāsyāmyupāsakaḥ || 12.205{3} ||
(rm 158)
bhadre nāmnā jinendrasya yāsye ratnākare punaḥ |
yadi yātrā bhavetsiddhi yāvallābhaṃ ca lapsyate || 12.206{4} ||
tadupārddhena ratnena sasaṃghaṃ sugataṃ tadā |
yathārhaṃ pūjayiṣye'hamiti saṃkalpitaṃ mayā || 12.207{5} ||
yadi daivādvipattiḥ syātsaṃbuddhanāma saṃsmaran |
sarvatīrthajalādhāne mṛto yāyāṃ sukhāvatīm || 12.208{6} ||
tadatra yadi me kāryyaṃ śubhaṃ kartuṃ samichasi |
tadviṣādaṃ parityajya dhairyyaṃ kṛtvā sukhaṃ vasa || 12.209{7} ||
iti bharttroditaṃ śrutvā sā bhāryyā bhadrikā satī |
tatheti pratisaṃmodya svāminaṃ taṃ samabravīt || 12.210{8} ||
yadyevaṃ te samīhāsti kṛtvetaṃ niścayaṃ prabho |
saṃbuddhasmaraṇaṃ dhṛtvā gacha svastiṃ samācara || 12.211{9} ||
yadi buddhakṛpādṛṣṭiḥ satveṣu vidyate kila |
tathāvaśyaṃ sa saṃbuddhaḥ paśyan tvāṃ cāpyacetkhalu || 12.212{10} ||
tadā te maṅgalaṃ nityaṃ yātrā svastimatī bhavet |
tataste janma saṃsāre sāphalyaṃ ca bhavetkhalu || 12.213{11} ||
tena svāmiṃ jineṃdrasya nāmasvastyayanaṃ dadhat |
gacha yātrā susiddhāstu svasti pratyāgatāśu ca || 12.214{12} ||
iti bhāryoditaṃ śrutvā sa sārthādhipatirmudā |
bhūyaḥ sārthagaṇān sarvān samāhūyābravīttathā || 12.215{13} ||
bhavanta śrūyatāṃ vākyaṃ yanmayātra samīhitam |
ahaṃ ratnākaraṃ bhūyo gantumichāmi sāṃpratam || 12.216{14} ||
tadasti yadi vo vāṃchā yaśaḥsaṃpatsukhāptaye |
samāyāta mayā sārddhaṃ ratnākaraṃ vrajemahi || 12.217{15} ||
yadi bhāgyāddhi no yātrā siddhā svastimatirbhavet |
yāvajīvaṃ sukhaṃ dharmaṃ kṛtvā svargaṃ vrajemahi |
atha vā syādvipattirnaḥ sarvatīrthajalāśraye |
mṛtāḥ svargaṃ gamiṣyāmo hyavaśyaṃ maraṇe sati || 12.218{16} ||
paśuvatkiṃ gṛhe sthitvā sukhaṃ vā kiṃ yaśo vinā |
yaśonvitā mṛtā ye hi tatpumāṃso narottamāḥ || 12.219{17} ||
yadarthameva jīvāmastadarthaṃ yanna vidyate |
kiṃ tena jīvitenāpi kevalaṃ duḥkhabhāginaḥ || 12.220{18} ||
varamevādya me mṛtyurna vyarthaṃ cirajīvitam |
(rm 159)
yasmācciramapi sthitvā mṛtyurkena nivāryyate || 12.221{19} ||
iti mṛtyubhayaṃ dṛṣṭvā sarvatrāpyanivāraṇam |
tyaktvā viṣādakauśīdyaṃ prodyamahyaṃ yaśorjane || 12.222{20} ||
evaṃ nānāprakāreṇa tena sārthabhṛtāpi te |
bodhyamānā vaṇiksaṃghāḥ kiṃ cinnaiva samūcire || 12.223{21} ||
abhadro'yaṃ pumāṃ cāpi yato yātrā na siddhyati |
iti tena mahāṃbhodhau naiko'pi gantumaichata || 12.224{22} ||
atha sārthapatirdṛṣṭvā tān sarvān viratotsavān |
eko'pi vā svayaṃ gantumaichadratnākaraṃ prati || 12.225{23} ||
tato bhāryāṃ samāmaṃtrya paribodhya prasannadhīḥ |
svastyayanavidhiṃ kṛtvā pratasthe bhārakaiḥ saha || 12.226{24} ||
tataḥ paṇyaṃ samādāya bhāravāhajanaiḥ saha |
purādvahi vinirgatya kramāddeśāṃtaraṃ yayau || 12.227{25} ||
kramāccaṃcūryyamāno'sau grāmāraṇyavanasthalīḥ |
parikraman samullaṃghya prāpustīraṃ mahodadheḥ || 12.228{26} ||
tatrāsau sārthabhṛddṛṣṭvā taṃ samudraṃ mahāmbudhim |
kṛtāṃjalipuṭo natvā prārthayaccharaṇaṃ gataḥ || 12.229{27} ||
namaste jīvamādhāra sarvasatvārthasaṃpade |
tvadāsayā samāyāmi tadāśāṃ me prapūraya || 12.230{28} ||
tato'sau karṇadhāraṃ ca toṣayitvā prabodhayan |
kṛtāṃjalipuṭo natvā saṃdhṛtvā samayācata || 12.231{29} ||
karṇadhāra mahāvāho tavāhaṃ śaraṇaṃ vraje |
tatsvasti cārayannaukāṃ yātrāsiddhiṃ prasādhaya || 12.232{30} ||
tathāsau vahanaṃ kṛtvā dṛḍhītaṃ gāḍhavaṃdhitam |
kṛtāṃjalipuṭo natvā saṃdhṛtvā samayācata || 12.233{31} ||
yathā mātā ca garbhasthaṃ vālakaṃ saṃprarakṣate |
tathā māmeva garbhasthe nauke mātaḥ prarakṣatām || 12.234{32} ||
tataśca sugataṃ smṛtvā triratnaśaraṇaṃ gataḥ |
kṛtāṃjalipuṭo bhūtvā praṇamyaivamayācata || 12.235{33} ||
namaste bhagavannātha yathā trāsi jagattrayam |
tathā māṃ kṛpayā dṛṣṭvā pāhi vraje ratnākare || 12.236{34} ||
ityevaṃ sārthavāho'sau praṇatvā sugataṃ smaran |
(rm 160)
bhāṇḍānyāropya naukāyaṃ samārohajjanaṃ saha || 12.237{35} ||
tato'sau naukrameṇaiva karṇadhāraprayatnataḥ |
marutāṃ cānukūlena saṃjagāha mahāmbudhau || 12.238{36} ||
yattatra sārthavāho'sau saṃsmaran sugataṃ muhuḥ |
tena puṇyabalenaiva sā naukā svastimācaran || 12.239{37} ||
tathā kramāccarantyevaṃ dvīpalokāntarāṇi ca |
vyatikramya suśighreṇa ratnākaraṃ samāyayau || 12.240{38} ||
tatra ratnākare prāpya ratnāni samasādhayan |
tato ratnāni saṃgṛhya pratasthe vahanāsthitaḥ || 12.241{39} ||
tataḥ kramādvyatikramya dvīpalokāntarāṇi ca |
tīraṃ prāpuḥ suśīghreṇa buddhadṛṣṭiprasādataḥ || 12.242{40} ||
tataḥ sthalaṃ samāsādya śailāraṇyavanasthalīḥ |
grāmāṃ deśāṃ parikramya śrāvastyāṃ puramāyayau || 12.243{41} ||
tato gṛhe praviṣṭo'sau bharyyayā saha moditaḥ |
suhṛnmitrajanaiścāpi kṛtvālāpaṃ nyaṣīdata || 12.244{42} ||
tataḥ sadyargṛhastho'sau bhāryyayā saha naṃditaḥ |
bhāṇḍamudghāṭya ratnāni pratyavekṣitumārabhat || 12.245{43} ||
tatra divyavicitrāṇi ratnāni vividhāni ca |
dṛṣṭvā harṣaṃ samāsādya punarevaṃ vabhāṣate || 12.246{44} ||
mamaitāni suratnāni divyānyetādṛśāni hi |
tatra divyavicitrāṇi nānāratnakulāni ca || 12.247{45} ||
labdhāni tajjinendrasya kṛpādṛṣṭiprasādataḥ |
tasmādyathāpratijñātaṃ śraddhayā sāṃprataṃ mayā || 12.248{46} ||
yadupārddhaṃ pradātavyaiḥ śravaṇāyārhate'pi naḥ |
tadupārddhaṃ kathaṃ cānudātavyaṃ munaye'rhate || 12.249{47} ||
yadi rājā vijānīyādetadarddhaṃ hare mama |
ratnalubdho nareśo yaṃ daṇḍaṃ vā praṇayaṃ mayi || 12.250{48} ||
tadā me syādvipattiśca tatkathaṃ kriyate mayā |
aho daivādvipattirmāmadyāpi ca na muṃcati |
tatkathaṃ vā pratijñātaṃ pūrayituṃ na manyate |
namaste bhagavannātha sarvajño'si munīśvaraḥ || 12.251{49} ||
rakṣa māṃ pāpinaṃ mūḍhanimagnaṃ duḥkhasāgare |
ityasau sugataṃ smṛtvā taccintāvyathitāśayaḥ || 12.252{50} ||
kapolaṃ svakare dhṛtvā tasthau koṣṭhe nirutsavaḥ |
(rm 161)
tathaivaṃ sthitamālokya svāmi naṃ taṃ viṣāditam || 12.253{51} ||
sā bhāryā bhadrikopetya puraḥ sthitvābravīttathā |
svāmiṃ bharttaḥ kimeva tvaṃ sthito'si duḥkhito yathā || 12.254{52} ||
kiṃ vā te jāyate duḥkhaṃ tadvaca cetpriyāsmi te || 12.255{53} ||
iti bhāryoditaṃ śrutvā sa sārthādhipatiḥ punaḥ |
tāṃ bhāryyāṃ samupāmaṃtrya punaścaivamabhāṣata || 12.256{54} ||
bhadre yajjāyate duḥkhaṃ tatpravakṣye śṛṇu priye |
yathā mayā pratijñātaṃ pūrayituṃ kathaṃ tathā || 12.257{55} ||
yadyupārddhaṃ pradāsyāmi śravaṇāyārhate'pi nu |
etadarddhaṃ haredrājā lobhānme daṇḍayedapi || 12.258{56} ||
ityevaṃ tadviṣādena vyathito'haṃ nirutsavaḥ |
tanme nātra pratīkāro kathaṃ kartuṃ na manyate || 12.259{57} ||
iti bharttroditaṃ śrutvā sā bhāryā punarabravīt |
viṣādaṃ mā kṛthāḥ svāmiṃ śṛṇu yanme samīhitaiḥ || 12.260{58} ||
tāvadimāni saṃkalpaḥ paścāttatsamayāgate |
pūjayā saha bhojaiśca sasaṃghāyārhate'rcayet || 12.261{59} ||
evaṃ kṛte tathā svāmin pratijñā tena setsyate |
bhītiścāpi na vidyeta tasmādevaṃ kuru prabho || 12.262{60} ||
iti bhāryāvacaḥ śrutvā sa sārthādhipatirmudā |
tatheti pratisaṃśrutya punarevamabhāṣata || 12.263{61} ||
tāvadimāni sarvāṇi ratnāni te samarppaye |
paścātkāle sasaṃghaṃ taṃ pūjayiṣye munīśvaram || 12.264{62} ||
idānīṃ sugaṃdhadhūpena pūjayeyaṃ munīśvaram |
ityuktvā sarvaratnāni bhāryāyāṃ sa nyadhāpayet || 12.265{63} ||
tato'sau cāguruṃ krītvā kārṣāpaṇadvayena vā |
gatvā jetavane vihāre tatra dvāre sthito gurumadhūpayet || 12.266{64} ||
tatrāpatramāno'sau saṃbuddhaśaraṇaṃ gataḥ |
kṛtāṃjalipuṭo natvā saṃsmaran prārthayattathā || 12.267{65} ||
bhagavacchāstāsi sarvajño vijānīyā mamāśayam |
tadyathā me pratijñātaiḥ pūrayituṃ samarhasi || 12.268{66} ||
athāsau bhagavān tasya matvāśayaviśuddhatām |
(rm 162)
mahadṛdhyabhisaṃskāraṃ cakāra tatprasādataḥ || 12.269{67} ||
yena sa dhūpa ākāśe prābhyudgamya purīṃ ca tām |
sphuritvā khe punarabhrakūṭībhūtvābhyaśobhayat || 12.270{68} ||
tatrāsau sārthabhṛddṛṣṭvā prātihāryaṃ tadadbhutam |
mahānaṃdaprasaṃnātmā punarevaṃ vyaciṃtayat || 12.271{69} ||
naitanme pratirūpaṃ syādyadayaṃ sugato jinaḥ |
mayā nābhyarcito ratnairdhigme cittabhayāddrutam || 12.272{70} ||
yanvahaṃ sāṃprataṃ nāthaṃ prārthayitvā sasāṃghikam |
saratnaiḥ pūjayiṣyāmi bhojanaiśca svake gṛhe || 12.273{71} ||
ityasau niścayaṃ kṛtvā kṛtāṃjalipuṭo mudā |
saṃbuddhasaṃmukhaṃ gatvā praṇatvaivaṃ vyajijñapat || 12.274{72} ||
bhagavannātha sarvajña kṣamasva me'parādhatām |
bhavatāṃ pūjanāṃ kartumichāmi me prasīdatu || 12.275{73} ||
ityevaṃ prārthite tena sa saṃbuddho munīśvaraḥ |
tatheti pratisaṃmodya tūṣṇībhūtvā vyavasthitaḥ || 12.276{74} ||
tato'sau sārthabhṛnmatvā sugatenādhivāsitam |
praṇatvā ca muneḥ pādau sahasā svagṛhaṃ yayau || 12.277{75} ||
tatra sa bhāryyayā sārddhaṃ vaṃdhumitra janairapi |
praṇītaṃ bhojyapūjāṃgaṃ sādhayāmāsa satvaram || 12.278{76} ||
tato bhojyārcanāṃgāni sajjīkṛtya pramoditaḥ |
vihāre sahasā gatvā natvā taṃ prārthayanmunim || 12.279{77} ||
bhagavannātha saṃbuddha samayo'yaṃ pravarttate |
tadbhavān sāṃghikaiḥ sārddhaṃ samāgantuṃ samarhati || 12.280{78} ||
ityevaṃ prārthite tena sa saṃbuddhaḥ sasāṃghikaḥ |
pratasthe sūryyavanmārge prātihāryaṃ pradarśayan || 12.281{79} ||
tataḥ kramātpurīṃ prāpya tadgṛhaṃ samupāviśat |
taddatte pādyamādāya śuddhāsane nyaṣīdata || 12.282{80} ||
tatastān sāṃghikān sarvān saṃbuddhapramukhān kramāt |
svasvāsanasamāsīnān dṛṣṭvā samyaksamarcayat || 12.283{81} ||
yathārhaṃ bhojanairvāpi varṇagandharasānvitaiḥ |
saṃbuddhapramukhān saṃghān saṃtarpya paryatoṣayat || 12.284{82} ||
tataśca bhojanānte sa śodhayitvā bhujādikam |
(rm 163)
apanīya ca pātrāṇi tāmbūlādīnaḍhaukayat || 12.285{83} ||
tato yāni suratnāni pūrvasaṃkalpatāni hi |
etaiḥ sarvaṃ suratnaistaṃ saṃbuddhaiḥ samavākiran || 12.286{84} ||
tāni ratnāni sarvāṇi saṃbuddhasyānubhāvataḥ |
munerupari khe gatvā chatrībhūtvā sthitā vabhau || 12.287{85} ||
tatastaṃ sa gṛhī dṛṣṭvā dviguṇasaṃpramoditaḥ |
pādau tasya munernatvā praṇidhānaṃ vyadhāttathā || 12.288{86} ||
anena dānadharmeṇa puṇyena kuśalena ca |
bhaveyaṃ sugato buddho loke'ndhe'pariṇāyake || 12.289{87} ||
amukto mocayiṣyāmi tārayiṣyāmyatāritān |
bhītānāśvāsya saṃbodhau sthāpayiṣyāmi nirvṛtau || 12.290{88} ||
tathāsau bhagavān buddhastathāśayaviśodhitam |
saṃbodhau nihitaṃ cittaṃ jñātvā smitaṃ vyadhāttathā || 12.291{89} ||
tadā tasya munīndrasya mukhapadmādvinirgatāḥ |
paṃcavarṇā vibhāsanto viniśceruḥ subhānavaḥ || 12.292{90} ||
tataste bhāsvarāḥ sarve pravisṛtāḥ samaṃtataḥ |
kecidūrddhaṃ gatāḥ kecinmadhyaṃ kecidrasātale || 12.293{91} ||
kecidadho gatā yāste sarve te narakeṣu ca |
nipataṃto vibhāsanto pravisasruḥ samaṃtataḥ || 12.294{92} ||
ye uṣṇanarakāsteṣu śitībhūtā vicerire |
ye śītanarakāsteṣu nyapataduṣṇikāśca te || 12.295{93} ||
ye ye taiḥ kiraṇaiḥ spṛṣṭāste te sarve sukhānvitāḥ |
yadā te sukhasaṃprāptāstadā te vismayānvitāḥ || 12.296{94} ||
aho naḥ sukhamadyaivaṃ kathametadvyacintayan |
kiṃ nu vayamitaścyutvā kutra yātāsma sāṃpratam || 12.297{95} ||
āhosvidupapannāḥ syuranyatra bhuvaneṣvapi |
iti saṃdehināṃ teṣāṃ satvānāṃ paribodhane || 12.298{96} ||
bhagavānnirmite buddhaṃ preṣayannarakān prati |
dṛṣṭvā taṃ nirmitaṃ muniṃ vismitā harṣitāśca vai || 12.299{97} ||
te sarve narakā lokāḥ saṃmīlya saṃvabhāṣire |
na bhavanta itaścyutvā gatāsmānyatra bhūmiṣu || 12.300{98} ||
kiṃstvayaṃ nu mahāsatvaḥ hyapūrvadarśanaḥ pumān |
(rm 164)
nūnamasyānubhāvena duḥkhāni samitāni ca || 12.301{99} ||
tadvayamenamānamya vaṃde mayā pramuktaye |
iti saṃbhāṣya te sarve natvā taccharaṇaṃ gatāḥ || 12.302{100} ||
namo buddhāya dharmāya saṃghāyeti vavandire |
iti te nirmite tasmin prasādya cittamāratāḥ || 12.303{1} ||
sarvapāpavinirmuktā martyadevālayaṃ yayuḥ |
evaṃ te bhānavaḥ sarve sarvatra narakeṣvapi || 12.304{2} ||
prodyante nārakān sarvān pratyāyayurmuneḥ puraḥ |
tathā tadbhānavaścaivamupariṣṭāṃ gatā api || 12.305{3} ||
te'pi sarve caturlokapālānāṃ bhuvanāni ca |
avabhāsya kramātsarvān devalokānabhāsayan || 12.306{4} ||
tathākaniṣṭhaparyantaṃ traidhātukānabhāsayan |
evaṃ sarvāśca tān sarvānavabhāsya samaṃtataḥ || 12.307{5} ||
gāthāḥ prodghoṣayantaśca pralayaṃ saugate vṛṣe |
anityaṃ khalu saṃsāraṃ duḥkhaśūnyaṃ hyanātmakam || 12.308{6} ||
tasmātmāraṃ parityajya gachadhvaṃ śaraṇaṃ muneḥ |
niṣkrāmatārabhadhvaṃ kaṃ yujyadhvaṃ buddhaśāsane || 12.309{7} ||
mṛtyusainyaṃ dhunītāśu naḍāgāraṃ karī yathā |
yo'pyasmin dharmavinaye hyapramattaścariṣyati || 12.310{8} ||
tyaktvā jātisasaṃsāre duḥkhasyāntaṃ kariṣyati || 12.311{9!} ||
atha te bhānavaḥ sarve traidhātubhuvaneṣvapi |
sarvān satvān samuddhṛtya punaḥ pratyāyayurmuneḥ || 12.312{10} ||
atha te bhānavaḥ sarve bhagavantaṃ munīśvaram |
pradakṣiṇatrayaṃ kṛtvā mūrddhni cāntardadhurmuneḥ || 12.313{11} ||
athaitatsabhāsīnā lokāḥ sarve sasāṃghikāḥ |
tattadadbhutamālokya vabhūvurvismayoddhatāḥ || 12.314{12} ||
athānaṃdaḥ samutthāya kṛtāṃjalipuṭo mudā |
bhagavantaṃ guruṃ natvā papracha smitakāraṇam || 12.315{12!} ||
bhagavan bhavato vaktrātpaṃcavarṇāḥ subhāṃsavaḥ |
vinirgatā diśaḥ sarvānavabhāsya śatā punaḥ || 12.316{13} ||
bhavān hi sugato śāstā saṃbuddho bhagavañjinaḥ |
(rm 165)
nirmado nirahaṃkāraḥ mānamātsaryyavarjitaḥ || 12.317{14} ||
tadbhava bhagavaṃ kasmātsmitaiḥ karoti sāṃpratam |
dṛṣṭvaivaṃ taṃ smitaṃ sarve ime lokāḥ pravismitāḥ || 12.318{15} ||
nāhetuḥ sugatā buddhā arhanto vigatoddhavāḥ |
darśayanti smitaṃ kecittatkena bhavataḥ smṛtam || 12.319{16} ||
tadetaddhetumichanti śrotuṃ sarve ime janāḥ |
tadetatkāraṇaṃ śāstaḥ samādeṣṭuṃ samarhati || 12.320{17} ||
ityevaṃ prārthite tena śiṣyeṇānaṃdayoginā |
tatheti pratisaṃmodya tamānaṃdaṃ vabhāṣata || 12.321{18} ||
evametattathānaṃda yathaiva bhāṣasi tvayā |
tatsmitaṃ yadarthaṃ me tadvakṣye śṛṇutādarāt || 12.322{19} ||
paśyānaṃda mamānena sārthavāhena sādhunā |
ebhī ratnaiśca satkṛtya kṛtā pūjā svabhaktitaḥ || 12.323{20} ||
anena kuśalenaiva sārthavāho mahāsudhīḥ |
kramādbodhicarīṃ prāpya pūryya pāramitā daśa || 12.324{21} ||
saṃbodhipākṣikān dharmān prāpya brahmavihārikaḥ |
saṃbodhiṃ samavāpyaivaṃ jitvā māragaṇānapi || 12.325{22} ||
buddho ratnottamo nāma sarvajño'rhaṃ tathāgataḥ |
dharmarājo jagaṃnātho loke śāstā bhaviṣyati || 12.326{23} ||
tadanumodanāṃ kṛtvā saddharmaṃ bhajatādarāt |
dharmeṇaiva jagajjitvā māracaryāvinirgataḥ || 12.327{24} ||
bhadracarī samāsādya saṃbodhipadamāpnuyāt |
dharnio hi jagatāṃ mitramihāmutra samantataḥ || 12.328{25} ||
hitakārī suhṛdiṣṭaḥ sarvadāpi sahānugaḥ |
tasmāddharmaṃ samādhāya saṃcaradhvaṃ samāhitaḥ || 12.329{26} ||
tena vā maṃgalaṃ nityaṃ sarvabhāvī bhaveddhruvam || 12.330{27} ||
iti śāstrā samādiṣṭaṃ śrutvānaṃdaḥ sapārṣadaḥ |
tatheti moditastaṃ ca guruṃ natvā sa naṃdataḥ || 12.331{28} ||
so'pi sārthapatiścaiva svakaṃ vyākaraṇaṃ kila |
munīndreṇa samādiṣṭaṃ śrutvātīvābhyanaṃdatat || 12.332{29} ||
tataścābhiprasannātmā samutthāya kṛtāṃjaliḥ |
bhagavaṃtaṃ sasaṃghaṃ taṃ praṇatvaivaṃ vyatijñapat || 12.333{30} ||
namaste bhagavannātha sarvadharmādhipeśvaraḥ |
(rm 166)
bhavatāṃ śaraṇaṃ kṛtvā bhavāmyahamupāsakaḥ || 12.334{31} ||
yanmayā prakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ kāritaṃ cānumoditam |
tatsarvaṃ deśayiṣyāmi tanme dehi śubhāṃ carim || 12.335{32} ||
bodhau cittaṃ samādhāya triratnaṃ samupāśraye |
kṣamasva me'parādhatvaṃ tanmāṃ rakṣa mudā tathā || 12.336{33} ||
evaṃ tenārthitaṃ śrutvā sa saṃbuddho'bhyadhātpunaḥ |
yathā te'bhihitaṃ sādho tathā saṃsetsyate dhruvam || 12.337{34} ||
tasmāccittaṃ samādhāya triratnaśaraṇaṃ gataḥ |
sarvasatvahitaṃ kṛtvā bodhicaryāṃ samācara || 12.338{35} ||
tatastvaṃ kramato hyevaṃ pūrya pāramitā drutam |
bodhisatvo mahāsatvo bhavedbodhimavāpnuyāt || 12.339{36} ||
etatsatyaṃ parijñāya saddharmaṃ saṃprasādhaya |
tena te maṃgalaṃ nityaṃ sarvatrāpi bhaveddhruvam || 12.340{37} ||
jayo'stu te sadā bodhau siddhyatu praṇihitaṃ drutam |
ciraṃ jīvaṃ hitaṃ loke dharmaṃ dhṛtvā sukhaṃ vasa || 12.341{38} ||
ityasau bhagavān buddhastasmai datvāśiṣaṃ śubhām |
saṃghaiḥ saha samutthāya svaṃ vihāraṃ yayau tataḥ || 12.342{39} ||
atha sārthapatiścāsau sadāraḥ saṃpramoditaḥ |
triratnaśaraṇaṃ kṛtvā saddharmeṣu sadācarat || 12.343{40} ||
tathā sarve'pi satvāśca śrutvā tasya pravṛttitām |
bodhicittaṃ samādhāya triratnaśaraṇaṃ yayuḥ || 12.344{41} ||
etatme guruṇākhyātaṃ saṃśrutaṃ yathā mayā |
tathātraivaṃ tava prītyā kathyate saṃprabudhyatām || 12.345{42} ||
tathā rājan tvayāpyevaṃ svayaṃ dhṛtvā vṛṣaṃ śubhe |
bodhayitvā prajāścāpi sthāpanīyāḥ sadā śubhe || 12.346{43} ||
evaṃ cette sadāpyevaṃ sarvatrāpi sumaṃgalam |
bhavennūnaṃ mahārājan satyametanna saṃśayaḥ |
dharmeṇa nirjitaṃ pāpaṃ dharmeṇa jīyate kudhīḥ |
dharmeṇa nirjitā mārā dharmeṇa bodhimāpnuyāt |
tasmādbodhipadaṃ prāptuṃ yadīcchatha narādhipaḥ |
bodhicittaṃ samādhāya sadā ratnatrayaṃ bhaja |
tathā taṃ kramaṇo rāja bodhicaryyāhitāya ca |
sarvamāragaṇāṃ jitvā saṃbodhimāpnuyā dhruvam |
etattenopaguptena guruṇādiṣṭaṃ samādarāt |
(rm 167)
śrutvā rājo tathetyevaṃ pratyanandan sapārṣadaḥ |
ye śṛṇvantīdamevaṃ praṇihitamanasaḥ sārthavāhāvadānam |
ye cāpi śrāvayanti pramuditamanasaḥ sarvalokahitārtham |
te sarve bodhisatvā vijitakalimalo buddhadharmānuraktāḥ |
saṃbhuktvā satsukhāni prathitaguṇayutā buddhakṣetre prayānti |

++ iti śrīratnamālāvadānakathāyāṃ sārthavāho'vadānaṃ nāma dvādaśa ++


(rm 168)
xiii vaḍikāvadāna
oṃ namaḥ śrīsarvabuddhabodhisatvebhyaḥ |
yaḥ śrīmāñchrīghano loke saddharmaiḥ samupādiśat |
śāsanāni trilokeṣu jayantu tasya sarvadā || 13.1{1} ||
avadānatatvaṃ vakṣyāmi natvā taṃ śrīghanaṃ gurum |
purābhūtpāṭalīputre nagare svargasannibhe || 13.2{2} ||
aśoko nṛparājendrastriratnasevakaḥ kṛtī |
ekasmin samaye tatra sa rājā svajanaiḥ saha || 13.3{3} ||
paurikaiśca sabhāṃ kṛtvā saddharmaṃ śrotumaicchata |
tadā sa saugato bhikṣurupagupto jināṃśajaḥ || 13.4{4} ||
lokān dharmotsukān dṛṣṭvā samādheḥ sahasotthitaḥ |
tatsabhāyāṃ samākramya siṃhāsane śubhāsane || 13.5{5} ||
bhāsayaṃstatsabhālokāṃstasthau pūrṇasudhāṃśuvat |
athāśoko mahīpālaḥ sa maṃtripaurikaiḥ saha || 13.6{6} ||
pūjāṅgaiḥ pūjayitvā tamupaguptamavandata |
sthitvā bhūmau svajānubhyāmuttarāsaṅgamudvahan || 13.7{7} ||
kṛtāñjaliḥ puṭo natvā punarevamabhāṣata |
bhadanta śrotumicchāmi saddharmaṃ sukhasaṃpradam || 13.8{8} ||
yathoktaṃ śrīmunīndreṇa tathā deṣṭuṃ ca me'rhati |
evaṃ tena mahīndreṇa prārthite sa yatīśvaraḥ || 13.9{9} ||
upagupto narendraṃ taṃ samāmantryaivamabravīt |
sādhu suṣṭhu mahārāja śṛṇu saddharmamādarāt || 13.10{10} ||
yathā me guruṇā khyātaṃ tathā vakṣyāmi te hite |
purā sa bhagavān buddhaḥ śakyasiṃho śubhākaraḥ || 13.11{11} ||
dharmarājo jagacchāstā sarvajñaḥ sugato jinaḥ |
sarvavidyākalāvijñaḥ ṣaḍabhijño munīśvaraḥ || 13.12{12} ||
mārajillokavinnātho vināyakastathāgataḥ |
śrāvastyāṃ jetakāraṇye mahodyāne manorame || 13.13{13} ||
anāthapiṇḍadākhyasya gṛhasthasya mahātmanaḥ |
nānāvṛkṣasamākīrṇe nānāpuṣpapraśobhitaiḥ || 13.14{14} ||
nānāphalabharānamraiḥ kalpavṛkṣasamānikaiḥ |
aṣṭāṅgaguṇasaṃpannajalaiḥ padmotpālādibhiḥ || 13.15{15} ||
supuṣpaiḥ paripūrṇābhiḥ puṣkariṇībhirāśrite |
nānājantuguṇaiścāpi mithaḥ snehānuvandhitaiḥ || 13.16{16} ||
nānāpakṣigaṇaiścāpi saṃharṣairupasevite |
tasmiṃ divyamanoramye munisaṃghaniṣevite || 13.17{17} ||
puṇyakṣetre jināvāse vihāre maṇimaṇḍite |
śrāvakairbhikṣubhiḥ sārddhaṃ bodhisatvairupāsakaiḥ || 13.18{18} ||
sarvasatvahitārthena tasthau dharmmaṃ prakāśitum |
(rm 169)
evaṃ tatra sabhāsīnaṃ saṃbuddhaṃ dharmabhāṣiṇam || 13.19{19} ||
dṛṣṭvā dharmāmṛtaṃ pātuṃ sarvalokāḥ samāyayuḥ || 13.20{19!} ||
daivā daityāśca siddhāśca yakṣagaṃdharvakinnarāḥ |
grahā vidyādharāḥ sādhyā nāgeṃdrā garuḍā api || 13.21{20} ||
sarve'pi lokapālāśca munayaśca maharṣayaḥ |
yatayo yoginaścāpi tīrthikāśca tapaśvinaḥ || 13.22{21} ||
brāhmaṇāḥ kṣatriyāścāpi vaiśyāḥ śūdrāśca maṃtriṇaḥ |
amātyāḥ koṭṭavārāśca sārthavāhā mahājanāḥ || 13.23{22} ||
śilpino vaṇijaścāpi gṛhasthāḥ paurikā janāḥ |
tathā jānapadāścāpi grāmyāḥ kārpaṭikā api || 13.24{23} ||
evamanye'pi satvāśca sarvajātisamudbhavāḥ |
pūjāpaṃcopahārāṇi gṛhītvā bhaktimānasā || 13.25{24} ||
saddharmaṃ śraddhayā śrotumanumodya samāyayuḥ |
tatra sarve praviṣṭāste dṛṣṭvā taṃ sugataṃ jinam || 13.26{25} ||
vanditvānanditāḥ sarve pūjāṃ cakruryathākramam || 13.27{26!} ||
tato natvā samāsīnāḥ parivṛtya pramoditāḥ |
dharmmaṃ śrotuṃ puraskṛtya tasthuḥ sāñjalayo mudā || 13.28{27} ||
atha sa bhagavān dṛṣṭvā tāṃllokān dharmavāñchinaḥ |
ādimadhyāntakalyāṇaṃ dideśa dharmamuttamam || 13.29{28} ||
evaṃ sa bhagavānnityaṃ sarvasatvahitārthikaḥ |
dharmaṃ prakāśayaṃ loke tasthau dīptāṃśumāniva || 13.30{29} ||
tasminnavasare tatra śrāvastyāṃ purisaṃbhavaḥ |
āḍhyaḥ śreṣṭhī mahābhogaḥ suvistīrṇaparigrahaḥ || 13.31{30} ||
śrāddho bhadrāśayo dhīraḥ sarvalokasuhṛtpriyaḥ |
tīrthikasevako mānī vabhūva śrīmadānvitaḥ || 13.32{31} ||
tadā sa sundarībhāryāṃ kuladharmasamānikām |
ādāya ratisaṃrakto reme nityaṃ yathāsukham || 13.33{32} ||
tasyaivaṃ ramamāṇasya bhāryā sābhūtsugarbhiṇī |
tataḥ pravṛddhagarbhā sā pāṇḍuvarṇā vyarājata || 13.34{33} ||
tataḥ sā garbhasaṃjātaṃ putraṃ tasyāmupāśritam |
putraṃ matvābhinaṃdaṃtī bharttāramevamabravīt || 13.35{34} ||
svāmin garbhe prajāto me yatkukṣau dakṣiṇāśritaḥ |
dārako'yaṃ bhavetputraḥ nūnaṃ tasmātpramodaya || 13.36{35} ||
iti bhāryoditaṃ śrutvā sa gṛhasthaḥ praharṣitaḥ |
garbhe sa vyāśritaṃ dṛṣṭvā mudaivaṃ samudānayat || 13.37{36} ||
vata me jāyate putrastadahaṃ bhāgyavān bhave |
putramukhaṃ hi paśyeyaṃ vaṃśasthitirbhavedapi || 13.38{37} ||
yanmama vidyate puṇyaṃ dānaśīlādisaṃcitam |
tenānayordvayorastu jananīputrayoḥ śivam || 13.39{38} ||
evaṃ proktvā gṛhasthaḥ sa śraddhayā saṃpramoditaḥ |
bhikṣvarthibrāhmaṇebhyaśca dadau dānaṃ yathepsitam || 13.40{39} ||
(rm 170)
kuleṣṭadevatāścāpi samabhyarcya yathāvidhi |
tacchubhaṃ prārthayannityaṃ tasthau putrasamutsukaḥ || 13.41{40} ||
tataḥ sā bhadrikā nārī pathyopacārabhoginī |
asūta samaye putraṃ suṃdaraṃ lakṣaṇānvitam || 13.42{41} ||
tataḥ sa janakaḥ śrutvā saṃprajātaṃ sutaṃ mudā |
sahasā samupāsritya dadarśa taṃ sasuṃdaram || 13.43{42} ||
darśanīyaṃ subhadrāṅgaṃ prāsādikaṃ manoharam |
dṛṣṭvāpi suciraṃ paśyanneva tasthāvatṛptitaḥ || 13.44{43} ||
tato jātimahaṃ kṛtvā tasya pitrā prasādinā |
vaḍika iti nāmnā prakhyāpito'bhūtsa dārakaḥ || 13.45{45!} ||
tataḥ sa vālako'ṣṭābhyo dhātrībhyaḥ pratipālane |
mānayitvānusaṃbhāṣya pitrā samarpito'bhavat || 13.46{46} ||
tathā sa vaḍiko vālo'ṣṭābhirdhātrībhirādarāt |
pālyamānaḥ pravṛddho'bhūthradasthamiva paṃkajam || 13.47{47} ||
yadā sa dārakaḥ paṃcavarṣito'bhūnmadānvitaḥ |
tadā vidyopalabdhārthī lipiśālāmupāviśat || 13.48{48} ||
tatra sa sadguruṃ natvā kramāllipīnmudāgrahīt |
gurubhaktiprasādena lipipāraṃ yayau laghu || 13.49{49} ||
tataḥ sa gurubhiḥ samyacchiṣyamāṇaḥ subuddhimān |
adhītya sarvaśāstrāṇi vidyāpāraṃ yayau laghu || 13.50{50} ||
tadā tadvaḍikasyāpi pūrvakarmavipākataḥ |
dehe kasmātsamutpanno mahāvyādhiḥ parākramat || 13.51{51} ||
tadā sa vyādhinā tena pīḍyamāno'bhimūrchitaḥ |
asahyavedanākrānto virurāva divāniśam || 13.52{52} ||
tathā taṃ vyādhināghrātamasahyavedanāturam |
krandantaṃ sa pitā dṛṣṭvā kṛpayaivaṃ vyaciṃtayat || 13.53{53} ||
hā me daivaviruddhena putro'yaṃ vyādhimūrchitaḥ |
asahyavedanākrānto bhoktumapi na vāñchati || 13.54{54} ||
avaśyaṃ maraṇaṃ yāyātkathaṃ jīvedabhuktavān |
mṛte putre mariṣye'haṃ snehaduḥkhaṃ kathaṃ sahe || 13.55{55} ||
iti cintāvyathākrānto bhogye'pi viratotsavaḥ |
tameva svātmajaṃ paśyaṃstasthau snehābhivandhitaḥ || 13.56{56} ||
mātāpi taṃ sutaṃ dṛṣṭvā snehaduḥkhāgnitāpitā |
(rm 171)
mṛtyuśaṃkā paritrastā rudantī samupācarat || 13.57{57} ||
tadā tasya suhṛnmitravāṃdhavāḥ samupāgatāḥ |
sarve te rogiṇaṃ dṛṣṭvā snehārttā evamabruvan || 13.58{58} ||
kiṃ bho svasthaṃ na te dehe bhoktuṃ kiṃ vā na vāñchasi |
bhuṃkṣva yathepsitaṃ pathyaṃ tiṣṭha dhairyaṃ samāśrayan || 13.59{59} ||
iti prokte'pi taiḥ sarvaiḥ sa vaḍiko jvarānvitaḥ |
dṛṣṭvaiva tānmuhuḥ paśyannaiva kiñcidabhāṣata || 13.60{60} ||
tataḥ sarve'pi te dṛṣṭvā taṃ vaḍikaṃ rujāturam |
mṛtyuśaṃkāviṣādigdhaṃ pitaraṃ taṃ tathāvadan || 13.61{61} ||
sādho'sya varddhate rogastadrogavyupāśāntaye |
sahasā vaidyamāhūya darśayasva samāhitaḥ || 13.62{62} ||
upadiṣṭaṃ yathā tena vaidyena sudhiyā tathā |
paricaryyāṃ samādhāya kurūpacāramādarāt || 13.63{63} ||
tathā pathyopacāreṇa siddhauṣadhyasahairapi |
kramādasya śarīrastho rogaḥ śāntiṃ vrajeddhruvam || 13.64{64} ||
tataste'yaṃ suto bhadraḥ paripuṣṭatanuḥkṛtī |
svakuladharmamādhāya sukhaṃ jīvecchubhe caran || 13.65{65} ||
iti matvā mahābhāga mā kṛthāstadviṣādanam |
dhairyamālamvya devāṃśca smṛtvā bhuktvā sukhaṃ vasa || 13.66{66} ||
ityevaṃ samupādiśya tadgṛhasthātsamutthitāḥ |
sarve te snehaduḥkhārttāḥ svasvagehamupācarat || 13.67{67} ||
tataḥ sa gṛhabhṛttasya putrasya rogaśāṃtaye |
sadvaidyaṃ samupāhūya sādaraṃ samadarśayat || 13.68{68} ||
tasya roganimittaṃ sa vaidyo dṛṣṭvā samīkṣya ca |
pathyauṣadhyupacāreṇa samāhita upācarat || 13.69{69} ||
tathāpi tasya tadrogaḥ parivṛddho dine dine |
kenāpi hyupacāreṇa naiva śāntimupāyayau || 13.70{70} ||
tadrogaṃ varddhitaṃ dṛṣṭvā janakaḥ sa viṣāditaḥ |
bhūtikaṃ sahasāhūya rogahetumapṛcchata || 13.71{71} ||
tataḥ sa bhūtiko dṛṣṭvā tasya rogapraśāntaye |
sa bhūtadevatābhyaśca valiṃ prādādyathāvidhi || 13.72{72} ||
tathāpi vṛṃhito rogastasya karmavipākataḥ |
kiñcidapi viśeṣeṇa naiva śāntimupāyayau || 13.73{73} ||
tataḥ sa janako dṛṣṭvā tadrogaṃ parivarddhitam |
āyurvidamupāmaṃtrya rogahetumapṛcchata || 13.74{74} ||
(rm 172)
sa ayurvidako dṛṣṭvā tasya grahadaśāphalam |
kudaśāvyupaśāntyarthaṃ grahapūjāmupādiśat || 13.75{75} ||
tathā sa janakastasya rogiṇo grahaśāntaye |
brāhmaṇaṃ samupāmantrya grahapūjāmakārayat || 13.76{76} ||
tathāpi tasya tadrogo varddhito na śamaṃ yayau |
daivena samupāghrāte tadupāye na siddhyate || 13.77{77} ||
tathā sa janakastasya putrasya rogavṛddhitam |
dṛṣṭvā nirāśayā bhinnacitto mūrcchāmupāyayau || 13.78{78} ||
tadā sā supriyā bhāryā bharttāraṃ taṃ vimohitam |
dṛṣṭvaiva samupāgṛhya samāśvāsyaivamabravīt || 13.79{79} ||
svāmin dhairyyaṃ samālambya tyaja citte vimohatām |
kimasmākaṃ prayatnena daivasyātra pramāṇike || 13.80{80} ||
sarvathā bhāvino bhāvāḥ phalaṃti sarvajīvinām |
yadabhāvi na tadbhāvi bhāvi cenna tadanyathā || 13.81{81} ||
tasmāddaivaprasādāya svakuleśeṣṭadevatāḥ |
anusmṛtya samārādhya yācasva putrajīvitam || 13.82{82} ||
iti bhāryoditaṃ śrutvā sa gṛhastho'numoditaḥ |
kuladevaṃ samabhyarcya prārthayadevamādarāt || 13.83{83} ||
prasīda me kuleśa tvaṃ kṣamasva hyaparādhatām |
tavaiva śaraṇastho'haṃ tanme putraṃ prarakṣatām || 13.84{84} ||
evaṃ tena gṛhasthena prārthite'pi divāniśam |
tasya rogo vivṛddho'bhūnnaiva śāmyamupākramat || 13.85{85} ||
tathā brahmādidevāṃśca sarvān lokādhipānapi |
vihāramaṇḍapārāmakṣetrāraṇyasamāśritān || 13.86{86} ||
nadīkūpataḍāgādijalāśrayapratisthitān |
balibhoktṝn kalaṃkasthānmārgaśṛṅgāṭakāśritān || 13.87{87} ||
evamanyatra sarvatra pratisthitān samarcayan |
putrasyārogyakauśalyaṃ jīvitaṃ sa samarthayan || 13.88{88} ||
tathāpi tasya rogo na śamito'bhūtpravarddhitaḥ |
tatastaṃ janako dṛṣṭvā nirāśā mūrchito'patat || 13.89{89} ||
tadā sa vaḍiko rogī pitaraṃ taṃ vimūrchitam |
pṛthivyāṃ patitaṃ dṛṣṭvā śanairnīcasvaro'vadat || 13.90{90} ||
hā hā me jāvate pāpaṃ yenāyaṃ patitaḥ pitā |
(rm 173)
snehaduḥkhāgnisaṃtaptaḥ putrato maraṇaṃ vrajet || 13.91{91} ||
hā tātottiṣṭha māṃ paśya mā tyajātmajamāturam |
dhairyyamālamvya saṃdhāya rakṣa māṃ samupasthitaḥ || 13.92{92} ||
ityasau vilapan putraḥ pitaraṃ taṃ vimūrchitam |
dṛṣṭvā bhojanaketyaśru muñcaṃstasthau nirāśritaḥ || 13.93{93} ||
tadā sā supriyā bhāryyā bharttāraṃ taṃ vimūrchitam |
dṛṣṭvaiva sahasāliṃgya samutthāpyaivamabravīt || 13.94{94} ||
svāmin dhairyaṃ samālaṃbya paśya māṃ te priyaṃ satīm |
tyaktvā dainyaṃ samādhāya paśyan rakṣātmakaṃ priyam || 13.95{95} ||
iti bhāryoditaṃ śrutvā sa gṛhastho samutthitaḥ |
dṛṣṭvā tāṃ supriyāṃ bhāryāṃ putraṃ caivamabhāṣata || 13.96{96} ||
hā daiva kiṃ mayā pāpaṃ prakṛtaṃ dāruṇaṃ purā |
yenāyaṃ sukṛtī putraḥ tyaktvā māṃ kva prayāsyati || 13.97{97} ||
hā hā me saṃpadaścaitāḥ samṛddhāḥ samupārjitāḥ |
ko nu bhoktā bhavedāsāṃ vyarthaṃ nakṣyanti sarvaśaḥ || 13.98{98} ||
kimatrāhaṃ kariṣyāmi yāsyāmi śaraṇaṃ ca kam |
ko nu māṃ sahasā rakṣeddatvā putrasya jīvitam || 13.99{99} ||
kimatrāsya rujaḥ śāntyai kariṣyāmi pratikriyām |
na jāne parimūḍho'smi cetanā me na vidyate || 13.100{100} ||
iti cintāparītātmā putrasnehavimohitaḥ |
kāruṇākulacitto'pi punarevaṃ vyacintayat || 13.101{101} ||
yadime tīrthikā vijñā bhagavanto vicakṣaṇāḥ |
sarvajñāḥ sadguṇādhārāḥ śāstāro me hitaṃkarāḥ || 13.102{102} ||
tattān sarvān samāmaṃtrya gṛhe bhokṣyaiḥ samarcayan |
imaṃ putraṃ puraḥ sthāpya darśayeyaṃ śubhāptaye || 13.103{103} ||
tatsudṛṣṭiprasādena nūnamasya bhavecchuvam |
tato hi kramaśo rogāḥ śāmyeyuḥ sarvathā laghu || 13.104{104} ||
tadāyamātmajaḥ puṣṭo na rogī sutanuḥ sukhī |
kṛtī śubharato bhogī kuladharme samācaret || 13.105{105} ||
iti niścitya sa śreṣṭhī tatra tīrthyāśrame gataḥ |
tān sarvāṃstīrthikānnatvā prārthayedevamādarāt || 13.106{106} ||
namo vo brahmavijñebhyo bhavatāṃ śaraṇaṃ vraje |
vijñapyate yadarthaṃ tadanugrahītumarhata || 13.107{107} ||
(rm 174)
iti tenoditaṃ śrutvā tīrthikāste'bhimānikāḥ |
sarve taṃ samupāmaṃtrya pramodayitumabruvan || 13.108{108} ||
sādhu vada mahāsādho yatte kāryaṃ samīhitam |
tatsarvaṃ sarvathāsmābhiḥ kṛtamevaṃ kariṣyate || 13.109{109} ||
iti taistīrthikaiḥ prokte sa śreṣṭhī saṃprasāditaḥ |
kṛtāñjalipuṭo natvā punarevamabhāṣata || 13.110{110} ||
bhagavanto mahāvijñā bhavanto guravo mama |
tatsarvaṃ pratijānīta yadarthaṃ samupācare || 13.111{111} ||
tathāpi cārthayiṣye'tra yanme kaṣṭaṃ pravarttate |
tadvo vijñāpanaṃ kartuṃ bhavataḥ śaraṇaṃ vraje || 13.112{112} ||
yanmayā prakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ tena me svātmajādhunā |
rogaiḥ saṃpīḍyate'tīvaniḥsvastho maraṇaṃ vrajet || 13.113{113} ||
nānāsvastividhānairhi tasya rogo na śāmyati |
bhiṣajāṃ kṛtayatnāni na siddhyanti kathaṃ cana || 13.114{114} ||
anekā devatāścāpi prābhyarcya prārthito mayā |
tathāpi śāmyate naiva tasya rogo vivarddhitaḥ || 13.115{115} ||
yathā yathā rujāśāntisvastyupāyakṛtāni hi |
tathā tathā na tadrogaḥ praśāmyati pravarddhyate || 13.116{116} ||
kimupāyaṃ kariṣyāmi kena śāmyeta tadgadaḥ |
tadupāyaṃ pradātavyaṃ yenāśu śāmyate kramāt || 13.117{117} ||
iti vijñāpanāṃ kartuṃ bhavatāṃ śaraṇaṃ vraje |
tanme'nukampayāsu no rogaśāntirvidhīyatām || 13.118{118} ||
bhavanto me'nuśāstāraḥ sarvajñā brahmacāriṇaḥ |
tanme sukṛpayā dṛṣṭyā paritrātuṃ samarhata || 13.119{119} ||
iti tenārthitaṃ śrutvā sarve te tīrthikā api |
śreṣṭhinaṃ taṃ samāmaṃtrya punarevaṃ samabruvan || 13.120{120} ||
śṛṇu sādho yadasmābhirhitārthaṃ te pracakṣyatām |
tatsatyaṃ naḥ parijñāya pramāṇīkriyatāṃ kila || 13.121{121} ||
vayaṃ hi sarvalokānāṃ śāstāro hitasādhakāḥ |
tasmādatra viṣādatvaṃ mā kṛthā dhairyamāśraya || 13.122{122} ||
adyaiva sahasā gatvā vayaṃ dṛṣṭvā tavātmajam |
niḥpāpaṃ nīrujaṃ svāsthyaṃ kariṣyāmaḥ sujīvitam || 13.123{123} ||
apyetadvacanaṃ satyaṃ śrutvā dṛṣṭvā pramāṇaya |
(rm 175)
anyathā na bhavetkvāpi hyasmākaṃ brahmacāriṇām || 13.124{124} ||
iti taistīrthikaiḥ proktaṃ śrutvā sa gṛhasattamaḥ |
tatheti pratiharṣitvā natvā tān svagṛhaṃ yayau || 13.125{125} ||
tatra sa gṛha āsādya dāsidāsajanaiḥ saha |
tadarhabhojyapūjāṅgasāmagrīṃ samasādhayat || 13.126{126} ||
tasminneva kṣaṇe tatra sarve te tīrthikā mudā |
sahasā samupāgatya svasvāsane upāviśat || 13.127{127} ||
tathā tān samupāsīnān dṛṣṭvā śreṣṭhī sa moditaḥ |
pūjāṅgaiḥ kramato'bhyarcya bhojanaiḥ samatoṣayat || 13.128{128} ||
tataśca bhojanānte sa vaḍikaṃ taṃ priyātmajam |
rogiṇaṃ samupāhṛtya teṣāṃ puro'bhyadarśayat || 13.129{129} ||
te sarve tīrthikā dṛṣṭvā vaḍikaṃ taṃ rujānvitam |
tadāmayanimittaṃ ca nirīkṣyaivamupādiśat || 13.130{130} ||
aho hyasya mahārogaṃ mahāpātakasaṃbhavam |
tatpātakavimuktyai tu dātavyaṃ dānamādarāt || 13.131{131} ||
tadyathā navaratnāni hemalakṣapalāni ca |
śraddhayā brahmacāribhyaḥ saṃpradadyātsamarcayan || 13.132{132} ||
yadyetaddīyate dānaṃ tvayā gṛhapate tathā |
bhagnakuṣmāṇḍadarinamahiṣīśatadakṣiṇā || 13.133{133} ||
purā dattā naṃdarājñā asmākaṃ brahmacāriṇām |
pādasphoṭāmayastasya śāṃto'bhūttatprabhāvataḥ || 13.134{134} ||
tathāsya sarvapāpāni vinakṣyanti na saṃśayaḥ |
tataḥ sarve'pi rogāśca vinakṣyanti kramāddrutam || 13.135{135} ||
tato rogairvimukto'yaṃ kuśalī svāsthyamāpnuyāt |
tataḥ puṣṭo viśuddhāṅgaḥ suśīlaḥ sukṛtī sudhīḥ |
svakulācāradharmiṣṭho vaṃśāṃścāpi samuddharet || 13.136{136} ||
iti matvā tvayā sādho dātavyaṃ dānamādarāt |
anyathā na bhavetsvasti tavāsya naṃdanasya ca || 13.137{167} ||
iti tairgaditaṃ śrutvā sa śreṣṭhī paribodhitaḥ |
tasyātmajasya trāṇārthaṃ tathā dātumudācarat || 13.138{[138]} ||
tataḥ sa punarabhyarcya tān sarvānstīrthikān patīn |
pradadau navaratnāni svarṇalakṣapalāni ca || 13.139{139} ||
tataste tīrthikāḥ sarve dṛṣṭvā tāni pramoditāḥ |
sarvānyapi pratigṛhya tathāśīrvacanaṃ daduḥ || 13.140{140} ||
svastyastu te sadā sādho kalyāṇamastu sarvathā |
(rm 176)
nīrogī suciraṃ jīvyā jayo'stu te samantataḥ || 13.141{141} ||
etadeva samādiśya sarve te tīrthikāstataḥ |
tāni sarvāṇi vastūni pratigṛhyāśramaṃ yayuḥ || 13.142{142} ||
tathāpi tasya rogā na śamitā varddhitāḥ kramāt |
svadaivaphalabhogyāni bhoktavyāni hi sarvathā || 13.143{143} ||
tatastajjanako dṛṣṭvā svātmajaṃ taṃ rujāturam |
nirāśāmūrchito bhūmau nipataṃ vyalapattathā || 13.144{144} ||
hā daiva kiṃ mayā pāpaṃ dāruṇaṃ prakṛtaṃ purā |
yato'yaṃ svātmajaḥ putraḥ puro me maraṇaṃ vrajet || 13.145{145} ||
kṛtānyupāyayatnāni dānāni vividhāni ca |
sarvāṇyetāni matpāpairnisphalāni bhavanti hi || 13.146{146} ||
kiṃ mayātrāpi karttavyaṃ ko'tra me hitamādiśet |
sarvathāhaṃ vinaṣṭo'smi ko'pi trātā na vidyate || 13.147{147} ||
kasyātra śaraṇaṃ yāsye ko no rakṣeddayādṛśā |
sarve vayaṃ vinaṣṭāsma hā daiva śaraṇaṃ vraje || 13.148{148} ||
ityevaṃ vilapaṃtaṃ taṃ janakaṃ vaḍiko'pi saḥ |
dṛṣṭvā śanaiḥ samāhūya pura evamabhāṣata || 13.149{149} ||
bho tāta kiṃ tvayātrāpi karttavyaṃ hi mayāpi ca |
sarveṣāmapi jantūnāṃ daivagatiḥ pramāṇikā || 13.150{150} ||
iti matvā viṣādatvaṃ hṛdaye mā kṛthāstyaja |
saṃbuddhaśaraṇaṃ kṛtvā bhaja nityamanusmaran || 13.151{151} ||
buddha eva jagacchāstā trātā lokahitaṃkaraḥ |
durgatitārako nātho bharttā sadgatināyakaḥ || 13.152{152} ||
tadevaṃ sugataṃ smṛtvā saddharmaṃ samupāśrayan |
saṃghānāṃ bhajanaṃ kṛtvā cinu puṇyaṃ samāhitaḥ || 13.153{153} ||
triratne sādhitaṃ puṇyaṃ na kṣiṇuyātkadā cana |
yadi daivādvipattiḥ syātpuṇyaṃ tu labhate varam || 13.154{154} ||
puṇyena rakṣyate lokaḥ puṇyena sadgatiṃ vrajet |
tasmātpuṇyaṃ mahāratnaṃ cinu dhairyaṃ samāśrayan || 13.155{155} ||
avaśyamaraṇe loke kiṃ viṣādena siddhyate |
iti matvāpi taṃ buddhaṃ smṛtvā bhaja samāhitaḥ || 13.156{156} ||
tathāhaṃ vā tamevātra saṃbuddhaṃ śaraṇaṃ vrajan |
saṃsmṛtvā bhajanaṃ kurvan vrajeya maraṇaṃ dhruvam || 13.157{157} ||
yadi tasya jinendrasya kṛpāsti mayi pāpini |
(rm 177)
tatkṛpādṛkprasādena jīveya nīrujaḥ punaḥ || 13.158{158} ||
yadi daivabalenāhaṃ mṛte tatsmṛtipuṇyataḥ |
sarvathā durgatiṃ tyaktvā sadgatiṃ punarāpnuyām || 13.159{159} ||
evaṃ jñātvā tvayā tāta mā kriyatāṃ viṣādatā |
saṃsāre'vaśyaṃ marttavyaṃ sarveṣāmapi janminām || 13.160{160} ||
ityuktvā sa rujārtto'pi vaḍiko dhairyyamāśrayan |
saṃbuddhaṃ śaraṇaṃ kṛtvā smṛtvā tasthau samāhitaḥ || 13.161{161} ||
tathā sa gṛhabhṛcchreṣṭhī śrutvaitadātmajoditam |
satyametatparijñāya buddhaṃ smṛtvā tathāvadat || 13.162{162} ||
namo buddhāya dharmāya saṃghāya ca sadā smare |
adyārabhya sadā nityaṃ bhavatāṃ śaraṇaṃ vraje || 13.163{163} ||
yadi vo'sti kṛpā loke rakṣantu naḥ suduḥkhinaḥ |
sarvathā kṛpayā dṛṣṭyā dṛṣṭvāśu trātumarhati || 13.164{164} ||
yathānye tribhave lokā bhavadbhiḥ pratipālitāḥ |
tathāsmān kṛpayā dṛṣṭvā trātumarhati sarvathā || 13.165{165} ||
rakṣatu bhagavannātha jīvayeyaṃ mamātmajam |
kṛpayānugrahaṃ kṛtvā putraratnaṃ prayaccha me || 13.166{166} ||
yadi jīvedayaṃ putro bhavaddṛṣṭiprasādataḥ |
bhavatāṃ śaraṇe sthitvā caratu vratamādarāt || 13.167{167} ||
ityevaṃ smaraṇaṃ kṛtvā saṃbuddhaśaraṇaṃ vrajan |
sa śreṣṭhī taṃ sutaṃ dṛṣṭvā tasthau mohavinoditaḥ || 13.168{168} ||
tasminneva kṣaṇe lokāṃ trātuṃ sa bhagavānmuniḥ |
saṃpaśyan kṛpayā dṛṣṭyā prādrākṣīttān saduḥkhinaḥ || 13.169{169} ||
tathā tān duḥkhino dṛṣṭvā bhagavān sa jineśvaraḥ |
sahasā tān paritrātuṃ buddharasmiṃ vyamuñcata || 13.170{170} ||
tābhiḥ kanakavarṇābhiḥ saṃbuddharaśmibhistadā |
samantātsamatikramya tadgṛhamavabhāsitam || 13.171{171} ||
tato maitryaṃśavaścāpi tena bhagavatā punaḥ |
samutṣṛṣṭāstanau tasya paryaspṛśan prasāditāḥ || 13.172{172} ||
tatastasya tanustaiḥ saṃspṛṣṭamātre samaṃtataḥ |
prahlāditā sukhaprāptā svāsthyatāṃ samupāyayau || 13.173{173} ||
tataḥ sa vaḍikaḥ svāsthyaṃ prāpya nirmalamānasaḥ |
vismitaḥ pitaraṃ paśyan samāmaṃtryaivamabravīt || 13.174{174} ||
aho buddhasya māhātmyamaho dharmānubhāvatā |
aho saṃghaprabhāvatvaṃ paśya tāta muneḥ prabhām || 13.175{175} ||
(rm 178)
yasyānusmṛtimātreṇa kṛpā loke prasāryate |
dhanyo'yaṃ bhagavān buddho jayatvevaṃ sadā sthitaḥ || 13.176{176} ||
yasya dayānubhāvena jīvema nīrujaḥ sukhī |
pāpaiścāpi vimukto'hamiti satyaṃ pramanyate || 13.177{177} ||
tasmāttameva śāstāraṃ saṃbuddhaṃ śaraṇaṃ vraje |
yāvadbhavamanusmṛtvā bhajeya samupāśritaḥ || 13.178{178} ||
evaṃ buddhaguṇān smṛtvā vaḍikaḥ sa pramoditaḥ |
kṛtāñjaliḥ smaran bhūyaḥ praṇatvaivamayācata || 13.179{179} ||
namaste bhagavannātha bhavatāṃ śaraṇaṃ vraje |
yāvadbhavaṃ triratnānāṃ bhaveyaṃ sevakaḥ sadā || 13.180{180} ||
ityevaṃ sugataṃ smṛtvā vaḍikaḥ so'bhibodhitaḥ |
bhūyo bhūyaḥ smarannatvā tasthau svāsthyatvamāgataḥ || 13.181{181} ||
tadā sa bhagavān buddho dṛṣṭvā taṃ śuddhamānasam |
saddharmaṃ samupādeṣṭuṃ taddvāre samupāsarat || 13.182{182} ||
taddvārasamupāsīnaṃ taṃ munīndraṃ raviprabham |
dṛṣṭvaiva vismito dvāsthaḥ sahasā gṛhamāviśat || 13.183{183} ||
tatra gṛhapatiṃ dṛṣṭvā vaḍikaṃ taṃ ca sādaram |
purataḥ samupetyaiva vyajñāpayatsasatvaraḥ || 13.184{184} ||
gṛhapate munīndro'tra gṛhadvāra upasthitaḥ |
tadatra sahasopetya samāmaṃtrya praveśaya || 13.185{185} ||
iti tenoditaṃ śrutvā vaḍikaḥ sa prasāditaḥ |
utthitaḥ sahasopetya taṃ munīndraṃ mudānamat || 13.186{186} ||
natvā taṃ sugataṃ gehe prajñapya svāsanaṃ mudā |
vijñapya samupāmaṃtrya praveśayaṃstathārthayan || 13.187{167!} ||
svāgataṃ bhagavan vāṃche bhavatāṃ śaraṇaṃ mune |
praviśatu jagannātha vijayasvāsane śubhe || 13.188{168} ||
iti tenodite buddho bhagavān sa samāviśan |
svāsane samupāsīno vaḍikaṃ taṃ tathāvadat || 13.189{1691} ||
kiṃ te vaḍika duḥkhatvaṃ vādhakaśca kathaṃ vada |
iti bhagavatā pṛṣṭo vaḍikaḥ sa tathāvadat || 13.190{190} ||
kāyikaṃ jāyate duḥkhaṃ cetasikaṃ ca me mune |
tadbhavān kṛpayā dṛṣṭyā śamayettatprabādhanam || 13.191{191} ||
iti tadvijñāpitaṃ śrutvā bhagavān sa munīśvaraḥ |
(rm 179)
taccittaduḥkhaśāntyarthaṃ maitrīdharmamupādiśat || 13.192{192} ||
vaḍikātra sadā loke maitracittaṃ samācara |
anena te manodāhaṃ praśāmyeta sadāpi hi || 13.193{193} ||
ityādiśya munīndraḥ sa punarevaṃ vyaciṃtayat |
vatendrakṣīrikāṃ nāma mahauṣadhāṃ samānayet || 13.194{194} ||
iti cittamataṃ śāstuḥ parijñāya surādhipaḥ |
ādāya kṣīrikāṃ nāma mahauṣadhīmupānayat || 13.195{195} ||
tatra so surarājastaṃ bhagavantaṃ praṇamya tām |
mahauṣadhīmupasthāpya pura evaṃ samabravīt || 13.196{196} ||
bhagavan pratigṛhṇātu divyauṣadhīmimāṃ bhavān |
anayātra yathākāryaṃ sādhayatu sukhāptaye || 13.197{197} ||
ityukte tena śakreṇa dṛṣṭvā tāṃ bhagavānmuniḥ |
gṛhītvā vaḍikāyaivaṃ pradatvā caivamādiśat || 13.198{198} ||
gṛhāṇa vaḍikemāṃ te kāyikaduḥkhaśāminīm |
anayā te śarīre saṃskriyatāmupacāraṇaiḥ || 13.199{199} ||
ityādiṣṭe munīndreṇa vaḍikaḥ so'numoditaḥ |
tatheti tāṃ samādāya yathādiṣṭamupācarat || 13.200{200} ||
tatastasya tanū rogairvimuktā paripuṣṭitā |
krameṇa śāmyasaundaryaṃ prāpyābhiparyaśobhata || 13.201{201} ||
tataḥ śāstrā yathādiṣṭaṃ vaḍikena tathā hṛdi |
maitrī prabhāvitā loke svātmaje supriye yathā || 13.202{202} ||
tadā tasya mahadduḥkhaṃ cetasikaṃ śaśāma tat |
mahāmaitryā pariṣikte hṛdaye'gnirivāmbubhiḥ || 13.203{103!} ||
tataḥ sa vaḍikaḥ svasthaḥ pariśuddhendriyaḥ sudhīḥ |
bhagavantaṃ tamānamya prārthayadevamādarāt || 13.204{104} ||
vande'haṃ bhavatāṃ pādau sarvadā śaraṇaṃ vraje |
tathā ca sarvadā nātha kṛpayā trātumarhati || 13.205{105} ||
yadyevaṃ bhavatāṃ śāstaḥ kṛpādṛṣṭirna vidyate |
mṛto'haṃ narake'dyāpi gato duḥkhānyavāpnuyām || 13.206{106} ||
yadevaṃ svasthito jīve tatte kṛpāmbubhāvataḥ |
tathā me'nugrahaṃ kartuṃ nityamarhati sarvadā || 13.207{107} ||
ityevaṃ prārthanāṃ kṛtvā bhūyaḥ sa vaḍiko mudā |
sāñjaliḥ praṇatiṃ kṛtvā paśyaṃstasthau munīśvaram || 13.208{108} ||
tathā tajjanakaḥ śreṣṭhī sadāraḥ saṃpramoditaḥ |
triratne śaraṇaṃ kṛtvā saṃbuddhaṃ taṃ samarcayat || 13.209{109} ||
(rm 180)
tataḥ sa gṛhabhṛcchreṣṭhī dṛṣṭvā taṃ sugataṃ mudā |
kṛtāñjalipuṭo natvā prārthayadevamādarāt || 13.210{210} ||
vande te caraṇau śāstaḥ sarvadā śaraṇaṃ vraje |
evaṃ no'nugrahaṃ kartuṃmarhati kṛpayā sadā || 13.211{211} ||
tataḥ sa bhagavān buddhastathāstviti samādiśat |
ṛddhyākāśaṃ samutplutya svamāśramamupāyayau || 13.212{212} ||
tatra sa bhagavān prāptaḥ sabhāmadhye samāśritaḥ |
etadvṛṣṭi samākhyāya tasthau dharmaṃ samādiśat || 13.213{213} ||
tadā sa vaḍiko bhūyo buddhaguṇānanusmaran |
sa saṃghaṃ sugataṃ bhojyaiḥ pūjayituṃ samaichata || 13.214{214} ||
tadā sa nṛpate rājñaḥ prasenajita ānataḥ |
etatsarvaṃ pravṛttāṃtaṃ nivedyaivamabhāṣata || 13.215{215} ||
rājannahaṃ mahārogī triratnaśaraṇaṃ vraje |
tattriratnakṛpādṛṣṭerjīve rogairvimocitaḥ || 13.216{216} ||
tanmayā sa munīndro'pi sarvasaṃghaiḥ sahādhunā |
samāmaṃtrya gṛhe bhojyaiḥ pūjayituṃ samicchyate || 13.217{217} ||
tanme'nugrahaṇaṃ kṛtvā lokānāṃ puṇyavṛddhaye |
tadadhivāsanāṃ kṛtvā samanujñāpaya prabho || 13.218{218} ||
iti tenārthitaṃ śrutvā sa rājābhyanumoditaḥ |
vaḍikaṃ taiḥ samālokya praśaṃsyaiva samādiśat || 13.219{219} ||
vaḍika tvaṃ mahāsādhustadbuddhaśaraṇaṃ gataḥ |
tathānuśraddhayā nityaṃ bhaja ratnatrayaṃ sadā || 13.220{220} ||
iti rājñābhyanujñātaṃ śrutvā sa vaḍiko mudā |
nṛpatiṃ taṃ praṇatvaivaṃ svagṛhaṃ samupāyayau || 13.221{221} ||
tatra gṛhe samāsādya kṛtvānumataṃ pituḥ |
śāstu nimaṃtraṇāṃ kartuṃ vihāre samudācaran || 13.222{222} ||
tatra sa samupāviṣṭo dṛṣṭvā taṃ śrīghanaṃ mudā |
kṛtāñjalipuṭo natvā prārthayadevamādarāt || 13.223{223} ||
bhagavaṃstadvijānīyādyadarthaṃ samupāvraje |
tathāpyatra bhavadagre prārthaye'nugrahaṃ kuru || 13.224{224} ||
yadahaṃ roganirmuktaḥ puna janma ivāptavān |
tadbhavatāṃ kṛpādṛṣṭeḥ prasādāditinmanyate || 13.225{225} ||
tadahaṃ bhavatāṃ sarvasaṃghānāṃ bhojanairgṛhe |
pūjayituṃ samiche tadanugrahītumarhati || 13.226{226} ||
(rm 181)
iti saṃprārthitaṃ tena śrutvā sa bhagavānmuniḥ |
tathetyabhyanumoditvā tūṣṇībhūtvādhyuvāsa tat || 13.227{227} ||
tataḥ sa vaḍiko matvā bhagavatādhivāsitam |
mudā tasya muneḥ pādau natvāśu svagṛhaṃ yayau || 13.228{228} ||
tatra sa sahasā gehe vandhumitrajanaiḥ saha |
saṃghārhabhojyapūjāṅgaṃ sāmagrīṃ samasādhayat || 13.229{229} ||
tataḥ sa sarvasāmagrīṃ sādhayitvāsanāni ca |
prajñapya śuddhite bhūmau maṃdayitvā samantataḥ || 13.230{230} ||
tatastaṃ śrīghanaṃ buddhaṃ sasaṃghaṃ svajanaiḥ saha |
āmaṃtrituṃ vihāre sa sahasā samupācarat || 13.231{231} ||
tatra sa samupāviṣṭo dṛṣṭvā taṃ śrīghanaṃ mudā |
sasaṃghaṃ sāṃjalirnatvā prārthayadevamādarāt || 13.232{232} ||
bhagavan sādhyate sādhyaṃ samayo varttate'dhunā |
tadbhavāṃ sāṃghikaiḥ sārddhaṃ vijayituṃ samarhati || 13.233{233} ||
iti tenārthitaṃ śrutvā tatheti sa munīśvaraḥ |
pātracīvaramādhāya sarvasaṃghaiḥ sahācarat || 13.234{234} ||
tatra mārgeṣu sarvatra nagare ca samantataḥ |
prakāśayan prātihāryaṃ tadgṛhaṃ samupāviśat || 13.235{235} ||
tatra gṛhe samāsādya pādārghaṃ pratigṛhya saḥ |
svāsane samupāviśya kramāttasthau sasāṃghikaḥ || 13.236{236} ||
tadā sa vaḍiko dṛṣṭvā taṃ buddhaṃ svāsane sthitam |
sasaṃghaṃ sarvapūjāṅgaiḥ sarvapuṣpaiḥ samarcayat || 13.237{237} ||
tataśca surasairbhojyaiḥ supraṇītaiḥ suhādhitaiḥ |
varṇagandharasopetaiḥ sarvaistāṃ samatoṣayat || 13.238{268!} ||
tataśca bhojanānte sa pātrādīnyapanīya ca |
mukhādīṃ śodhayitvā ca pūgauṣadhīn praḍhaukayat || 13.239{269} ||
tataḥ sa prāñjalirnatvā sasaṃghaṃ sugataṃ mudā |
purastho manasā hyevaṃ praṇidhānaṃ vyadhātsmaran || 13.240{270} ||
anena kuśalenāhaṃ loke'ndhe'pariṇāyake |
yathāyaṃ bhagavān buddhastathā bhūyāsamātmavit || 13.241{271} ||
yathānena munīndreṇa jagalloko'bhipālitaḥ |
tathāhaṃ sarvalokānāṃ pālayeyaṃ samantataḥ || 13.242{272} ||
atīrṇāṃstārayiṣyāmi mocayiṣyāmyamocitān |
anāsvastān samāśvāsya sthāpayiṣyāmi nirvṛtau || 13.243{273} ||
(rm 182)
ityevaṃ manasā tena praṇidhānaṃ kṛtaṃ mudā |
matvā sa bhagavān buddho smitaṃ prāvirvyadhānmudā || 13.244{274} ||
tadā tasya munīndrasya mukhātpaṃca surasmayaḥ |
nisṛtya triṣu lokeṣu bhāsayantaḥ samāsaran || 13.245{275} ||
yāpi kāścidadholoke prasṛtā narakeṣu tāḥ |
uṣṇeṣu śītalībhūtā śītaleṣu tathoṣṇikāḥ || 13.246{276} ||
tadā tābhiḥ parispṛṣṭāḥ sarve nairayikā janāḥ |
mahatsaukhyaṃ samāsādya vismitāścaivamālapat || 13.247{277} ||
aho saukhyaṃ kathaṃ hyevamanyatra kiṃ gatā vayam |
kasya puṇyānubhāvena kvāsmākaṃ jāyate sukham || 13.248{278} ||
iti cintābhidagdhānāṃ teṣāṃ cittaprabodhane |
bhagavānnirmitaṃ buddhaṃ saṃpreṣya samadarśayat || 13.249{279} ||
tatra taṃ nirmitaṃ buddhaṃ dṛṣṭvā sarve'pi te mudā |
vismitāḥ sahasaṃmīlya punarevaṃ vabhāṣire || 13.250{280} ||
aho samantabhadro'yaṃ puruṣo dṛśyate'dhunā |
nūnamasyānubhāvena saukhyaṃ no jāyate khalu || 13.251{281} ||
iti saṃbhāṣya te sarve tasmin buddhe prasāditāḥ |
namo buddhāya dharmāya saṃghāya ceti prāvadan || 13.252{282} ||
tataḥ sarve'pi te satvāstriratnasmṛtipuṇyataḥ |
te durgativinirmuktāḥ sadgatiṃ samupāyayuḥ || 13.253{283} ||
evaṃ tā raśmayaḥ sarvā avabhāsya samaṃtataḥ |
sarvasatvān samuddhṛtya muneḥ pratyāyayuḥ punaḥ || 13.254{284} ||
tathā yā raśmayaḥ kāścidupariṣṭātprasāritāḥ |
tāḥ sarvān devalokānāmavabhāsya samantataḥ || 13.255{285} ||
yāvadbhavāgraparyaṃtaṃ bhāsayantaḥ prasāritāḥ |
evaṃ cāpi mahāśabdamudghuṣya samudācaran || 13.256{286} ||
anityaṃ khalu saṃsāraṃ duḥkhaṃ śūnyamanātmakam |
tadbhave sadgatiṃ hitvā saddharmaṃ bhajatādarāt || 13.257{287} ||
niṣkrāmatārabhadhvaṃ ca yujyadhvaṃ buddhaśāsane |
dhunīta mṛtyusainyaṃ ca naḍāgāramiva dvipaḥ || 13.258{288} ||
yo'pyasmin dharmavaineye hyapramattaścaretsadā |
(rm 183)
sa hitvā jātisaṃsāraṃ duḥkhasyāntaṃ kariṣyati || 13.259{289} ||
ityevaṃ tanmahāśabdaṃ śrutvā sarve'pi te surāḥ |
tathetyabhyanumodantastriratnaśaraṇaṃ yayuḥ || 13.260{290} ||
tathā tā arciṣaḥ sarvā avabhāsya samaṃtataḥ |
punaḥ pratyāgatāstasya munerantikamāyayuḥ || 13.261{291} ||
tatastā arciṣaḥ sarvāḥ saṃmīlya samupāsthitāḥ |
kṛtvā pradakṣiṇāṃ traidhamuṣṇīṣe'ntardadhau muneḥ || 13.262{292} ||
athānaṃdastamālokya vismitaḥ sahasotthitaḥ |
kṛtāñjalipuṭo natvā paprachaivaṃ munīśvaram || 13.263{293} ||
bhagavan bhavato vaktrātpaṃcavarṇāḥ suraśmayaḥ |
vinisṛtā diśaḥ sarvā bhāṣayanti samantataḥ || 13.264{294} ||
nākāraṇaṃ jinā buddhā darśayanti smitaṃ kvacit |
bhavatsmitaṃ samālokya sarve bhavanti vismitāḥ || 13.265{295} ||
taddhetu śrotumichaṃti sarve'pīme sabhāśritāḥ |
tadeṣāṃ saṃśayaṃ chetuṃ taddhetu samupādiśa || 13.266{296} ||
iti tenārthitaṃ śrutvā bhagavān sa munīśvaraḥ |
tāṃ ca sarvāṃ sabhāṃ dṛṣṭvā tathānandamabhāṣata || 13.267{297} ||
paśyānaṃda mamānena vaḍikenānumodinā |
saṃgheṣu śraddhayā bhaktyā satkṛtya bhajanaṃ kṛtam || 13.268{298} ||
etatpuṇyavipākena vaḍiko'yaṃ sudhīḥ kṛtī |
kramādbodhicarīḥ pūrya daśabhūmīśvaro bhavan || 13.269{299} ||
sarvamāragaṇāṃ jitvā saṃbodhiṃ samavāptavān |
ślakṣṇavāṇyabhidho buddhastathāgato bhaviṣyati || 13.270{300} ||
yadanena triratneṣu śaraṇaṃ saṃprasādinā |
praṇidhānaṃ kṛtaṃ tena saṃbuddho'yaṃ bhaveddhruvam || 13.271{301} ||
evaṃ matvā tathānaṃda triratnaśaraṇaṃ gataḥ |
satkṛtya samupāśritya bhaja nityaṃ samāhitaḥ || 13.272{302} ||
ye satvāḥ śraddhayā smṛtvā triratnaśaraṇaṃ gatāḥ |
durgatiṃ te na gachanti bhavanti bodhibhāginaḥ || 13.273{303} ||
evaṃ matvā triratnānāṃ satkṛtya śraddhayā mudā |
saṃsmṛtya śaraṇaṃ gatvā bhaktavyaṃ bodhivāṃchibhiḥ || 13.274{304} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa śrutvānaṃdaḥ sa moditaḥ |
sāṃghikaṃ saha lokaiśca tatheti prābhyanaṃdata || 13.275{305} ||
tataḥ sa bhagavāṃstasmai vaḍikāya subhāgine |
datvāśīrvacanaṃ bhūyaḥ samāmaṃtrya tamabravīt || 13.276{306} ||
(rm 184)
vaḍika tvaṃ mahābhāga triratnaṃ śaraṇaṃ gataḥ |
bodhicaryyāṃ carannityaṃ saṃbodhiṃ samavāpnuyāḥ || 13.277{307} ||
iti satyaṃ parijñāya sarvasatvahitārthabhṛt |
tathā ratnatrayaṃ smṛtvā bhaja nityaṃ samāhitaḥ || 13.278{308} ||
ityādiśya munīndraḥ sa saṃghaiḥ sārddhaṃ samutthitaḥ |
vaḍikaṃ taṃ samāmaṃtrya svavihāraṃ yayau tathā || 13.279{309} ||
tathā sa vaḍiko nityaṃ triratnaśaraṇaṃ gataḥ |
sadā lokahitaṃ kṛtvā bodhicaryāṃ samācarat || 13.280{310} ||
etanme guruṇādiṣṭaṃ śrutaṃ mayā narādhipa |
tathā te kathyate rājaṃ śrutvā caivaṃ prakāśaya || 13.281{311} ||
tathā tvayāpi rājendra satvānāṃ hitasādhane |
triratnaśaraṇaiḥ kṛtvā karttavyā bodhicārikāḥ || 13.282{312} ||
tathā te sarvadā nityaṃ sarvatra maṃgalaṃ bhavet |
kramādbodhicarīṃ pūrya saṃbodhimapi cāpnuyāḥ || 13.283{313} ||
iti matvā mahārāja prajāścāpi prabodhayan |
saddharme saṃpratisthāpya sarvathā trātumarhasi || 13.284{314} ||
iti tenopaguptena samādiṣṭaṃ niśamya saḥ |
rājā tatheti vijñapya prābhyanandatsapārṣadaḥ || 13.285{315} ||
śrutvāvadānaṃ vaḍikasya sādhoridaṃ narā ye'bhyanumodayanti |
sarve'pi te lokahitānuraktā bhavanti bodhiguṇalābhikā hi || 13.286{316} ||

++ iti ratnāvadānatatve vaḍikāvadānaṃ samāptam ++


(rm 185)
xivgāndharvikāvadāna
athāśoko mahīpālaḥ śrotumanyatsubhāṣitam |
upaguptaṃ yatiṃ natvā prārthayadevamādarāt || 14.1{1} ||
bhadanta śrotumicchāmi punaranyatsubhāṣitam |
tadyathā guruṇākhyātaṃ tathādeṣṭuṃ ca me'rhati || 14.2{2} ||
iti tena narendreṇa prārthitaṃ sa jinātmajaḥ |
śrutvā taṃ nṛpatiṃ dṛṣṭvā punarevaṃ samādiśat || 14.3{3} ||
śṛṇu rājanmayākhyātaṃ yathā me guruṇoditam |
tathā śrūtvānumoditvā śubhe caritumarhasi || 14.4{4} ||
purā sa bhagavān buddhaḥ śrāvastyāṃ bahirāśrame |
jetāraṇye mahodyāne vihāre maṇimaṇḍite || 14.5{5} ||
bhikṣubhiḥ śrāvakaiḥ sārddhaṃ cailakairvratibhistathā |
bhikṣuṇībhistathānyaiścā mahāsātvairupāsakaiḥ || 14.6{6} ||
vihṛtya sarvasatvānāṃ samitisthitaḥ |
saṃbodhisādhānaṃ dharmamupādeṣṭuṃ samārabhat || 14.7{7} ||
tadā tasya munīndrasya vaktrapadmādvinisṛtam |
tatsaddharmāmṛtaṃ pātuṃ sarve satvā upagatāḥ || 14.8{8} ||
brahmaśakrādi devendrāḥ sarve lokādhipā api |
daityendrāḥ sagaṇā yakṣāḥ siddhagaṃdharvarākṣasāḥ || 14.9{9} ||
vidyādharagaṇāścapi nāgeṃdrāśca mahoragāḥ |
garuḍāḥ kinnarendrāśca tathānye'pi samāgatāḥ || 14.10{10} ||
brāhmaṇā ṛṣayaścāpi tāpasā brahmacāriṇaḥ |
yatayo yogino vijñāstathānye tīrthikā api || 14.11{11} ||
rajānaḥ kṣatriyāścāpi vīrā rājakumārakāḥ |
amātyā maṃtriṇaścāpi vaiśyāścāpi prajādhipāḥ || 14.12{12} ||
śreṣṭhino vaṇijaścāpi sārthavāhā mahājanāḥ |
paurā jānapadā grāmyāstathā kārpaṭikā api || 14.13{13} ||
evamanye'pi satvāśca sarve lokāḥ samāgatāḥ |
tatsaddharmāmṛtaṃ pātuṃ tadvihāre upāviśat || 14.14{14} ||
tatra taṃ śrīghanaṃ dṛṣṭvā samabhyarcya praṇamya ca |
satkṛtya samupāśritya parivṛtya sāmāntataḥ || 14.15{15} ||
taṃ muṇīndraṃ samālokya śrotuṃ taddharmadeśanām |
sāñjalayaḥ prasannāsyā upatasthuḥ samāhitāḥ || 14.16{16} ||
tathā tān samupāsīnān dṛṣṭvā sa bhagavāñjinaḥ |
āryasatyaṃ samārabhya saddharmaṃ samupādiśat || 14.17{17} ||
tatsaddharmāmṛtaṃ pītvā sarve satvāḥ pramoditāḥ |
(rm 186)
triratnabhajanaṃ kṛtvā vabhūvurbodhicāriṇaḥ || 14.18{18} ||
tadāpi paṃcamātrāṇi gāṃdharvikaśatāni ca |
śrāvastyāṃ puri jātāni samadāni pracerire || 14.19{19} ||
tadā tāṃ samadān dṛṣṭvā gaṃdharvādhipateḥ sutaḥ |
supriyo nāma tanmādamīrṣyayā hantumudyayau || 14.20{20} ||
tataḥ sa supriyaḥ sarvagāndharvikasukauśalaḥ |
divyavīṇāṃ samādāya tatraiva samupācarat || 14.21{21} ||
saptasvarānekataṃtryāmekaviṃśatimūrchanāḥ |
samudghoṣayamānaḥ ca svamatiṃ darśayan yayau || 14.22{22} ||
ṣaṇmahānagareṣvevaṃ paṭṭaneṣu samantataḥ |
samudghoṣayamānaśca śrāvastīṃ sa upāviśat || 14.23{23} ||
tatra taṃ samupāviṣṭaṃ dṛṣṭvā sarve'pi paurikāḥ |
śrutvā tadadbhutaṃ nādaṃ vismitāḥ samupācaran || 14.24{24} ||
tāni ca paṃcamātrāṇi gāṃdharvikaśatānyapi |
śrutvā dṛṣṭvā ca sarvāṇi vismayaṃ samupāyayuḥ || 14.25{25} ||
tada tāni ca sarvāṇi dṛṣṭvā paṃcaśatānyapi |
svasvamadābhimānatvāṃ pravihāya viṣedire || 14.26{26} ||
tataḥ sarve'pi te tena sahavāde bhayānvitāḥ |
nṛpaterantikaṃ gatvā tatpravṛttiṃ nyavedayan || 14.27{27} ||
jaya prabho vijānīyādyadvayaṃ samupāgatāḥ |
tadatra no bhayatrastāṃ trātumarhati sarvathā || 14.28{28} ||
atrāsau supriyo nāma gandharvādhipateḥ sutaḥ |
nūnaṃ no madaghātāya vijetuṃ samupāgataḥ || 14.29{29} ||
tattasyāpyabhimānatvāṃ nihaṃtuṃ satpratikriyām |
kriyatāṃ sahasāsmākamiti vijñāpanāṃ śṛṇu || 14.30{30} ||
iti vijñāpitaṃ sarvaistaiḥ śrutvā sa narādhipaḥ |
sarvānstān samupāmaṃtrya pura evaṃ samādiśat || 14.31{31} ||
alpotsukā bhavanto'tra bhavate mā viṣīdata |
bhaviṣyāmo'tra kālajñāścaratu sa yathechayā || 14.32{32} ||
iti rājñā samādiṣṭaṃ śrutvā te'bhyanumoditāḥ |
tadvivādabhayātaṃkaṃ tyaktvā sarve prasedire || 14.33{33} ||
tadā sa supriyastatra dṛṣṭvā tānabhimānikān |
teṣāṃ mānavighātārthaṃ manasaivaṃ vyacintayat || 14.34{34} ||
ayaṃ rājapi gāṃdharvavidyābhimāniko bhavet |
tadanena sahātrāhaṃ vādaṃ kuryāṃ sabhāśraye || 14.35{35} ||
ityevaṃ niścayaṃ kṛtvā supriyaḥ so'bhimānikaḥ |
nṛpatiṃ tamupālabdhuṃ tatsabhāṃ samupācarat || 14.36{36} ||
tatra sa nṛpatirdṛṣṭvā supriyaṃ tamupāgatam |
prajñapyāsanamāmaṃtrya praṇatvaivamabhāṣata || 14.37{37} ||
svāgataṃ te bhavannatra tiṣṭhāsane prasīda me |
kimarthe'tra samāyāsi tadādeṣṭuṃ hi me'rhasi || 14.38{38} ||
(rm 187)
iti tenoditaṃ śrutvā supriyaḥ sa sabhāsthitaḥ |
nṛpatiṃ taṃ samālokya pura evamabhāṣata || 14.39{39} ||
śṛṇu rājanmayākhyātaṃ śrutaṃ yatte yaśo mahat |
tadguṇān bhavato draṣṭumihāhaṃ samupācare || 14.40{40} ||
tanme darśaya rājendra gāndharvaguṇāmātmanaḥ |
kaste sāstā gururvāsti taṃ cāpi me pradarśaya || 14.41{41} ||
iti tenoditaṃ śrutvā sa rājā pratibhāṇavān |
supriyaṃ taṃ samālokya tathetyevamabhāṣata || 14.42{42} ||
sādhu paśya mahābhāga darśayisyāmi taṃ gurum |
yo me śāstā mahābhijñaḥ sarvavidyāguṇādhipaḥ || 14.43{43} ||
sa me śāstādhunehaiva sarvasatvahitārthataḥ |
jetāraṇye mahodyāne nivaśati vṛṣaṃ diśan || 14.44{44} ||
sādhu paśya mahābhāga taṃ guruṃ me hitārthadam |
tadehi saha yāsyāma śāstāraṃ darśayāmi tam || 14.45{45} ||
iti tena narendreṇa samākhyātaṃ sa supriyaḥ |
śrutvā tatheti pramodya gantumabhāṣata || 14.46{46} ||
sādhu rājan samichāmi śāstāraṃ tatpradarśaya |
tena saha vivādāni kuryāṃ tatra nayāśu mām || 14.47{47} ||
iti tenoditaṃ śrutvā nṛpatiḥ sa tatheti hi |
supriyaṃ taṃ samādāya vihāre gantumaichata || 14.48{48} ||
tataḥ sa nṛpatistena supriyeṇābhimāninā |
taiśca paṃcaśatairanyairgāndharvikaiḥ sahācarat || 14.49{49} ||
tasminnavasare buddho bhagavān sa munīśvaraḥ |
loke satvān samuddhartuṃ kṛpayā samapaśyata || 14.50{50} ||
tadā tena nareśena sārddhamevamupāgatam |
supriyaṃ taṃ sa ālokya manasaiva vyacintayat || 14.51{51} ||
aho vata sudhīryā sā gandharvādhipanandanaḥ |
pañcaśikho'bhidho vīṇāmādāyehopasaṃkramet || 14.52{52} ||
iti tena munīndreṇa manasā paricintitam |
matvā pañcaśikhaḥ so'pi tathāgantuṃ samaichata || 14.53{53} ||
tataḥ sa saptagaṃdharvasahasreṇa sahānvitaḥ |
vaiḍūryadaṇḍavīṇāṃ saṃprādāya sahasācaran || 14.54{54} ||
tathā sa saṃkramaṃstatra vihāre samupāviśan |
dṛṣṭvā taṃ śrīghanaṃ natvā tāṃ vīṇāṃ samupānayat || 14.55{55} ||
tataḥ sa sugataṃ bhaktyā samabhyarcya prasannadhīḥ |
tatsaddharmāmṛtaṃ pātuṃ tatsabhāyāmupāśrayat || 14.56{56} ||
tadā sa supriyo'pyenaṃ dṛṣṭvā mānābhigarvitaḥ |
tāṃ vīṇāṃ kvanayannagre'nuśrāvituṃ samārabhat || 14.57{57} ||
tatprakvāṇātsvarā sapta ekai viṃśati mūrchanāḥ |
samutthitāḥ sarvalokacetānsi paryamohayan || 14.58{58} ||
tadā sarve'pi te lokāḥ śrutvā tatprakvaṇānmudā |
nṛpatipramukhāścāpi paraṃ vismayamāyajuḥ || 14.59{59} ||
(rm 188)
tataḥ sa bhagavāṃścāpi tasya mānābhighātane |
vaiḍūryadaṇḍavīṇāṃ tāmanuśrāvitumārabhat || 14.60{60} ||
ekaikasyāṃ hi tattaṃtryā naikasvaraviśeṣitāḥ |
mūrchanāśca tathā nānāvidhā niścāritāstathā || 14.61{61} ||
tataḥ śabdātsamuccerurgāthā evaṃ pracodināḥ |
tadyatheha bhave sarvaṃ duḥkhaṃ śūnyaṃ hyanātmakam || 14.62{62} ||
idaṃ kāyaṃ ca vīṇāvadindriyāṇi svarānī ca |
mūrchanā iva cittāni jñātvā bodhau samācara || 14.63{63} ||
etadgāthāmanuśrutvā sarve lokā nṛpādayaḥ |
satyametaditi jñātvā bodhicittaṃ pralehire || 14.64{64} ||
tadā sa supriyo'pyevaṃ śrutvā matvānumoditaḥ |
paritatyāja gāṃdharvavidyāmadābhimānatām || 14.65{65} ||
tato niryātya vīṇāṃ sa gandhakuṭyāṃ virāgitaḥ |
bhagavacchāsane tatra pravrajituṃ samaichata || 14.66{66} ||
tadā taṃ śrīghanaṃ natvā supriyaḥ so'numoditaḥ |
kṛtāñjaliḥ puraḥ sthitvā pravrajyāṃ samayācata || 14.67{67} ||
vaṃde te caraṇau śāsta bhavatāṃ śaraṇaṃ vraje |
tanme'trānugrahaṃ kṛtvā pravrajyāṃ dātumarhati || 14.68{68} ||
iti tenārthitaṃ śrutvā bhagavān sa munīśvaraḥ |
dṛṣṭvā tadāśayaṃ śuddhaṃ gaṃdharvaṃ taṃ tathāvadat || 14.69{69} ||
gaṃdharva saugate dharme pravrajituṃ yadīchasi |
pitrorājñāṃ samāsādya prehi dāsyāmi te vratam || 14.70{70} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa śrutvā sa supriyo mudā |
tatheti taṃ muniṃ natvā sahasarddhyā gṛhaṃ yayau || 14.71{71} ||
tatra sa sahasā prāpto natvā tau pitarau mudā |
sāñjaliḥ purataḥ sthitvā prārthayadevamādarāt || 14.72{72} ||
śṛṇu me vāñchitaṃ tāta yadi te kṛpayā mayi |
tanmamānugrahaṃ kṛtvā saddharme māṃ niyojaya || 14.73{73} ||
ahaṃ hi saugataṃ dharmaṃ dṛṣṭvā vāñchāmi tadvratam |
tatpravrajyāṃ samāsādya prāptumichāmi nirvṛtim || 14.74{74} ||
anityaṃ khalu saṃsāraṃ tadatrāpi na me ratiḥ |
avaśyaṃ hi bhavenmṛtyuḥ sarveṣāṃ prāṇināmapi || 14.75{75} ||
evaṃ dṛṣṭvātra saṃsāre bhogyaṃ nechāmi niḥspṛhaḥ |
tatpravrajyāvrataṃ dhṛtvā prāptumiche sunirvṛtim || 14.76{76} ||
api cāsau munīndro hi sadātraiva na tiṣṭhati |
buddhakāryaṃ samāpyaiva nirvṛtiṃ sarvathā vrajet || 14.77{77} ||
kiṃ cātra sarvadā naivamutpatsyate munīśvaraḥ |
kadā cidevamutpanno loke bodhiṃ samādiśet || 14.78{78} ||
tathā ca durlabhaṃ janma sadgatau ca kṣaṇaṃ tathā |
tatkṣaṇe'labhyamāne'tra kathaṃ dharmānulapsyate || 14.79{79} ||
dharmaṃ vinātra saṃsāre kiṃ sāraṃ sukhino'pi hi |
kṣaṇadhvansi śarīraṃ hi jīvitaṃ ca tṛṇāmbuvat || 14.80{80} ||
(rm 189)
dharmeṇa sadgatiṃ yāyātsaṃbodhimapi prāpnuyāt |
tatpravrajyācariṃ dhṛtvā prāptumiche'tra nirvṛtim || 14.81{81} ||
tadātra yadi vāṃ tāta kṛpāsti svātmaje mayi |
sarvathānugrahaṃ kṛtvā tadanujñāṃ pradehi me || 14.82{82} ||
iti tenārthitaṃ śrutvā sa gandharvādhipaḥ sudhīḥ |
suciraṃ taṃ sutaṃ dṛṣṭvā snehādevamabhaṣata || 14.83{83} ||
satyamevaṃ sutākhyātaṃ tathāpi me vacaḥ śṛṇu |
pravrajyā duṣkarīcaryyā tatkathaṃ tvaṃ samācareḥ || 14.84{84} ||
tvaṃ hi yuvā pramatto'si madanākrāṃtamānasaḥ |
kāmabhogyasukhāraktastatkathaṃ tadvrataṃ careḥ || 14.85{85} ||
pravrajito viraktātmā saṃsārabhogyanispṛhaḥ |
sarvān parigrahānstyaktvā sadaikāntī vane vase || 14.86{86} ||
sukule duḥkule cāpi yācitvā viraśāsanam |
bhuṃjīthāstatkathaṃ bhuktvā samādhiṃ saṃpradhāsyasi || 14.87{87} ||
ityevaṃ duṣkaraṃ jñatvā pravrajyāvratasādhane |
sahasā mā kṛthāścittaṃ nivāraya samāhitaḥ || 14.88{88} ||
yadyativāṃchase putra pravrajyāgrahaṇe tathā |
vṛddhatve samupāśritya triratnaṃ tadvrataṃ cara || 14.89{89} ||
tattvaṃ yāvadyuvā gehe saukhyaṃ bhuktvā yathepsitam |
vṛddhatve tadvrataṃ dhṛtvā triratnāni sadā bhaja || 14.90{90} ||
tathā cette bhavejjanma sāphalyaṃ bhavacāraṇe |
sukhaṃ bhuktvā vrataṃ dhṛtvā nirvṛtiṃ vā samāpnuyāḥ || 14.91{91} ||
iti me vacanaṃ śrutvā matvaivaṃ tatprabodhitaḥ |
tāvadgṛhe sukhaṃ bhuktvā smṛtvā ratnatrayaṃ vasa || 14.92{92} ||
iti pitroditaṃ śrutvā supriyaḥ so'bhiśaṃkitaḥ |
punastaṃ pitaraṃ dṛṣṭvā bodhayannevamabravīt || 14.93{93} ||
satyamevaṃ bhave tāta tathāpi vakṣyate mayā |
tacchrutvāpi tvayā parijñāya vibudhyatām || 14.94{94} ||
satkule durlabhaṃ janma tatrāpi durlabhaṃ kṣaṇam |
durlabhaṃ sugatotpādaṃ durlabhaṃ tadvrataṃ bhave || 14.95{95} ||
iti matvātra saṃsāre kleśamānamadākule |
avaśyaṃ maraṇaṃ dṛṣṭvā prāptumiche'tra nirvṛtim || 14.96{96} ||
tadatra kṛpayā mahyamanujñāṃ dātumarhasi |
yūyaṃ cāpi hi taddharmabhāṇaiḥ sadgatimāpsyatha || 14.97{97} ||
iti hetoḥ pita tatra vilambaṃma kṛthā ciram |
sahasānugrahaṃ kṛtvā tadanujñāṃ pradehi me || 14.98{98} ||
iti tenārthitaṃ śrutvā sa gaṃdharvādhipaścirāt |
snehaduḥkhāgnisaṃtapto galadaśrumukho'vadat || 14.99{99} ||
ayi supriya putra tvaṃ snehānmayā nivāryasa |
yadyativāṃchase prāptuṃ nirvṛtiṃ tan samācara || 14.100{100} ||
saṃbuddhaśaraṇaṃ kṛtvā pravrajya saṃvaraṃ dadhat |
(rm 190)
triratnabhajanaṃ kṛtvā cara nityaṃ samāhitaḥ || 14.101{1} ||
iti pitroditaṃ śrutvā supriyaḥ so'bhimoditaḥ |
pitroḥ pādān praṇatvaiva sahasā niryayau gṛhāt || 14.102{2} ||
tataḥ sa sahasā gatvā vihāraṃ samupācarat |
tatra prāpto munīndraṃ taṃ dṛṣṭvaiva samupāviśat || 14.103{3} ||
tatra taṃ śrīghanaṃ nātvā prāñjaliḥ pura āśrayan |
suprasannamukhāṃbhojaḥ prārthayadevamādarāt || 14.104{4} ||
sarvajña bhagavāñchāstarādiṣṭaṃ bhavatāyathā |
tathānujñāṃ pituḥ prāpya prāgatāhamihādhunā || 14.105{5} ||
tadatra bhavatāṃ śāstaḥ śaraṇaṃ samupāgataḥ |
brahmacaryaṃ cariṣyāmi tatpravrajyāṃ pradehi me || 14.106{6} ||
iti tenārthitaṃ śrutvā bhagavān sa jagadguruḥ |
dṛṣṭvā tasyāśayaṃ śuddhaṃ punareva samādiśat || 14.107{7} ||
ehi supriya bhikṣo'tra cara brahmacariṃ śivām |
triratnaṃ śaraṇaṃ kṛtvā samādhiṃ samupāśrayat || 14.108{8} ||
tato dṛṣṭvā ca saṃsāraṃ kleśasaṃghasamākulam |
paṃcagaṃḍamayaṃ dehaṃ jīvitaṃ ca trināmvuvat || 14.109{9} ||
samādhisaṃvaraṃ dhṛtvā saṃbodhisādhanodyataḥ |
sarvakleśaripuñjitvā sākṣādarhatpadaṃ yayau || 14.110{10} ||
tataḥ supriyo bhikṣuḥ prāptābhijñaḥ kṛtī sudhīḥ |
bhindan sarvamavidyāṅgaṃ nirvikalpo'bhavadyatiḥ || 14.111{11} ||
samaloṣṭasuvarṇaśca pariśuddhatrimaṃḍalaḥ |
vāsīcaṃdanakalpo'bhūdākāśasamamānasaḥ || 14.112{12} ||
saṃsārabhogyasatkāramānyalābhaparāṅmukhaḥ |
niḥkleśaḥ pariṣuddhātmā brahmacārī niraṃjanaḥ || 14.113{13} ||
sadevāsuralokānāṃ sarvabhuvanavāsinām |
vaṃdyaḥ pūjyo'bhinaṃdyaśca mānanīyo bhavan babhau || 14.114{14} ||
tadaivaṃbhūtamarhantaṃ supriyaṃ taṃ śubhendriyam |
prālokya vismitāḥ sarve tatsabhāsthāḥ prasedire || 14.115{15} ||
tadā sarve'pi te lokā devā daityāśca kinnarāḥ |
yakṣāḥ siddhāśca gaṃdharvā nāgāśca rākṣasā api || 14.116{16} ||
garuḍāśca tathānye'pi vidyādharāśca sāpsarāḥ |
sarve lokādhipāścāpi triratnaśaraṇaṃ gatāḥ || 14.117{17} ||
saṃbuddhaśāsane tatra sāṃghikānāṃ samantataḥ |
rakṣāvaraṇasaṃguptiṃ kṛtvābhajan samudyatāḥ || 14.118{18} ||
taddṛṣṭvā tāni pañcāpi gāṃdharvikaśatāni ca |
anumodya prasannāni saṃmīlyaivaṃ vabhāṣire || 14.119{19} ||
ete sarve vayaṃ nīce karmaṇi parivarttikāḥ |
sukhānabhijñaduḥkhārttāḥ kleśinaḥ kṛchravṛttayaḥ || 14.120{20} ||
yadasmābhiḥ purā puṇyaṃ kiṃ cidvāpi na sādhitam |
tenāsyātra vayaṃ sarve duḥkhino nīcavṛttayaḥ || 14.121{21} ||
tadasmābhirayaṃ śāstā munīśvaraḥ sasāṃghikaḥ |
satkṛtya bhojanairvāpi pūjanīyo yathāvidhiḥ || 14.122{22} ||
nūnametadvipākena bhavemahi subhāginaḥ |
(rm 191)
tataḥ puṇyānusāreṇa bodhimārge caremahi || 14.123{23} ||
tataḥ krameṇa saṃprāpya bodhicaryyāṃ śubhāṃ carīm |
carantastadvipākaiśca nirvṛtiṃ saṃlabhemahi || 14.124{24} ||
iti matvā vayaṃ sarve vijñapya nṛpatiṃ tathā |
sasaṃghaṃ sugataṃ bhojyairarhāmahe samarcitum || 14.125{25} ||
tatheti saṃmataṃ kṛtvā sarve te'bhyanumoditāḥ |
nṛpatervijñāpanāṃ kartuṃ sahasā samupācaran || 14.126{26} ||
tatra te samupāsṛtya dṛṣṭvā taṃ nṛpatiṃ mudā |
kṛtāñjalipuṭo natvā prārthayannevamādarāt || 14.127{27} ||
jaya deva mahārāja lokān dharmeṇa pālaya |
kiñcidvijñāpanāṃ karttuṃ vayamupāgatāḥ || 14.128{28} ||
vijānīyādbhavān hyetattathāpi prārthayāmahe |
śrutvā no'nugrahaṃ kṛtvā kṛpayā tatprasīdatu || 14.129{29} ||
yadvayaṃ deva sarve'tra nīcakarmānucāriṇaḥ |
tatsasaṃghaṃ muniṃ bhojyaiḥ samichāmaḥ samarcitum || 14.130{30} ||
tadanujñāṃ pradatvā naḥ kṛpayoddhartumarhasi |
etadvijñāpanāṃ kurmastatprasīda nṛpādhipa || 14.131{31} ||
iti taiḥ prārthitaṃ śrutvā nṛpatiḥ so'numoditaḥ |
tān sarvān samupāmaṃtrya punarevaṃ samādiśat || 14.132{32} ||
sādhu bhavatu sarveṣāṃ yuṣmākaṃ satsamīhitam |
tattathā kriyatāṃ buddhe satkāraṃ śraddhayādarāt || 14.133{33} ||
saṃbuddhe yatkṛtaṃ karma tatphalaṃ kṣiṇuyānna hi |
kramādbodhicariṃ pūrya nirvṛtiṃ samavāpnuyāt || 14.134{34} ||
tadatra sarvathā yūyaṃ sasaṃghaṃ śrīghanaṃ munim |
yathārhabhojanaṃ bhaktyā pūjayadhvaṃ samādarāt || 14.135{35} ||
ityādiṣṭam ḥ - ndreṇa śrutvā sarve'pi te mudā |
tatheti pratinaṃditvā nṛpaṃ natvā tato'caran || 14.136{36} ||
tataste saṃmataṃ kṛtvā vihāre sahasā gatāḥ |
dṛṣṭvā taṃ śrīghanaṃ natvā prārthayannevamādarāt || 14.137{37} ||
bhagavaṃstadvijānīyādyadarthaṃ samupāgatāḥ |
tathāpi prārthayeyaivamanugrahītumarhati || 14.138{38} ||
yadvayaṃ bhavatāṃ sarvasāṃghikānāṃ yathāvidhim |
pūjayituṃ samichāmo satkartuṃ bhojanairapi || 14.139{39} ||
tadbhavāṃ kṛpayā dṛṣṭvā kṛtvāsmākamanugraham |
pure śvaḥ sāṃghikaiḥ sārddhaṃ vijayituṃ samarhasi || 14.140{40} ||
iti taiḥ prārthite sarvairbhagavān sa munīśvaraḥ |
teṣāṃ dharmapravṛddhyarthaṃ tūṣṇībhūtvādhyuvāsa tat || 14.141{41} ||
tatassarve'pi te matvā bhagavatādhivāsitam |
bhūyastaṃ śrīghanaṃ natvā svaṃ puraṃ sahasā yayuḥ || 14.142{42} ||
tatra sarve'pi te bhūmiṃ śodhayitvā samantataḥ |
prajñapya svāsanānyevaṃ sāmagriṃ samasādhayan || 14.143{43} ||
(rm 192)
tataḥ pūjopahārāṇi bhojyāṅgāni rasāni ca |
peyāni supraṇītāni sādhayitvā pramoditāḥ || 14.144{44} ||
tatra taṃ sugataṃ sarvaṃ saṃghaṃ cāmaṃtrituṃ yayuḥ |
vihāre samupasṛtya śrīghanaṃ saṃpraṇemire || 14.145{45} ||
kṛtāñjalipuṭo natvā sarve te samupasthitāḥ |
saṃbuddhapramukhaṃ saṃghaṃ prārthayannevamādarāt || 14.146{46} ||
sarvajña bhagavaṃ chāstaḥ samayo varttate'dhunā |
tadbhavān saṃghikaiḥ sārddhaṃ vijayituṃ samarhati || 14.147{47} ||
iti taiḥ prārthite dṛṣṭvā bhagavān sa sasāṃghikaḥ |
pātracīvaramādaya pratasthe saṃprabhāsayan || 14.148{48} ||
tatra mārgeṣu sarvatra prātihāryaṃ pradarśayan |
sarveṣāṃ śubhatāṃ kṛtvā tatpure samupāviśat || 14.149{49} ||
pradakṣiṇakrameṇaivaṃ caraṃstatra samaṃtataḥ |
bhāsayaṃ bhadratāṃ kurvan tatpradeśe samācaran || 14.150{50} ||
tatra pādyārghamādāya krameṇa saha sāṃghikam |
svāsane samupāviśya tasthau bhānurivojvalan || 14.151{51} ||
tadā taṃ śrīghanaṃ sarvasaṃghaṃ ca svāsane sthitam |
dṛṣṭvā sarve'pi te harṣātpūjayituṃ samārabhan || 14.152{52} ||
tathā krameṇa saṃpūjya taṃ buddhapramukhaṃ gaṇam |
yathārhabhojanaiḥ sarvaṃ saṃtṛptaṃ samatoṣayan || 14.153{53} ||
tataste bhojanāṃte tatpātrāṇi vyapanīya ca |
karādīñchodhayitvā tadbhūmiṃ ca paryaśodhayan || 14.154{54} ||
tataḥ pūgādikaṃ datvā punaḥ sarve'pi te mudā |
kṛtāñjalipuṭā natvā samupatasthire punaḥ || 14.155{55} ||
te ca gāṃdharvikāḥ sarve triratnaśaraṇaṃ gatāḥ |
bodhipraṇidhimādhāya prabhejire samāhitāḥ || 14.156{56} ||
veṇuvīṇāmṛdaṃgādi tūryasaṃgītivādanaiḥ |
upasthitya sasaṃghaṃ taṃ śrīghanaṃ samatoṣayat || 14.157{57} ||
tada sa bhagavāṃ dṛṣṭvā teṣāṃ bhaktiprasādinīm |
sarveṣāṃ śuddhacittāni vyamuñcatsmitamuddhavāt || 14.158{58} ||
tatsaṃsmitasahotpannāḥ paṃcavarṇāḥ śubhāṃśavaḥ |
sarvatra bhuvane gatvā praceruramṛtāṃśuvat || 14.159{59} ||
teṣāṃ ke cidadhogatvā narakeṣu samantataḥ |
avabhāsya parispṛṣṭvā sarvatrāpi sukhaṃ daduḥ || 14.160{60} ||
tadā te nārakīyāstatprabhāspṛṣṭāḥ sukhānvitāḥ |
kimetaditi saṃdigdhāḥ saṃmīlyaivaṃ vabhāṣire || 14.161{61} ||
aho nu jāyate saukhyamasmākaṃ sāṃprataṃ katham |
