vatānyatra gatāḥ smo'dya kasya puṇyānubhāvataḥ || 14.162{62} ||
iti saṃdigdhacittānāṃ teṣāṃ manaḥ prabodhane |
bhagavānnirmitaṃ buddhaṃ saṃpreṣayan samaṃtataḥ || 14.163{63} ||
tatrā taṃ saugataṃ dṛṣṭvā sarve te narakasthitāḥ |
vismitāḥ samupāsṛtya vavandire prasāditāḥ || 14.164{64} ||
(rm 193)
namo buddhāya dharmāya saṃghāya ca namassadā |
iti proktvā praṇatvā taccharaṇaṃ samupāyayuḥ || 14.165{65} ||
etatpuṇyānubhāvena sarve te narakotthitāḥ |
sahasā svargatiṃ yātāḥ saddharme samudācaran || 14.166{66} ||
evaṃ tān sarvasatvāṃśca samuddhṛtya samantataḥ |
sarve te'pyaṃśavo bhūyassaṃbuddhāntikamupācaran || 14.167{67} ||
ke cidūrddhvagatā yāvadakaniṣṭhaṃ samantataḥ |
avabhāsya samudghuṣya tatraivaṃ samacodayan || 14.168{68} ||
anityaṃ khalu saṃsāraṃ śūnyaṃ hyanātmakam |
iti satyaṃ parijñāya triratnaṃ bhajatādarāt || 14.169{69} ||
niṣkrāmatārabhadhvaṃ ca yūjyadhvaṃ buddhaśāsane |
dhunīta mārasainyāśca naḍāgāraṃ yathā karī || 14.170{70} ||
yo'pyasmin saugate dharme hyapramattaścaretsadā |
sa tyaktvā janma saṃsāre duḥkhasyāntaṃ kariṣyati || 14.171{71} ||
iti saṃcodayantastāṃ sārvān devāṃśca sarvataḥ |
te sarve'pyaṃśavo bhāyaḥ sugatāntikamāyayuḥ || 14.172{72} ||
tataste'pyaṃśavaḥ sarve saṃmīlya saṃgatā muneḥ |
pradakṣiṇatrayaṃ kṛtvā tasyorṇāyāṃ samāviśan || 14.173{73} ||
taddṛṣṭvā te sabhālokā vismayoddhatamānasāḥ |
kimetaditi saṃcintya sarve tasthuḥ śakautukāḥ || 14.174{74} ||
athānaṃdaḥ samālokya tān sarvān vismayoddhavān |
utthāya sāñjalirnatvā śāstāraṃ taṃ samabravīt || 14.175{75} ||
bhagavanste mukhādbhāsiḥ paṃcavarṇā vinirgatā |
avabhāsya diśaḥ sarvā ūrṇāyāṃ ca viśanti tāḥ || 14.176{76} ||
etaddṛṣṭvā sabhālokāḥ sarve'pīme savismayāḥ |
taddhetuṃ śrotumichanti tadupādeṣṭumarhati || 14.177{77} ||
nāhetu hi smitaṃ buddhā darśayanti kadā cana |
yaddhetau bhavatā śāsturhasitaṃ tatsamādiśa || 14.178{78} ||
iti saṃprārthitaṃ śiṣyena sa munīśvaraḥ |
tamānaṃdaiḥ sabhāṃ cāpi samālokyaivamādiśat || 14.179{79} ||
paśyānaṃda yadetairhi sarvai gāndharvikairapi |
mama sasāṃghikasyaivaṃ satkāraṃ prakṛtaṃ mudā || 14.180{80} ||
etatpuṇyavipākena sarve'pīme bhavāntare |
kramādbodhicarīṃ prāptā niḥkleśā vimalāśayāḥ || 14.181{81} ||
māracaryāvinirmuktāḥ pariśuddhāstrimaṇḍalāḥ |
varṇasvarābhidhāḥ sarve pratyekasugatā jināḥ || 14.182{82} ||
prāntāśayyāsānāsīnā hīnadīnānukampakāḥ |
dakṣiṇīyā mahāsatvā bhaviṣyanti śubhaṃkarāḥ || 14.183{83} ||
evametātsamālokya yūyaṃ yadi samichatha |
tathātra śraddhayā nityaṃ triratnaṃ bhajatādarāt || 14.184{84} ||
ye triratnaṃ sadā bhaktyā satkṛtyaivaṃ bhajanti te |
vihāya durgatiṃ nityaṃ sadgatiṃ samavāpnuyuḥ || 14.185{85} ||
(rm 194)
iti matvātra saṃsāre yeṣāṃ vāṃchā sukhe śive |
te satkṛtya triratneṣu śraddhayā bhajatāniśam || 14.186{86} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa śrutvā sarve ca te janāḥ |
satyamevaṃ pratijñāya triratnaṃ sarvadābhajan || 14.187{87} ||
taddṛṣṭvā bhikṣavaḥ sarve vismayoddhatamānasāḥ |
kṛtāñjalipuṭā natvā paprachustaṃ munīśvaram || 14.188{88} ||
yadevaṃ bhagavannebhiḥ satkāraṃ prakṛtaṃ mahat |
tatte kena supuṇyena samyagādeṣṭumarhati || 14.189{89} ||
evaṃ tairbhikṣubhiḥ sarvaiḥ prārthite sa jineśvaraḥ |
sarvānstāñchrāvakān bhikṣūn samālokyaivamādiśat || 14.190{90} ||
śṛṇudhvaṃ bhikṣavaḥ puṇyaṃ yanmayā prakṛtaṃ purā |
tadatra kathitaṃ śrutvā śraddhayābhyanumodata || 14.191{91} ||
purāsīdbhagavān buddhaḥ prabodhanābhidho jinaḥ |
sarvajñaḥ sugataḥ śāstā dharmarājo munīśvaraḥ || 14.192{92} ||
sarvavidyādhipo nāthastathāgato vināyakaḥ |
mārajillokavidbharttā subhadro'rhan hitaṃkaraḥ || 14.193{93} ||
tadā sa bhagavān buddho janapadeṣu cārikām |
carannanyāṃ rājadhānīmanuprāptaḥ sasāṃghikaḥ || 14.194{94} ||
tatra tasya narendrasya krīḍodyāne taroradhaḥ |
praviśya svāsanāsīnastasthau vahnirivojvalan || 14.195{95} ||
tadaikasmin dine tatra rājā kāntāpravādinaiḥ |
saṃgītaiḥ saha saṃrakto ramituṃ samupāviśat || 14.196{96} ||
tatrasthaṃ taṃ samāsīnaṃ sugataṃ suprabhojvalam |
dṛṣṭvaiva sahasopetya natvā kṛtvā pradakṣiṇām || 14.197{97} ||
samādhisthaṃ tamālokya taṣṭhau ciramupāśrayan |
tatastanmukhapadmotthasaddharmāmṛtalipsayā || 14.198{98} ||
vādyamānaiḥ satairvādyairmuniṃ prābodhayannṛpaḥ |
tataḥ sa sugato buddho samādheḥ śīghramutthitaḥ || 14.199{99} ||
sāntaḥpuraṃ nareṃdraṃ taṃ prādrākṣītsamupasthitam |
tadā taṃ nṛpatiṃ dṛṣṭvā sa saṃbuddhaḥ prabodhanaḥ || 14.200{100} ||
saddharmmaṃ samupādeṣṭuṃ samāmaṃtryaivamabravīt |
ehi rājanmahāvāho svāgataṃ te mahīpate || 14.201{1} ||
saddharmaṃ samupāśritya bodhicaryāṃ samācara |
sadā dānaṃ pradatvātra śīlaṃ dhṛtvā samāhitaḥ || 14.202{2} ||
kṣāntyā prasādayallokān dharmavīryaṃ prasādhaya |
bodhau cittaṃ samādhāya prajñāratnamupārjaya || 14.203{3} ||
tadratnānubhāvena saṃbodhimārgamāpnuyāḥ |
tataste maṅgalaṃ nityamihāmutre sadā bhavet || 14.204{4} ||
sarvasatvahitaṃ kurvan sadgatimeva prāpnuyāḥ |
iti matvā mahārāja saddharmaṃ samupāśrayan || 14.205{5} ||
triratnaṃ śaraṇaṃ kṛtvā satkṛtya bhaja sarvadā |
triratneṣu kṛtaṃ puṇyaṃ na kṣiṇoti kadā cana || 14.206{6} ||
(rm 195)
sarvadā satphalānyeva sūte bodhiṃ samarpayet |
iti matvā mahārāja triratnaśaraṇaṃ gataḥ || 14.207{7} ||
bodhicaryāvrataṃ dhṛtvā cara nityaṃ samāhitaḥ |
tathā te maṃgalaṃ nityaṃ sarvatrāpi bhaveddhruvam || 14.208{8} ||
kramādbodhiṃ samāsādya saṃbuddhapadamāpnuyāḥ |
iti tenārhatādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa sa bhūpatiḥ || 14.209{9} ||
śrutvā tatheti vijñapya prābhyanandatprabodhitaḥ |
tataḥ sa mudito rājā taṃ munīndraṃ sasāṃghikam || 14.210{10} ||
yathāvidhi samabhyarcya bhojanaiḥ samatoṣayat |
tataśca bhojanānte sa nṛpatiḥ sāñjalirmudā |
taṃ munīndraṃ praṇatvaivaṃ praṇidhānaṃ hṛdo vyadhāt || 14.211{11} ||
anena kuśalenāhaṃ loke'ndhe'pariṇāyake |
samyagbodhiṃ samāgamya bhaveyaṃ sugato jinaḥ || 14.212{12} ||
etattena narendreṇa praṇidhānaṃ kṛtaṃ mudā |
matvā sa sugatastasya tathāśīrvacanaṃ dadau || 14.213{13} ||
jayo'stu te sadā rājaṃ dharmeṇa pālaya prajāḥ |
praṇidhānaṃ yathā bodhau tathā te siddhyatu dhruvam || 14.214{14} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa tena śrutvā sa bhūpatiḥ |
muditaḥ sāñjalirnatvā taṃ munīndraṃ samabravīt || 14.215{15} ||
adyārabhya sadā śāstastriratnaśaraṇaṃ gataḥ |
saṃbodhisaṃvaraṃ dhṛtvā cariṣyāmi samāhitaḥ || 14.216{16} ||
tadbhavān kṛpayā dṛṣṭyā sadā māṃ draṣṭumarhati |
sarvadā bhavatāmeva śaraṇe'smi samāsthitaḥ || 14.217{17} ||
iti saṃprārthanāṃ kṛtvā rājā sa sajano mudā |
bhagavaṃtaṃ tamānamya sānaṃdaḥ svapuraṃ yayau || 14.218{18} ||
tataḥ sa bhagavāñchāstā samutthāya sasāṃghikaḥ |
sarvatra bhadratāṃ kṛtvā svāśrame samupāyayau || 14.219{19} ||
manyatāṃ bhikṣavo yo'sau rājā saṃbuddhasevakaḥ |
ahameva tadābhūvannānyo draṣṭavya eva saḥ || 14.220{20} ||
yanmayā śraddhayā tasya prabodhanasya tāyinaḥ |
pūjākāri mahotsāhaistūryasaṃgītivādanaiḥ || 14.221{21} ||
tena puṇyavipākena mamātraiva mahotsavaiḥ |
etairgāndharvikairevaṃ satkāraḥ prakṛtādhunā || 14.222{22} ||
evaṃ matvā mahatpuṇyaṃ saṃbuddhabhajanodbhavam |
triratnaṃ śaraṇaṃ kṛtvā bhajanīyaṃ śubhārthibhiḥ || 14.223{23} ||
yūyaṃ cāpi sadā nityaṃ śāstāraṃ sadguruṃ jinam |
saṃbuddhaṃ samupāśritya viharadhvaṃ samāhitāḥ || 14.224{24} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa śrutvā te bhikṣavo mudā |
sarve tatheti vijñapya triratnāni sadābhajan || 14.225{25} ||
iti me guruṇādiṣṭaṃ śrutaṃ mayā narādhipa |
tathādhunā samākhyātaṃ tava puṇyapravṛddhaye || 14.226{26} ||
evaṃ rājaṃstvayāpyevaṃ triratnaṃ śaraṇaṃ gataḥ |
saṃbuddhaśāsanaṃ dhṛtvā cara dharmaṃ samāhitaḥ || 14.227{27} ||
(rm 196)
prajāścāpi tathā rājaṃ bodhayitvā prayatnataḥ |
saṃbodhisādhane dharme yojanīyāstvayādarāt || 14.228{28} ||
tathā cette sadā bhadraṃ bhavennūnaṃ samantataḥ |
kramādbodhiṃ samāsādya saṃbuddhapadamāpnuyāḥ || 14.229{29} ||
iti śāstrā samādeṣṭaṃ śrutvāśokaḥ sa bhūpatiḥ |
tatheti pratimoditvā prābhyanandatsapārṣadaḥ || 14.230{30} ||
gāṃdharvikānāmavadānametacchrutvānumodanti narā mudā ye |
te sarva enaḥparimuktacittā bhuktvā sukhānyeva labhanti bodhim |

++ iti ratnāvadānatatve gāṃdharvikāvādanaṃ samāptam ++


(rm 197)
xvsūkṣmatvacovadāna
athāśoko mahīpālaḥ kṛtāṃjalipuṭo mudā |
upaguptaṃ guruṃ natvā punarevamabhāṣata || 15.1{1} ||
bhadanta śrotumichāmi punaranyatsubhāṣitam |
tadyathā guruṇākhyātaṃ tathādeṣṭuṃ ca me'rhasi || 15.2{2} ||
iti saṃprārthite rājñā sa yatiḥ sugatātmajaḥ |
aśokaṃ taṃ nṛpaṃ dṛṣṭvā punarevamupādiśat || 15.3{3} ||
śṛṇu rājanmahābhāga yathā me guruṇoditam |
tathāhaṃ te pravakṣyāmi śrutvāpyevaṃ pramodaya || 15.4{4} ||
purā sa bhagavān buddhaḥ śākyasiṃho jagadguruḥ |
vihāre jetakāraṇye vijahāra sasāṃghikaḥ || 15.5{5} ||
tasmiṃśca samaye tatra sarve bhikṣugaṇā api |
śrāvakāścailakāścaiva sarva upāsakā api || 15.6{6} ||
sarve'pi bhikṣuṇīsaṃghā upāsikāgaṇā api |
bodhisatvagaṇāścāpi saddharmasādhanodyatāḥ || 15.7{7} ||
sabhāmadhyāsanāsīnaṃ śrīghanaṃ taṃ jagadgurum |
upatasthuḥ puraskṛtya parivṛtya samaṃtataḥ || 15.8{8} ||
tadā tasya munīndrasya mukhāmbhojavinirgatam |
bhadradharmāmṛtaṃ pātuṃ sarve lokāḥ samāgatāḥ || 15.9{9} ||
śakrādayaḥ surendrāśca brahmādayo maharddhikāḥ |
lokapālā mahāvīrāḥ sasainyaparivārakāḥ || 15.10{10} ||
yogino yatayaścāpi maharṣayastapaśvinaḥ |
brāhmaṇāśca mahāvijñāḥ saddharmaguṇavāṃchinaḥ || 15.11{11} ||
rājāno rājaputrāśca vaiśyā maṃtrijanā api |
amātyāḥ śreṣṭhino bhaṭṭāḥ paurikāśca gṛhādhipāḥ || 15.12{12} ||
sainyādhipāśca yodhāraḥ sārthavahā mahājanāḥ |
vaṇijaḥ śilpinaścāpi grāmyāḥ kārpaṭikā api || 15.13{13} ||
evamanye'pi lokāśca saddharmaguṇavāñchinaḥ |
sarve dharmāmṛtaṃ pātuṃ vihāre samupāgatāḥ || 15.14{14} ||
tatra prāptāḥ sabhāmadhye samāsīnaṃ munīśvaram |
śrīghanaṃ taṃ samālokya praṇatvā samupācaran || 15.15{15} ||
tatra te samupāsṛtya sarve taṃ śrīghanaṃ munim |
tridhā pradakṣiṇīkṛtvā samabhyarcya praṇemire || 15.16{16} ||
tataḥ sarve'pi te lokāḥ parivṛtya samantataḥ |
sāñjalayaḥ prasannāsyā upatasthuḥ samāhitāḥ || 15.17{17} ||
tadā sa bhagavān dṛṣṭvā sarvāṃstān samupasthitān |
ādimadhyāntakalyāṇamāryadharmamupādiśat || 15.18{18} ||
tatsaddharmāmṛtaṃ pītvā sarve te tridaśādayaḥ |
(rm 198)
lokā dharmaviśeṣatvaṃ vijñāya prāṇanandire || 15.19{19} ||
tasminnavasare tatra śrāvastyāṃ pauriko gṛhī |
śreṣṭhī mahājanaḥ śrimāñchrāddho bhadrāśayābhavat || 15.20{20} ||
sa ekasmiṃ dine svasmin bhāṇḍālakoṣṭhake viśan |
svasaṃpannicayaṃ dṛṣṭvā manasaivaṃ vyacintayat || 15.21{21} ||
aho me jāyate saṃpaddīyatī saṃpravarddhitā |
tathāpi varddhyate tṛṣṇā yataḥ saukhyaṃ na kiṃ cana || 15.22{22} ||
yadime caṃcalā bhogā jalacaṃdranibhā mama |
anityā asthirāḥ padmapatrasthitajalopamā || 15.23{23} ||
tadatra kiṃ mano'dyāpi tṛṣṇayākṛṣyate mama |
yena carāmyahaṃ loke duḥkhī bhavikalāśayaḥ || 15.24{24} ||
tatkiṃ me saṃpadā sāraṃ yataḥ saukhyaṃ na vidyate |
dharmārthe gocaraṃ naiva kevalaṃ madavṛddhaye || 15.25{25} ||
madena varddhate mānaṃ mānālloke pramādatā |
pramādāddīpyate cerṣyā kopāgnirdīpyate tataḥ || 15.26{26} ||
kopātkrūrāśayaścaṇḍo nirdayo duritāśayaḥ |
pāpātmā hiṃsako duṣṭo daśākuśalasādhakaḥ || 15.27{27} ||
svayaṃ naṣṭaḥ parāṃścāpi nāśayati durāśayaḥ |
tato'tipāpasaṃrakto durācaro'tidurmanāḥ || 15.28{28} ||
tato'tidurmatirdrohī saṃvṛttidharmaniṃdakaḥ |
triratnāni pratikṣipya mahāpātakamācaret || 15.29{29} ||
tato'tipāpasaṃkliṣṭāḥ pramūḍho narakaṃ vrajet |
narakeṣu bhramannityaṃ duḥkhānyeva sadāśnuyāt || 15.30{30} ||
yadvātrāpi ca saṃsāre jīvitamadhruvaṃ khalu |
kṣaṇadhvaṃsi śarīraṃ ca sārveṣāṃ maraṇaṃ dhruvam || 15.31{31} ||
tadatrāhamasārebhyo bhogebhyaḥ sāramuttamam |
saddharmaṃ samupādātuṃ yatnaṃ kuryāṃ samādarāt || 15.32{32} ||
saddharmaṃ saugataṃ dharmamityākhyātaṃ purāvidaiḥ |
tadatra saugate kṣetre dānaṃ kurvīya sāṃpratam || 15.33{33} ||
bauddhakṣetre kṛtaṃ dānaṃ bodhivījaṃ bhaveddhruvam |
etadevātra saṃsāre sādhanīyaṃ śubhāptaye || 15.34{34} ||
dānena śuddhyate cittaṃ sucittaḥ sumatiṃ labhet |
subuddhiḥ sadguṇāraktaḥ saṃvṛttiśīlabhṛtkṛtī || 15.35{35} ||
suśīlaḥ kṣāntimān dhīraḥ parātmasamabhāvikaḥ |
sudhīro vīryavān sarvasatvārthasādhanodyataḥ || 15.36{36} ||
apradhṛṣyo mahāsatvaḥ kleśāridamako bhavet |
tataḥ supariśuddhātmā samādhisusthito bhavet || 15.37{37} ||
tatsamādhivalādhānaiḥ prajñābdhipāramāgataḥ |
bodhiratnaṃ samāsādya bodhisatvo'dhipo bhavet || 15.38{38} ||
etadratnānubhāvena bhadraṃkalā samantataḥ |
(rm 199)
sarvamārān vinirjitya saṃbuddhapadamāpnuyāt || 15.39{39} ||
ityarhadbhirmahābhijñaiḥ samākhyātaṃ mayā śrutam |
tatsaṃbuddhapadāṃ praptuṃ buddhaṃ bhajeya sāṃpratam || 15.40{40} ||
nūnaṃ buddhānubhāvena bhāvitaṃ setsyate dhruvam |
tadbuddhapramukhaṃ sarvasaṃghaṃ pūjeya sādaram || 15.41{41} ||
iti niścitya sa śrīmān gṛhasthaḥ saṃpramoditaḥ |
bhagavaṃtaṃ sasaṃghan taṃ pūjayituṃ samaichata || 15.42{42} ||
tataḥ sa sajanaḥ śreṣṭhī triratnabhajanotsukaḥ |
vihāre jetakāraṇye prayayau saṃpraharṣitaḥ || 15.43{43} ||
tatra taṃ śrīghanaṃ dṛṣṭvā sa gṛhasthaḥ pramoditaḥ |
natvā pradakṣiṇīkṛtya samarcayadyathāvidhiḥ || 15.44{44} ||
tataḥ sa sajano natvā sāñjaliḥ saṃpramoditaḥ |
munīndraṃ taṃ samālokya puraḥ sthitvaivamabravīt || 15.45{45} ||
sarvajña bhagavaṃ chāstarbhavatāṃ śaraṇaṃ vraje |
tato'bhilaṣite kārye prasīdatu jagadguro || 15.46{46} ||
bhagavaṃ bhavataḥ śāstuḥ sasaṃghasya jagadguroḥ |
pūjayituṃ samichāmi tadanujñāṃ dadātu me || 15.47{47} ||
iti saṃprārthite tena bhagavān sa munīśvaraḥ |
tathāstviti pratijñāya tūṣṇībhūtvādhyuvāsa tat || 15.48{48} ||
tataḥ sa gṛhabhṛnmatvā bhagavatādhyuvāsa tam |
sāñjalistaṃ muniṃ natvā muditaḥ svagṛhaṃ yayau || 15.49{49} ||
tatra sa svajanaiḥ svasmiṃ gṛhe margeṣu sarvataḥ |
pariśodhya sugaṃdhaiśca pariṣiṃcyābhyaśobhayat || 15.50{50} ||
tatrāsanāni prajñapya vitānāni vitatya ca |
muktapuṣpaiḥ samākīrya surabhiṃ samadhūpayat || 15.51{51} ||
tataḥ sa bhojyasāmagrīṃ sādhayitvā svavaṃdhubhiḥ |
tathopahārasāmagrīṃ pariśuddhāmasādhayat || 15.52{52} ||
tato'nye dyurgṛhasthaḥ sa snātvā śuddhāṃvarāvṛtaḥ |
saṃbuddhāmaṃtraṇaṃ kartuṃ vihāre sajano yayau || 15.53{53} ||
tatra taṃ śrīghanaṃ dṛṣṭvā sasaṃghaṃ sa purogataḥ |
kṛtāñjalipuṭo natvā prārthayadevamādarāt || 15.54{54} ||
bhagavannātha sarvajña samayo vartate'dhunā |
tatsasaṃgho mamāvāse bhāvānāgantumarhati || 15.55{55} ||
iti saṃprārthite tena bhagavān sa sasāṃghikaḥ |
pātracīvaramādāya saṃpratasthe prabhāsayan || 15.56{56} ||
tatra sa bhagavānmārge kṛtvā bhadraṃ prabhāsayan |
bhikṣusaṃghagaṇaiḥ sārddhaṃ śrāvastīṃ samupāviśan || 15.57{57} ||
tadābhūnnagare tatra sarvatrāpi sumaṅgalam |
upadravāṇi sarvāṇi sahasā vilayaṃ yayuḥ || 15.58{58} ||
andhā rūpāṇi paśyantaḥ śṛṇvanto vadhirā ravān |
hīnendriyāśca ye te'pi pratilabdhendriyā vavuḥ || 15.59{59} ||
ksutpipāsāhatā ye ca te'bhavan parituṣṭitāḥ |
rogiṇo nīrujā āsan kuvelā rucirāmvarāḥ || 15.60{60} ||
(rm 200)
mūkā āsan pravaktāro jaḍāścāpi suvāgminaḥ |
ciravairābhisaṃnaddhāste'pyāsanmaitrabhāvitāḥ || 15.61{61} ||
ye ca caṇḍāśāyāḥ krūrāste'pyabhūvan dayālavaḥ |
ye ca duṣṭā durācārāste santassamyamodyatāḥ || 15.62{62} ||
daridrā dhaninaḥ proḍhyā dūrvalā valino'bhavan |
ye śaṭhā vaṃcakāścauraste āryāḥ satyavādinaḥ || 15.63{63} ||
evaṃ ye duḥkhinaḥ satvāste sarve sukhino'bhavan |
evaṃ sa bhagavāṃstatra kṛtvā bhadraṃ samantataḥ || 15.64{64} ||
pradakṣiṇakrameṇaivaṃ sasaṃghaṃ tadgṛhe viśan |
tatra tasya munīndrasya sa gṛhastho yathākramam |
pādyaṃ dadau sasaṃghasya sadārāḥ śucināmbunā || 15.65{65} ||
tataḥ sa suprāsannātmā sasaṃghaṃ taṃ munīśvaram |
prajñapte svāsane tatra yathākramaṃ nyaveśayat || 15.66{66} ||
tatra te sāṃghikāḥ sarve saṃbuddhapramukhāḥ kramāt |
svasvāsane samāviśya samātasthuḥ samāhitāḥ || 15.67{67} ||
tataḥ svasvāsanāsīnān saṃbuddhapramukhān yatīn |
tān sarvān sa gṛhī dṛṣṭvā yathāvidhi samarcayat || 15.68{68} ||
tataḥ sa sajanaḥ śreṣṭhī svahastena sasāṃghikam |
taṃ munīndraṃ sudhākalpairbhojanaiḥ samatarpayet || 15.69{69} ||
tataḥ saṃtṛptitaṃ dṛṣṭvā sasaṃghaṃ taṃ munīśvaram |
apanīya sa pātrāṇi taddhastādīn vyaśodhayat || 15.70{70} ||
tataḥ sa sāñjalirnatvā tān buddhapramukhān yatīn |
puro nīcāsanāsīno dharmaṃ śrotumupāśrayat || 15.71{71} ||
tataḥ sa bhagavān dṛṣṭvā taṃ gṛhaṣṭhaṃ subhāvinam |
ādimadhyāntakalyāṇaṃ dharmāmādeṣṭumabravīt || 15.72{72} ||
ārogyamastu te nityaṃ gṛhapate sadā śivam |
bodhau cittaṃ samādhāya cara dharmaṃ samāhitaḥ || 15.73{73} ||
dharmeṇa jīyate pāpaṃ niṣpāpaḥ sadgatiṃ vrajet |
sadgatau satsukhānyeva bhuktvā saṃcarate sadā || 15.74{74} ||
dharmeṇa puruṣo vidvān sarvavidyākalādhipaḥ |
sudhiro guṇavān vijño bhavecchrīmānmaharddhikaḥ || 15.75{75} ||
dharmeṇa suciraṃ jīvedārogyaṃ ca sadā tanau |
sarvatrāpi bhavennityaṃ maṃgalaṃ nirupadravam || 15.76{76} ||
dharmo na naśyate kvapi hriyate nāpi kena cit |
kenāpi naśyate naiva cālayituṃ kadā cana || 15.77{77} ||
nāgnibhirdahyate dharmaḥ klidyate nāpi codakaiḥ |
vāyubhiḥ śuṣyate naiva kṣīyate nāpi bhūmiṣu || 15.78{78} ||
dharmeṇa bhūḥ sthirībhūtā dharmeṇa dīpyate'nalaḥ |
dharmeṇa calate vāyurdharmeṇa vahate jalaḥ || 15.79{79} ||
dharmeṇa dīpyate bhānurdharmeṇaivaṃ sudhākaraḥ |
(rm 201)
meghā dharmeṇa varṣāṇā bhūmiḥ śaṣyauṣadhībharāḥ || 15.80{80} ||
dharmeṇaivaṃ tathā sarve lokapālā maharddhikāḥ |
sarvaduṣṭān vinirjitya pālayanti jagattraye || 15.81{81} ||
dharmo na śīyate kvāpi dharmo na paribhūyate |
sarvatra jayate dharmo dharmo hi śobhate sadā || 15.82{82} ||
dharmahīnā na śobhante śrimanto guṇino'pi ca |
dharmmiṣṭhā eva śobhante daridrā nirguṇā api || 15.83{83} ||
dharmeṇa rakṣitā vīrāḥ saṃgrāme vijitārayaḥ |
yaśomānyamahatsaṃpatsukhāni te labhanti ca || 15.84{84} ||
tathā vīrā vaṇiksārthā dharmeṇaivābhirakṣitāḥ |
mahāmbudhiṃ samuttīrya ratnānyapi labhanti ca || 15.85{85} ||
dharmavanto narā dhīrā buddhimanto vicakṣaṇāḥ |
sarvavidyākalāvijñā jayante paravādinaḥ || 15.86{86} ||
tathā dharmānubhāvena śilpavidyākalāvidaḥ |
yaśomānyaprasādaśrīsatsukhāni labhanti ca || 15.87{87} ||
tathā ca maṃtriṇo'mātyā dharmavanto vicakṣaṇāḥ |
rājyakāryāṇi sarvāṇi sādhayanto jayantyarīn || 15.88{88} ||
rājānaḥ kṣatriyāścaivaṃ dharmanītivicakṣaṇāḥ |
sarvaduṣṭān vinirjitya virājante nārādhipāḥ || 15.89{89} ||
tathā dharmeṇa rājendraścakravarttī mahīśvaraḥ |
saptaratnasamāyuktaḥ sarvaśāstā virājate || 15.90{90} ||
tathā dharmadharā eva yatayo yogino'pi ca |
sarvakleśagaṇāñjitvā labhanti paramaṃ padam || 15.91{91} ||
tatharṣayo mahābhijñā dharmabhṛto maharddhikāḥ |
yathābhilaṣitāṃ siddhiṃ prāpya mokṣaṃ vrajanti ca || 15.92{92} ||
tathā dharmānusāreṇa maṃtravidyārthasādhakāḥ |
siddhisamṛddhisaṃprāptāḥ sādhayanti yathehitam || 15.93{93} ||
tathā ca mānavāḥ sarve lokā dharmānusārataḥ |
nihīnamadhyamotkṛṣṭāḥ prabhavanti mahītale || 15.94{94} ||
tathā dharmānusāreṇa lokā daityādayo'pi ca |
nihīnamadhyamotkṛṣṭā pracaranti rasātale || 15.95{95} ||
tathā svargeṣu devāśca svasvadharmānubhāvataḥ |
sukhāni suciraṃ bhuktvā pravasanti pramoditāḥ || 15.96{96} ||
evaṃ sarvatra lokeṣu svasvadharmānubhāvataḥ |
sarve satvāḥ sukhaprāptāḥ pracaranti yathechayā || 15.97{97} ||
evaṃ traidhātukotpannāḥ satvāḥ sarve surādayaḥ |
svasvadharmānusāreṇa bhramanti ṣaḍgatiṣvapi || 15.98{98} ||
kimatra vahunoktena dharma eva jagatprabhuḥ |
dharmeṇaiva sadā nityaṃ rakṣitaḥ pracarecchubhe || 15.99{99} ||
ṣubhena siddhyate cittaṃ śuddhacittaḥ sudhīrbhavet |
subuddhiḥ sadguṇādhāraṃ sadguruṃ samupāśrayet || 15.100{100} ||
tadgurorupadeśena carannityaṃ samāhitaḥ |
bodhicittaṃ samādhāya triratnāni sadā bhajet || 15.101{1} ||
etatpuṇyānubhāvena saddharmasādhanodyataḥ |
(rm 202)
bodhisatvo mahāsatvo bodhicaryāṃ vrataṃ caret || 15.102{2} ||
tato māragaṇāñjitvā pūrya pāramitāḥ kramāt |
trividhāṃ bodhimāsādya saṃbuddhapadamāpnuyāt || 15.103{3} ||
evaṃ dharmavaraṃ matvā triratnaśaraṇaṃ gataḥ |
saddharmaṃ samupaśritya cara bodhipadāptaye || 15.104{4} ||
mṛtyukāle vipattau ca dharmma evānugassuhṛt |
tadā sarve tyajantyāśu ko'pi naiva sahānugaḥ || 15.105{5} ||
dharma eva tadā trātā sarvatrāpyanugaḥ suhṛt |
tasmāddharmo mahāratnaṃ sādhanīyaṃ prayatnataḥ || 15.106{6} ||
evaṃ sa bhagavāñchāstā dharmasyābhyanubhāvatām |
samādiśya kṣaṇaṃ tasthau tūṣṇībhūtvā samāhitaḥ || 15.107{7} ||
tatastasya munīndrasya pādau sa mudito gṛhī |
praṇatvā manasā caivaṃ praṇidhānaṃ sāmadadhe || 15.108{8} ||
anena kuśalenāhaṃ sarvakleśavivarjitaḥ |
pratyekāṃ bodhimāsādya nirvṛtiṃ samavāpnuyāt || 15.109{9} ||
tataḥ sa suprasannāsyo gṛhasthaḥ sajano mudā |
bhagavataṃ tamālokya purastasthau samāhitaḥ || 15.110{10} ||
tadā sa bhagavāṃstasya śrīmato hṛdaye sthitam |
praṇidhānaṃ parijñāya susaṃsmitaṃ vyamuñcata || 15.111{11} ||
tatastasya munīndrasya mukhapadmātsurasmayaḥ |
paṃcavarṇā vinirgatya sarvatra samudācaran || 15.112{12} ||
tāsāṃ yā raśmayo yātā adholoke samantataḥ |
tāḥ sarvanarakeṣvevamavabhyāsya samāsarat || 15.113{13} ||
tadā tābhiḥ parispṛṣṭāḥ sarve te narakasthitāḥ |
mahatsaukhyaṃ samāsādya vismayaṃ samupāyayuḥ || 15.114{14} ||
tatra sarve'pi te satvāḥ parasparasamāgatāḥ |
sarvaduḥkhavinirmuktāḥ saṃmīlyaivaṃ samabruvan || 15.115{15} ||
aho citraṃ kimadyaivaṃ mahatsaukhyaṃ prajāyate |
kiṃ nu vayaṃ bhavanto'smāccyutā anyatra cāgatāḥ || 15.116{16} ||
yadasmākaṃ mahadduḥkhaṃ tatsarvaṃ śāmyate'dhunā |
saṃjāyate mahāsaukhyaṃ tadadbhutaṃ na manyate || 15.117{17} ||
nūnamatrādhunā ko'pi bodhisatvaḥ samāgataḥ |
yasmādiyaṃ mahāraśmiravabhāsya prasaryati || 15.118{18} ||
iti ciṃtābhidagdhānaṃ teṣāṃ cittaprabodhane |
bhagavānnirmitaṃ buddhaṃ sarvatra tatra preṣayet || 15.119{19} ||
tadā taṃ saugatiṃ mūrttiṃ dṛṣṭvā sarve'pi te mudā |
aho citramiti proktvā vismitāścaivamabruvan || 15.120{20} ||
nehaivetaścyutāḥ sarve nāpyanyatra gatā vayam |
ihaiva saṃsthitāḥ sarve narake pravasāmahe || 15.121{21} ||
api tvayaṃ mahān satvo'pūrvadarśana āgataḥ |
nūnamasyānubhāvena saṃjāyante sukhāni naḥ || 15.122{22} ||
tadasmiṃ sugatākare vayaṃ sarve prasāditāḥ |
upetya praṇatiṃ kṛtvā sarvathā saṃbhajemahi || 15.123{23} ||
iti saṃbhāṣya sarve te nārakīyāḥ pramoditāḥ |
(rm 203)
upetya taṃ mahāsatvaṃ sāñjalayo'bhinemire || 15.124{24} ||
namo buddhāya dharmāya saṃghāye ca namaḥ sadā |
ityuktvā ca triratnānāṃ saṃsmṛtvā śaraṇaṃ yayuḥ || 15.125{25} ||
iti te nirmite tasmiṃ cittaṃ prasādya nirmalāḥ |
sarvanirmuktakarmāṇaḥ sahasā sadgatiṃ yayuḥ || 15.126{26} ||
tathā corddhagatā yāstāḥ kramāccāturmahādhipān |
samārabhya trayastriṃśān sarvāṃścāpi surālayāt || 15.127{27} ||
yāvadbhavāgraparyantamavabhāsya prasāritāḥ || 15.128{27} ||
tathā cāpi samodghuṣya sarvān devānanodayan |
anityaṃ khalu saṃsāraṃ duḥkhaṃ śūnyamanātmakam |
iti matvā śubhe nityaṃ caritavyaṃ sadāpi ca || 15.129{28} ||
niṣkrāmatārabhadhvaṃ tadyujyadhvaṃ buddhaśāsane |
dhunīta mṛtyusainyāni naḍāgāraṃ karī yathā || 15.130{29} ||
yo hyasmiṃ dharmavinaye hyapramattaścariṣyati |
tyaktvā janmātra saṃsāre duḥkhasyāntaṃ kariṣyati || 15.131{30} ||
iti matvātra saṃbodhipadaṃ yadi samichatha |
tacchraddhayā triratnāni bhajadhvaṃ sarvadādarāt || 15.132{31} ||
etacchabdaṃ samākarṇya sarve devāḥ prabodhitāḥ |
triratnaṃ śaraṇaṃ kṛtvā prabhejire sadādṛtāḥ || 15.133{32} ||
evaṃ tā raśmayaḥ sarvāḥ sarvāṃllokāṃcchubhe vṛṣe |
bodhayitvā pratisthāpya pratyāyayurmuneḥ puraḥ || 15.134{33} ||
tatra tā raśmayaḥ sarvāḥ piṇḍitāstasya tāyinaḥ |
tridhā pradakṣiṇīkṛtvā sūrṇāyāṃ nyaviśatpunaḥ || 15.135{34} ||
taddṛṣṭvā vismitāḥ sarve lokāste samitisthitāḥ |
kiṃ dharmamādiśedbuddha iti saṃciṃtya tasthire || 15.136{35} ||
athānando mahābhijñaḥ samutthāya kṛtāñjaliḥ |
puraḥ sthito munīndraṃ taṃ praṇatvā prārthayattathā || 15.137{36} ||
bhagavānnānāvidhā raśmirbhavanmukhābjanirgatāḥ |
avabhāsya jagallokaṃ bhavadūrṇāṃ niveśitā || 15.138{37} ||
mārajinnirmalasvānto nirvikalpo niraṃjanaḥ |
nāhetvapratyayaṃ buddhā nirdainyā nirmadoddhavāḥ |
smitaṃ caṃdrakarāhāsaṃ darśayanti kadācana || 15.139{38} ||
tadyadarthaṃ bhavān smitaṃ darśayati munīśvara |
tadarthaṃ śrotumicchaṃti sarve lokā ime'dhunā || 15.140{39} ||
tadbhavāṃ yatsamādeṣṭumichati sāṃprataṃ mune |
taddharmaṃ samupādiśya prasādayatvimāṃ sabhām || 15.141{40} ||
iti tena vaśiṣyeṇa prārthite sa munīśvaraḥ |
tamāyuṣmantamānandaṃ samāmaṃtryaivamabravīt || 15.142{41} ||
evametatsadānaṃda nāhetvapratyayaṃ smitam |
saṃbuddhā api te sarve darśayanti kadā cana || 15.143{42} ||
yadarthe smitamānaṃda darśitaṃ sāṃprataṃ mayā |
(rm 204)
tadarthaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi tacchṛṇudhvaṃ samāhitāḥ || 15.144{43} ||
paśyasi tvaṃ tadānaṃda śreṣṭhinānena yanmama |
evaṃvidhaṃ sasaṃghasya satkāraṃ śraddhayā kṛtam || 15.145{44} ||
etatpuṇyavipākena mayi cittaprasādataḥ |
ayaṃ śrīmānmahāsatvo bodhisatvo bhavetsudhīḥ || 15.146{45} ||
tataḥ kleśavimuktātmā pariśuddhatrimaṃḍalaḥ |
sūkṣmatvagiti vikhyātaḥ pratyekabuddha ātmavit || 15.147{46} ||
bhaviṣyati mahābhijñaḥ saṃvṛtiparamārthavit || 15.148{47!} ||
evametatmahatpuṇyavījamārādhitaṃ mama |
sāsane śrīmatānena śraddhayā bodhicetasā || 15.149{48} ||
buddhakṣetre kṛtaṃ puṇyaṃ na kṣiṇoti kadā cana |
saṃbodhisatphalaṃ dadyānnānyaddharmaphalaṃ khalu || 15.150{49} ||
evaṃ mahattaraṃ puṇyaṃ buddhakṣetreṣu satkṛtam |
matvā taccharaṇaṃ kṛtvā triratnaṃ bhajatādarāt || 15.151{50} ||
ye buddaśaraṇaṃ gatvā bhajanti sarvadādarāt |
te sadā sadgatiṃ yātāścaraṃti bodhisaṃvaram || 15.152{51} ||
ye dharmaṃ śaraṇaṃ kṛtvā śrutvā bhajanti sādaram |
sarve te'kleśino bhadrāḥ saṃprayānti jinālayam || 15.153{52} ||
ye saṃghe śaraṇaṃ gatvā bhajanti śraddhayā sadā |
na te gacchanti duḥkhatvaṃ sadā yānti sukhāvatīm || 15.154{53} ||
iti matvā tathā yūyaṃ sadā nityaṃ samāhitāḥ |
triratnaśaraṇaṃ kṛtvā bhajadhvaṃ śraddhayādarāt || 15.155{54} ||
tena puṇyavipākena yūyaṃ sarve'pi sarvathā |
trividhāṃ bodhimāsādya saṃbuddhapadamāpsyatha || 15.156{55} ||
iti śāstrā munīndreṇa samādiṣṭaṃ niśamyate |
sarve'pi sāṃghikāḥ sarve lokāśca saṃpramoditāḥ || 15.157{56} ||
tatheti pratisaṃśrutya triratnaṃ śaraṇaṃ gatāḥ |
satkṛtya śraddhayā nityaṃ prabhejire samāhitāḥ || 15.158{57} ||
so'pi śreṣṭhī tathā śāstrā samādiṣṭaṃ tadātmanam |
vyākaraṇaṃ samākarṇya prābhyanandatprabodhitaḥ || 15.159{58} ||
tataḥ prabhṛti sa śrīmāṃstriratnaṃ śaraṇaṃ gataḥ |
satkṛtya śraddhayā nityaṃ sadā bheje pramoditaḥ || 15.160{59} ||
tataḥ sa bhagavāñchāstā sasāṃghikaḥ samutthitaḥ |
tathā sarvatra bhadrāṇi prakurvan svāśramaṃ yayau || 15.161{60} ||
ityevaṃ guruṇākhyātaṃ tathā mayātra kathyate |
tvamapyevaṃ mahārāja śrutvā dharmaṃ śubhe cara || 15.162{61} ||
prajāścāpi tathā dharmaṃ śrāvayitvā prabodhayan |
bodhimārge pratiṣṭhāpya śubhe'bhyava sadādarāt || 15.163{62} ||
tathā te sarvadā nityaṃ maṃgalaṃ nirupadravam |
bhavennūnaṃ mahārāja satyametatprabudhyatām || 15.164{63} ||
etatpuṇyavipākena daśapāramitā api |
paripūryya krameṇaivaṃ saṃbodhimapi cāpsyai || 15.165{64} ||
evaṃ satyaṃ parijñāya triratnaṃ śaraṇaṃ gataḥ |
satkṛtya śraddhayā nityaṃ bhaja bodhiṃ yadīchasi || 15.166{65} ||
(rm 205)
iti tenārhatā śāstrā samādiṣṭaṃ niśamya saḥ |
nṛpastatheti saṃśrutya prābhyanandatsapārṣadaḥ || 15.167{66} ||
sūkṣmatvaco'vadānaṃ yattadidaṃ ye narā mudā |
satkṛtya śraddhayā bhaktyā śṛṇvanti śrāvayanti ca || 15.168{67} ||
te sarve sadgatiṃ yātā bhuktvā saukhyāni sarvadā |
saddharmaṃ samupāśritya saṃprayānti jinālayam || 15.169{68} ||

++ iti ratnāvadānatatve sūkṣmatvaco'vadānaṃ samāptaḥ ++

(rm 206)
xvi pretikāvadāna
athāśoko mahārājaḥ kṛtāñjalipuṭo mudā |
upaguptaṃ yatiṃ natvā punareva samabravīt || 16.1{1} ||
bhadanta śrotumichāmi punaranyatsubhāṣitam |
tadyathā guruṇādiṣṭaṃ tathādeṣṭuṃ ca me'rhasi || 16.2{2} ||
iti saṃprārthitaṃ tena rājñā sa yatirātmavit |
tamaśokaṃ mahīpālaṃ samālokyaivamādiśat || 16.3{3} ||
śṛṇu rājanmahābhāga yathā me guruṇoditam |
tathāhaṃ te pravakṣyāmi śrutvānumodanāṃ kuru || 16.4{4} ||
purā sa bhagavān buddhaḥ śākyasiṃho jagadguruḥ |
sarvajñaḥ sugataḥ śāstā dharmarājastathāgataḥ || 16.5{5} ||
bhikṣubiḥ śrāvakaiḥ sārddhaṃ cailakairbhikṣuṇīgaṇaiḥ |
upāsakairmahāśrāddhaistathā copāsikāgaṇaiḥ || 16.6{6} ||
bodhisatvagaṇaiścāpi saddharmasādhanodyataiḥ |
kṛtakṛtyairmahābhijñaiścaturbrahmavihārakaiḥ || 16.7{7} ||
rājagṛhe puropānte veṇuvane śubhāṅkite |
karaṇḍakanivāpākhye mahodyāne jināśrame || 16.8{8} ||
vihṛtya sarvasatvānāṃ hitārthena sabhāśritaḥ |
ādimadhyāntākalyāṇaṃ dharmamādeṣṭumārabhat || 16.9{9} ||
tadā sarve'pi devendrāḥ sadevāḥ kamalāsanaḥ |
sarve lokādhipāścāpi yakṣagaṃdharvakinnarāḥ || 16.10{10} ||
siddhā vidyādharāḥ sādhyā rākṣasā dānavādhipāḥ |
nāgeṃdrā garuḍāścāpi sajanaparivārakāḥ || 16.11{11} ||
ṛṣayo brahmaṇāścāpi yatayo yogino'pi ca |
rājāno rājaputrāśca maṃtriṇo'pi ca || 16.12{12} ||
amātyāḥ sainyalokāśca sārthavāhā mahājanāḥ |
vaṇijaḥ śilpinaścāpi gṛhasthāḥ paurikā janāḥ || 16.13{13} ||
grāmyā jānapadāścāpi kārpaṭikāśca nairgamāḥ |
tathānye'pi samāyātāḥ saddharmaguṇavāṃchinaḥ || 16.14{14} ||
tatra sarve'pi te lokā vihāre samupāgatāḥ |
taṃ munīndraṃ samāsīnaṃ dṛṣṭvā natvā pramoditāḥ || 16.15{15} ||
tridhā pradakṣiṇīkṛtvā samabhyarcya yathākramam |
natvā sāñjalayaḥ sarve parivṛtya samaṃtataḥ || 16.16{16} ||
tatsaddharmāmṛtaṃ pātuṃ suprasannamukhāmbujam |
śrīghanaṃ taṃ samālokya samātasthuḥ samāhitāḥ || 16.17{17} ||
tadā sa bhagavān dṛṣṭvā tān sarvān samupasthitān |
āryasatyaṃ samārabhya saddharmaṃ samupādiśat || 16.18{18} ||
tatsaddharmāmṛtaṃ pītvā lokāḥ sarve'pi te mudā |
(rm 207)
saṃbodhisādhanotsāhāḥ pracerire pramoditāḥ || 16.19{19} ||
tasminnavasare tatra rājagṛhāntike vane |
veṇuvanāntike paṃcapretaśatāni cerire || 16.20{20} ||
nagnānyatikṛtāṅgāni dagdhasthūṇākṛtīni vai |
romavastrāṇi rukṣāṇi śailopamodarāṇi ca || 16.21{21} ||
sūcīchidramukhānyasthiyaṃtravaducchṛtānyapi |
kṣutpipāsābhidagdhāni hīnadīnasvarāṇyapi || 16.22{22} ||
tīvraduḥkhāni taptāni pralapanti vihāyasi |
vibhramanti sadā kvāpi kiñci sthitiṃ na lebhire || 16.23{23} ||
tadaikasmin dine tatra maudgalyāyana ātmavit |
āyuṣmān saugato bhikṣurmahābhijño dayānvitaḥ || 16.24{24} ||
pūrvāhne pātramādāya kāṣāyacīvarāvṛtaḥ |
rājagṛhe pure piṇḍaṃ yācituṃ samupācaran || 16.25{25} ||
tatra yatiḥ sa tāṃ pretāṃ sarvān vibhramato'mvare |
dṛṣṭvene ka iti dhyātvā paśyañjanairupācaran || 16.26{26} ||
te sarve pretakāścāpi dṛṣṭvā taṃ bhikṣumāgatam |
sahasopetya natvāgre rudanta etadabruvan || 16.27{27} ||
bhadanta bhavatāṃ pādau vandāmahe samānatāḥ |
tadasmākaṃ hitaṃ kṛtvā kṛpayā trātumarhati || 16.28{28} ||
vayaṃ rājagṛhe jātāḥ paurāḥ paṃcaśatānyapi |
svakarmaparibhuṃjānaḥ pretībhūtāścarāmahe || 16.29{29} ||
tadā vayamime sarve gṛhasthāḥ śreṣṭhino narāḥ |
mahādhanā mahābhogāstīrthikā dharmasevakāḥ || 16.30{30} ||
yadvayaṃ matsārākrāntamānasāḥ kuṭukuñcakāḥ |
āgṛhītapariṣkārāḥ kevalārjanasādhakāḥ || 16.31{31} ||
naiva kiñcittadā dānamadadmahi kadā cana |
arthinamāgataṃ dṛṣṭvā prākuṣyāmahi roṣitāḥ || 16.32{32} ||
paradattānyapi śrutvā dṛṣṭvā cāpi praroṣitāḥ |
dīyamāneṣu vighnāni prākurmahi prayatnataḥ || 16.33{33} ||
saugatā api cārhantaḥ pretā iveyamāgatāḥ |
ityasmābhiḥ pramohāndhaiḥ paribhāṣyābhininditāḥ || 16.34{34} ||
tata ete vayaṃ sarve kālaṃ kṛtvā ca sāṃpratam |
evaṃ pretālayotpannā duḥkhārditāścarāmahe || 16.35{35} ||
iti bhadanta vijñāya gatvā rājagṛhe bhavān |
kṛpayāsmatpravṛttāṃtaṃ jñātīnāṃ purato vadan || 16.36{36} ||
chandakabhikṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā buddhādiṃ sarvasāṃghikam |
asmannāmnā samabhyarcya bhojayituṃ samarhati || 16.37{37} ||
saṃbuddhapramukhebhyaśca saṃghebhyo bhavatādarāt |
asmannāmnā prakarttavyaṃ praṇāmamapi sadguro || 16.38{38} ||
dakṣiṇādeśanāpyasmannāmnā buddhādisāṃghikaiḥ |
(rm 208)
bhavatā kārayitavyā ityasmāsu kṛpāṃ kuru || 16.39{39} ||
yathāsmākamitaḥ pretalokā muktirbhaveddhruvam |
tathāsmāsu bhavāmcchāstā kṛpayā kartumarhāti || 16.40{40} ||
iti taiḥ prārthitaṃ sarvaiḥ sa maudgalyāyanaḥ sudhīḥ |
śrutvā tatheti saṃśrutya tūṣṇībhūtvādhyuvāsa tat || 16.41{41} ||
tatastān pretikān sarvān sa maudgalyāyano yatiḥ |
samālokya samāśvāsya punaretatsamabravīt || 16.42{42} ||
tatra yūyaṃ ca sarva śvaḥ pravṛtte saṃghabhojane |
sasaṃghaṃ śrīghanaṃ draṣṭuṃ samāyāta samādarāt || 16.43{43} ||
iti tenārhātādiṣṭaṃ śrutvā te pretakā api |
tatheti hi pratijñāya praṇatvā taṃ vane yayuḥ || 16.44{44} ||
tataḥ sa saugato bhikṣurmaudgalyo yatirātmavit |
gatvā rājagṛhe teṣāṃ jñātīnāṃ samupācaran || 16.45{45} ||
tatra sarvatra teṣāṃ tatjñātīnāṃ bhavaneṣu sa |
gatvā teṣāṃ pravṛttāṃtaṃ sarvamevaṃ nyavedayat || 16.46{46} ||
tataḥ sa tatra sarvāṃstāṃ jñātīnāhūya sādaram |
teṣāṃ pretagatermuktihetorevaṃ samabravīt || 16.47{'47} ||
śṛṇuta bho mahābhāgya yūyaṃ sarve samāhitāḥ |
yuṣmākaṃ jñātivargāṇāṃ muktihetuṃ pravakṣyate || 16.48{48} ||
yato yuṣmatsagotrāste matsariṇo durāśayāḥ |
āgṛhītapariṣkārā daśākuśalasaṃcarāḥ || 16.49{49} ||
nāpi dānaṃ pradattaṃ taiḥ kasmai ci kvāpi kiṃ cana |
paradattānyapi śrutvā dṛṣṭvā vā paryabhāṣyata || 16.50{50} ||
arhanto bhikṣavo vā tairyācakāśca gṛhāgatāḥ |
paribhāṣyābhisaṃtarjya niṣkāsitā gṛhādvalāt || 16.51{51} ||
pretā iva sadā bhikṣāṃ yācitveme'bhibhuṃjate |
ityarhato yatī cāpi dṛṣṭvaiva te'bhyanindayan || 16.52{52} ||
etatkarmavipākaiste pretībhūtā vane'dhunā |
kṣutpipāsāhatā nityaṃ vibhramyante'bhiduḥkhitāḥ || 16.53{53} ||
tattesāṃ tanmahatpāpamuktihetorihādhunā |
saṃbuddhaśāsane dānaṃ kartumichāmyahaṃ khalu || 16.54{54} ||
mayaitaddakṣiṇādeśāstannāmnā kārayiṣyate |
tadā tatpāpamuktāste sarve yāyurdivaṃ dhruvam || 16.55{55} ||
tadatra śraddhayā yūyaṃ yathāśakti mayārthitāḥ |
saṃghabhojanasāmagriṃ sarve pradātumarhaya || 16.56{56} ||
saṃghabhojye pravṛtte'tra sarve te pretikā api |
draṣṭuṃ samāgamiṣyanti yūyaṃ cāgaṃtumarhatha || 16.57{57} ||
ityuktvā prārthite tena maudgalyena dayālunā |
śrutvā tajjātayaḥ sarve vismayāṃ samupāyayuḥ || 16.58{58} ||
tataste vismitāḥ sarve tatpretagatimuktaye |
(rm 209)
tatbhojyasāmagrīṃ śraddhayā dātumicchire || 16.59{59} ||
ke cittasmai suvarṇāni ke cidratnāni saṃdaduḥ |
ke cidropyādi dhātūṃśca dravyāni vividhāni cā || 16.60{60} ||
taṇḍulāni daduḥ ke ciddadurdhānyāni ke cana |
ke ciddaduḥ kalāyāṃśca godhūmāṃśca yavānapi || 16.61{61} ||
kulmāṣānmasurāṃścāpi māṣacanakasarṣapān |
mudgāṃścāpi tathānyāṃśca sarvān vrīhin daduḥ pare || 16.62{62} ||
tilapriyaṃgusiddhārthān bhaṃgāśca rājikā api |
mūlaśākakadamvāṃśca patrapuṣpāṃkurāṇi ca || 16.63{63} ||
śṛṅgaverātipakvāni phalāni vividhāni ca |
surasāni supathyāni pradaduścāpare mudā || 16.64{64} ||
tiktāmlalavaṇādīni rasāni vividhāni ca |
śunṭhīśca pippirīścāpi maricāni dadurmudā || 16.65{65} ||
hiṅguṃ ca jīrakāṃścānye viḍarkasaiṃdhavādi ca |
anye ghṛtāni tailāni pradaduḥ śraddhayā mudā || 16.66{66} ||
śaktūṃśca piṣṭakāṃścānyamodakāṃśca dadurmudā |
tathā dadhīni dugdhāni madhuni ca guḍāni ca || 16.67{67} ||
tathānye sarkarāścāpi pradaduḥ śraddhayā mudā |
tathauṣadhāni pathyāni pācakāni daduḥ pare || 16.68{68} ||
lāṅgalīpūgatāmbūlaravaṃgādīni saṃdaduḥ |
sarvapeyāni bhojyāni vividhānyapi |
sarve te jñātayasteṣāṃ saṃghabhokṣyāya saṃdaduḥ || 16.69{69} ||
evaṃ te sarvavastūni datvā sarve pramoditāḥ |
sāṃjalayaḥ praṇatvā taṃ maudgalyaṃ yatimūcire || 16.70{70} ||
bhadanto'nugrahaṃ kṛtvā saṃbuddhapramukhaṃ bhavān |
sarvasaṃghaṃ samabhyarcya bhojayatu pratoṣayan || 16.71{71} ||
vayamapyāgamiṣyāmaḥ pravṛtte saṃghabhojane |
tān pretān samupāyātān sarvān draṣṭuṃ sabāndhavāḥ || 16.72{72} ||
iti taiḥ prārthitaṃ śrutvā tatheti sa prabodhayan |
tāni dravyāni sarvāṇi gṛhītvā svāśrame yayau || 16.73{73} ||
tataḥ sa bhikṣurāyuṣmān vihāre samupācaran |
dṛṣṭvā taṃ śrīghanaṃ natvā sāñjaliḥ samupāśrayan || 16.74{73} ||
tatra sa bhagavān dṛṣṭvā taṃ maudgalyamupasthitam |
prahasan suprasannāsyaḥ samāmaṃtryaitadabravīt || 16.75{74} ||
kaṃ citte kuśalaṃ vatsa kuto'trāsi samāgataḥ |
lokeṣu kiṃ pravṛttāṃtaṃ tan pracakṣva mahāmate || 16.76{75} ||
ityādiṣṭe munīndreṇa sa maudgalyāyanaḥ sudhīḥ |
bhagavantaṃ tamānatvā sāñjaliretadabravīt || 16.77{76} ||
bhagavan kṛpayā śāstaḥ sarvatra kauśalaṃ mama |
rājagṛhe pure piṃḍaṃ yācituṃ samupācaran || 16.78{77} ||
tatra mārge vanopānte paṃca pretaśatānyapi |
dṛṣṭvā māṃ samupasṛtya praṇatvaitadvadanti vai || 16.79{78} ||
bhadanta tadvijānīyā yadvayaṃ pretikā ime |
sarve rājagṛhe jātā gṛhasthāḥ paurikāḥ khalu || 16.80{79} ||
(rm 210)
tadā vayaṃ svayaṃ naṣṭā daśākuśalacāriṇaḥ |
ete svadaivayogena pretībhūtāścarāmahe || 16.81{80} ||
tadbhavān kṛpayāsmākaṃ pretagativimuktaye |
jñātīnāmapi sarveṣāṃ karmaplotiṃ nivedya hi || 16.82{81} ||
chandakabhikṣaṇaṃ kṛtvāpyasmannāmnā sasāṃghikam |
bhagavantaṃ samabhyarcya bhojayituṃ samarhati || 16.83{82} ||
asmannāmnāya saṃbuddhaṃ sasaṃghaṃ namatāṃ bhavān |
dakṣiṇādeśanāścāsmannāmnā deśayatumarhasi || 16.84{83} ||
tato nūnaṃ vayaṃ sarve pretagativimocitāḥ |
nirmuktapāpakāḥ śuddhāḥ prāpnuyāma surālayam || 16.85{84} ||
ityasmatprārthanāṃ kṛtvā kṛpayāsmān samuddharan |
bodhimārge pratisthāpya pātumarhati sarvadā || 16.86{85} ||
iti teṣāṃ vacaḥ śrutvā tathā rājagṛhe gataḥ |
tadjñātīnāṃ purasteṣāṃ pravṛttāṃtaṃ nivedya ca || 16.87{86} ||
chandakabhikṣaṇaṃ tebhyo yācitvāhamihācare |
tathārcayitumichāmi bhagavantaṃ sasāṃghikam || 16.88{87} ||
iti me prārthanāṃ kṛtvā bhavāṃ chāstā jagadguruḥ |
tadadhivāsanāṃ kṛtvā kṛpayā tān samuddhara || 16.89{88} ||
iti saṃprārthitaṃ tena bhagavān sa munīśvaraḥ |
tatheti saṃpratiśrutya tūṣṇībhūtvādhyuvāsa tat || 16.90{89} ||
tato matvā sa maudgalyo bhagavato'dhivāsitam |
sahasā bhojyasāmagrīṃ sapauraiḥ samasādhayat || 16.91{90} ||
tato'nye dyuḥ prabhātāyāṃ sarvasādhitasiddhite |
sāñjalistaṃ munīndraṃ ca praṇatvaitatsamabravīt || 16.92{91} ||
bhagavan sarvasāmagrīṃ saṃsādhitaṃ susiddhyate |
tatsarvasāṃghikaiḥ sārddhaṃ sahasāgaṃtumarhati || 16.93{92} ||
iti saṃprārthite tena bhagavāṃ sa munīśvaraḥ |
bhikṣuṃ prāha samāhūya gaṇḍīmākoṭyatāmiti || 16.94{93} ||
tadādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa śrutvā bhikṣustatheti saḥ |
āśrutya sahasādāya gaṇḍīmākoṭayattadā || 16.95{94} ||
tadgaṇḍīśabdamākarṇya sarve te sāṃghikā api |
vihāre sahasāgatya bhagavantaṃ praṇemire || 16.96{95} ||
tataḥ sa bhagavāṃstatra svāsane samupāviśat |
sarve te sāṃghikāścāpi kramātsvasvāsane'viśan || 16.97{96} ||
tatastadjñātayaḥ sarve tatrānye paurikā api |
tān pretān draṣṭumāyātā upatasthuḥ sakautukāḥ || 16.98{97} ||
tadā te pretikāḥ sarve prakṣiptāḥ karmavāyubhiḥ |
bahulokāntare dure yātāstasthurvimohitāḥ || 16.99{98} ||
tadā sa yatirālokya sarvasaṃghān samāsthitān |
tadjñātibandhuvargāṃśca pretān draṣṭuṃ samācarat || 16.100{99} ||
tatra pretānadṛṣṭvā sa maudgalyo vismitāśayaḥ |
kiṃ te'tra nāgatā veti samantato vyalokayat || 16.101{100} ||
(rm 211)
vilokayan sa sarvatra nādrakṣīttāṃ samaṃtataḥ |
sa saṃdigdhaḥ punastatra samyagdṛṣṭyā vyalokayat || 16.102{1} ||
sarvatrāpi sa tāṃ pretānadṛṣṭvā vismayānvitaḥ |
sarvatra magadhe'paśyatsamyagdṛśā samāhitaḥ || 16.103{2} ||
tatrāpi ca sa sarvatra naiva kaṃ cidapaśyate |
tato'tivismito'nyatra divyadṛśābhyalokayat || 16.104{3} ||
aṃgavaṃgakaliṃgeṣu saurāṣṭramagadheṣu ca |
tatrāpi tānadṛṣṭvā sa narakeṣu samantataḥ |
divyena cakṣuṣā paśyanna ca tatrāpyapaśyata || 16.105{4} ||
tataścaturmahārājabhuvaneṣu samantataḥ |
sa divyacakṣuṣā paśyaṃstatrāpi nābhyapaśyata || 16.106{5} ||
tatrāpi tānadṛṣṭvā sa maudgalyo vismayānvitaḥ |
devālayeṣu sarvatra krameṇa samalokayat || 16.107{6} ||
tatrāpi tāṃ samanteṣu sa maudgalyo'tivismitaḥ |
tānadṛṣṭvātisaṃdigdho bhagavatāntike'carat || 16.108{7} ||
tatra sa purato gatvā śāstāraṃ taṃ munīśvaram |
kṛtāñjalipuṭo natvā paprachaitatpravṛttitām || 16.109{8} ||
bhagavan sarvalokeṣu paśyatā divyacakṣusā |
mayā na dṛśyate kaścitte pretāḥ kvādhunā gatāḥ || 16.110{9} ||
teṣāṃ nāmnā bhavatpūjāṃ karttumetatprasādhitam |
yadatra nāgatāḥ kecittatte pretā gatāḥ kuha || 16.111{10} ||
dṛśyante na mayā kvāpi sarvatrāpi samīkṣyate |
eko'pi nāgato hyatra kva gatāste'dhunā khalu || 16.112{11} ||
tanme cittaṃ prasaṃdigdhaṃ dṛṣṭvā śāstā bhavān sudṛk |
teṣāṃ gatiṃ samākhyāya saṃbodhayitumarhati || 16.113{12} ||
iti saṃprārthite tena maudgalyena sa sarvavit |
bhagavāṃstaṃ samālokya maudgalyametadabravīt || 16.114{13} ||
maudgalya mā kṛthā yatnaṃ khedamevābhipatsyase |
gatāste pretikāḥ sarve sudūrabhuvane'dhunā || 16.115{14} ||
sarve te yatidevarṣipratyekasugatairapi |
adṛśye viṣaye yātāḥ preritāḥ karmavāyubhiḥ || 16.116{15} ||
yadi sarvāṃ ca tāṃ draṣṭuṃ vāṃchasi tvamihāśritaḥ |
sarvāṃstāṃ darśayiṣyāmi paśya buddhānubhāvatām || 16.117{16} ||
tadatra tvaṃ samādāya gaṇḍīmākoṭaya drutam |
oṃ namo bhagavate aparimitāyurjñānasuviniścitatejorājāya tathāgatāyārhate samyakambuddhāya || tadyathā ||
oṃ puṇye puṇye mahāpuṇye aparimitapuṇye aparimitapuṇyajñānasaṃbhāropacite |
oṃ sarvasaṃskārapariśuddhe dharmate gagaṇasamudgate svabhāvaviśuddhe mahānayaparivāre svāhā |
aṣṭottaraśatākṣaryyānayāsaṃlabdhacetanāḥ || 16.118{17} ||
sarve te'trāgamiṣyanti gaṇḍīśabdābhināditāḥ |
iti śāstrā samādiṣṭaṃ śrutvā sa pratibodhitaḥ || 16.119{18} ||
sahasā tatsabhāmadhye gaṇḍīmākoṭayattathā |
tadgaṇḍīśabdamākarṇya sarve te pretikā api |
(rm 212)
pariśuddhāśayā draṣṭuṃ vihāre samupācaran || 16.120{19} ||
tatra sarve'pi te pretā dṛṣṭvā taṃ śrīghanaṃ mudā |
tridhā pradakṣiṇīkṛtya sāñjalayaḥ praṇemire || 16.121{20} ||
kramāttāṃ sarvasaṃghāṃśca praṇatvā te pramoditāḥ |
sarve'pyekāntamāśritya paśyanta upatasthire || 16.122{21} ||
tatra tadjñātayaḥ sarve paurikāścāpare'pi ca |
tān pretān samupāsīnān dṛṣṭvā tasthuḥ sakautukāḥ || 16.123{22} ||
evaṃ sarve'pi te pretāḥ svasvajñātīnupasthitān |
bhātrādīn bandhuvargāṃśca dṛṣṭvaivamupatasthire || 16.124{23} ||
tataḥ so'rhanmahābhijño maudgalyastānupasthitān |
pretān sarvān samālokya natvāhaivaṃ munīśvaram || 16.125{24} ||
bhagavannātha sarvajña te pretāḥ sarva āgatāḥ |
tadeṣāṃ bhagavāṃ cchāstānugrahaṃ karttumarhati || 16.126{25} ||
nivedyeti sa maudgalyaḥ saṃbuddhapramukhaṃ kramāt |
sarvaṃ saṃghaṃ samabhyarcya bhojayan samatoṣayat || 16.127{26} ||
tataḥ sa sarvasaṃghaṃ taṃ dṛṣṭvā saṃtṛptikaṃ mudā |
apanīyāśu pātrāṇi hastādīn samaśodhayat || 16.128{27} ||
tataḥ khapurasūkṣmailātāmbūlādi rasāyanam |
datvā kṣamārthanāṃ kṛtvā prārthayatsa munīśvaram || 16.129{28} ||
bhagavannātha sarvajña bhavāṃ chāstā sasāṃghikaḥ |
dakṣiṇādeśanāmebhyaḥ pretebhyaḥ kartumarhati || 16.130{29} ||
iti saṃprārthitaṃ śrutvā sarve te pretikā mudā |
sasaṃghaṃ taṃ muniṃ natvā sādaramupatasthire || 16.131{30} ||
tataḥ sa bhagavān dṛṣṭvā sasaṃghastānupasthitān |
dakṣiṇādeśanāṃ tebhyaḥ sarvebhyo vidadhe śubhām || 16.132{31} ||
ito dānāddhi yatpuṇyaṃ tatpretānanugachatu |
uttiṣṭhantaḥ prayāntyete sarve pretāḥ surālayam || 16.133{21} ||
ityādiśya punastatra bhagavāṃ sa munīśvaraḥ |
āryyasatyaṃ samārabhyāparimitāyuṣastadā || 16.134{22} ||
dhāraṇyā guṇamāhātmyaṃ saddharmaṃ samupādiśat || 16.135{23} ||
saṃsāre mānuṣe janma durlabhaṃ bhavacāriṇām |
mānuṣye labhyamāne'pi kṣaṇasaṃpatsudurlabhā || 16.136{24} ||
mānuṣye'labhyamāne hi jantūnāṃ sukhatā kutaḥ |
duḥkhānyeva bhave nityaṃ puṇye matiḥ kathaṃ caret || 16.137{25} ||
vinā puṇyamatiṃ jantuḥ saddharme kathamācaret |
vinā saddharmabhāvena sadgatiṃ kathamāpnuyāt || 16.138{26} ||
preteṣu sarvadā nityaṃ kṣutpipāsāvidāhitaḥ |
tiryakṣu narakeṣveva bhraman duḥkhānyavāpnuyāt || 16.139{27} ||
tadatra mānuṣe janmaprāptairyatnena mānavaiḥ |
satkṛtya śraddhayā nityaṃ śrotavyaṃ sadvṛṣaṃ mudā || 16.140{28} ||
saddharmaśrutamātreṇa mahatpuṇyamavāpnuyāt |
etatpuṇyavipākena sadgurau sarvadā bhajet || 16.141{29} ||
(rm 213)
sadgurorupadeśena saddharmeṣveva sadācaret |
etatpuṇyavipākena sadgatiṃ sarvadā vrajet || 16.142{30} ||
tatra sukhāni saṃbhuktvā saṃcareta śubhe sadā || 16.143{31} ||
etaddharmānubhāvena mahatsamṛddhimāpnuyāt |
mahatsamṛddhisaṃpatsu karttavyaṃ dānamarthiṣu || 16.144{32} ||
dānena varddhyate lakṣmīrlakṣmīmāṃ cchobhate pumān |
sadbuddhiḥ sadguṇādhāro yaśobhāgyasukhānvitaḥ || 16.145{33} ||
tataḥ saugatadharmāṇi śrutvā nityaṃ prabodhitaḥ |
triratnaśaraṇaṃ kṛtvā bhajedbhaktyā sadādarāt || 16.146{34} ||
etatpuṇyānubhāvena bodhicittaṃ samāpnuyāt |
bodhicitte hi labdhe hi bodhisatvo bhavetkṛtī || 16.147{35} ||
tataḥ sa bodhicittena daśapāramitāḥ kramāt |
paripūrya mahāsatvastrividhāṃ bodhimāpnuyāt || 16.148{36} ||
tato māragaṇāṃ jitvā pariśuddhastrimaṇḍalaḥ |
ṝsākṣādarhatpadaṃ prāpya nirvṛtipadamāpnuyāt || 16.149{37} ||
evaṃ vijñāya martyena karttavyaṃ dānamarthine |
dānena sadgatiṃ yāyādadātā durgatiṃ vrajet || 16.150{38} ||
durgatiṣu sadā bhuktvā duḥkhāni vividhāṃ sa |
sarvadā duḥkhasaṃkliṣṭaḥ pāpeṣvevārataścaret || 16.151{39} ||
etatpāpavipākena daśasvakuśaleṣvapi |
nirato hyaviśaṃkena saṃcareta pramādataḥ || 16.152{40} ||
tataḥ so hyatipāpiṣṭhaḥ saddharmanindakaḥ kudhīḥ |
svayaṃ naṣṭaḥ parāṃścāpi nāśayennāstikaḥ śaṭhaḥ || 16.153{41} ||
tato'tipāpasaṃkliṣṭaḥ pratikṣipya jinānapi |
ghoreṣu narakeṣveva gatvā bhraman sadā vaset || 16.154{42} ||
sadāpi narakeṣvevaṃ bhramatastasya duḥkhinaḥ |
kaścinnaiva samuddhartuṃ śaknuyātsaugatādṛte || 16.155{43} ||
yāvanna smarate buddhaṃ tāvatsa narake vaset |
yadeva smarate buddhaṃ tadā syācchuddhitāśayaḥ || 16.156{44} ||
tadā taṃ kṛpayā buddho dṛṣṭvā bauddhena cakṣuṣā |
puṇyakareṇa saṃspṛṣṭvā samuddharedaraṃ tataḥ || 16.157{45} ||
tatastaṃ pāpinaṃ bauddhā dharmāmbubhirviśodhya ca |
krameṇa bodhimārgeṣu niyojayetprabodhayan || 16.158{46} ||
tataḥ sa pariśuddhātmā saṃbuddhaśāsane rataḥ |
triratnaśaraṇaṃ kṛtvā saddharma eva prācaret || 16.159{47} ||
tataḥ satvahitārthe sa bodhicaryāsamudyataḥ |
kramātpāramitāḥ sarvā pūrayituṃ samudyamet || 16.160{48} ||
tataḥ pāramitāḥ sarvāḥ pūrayitvā samāhitaḥ |
bodhisatvo mahāsatvo buddhātmajo bhavedapi || 16.161{49} ||
tataḥ kleśagaṇāñjitvā pariśuddhatrimaṃḍalaḥ |
trividhāṃ bodhimāsādya nirvṛtipadamāpnuyāt || 16.162{50} ||
evaṃ matvā sadā nityaṃ triratnaśaraṇaṃ gatāḥ |
saddharmaṃ samupāśritya caradhvaṃ mānavā mudā || 16.163{51} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa śrutvā te brāhmaṇādayaḥ |
(rm 214)
sarve lokā parijñāya dṛṣṭasatyāstathācaran || 16.164{52} ||
tathā sarve'pi te pretāḥ śrutvā taddharmadeśanām |
mudā taṃ śrīghanaṃ natvā śuddhātmānastato'caran || 16.165{53} ||
tatastadjñātayaḥ sarve dṛṣṭvā śrutvā sakautukāḥ |
sasaṃghaṃ taṃ muniṃ natvā svasvālayaṃ yayurmudā || 16.166{54} ||
tataḥ sa bhagavān buddhaḥ samutthāya saṣāṃghikaḥ |
dhyānālayaṃ samāśritya tasthau dhyānasamāhitaḥ || 16.167{55} ||
sa maudgalyāyanaścāpi kṛtakṛtyaḥ pramoditaḥ |
śāstāraṃ taṃ praṇatvaivaṃ svamāśramaṃ samāśrayat || 16.168{56} ||
tadā sarve'pi te pretāḥ saṃbuddhe'bhiprasāditāḥ |
triratnasmaraṇaṃ kṛtvā tataścyutā divaṃ yayuḥ || 16.169{57} ||
tatrotpannā hi te sarve mahatsaukhyasamanvitāḥ |
pariśuddhasubhadrāṅgā vismitā etadabruvan || 16.170{58} ||
aho'smākaṃ mahatsaukhyaṃ kimevaṃ jāyate'dhunā |
kutaścyutāḥ kuhāyāmaḥ karmaṇā kena vā vayam || 16.171{59} ||
atha buddhānubhāvena sarve te pretapūrvakāḥ |
devaputrā anusmṛtvā prabodhitāḥ samabruvan || 16.172{60} ||
bhavanto yadvayaṃ sarve pretā buddhānubhāvataḥ |
maudgalyasyārhato bhikṣoḥ kṛpādṛṣṭiprayatnataḥ || 16.173{61} ||
saṃbuddhadarśanaprāptāstriratnaśaraṇaṃ gatāḥ |
etatpuṇyaparispṛṣṭāḥ pariśuddhatrikāyikāḥ || 16.174{62} ||
tataḥ pretagateścyutvā svargaloke samāgatāḥ |
mahaddivyasukhānyevamatra labhāmahe'dhunā || 16.175{63} ||
etatsatyaṃ parijñāya tasya śāsturjagadguroḥ |
punaḥ sarve vayaṃ gatvā satkṛtya prabhajemahi || 16.176{64} ||
iti saṃbhāṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā sarve te pretapūrvakāḥ |
devaputrāḥ śubhāmbhobhiḥ snātvā divyāmvarāvṛtāḥ || 16.177{65} ||
divyasugaṃdhaliptāṅgā divyālaṃkārabhūṣitāḥ |
divyapūjopacārāṇi gṛhītvā mahadutsavaiḥ |
sarvaṃ veṇuvanaṃ bhābhiravabhāsya samantataḥ || 16.178{66} ||
vihāre samupāsṛtya dṛṣṭvā taṃ śrīghanaṃ mudā |
natvā pradakṣiṇīkṛtvā praharṣitā upācaran || 16.179{67} ||
tatra divyopacāraistaiḥ pūjāṃgaistaṃ munīśvaram |
sarve'pi te samabhyarcya satkṛtya śraddhayābhajan || 16.180{68} ||
tatra caikāntamāśritya saddharmmaśravaṇotsukāḥ |
kṛtāñjalipuṭāḥ sarve upatasthuḥ samāhitāḥ || 16.181{69} ||
atha sa bhagavān dṛṣṭvā tān sarvān samupasthitān |
āryasatyaṃ samārabhaḥ saddharmaṃ samupādiśat || 16.182{70} ||
tatsaddharmāmṛtaṃ pītvā sarve te pretapūrvakāḥ |
devaputrāśca dharmāṇāṃ viśeṣatvaṃ pralebhire || 16.183{71} ||
tataste muditāḥ sarve saṃbodhipadavāṃchinaḥ |
śāstāraṃ taṃ punarnatvā samālokyaitadabruvan || 16.184{72} ||
bhagavan yadvayaṃ sarve vimuktakarmabaṃdhanāḥ |
(rm 215)
pretalokātparicyutvā svarge yātāḥ sma sāṃpratam || 16.185{73} ||
divyasukhāni sāmbhuktvā devaiḥ saha pramoditāḥ |
saddharmaguṇamākarṇya carāmahe sadādhunā || 16.186{74} ||
tatkṛpālo yatastasya maudgalyasya mahāmateḥ |
kṛpadṛṣṭiprasādena nūnaṃ manyāmahe vayam || 16.187{75} ||
tadbhavaddarśanaṃ prāpya pītvā dharmāmṛtānyapi |
muditāḥ śraddhayā bhaktyā triratnaṃ saṃbhajāmahe || 16.188{76} ||
etatpuṇyaiḥ parītāṅgāḥ pariśuddhāśayā mudā |
bhavatāṃ darśanaṃ kartuṃ bhūyaḥ prāyāmahe vayam || 16.189{77} ||
tathā ca bhavatāṃ śāstaḥ kṛpādṛṣṭiprasādataḥ |
āryadharmāmṛtaṃ pītvā saṃtuṣṭiṃ na labhāmahe || 16.190{78} ||
tadvayaṃ bhavatāmevaṃ sarvadā śaraṇaṃ gatāḥ |
satkṛtya samupāsṛtya bhaktumichāmahe punaḥ || 16.191{79} ||
tadbhavān kṛpayāsmā kaṃ kṛtvānugrahamābhavam |
saddharmaṃ samupādiśya sarvadā trātumarhati || 16.192{80} ||
bhavatāṃ kṛpayāsmābhi duḥkhābdhiḥ parilaṃghitaḥ |
satkāyadṛṣṭiśailaṃ ca jñānavajreṇa bhidyate || 16.193{81} ||
jñānacakṣuśca saṃprāptaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ māyāmayaṃ bhavam |
āryamārgaśca saṃprāptaḥ prāptā ca nirvṛtiśrutiḥ || 16.194{82} ||
dhanyā ime vayaṃ sarve yadbhavaccharaṇaṃ gatāḥ |
āryadharmāmṛtaṃ pītvā mahānandaṃ labhāmahe || 16.195{83} ||
dhanyāste puruṣāḥ sarve ye buddhaśaraṇaṃ gatāḥ |
sadā dharmāmṛtaṃ pītvā kārāṃ kurvanti śāsane || 16.196{84} ||
evaṃ bhavāṃ jagacchāstā sarvasatvānukampayā |
saddharmaṃ samupādiśya tiṣṭhatu sarvadā sukham || 16.197{85} ||
iti prabhākhya sarve te devaputrāḥ pramoditāḥ |
taṃ munīndraṃ praṇatvā ca suprasannā divaṃ yayuḥ || 16.198{86} ||
tatra sarve'pi te devāstriratnaśaraṇaṃ gatāḥ |
prabhejire sadā smṛtvā saṃbodhipadavāṃchinaḥ || 16.199{87} ||
tataḥ prātaḥ samutthāya bhagavān so'numoditaḥ |
taṃ maudgalyāyanaṃ śiṣyaṃ samāmaṃtryaivamabravīt || 16.200{88} ||
sādhu maudgalya saṃvṛttaṃ saphalaṃ te kṛpāmate |
yattvayā prakṛtaṃ kāyaṃ vaiyāvṛttaṃ suśobhitam || 16.201{89} ||
yannāmnā bhikṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā buddhapūjā kṛtā tvayā |
te sarve devalokeṣu samutpannāḥ pratisthitāḥ || 16.202{90} ||
te sarve'dya niśāyāṃ matsakāśaṃ samupagatāḥ |
teṣāṃ bhadrāśayaṃ dṛṣṭvā saddharmo deśito mayā || 16.203{91} ||
tatsaddharmāmṛtaṃ pītvā te sarve'pi pramoditāḥ |
dṛṣṭasatyāḥ prakrāntāśca gachanti tridaśālaye || 16.204{92} ||
tatra svarge'pi te sarve triratnaśaraṇaṃ gatāḥ |
prabhajanti sadā nityaṃ saṃbodhiguṇavāṃchinaḥ || 16.205{93} ||
yannāmnā yatkṛtaṃ karma tatphalaṃ te prabhuṃjate |
(rm 216)
evaṃ lokahitaṃ kartuṃ karttavyaṃ puṇyasādhanam || 16.206{94} ||
evaṃ lokahitaṃ kṛtvā prakurvanti śubhāni ye |
te pumāṃso mahāsatvā bodhisatvā jinātmajāḥ || 16.207{95} ||
evaṃ vijñāya lokānāṃ hitaṃ kartuṃ samudyataḥ |
saddharmasādhaneṣveva caritavyaṃ samāhitaḥ || 16.208{96} ||
iti śāstrā samādiṣṭaṃ śrutvā so'rhaṃ mahāmatiḥ |
satyamiti pratijñāya prābhyanandatprabodhitaḥ || 16.209{97} ||
tataḥ so'rhanmahābhijñastacchāstrādiṣṭamādarāt |
tadjñātīnāṃ purogatvā sarvamevaṃ nyavedayat || 16.210{98} ||
te'pi ca jñātayaḥ sarve tenārhatā niveditam |
śrutvā satyamiti jñātvā prāmodyaṃ pratilebhire || 16.211{99} ||
tataste jñātayaḥ sarve triratnaśaraṇaṃ gatāḥ |
satkṛtya śraddhayā nityaṃ prabhejire samādarāt || 16.212{100} ||
iti me guruṇādiṣṭaṃ śrutaṃ mayā narādhipa |
lokaprabodhanārthena tava prītyā prakathyate || 16.213{1} ||
evaṃ vijñāya rājendra pretānāṃ śubhasādhanam |
triratnabhajanaṃ lokaiḥ kārayituṃ tvamarhasi || 16.214{2} ||
tathā te maṃgalaṃ nityaṃ bhavennūnaṃ samaṃtataḥ |
sarvasatvahitodbhutaṃ mahatpuṇyaṃ hi satphalam || 16.215{3} ||
iti matvā mahārāja lokān sarvān prabodhayan |
pratiṣṭhāpya śubhe dharme pālayasva samāhitaḥ || 16.216{4} ||
iti śāstrā samādiṣṭaṃ śrutvāśokaḥ sa bhūpatiḥ |
tatheti pratimoditvā prābhyanandatsapārṣadaḥ || 16.217{5} ||
pretāvadānaṃ manujā idaṃ ye śṛṇvanti ye cāpi niśāmayanti |
te sarva evaṃ pratilabdhapuṇyā bhuktvā sukhāni pracaranti loke || 16.218{6} ||

++ iti ratnāva dānatatve pretikāvadānaṃ samāptam ++


(rm 217)
xvii kacaṃgalāvadāna
athāśoko mahārājaḥ pramoditaḥ |
upaguptaṃ yatiṃ natvā punarevamabhāṣata || 17.1{1} ||
bhadanta śrotumicchāmi punaranyatsubhāṣitam |
tadyathā guruṇādiṣṭaṃ tathādeṣṭuṃ ca me'rhati || 17.2{2} ||
iti saṃprārthitaṃ rājñā śrutvā so'rhan yatiḥ sudhīḥ |
upagupto nareṃdrāṃ taṃ samalokyaivamādiśat || 17.3{3} ||
sādhu śṛṇu mahārāja yathā me guruṇoditam |
tathāhaṃ te pravakṣyāmi śrutvā cāpyanumodaya || 17.4{4} ||
puraikasamaye buddhaḥ sākyasiṃho munīśvaraḥ |
sarvajñaḥ sugataḥ sāstā dharmarājo vināyakaḥ || 17.5{5} ||
śrāvastyā nikaṭe'raṇye jetāśrame śubhālaye |
vihāre śrāvakaiḥ sārddhaṃ bhikṣubhiścailakairapi || 17.6{6} ||
bhikṣuṇībhiḥ suśilābhirupāsikāgaṇairapi |
upāsakaistathānyaiśca triratnaśaraṇāgataiḥ || 17.7{7} ||
bodhisatvagaṇaiścāpi satvoddharaṇatatparaiḥ |
vijahāra jagallokaṃ dharmāṃśubhiḥ prabhāsayan || 17.8{8} ||
tatsaddharmāmṛtaṃ pātuṃ sarve lokāḥ samāgatāḥ |
śakrādayaḥ suparvāṇabrahmādi lokapālakāḥ || 17.9{9} ||
daityā nāgāśca gaṃdharvā yakṣakinnararākṣasāḥ |
garuḍā vidyādharāścāpi sarve te saṃpracerire || 17.10{10} ||
tataḥ sarve'pi te tatra puraskṛtya samantataḥ |
parivṛtya sasaṃghaṃ tamupatasthuḥ samāhitāḥ || 17.11{11} ||
tadā sa bhagavān dṛṣṭvā tān sarvān samupasthitān |
ādimadhyāntakalyāṇaṃ dideśa dharmamuttamam || 17.12{12} ||
tatsaddharmāmṛtaṃ pītvā sarve te saṃpramoditāḥ |
dharmaviśeṣamājñāya prābhyanandañchūbhāśayāḥ || 17.13{13} ||
tasminnavasare tatra vṛddhārāmodakārthinī |
kacaṅgalābhidhā kumbhaṃ dhṛtvā kūpāntike'carat || 17.14{14} ||
tatra tāṃ bhagavān dṛṣṭvā matvā janmāntaraprasūm |
ānaṃdaṃ samupāmaṃtrya pura evamabhāṣata || 17.15{15} ||
gachānaṃda tvametasyāṃ vṛddhāyāṃ prārthayāmṛtam |
bhagavāṃstṛṣito mātastadambu dīyatāmiti || 17.16{16} ||
etadbhagavatādiṣṭaṃ śrutvānaṃdastatheti saḥ |
sahasā samupāśritya tāṃ vṛddhāmevamabravīt || 17.17{17} ||
mātarme bhagavāñchāstā tṛṣito'yamihādhunā |
tatpānīyaṃ pradatvā me mahaddharmamavāpnuhi || 17.18{18} ||
(rm 218)
iti saṃprārthitaṃ tena śrutvā sā sthavirā satī |
tamānandaṃ samālokya muditaivamabhāṣata || 17.19{19} ||
bhadanta sādhu tatrāhamamṛtapūritaṃ ghaṭam |
svayameva samānīya dāsyāmi vrajatāṃ bhavān || 17.20{20} ||
iti tayoditaṃ śrutvā sa ānaṃdaḥ prabodhitaḥ |
upetyaivaṃ samākhyāya śāstuḥ pura upāśrayat || 17.21{21} ||
tadā sā sahasā kṛtvā ghaṭaṃ śuddhāmbupūritam |
svayameva samādāya tatrābhisaṃmukhācarat || 17.22{22} ||
tatra sā samupāśritya prādrākṣīttaṃ munīśvaram |
dvātriṃśallakṣaṇāśītivyaṃjanapratimaṇḍitam || 17.23{23} ||
vyāmaprabhābhibhāsvaṃtaṃ śatasūryādhikaprabham |
jaṃgamamiva ratnābhaṃ saumyaṃ samantabhadrakam || 17.24{24} ||
saha taddarśaṇāttatra putrasnehasamudbhavāḥ |
stanābhyāṃ praśrutāstasyāḥ kṣīradhārā niraṃtaram || 17.25{25} ||
tato dharmānubhāvena smṛtvā pūrvabhavātmajam |
putra putreti soktvā taṃ pariṣvektumupācarat || 17.26{26} ||
tadā te bhikṣavaḥ sarve evaṃ tāṃ samupadrutām |
dṛṣṭvaiva sahasotthāya pradhārayitumārabhan || 17.27{27} ||
taddṛṣṭvā bhagavāṃ chastā sarvāṃstāṃ sāṃghikānapi |
sahasā samupāmaṃtrya punarevaṃ samādiśat || 17.28{28} ||
mā bhikṣava imāṃ vṛddhāṃ dhārāyatātra muñcata |
paṃcajanmaśatānyeṣā mātā me bhūyataḥ purā || 17.29{29} ||
tatpūrvajanmamāteyaṃ putrasnehānubhāvinī |
samāliṃgatumīchantī mama gātre pradhāvati || 17.30{30} ||
yadyatraiṣā nivāryyete gātra me śleṣmaṇādapi |
idānīṃ rudhiraṃ hyuṣṇaṃ kamṭhādasyāḥ kṣaretkṣaṇāt || 17.31{31} ||
kṛtajñatāmanusmṛtvā dṛṣṭvemāṃ putralālasām |
kāruṇyādgātrasaṃśleṣaṃ dadāmyatrānukaṃpayā || 17.32{32} ||
iti śāstrā samādiṣṭaṃ śrutvā te sarvasāṃghikāḥ |
tāṃ śāstuḥ pūrvaprasūṃ matvā vyavatasthuḥ savismayāḥ || 17.33{33} ||
tatkṣaṇādurddhavāhuḥ sā samīkṣya taṃ munīśvaram |
sahasā saṃpradhāvitvā samāliṃgya namacciram || 17.34{34} ||
tataścirātsamutthāya sā vṛddhā sāñjalirmudā |
śrīghanaṃ taṃ samālokya purastasthau samāhitā || 17.35{35} ||
tadā sa bhagavān dṛṣṭvā tāṃ puraḥ samupāsthitām |
āryyasatyaṃ samārabhya saddharmaṃ samupādiśat || 17.36{36} ||
tatsaddharmāmṛtaṃ pītvā sa vṛddhāpi kacaṃgalā |
pariśuddhāśayā bhadrā bodhicaryā prabodhitā || 17.37{37} ||
satkāyadṛṣṭibhūmīdhraṃ viṃśatiśikharodgatam |
nirbhidya jñānavajreṇa dṛṣṭasatyaprabodhitā || 17.38{38} ||
saṃprāpya śrotaāpattiphalaṃ sākṣatkṛtodyamā |
sāṃjalistaṃ muniṃ natvā gāthā imā abhāṣata || 17.39{39} ||
(rm 219)
yatkarttavyaṃ svaputreṇa māturduṣkarakāriṇā |
tatkṛtaṃ bhavatā me'tra cittaṃ mokṣaparāyaṇam || 17.40{40} ||
durgatibhyaḥ samuddhṛtya svarge mokṣaṃ ca sāṃpratam |
sthāpito'haṃ prayatnena viśeṣasaṃprabodhane || 17.41{41} ||
tathā me'nugrahaṃ kṛtvā saṃbodhipadasādhanam |
pravrajyāsaṃvaraṃ dātumarhati sāṃprataṃ bhavān || 17.42{42} ||
bhavatāṃ śaraṇaṃ kṛtvā triratnabhajanodyatā |
pravrajyāsaṃvaraṃ dhṛtvā cariṣye brahmacārikām || 17.43{43} ||
ityarthitaṃ tayā śrutvā bhagavān sa munīśvaraḥ |
tasyāścittaviśuddhatvaṃ matvaivaṃ samupādiśat || 17.44{43!} ||
yadi śraddhāsti te mātaḥ pravrajyāsadhane khalu |
bhartturanujñāṃ samāsādya prāgacha cara saṃvaram || 17.45{44} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa śrutvā sā saṃpramoditā |
tathā hīti pratijñāya muniṃ natvā tato'carat || 17.46{45} ||
tatra sā sahasā gehe gatvā taṃ svāminaṃ mudā |
kṛtāṃjalipuṭo natvā puraḥ sthitvaivamabravīt || 17.47{46} ||
svāmi cchṛṇvadbhutaṃ vakṣye yadadyāhaṃ jalārthinī |
ghaṭaṃ dhṛtvā vahirdeśe kūpāntikamupācare || 17.48{47} ||
tatra māṃ samupāyātāṃ dṛṣṭvānando mahāyatiḥ |
sahasā samupāśritya bhagavate'mbu yācate || 17.49{48} ||
tato'haṃ sahasā śuddhaiḥ pūrayitvā jalairghaṭam |
dhṛtvā svayaṃ pradātuṃ tatsabhāyāmupasaṃcare || 17.50{49} ||
tatra taṃ śrīghanaṃ dṛṣṭvā cittaṃ me snehamohitam |
kṣāradhārā stanābhyāṃ ca saṃpraśrutā niraṃtaram || 17.51{50} ||
tatrāhaṃ sneharāgāṃdhā svātmajamiva taṃ munim |
ūrddhavāhuḥ pariṣvaktuṃ pradhāvāmi bhavāntare || 17.52{51} ||
evaṃ māmabhidhāvantīṃ dṛṣṭvā te sāṃghikā api |
sarve pradhātumichantaḥ pradhāvanti samutthitāḥ || 17.53{52} ||
tadā sa bhagavān dṛṣṭvā tān sarvānevamutthitān |
sahasā samupāmaṃtrya pura evaṃ samādiśat || 17.54{53} ||
mā imāṃ sarvathā yūyaṃ nivārayitumarhatha |
yanmameyaṃ puṇyaṃ ca śatajanmaprasū kila || 17.55{54} ||
tadatra māṃ samālokya svātmajasneharāgiṇī |
pariṣvaktuṃ samīchantī pradhāvatīyamunmukhī || 17.56{55} ||
yadyatreṣātra dhāryyeta purato me vimūrchitā |
uṣṇaṃ rudhiramutsṛjya mṛtyuṃ yāyānna saṃśayaḥ || 17.57{56} ||
etacchāstrā samādiṣṭaṃ śrutvā te sāṃghikāstathā |
prabodhitāḥ praṇatvā māṃ svasvāsane samāśritāḥ || 17.58{57} ||
evaṃ tena munīndreṇa samākhyātaṃ niśamya tat |
sarvaṃ prabudhyamānāhaṃ purāvṛttaṃ smarāmi hi || 17.59{58} ||
tato'haṃ vismayākrāṃtahṛdayātyabhinaṃditā |
(rm 220)
tatsaddharmāmṛtaṃ pātumupatiṣṭhāmi sādaram || 17.60{59} ||
tatra sa bhagavān dṛṣṭvā māmevaṃ samupasthitām |
āryasatyaṃ samārabhya samādiśati sadvṛṣam || 17.61{60} ||
tatsaddharmāmṛtaṃ pītvā mano me'tipramodite |
bhūyo'pi tadrasāsvādatṛṣitaṃ pātumichate || 17.62{61} ||
sarve te sāṃghikāstatra saddharmāmṛtatṛptitāḥ |
śuddhātmānaḥ subhadrāṃgāḥ pariśuddhāstrimaṃḍalāḥ || 17.63{62} ||
śuddhaśīlāḥ śubhācārā dayākāruṇyabhāvinaḥ |
sarvasattvahitādhānāḥ saṃsāralābhanispṛhāḥ || 17.64{63} ||
samādhidhāraṇīvidyā ghaṭamānāḥ samāhitāḥ |
sākṣādarhatpadaprāptā nirvikalpā niraṃjanāḥ || 17.65{64} ||
saṃbuddhaśaraṇaṃ kṛtvā saṃbodhisādhanodyatāḥ |
vihāre samupāsīnāḥ pradṛśyante mayā prabho || 17.66{65} ||
tatsabhāgaṃ cariṃ prāptuṃ pravrajyāvratamichyate |
tadatra kṛpayā bharttastadanujñāṃ pradehi me || 17.67{66} ||
iti bhāryāsamākhyātaṃ śrutvā so'tidaridritaḥ |
sāścaryastambhitastasthau paśyaṃstāmeva niścalaḥ || 17.68{67} ||
tataḥ sa suciraṃ bhāryāṃ tāmevamabhilokayan |
viyogaśaṃkayā trastaḥ śanairevamabhāṣata || 17.69{68} ||
are nāri pramūdhāsi kimevaṃ tvaṃ prabhāṣase |
kathamevaṃ tvayākhyātaṃ ko'tra satyaṃ pratīṣyati || 17.70{69} ||
yadasau bhagavān buddhaścakravarttinṛpātmajaḥ |
sarvatraidhātukādhīśo dharmarājo vināyakaḥ || 17.71{70} ||
jagacchāstā jagannāthaḥ sarvajñaḥ sugato jinaḥ |
munīndro mārajidarhan ṣaḍabhijñastathāgataḥ || 17.72{71} ||
bodhisatvo mahāsatvaḥ samaṃtabhadrarūpakaḥ |
daśabalo maheśākhyo brahmacārī jitendriyaḥ || 17.73{72} ||
subrahmāmaradaityādilokānāṃ sarvadehinām |
śaraṇyo mānanīyo hi vandyaḥ pūjyo bhavatyapi || 17.74{73} ||
tvamevaṃ durbhagā nārī daridratātiduḥkhinī |
dāsī paravatī preṣyā kṛpaṇā duḥkṛtākṛtiḥ || 17.75{74} ||
kathamevaṃvidhāyāste garbhe jāto'pyasau mahān |
bodhicittaṃ samāsādya prācaradbodhicārikām || 17.76{75} ||
yataḥ puṇyavipākena pūrya pāramitāḥ kramāt |
adhunā bodhimāsādya saṃbuddho'yaṃ bhavatyapi || 17.77{76} ||
etatpuṇyavibhāgena kathaṃ te īdṛśī gatiḥ |
adyāpīdṛgavasthā hi kiṃ citsaukhyaṃ na vidyate || 17.78{77} ||
tadatra mā vadaivaṃ ca tathoktvā naiva śobhase |
kastavaitadvacaḥ śrutvā supratītaḥ pramāṇayet || 17.79{78} ||
dhikpravādāgnidagdhāṅgā mahatkaṣṭābhikhedītā |
vamitvā rudhiraṃ hyuṣṇaṃ tvaṃ nūnaṃ maraṇaṃ vrajeḥ || 17.80{79} ||
mithyāvādābhisaṃkalpādapi hi narake vrajet |
tvamapi ca tathā nūnaṃ narake na vrajeḥ katham || 17.81{80} ||
(rm 221)
narakeṣu sadā nityaṃ duḥkhāni vividhāni vai |
bhuktvātivedanākrāntā suciraṃ sthāsyase dhruvam || 17.82{81} ||
iti bharttroditaṃ śrutvā sā vṛddhā strī kacaṃgalā |
vibhinnahṛdayā tasthau tannirāśābhimohitā || 17.83{82} ||
tadā sā sucireṇaiva saṃprāpya cetanāṃ tataḥ |
bharttāraṃ taṃ punardṛṣṭvā bodhayitumabhāṣata || 17.84{83} ||
svāminnatra prasīda tvaṃ māmevaṃ vada sarvathā |
satyametanmayā proktaṃ na mithyeti prakalpaya || 17.85{84} ||
yadi mithyāvacaḥ syānme tenāhaṃ narake vraje |
atha me vacanaṃ satyamatrāhaṃ sadgatiṃ vraje || 17.86{85} ||
tenātrobhayathāpyeva viyogaṃ nau bhavetkhalu |
sarveṣāmapi jaṃtūnāmavaśyaṃ maraṇaṃ bhave || 17.87{86} ||
kimevaṃ jīvitenāpi kevalaṃ duḥkhabhāginā |
yatra na vidyate dharmmaṃ tatra kiṃ sukhatāpi ca || 17.88{87} ||
dharmārthameva saṃsāre janmadehārthasādhanam |
dharmaṃ vinā kimetairnaḥ kevalaṃ duḥkhatāptaye || 17.89{88} ||
iti matvā mahānto'pi śrīmanto'pi vicakṣaṇāḥ |
sarvān parigrahān tyaktvā pravrajanti jināśrame || 17.90{89} ||
tathātrāhaṃ parijñāya saṃbuddhaśaraṇaṃ gatā |
saddharmasādhanaṃ karttumichāmi sāṃprataṃ prabho || 17.91{90} ||
tatpravrajyāvratānujñāṃ pradātuṃ me samarhasi |
etatpuṇyavibhāgena tvaṃ cāpi sadgatiṃ vrajeḥ || 17.92{91} ||
yadi na dīyate'nujñā pravrajyāvratasādhane |
akasmātmṛtyunāghrāte tadā kathaṃ nivāraye || 17.93{92} ||
kiyatkālaṃ ca jīveyaṃ vṛddhāhaṃ jirṇitendriyā |
iti matvātra māṃ naivaṃ nivārayitumarhasi || 17.94{93} ||
kiṃ cāhaṃ jaratī vṛddhā tatte kiṃ snehatā mayi |
vṛddhayā durbalāṅgātra kārye kiṃ nu prayojanam || 17.95{94} ||
tvaṃ cāpi jarayākrānto vṛddho'si durbalendriyaḥ |
tatkiṃ te sneharāgeṇa vṛddhāyā mayi sāṃpratam || 17.96{95} ||
adyāpi kiṃ sukhāśā nau jarākrāntaśarīrayoḥ |
avaśyaṃ mṛtyuratrāvāṃ samākramya grasiṣyati || 17.97{96} ||
tadāhaṃ kiṃ kariṣyāmi tvaṃ cāpi kiṃ kariṣyasi |
śocitveva niṣatsyāmi tvamapyevaṃ niṣatsyasi || 17.98{97} ||
avaśyamatra saṃsāre bhāvi bhāvā phalantyapi |
kasyāpi bhūyate nātra bhāvi bhāvānyathā kvacit || 17.99{98} ||
svakṛtakarma evātra gṛhītvā sarvajaṃtavaḥ |
yātā yānti ca yāsyaṃti dehaṃ tyaktvā yamālayam || 17.100{99} ||
tatra yamājñayā sarve svakṛtakarmabhāginaḥ |
dharmiṇaḥ sadgatiṃ yātāḥ pāpino durgatiṃ gatāḥ || 17.101{100} ||
evaṃ sarve ime satvāḥ svadaivaphalabhoginaḥ |
sasukhāni ca duḥkhāni bhuktvā bhramanti sarvadā || 17.102{1} ||
(rm 222)
evaṃ svāmin parijñāya sarvadā sukhavāṃchibhiḥ |
dharmamevātra saṃsāre karttavyaṃ yatnataḥ sadā || 17.103{2} ||
dharman tu pravaraṃ śreṣṭhaṃ saugatasaṃvarodbhavam |
iti buddhaiḥ samākhyātaṃ tvayāpyetacchrutaṃ na kim || 17.104{3} ||
tasmādahaṃ munīndrasya śāsane śaraṇaṃ gataḥ |
pravrajyāsaṃvaraṃ dharttumichāmi sāṃprataṃ prabho || 17.105{4} ||
etatpuṇyavipākena pariśuddhāstrimaṇḍalāḥ |
sākṣādarhatpadaṃ prāpya nirvṛtipadamāpnuyām || 17.106{5} ||
etaddharmavibhāgena tvaṃ cāpi sadgatiṃ vrajeḥ |
iti matvā tathā svāmiṃstadanujñāṃ dadātu me || 17.107{6} ||
tvamatra svagṛhe sthitvā triratnaśaraṇaṃ gataḥ |
yāvajjīvaṃ sukhaṃ bhuktvā śubhe cara sadādarāt || 17.108{7} ||
etatpuṇyavipākena tvaṃ cāpyevaṃ sudhīrbhaveḥ |
tataste saugate dharme vāṃchā jāyeta cetasi || 17.109{8} ||
tatastvaṃ śraddhayā gatvā saṃbuddhaśāsane svayam |
pravrajyāsaṃvaraṃ prāpya sākṣādarhatvamāpnuyāḥ || 17.110{9} ||
iti matvā bhavān svāmin saddharmaṃ yadi vāñchati |
tatpravrajyāvratānujñāṃ mahyaṃ pradātumarhati || 17.111{10} ||
iti tayoditaṃ śrutvā sa daridraḥ prabodhitaḥ |
tāṃ bhāryāṃ suciraṃ dṛṣṭvā śanairevamabhāṣata || 17.112{11} ||
hā priye kathameva tvaṃ māṃ vihātuṃ samichasi |
kimatrāhaṃ vadiṣyāmi tvayaivaṃ pratibhāṣate || 17.113{12} ||
yadi te'sti mano dhairyyaṃ kṛtvā śraddhānvitāśrayā |
pravraja saṃvaraṃ dhṛtvā cara satyasamāhitā || 17.114{13} ||
iti bhartroditaṃ śrutvā sā vṛddhāpi kacaṃgalā |
bharttāraṃ taṃ praṇatvaivaṃ tato'caratpramoditā || 17.115{14} ||
tataḥ sā sahasā gatvā tatrāśrame upāgatā |
śrīghanaṃ taṃ samālokya purataḥ samupācarat || 17.116{15} ||
tatra tatpurato gatvā sāñjaliḥ sā pramoditā |
munīndraṃ taṃ praṇatvaivaṃ pravrajyāṃ samayācata || 17.117{16} ||
bhagavannātha sarvajña bhavatāṃ śaraṇaṃ vraje |
tadbhavāṃ kṛtvā kṛpayā mahyaṃ pravrajyāṃ dātumarhati || 17.118{17} ||
tayeti prārthitaṃ śrutvā bhagavān sa munīśvaraḥ |
savyena pāṇinā tasyā vṛddhāyāḥ śirasi spṛśan || 17.119{18} ||
ehi mataścara brahmacārikāṃ tvaṃ samāhitā |
ityuktvā tāṃ samāgṛhya sāṃghike punarabravīt || 17.120{19} ||
gacha māta prajāpatyā gautamyāḥ śaraṇaṃ vraja |
sārhantī bhikṣuṇīmātā pravrajyāṃ te pradāsyati || 17.121{20} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa śrutvā sā pratinaṃditā |
tathetyāśrutya śāstāraṃ taṃ praṇatvā tato'carat || 17.122{21} ||
tatra sā samupāsṛtya dṛṣṭvā tāṃ gautamīṃ mudā |
(rm 223)
upetya sāṃjalirnatvā pravrajyāṃ samayācata || 17.123{22} ||
mātaratra samāyāmi bhavatyāḥ śaraṇaṃ mudā |
tadbhavantī jaganmātā pravrajyāṃ pradadātu me || 17.124{23} ||
tayeti prārthitaṃ śrutvā gautamī sānumoditā |
hastena śirasi spṛṣṭvā sāṃghikatāṃ samagrahīt || 17.125{24} ||
tatra sāśu prajāpatyāṃ pravrajitā yathāvidhiḥ |
muṇḍitā khikkhirīpātrahastā kākhāyacīvarā || 17.126{25} ||
saumyendriyā subhadraṃgā vṛddhāpi sā kacaṃgalā |
bhikṣuṇīgaṇamadhyasthā śāntarūpātyaśobhata || 17.127{26} ||
tataḥ sā bhikṣuṇī śāstu gautamyāśca prasādataḥ |
samādhidhāraṇīvidyādhyayanābhisamudyatā || 17.128{27} ||
hitvāvidyāgaṇaṃ prāptavidyābhijñāviśāradā |
pratisaṃvidguṇādyāgu pariśuddhākṛtirvabhau || 17.129{30!} ||
atha sā parivijñāya bhavacakraṃ calācalam |
viditvā saṃskāragatīścābhivighātinīstataḥ || 17.130{31} ||
sarvakleśagaṇāṃ jitvā pariśuddhastrimaṇḍalā |
sākṣādarhatpadaṃ prāpya vabhūva brahmacāriṇī || 17.131{32} ||
tadā sā nirjitakleśā nirvikalpā niraṃjanā |
caṃdanakalpasaurabhyā samaloṣṭhasuvarṇikā || 17.132{33} ||
saṃsārabhogasatkāraniṣpṛhākāśasannibhā |
sarvasatvahitādhānā sarvasūtrāṃtapāragā || 17.133{34} ||
sadevāsuralokānāṃ tridhātukanivāsinām |
vaṃdyā pūjyābhimānyā ca vabhūva sā subhāṣiṇī || 17.134{35} ||
yadā sa bhagavañchāstā bhikṣuṇīnāṃ subhāṣitam |
saṃkṣepeṇa samuddiśya dhyānāgāre samāśrayat || 17.135{36} ||
tadā sā bhikṣuṇī tatra kacaṃgalā subhāṣiṇī |
bhikṣuṇīnāṃ sabhāmadhye sutrāntaṃ samupādiśat || 17.136{37} ||
tacchrutvā bhagavāṃstatra tayā sūtrāṃtadeśite |
sarvānstān sāṃghikān bhikṣūn samāmaṃtryaivamādiśat || 17.137{38} ||
eṣo'grā bhikṣavo mātā bhikṣuṇīnāṃ mamāśrame |
sūtrāntabhāgakartṝṇāṃ yaduteyaṃ kacaṃgalā || 17.138{39} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa śrutvā sarve'pi sāṃghikāḥ |
savismayā munīndraṃ taṃ paprachustatpurākṛtam || 17.139{40} ||
bhagavannanayā karmaṃ kiṃ prakṛtaṃ purākṛtam |
imān sarvān sabhāsīnān prabodhayitumarhati || 17.140{41} ||
yeneyaṃ śāsane bauddhe vṛddhā pravrajitādhunā |
sākṣādarhatvamāsādya bhavati brahmacāriṇī || 17.141{42} ||
kiṃ vānayā purākarma kṛtaṃ kutra kadā katham |
sūtrāntabhāgakartṝṇāṃ yenāgreyaṃ bhavatyapi || 17.142{43} ||
kiṃcāpi prakṛtaṃ karmaṃ purānayā kadā katham |
yenātra paścime garbhāvāse na dhārito bhavān || 17.143{44} ||
etatsarvaiḥ parijñāya bhagavānnasyāḥ purākṛtam |
imān sarvān sabhāsīnān prabodhayitumarhati || 17.144{45} ||
(rm 224)
iti tairbhikṣubhiḥ sarvaiḥ prārthite sa munīśvaraḥ |
sarvāṃstān sāṃghikān dṛṣṭvā samāmaṃtryaivamādiśat || 17.145{46} ||
śṛṇuta bhikṣavaḥ sarve yadyatkṛtaṃ purānayā |
tattatsarvaṃ pravakṣyāmi yuṣmākaṃ cittabodhane || 17.146{47} ||
purā me bodhisatvasya bodhicaryānucāriṇaḥ |
paṃcajanmaśatānyeṣā jananyāsīnniraṃtaram || 17.147{48} ||
tadā me dadato dānamantarāyaḥ kṛto'nayā |
tenādyāpi daridreyaṃ durbhagā ca bhavatyapi || 17.148{49} ||
pravrajyāyai tadai caiṣā vahudhā prārthitā mayā |
naivānujñāṃ dadau mahyaṃ vighnameva vyadhāddhaṭhāt || 17.149{50} ||
etatkarmavipākena vṛddhaiṣā duḥkhinī satī |
pravrajyāsaṃvaraṃ prāptā carati mama śāsane || 17.150{51} ||
yadi naiṣā tadā dānaṃ vighnā mamākariṣyati |
naiṣādhunā daridrātra bhavati hi kadā cana || 17.151{52} ||
pravrajyāyāṃ ca me vighnaṃ yadi naiṣākariṣyata |
acariṣyadvrataṃ vālye na tu vṛddhātra sāṃpratam || 17.152{53} ||
yanmamaivaṃ kṛtaṃ vighnaṃ dāne vrate tadānayā |
tenādhunā daridraiṣā vṛddhā vrataṃ caratyapi || 17.153{54} ||
yattadā sukṛtaṃ kiṃ citprakṛtaṃ nānayā purā |
tenāhaṃ paścime garbhāvāse'dhunā na dhāritaḥ || 17.154{55} ||
yatsā māyā mahādevī mahāpuṇyārthasādhinī |
tenāhaṃ paścime garbhāvāse tayātra dhāritaḥ || 17.155{56} ||
bhūyo'pyeṣā purā tatra vāraṇasyāṃ dvijātmajāḥ |
kleśāgniparidagdhāṃgā daridrā kṛpaṇābhavat || 17.156{57} ||
tadā tatra munīndrasya kāśyapasya jagadguroḥ |
śāsane śaraṇaṃ gatvā pravrajitācaradvratam || 17.157{58} ||
tatra sa sarvaśāstrārthakovidātivicakṣaṇā |
sarvasūtrāntasaddharmaṃ bhikṣuṇīnāmupādiśat || 17.158{59} ||
tadā sā bhikṣuṇī tatra kiñcitkāryārtharoṣitāḥ |
sarvāstā bhikṣuṇīrdāsīvādena paryabhāṣata || 17.159{60} ||
etatkarmavipākena saiṣādhunātiduḥkhitā |
parasevākarā dāsī bhavati durbhagākṛtiḥ || 17.160{61} ||
yaccānayā tadā tatra vidyābhyāsīkṛtaṃ mudā |
tenaiṣā sahasārhantī bhavati brahmacāriṇī || 17.161{62} ||
yaccāpyesā tadā tatra sūtrāntaṃ samupādiśat |
tena sūtrāntavaktānāmagrā bhavati sāṃpratam || 17.162{63} ||
evaṃ hi bhikṣavaḥ sarve manyadhvaṃ karmasaṃbhavam |
sukhaṃ duḥkhaṃ ca saṃsāre bhramatāṃ sarvadehinām || 17.163{64} ||
yenaiva yatkṛtaṃ karmaṃ tenaivaṃ bhujyate phalam |
abhuktaṃ kṣīyate naiva karmaṃ kvāpi kadā cana || 17.164{65} ||
nāgnibhirdahyate karmaṃ klidyate nodakairapi |
vāyubhiḥ śuṣyate naiva kṣīyate na ca bhūmiṣu || 17.165{66} ||
(rm 225)
ahyathāpi bhavennaiva vipāke karmaṇaḥ phalam |
yaduptaṃ bhūtale bījaṃ tadeva sūyate phalam || 17.166{67} ||
anyathā na bhavetkvāpi kṛtaṃ karmaṃ phalaṃ bhave |
śubhasya karmaṇaḥ pāke śubhataiva sadā bhave || 17.167{68} ||
kṛṣṇasya kṛṣṇataiva syā miśritasyāpi miśritam |
evaṃ vijñāya kṛṣṇāni vihāya miśritāni ca || 17.168{69} ||
śubha eva sadāpyatra caritavyaṃ śubhārthibhiḥ |
śubhena sadgatiṃ yāyātkṛṣṇena durgatiṃ sadā || 17.169{70} ||
miśritena tathohe ca gatīryāyādbhaveddhruvam |
iti matvātra saṃsāre sarvadā śubhavāñchibhiḥ |
triratnabhajanaṃ kṛtvā caritavyaṃ śubhe sadā || 17.170{71} ||
ye bhajanti sadā buddhaṃ na te gacchanti durgatim |
sarvadā sadgatau bhadraṃ kṛtvā yānti jinālayam || 17.171{72} ||
ye śṛṇvanti sadā dharmmaṃ na yānti te'pi durgatim |
sarvadā bhadrasaukhyāni bhuktvā yānti sukhāvatīm || 17.172{73} ||
dānaṃ dadanti ye saṃghe te'pi na yānti durgatim |
sarvadā śubhasaukhyāni bhuktvā yānti jināśrayam || 17.173{74} ||
evaṃ matvātra saṃsāre triratnaṃ śaraṇaṃ gatāḥ |
satkṛtya śraddhayā nityaṃ bhajadhvaṃ samupāsthitāḥ || 17.174{75} ||
triratneṣu kṛtaṃ karmaṃ tadvipāke mahatphalam |
aprameyamasaṃkhyeyaṃ saṃbodhipadasādhanam || 17.175{76} ||
etatpuṇyānubhāvena bodhisatvāḥ sadā bhave |
sarvasatvahitaṃ kṛtvā pracaranti yathechayā || 17.176{77} ||
nānārūpadharāḥ sarve satvārthasādhanodyatāḥ |
saddharmaṃ samupādiśya pālayanti jagattrayam || 17.177{78} ||
kramātpāramitāḥ sarvāḥ paripūrya śubhānvitāḥ |
sarvakleśagaṇāñjitvā pariśuddhāstrimaṇḍalāḥ || 17.178{79} ||
jitvā māragaṇāñcāpi prāpya saṃbodhimuttamām |
sarvatra dharmasotkṛtvā tato nirvṛtimāyayuḥ || 17.179{80} ||
ye'tītā jināste'pi triratnabhajanodyamāt |
kramādbodhiṃ samāsādya nirvṛtipadamāgatāḥ || 17.180{81} ||
varttamānā jināste'pi triratnabhajanodbhavaiḥ |
puṇyairbodhiṃ samāsādya gacheyurnirvṛtiṃ tathā || 17.181{82} ||
anāgatā jināste'pi triratnabhajanodbhavaiḥ |
puṇyairevaṃ kramādbodhiṃ prāpya yāsyanti nirvṛtim || 17.182{83} ||
ahaṃ cāpi tathā nūnaṃ triratnabhajanodyamī |
etatpuṇyānubhāvena bodhiṃ prāpnomi sāṃpratam || 17.183{84} ||
tathāhamapi sarvatra kṛtvā dharmamayaṃ jagat |
sarvān bodhau niyojyaivaṃ yāsyāmi nirvṛtiṃ dhruvam || 17.184{85} ||
evaṃ ye ye bhajantyatra triratnaśaraṇaṃ gatāḥ |
te te sarve'pi saṃbodhiṃ prāpya nirvṛtimāyayuḥ || 17.185{86} ||
(rm 226)
iti matvātra saṃsāre nirvṛtipadavāṃchinaḥ |
triratnabhajanaṃ kṛtvā pracaradhvaṃ śubhe sadā || 17.186{87} ||
tathā kṛtvātra saddharmaṃbhuktvaiva satsukhānyapi |
kramādbodhiṃ samāsādya nirvṛtiṃ samavāpsyatha || 17.187{88} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa śrutvā sarve'pi te janāḥ |
sāṃghikāśca tathetyuktvā prābhyanaṃdatprabodhitāḥ || 17.188{89} ||
tadā tatra janāḥ sarve triratnaśaraṇaṃ gatāḥ |
satkṛtya śraddhayā nityaṃ prabhejire'numoditāḥ || 17.189{90} ||
etatpuṇyānubhāvaistu tadā samaṃtataḥ |
sarvadā maṃgalotsāhaṃ babhuva nirupadravam || 17.190{91} ||
iti me guruṇākhyātaṃ śrutaṃ mayā tathocyate |
śrutvā rājanstvamapyevaṃ triratnaṃ bhaja sarvadā || 17.191{92} ||
prajāścāpi tathā dharmaṃ śrāvayitvā prabodhayan |
triratnabhajanaṃ nityaṃ kārayituṃ sadārhasi || 17.192{93} ||
tathā te maṃgalaṃ nityaṃ sarvatrāpi bhaveddhruvam |
kramātpāramitā pūrya bodhiṃ cāpi samāpsyasi || 17.193{94} ||
iti tenārhatādiṣṭaṃ śrutvāśokaḥ sa bhūpatiḥ |
tathā hīti pratijñāya prābhyanandan sapārṣadaḥ || 17.194{95} ||
kacaṃgalāyā avadānametacchṛṇvanti ye śrāvayatīha yaśca |
vihāya pāpaṃ khalu te'pi sarve bhuktvā sukhānyeva sugatiṃ vrajanti || 17.195{96} ||

++ iti ratnamālāyāmavadānatatve kacaṃgalāvadānaṃ samāptam ++


(rm 227)
xviii dhanikāvadāna
athāśoko mahīpālaḥ kṛtāṃjalipuṭo mudā |
upaguptaṃ guruṃ natvā punarevaṃ samabravīt || 18.1{1} ||
bhadanta śrotumicchāmi punaranyatsubhāṣitam |
tadyathā guruṇādiṣṭaṃ tathādeṣṭuṃ ca me'rhati || 18.2{2} ||
iti saṃprārthitaṃ rājñā śrutvā so'rhan sudhīryatiḥ |
upagupto narendraṃ taṃ samālokyaivamabravīt || 18.3{3} ||
śṛṇu rājan samādhāya yathā me guruṇoditam |
tathāhaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi tava puṇyapravṛddhaye || 18.4{4} ||
puraikasamaye sa śrīśākyasiṃho munīśvaraḥ |
sarvajñaḥ sugataḥ śāstā dharmarājo vināyakaḥ || 18.5{5} ||
bhikṣubhiḥ śrāvakaiḥ sārddhaṃ bhikṣuṇībhiśca cailakaiḥ |
upāsakai mahāśrāddhairupāsikāgaṇairapi || 18.6{6} ||
bodhisatvagaṇaiścāpi mahāsatvaiḥ śubhārthibhiḥ |
sarvasatvahitārthāya bodhicaryāṃ prakāśibhiḥ || 18.7{7} ||
vṛjiṣu puri vaiśālyāṃ markaṭākhyahradāntike |
kūṭāgāraguhāvāse vijahāra prabhāsayan || 18.8{8} ||
tatsaddharmāmṛtaṃ pātuṃ tatra sarve samāgatāḥ |
brahmaśakrādidevendrā lokapālā digāsthitāḥ || 18.9{9} ||
grahāḥ siddhagaṇāḥ sādhyā yakṣagaṃdharvakinnarāḥ |
garuḍā nāgarājāśca vidyādharāśca dānavāḥ || 18.10{10} ||
rākṣasāḥ sagaṇāścaiva ṛṣayo brahmacāriṇaḥ |
brahmaṇāḥ kṣatriyā bhūpā rājāno'pi narādhipāḥ || 18.11{11} ||
tathā rājakumārāśca vaiśyā maṃtrijanā api |
gṛhasthāḥ śreṣṭhinaḥ paurāḥ sārthavāhā mahājanāḥ || 18.12{12} ||
vaṇijaḥ śilpinaścāpi tathānye'pi śubhārthinaḥ |
grāmyā jānapadāścāpi tathā kārpaṭikā api || 18.13{13} ||
evamanye'pi lokāśca saddharmāmṛtavāṃchinaḥ |
sarve te samupāyātāstatrādrākṣustamīśvaram || 18.14{14} ||
sabhāmadhyāsanāsīnaṃ sarvasaṃghapuraskṛtam |
śrīghanaṃ taṃ samālokya sarve te samupāśritāḥ || 18.15{15} ||
natvā pradakṣiṇīkṛtya samabhyarcya yathākramam |
punaḥ sāñjalayo natvā parivṛtya samaṃtataḥ |
tatsaddharmāmṛtaṃ pātumupatasthuḥ samāhitāḥ || 18.16{16} ||
tadā sa bhagavān dṛṣṭvā tān sarvān samupasthitān |
ādimadhyāntakalyāṇīṃ bodhicaryāṃ samādiśat || 18.17{17} ||
(rm 228)
tadāryadharmamākarṇya sarve lokāḥ pramoditāḥ |
tadā te dharmavaiśeṣyaṃ matvā pratinanaṃdire || 18.18{18} ||
tadā tatra mahāpūryyāṃ vaiśālyāṃ pauriko mahān |
gṛhastho dhaniko nāma śrīmān sarvaguṇī sudhīḥ || 18.19{19} ||
śrāddho bhadrāśayo dātā śuddhaśīlo vicakṣaṇaḥ |
sarvasatvahitādhānādāsītsatpuruṣottamaḥ || 18.20{20} ||
patnī dharmasakhī tasya śīlavatyabhidhābhavat |
tanayaśca vaṃdānyākhyaḥ satyavatyabhidhā snuṣā || 18.21{21} ||
tadā tatra dvijā sarve bhaktimanto jagadguroḥ |
pūjānimaṃtraṇe cakruḥ samayaṃ saṃmatāstathā || 18.22{22} ||
bhavanto yanmunīndro'yamatrāsmākaṃ hitechayā |
saddharmaṃ samupādeṣṭuṃ sasaṃghaḥ samupāgataḥ || 18.23{23} ||
tadasmābhirmunīndro'yaṃ pūjanīyaṃ samādarāt |
ato nimaṃtraṇīyo'tra sarvaiḥ saṃbhūya nānyathā || 18.24{24} ||
ekaścetkurute dravyamadādasya nimaṃtraṇam |
sarvairnirvāsanīyo'sau yato'nyadharmavighnakṛt || 18.25{25} ||
tadā sa dhaniko'jñātvā samayaṃ taistathā kṛtam |
svayameva munīndraṃ taṃ pūjayituṃ samichata || 18.26{26} ||
tataḥ sa dhanikaḥ śrīmān divyasaṃpatsamṛddhimān |
svayaṃ nimaṃtraṇaṃ karttuṃ sasaṃghasya muneryayau || 18.27{27} ||
tatra sa samupāsṛtya natvā taṃ śrīghanaṃ mudā |
sasaṃghaṃ tatpuraḥ sthitvā prārthayadeva sāṃjaliḥ || 18.28{28} ||
bhagavannātha sarvajña bhagavaṃtaṃ sasāṃghikam |
pūjayituṃ samichāmi paśyanme'nugrahaṃ kuru || 18.29{29} ||
iti saṃprārthitaṃ tena bhagavān sa munīśvaraḥ |
tathā hīti pratiśrutvā tūṣṇībhūtvādhyuvāsa tat || 18.30{30} ||
tadā te brāhmaṇāḥ sarve saṃbhūya samupāgatāḥ |
bhagavataścaraṇau natvā cakrurbhojyanimaṃtraṇam || 18.31{31} ||
tacchrutvā bhagavān prāha tān dṛṣṭvā bhaktimānasān |
pūrvameva sasaṃghāhamanenodya nimaṃtritaḥ || 18.32{32} ||
etadākarṇya sarve te brāhmaṇā bheditāśayāḥ |
paraṃ pratīkṣyamānāśca śanaiḥ svasvagṛhaṃ yayuḥ || 18.33{33} ||
tataḥ sa dhaniko matvā munīndreṇādhyuvāsitam |
muditastaṃ muniṃ natvā sahasā svagṛhaṃ yayau || 18.34{34} ||
tatra sa svagṛhe gatvā vāṃdhaveṣṭajanaissaha |
tadarhabhojyasāmagrīṃ sahasā samasādhyayat || 18.35{35} ||
tato bhojyasthalaṃ tatra śodhayitvā samantataḥ |
ratnadhvajairvitānaiśca sa patākairamaṇḍayat || 18.36{36} ||
tato yathākramaṃ tatra divyaratnamayāni ca |
svāsanāni samāstīryya sugaṃdhīḥ samadhūpayat || 18.37{37} ||
(rm 229)
tataḥ sa prātarutthāya snātvā śuddhāmvarāvṛtaḥ |
pūjāpādyārghasāmagrīṃ pratiṣṭhāpya pramoditaḥ || 18.38{38} ||
savāṃdhaveṣṭajanastatra kūṭāgāre samācarat |
sāñjalistaṃ muniṃ natvā prārthayadevamādarāt || 18.39{39} ||
bhagavannātha sarvajña samayo varttate'dhunā |
tatsasaṃgho mamāvāse vijayituṃ samarhasi || 18.40{40} ||
iti saṃprārthite tena bhagavān sa sasāṃghikaḥ |
khikkhirīpātramādāya pratasthe cīvarāvṛtaḥ || 18.41{41} ||
tatra sa bhagavānmārge kṛtvā bhadraṃ samantataḥ |
prabhāsayan krameṇaivaṃ vaiśālyāṃ samupaviśat || 18.42{42} ||
tathāyātaṃ munīndraṃ taṃ śrīghanaṃ sāṃghikānvitam |
dṛṣṭvā vaiśālikāḥ lokāḥ sarve'pi samapūjayan || 18.43{43} ||
evamabhyarcyamāno'bhivaṃdyamāno'bhinaṃditaḥ |
bhāsayan bhadratāṃ kṛtvā saṃghaiḥ saha samācarat || 18.44{44} ||
tatra tasya gṛhe gatvā sasaṃghaḥ sa munīśvaraḥ |
taddattaṃ pādyamādāya svāsane samupāśrayat || 18.45{45} ||
tataste sāṃghikāścāpi sarve tatra yathākramam |
taddattaṃ pādyamādāya svasvāsane samāśrayan || 18.46{46} ||
tataḥ sa dhaniko dṛṣṭvā sasaṃghaṃ taṃ munīśvaram |
svasvāsane samāsīnaṃ pūjāṅgaiḥ samapūjayat || 18.47{47} ||
tato divyopacāraistaṃ bhagavantaṃ sasāṃghikam |
supraṇītai rasopetairbhojanaiḥ samatoṣayat || 18.48{48} ||
tadbhojyaṃ surasaṃ bhuktvā bhagavān sa sasāṃghikaḥ |
amṛtairiva saṃtuṣṭastṛptiṃ yayau pramoditaḥ || 18.49{49} ||
asaṃghaiḥ taṃ muniṃ tṛptaṃ dṛṣṭvā sa dhaniko mudā |
tato'panīya pātrāṇi taddhastādīnyaśodhayat || 18.50{50} ||
tataḥ śuddhauṣadhīpūgatāmvūlādi rasāyanam |
buddhapramukhasaṃghebhyo muditaḥ sa svayaṃ dadau || 18.51{51} ||
tataḥ sa harṣito natvā taṃ munīndraṃ sasāṃghikam |
purataḥ sāñjaliḥ sthitvā prārthayadevamādarāt || 18.52{52} ||
bhagavannātha sarvajña bhavatāṃ śaraṇaṃ gataḥ |
bhaktyaiva bhajanaṃ kurve tatkṣamasvāparādhatām || 18.53{53} ||
sadaivaṃ kṛpayā dṛṣṭya paśyan bhavān sasāṃghikaḥ |
mamānugrahamādhāya trātumarhati sarvathā || 18.54{54} ||
tataḥ sa bhagavāñchāstā sasāṃghiko'numoditaḥ |
dhanikaṃ taṃ samālokya naṃdayannevamādiśat || 18.55{55} ||
gṛhapate sadā nityamārogyamastu vo dhruvam |
bodhipraṇidhisiddhiśca bhūyādbhadraṃ ca sarvadā || 18.56{56} ||
tatastasya priyā bhāryā śīlavatyanumoditā |
sāñjaliḥ praṇatiṃ kṛtvā prārthayattaṃ munīśvaram || 18.57{57} ||
bhagavannātha sarvajña śvaścātra bhavatāmaham |
(rm 230)
pūjāṃ kartuṃ samicche tadanugrahītumarhati || 18.58{58} ||
tayeti prārthite dṛṣṭvā bhagavān sa tadāśayam |
śraddhānvitaṃ pratijñāya tuṣṇībhūtvādhyuvāsa tat || 18.59{59} ||
tataḥ sa bhagavāñchāstā samutthāya sasāṃghikaḥ |
bhāsayaṃ bhadratāṃ kṛtvā svāśrame ca samāśrayat || 18.60{60} ||
tataḥ paradine prātaḥ snātvā śuddhāṃvarāvṛtā |
sādhitvā sarvasāmagrīṃ sā dūtaṃ preṣayanmunau || 18.61{61} ||
sa dūtaḥ sahasā gatvā vihare taṃ munīśvaram |
sasaṃghaṃ sāṃjalirnatvā punarevaṃ nyavedayat || 18.62{62} ||
bhagavannātha sarvajña samayo varttate'dhunā |
tatsasaṃgho bhavāṃstatra sahasāgantumarhati || 18.63{63} ||
tataḥ sa bhagavānnevaṃ bhadraṃ kṛtvā sasāṃghikaḥ |
dhanikasya gṛhe gatvā pādyaṃ gṛhyāsane'viśat || 18.64{64} ||
tatrāsane samāsīnaṃ sasaṃghaṃ taṃ munīśvaram |
dṛṣṭvā śīlavatī sātha yathāvidhi samarcayat || 18.65{65} ||
tataśca sā muditā sarvaṃ saṃghaṃ buddhādikaṃ tathā |
divyopacārasaṃyuktairbhojanaiḥ samatoṣayat || 18.66{66} ||
tataḥ saṃtarppitaṃ dṛṣṭvā śodhayitvā karādikam |
datvā tāmvurapūgādiṃ natvā sā samupāśrayat || 18.67{67} ||
tataḥ sa bhagavān tasmai datvāśiṣaṃ sasāṃghikaḥ |
samutthāya tataścaivaṃ svavihāre samāśrayat || 18.68{68} ||
tathā sarve tadānyo'pi dhanikasyātmajaḥ sudhīḥ |
tatparedyu vihāraṃ taṃ gatvā natvā nyamaṃtrayat || 18.69{69} ||
bhagavānnapi tasyaivaṃ dṛṣṭvā śraddhānvitaṃ manaḥ |
tatheti pratimoditvā tūṣṇībhūtvādhyuvāsa ca || 18.70{70} ||
tataḥ sastaṃ muniṃ natvā svagṛhaṃ sahasāgataḥ |
bāṃdhavaiḥ sarvasāmagrīṃ muditaḥ samasādhayat || 18.71{71} ||
tataḥ prātaḥ samutthāya snātvā śuddhāmvārāvṛtaḥ |
vihāre samupāsṛtya natvaivaṃ munimabravīt || 18.72{72} ||
bhagavannātha sarvajña samayo varttate'dhunā |
tatsaṃgho bhavān gehe mamāgantuṃ samarhati || 18.73{73} ||
