tataḥ sasaṃgha utthāya bhagavān sa tathācarat |
tadgṛhe pādyamādāya tatrāsane samāśrayat || 18.74{74} ||
tataḥ sa mudito buddhapramukbaṃ sarvasāṃghikam |
samabhyarcya tathā divyabhojanaiḥ samatoṣayat || 18.75{75} ||
tato'panīya pātrāṇi śodhayitvā karādikam |
datvā pūgādikaṃ natvā purataḥ samupāśrayat || 18.76{76} ||
punaḥ sa sāṃjalirnatvā prakṛtvā ca kṣamārthanām |
sasaṃghaṃ taṃ munīndraṃ taṃ saṃdṛṣṭvaivaṃ samupāśrayat || 18.77{77} ||
tadā tasya snuṣā cāpi satyavatī pramoditā |
bhagavaṃtaṃ sasaṃghaṃ taṃ praṇatvaivaṃ nyamaṃtrayat || 18.78{78} ||
(rm 231)
bhagavānnapi tasyāśca dṛṣṭva śraddhānvitaṃ manaḥ |
tatheti pratimoditvā tuṣṇībhūtvā radhāṃ vyadhāt || 18.79{79} ||
tataḥ sa bhagavāṃstasmai datvāśiṣaṃ sasāṃghikaḥ |
tathā kṛtvā śubhaṃ loke gatvāśrame samāśrayat || 18.80{80} ||
tataḥ sāpi tathā prātaḥ snātvā śuddhāmvarāvṛtā |
dūtenāpi munīndraṃ taṃ sasaṃghaṃ samacodayat || 18.81{81} ||
tathā sa bhagavāṃścāpi sasāṃghikaḥ samutthitaḥ |
pracaran bhadratāṃ kṛtvā tasyā gehe samāviśat || 18.82{82} ||
tatra pādyārghamādāya bhagavān sa sasāṃghikaḥ |
śubhāsane samāsīnastasthau tatra prabhāsayan || 18.83{83} ||
tathāsthitaṃ munīndraṃ taṃ dṛṣṭvā saṃpramoditā |
yathāvidhi samabhyarcya tathā bhojyairatoṣayat || 18.84{84} ||
tātastaṃ sugataṃ tṛptaṃ sasāṃghikaṃ samīkṣya sā |
tatrāpanīya pātrāṇi hastādīṃ paryaśodhayat || 18.85{85} ||
tataḥ pūgādikaṃ datvā prārthayitvā kṣamāṃ tathā |
sasaṃghaṃ taṃ muniṃ natvā sā sāñjalirupāśrayat || 18.86{86} ||
tathā sa bhagavāṃstasyai datvāśīṣaṃ sasāṃghikaḥ |
tato vihāyasā gatvā svāśrame ca samāśrayat || 18.87{87} ||
tathā sa dhaniko bhaktyā bhagavantaṃ sasāṃghikam |
divyādbhutarddhisaṃbhāraiḥ punarevaṃ nyamaṃtrayat || 18.88{89!} ||
tatputreṇa tṛtīye'hni caturthe snuṣayā tathā |
sasaṃgho bhagavānnevaṃ divyabhogairnimaṃtritaḥ || 18.89{90} ||
tacchrutvā taddvijāḥ sarve vaiśālikāḥ prajā api |
alabdhāvasarāḥ śāstuḥ pūjāyāṃ praticukruśuḥ || 18.90{91} ||
tataste saṃmataṃ kṛtvā sarve viprāḥ sapaurikāḥ |
dhanikāya puraṃ kruddhāścakrurnikāsanodyamam || 18.91{92} ||
evaṃ tatsamataṃ jñātvā bhagavān sa munīśvaraḥ |
dhanikaṃ taṃ samāhūya pura evamupādiśat || 18.92{93} ||
sādho gṛhapate sarve ime vaiśālikā dvijāḥ |
kruddhāste saṃghabhojye'tra hyalabdhāvasarā mama || 18.93{94} ||
tadatra tvaṃ kṣamāṃ kṛtvā dvijān sarvān prabodhaya |
kṛtvātra vinayaṃ dharme prasādayitumarhasi || 18.94{95} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa śrutvā sa dhanikastathā |
ityāśrutya dvijān sarvān samupetyaivamabravīt || 18.95{96} ||
bhavanto na mayā jñātaṃ bhavatāṃ kopakāraṇam |
tadatra me samākhyāta yadarthe kupyate'dhunā || 18.96{97} ||
iti tenoditaṃ śrutvā sarve te brāhmaṇā |
kopāgniparidagdhāsyā dhanikaṃ taṃ ninindire || 18.97{98} ||
tatraiko brāhmaṇaḥ sādhurdayāluḥ sukṛtārthavit |
dhanikaṃ taṃ samāmaṃtrya bodhayituṃ samabravīt || 18.98{99} ||
sādho gṛhapate'smābhiryathātra samataṃ kṛtam |
(rm 232)
tathā tatte pravakṣyāmi śrutvā tatpratibudhyatām || 18.99{100} ||
bhavanto yanmunīndro'yamatrāsmākaṃ hitechayā |
saddharmaṃ samupādeṣṭuṃ sasāṃghikaḥ samāgataḥ || 18.100{1} ||
tadasmābhirmunīndro'yaṃ pūjanīyaḥ samādaraiḥ |
ato nimaṃtraṇīyo'tra sarvaiḥ saṃbhūya nānyathā || 18.101{2} ||
ekaścetkurute vittamadādasya nimaṃtraṇam |
sarvairnirvāsanīyo'sau yato'nyadharmavighnakṛt || 18.102{3} ||
ityasmābhiḥ kṛtaṃ sādho samayaṃ sarvasaṃmatam |
tadvilaṃghya tvayaikena śāstuḥ pūjā kṛtā sadā || 18.103{4} ||
etaddheto ime sārve brāhmaṇā ruṣitāstathā |
niṣkāsayitumichanti tvāmanyadharmavādhakam || 18.104{5} ||
kiṃ cāpi bhagavānatra sarvadā sthāsyate na hi |
tatsarve bhagavatpūjāṃ kartuṃ vāṃchanti sāṃpratam || 18.105{6} ||
yaccāyaṃ bhagavāñchāstā dharmarājo munīśvaraḥ |
sarvasatvahitārthāya samutpanna ihā'dhunā || 18.106{7} ||
tathātra sarvasatvānaṃ hitaṃ kartumupācaran |
saddharmaṃ samupādiśya caratyayaṃ samaṃtataḥ || 18.107{8} ||
tathātrāpi hitaṃ kartumasmākaṃ samupāgataḥ |
saddharmaṃ samupādiśya vijayate sasāṃghikaḥ || 18.108{9} ||
tadatra sarvalokānāṃ triratnabhajanotsavam |
dātumarhasi sarveṣāmapi puṇyapravṛddhaye || 18.109{10} ||
eka eva tvamevaṃ tu mā kṛthā bhajanaṃ sadā |
sarvaiḥ saṃbhūya te sāstuḥ karttavyaṃ bhajanaṃ sadā || 18.110{11} ||
iti tenoditaṃ śrutvā dhanikaḥ sa vivodhitaḥ |
brāhmaṇaṃ taṃ samālokya praṇatvaivamabhāṣata || 18.111{12} ||
mayā na jñāyate hyevaṃ bhavadbhiḥ samayaṃ kṛtam |
tadatra me'parādhatvaṃ kṣantumarhanti sarvathā || 18.112{13} ||
yadi vo vidyate śraddhā triratnabhajane sadā |
kurudhvaṃ sarvadāpyatra saṃbuddhabhajanaṃ khalu || 18.113{14} ||
yadā na vidyate pūjā saṃbuddhe'smin sasāṃghike |
tadaivāhaṃ kariṣyāmi saṃbuddhabhajanaṃ khalu || 18.114{15} ||
iti tena samākhyātaṃ śrutvā sarve'pi te dvijāḥ |
satyamiti parijñāya vabhūvustatprasāditāḥ || 18.115{16} ||
tataste brāhmaṇāḥ sarve saṃbhūya saha paurikaiḥ |
bhagavataḥ sasaṃghasya nimaṃtritumupāsaran || 18.116{17} ||
tatra te brāhmaṇāḥ sarve sametya saha paurikaiḥ |
sāñjalayo munīndraṃ taṃ natvaivaṃ prārthayanmudā || 18.117{18} ||
bhagavannātha sarvajña trimāsyaṃ bhavatāṃ sadā |
satkāraṃ kartumichāma tvadanujñāṃ dadātu naḥ || 18.118{19} ||
iti taiḥ prārthite sarvairdṛṣṭvā sa bhagavānmuniḥ |
teṣāmāśayaśuddhatvaṃ tūṣṇībhūtvādhyuvāsa tat || 18.119{20} ||
(rm 233)
tataste brāhmaṇāḥ sarve bhagavatādhivāsitam |
matvā sapaurikā natvā taṃ muṇiṃ svapure'caran || 18.120{21} ||
tatra te paurikā viprāḥ śodhayitvā samamṭataḥ |
bhojyakṣatre dhvajairuccairvitānaiḥ paryaśobhayan || 18.121{22} ||
tatrāsanāni prajñapya sarvasāmagrasādhanam |
kṛtvā sarve'pi te lokā mahotsāhaṃ pracakrire || 18.122{23} ||
tataste brāhmaṇāḥ prātaḥ snātvā śuddhāmvarāvṛtāḥ |
tatrāśrame sametyaivaṃ natvā taṃ prārthayanmunim || 18.123{24} ||
bhagavannātha sarvajña samayo varttate'dhunā |
tatsasaṃgho bhavāṃstatra pure āgantumarhati || 18.124{25} ||
tataḥ sa bhagavān buddhaḥ sasaṃghaścīvarāvṛtaḥ |
khikkhiripātramādāya saṃpratasthe prabhāsayan || 18.125{26} ||
tatra mārgeṣu sarvatra bhagavān sa sasāṃghikaḥ |
bhāsayaṃ bhadratāṃ kṛtvā pracaraṃstatpure'viśat || 18.126{27} ||
tatra sa bhagavān kṣetre suśodhite sasāṃghikaḥ |
taddattaṃ pādyamādāya svāsane samupāśrayat || 18.127{28} ||
saṃbuddhapramukhaṃ sarvaṃ saṃghaṃ te samatoṣayat |
tatastaṃ sugataṃ tṛptaṃ saṃghaṃ cāpi vilokyate || 18.128{29} ||
tatpātrāṇyupanītvā taddhastādīkaṃ vyaśodhayat |
tataḥ sa pūgatāmbūramahauṣadhyarasāya nam || 18.129{30} ||
datvā kṣamārthanaṃ kṛtvā taṃ sasaṃghaṃ praṇemire |
tataste brāhmaṇāḥ sarve paurāścāpi pramoditāḥ |
tatsaddharmāmṛtaṃ pātimupātasthuḥ samāhitāḥ || 18.130{31} ||
tatra sa bhagavān dṛṣṭvā sarvāṃstān samupasthitān |
āryyasatyaṃ samārabhya saddharmaṃ samupādiśat || 18.131{32} ||
tato bhadrāśiṣaṃ datvā bhagavān saha saṃghikaiḥ |
bhāsayaṃ bhadratāṃ kurvan gatvā svāśramamāśrayat || 18.132{33} ||
evaṃ vaiśālikā lokāste traimāsyaṃ niraṃtaram |
yathārhabhojanaiḥ śāstuḥ sasaṃghasya prabhejire || 18.133{34} ||
tato'nte dhanikaścāpi patnī putraḥ snuṣāpi ca |
saṃbuddhapramukhaṃ saṃghaṃ divyabhogyaiḥ samārcayat || 18.134{35} ||
bhagavānnapi tathā tebhyo datvā śubhāśiṣaṃ tataḥ |
samutthāya sasaṃghaśca gatvāśrame samāśrayat || 18.135{36} ||
tataḥ sa dhanikaḥ patnīputrasnuṣāsamanvitaḥ |
dṛṣṭasatyaḥ sadā ratnatrayasevārato'bhavat || 18.136{37} ||
etatpuṇyānubhāvena bhūyo'tiśrīsamṛddhimān |
sarvalokahitaṃ kṛtvā pracacāra śubhe sadā || 18.137{38} ||
taddṛṣṭvā vismitāḥ sarve bhikṣavaḥ samupāśritāḥ |
bhagavantaṃ praṇatvaivaṃ paprachustatpurākṛtam || 18.138{39} ||
bhagavan kiṃ purānena dhanikena vṛṣaṃ kṛtam |
kutra kathaṃ kadaitannaḥ sarvamākhyātumarhati || 18.139{40} ||
(rm 234)
iti tairbhikṣubhiḥ pṛṣṭe bhagavān sa munīśvaraḥ |
sarvānstāṃ saṃghikān dṛṣṭvā tadaivaṃ samupādiśat || 18.140{41} ||
śṛṇuta bhikṣavaḥ sarve yadanena vṛṣaṃ kṛtam |
tatpatnyā ca putreṇa snuṣayā ca taducyate || 18.141{42} ||
varāṇasyāmabhūtpūrvaṃ mālikaḥ kamalābhidhaḥ |
durbhikṣakṣapite kāle parāṃ durgatimāgataḥ || 18.142{43} ||
patnī patnavikā nāma putraḥ kuvaliyābhidhaḥ |
pāṭalokhyā snuṣā ceti vabhūvustasya saṃmatāḥ || 18.143{44} ||
tadaikasmin dine tasya gṛhāntike prapuṣpite |
udyāne sāyamāgatya pratyekabuddha āśrayet || 18.144{45} ||
te sarve ekavastrā hi paryyāyena nṛpālaye |
datvā puṣpāṇi prāyātāḥ svabhavane niṣīdire || 18.145{46} ||
tadā sa ca gṛhasthāste tatrodāne jvalatprabhām |
dṛṣṭvā kiṃ mahojvālamudyāne iti vismitāḥ || 18.146{47} ||
sahasā te samutthāya tatrodyāne nirīkṣitum |
sarvagatāḥ samīkṣantaḥ dadṛśustaṃ prabhāsayam || 18.147{48} ||
tatra te samupāśritya dṛṣṭvā taṃ dhyānasaṃsthitam |
sāñjalayaḥ praṇatvaiva muditā svagṛhaṃ yayuḥ || 18.148{49} ||
tatra te svagṛhāsīnāḥ prātastatpūjanotsukāḥ |
nirddhanāstadvyathāṃ prāpu lūnapakṣāḥ khagā iva || 18.149{50} ||
tadā sa mālikastasmai pūjāṃ kartuṃ samutsukaḥ |
taddravyaṃ svagṛhe samyaganviṣya samalokayat || 18.150{51} ||
tatra gṛhe sa sarvatra samanviṣya samaṃtataḥ |
kañci mātramapi dravyamalabdhaivaṃ vyaciṃtayat || 18.151{52} ||
aho mayā purā pāpaṃ prakṛtaṃ kiṃ yato'dhunā |
akiṃcanyo daridro'smi kimatra jīvitena me || 18.152{53} ||
yato na vidyate dravyaṃ kiṃ cidapi gṛhe mama |
tatkena kariṣyāmi pūjāmasya mahātmanaḥ || 18.153{54} ||
atrāyaṃ svayamāgatya mamodyāne samāśritaḥ |
pratyekabuddha ātmajo dhyātvā tiṣṭhati bhāsayan || 18.154{55} ||
tadasya sugatasyātra karttavyā satkṛtirmayā |
kadāyaṃ puri ramyatra samāgachetsvayaṃ punaḥ || 18.155{56} ||
tatprātaḥ śūnyapātreṇa gachedito mamātmavit |
tanmamātra bhave śūnyamiva janmāpi nisphalam || 18.156{57} ||
yato mānyo'bhipūjyo yaṃ pūjyate na kathaṃ cana |
tatra kiṃ jāyate bhadraṃ puṇyaṃ vāsya kathaṃ kutaḥ || 18.157{58} ||
puṇyaṃ vinātra saṃsāre kiṃ sāraṃ janma nisphalam |
bhuktvāpi kiṃ sukhānyatra suciraṃ jīvite nanu || 18.158{59} ||
puṇyārthe jīvitaṃ janma tadatra yadi naśyati |
kimeva jīvitenāpi janmanā pāpasādhinā || 18.159{60} ||
tanmṛtyurme varaṃ hyatra na mithyācirajīvitam |
avaśyameva saṃsāre sarvatra maraṇaṃ dhruvam || 18.160{61} ||
kimevaṃ dehapuṣṭena bhuktvā duḥkhāṃ sarvadā |
tatkenāpi pradānena pūjayeyamimaṃ jinam || 18.161{62} ||
(rm 235)
puṇyena sadgatiṃ yāyāṃ pāpena durgatiṃ sadā |
tatpāpaṃ parityaktuṃ puṇyaṃ prāptu yateya hi || 18.162{63} ||
yadyatra pūjyate nāyaṃ drakṣyate ca kadā kuha |
buddhapūjāṃ vinā bhadre puṇyaṃ ca lapsyate kutaḥ || 18.163{64} ||
yāvanna prāpyate puṇyaṃ tāvaddurgaticāraṇam |
tasmātpuṇyaṃ prayatnena karttavyaṃ sukhatāptaye || 18.164{65} ||
tadatra śraddhayā kiṃcinmātreṇāpi svavastunā |
satkṛtyainaṃ mahābhijñaṃ bhajeyaṃ samupasthitaḥ || 18.165{66} ||
iti dhyātvā viniścitya mālikaḥ saṃpramoditaḥ |
bhāryāṃ putraṃ snuṣāṃ cāpi samāmaṃtryaivamabravīt || 18.166{67} ||
aye priye snuṣe putra yūyaṃ śṛṇuta madvacaḥ |
yadatra kartumichāmi tatra yūyaṃ prasādataḥ || 18.167{68} ||
yadatrāyaṃ mahābhijñaḥ svayameva samāgataḥ |
asmākaṃ gṛhe dravyaṃ kiñcitmātraṃ na vidyate || 18.168{69} ||
tatkenātra kariṣyāmaḥ pūjāmasya mahātmanaḥ || 18.169{69!} ||
yadi na pūjyate'smābhiḥ sugato'yaṃ muniryatiḥ |
tadā puṇyaṃ vayaṃ kutra lapsyāmahe kadā katham || 18.170{70} ||
puṇyaṃ vinātra saṃsāre nisphalaṃ janma jīvitam |
tadvayaṃ puṇyato prāptuṃ yatema sarvathā vayam || 18.171{71} ||
puṇyena sadgatiṃ prāpya pāpena durgatiṃ sadā |
tatsadgatisukhaprāptyai puṇyaṃ kurvīmahi dhruvam || 18.172{72} ||
caturṇāṃ vidyate'smākamekaṃ sarvasvamamvaram |
anenāpyenamāchādya prabhajamahi sāṃpratam || 18.173{73} ||
nādyarājakule nagnā gachāmaḥ kiṃ bhaviṣyati |
etatme vacanaṃ śrutvābhyanumoditumarhatha || 18.174{74} ||
iti tenoditaṃ śrutvā patnī putraḥ snuṣāpi te |
sarve'pyabhyanumoditvā tathā kurviti procire || 18.175{76!} ||
ityetatsamayaṃ kṛtvā sarve te śraddhayānvitāḥ |
gatvā tenaikavastreṇa taṃ prāchādya samārcayan || 18.176{77} ||
tataste muditāḥ sarve kṛtāñjalipuṭā muneḥ |
tasya pādau praṇatvaivaṃ praṇidhānaṃ pracakrire || 18.177{78} ||
anena vastradānena vayaṃ sarve śubhāśayāḥ |
bhaveyaṃ sadgatiṃ yātā divyasaṃpattihāninaḥ || 18.178{79} ||
śāstāraṃ sugataṃ buddhaṃ samārādhya sadā bhave |
triratnabhajanaṃ kṛtvā bhavema bodhicāriṇaḥ || 18.179{80} ||
iti tairmuditaiḥ sarvaiḥ praṇidhānaṃ kṛtaṃ tathā |
viditvā sa mahāvijñastathāstviti samādiśat || 18.180{81} ||
tatastesāṃ manoharṣaṃ kṛtvā sa sugato muniḥ |
tata uḍḍīya pakṣīva bhāsayan svāśrame yayau || 18.181{82} ||
taddṛṣṭvā te nanditāḥ sarve buddhadharmānuśaṃsinaḥ |
triratnasmaraṇaṃ kṛtvā pracerire sukhaṃ sadā || 18.182{83} ||
tatpuṇyapraṇidhānena dhaniko'yaṃ sa mālikaḥ |
(rm 236)
jāto divyaprabhāvarddhiḥ patnīputrasnuṣānvitaḥ || 18.183{84} ||
manaḥśuddhividhānena dānenātiglaghīyasā |
bhavantyalaghvyāḥ saṃkalpaiḥ saṃpadaḥ satvaśālinām || 18.184{85} ||
buddhakṣetreṣu yatkarma sukṛtaṃ duḥkṛtaṃ tathā |
mahattaratvamāsādya phalatyeva sadā bhave || 18.185{86} ||
iti matvātra saṃsāre karttavyaṃ sukṛtaṃ sadā |
bauddhe satkṛtapuṇyena labhanti bodhisaṃpadaḥ || 18.186{87} ||
iti śāstrā samādiṣṭaṃ śrutvā sarve'pi sāṃghikāḥ |
menire cittavaimalyamūlaṃ dānaphalaśriyam || 18.187{88} ||
tacchrutvā brāhmaṇāste'pi tatpuṇyavismitā mudā |
bhagavaṃtaṃ sadā smṛtvā prabhejire'numoditāḥ || 18.188{89} ||
tathā te brāhmaṇāścāpi sarve'pi paurikāstathā |
tatpuṇyapariśuddhāṃśā vabhuvuḥ kuśalodyatāḥ || 18.189{90} ||
helārpitaṃ ratnadhanaṃ tṛṇāgraṃ śraddhāvatīrṇaṃ tṛṇamapyanargham |
anekaśobhānubhavaṃ hi cittaṃ cittaṃ nimittaṃ śubhasaṃbhavānām || 18.190{91} ||
iti me guruṇādiṣṭaṃ śrutaṃ mayā tathocyate |
śrutvāpyevaṃ mahārāja karttavyaṃ sukṛtaṃ sadā || 18.191{92} ||
prajāścāpi mahārāja bodhayitvā prayatnataḥ |
kramādbodhiṃ samāsādya saṃbuddhapadamāpnuyāḥ || 18.192{94} ||
iti tenārhatākhyātaṃ śrutvāśokaḥ sa bhūpatiḥ |
tathā hīti pratijñāya prābhyanandatsapārṣadaḥ || 18.193{95} ||
idaṃ narā ye dhanikāvadānaṃ śṛṇvanti ye cāpi niśāmayanti |
sukhāni bhuktvā sa śubhe sadā te sarve'pi śamyānti jinālayan te |

++ iti ratnāvadānatatve dhanikāvadānaṃ samāptam ++


(rm 237)
xix raivatāvadāna
athāśoko narendraḥ sa kṛtāṃjalipuṭo mudā |
upaguptaṃ guruṃ natvā punarevamabhāṣata || 19.1{1} ||
bhadanta śrotumichāmi punaranyatsubhāṣitam |
tadyathā guruṇākhyātaṃ tathādeṣṭuṃ ca me'rhati || 19.2{2} ||
iti sāṃprārthitaṃ rājñā śrutvā so'rhaṃ mahāmatiḥ |
upagupto narendraṃ taṃ samālokyaivamādiśat || 19.3{3} ||
sādhu śṛṇu mahārāja yathā me guruṇoditam |
tathāhaṃ te pravakṣyāmi śrutvaivaṃ paribudhyatām || 19.4{4} ||
krauryeṇa dvirasanāḥ śuciveṣṭitānāṃ mithyāpavādaviṣamaṃ viṣamutsṛjanti |
te pāpaśāpaparitāpaparaṃparārttāstīvravyalīkatimiravivaraṃ viśanti || 19.5{5} ||
tadyathābhūtpurā bhikṣuḥ kāsmīre patitāśrame |
raivatakābhidho bauddhaḥ sarvabhūtadayāśrayaḥ || 19.6{6} ||
sa kadāciddhanoddeśe vivikte sughaṭāntare |
tarutvacaḥ kaṣāyena cakre cīvararaṃjanam || 19.7{7} ||
tasminnavasare tatra piśunākhyadvijanmanaḥ |
dhenuvatsā vane naṣṭā vabhūvuryūthanirgatāḥ || 19.8{8} ||
tadā sa brāhmaṇo dṛṣṭvā taṃ duṣṭāśitaśaṃkitaḥ |
sarvatrāpi samanviṣṭā tatrāpyanveṣituṃ yayau || 19.9{9} ||
tatra taccīvarakvāthavāke vahnisamudgatam |
dhūmaṃ sa dvija ālokya vatsapākamamanyata || 19.10{10} ||
tataḥ sa brāhmaṇaḥ sārddhaṃ svajanaiḥ śastrapāṇibhiḥ |
taddraṣṭuṃ śailamāruhya sahasāgāttadantike || 19.11{11} ||
tatra taṃ raivataṃ bhikṣuṃ dṛṣṭvā sa dvija ādarāt |
upetya cūḍikeḍyāhataṃ kumbhamapaśyat || 19.12{12} ||
taddṛṣṭvā śaṃkitaścāsau vinayān samupāsaran |
kimetatkriyate bhikṣoriti taṃ paryapṛchata || 19.13{13} ||
iti pṛṣṭe dvijenāsau raivato bhikṣurādarāt |
tatra cīvararāgo'yamiti taṃ dvijamabravīt || 19.14{14} ||
asminnavasare tasyā pūrvakarmavipākataḥ |
gomāṃsaraktapāṇīyaḥ sapākaḥ samapadyata || 19.15{15} ||
sukhaṃ duḥkhatvamānāti śuklamapyeti kālatām |
vidhau vidhuratāṃ yāte dharmo'pyāyātyadharmatām || 19.16{16} ||
mithyāpāpaprakaṭatā janakopaḥ padacyutiḥ |
(rm 238)
apuṇyaparipākānāmetatpratyakṣalakṣaṇam || 19.17{17} ||
doṣaḥ samunmiṣati yātyaguṇaḥ prakāśaṃ kāryaṃ viparyayamupeti viśīryate dhīḥ || 19.18{18} ||
puṃsāṃ purāvihitaḥ duḥkṛtapākakāle ke ke na nāma nipatanti mahābhighātāḥ || 19.19{19} ||
tataḥ sa brāhmaṇo'pyatra kumbhe kimiti śaṃkitaḥ |
paśyāmīti viniścitya nirīkṣitumupācarat || 19.20{20} ||
viruddhāmiṣagaṃdhena rudhireṇa ca śaṃkitaḥ |
apaśyan sa dvijaḥ kumbhe māṃsapiṇḍaṃ supakvitaiḥ || 19.21{21} ||
pratyakṣadoṣamālokya dvijaḥ krodhito ruṣā |
tamabhāṣata nirbhatsya tīvravaiśaśakaṃpitaḥ || 19.22{22} ||
aho vata sadācāraḥ sthito'yaṃ vijane vane |
yasyedṛśāni karmāṇi na kasyacidiha paśyati || 19.23{23} ||
pravrajyāraṃjitaḥ kāyaḥ kriyā mlechajanocitā |
jānāti channapāpānāṃ kaḥ kūṭavrataśāntitām || 19.24{24} ||
ityukte tena sākṣepaṃ sa raivato'pyaciṃtayat |
doṣe pratyakṣalakṣyasmin kiṃ bravīmi niruttaraḥ || 19.25{25} ||
mama daivāya ghāto'yamityukte ko'numanyate |
hāsyāyatanatāmeti pratyakṣyāpahnavī janaḥ || 19.26{26} ||
upasthitaṃ sahe sarvaṃ maunamālambya kevalam |
ayaṃ me niḥpratīkāraḥ salilādagnidutthitaḥ || 19.27{27} ||
doṣe guṇātiśayamāśu guṇe'pi doṣaṃ paiyūṣadhāmni viṣamapyamṛtaṃ viṣe ca ||
saṃdarśayatyaniśamadbhutarūpameva kālendrajālikāvadhūrbhavitavyeyam || 19.28{28} ||
ityevaṃ manasā dhyātvā raivataḥ sa mahāmatiḥ |
svadaivasmaraṇaṃ kṛtvā tasthau yogasamāhitaḥ || 19.29{29} ||
iti dhyānaṃ samālambya sthitaṃ taṃ raivataṃ munim |
dṛṣṭvātiruṣitaścāsau saṃtaryya paryabhāṣata || 19.30{30} ||
are re durmate bhikṣo kimatra durjane vane |
sthitvevaṃ dāruṇaṃ pāpaṃ sādhayitvā niṣīdasi || 19.31{31} ||
are pāpiṣṭha bhāṣasva yadarthe vālakāṃ vṛṣam |
hatvātra pacyate māṃsa tvayātmano pareṇa vā || 19.32{32} ||
pareṇāpi hataṃ dṛṣṭvā tatpāpaiḥ parilipyase |
tattvayā vṛṣabhī hatvā kathaṃ pāpairna dahyase || 19.33{33} ||
dhigdhiktvāmatra rebhikṣo saṃbuddhaśāsane yataḥ |
pravrajyāsaṃvaraṃ dhṛtvā nirjane vana āśrayan || 19.34{34} ||
mahadduḥkarakarmāṇi kṛtvaivaṃ tapase'dhunā |
are re śrūyate kiṃ na tvayā saṃbuddhabhāṣitam || 19.35{35} ||
atha śrutvāpi te tatra śraddhā na vidyate khalu |
kimevaṃ pātakaṃ kṛtvā pravrajyāsaṃvaraṃ caran || 19.36{36} ||
kevalaṃ pāpakānyeva sādhayitvātra tiṣṭhase |
pravrajito viraktātmā pariśuddhatrimaṃḍalaḥ || 19.37{37} ||
sarvasatvadayādṛṣṭiścarennityaṃ samāhitaḥ || 19.38{37!} ||
adattaṃ kasya ciddravyaṃ gṛhīyānna kathaṃ cana |
(rm 239)
dayākaruṇyayuktātmā na kuryātprāṇipīḍinam || 19.39{38} ||
kāmabhogamadharmeṇa naiva bhuṃjyātkadā cana |
mṛṣāvādaṃ kadāpyatra naiva brūyātkathaṃ cana || 19.40{39} ||
pāruṣyavacanaṃ cāpi naiva brūyātkathaṃ cana |
paiśunyavacanaṃ vāpi vadenna kasya citpuraḥ || 19.41{40} ||
saṃbhinnapralāpaṃ ca na pravadetkasya cidapi |
vyāpādaṃ kasya ciccāpi bhāvayenna kathaṃ cana || 19.42{41} ||
abhidhyāṃ ca tathā naiva ciṃtayetkasya cidapi || 19.43{42} ||
mithyādṛṣṭiṃ sadā kvāpi bhāvayenna kadā cana |
etāni daśapāpānāṃ mūlāni sarvathā tyajet || 19.44{43} ||
etāni ye samālabhya pracaranti pramādataḥ |
te sarve kleśasaṃtaptā bhramanti duḥkhino bhave || 19.45{44} ||
tataḥ kleśāgnisaṃtaptāḥ pāpeṣveva samāhitāḥ |
duḥkhānyeva sadā bhuktvā narakeṣu bhramanti te || 19.46{45} ||
iti matvātra saṃsāre tyaktvaitāni daśāpi hi |
santo nityaṃ śubheṣveva pracaranti samāhitāḥ || 19.47{46} ||
śubhena sadgatiṃ yānti papina durgatiṃ sadā |
ityākhyātaṃ jinaiḥ sarvaiḥ kiṃ naitacchrūyate tvayā || 19.48{47} ||
kathametadavajñāya pravrajitastvamācareḥ |
dhikte cittaṃ yadevaṃ me govatsaṃ bhoktumichati || 19.49{48} ||
pravrajito hi śuddhātmā pariśuddhastrimaṇḍalaḥ |
sarvasatvadayācittaścarennityaṃ samāhitaḥ || 19.50{49} ||
sarvasatvahitaṃ kṛtvā brahmacaryaṃ samāhitaḥ |
bhikṣānnamātrasaṃkṛṣṭaḥ suniḥspṛhaścarecchubhe || 19.51{50} ||
samādhinirato yogī dhāraṇīparamārthabhṛt |
sarvakleśān vinirjitvā sarvamāragāṇānapi || 19.52{51} ||
sarvān parigrahān tyaktvā nirvikalpo niraṃjanaḥ |
sākṣādarhatvamāsādya brahmacaryaṃ samācaret || 19.53{52} ||
evaṃ pravrajito bhikṣuścarannityaṃ suśīlabhṛt |
śivāṃ bodhiṃ samāsādya nirvṛtipadamāpnuyāt || 19.54{53} ||
tvaṃ tu pravrajito'pyevaṃ mahaddāruṇapātakam |
kṛtvātra nirjane bhuktvā carase mlechacārikām || 19.55{54} ||
hā vatātra vinaṣṭo'si yadetatpātakānvitaḥ |
sahasā narake gatvā ciraṃ duḥkhāni bhokṣyasi || 19.56{55} ||
kiṃ tvayā prakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ purā janmāntare katham |
yadatra saugate dharmaṃ prāpto'pyevaṃ durāśayaḥ || 19.57{56} ||
taccarasva samādhāya prāyaścittavrataṃ punaḥ |
etatpāpābhisaṃśuddhyai punarvratasamāptaye || 19.58{57} ||
tathā te śuddhite kāye maṃgalaṃ ca bhavetkramāt |
pariśuddhastrikāyaśca pravrajyāsaṃvaraṃ cara || 19.59{58} ||
tadā tvaṃ sarvathā jitvā kleśānmāragaṇānnapi |
sākṣādarhatvamāsādya brahmacārī bhaviṣyati || 19.60{59} ||
(rm 240)
tadā tvaṃ pariśuddhātmā nirvikalpo niraṃjanaḥ |
śivāṃ bodhiṃ samāsādya nirvṛtipadamāpnuyāḥ || 19.61{60} ||
iti tenoditaṃ śrutvā raivataḥ sa prabodhitaḥ |
tathāpi maunamādhāya tathau daivābhyanusmaran || 19.62{61} ||
evaṃ sthitaṃ tamālokya brāhmaṇaḥ sa ruṣānvitaḥ |
evaṃ dvidhā tridhāpyenaṃ paribhāṣyābhyanindayat || 19.63{62} ||
evaṃ dvidhā tridhāpyukte brāhmaṇena sa raivataḥ |
svadaivamabhyanusmṛtvā maunenaiva nyaṣīdata || 19.64{63} ||
svadaivacintayastasya maunātsa brāhmaṇo ruṣā |
raivataṃ tāṃ katākṣeṇa dṛṣṭvainaṃ paryabhāṣata || 19.65{64} ||
are re durmate bhikṣo kimevaṃ tiṣṭhase'dhunā |
uttiṣṭhātra pravaṃdhitva nayāmi tvāṃ nṛpāntike || 19.66{65} ||
evaṃ tenodite'pyeva sthitaḥ sa raivato yatiḥ |
kiṃ cinnaivāvadenmaunamevālambya nyaṣīdata || 19.67{66} ||
tataḥ sa brāhmaṇastasya maunādatiprakopitaḥ |
murddhni pāpamiva sthūlaṃ laguḍaṃ marpayātayat || 19.68{67} ||
tathābhighātyamāno'pi raivataḥ sa samāhitaḥ |
daivamevābhyanusmṛtvā tasthau niḥkaṃpitāśayaḥ || 19.69{68} ||
tathāsthitaṃ taṃ munimātmavidyaṃ daivaṃ smaraṃtaṃ hyakṛtāparādham |
nināya paścātkṛtavāhudaṃḍaṃ sadastadā naṃdanabhūmibhartuḥ || 19.70{69} ||
tathāsthitaṃ tamālokya sa dvijaḥ pratiroṣitaḥ |
raktākṣaṃ raivataṃ vaddhvā sahasānayannṛpāntike || 19.71{70} ||
tatra sa brāhmaṇo nītvā taṃ rājñaḥ sthitaḥ |
tadvatsapiśitaṃ pakvamupasthāpya'bhyadarśayat || 19.72{71} ||
tataḥ sa brāhmaṇaḥ krūrastasya bhikṣo savistaram |
sarvavṛttāṃtamākhyāya taṃ nṛpaṃ paryakopayat || 19.73{72} ||
tadvatsamāṃsamālokya nṛpatiḥ sa prakopitaḥ |
raivataṃ taṃ yatiṃ vaddhvā cakre kārāgṛhātithim || 19.74{73} ||
nirdoṣaḥ kleśamāśnāti tambhate guptapātakaḥ |
jānāti kasya kaḥ śuddhiṃ citrākāreṣvasākṣiṣu || 19.75{74} ||
tatra sa vaṃdhanāgāre vaṃdhito'pi sa sanmatiḥ |
triratnasmaraṇaṃ kṛtvā tasthau daivānucintayan || 19.76{75} ||
tatraivaṃ vaṃdhanāgāre tasmin bhikṣau nivandhite |
kāle na visnito rājā tadvicāre'pi nāsmarat || 19.77{76} ||
tadānyasmin dine tatra te vatsāḥ sarva āgatāḥ |
mātṛbhiḥ saha saṃsaktāstṛṇaṃ bhuktvā niṣedire || 19.78{77!} ||
tān vatsānmātṛsaṃsaktān sarvānālokya sa dvijaḥ |
vismitaḥ punarālokya paśyannevaṃ vyaciṃtayat || 19.79{78} ||
aho vatsā ime sarve kutra gatvādhunāgatāḥ |
(rm 241)
sarvatrānviṣyamānā hi kutrāpi nābhyadṛśyate || 19.80{79} ||
hā mamātra mahatpāpo jāyate sāṃprataṃ katham |
yanmayā sahasā bhikṣorasamīkṣyāparādhyate || 19.81{80} ||
yathā bhikṣorghaṭe mānsaṃ dṛṣṭvaiṣāmiti cintayat |
caurāyamiti taṃ bhikṣuṃ paryabhāṣaṃ ruṣābhṛtam || 19.82{81} ||
aparādhaṃ vinārhaṃtamapi bhikṣuṃ pratāḍayat |
vaddhvā taṃ sahasā rājñaḥ puro'tra samupānayam || 19.83{82} ||
rājāpi mama vākyena sahasaiva nimaṃtrayan |
vaddhvāśu vaṃdhanāgāre prasthāpayati sāṃpratam || 19.84{83} ||
tadatra kiṃ kariṣyāmi vatsāḥ sarve ihāgatāḥ |
aparādhā yaterneti vadeya sāṃprataṃ katham || 19.85{84} ||
yadyavakṣyaṃ tathā rājā tadā me kupito nṛpaḥ |
sarvasvamapahṛtvāpi prāhariṣyate māmiha || 19.86{85} ||
atha yadi bhayādrājño nāvadiṣyaṃstathā kvacit |
nūnaṃ taṃ yatimarhantamapi rājā haniṣyate || 19.87{86} ||
tadāhaṃ kiṃ kariṣyāmi tadghorapātakānalaiḥ |
pacyamānassadāvīcau tiṣṭheyamatiduḥkhitaḥ || 19.88{87} ||
tadatra kiṃ kariṣyāmi yatropāyaṃ na vidyate |
sarvathāhaṃ vinaṣṭo'smi vrajeya śaraṇaṃ kuha || 19.89{88} ||
yadimaṃ saugataṃ bhikṣumarhantaṃ brahmacāriṇam |
anaparādhinaṃ hatvā kathaṃ pāpairna lepsyate || 19.90{89} ||
etairhi pātakairghorairahaṃ rājā janā ime |
sarve vatsyāmahe'vīcau bhuktvā duḥkhāni sarvadā || 19.91{90} ||
hā me daivādyateścāpi nirjane vasato'pi yat |
ghaṭe'bhidṛśyate vatsamānsībhūtaṃ hi cīvaram || 19.92{91} ||
avaśyaṃ bhāvino bhāvā bhavantyeva na cānyathā |
tadatra kiṃ kariṣyāmi bhikṣurvā kiṃ kariṣyati || 19.93{92} ||
tasyaiva karmadoṣeṇa māṃsībhūtaṃ hi cīvaram |
atha mamaiva daivena taditi manyate na hi || 19.94{93} ||
tadatra kiṃ vadeyāhaṃ daivaṃ hi sarvakarmakṛti |
iti mamārho'pyasya dūṣaṇaṃ nātra vidyate || 19.95{94} ||
tathapyetatpravṛttirna vaktavyaṃ kasya cidapi |
na gaṃtavyaṃ vahiḥ kvāpi sthātavyaṃ svagṛhe sadā || 19.96{95} ||
kadā citskhalitā buddhirvāṇī ca vismṛtasya me |
etaddhi pātakaṃ ghoraṃ samācakṣata kutra cit || 19.97{96} ||
tadāhaṃ niṃdyamāno'tra sarvalokairitastataḥ |
bhratsyamānaḥ kathaṃ loke cariṣye duṣṭajaṃtuvat || 19.98{97} ||
iti dhyātvā viniścitya brāhmaṇaḥ sa viṣāditaḥ |
kasyāpyetatpravṛttāṃtaṃ noce daurjanyalajjayā || 19.99{98} ||
tatpātakāgnisaṃtaptaḥ smṛtvārhantaṃ tameva saḥ |
prāyaścittavrataṃ kurvanniva tasthau gṛhāśritaḥ || 19.100{99} ||
evaṃ dvādaśavarṣāṇi nṛpeṇāpi sa vismṛtaḥ |
(rm 242)
raivato vaṃdhanāgāre tasthau dhyānasamāhitaḥ || 19.101{100} ||
atha dvādaśavarṣānte tacchiṣyāḥ sarva āgatāḥ |
tatpravṛttiṃ samākarṇya vismitāste'bhavaṃstadā || 19.102{1} ||
tataḥ sarve'pi te śiṣyāḥ saṃmīlya nṛpateḥ puraḥ |
upetyaitatpravṛttāṃtaṃ samākhyāya nyavedayan || 19.103{2} ||
ārogyamastu te rājan lokān dharmeṇa pālaya |
yadarthe vayamāyāmastatra bhavān prasīdatu || 19.104{3} ||
yadasmākaṃ gururbhikṣuraparādhaṃ vinā katham |
bhavatā vaṃdhanāgāre sthāpitastadvicāraya || 19.105{4} ||
iti tairbhikṣubhiḥ sarvaistacchiṣyaiḥ prārthite tadā |
vyomavāṇī tathākhyāya narendraṃ taṃ vyabodhayat || 19.106{5} ||
tatkhavāṇiṃ samākarṇya nṛpaḥ sa paribodhitaḥ |
tacchiṣyaiḥ sārito bhikṣuṃ taṃ mumocāśu vaṃdhanāt || 19.107{6} ||
aho grāvāgralikhitā niścalā karmasaṃtatiḥ |
prāptabhijño'pi yatprāpa krūrakleśadaśāṃ tathā || 19.108{7} ||
taṃ yatiṃ nirgataṃ dṛṣṭvā sa rājābhyānutāpitaḥ |
ninindā mandapuṇyatvaṃ pramādāddhatamātmanaḥ || 19.109{8} ||
tadā sa nṛpati rājā sahasā tasya pādayoḥ |
sāṃjaliḥ praṇatiṃ kṛtvā samutthāyaivamabravīt || 19.110{9} ||
bhadanto vicāryaiva pramadā yadbhavānmayā |
sthāpito vaṃdhane tanme'parādhaṃ kṣaṃtumarhati || 19.111{10} ||
vismṛtā me sahāyāṃ ca parijñātā na kena cit |
daṇḍaḥ patati śuddheṣu prāptaḥ pāpe mahīpatau || 19.112{11} ||
ityuktaḥ kṣitipālena kṣāntiprakṣālitāśayaḥ |
raivataḥ sa nareṃdraṃ taṃ samālokyaivamabravīt || 19.113{12} ||
rājanna te'parādhatvaṃ mamaitatkarmajaṃ phalam |
tadatra mā kṛthāḥ kheṭaṃ saugata ātmavidyutam || 19.114{13} ||
tatastaṃ yatimarhantaṃ sa rājā sahasādarāt |
cīvareṇa samāchādya piṃḍakena samārcayat || 19.115{14} ||
tadaitadvṛttimākarṇya brāhmaṇaḥ sa tvarānvitaḥ |
āgatya tatsabhāmadhye dattāśīḥ samupācarat || 19.116{15} ||
tatra sa samupāsṛtya rājñe datvā jayāśiṣām |
taṃ yatiṃ sāñjalirnatvā prārthayan vinayātkṣamām || 19.117{16} ||
bhadanta bhavatāmevamaparādhamakārayan |
tanme'parādhatāṃ kṣaṃtumarhati sarvathā bhavān || 19.118{17} ||
ityukte tena vipreṇa raivataḥ sa viśuddhadhīḥ |
brāhmaṇaṃ taṃ viṣaṇṇāsyaṃ samālokyaivamabravīt || 19.119{18} ||
na manyurvidyate vipra na tvayāpakṛtaṃ mayi |
mamaiva daivayā kena māṃsībhūtaṃ hi cīvaram || 19.120{19} ||
tatrāhaṃ maunamālambya sthito daivābhyanusmaran |
tenātra kiṃ vadiṣyāmi daivā hi valavān bhave || 19.121{20} ||
na tvayāpākṛtaṃ kiñcidvipulakleśapātitaḥ |
(rm 243)
tanmamopanataṃ pāke svakarmasadṛśaṃ phalam || 19.122{21} ||
yatsautkaṇṭhatayaiva sarvavipadaḥ kurvanti kaṇṭhagrahaṃ sarvāṅgaprasabhopabhogasubhagāḥ kliśyanti yatsaṃpadaḥ |
yatsvāchaṃdyasukhāspadaṃ viharaṇaṃ dīrghaṃ ca yadvaṃdhanaṃ tatpuṃsāṃ nijakarmapākaśavalaṃ saṃsāravallīphalam || 19.123{22} ||
iti tenārhatākhyātaṃ śrutvā naṃdaḥ sa bhūpatiḥ |
vismitaḥ kautukāccaivaṃ dṛṣṭvā taṃ yatimabravīt || 19.124{23} ||
tavāpi sumate kasya phalametatkukarmaṇaḥ |
kiṃ purā prakṛtaṃ karman tatsamākhyātumarhati || 19.125{24} ||
iti pṛṣṭe narendreṇa raivataḥ sa sudhiryatiḥ |
taṃ nareṃdraṃ samālokya bodhayituṃ tathāvadat || 19.126{25} ||
sādhu śṛṇu mahārāja yanmayā prakṛtaṃ purā |
tadatrāhaṃ pravakṣyāmi sarvalokaprabodhane || 19.127{26} ||
vārāṇasyāṃ purā krūracarito'bhūtkuthābhidhaḥ |
gocauraḥ kutsikācāro gomāṃsavihitāśanaḥ || 19.128{27} ||
kadā citsa vane gatvā hatvā govatsamātmanā |
tadvatsamāṃsamādāya sahasā tato viniryayau || 19.129{28} ||
tatra gorakṣiṇo vatsanaṣṭamanviṣya sarvataḥ |
dṛṣṭvā taṃ māṃsabhārārttaṃ kopātsamabhidudruvuḥ || 19.130{29} ||
tatra sa kutha ālokya tān sarvān samupadrutān |
sahasopadruto'raṇye pradudrāva vanāṃtare || 19.131{30} ||
tatra pratyekabuddhasya dhyānalīnasya so'grataḥ |
upanikṣipya tanmāṃsaṃ tatraikānte nyalīyata || 19.132{31} ||
te'pi gorakṣiṇaḥ sarve sānucarāmabhidrutāḥ |
tatra pratyekabuddhaṃ tamālokya samupācaran || 19.133{32} ||
tatra sa kutha āgatya teṣāṃ gorakṣiṇāṃ puraḥ |
gomāṃsaṃ darśayitvā taṃ caurāyamityadarśayat || 19.134{33} ||
tatra te gobhṛtaḥ sarve gomāṃsaṃ tanmuneḥ puraḥ |
dṛṣṭvaiva sahasā kopāttaṃ munimabhyaghātayat || 19.135{34} ||
vadhyo'yamiti taṃ vadhvā gopālāste prakopitāḥ |
ākruśya sahasānītvā kārāgāre nyavaṃdhayat || 19.136{35} ||
tataḥ sa kutha ākarṇya taṃ yatiṃ vaṃdhane dhṛtam |
paścāttāpāgnisaṃtaptastahītyevaṃ vyaciṃtayat || 19.137{36} ||
hā mayā pāpināpyevaṃ sādhyate pātakāni ca |
avaśyaṃ vahnināvīcau kuṃhbhyāṃ pakṣye rataṃściram || 19.138{37} ||
yadayaṃ nirjane dhyāto pratyekabuddha ātmavit |
caurāyamiti saṃdarśya vaṃdhane sthāpitomayā || 19.139{38} ||
etatpāpavipāke hi sarvadā narake sthitaḥ |
tīvraduḥkhāgnisaṃtapta ante māṃ svakṛte phalam || 19.140{39} ||
hā mayaivaṃ mahāghoraṃ pātakaṃ durdhiyā kṛtam |
kadā tatpātakānmukto vrajeyaṃ sadgatiṃ katham || 19.141{40} ||
(rm 244)
hā sarvajña namastubhyaṃ paśya māmatipāpinam |
bhavānneva jagattrātā tanme'pi trātumarhati || 19.142{41} ||
mayā mūḍhena duṣṭena mahāpātakamācitam |
tadvimuktyāmupāyaṃ me kṛpayā dātumarhati || 19.143{42} ||
iti kuthaḥ sa cāṇḍālaḥ smṛtvā saṃbuddhamādarāt |
paścāttāpābhisaṃtaptastasthau gehe pramohitaḥ || 19.144{43} ||
tataḥ sa cetanaṃ prāpya saṃbuddhasyānubhāvataḥ |
sahasā tata utthāya tadantike upācarat || 19.145{44} ||
tatra sa samupāsṛtya tesāṃ gorakṣiṇaṃ puraḥ |
kṛtāṃjalipuṭo natvā yācitvābhayamabravīt || 19.146{45} ||
bhavanto'tra mayā pāpaṃ kṛtaṃ tatpratidekṣyate |
tadbhavaṃtaḥ samākarṇya kṣaṃtumarhaṃti sarvathā || 19.147{46} ||
asya pratyekabuddhasya hyaparādho na vidyate |
tatra mayātiduṣṭena cauro'yamiti darśitaḥ || 19.148{47} ||
tadatra mucyatāmeṣa pratyekasugato muniḥ |
satkṛtyābhyarcya piṃḍena mānanīyo hi sarvadā || 19.149{48} ||
atraitatpātakaṃ nāsya mamaiva mastake pacet |
tadahameva vadhyo'tra tadayaṃ mucyatāṃ yatiḥ || 19.150{49} ||
iti tenoditaṃ śrutvā sarve gorakṣiṇo'pi te |
ruṣṭāstaṃ sahasā vaddhvā paryyākhyāyātyatāḍayat || 19.151{50} ||
tattāḍanaravaṃ śrutvā sa pratyekajinaḥ sudhīḥ |
gopālāṃstān samāmaṃtrya punarevaṃ samādiśat || 19.152{51} ||
bhavanto mucyatāmeṣa māparādhe'pi hanyatām |
kṣamā hi paramaṃ dharmaṃ tatkurvantu kṣamāmiha || 19.153{52} ||
tantāsādyāgano yogaṃ kartumarhanti sarvathā |
kiṃ daivaṃ nihataṃ hatvā yuṣmākaṃ setsyate phalam || 19.154{53} ||
mayaitadvaṃdhanaṃ prāptaṃ mamaiva daivayogataḥ |
tadasyāpyaparādho'tra nāstītyeṣa vimucyatām || 19.155{54} ||
iti pratyekabuddhena tenādiṣṭaṃ niśamyate |
tatheti pratibuddhitvā tatkopaṃ mumucustadā || 19.156{55} ||
atha te gobhṛtaḥ sarve śrutvā tasya munervacaḥ |
sahasā vinatiṃ kṛtvā vaṃdhanāttaṃ vyamuṃcayat || 19.157{56} ||
tataste gobhṛtaḥ sarve taṃ pratyekamuniṃ mudā |
cīvareṇa samāchādya samabhyarcyātyatoṣayan || 19.158{57} ||
tatra taṃ samupāśritya kṛtāṃjalipuṭo natāḥ |
tadaparādhasaṃtaptāḥ prārthayannevamādarāt || 19.159{58} ||
bhagavañjñāyate'smābhirbhavato nāparādhatā |
tenāsmākaṃ kṣamāṃ kartumarhatyatra bhavānapi || 19.160{59} ||
iti taiḥ prārthitaṃ śrutvā sa pratyekajinaḥ |
tān sarvān gobhṛto dṛṣṭvā samāśvāsyaivamabravīt || 19.161{60} ||
bhavanto mā viṣīdantu dūṣaṇaṃ vā na vidyate |
etadvṛttiṃ vijānāmi saugatā hi bhavārthavit || 19.162{61} ||
(rm 245)
ityādiśya sa sarvajñaḥ pratyekabuddha utthitaḥ |
tata ākāśa utplutya svāśramaṃ gate āśrayan || 19.163{62} ||
taddṛṣṭvā te janāḥ sarve gopālāḥ prativismitāḥ |
tameva sugataṃ smṛtvā saddharme sarvadācaran || 19.164{63} ||
yo'sau kuthābhidho goghnaḥ praduṣṭo'haṃ tadābhavat |
tatpāpairnarakeṣvevaṃ ciraṃ duḥkhāni bhuktavān || 19.165{64} ||
asmiñjanmanyapi prāptakleśadvādaśavārṣikam |
tadasyātrāparādhaṃ na mamaitaddaivayogataḥ || 19.166{65} ||
iti mahārāja śubhāśubhaṃ svadaivataḥ |
saṃsāre'tra sadā puṇyaṃ karttavyaṃ satsukhārthibhiḥ || 19.167{66} ||
puṇyamevātra saṃsāre sārasarvārthasiddhidam |
puṇyaṃ vinā na siddhyante kṛtakarmāṇi sarvathā || 19.168{67} ||
puṇyena jāyate svarge puṇyena yānti sadgatim |
puṇyena sarvadā bhadraṃ kṛtvā yāṃti jinālayam || 19.169{68} ||
daṃḍena mucyate vadhyaḥ puṇyasyaivānubhāvataḥ |
daṃḍārho'pi prahāreṇa prahārārho'pi roṣataḥ || 19.170{69} ||
roṣārhā romaharṣanena mucyate puṇyato nṛpa |
yāvantaḥ sūkhinaḥ sarvasaṃpatsamanvitāḥ || 19.171{70} ||
sarve te puṇyakarttāra iti jñeyā narādhipa |
puṇyena śuddhyate cittaṃ śuddhāśayo bhavetsudhīḥ || 19.172{71} ||
subuddhiḥ sarvadā nityaṃ śubheṣveva samācaret |
śubhācāro viśuddhāṃgaḥ satyavādī jitendriyaḥ || 19.173{72} ||
pariśuddhāśayo dhīro bhavetsatvahitārthabhṛt |
tathaitatpuṇyayuktātmā sarvavidyākalārthavit || 19.174{73} ||
bhaveddharmārthavijñaśca suguṇaśrīsamanvitaḥ |
tato māheśvarīsaṃpatprāpto lokahitodyataḥ || 19.175{74} ||
dānaṃ kṛtvā sadā saukhyaṃ bhuktyā nityaṃ śubhe caret |
etatpuṇyavipākena pariśuddhastrimaṇḍalaḥ || 19.176{75} ||
śubhraśīlo viśuddhātmā sadāryavratamācaret |
etatpuṇyānubhāvena kṣantidharmārthasādhakaḥ || 19.177{76} ||
svaparātmasamābhāvī caretmaitrīsukhānvitaḥ |
etatpuṇyavipākena dharmotsāhaguṇānvitaḥ || 19.178{77} ||
sarvaduṣṭagaṇāñjitvā caretsatvahite kṛtī |
etatpuṇyaprabhāvaiśca sarvakleśān vinirjayan |
samādhidhāraṇīvijñaścareddhyānaṃ samāhitaḥ || 19.179{78} ||
etatpuṇyavipākaiśca sarvaśāstrābdhipāragaḥ |
prajñāratnaṃ samāsādya mahābhijñapadaṃ labhet || 19.180{79} ||
etatpuṇyānubhāvaiścia sarvopāyavidhānavit |
sarvasatvahitaṃ kṛtvā śubhe nityaṃ samācaret || 19.181{80} ||
etatpuṇyānubhāvaiśca bodhipraṇidhimānasaḥ |
sarveṣāṃ maṃgalaṃ kṛtvā bhadracaryāṃ samācaret || 19.182{81} ||
etatpuṇyaprabhāvaiśca daśabalasamanvitaḥ |
sarvamārān vinirjitya caredbodhivrataṃ sadā || 19.183{82} ||
etatpuṇyabalenaiva saṃbodhijñānamuttamam |
(rm 246)
mahāratnaṃ samāsādya bhavetsarvavināyakaḥ || 19.184{83} ||
tataḥ sarvatra lokeṣu kṛtvā bhadrāṇi sarvadā |
bodhisatvo mahāsatvaḥ saṃbuddhapadamāpnuyāt || 19.185{84} ||
etatpuṇyānubhāvaiśca sarvāṃstraidhātukāsthitān |
satvān bodhau pratisthāpya kṛtvā dharmamayaṃ jagat || 19.186{85} ||
etatpuṇyavipākena dharmarājastathāgataḥ |
nirvṛta ālayalīno mahābuddho bhaveddhruvam || 19.187{86} ||
evaṃ matvā mahārāja nirvṛtipadalabdhaye |
yadīchasi tathā puṇyameva karttavyamābhavaiḥ || 19.188{87} ||
puṇyameva mahāratnaṃ sarvatrāpi mahānugam |
sarvārthasiddhidaṃ bhadraṃ saṃbuddhapadasādhanam || 19.189{88} ||
iti vijñāya rājendra hitvā pāpānurāgatām |
puṇyānyevātra saṃsāre karttavyāni hi sarvadā || 19.190{89} ||
pāpena sarvadāpyevaṃ durgatīṣveva saṃgatāḥ |
mūḍhā kleśāgnisaṃtaptā bhramanti bhavacārake || 19.191{90} ||
yāvaṃto duḥkhito duṣṭā saṃsāre kleśatāpitāḥ |
sarve te pāpakarttāra iti buddhaiḥ prakathyate || 19.192{91} ||
iti matvā mahārāja sadā bhadraṃ yadīchasi |
hitvā pāpānurāgatvaṃ saddharmaṃ cinu sādaram || 19.193{92} ||
saddharmaṃ saugataṃ dharmaṃ yallokaṃ hitasādhanam |
tenātra sarvadharmāṇāṃ pravaramagramucyate || 19.194{93} ||
tadetaddharmasaṃprāptyai triratnaśaraṇaṃ gataḥ |
satkṛtya śraddhayā nityaṃ bhajasva samupasthitaḥ || 19.195{94} ||
triratne yatkṛtaṃ karmaṃ śubhaṃ vāpyaśubhaṃ tathā |
tatphalamaprameyamasaṃkhyeya mahattaram || 19.196{95} ||
etatpuṇyānubhāvena sarve buddhā munīśvarāḥ |
jagaddharmamayaṃ kṛtvā niḥkleśā parinirvṛtāḥ || 19.197{96} ||
evaṃ vijñāya bhūmīndra hitvā pāparatiṃ sadā |
triratnabhajanaṃ kṛtvā cara nityaṃ sadā śubhe || 19.198{97} ||
abhuktaṃ kṣīyate naiva kvāpi karma śubhāśubham |
yenaiva yatkṛtaṃ karmaṃ tenaiva bhujyate phalam || 19.199{98} ||
nāgnibhirdahyate karmaṃ dahyate deha eva hi |
nodakaiḥ klidyate karmaṃ klidyate tanureva ca || 19.200{99} ||
nānilaiḥ śuṣyate karmaṃ kāya evābhiśuṣyate |
na kṣītau kṣīyate karmaṃ kṣīyate tanureva hi || 19.201{100} ||
anyathāpi bhavennaiva vipāke karmaṇaḥ phalam |
yathaiva yatkṛtaṃ yena tenaiva bhujyate tathā || 19.202{1} ||
kṛṣṇasya karmaṇaḥ pāke duḥkhataiva sadā bhave |
śubhasya sukhatā nityaṃ miśritasyobhayaṃ tathā || 19.203{2} ||
evaṃ karmavipākotthaṃ śubhāśubhaphalaṃ sadā |
bhuktvā sarvatra saṃsāre bhramanti sarvajaṃtavaḥ || 19.204{3} ||
evaṃ matvā mahārāja kṛṣṇāni miśritāni ca |
vihāyātra śobheṣveva caritavyaṃ sadā bhave || 19.205{4} ||
iti tenārhatādiṣṭaṃ śrutvānaṃdaḥ sa bhūpatiḥ |
(rm 247)
tatheti prativijñapya sajanaḥ prābhya naṃdataḥ || 19.206{5} ||
tataḥ sa nṛpatirbhūyo raivataṃ taṃ mahāmatim |
sucīvaraiḥ samāchādya sāṃjaliḥ praṇatiṃ vyadhāt || 19.207{6} ||
punarutthāya bhūpālastamarhantaṃ kṛtāṃjaliḥ |
praṇatvā vinayaṃ kurvan kramācca prārthayattathā || 19.208{7} ||
bhadantātra prasīdasva kṣamasva me'parādhatām |
sarvadā darśanaṃ datvānugrahaṃ kartumarhasi || 19.209{8} ||
ityukte'nena bhūpena raivataḥ sa prasāditaḥ |
haṃsarāja ivāplutya bhāsayan khe samāyayau || 19.210{9} ||
tatrākāśe samāsīnaḥ prātihāryaṃ pradarśayan |
lokaiḥ saṃdṛśyamāno'sau dhyātvā tasthau samujvalan || 19.211{10} ||
tatastairbhikṣubhiḥ śiṣyaiḥ samanvitaḥ prabhāsayan |
tathākāśāścaraṃ gatvā svāśrame samupāśrayan || 19.212{11} ||
taddṛṣṭvā sa nṛpo lokaiḥ sahātivismayānvitaḥ |
triratnabhajanaṃ kurvan pracacāra sadā mudā || 19.213{12} ||
evaṃ hyadoṣena viśeṣayuktyā pratyakṣaṇakṣyīkṛtalakṣaṇena |
alaṃkṛtaḥ sādhujanaḥ khalena nighnīkṛto'pi na vikārameti || 19.214{13} ||
evaṃ svadaivayogena jāyate hyaparādhatā |
tannānyasyāparādhatvaṃ vaktavyaṃ kena citkva cit || 19.215{14} ||
iti me guruṇākhyātaṃ tathātra kathyate mayā |
iti matvā mahārāja sadā dharme samācara || 19.216{15} ||
prajāścāpi tathā rājan bodhayitvā prayatnataḥ |
bodhimārge pratiṣṭhāpya pālayasva sadādarāt || 19.217{16} ||
tathā te maṃgalaṃ nityaṃ sarvaṃ cāpi bhavetsadā |
kramādbodhiṃ samāsādya saṃbuddhapadamāpnuyāḥ || 19.218{17} ||
iti satyaṃ parijñāya triratnaśaraṇaṃ gataḥ |
satkṛtya śraddhayā nityaṃ bhajasvātra samāhitaḥ || 19.219{18} ||
iti tenārhatādiṣṭaṃ śrutvāśokaḥ sa bhūpatiḥ |
tathā hīti pratijñapya prābhyanandatsapārṣadaḥ || 19.220{19} ||
satkṛtyedaṃ narā ye kalimatidaraṇaṃ raivatasyāvadanaṃ śṛṇvanti śrāvayanti pramuditamanaso bhaktiśraddhāprasannāḥ |
te sarve'pyevamatra vimalasumanasaḥ sarvadā satsukhāni bhuktvā kṛtvā subhadraṃ jinavaranilaye saṃprayānti pramodām || 19.221{20} ||

++ iti ratnāvadānatatve raivatāvadānaṃ samāptam ++


(rm 248)
xx mahiṣāvadāna
atha sa pārthivo'sokaḥ kṛtāñjalipuṭo mudā |
upaguptaṃ guruṃ natvā punarevamabhāṣata || 20.1{1} ||
bhadanta śrotumichāmi punaranyatsubhāṣitam |
tadyathā guruṇākhyātaṃ tathādeṣṭuṃ ca me'rhati || 20.2{2} ||
iti saṃprārthitaṃ rājñā śrutvā so'rhanmahāmatiḥ |
upagupto nareṃdraṃ taṃ samālokyaivamādiśat || 20.3{3} ||
sādhu śṛṇu mahārāja yathā me guruṇoditam |
tathāhaṃ te pravakṣyāmi sarve lokaprabodhane || 20.4{4} ||
tadyathā bhagavān buddhaḥ śākyasiṃho munīśvaraḥ |
sarvajñaḥ sugataḥ śāstā dharmarājo vināyakaḥ || 20.5{5} ||
ṣaḍabhijño jagannātho mārajittu tathāgataḥ |
puraikasamaye sārddhaṃ bhikṣubhiḥ śrāvakaiḥ saha || 20.6{6} ||
cailakairbhikṣuṇībhiścaivamupāsikāgaṇairapi |
upāsākaistathānyaiśca saddharmaguṇavāṃchibhiḥ || 20.7{7} ||
bodhisatvairmahāsatvairbrahmendrādisurādhipaiḥ |
sarve lokādhipaiścāpi dānavendraiḥ śubhārthibhiḥ || 20.8{8} ||
nāgendrairgaruḍendraiśca yakṣagaṃdharvakinnaraiḥ |
grahai vidyādharaiścāpi tathānyaiśca vṛṣārthibhiḥ || 20.9{9} ||
stūya mānārcyamānaśca vaṃdyamāno'bhinaṃditaḥ |
sarvasatvahitārthena janapadeṣu sarvataḥ || 20.10{10} ||
saddharmaṃ samupādiśya pracacāra sasāṃghikaḥ |
tathā ca kośale rāṣṭre varaṃ dharmamupādiśat || 20.11{11} ||
tato'nyatrāpi saddharmamupadeṣṭuṃ tato'carat |
tatrānyasmin vane prāpto bhagavān sa sasāṃghikaḥ || 20.12{12} ||
sarvatra maṃgalaṃ kṛtvā pracacāra śanaiḥ kramāt |
tatrārāṇye mahān yūtho mahiṣīṇāṃ samācarat || 20.13{13} ||
tatraiko mahiṣaścaṇḍo valavīryasamanvitaḥ |
mahāśṛṃgo mahākāyaḥ pratyavasanmadotkaṭaḥ || 20.14{14} ||
tatrāsan paṃcamātrāṇi mahiṣapālaśatāni ca |
sarve tanmahiṣīyūthamanurakṣyādhyuvāsata || 20.15{15} ||
tadaikasmin dine tatra bhagavān sa sasaṃghikaḥ |
pracaraṃstatpradeśe taṃ mahiṣīyuthamaikṣata || 20.16{16} ||
tadā te mahiṣīpālā taṃ munīṃdraṃ sasaṃghikam |
samupāyātamālokya dūrādevamaghoṣayan || 20.17{17} ||
bhagavānmātra samāyāhi pathānyena vrajādhunā |
astyatra mahiṣo duṣṭastatsahasā carānyata || 20.18{18} ||
(rm 249)
iti taiḥ śabditaṃ śrutvā bhagavān sa munīśvaraḥ |
sasaṃghaḥ samupāśritya tāṃ dṛṣṭvaivamahhāṣata || 20.19{19} ||
alpotsukābhavatvatra bhagavantaḥ kiṃ kuto bhayam |
kālajño'haṃ jinendro'smi tadarthe'tra samācare || 20.20{20} ||
tadā sa mahiṣo duṣṭastaṃ munīndraṃ sasāṃghikam |
dūrāddṛṣṭvā samutthāya pradudrāva tadantike || 20.21{21} ||
tatra tadgaṃdhamāghrāya dṛṣṭvā taṃ raktacīvaram |
ruṣā lāṃgūlamunnāmya saṃmukhaḥ prābhyadhāvata || 20.22{22} ||
tatra tena jinendreṇa pañcasiṃhā mahoddhatāḥ |
abhinirmāya tatpṛṣṭhe sthāpitā bhīmarūpiṇaḥ || 20.23{23} ||
tathā nirmāya tatrāgniskaṃdhau dvāvatiprojvalau |
ubhayoḥ pārśvayostasya sthāpito bhīmanisvanau || 20.24{24} ||
tathātra kūṭasaṃkāśo nirmāya mahatī śilā |
upariṣṭātpratiṣṭhāpya darśitā tasya bhītaye || 20.25{25} ||
tatra sa mahiṣo'drākṣīttān siṃhān pṛṣṭhasaṃsthitān |
pārśvayorubhayoragniskaṃdhauruparitāṃ śilām || 20.26{26} ||
evaṃ sa mahiṣo duṣṭo mahābhayamupasthitam |
samantataḥ samālokya pravikheṭābhimohitaḥ || 20.27{27} ||
tatra sa trasito dṛṣṭvā parāyituṃ caturdiśaḥ |
samantato bhayādvignastasthau trāṇanirāśrayaḥ || 20.28{28} ||
tataḥ sa cakitastasya munīndrasyābhisaṃmukham |
upetya caraṇau natvā śaraṇaṃ samupāyayau || 20.29{29} ||
tatra tadbhayabhinnāsyaḥ parikhinnāśayo rudan |
bhagavaṃtaṃ tamālokya tasthau'trāṇāśrayo puraḥ || 20.30{30} ||
tadā sa bhagavān dṛṣṭvā mahiṣyaṃ tamupasthitam |
śaraṇāgatamālokya dharmamevamupādiśat || 20.31{31} ||
bhadramukheti saṃskārāḥ sarve'nityā bhave dhruvāḥ |
sarvadharmā anātmanaḥ śāntaṃ nirvāṇameva hi || 20.32{32} ||
iti dharmaravaṃ śrutvā sa tatpuṇyānubhāvataḥ |
pūrvajanmapravṛttāṃtaṃ smṛtvā prarurodānutāpitaḥ || 20.33{33} ||
evaṃ taṃ ruditaṃ dṛṣṭvā bhagavān sa munīśvara |
bodhayituṃ samāśvāsya punargāthe'bhyabhāṣata || 20.34{34} ||
idānīṃ kiṃ kariṣyāmi tiryagyonigatasya te |
akṣaṇapratipannasya kiṃ rodiṣi nirarthakam || 20.35{25} ||
sādhu prasādyatāṃ cittaṃ mayi kāruṇike jine |
tiryagyoniṃ virāgyeha tataḥ svargaṃ gamiṣyasi || 20.36{26} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa śrutvā sa mahiṣastadā |
sarvaṃ smṛtvā purāvṛttaṃ manasaivaṃ vyaciṃtayat || 20.37{27} ||
hā mayā durdhiyājñena tyaktvā saddharmasaṃyamam |
krodhāgninā svayaṃ dagdhā saṃtāpare'pi tāpitā || 20.38{28} ||
yenāhaṃ hā paribhraṣṭaḥ saddharmamaṇinā'dhunā |
viṣarāgāgnisaṃtaptaḥ kṛṣṇāhiriva durmatiḥ || 20.39{29} ||
(rm 250)
tadatra kiṃ kariṣyāmi tiryakjātirihādhunā |
kiṃ mamānena kāyena duḥkhe pāpānusādhinā || 20.40{30} ||
yatra puṇyaṃ sukhaṃ nāsti tatra kiṃ jīvitena me |
varamevādya me mṛtyurna tvevaṃ cirajīvitam || 20.41{31} ||
avaśyaṃ mṛtyumāpnuyāṃ sarveṣāṃ maraṇaṃ dhruvam |
tadatra me śarīre kā snehatā jīvite'pi vā || 20.42{32} ||
dhikkāyaṃ jīvitaṃ cāpi yena puṇyaṃ na sādhyate |
puṇyameva bhave sāraṃ sarvatra satsukhapradam || 20.43{33} ||
puṇyaṃ vinātrā saṃsare kiṃ śrīsaṃpatsukhānvite |
sarvaṃ vihāya gaṃtavyaṃ puṇyāmekaṃ tadānugaḥ || 20.44{34} ||
puṇyameva jaganmitraṃ puṇyameva sadānugaḥ |
puṇyameva mahatsāraṃ puṇyameva hitārthadam || 20.45{35} ||
tasmātpuṇyaṃ prayatnena sādhanīyaṃ bhave sadā |
sarvatra sukhatāhetu puṇyameveti kathyate || 20.46{36} ||
tatpuṇyaratnasaṃprāptyai buddhasya śaraṇaṃ gataḥ |
sarvathā smaraṇaṃ kṛtvā bhajeyeha samāhitaḥ || 20.47{37} ||
ityevaṃ manasā dhyātvā mahiṣaḥ sa prabodhitaḥ |
taṃ buddhaṃ śrīghanaṃ natvānusmṛtvābhajānmudā || 20.48{38} ||
tataḥ sa bhagavānstasya dṛṣṭvāśayaviśuddhatām |
saddharmaṃ samupādiśya sasaṃghaḥ svāśrame yayau || 20.49{39} ||
tatrāśrame samāsīno bhagavān sasasāṃghikaḥ |
sarvasatvahitārthena saṃbodhidharmamādiśat || 20.50{40} ||
taddharmadeśanāṃ śrotuṃ sarve lokāḥ samāgatāḥ |
brahmaśakrādayo devā lokapālāśca dānavāḥ || 20.51{41} ||
grahā vidyādharāḥ siddhā yakṣagaṃdharvakinnarāḥ |
garuḍā nāgarājāśca rākṣasāśca śubhārthinaḥ || 20.52{42} ||
ṛṣayo brāhmaṇāścāpi nṛpā rājakumārakāḥ |
vaiśyācca maṃtriṇo'mātyā gṛhasthāśca mahājanāḥ || 20.53{43} ||
vaṇijaḥ sārthavāhāśca śilpinaḥ paurikā api |
grāmyā jānapadāścāpi tīrthyāḥ kārpaṭikā api || 20.54{44} ||
evamanye'pi lokāśca saddharmaguṇavāṃ chinaḥ |
sarve te samupāgatya tatrāśrame samāviśat || 20.55{45} ||
tatra taṃ śrīghanaṃ dṛṣṭvā bhikṣusaṃghapuraskṛtam |
natvā pradakṣiṇīkṛtvā samānarcuryathākramam || 20.56{46} ||
tataste praṇatiṃ kṛtvā parivṛtya samaṃtataḥ |
tatsaddharmāmṛtaṃ pātumupatasthuḥ samāhitāḥ || 20.57{47} ||
tadā sa bhagavān dṛṣṭvā tān sarvān samupasthitām |
ādimadhyāṃtakalyāṇaṃ saddharmaṃ samupādiśat || 20.58{48} ||
tatsaddharmāmṛtaṃ pītvā sarve te saṃprabodhitāḥ |
dharmaviśeṣamājñāya triratnaṃ sarvadābhajan || 20.59{49} ||
tatra sa mahiṣaḥ pūrvajanmavṛttimanusmaran |
tadā taddehamutsraṣṭuṃ manasaivaṃ vyaciṃtayat || 20.60{50} ||
hā duṣṭena mayā pūrvamācitaṃ ghorapātakam |
(rm 251)
tenāhaṃ mahiṣo duṣṭo bhavāmyatra yathoditam || 20.61{51} ||
tadatra kiṃ prakurvīya paśustatpāpaśodhaṇam |
tattriratnamanusmṛtvā careyāhamupoṣadham || 20.62{52} ||
datvātrāhamimaṃ kāyaṃ satvebhyaḥ śraddhayā'dhunā |
manasā sugataṃ dhyātvā martuṃ tiṣṭheya sāṃpratam || 20.63{53} ||
tadimaṃ dehamutkṛtya tvagmānsarudhirādikam |
asthimajjādi sarvaṃ ca bhujantu prāṇino mudā || 20.64{54} ||
ye ye satvā imaṃ dehaṃ bhujanti surasaṃ mudā |
te te sarve sadā dharmaṃ kṛtvā yāntu surālāyam || 20.65{55} ||
tathaitatpuṇyapākena muktvāsmān pāpakāśrayāt |
āsādya sadgatau janma bhajeyaṃ sugataṃ sadā || 20.66{56} ||
iti dhyātvā viniścitya mahiṣaḥ sa prasannadhīḥ |
tyaktvāhāraṃ tapaśvīva buddhaṃ smṛtvācarattapaḥ || 20.67{57} ||
tathā sa mahiṣo dhyātvā triratnaṃ cābhyanusmaran |
dehaṃ tyaktvā viśuddhātmā surālayaṃ samāyayau || 20.68{58} ||
tatra svarge sa utpanno mahāsukhasamanvitaḥ |
vismitābhiprasannātmā tadaivaṃ samaciṃtayat || 20.69{59} ||
aho hi jāyate saukhyaṃ kutaścyutvā kuhācare |
kasya puṇyavipākena mamaivaṃ jāyate śubham || 20.70{60} ||
iti ciṃtayatastasya mahiṣapūrviṇastathā |
vismayākrāṃtacittasya samutpannābhavanmatiḥ || 20.71{61} ||
aho tiryaggateścyutvā devaloka ihāsare |
saṃbuddhasmṛtipuṇyena devo bhavāmi sāṃpratam || 20.72{62} ||
tadahaṃ tasya munīndrasya draṣṭuṃ gacheya sāṃpratam |
śraddhayā samupāśritya saddharmaṃ śṛṇuyāṃ punaḥ || 20.73{63} ||
iti mahiṣapūrvī sa devaputraḥ pramoditaḥ |
tatra tasya munīndrasya darśane gaṃtumaichata || 20.74{64} ||
tataḥ sa muditaḥ snātvā śuddhadivyāṃvarāvṛtaḥ |
divyagaṃdhānuliptāṃgo divyālaṃkārabhūṣitaḥ || 20.75{65} ||
divyapūjopahārāṇi gṛhītvā devagaṇaiḥ saha |
tasyāṃ rātrau munīndrasya bhāsayannāśrame yayau || 20.76{66} ||
tatra taṃ śrīghanaṃ dṛṣṭvā muditaḥ samupāsaran |
natvā pradakṣiṇīkṛtya samānarcca yathāvidhiḥ || 20.77{67} ||
tatastasya munīndrasya sāṃjaliḥ sa praṇamya ca |
saddharmadeśanāṃ śrotuṃ purataḥ samupāśrayat || 20.78{68} ||
tathā te tatsahāyāśca devaputrāḥ prasāditāḥ |
bhagavaṃtaṃ sāṃjalayaḥ upāśrayan || 20.79{69} ||
tataḥ sa bhagavān dṛṣṭvā sarvānstān samupasthitān |
āryyasatyasamārabhya saddharmaṃ samupādiśat || 20.80{70} ||
tatsaddharmāmṛtaṃ pītvā saha devaiḥ pramoditaḥ |
devo mahiṣapūrvi sa dharmaviśeṣamāptavān || 20.81{71} ||
satkāyaduṣṭibhūmīndhraṃ viṃśatiśikharodgatam |
nirbhidya jñānavajreṇa śrotaāpattimāyayau || 20.82{72} ||
(rm 252)
tātaḥ sa dṛṣṭasatyastaṃ bhagavantaṃ munīśvaram |
kṛtāṃjaliḥ puṭo natvā trirudānamudānayat || 20.83{73} ||
bhagavannidamasmākaṃ kṛtaṃ na janakena hi |
na mātrā na ca rājñāpi nānyena kena citkhalu || 20.84{74} ||
bhavataiva yadasmākaṃ kṛtapāpaviśodhanam |
tenābhiśoṣitāśeṣarudhirāśrusāritpariḥ || 20.85{75} ||
laṃghitā asthiśailāśca pihitā pāpapaddhatiḥ |
vivṛtāḥ svargamārgāśca mokṣamārgaśca darśitaḥ || 20.86{76} ||
mayāptaṃ vimalaṃ cakṣuḥ śāntamāryapadaṃ tathā |
duḥkhārṇavaḥ samuttīrṇo bodhimārgo'pi dṛśyate || 20.87{77} ||
tadbhavatāṃ kṛpādṛṣṭeḥ prasādānmama sāṃpratam |
saṃsārajanma sāphalyaṃ buddhaputrāsmyahaṃ yataḥ || 20.88{78} ||
adyārabhya sadā nityaṃ bhavatāṃ śaraṇaṃ gataḥ |
bodhau cittaṃ samādhāya cariṣyāmi śubhāṃ carim || 20.89{79} ||
tadatrānugrahaṃ kṛtvā bhavāñchāstā jagaguruḥ |
kṛpayā sarvadā paśyannevaṃ māṃ trātumarhati || 20.90{80} ||
iti saṃprārthitaṃ tena suparvaṇā niśamya saḥ |
śāstā tasyāśayaṃ śuddhaṃ samalokyaivamādiśat || 20.91{81} ||
sadho śṛṇu hitaṃ vakṣye sadā bhadraṃ yadīchasi |
triratnaṃ śaraṇaṃ gatvā bhaja nityaṃ samādarāt || 20.92{82} ||
ye buddhaśaraṇaṃ kṛtvā bhajaṃti śraddhayā sadā |
māracaryābhimuktāste saṃprayāṃti jinālayam || 20.93{83} ||
ye dharmaṃ saugataṃ nityaṃ śṛṇvanti śraddhayā mudā |
sarvapāpābhimuktāste saṃprayānti sukhāvatīm || 20.94{84} ||
ye ca saṃghe prakurvanti satkāraṃ śraddhayādarāt |
te sarve durgatermuktvā saṃprayānti surālayam || 20.95{85} ||
triratnaśaraṇaṃ kṛtvā ye bhajanti sadādarāt |
durgatiṃ te na gachaṃti sadā gachaṃti sadgatim || 20.96{86} ||
sadgatau te sadā sthitvā triratnaṃ śaraṇaṃ gatāḥ |
kṛtvā lokahitaṃ saukhyaṃ bhuktvā ramanti sarvadā || 20.97{87} ||
tataḥ pāramitāḥ sarvāḥ paripūrya yathākramam |
trividhāṃ bodhimāsadya nirvṛtiṃ samavāpnuyuḥ || 20.98{88} ||
iti nirvāṇasatsaukhyaḥ prāptuṃ yadi samichasi |
triratnaṃ śaraṇaṃ kṛtvā bhaja nityaṃ samāhitaḥ || 20.99{89} ||
etatpuṇyānubhāvena pariśuddhastrimaṃḍalaḥ |
kramādbodhiṃ samāsādya saṃbuddhapadamāpnuyāḥ || 20.100{90} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa śrutvā sa paribodhitāḥ |
tathetyabhyanumoditvā punarevamabhāṣata || 20.101{91} ||
adya me saphalaṃ saṃpūritaṃ manoratham |
bodhiratnābhisaṃpanno buddhaputro'smi sāṃpratam || 20.102{92} ||
sarvadā bhavatāmeva śaraṇe'haṃ samāsthitaḥ |
triratnabhajanaṃ kurvañcariṣye bodhisaṃvaram || 20.103{93} ||
iti saṃprārthanāṃ kṛtvā devaputraḥ sa moditaḥ |
(rm 253)
śrīghanaṃ taiḥ punarnatvā sasahāyo divaṃ yayau || 20.104{94} ||
tatra svarge sa saṃprāpto devaiḥ saha samāśritaḥ |
triratnasmaraṇaṃ kurvan saukhyaṃ bhuktvā samācarat || 20.105{95} ||
tasyāṃ rātrau mahatkāntiṃ prabhāṃ dṛṣṭvā savismayāḥ |
sarve te mahiṣīpālā vabhūvuḥ śaṃkitāśayāḥ || 20.106{96} ||
tataḥ prātaḥ samutthāya sarve te kautukānvitāḥ |
saṃmīlya sahasā tatra buddhāśrame upācaran || 20.107{97} ||
tatra taṃ śrīghanaṃ dṛṣṭvā sarve te pratimoditāḥ |
kṛtāṃjalipuṭo natvā samupatasthurādarāt || 20.108{98} ||
tadā sa bhagāvān dṛṣṭvā tān sarvān samupasthitān |
ādimadhyāṃtakalyāṇaṃ dideśa dharmamuttamam || 20.109{9!} ||
tatsaddharmāmṛtaṃ pītvā sarve te pratibodhitāḥ |
bhagavaṃtaṃ punarnatvā paprachurevamādarāt || 20.110{100} ||
bhagavannadya rātrau ka ihāśrame prabhāsayan |
bhavatāṃ samupāyātastatsamādeṣṭumarhati || 20.111{1} ||
iti tairmahiṣīpālaiḥ paripṛṣṭe sa sarvavit |
bhagavāṃstān samālokya purāvṛttimupādiśat || 20.112{2} ||
yo'trāsau mahiṣo duṣṭo dṛṣṭvāsmān samupadrutaḥ |
samantato bhayaṃ dṛṣṭvā trasto me śaraṇaṃ gataḥ || 20.113{3} ||
tadā maddharmamākarṇya pūrvajātimanusmaran |
mayi cittaṃ prasādyaiva tyaktvāhārā tyajan tanum || 20.114{4} ||
tato'smatsmṛtipuṇyena pāpamukto viśuddhadhīḥ |
svargaloke samutpanno devo bhavati sāṃpratam || 20.115{5} ||
sa eṣa devaputro'dya niśāyāmatra bhāsayan |
devalokaiḥ sahāyāto darśanāya mamālāye || 20.116{6} ||
matsaddharmaṃ samākarṇya dṛṣṭasatyaḥ sa harṣitaḥ |
devasaṃghaiḥ saha svarge svālaye pragatastathā || 20.117{7} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa śrutvātivismayoddhatāḥ |
sarve te mahiṣīpālā mitha evaṃ samabruvan || 20.118{8} ||
aho citra hi yannāma tiryyagyonigato'pi saḥ |
buddhaṃ sadgurumāsādya dharmaṃ śrutvābhavatsudhīḥ || 20.119{9} ||
sadgurau sugate cittaṃ prasādya viratāśanaḥ |
trirātnasmaraṇaṃ kṛtvā prāṇaṃ tyaktvā divaṃ yayau || 20.120{10} ||
tato'pīha samāgatya saṃbuddhaṃ śaraṇaṃ gataḥ |
abhyarcya dharmamākarṇya dṛṣṭasatyo divaṃ yayau || 20.121{11} ||
svarge'pi tattathā nityaṃ sadguroḥ śaraṇaṃ gataḥ |
śraddhayā bhajanaṃ kurvaṃ chubhe'caratsamāhitaḥ || 20.122{12} ||
dhanyo'yaṃ sugataḥ śāstā sarvasatvaśubhaṃkaraḥ |
yatra cittaṃ prasādyaiva paśurapi divaṃ gataḥ || 20.123{13} ||
vayaṃ tu mānavāḥ sarve kathamenaṃ munīśvaram |
sadguruṃ samupāsādya śraddhayā na bhajemahi || 20.124{14} ||
(rm 254)
tathā saddharmamākarṇya prasādādasya sadguroḥ |
atkṛtya sarvadā bhadraṃ na lābhema kathaṃ vayam || 20.125{15} ||
viśeṣadharmamājñāya dṛṣṭasatyāḥ samāhitāḥ |
triratnabhajanaṃ kṛtvā nūnaṃ gachema sadgatim || 20.126{16} ||
tadatra sadgurorasya munīndrasyādhunā vayam |
sarve'pi śraddhayābhyarcya bhajemahi samādarāt || 20.127{17} ||
tatsāddharmaṃ samākarṇya śraddhayā samupasthitāḥ |
triratnabhajanaṃ kṛtvā caremahi śubhe sadā || 20.128{18} ||
tadādau sugatasyāsya sasaṃghasyā'dhunā vayam |
yathārhabhojanenātra pūjayema prapuṣṭaye || 20.129{18!} ||
iti saṃbhāṣaṇāṃ kṛtvā sarve te pratimoditāḥ |
tatheti saṃmataṃ kṛtvā tatra buddhāśrame yayuḥ || 20.130{19} ||
tatra taṃ śrīghanaṃ dṛṣṭvā sasaṃghaṃ taṃ pramoditāḥ |
sarvasāṃjalayo natvā prārthayannevamādarāt || 20.131{20} ||
bhagavannātha sarvajña bhagavaṃtaṃ sasaṃghikam |
pūjayituṃ samichāmastannatrānugrahaṃ kuru || 20.132{21} ||
iti taiḥ prārthitaṃ śrutvā bhagavān sa munīśvaraḥ |
teṣāṃ puṇyābhisaṃvṛddhyai tūṣṇībhūtvādhyuvāsa tat || 20.133{22} ||
tathādhyuvāsitaṃ matvā sarve te pariharṣitāḥ |
pādau tasya munīndrasya natvā nijālayaṃ yayuḥ || 20.134{23} ||
tatra te muditāḥ sarve dadhikṣīrodanādikam |
yathārhabhojyasāmagriṃ sahasā samasādhayan || 20.135{24} ||
tataste samaye gatvā tatra buddhāśrame mudā |
sasāṃghikaṃ munīndraṃ taṃ praṇatvaivaṃ nyavedayan || 20.136{25} ||
bhagavannātha sarvajña samayo varttate'dhunā |
tatsaṃgha bhagavān pūjāṃ pratigrahitumarhati || 20.137{26} ||
iti taiḥ prārthite sarve bhagavān sasasāṃghikaḥ |
utthāya bhojanakṣetre upācarān prabhāsayan || 20.138{27} ||
tatra pādyaṃ samādāya saṃbuddhapramukhāḥ kramāt |
sarve te sāṃghikāstatra svasvāsane samāśrayan || 20.139{28} ||
tāṃ buddhapramukhān sarvān sāṃghikān svāsanāśritān |
dṛṣṭvā te muditā sarve sāmānarcuryathākramam || 20.140{29} ||
tataste surasairbhojyaiḥ kṣīrodanaiḥ savyaṃjanaiḥ |
praṇītairbhagavaṃtaṃ taṃ sasaṃghaṃ samatoṣayan || 20.141{30} ||
tatastaṃ sugataṃ tṛptaṃ sasāṃghikaṃ samīkṣya te |
tatpātrāṇyapanīyāśu taddhastādi samaśodhayan || 20.142{31} ||
tataḥ sapūgatāmburāmahauṣadharasāyanam |
saṃbuddhapramukhebhyaste saṃghebhyaḥ pradadurmudā || 20.143{32} ||
tataste mahiṣīpālāḥ kṛtāṃjalipūṭā mudā |
śrīghanaṃ taṃ sasaṃghaṃ ca natvā samupatasthire || 20.144{33} ||
tathā tan samupāsīnān sarvān dṛṣṭvā sa sarvavit |
(rm 255)
āryadharmaṃ samādiśya bodhimārge'bhyayojayat || 20.145{34} ||
tatsaddharmāmṛtaṃ pītvā sarve te'bhyanumoditāḥ |
saṃbuddhaśāsane tatra pravrajituṃ samīkṣire || 20.146{35} ||
tatāste mahiṣīpālāḥ sarve kṛtvābhisaṃmatam |
kṛtāṃjalipuṭā natvā prārthayan taṃ munīśvaram || 20.147{36} ||
bhāgavaṃ nātha sarvajña bhavatāṃ śāsane vayam |
pravrajituṃ samichāmastanno'nvāhartumarhati || 20.148{37} ||
iti taiḥ prārthitaṃ śrutvā bhagavān sa munīśvaraḥ |
tān viśuddhāśayān sarvān samālokyaivamādiśat || 20.149{38} ||
mātāpitrorvinānujñāṃ pravrajyāṃ na dadāmyaham |
tadanujñāṃ samādāya preta dāsyāmi vastadā || 20.150{39} ||
ityādiṣṭe munīndreṇa sarve te pratinaṃditāḥ |
taṃ munīndraṃ praṇatvāśu svasvagehaṃ yayurmudā || 20.151{40} ||
tatra te sahasā gatvā samātāpitṛvāṃdhavān |
paribodhya prayatnena prāpyānujñāṃ samācaram || 20.152{41} ||
tatra te sahasā gatvā bhagavata upāsṛtāḥ |
pādau sāṃjalayornatvā prārthayannevamādarāt || 20.153{42} ||
bhagavan sarvavitpitṛprāptānujñāḥ samāgatāḥ |
tatrānugrahamādhāya pravrajyāṃ dātumarhati || 20.154{43} ||
iti taiḥ prārthite śāstā bhagavān savyapāṇinā |
teṣāṃ śirassusaṃspṛṣṭvā samālokyaivamādiśat || 20.155{44} ||
sameta bhikṣavaḥ sarve yūyaṃ śraddhāsamaṃgitāḥ |
pravrajyāvratamādhāya carata brahmacārikām || 20.156{45} ||
ityādiṣṭe munīndreṇa sarve te muṇḍitā vabhuḥ |
khikkhirīpātradharttāraḥ kākhāyacīvarāvṛtāḥ || 20.157{46} ||
tataḥ śastuḥ prasādena sarve te vimalāśayāḥ |
pralabdhabodhisaccittāḥ śuddhaśīlā jiteṃdriyāḥ || 20.158{47} ||
bhitvāvidyāguṇāḥ samyakprāptavidyāviśāradāḥ |
pratisaṃvitsusaṃprāptāḥ satyamārgasamanvitāḥ || 20.159{48} ||
samādhidhāraṇīvidyā ghaṭamānāḥ samāhitāḥ |
kṣamānvitāḥ śubhātmāno yogino bhikṣavo'bhavan || 20.160{49} ||
tataste sarvasaṃsāraṃ matvānityaṃ cālācalam |
dṛṣṭvā ca sarvasaṃskāragatīrahivighātinīḥ || 20.161{50} ||
sarvakleśagaṇān hitvā jitvā māragaṇānapi |
sākṣādarhatvamāsādya pracerurbrahmacārikāḥ || 20.162{51} ||
tataste bhikṣavaḥ sarve pariśuddhatrimaṃḍalāḥ |
vītasaṃgā mahābhijñā nirvikalpā niraṃjanāḥ || 20.163{52} ||
samaloṣṭakasauvarṇāḥ satkāralābhanispṛhāḥ |
akāśasamacittāṅgā bhave lābhaparāṅmukhāḥ || 20.164{53} ||
devāsurādi lokānāṃ traidhātukanivāsinām |
pūjyā mānyāśca vaṃdyāśca babhūvurbodhicāriṇaḥ || 20.165{54} ||
(rm 256)
taddṛṣṭvā bhikṣavaḥ sarve vismayoddhatamānasāḥ |
bhagavaṃtaṃ muniṃ natvā papracbustatpurākṛtam || 20.166{55} ||
bhagavan kāni karmāṇi tena mahiṣapūrviṇā |
kṛtāni devaputreṇa yairasau mahiṣo'bhavat || 20.167{56} ||
ebhiśca mahiṣīpālaiḥ kiṃ karma prakṛtaiḥ purā |
yenārhatvaṃ samāsādya bhavaṃti brahmacāriṇaḥ || 20.168{57} ||
etatsarvaṃ samādiśya bhavāñchāstātra sāṃpratam |
asmākamapi cetāṃsi prabodhayitumarhati || 20.169{58} ||
iti tairbhikṣubhiḥ pṛṣṭe bhagavān sa munīśvaraḥ |
tān sarvān sāṃghikān bhikṣūn samālokyaivamādiśat || 20.170{59} ||
śṛṇuta bhikṣavaḥ sarve tena mahiṣapūrviṇā |
purā kṛtāni karmāṇi tāni vakṣyāmi sāṃpratam || 20.171{60} ||
ebhiśca mahiṣipālaiḥ purā yāni kṛtānyapi |
tāni sarvāṇi karmāṇi vakṣyāmi ca tathā'dhunā || 20.172{61} ||
tadyathābhūtpurā buddhaḥ kāśyapo'rhanstathāgataḥ |
sarvajñaḥ sugataḥ śāstā dharmarājo vināyākaḥ || 20.173{62} ||
bhagavān sa tadā pūryyāṃ varāṇasyā upāśrame |
mṛgadāve samāśritya vijahāra sasāṃghikaḥ || 20.174{63} ||
tadā tasya munīndrasya śāsane tripiṭo yatiḥ |
bhikṣureko mahāvijño niścayārthaviśāradaḥ || 20.175{64} ||
tadaikasamaye bhikṣupaṃcaśataiḥ puraskṛtaḥ |
sa sabhāyāṃ samāsīnaḥ saddharmaṃ samupādiśat || 20.176{65} ||
tasya te bhikṣavaḥ śikṣāḥ śaikṣāśca saṃścayānvitāḥ |
tripiṭārthaṃ parijñātuṃ paprachustaṃ guruṃ muhuḥ || 20.177{66} ||
evaṃ tairbhikṣubhiḥ pṛṣṭe sa guruḥ pratiroṣitaḥ |
tān sarvāñchrāvakāñchiṣyāñchaikṣāṃśca paryabhāṣata || 20.178{67} ||
tataste śrāvakāḥ sarve dṛṣṭvā taṃ ruṣitaṃ gurum |
praṇatvā vinayaṃ kṛtvā punarevamabhāsire || 20.179{68} ||
bhavanta mā kṛthā roṣaṃ prasīdasva kṛpānidhe |
vyākhyātaṃ bhavatā samyaktadasmābhi na budhyate || 20.180{69} ||
tadbhūyo'pi bhavānetadarthaṃ samyaksuvistaram |
asmaccetaṃ prabodhārthaṃ samupādeṣṭumarhati || 20.181{70} ||
iti taiḥ prārthite bhūyastripiṭaḥ so'tiroṣitaḥ |
tathā tāñchrāvakān sarvān paribhāṣyaivamabravīt || 20.182{71} ||
are re mahiṣā yūyaṃ tatkiṃ jānītha madvacaḥ |
tadbhūyaḥ kiṃ pravakṣyāmi yacchrutvāpi na budhyate || 20.183{72} ||
iti tenoditaṃ śrutvā sarve te pariroṣitāḥ |
taṃ guruṃ yatimarhantamapi pratyavadastathā || 20.184{73} ||
yadvayaṃ mahiṣāḥ sarve tadbhavānmahiṣādhipaḥ |
yadasmākaṃ yathā śāsta tathā bhavatu pālakaḥ || 20.185{74} ||
iti taiḥ śrāvakaiḥ sarvaiḥ śikṣaiḥ śaikṣaṃ ca roṣitaiḥ |
sadgurorantike kopādyatkhalaṃ pratibhāṣitam || 20.186{75} ||
etatpāpābhiśaṃkārttāḥ paścāttāpābhitāpinaḥ |
(rm 257)
tamarhantaṃ guruṃ natvā punarevaṃ vabhāṣire || 20.187{76} ||
aho mūḍhā vayaṃ sarve yatkopātsadgurorapi |
pratyākhyāya vayo'smābhiḥ pratyuttaraṃ pradīyate || 20.188{77} ||
tadetatpātakaṃ ghoraṃ kathaṃ vayaṃ śahemahi |
tadbhavān kṛpayāsmākaṃ kṣaṃtumarhati sarvathā || 20.189{78} ||
tathā janmāṃtare cāpi bhavantameva sadgurum |
kalyāṇamitramāsādya prāpnuyāma susaṃvaram || 20.190{79} ||
iti saṃprārthanāṃ kṛtvā sarve te bhikṣavastathā |
tasya gurorupāśritya dharmaṃ śrutvābhajan sadā || 20.191{80} ||
tataste samaye sarve śrāvakāstripiṭo'pi saḥ |
kālaṃ gatāḥ punarjanma lehire durgatau tataḥ || 20.192{81} ||
khalavākkarmapākena paṃcajanmaśatānyapi |
sa tripiṭapatiḥ śāstā mahiṣo'yaṃ sabhābhavat || 20.193{82} ||
sarve te śrāvakāścāpi yathā vākkarmadoṣataḥ |
tatheme mahiṣīpalā vabhūvuḥ sarvadā bhave || 20.194{83} ||
atrāpi mahiṣo duṣṭastripiṭo'sau bhavatyapi |
ime'pi mahiṣīpālā bhavanti bhikṣavo'pi te || 20.195{84} ||
yaccāyaṃ mahiṣo'pyatra kāśyapakṛtapuṇyataḥ |
dṛṣṭvā māṃ suprasannātmā natvā me śaraṇaṃ gataḥ || 20.196{85} ||
etatpuṇyavipākena devaputro bhavatyayam |
āryasatyaṃ samāsādya dṛṣṭasatyo sudhīrapi || 20.197{86} ||
ime'pi mahiṣīpālāḥ kāśyapapuṇyapākātaḥ |
pravrajyārhatvamāsādya bhavanti brahmacāriṇaḥ || 20.198{87} ||
evaṃ karmavipākairhi sarve'pi jantavo bhave |
sukhaduḥkhāni bhuṃjānā bhramanti bhavacārake || 20.199{88} ||
nāgnibhirdahyate karmaṃ vāyubhirvā na śuṣyate |
klidyate nodakaiścāpi kṣīyate nāpi bhūmiṣu || 20.200{89} ||
abhuktaṃ kṣīyate naiva karmaṃ kvāpi kathaṃ cana |
sarvatrāpi bhavennaiva karmaṇo gatiranyathā || 20.201{90} ||
śubhasya karmaṇaḥ pāke śubhataiva sadā bhave |
tathā kṛṣṇasya pāke ca duḥkhataiva sadā bhave || 20.202{91} ||
miśritānāṃ tathā pāke miśritāni phalānyapi |
evaṃ matvā śubheṣveva caritavyaṃ sadā bhave || 20.203{92} ||
yenaiva yatkṛtaṃ karmaṃ tenaiva bhujyate phalam |
avaśyaṃ pariboktavyaṃ saṃsāre karmaṇaḥ phalam || 20.204{93} ||
na nasyaṃti hi karmāṇi kalpakoṭiśatairapi |
sāmagrīṃ prāpya kālaṃ ca phalanti prāṇināṃ khalu || 20.205{94} ||
iti matvātra saṃsāre triratnaṃ śaraṇaṃ gatāḥ |
satkṛtya śraddhayā nityaṃ bhajadhvaṃ sarvathādarāt || 20.206{95} ||
ye bhajaṃti sadā nityaṃ triratnaṃ śaraṇaṃ gatāḥ |
durgatiṃ te na gachaṃti yāntyeva sadgatiṃ sadā || 20.207{96} ||
ityādiṣṭaiḥ munīndreṇa śrutvā te saṃghikā mudā |
(rm 258)
sarve lokāśca satkṛtya triratnaṃ sarvadābhajam || 20.208{97} ||
etanme guruṇākhyātaṃ tathā mayocyate'dhunā |
tvayāpyevaṃ mahārāja caritavyaṃ sadā śubhe || 20.209{98} ||
prajāścāpi sadā dhārmaṃ śrāvāyitvā prabodhayan |
bodhimārge pratisthāpya pālayasva samāhitaḥ || 20.210{99} ||
tathā te maṃgalaṃ nityaṃ sarvatrāpi bhaveddhruvam |
kramādbodhiṃ samāsādya saṃbuddhapadamāpnuyāḥ || 20.211{100} ||
iti tenārhatādiṣṭaṃ śrutvāśokaḥ sa bhūpatiḥ |
tatheti'bhyanumoditvā prābhyanandatsapārṣadaḥ || 20.212{1} ||
idaṃ narā ye mahiṣāvādānaṃ śṛṇvaṃti ye capi niśāmayaṃti sukhāni bhuktvā khalu te'tra sarve samyāṃti nūnaṃ sugatālayaṃ te || 20.213{2} ||

++ ityavadānatatve mahiṣāvadānaṃ samāptam ++


(rm 259)
xxi nāvikāvadāna
athāśoko mahinātha upaguptaṃ yatiṃ gurum |
kṛtāṃjalipuṭo natvā punarevamabhāṣata || 21.1{1} ||
bhadaṃta śrotumichāmi punaranyatsubhāṣitam |
tadyathā guruṇākhyātaṃ tathādeṣṭuṃ ca me'rhati || 21.2{2} ||
saṃprārthite rājñā śrutvā so'rhan sudhīryatiḥ |
upagupto nareṃdraṃ taṃ samālokyaiyamabravīt || 21.3{3} ||
sādhu śṛṇu mahārāja yathā me guruṇoditam |
tathāhaṃ te pravakṣyāmi śrutvānumodanāṃ kuru || 21.4{4} ||
puraikasamaye buddho bhagavān sa munīśvaraḥ |
sarvajñaḥ sugataḥ śastā dharmarājo vināyakaḥ || 21.5{5} ||
sasaṃgho hāsayan tatra magadhe cārikāṃ caran |
sarvatra bhadratāṃ kṛtvā gaṃgātīramupāśrayan || 21.6{6} ||
tatra sa bhagavān sarvai bhikṣusaṃghaiḥ puraskṛtaḥ |
sabhāmadhyāsanāsīno dharmamādeṣṭumaichata || 21.7{7} ||
tatsaddharmāmṛtaṃ pātuṃ sarvalokāstadāgatāḥ |
brahmaśakrādayo devā lokapālā gaṇādayaḥ || 21.8{8} ||
daityeṃdro garuḍā nāgā yakṣagaṃdharvakiṃnarāḥ |
siddhā vidyādharāścāpi rākṣasāśca maharddhikāḥ || 21.9{9} ||
ṛṣayo brāhmaṇāścāpi yogino yatayo'pi ca |
rājānaḥ kṣatriyā bhūpā rājaputrāśca tīrthikaḥ || 21.10{10} ||
vaiśyāścā maṃtriṇo'mātyāḥ śreṣṭhinaḥ paurikāḥ prajāḥ |
gṛhasthāḥ sādhavo bhadrāḥ sārthavāhamahājanāḥ || 21.11{11} ||
vaṇijāḥ śilpinaścāpi grāmyā jānapadā api |
tathānye'pi samāyātāḥ saddharmaśravaṇārthinaḥ || 21.12{12} ||
tatra te samupāyātā dṛṣṭvā taṃ śrīghanaṃ mudā |
natvā pradakṣiṇīkṛtya samānarcuryathākramam || 21.13{13} ||
tataḥ sarve'pi te lokāḥ parivṛtya samaṃtataḥ |
samāhitāḥ samudvīkṣya dharmaṃ śrotuṃ samāśrayan || 21.14{14} ||
tadā sa bhagavān dṛṣṭvā sarvānstān samupasthitān |
bodhicaryāṃ samārabhya saddharmaṃ samupādiśat || 21.15{15} ||
tatsaddharmāmṛtaṃ pītvā sarve lokāḥ pramoditāḥ |
dharmāviśeṣamājñāya triratnaṃ prābhajan sadā || 21.16{16} ||
tataḥ sa bhagavān dṛṣṭvā sarvāllokāñchubhāśritān |
tathānyatra śubhaṃ kartuṃ gaṃgātīramupācarat || 21.17{17} ||
sasaṃgho bhagāvaṃstatra gaṃgātīra upāsṛtaḥ |
navikān samupāhūya samālokyaivamabravīt || 21.18{18} ||
(rm 260)
nāvikā gantumichāmi gaṃgāpāramito'dhunā |
tanmāmimāṃ nadīṃ gaṃgāṃ samuttārayata drutam || 21.19{19} ||
iti tatprārthitaṃ śrutvā sarve te nāvikā api || 21.20{20} ||
sasāṃghikaṃ munīndraṃ tananādṛtyaivamabruvan |
tarapaṇyaṃ vināsmābhiḥ kaści diśā na tāritaḥ || 21.21{21} ||
yadīchati bhavānstarttuṃ tarapaṇyaṃ prayachatu |
iti tairbhāṣitaṃ śrutvā bhagavān sa munīśvaraḥ || 21.22{22} ||
tān sarvānnāvikān dṛṣṭvā punarevamabhāṣata |
ahamapi bhavaṃto'tra nāviko'smi ti manyatām || 21.23{23} ||
mayā hi tārito naṃdo mahārāganadīkṛtaḥ purā |
dveṣābdhipatito duṣṭo'ṅgulimālo'pi tāritaḥ || 21.24{24} ||
mānavo mānakūpārapatitastārito mayā |
mohodadhinimagnaścoruvilvīkāśyapastathā || 21.25{25} ||
evamanye'pi lokāśca kleśābdhe tāritā mayā |
kasya cidapi satvasya tarapaṇyaṃ na kiṃ cana || 21.26{26} ||
gṛhyate yācyate nāpi mayā kvāpi kadā cana |
tathā yūyamapīhāsmān sarvān bhikṣūnnimāṃdakī || 21.27{27} ||
agāḍhāṃ sahasā tārya pāre nayatumarhatha |
evaṃ tena munīndreṇa prārthite'pi na ke cana || 21.28{28} ||
nāvikāste munīndraṃ taṃ tārayituṃ samīchire |
atra kaścitsudhīḥ sādhurnāvikastaṃ munīśvaram || 21.29{29} ||
mahābhijñaṃ parijñāya natvovāca kṛtāṃjaliḥ |
bhagavannātha sarvajña bhavantaṃ sarvasāṃghikān || 21.30{30} ||
apyahaṃ tārayiṣyāmi tatprasīdatu me sadā |
iti tenoditaṃ śrutvā bhagavān sa munīśvaraḥ || 21.31{31} ||
sarvānstān sāṃghikān bhikṣūn samālokyaivamādiśat |
bhikṣavo nāvamāruhya yūyaṃ sarve samāhitāḥ || 21.32{32} ||
imāṃ gaṃgāṃ samuttīrya tiṣṭhata tīramāśritāḥ |
ityādiṣṭe munīndreṇa sarve te bhikṣusaṃghikāḥ || 21.33{33} ||
sahasā nāvamāruhya saṃniṣeduḥ samāhitāḥ |
tadā sa nāviko dṛṣṭvā sarvānnaukāsamāśritān || 21.34{34} ||
tāṃ nāvaṃ sahasā gaṃgāmadhye saṃprāpayan kramāt |
tadā sa bhagavānnṛddhyā vihāyasā svayaṃ caran || 21.35{35} ||
tadagrataḥ samuttīrya tīrabhūmau samāśrayan |
taddṛṣṭvā nāvikā sarve te salokāḥ savismayāḥ |
paścāttāpāgnisaṃtaptāḥ paśyanta eva tasthire || 21.36{36} ||
so'pi ca nāvīko dṛṣṭvā taṃ munīndraṃ vihāyasā |
sahasā tīramāsadya sthitaṃ paśyannyaṣīdat || 21.37{37} ||
tataḥ sā niścarā naukā stambhitaiva jalāśritā |
taddṛṣṭvā nāvikaḥ so'tivismayākulato'bhavat || 21.38{38} ||
tatastaiḥ śrīghanaṃ tīrasamāsīnaṃ sa nāvikaḥ |
(rm 261)
dṛṣṭvā tatsmṛtimādhāya praṇanāma kṛtāṃjaliḥ || 21.39{39} ||
tataḥ sāsaṃcarā naukā vāyusaṃpreritā drutam |
sahasā tīramāsādya tasthau tatra suniścarā || 21.40{40} ||
tataste bhikṣavaḥ sarve samuttīrya vahitrataḥ |
sahasā tīramāgatya saṃbuddhāntikamāyayuḥ || 21.41{41} ||
tatra sa bhagavān dṛṣṭvā sarvāṃstān samupāgatān |
saddharmaṃ samupādeṣṭuṃ tasthau sabhāsanāśritaḥ || 21.42{42} ||
tataḥ sa nāvikaścāpi dṛṣṭvā taṃ śrīghanaṃ munim |
bhikṣusaṃghasabhāsīnaṃ praṇatuṃ samupācaran || 21.43{43} ||
tatra sa sāñjalirnatvā sarvān tan sāṃghikān kramāt |
pādāmbujau munīndrasya praṇanāma pramoditaḥ || 21.44{44} ||
tatra te nāvikāścāpi sarve te vismayānvitāḥ |
taddharmadeśanāṃ śrotuṃ muditāḥ samupācaran || 21.45{45} ||
tatra taṃ śrīghanaṃ dṛṣṭvā sasaṃghaṃ saṃpramoditāḥ |
natvā pradakṣiṇīkṛtya samupatasthurādarāt || 21.46{46} ||
tataḥ sa bhagavān dṛṣṭvā sarvānstān samupasthitān |
āryyasatyasamārabhya saddharmaṃ samupādiśat || 21.47{47} ||
tataddharmāmṛtaṃ pītvā nāvikaḥ saṃpramoditaḥ |
vijñāya dharmavaiśeṣyaṃ saddharmābhirato bhavet || 21.48{48} ||
tataḥ satkāyabhūmīndhraṃ viṃśatiśikharodgatam |
nirbhidya jñānavajreṇa dharmadṛṣṭi samāptavān || 21.49{49} ||
tataḥ sa śrotaāpattiphalaprāptaḥ prasannadhīḥ |
taṃ munīndraṃ samālokya trirudānamudānayat || 21.50{50} ||
bhagavan yatmayā prāptaṃ dharmacakṣuḥ sunirmalam |
śāntaṃ padamāryakāntaṃ tīrṇaścāpi bhavārṇavaḥ || 21.51{51} ||
satkāyadṛṣṭiśailāśca laṃghitāḥ sāṃprataṃ mayā |
pihito pāpamārgo me svargamārgaḥ prakāśitaḥ || 21.52{52} ||
mokṣamārgo'pi saṃprāpto bhavatāṃ kṛpayā'dhunā |
tathā bhavān sadā nityaṃ māṃ paritrātumarhati || 21.53{53} ||
evaṃ hi na kṛtaṃ mātrā nāpi pitrā kṛtaṃ mama |
na suhṛdiṣṭamitraiśca vāṃdhavaiḥ svajanairapi || 21.54{54} ||
śramaṇairbrāhmaṇaiścāpi gurubhiśca na vā nṛpaiḥ |
tathānyaiśca mahāvijñairna kṛtamāryadeśanām || 21.55{55} ||
bhavataiva kṛtaṃ nātha satyamārgopadeśanām |
tadāryasatyasaṃprāpta āryamārgaṃ labhāmyaham || 21.56{56} ||
adya me saphalaṃ janma buddhaputro'smi sāṃpratam |
ityuktvā sa munīndraṃ taṃ praṇatvā prābhyanaṃdata || 21.57{57} ||
tataḥ sa nāvikastatra triratnaṃ śaraṇaṃ gataḥ |
satkṛtya samupāśritya prābhajan sarvadā mudā || 21.58{58} ||
tatsamīkṣya paraścāpi nāviko'tyanutāpitaḥ |
(rm 262)
tatra tasya munīndrasya satkāraṃ kartumaichata || 21.59{59} ||
tataḥ sa nāvikastatra samutthāyānumoditaḥ |
bhagavaṃtaṃ praṇatvaiva saṃprabodhayatkṛtāṃjaliḥ || 21.60{60} ||
bhagavan yanmayā lobhapraluptadharmacetasā |
tarapaṇyaṃ vinā gaṃgāṃ tārito na bhavānapi || 21.61{61} ||
tanmahānaparādho me bhavatyatrāhi jāyate |
tatkṣamatāṃ jagannātha kṣamāratnākaro bhavān || 21.62{62} ||
yaccātra bhavatāṃ pūjāṃ kartumichāmi sāṃpratam |
tānmamānugrahaṃ kṛtvā pratigrahītumarhati || 21.63{63} ||
iti saṃprārthitaṃ tena śrutvā sa bhagavānmuniḥ |
tatheti pratimoditvā tūṣṇībhūtvādhyuvāsa tat || 21.64{64} ||
tathādhivāśitaṃ śāstrā nāvikaḥ saṃpramoditaḥ |
sa pūjābhojyasāmāgrīṃ sahasā samasādhaya || 21.65{65} ||
tataḥ sa bhagavaṃtaṃ taṃ sasāṃghikaṃ yathākramam |
abhyarcya surasaiḥ śuddhairbhojanaiḥ samatoṣayat || 21.66{66} ||
tataste sāṃghikāḥ sarve saṃbuddhapramukhā api |
tadbhojyaṃ surasaṃ bhuktvā samātṛptiṃ samāyayuḥ || 21.67{67} ||
tataḥ sa nāviko dṛṣṭvā tṛptaṃ muniṃ sasāṃghikam |
apanīyāśu pātrāṇi taddhastādi vyaśodhayat || 21.68{68} ||
tataḥ pūgādi tāṃbūlamahauṣadharasāyanam |
datvā sa nāvikastatra sāṃjaliḥ prārthayatkṣamām || 21.69{69} ||
tataḥ sa sāṃjalirnatvā sasaṃghaṃ taṃ munīśvaram |
bodhicittaṃ samālamvya tatpuraḥ samupāśrayat || 21.70{70} ||
tataḥ sa bhagavāṃstasya nāvikasya manogatam |
bodhyabhilāṣamālokya tadā smitaṃ vyamuṃcata || 21.71{71} ||
tatastasya munīṃdrasya nānāvarṇā marīcayaḥ |
mukhapadmādviniryātāḥ praserurbhuvanatraye || 21.72{72} ||
yā adhastādgatāstatra narakeṣu samaṃtataḥ |
tāḥ saumyā raśmayaḥ sarvāḥ prasṛtya samahāsayan || 21.73{73} ||
tatra tābhiḥ parispṛṣṭāḥ sarve te narakāśritāḥ |
mahatsaukhyaṃ samāsādya vismitā evamabruvan || 21.74{74} ||
aho citraṃ mahatsaukhyamadhunā jāyate katham |
kutaśceyaṃ prabhā jātā kasya ceha samāgatāḥ || 21.75{75} ||
yatprabhābhiḥ parispṛṣṭā vayaṃ saukhyasamanvitāḥ |
sarve duḥkhābhinirmuktāstadidaṃ mahadadbhutam || 21.76{76} ||
kiṃ nu cyutā ito'ho svidanyatropagatā vayam |
yanno duḥkhāni śāṃyaṃte jāyaṃte sasukhānyapi || 21.77{77} ||
iti ciṃtāsamākrāntahṛdayāste savismayāḥ |
ṣarve'pyekatra saṃmīlya bhāṣaṃta upatasthire || 21.78{78} ||
evaṃ sarve'pi te satvāḥ sarvatra narakeṣvapi |
nirduḥkhā sukhasaṃpannā upatasthuḥ savismayāḥ || 21.79{79} ||
tadā teṣāṃ prabodhārthaṃ nirmāya bhagavānmunim |
(rm 263)
pratyekaṃ narākeṣvevaṃ sarvatrāpi vyasarjayat || 21.80{80} ||
taṃ buddhaṃ nirmitaṃ dṛṣṭvā sārve te narakāśritāḥ |
bhūyo'tivismayākrāṃtahṛdayāścaivamabruvan || 21.81{81} ||
na hyevetaccyutāssarve nāpyānyatra gatā vayam |
ihaiva narake sthāne sthitāḥ sarve vayaṃ khalu || 21.82{82} ||
yadihāyaṃ samāyātaḥ puruṣo pūrvadarśanaḥ |
nūnamasya prabhākāntiriyamatrābhiprasṛtā || 21.83{83} ||
tadasmākaṃ hi duḥkhāni praśāntāṃ samaṃtataḥ |
mahatsaukhyāni jayante nūnamasyānūbhāvataḥ || 21.84{84} ||
ayaṃ hi sugato buddhaḥ sarvasatvānukaṃpakaḥ |
sarvānno duḥkhitān dṛṣṭvā samuddhartumihāgataḥ || 21.85{85} ||
tadasya śaraṇaṃ gatvā vayaṃ sarve samādarāt |
satkṛtya śraddhayā natvā yathāśakti bhajemahi || 21.86{86} ||
iti saṃbhāṣya te sarve nārakīyāstathā mudā |
sahasā taṃ purogatvā praṇatvaivaṃ vabhāṣire || 21.87{87} ||
namaste bhagavannātha śaraṇaṃ te vrajāmahe |
evamasmān sadā dṛṣṭvā kṛpayā trātumarhasi || 21.88{88} ||
iti saṃprārthanāṃ kṛtvā sarve te pratimoditāḥ |
tasyaiva śaraṇaṃ kṛtvā prabhejire samāhitāḥ || 21.89{89} ||
etatpuṇyaparītāste sarve vimuktapāpakāḥ |
narakebhyaḥ samutthāya sahasā sadgatiṃ yayuḥ || 21.90{90} ||
evaṃ tā rasmayaḥ sarvā narakebhyaḥ samaṃtataḥ |
sarvān satvān saṃuddhṛtya pratyāyayurmuneḥ puraḥ || 21.91{91} ||
yāścāpyurddhagatābhāsaprasṛtāḥ tāḥ samaṃtataḥ |
avabhāsya diśaḥ sarvān samahārājikālayāḥ || 21.92{92} ||
sarvān devālayān yāvadakaniṣṭhaṃ bhavālayam |
avabhāsyamarān sarvān gāthābhiḥ samacodayat || 21.93{93} ||
anityaṃ khalu saṃsāre sarvaṃ śūnyamanātmakam |
iti matvātra saṃsāre carata sarvadā śubhe || 21.94{94} ||
niṣkrāmatārabhadhvaṃ tadyujyadhvaṃ buddhaśāsane |
dhunīta mṛtyusainyāni naḍāgāramiva dvipaḥ || 21.95{95} ||
yo hyasmin saugate dharme cariṣyati samāhitaḥ |
sa hitvā janma saṃsāre duḥkhasyāntaṃ kariṣyati || 21.96{96} ||
iti tacchabdamākarṇya sarve devāḥ prabodhitāḥ |
triratnaśaraṇaṃ kṛtvā prabhejire samāhitāḥ || 21.97{97} ||
evaṃ tā rasmayaḥ sarvāḥ sarvān devān samaṃtataḥ |
codayitvā tatastatra pratyāyayurmuneḥ puraḥ || 21.98{98} ||
tatra tā rasmayaḥ sarvā ekībhūtā supiṇḍitāḥ |
muneḥ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya bhrumadhye'ntarhito'bhavat || 21.99{99} ||
taddṛṣṭvā te sāṃghikāḥ sarve lokāścānye'pi vismitāḥ |
śāstā kimādiśeddhārmamiti dhyātvā niṣedire || 21.100{100} ||
(rm 264)
tadānaṃdaḥ samutthāya śāstāraṃ taṃ munīśvaram |
kṛtāṃjalipuṭo natvā paprachaivaṃ puraḥ sthitaḥ || 21.101{1} ||
bhagavannātha sarvajña bhavadvaktrātsudhākarāt |
nānā varṇāḥ prabhāḥ kāṃtā viniḥsṛtya prasāritāḥ || 21.102{2} ||
sarvāṃstraidhātukānnavabhāsya samaṃtataḥ |
punaḥ pratyāgātāḥ sarvā ekībhūtā supiṃḍitā || 21.103{3} ||
tava pradakṣiṇīkṛtya bhrumadhye pravisaṃti tāḥ |
nāhetupratyaye buddhā darśayanti smitaṃ kvacit || 21.104{4} ||
tadbhavān hetunā kena smitaṃ muṃcasi sāṃpratam |
yaddṛṣṭvā sāṃghikaḥ sarve ime lokāśca vismitāḥ || 21.105{5} ||
śāstā kimādiśeddharmamiti saṃdigdhamānasāḥ |
kutūhalasamākrāṃtahṛdayāḥ sadguṇārthinaḥ || 21.106{6} ||
tvatsaddharmāmṛtaṃ pātumichanti tṛṣitā iva |
yadarthe'tra bhavān smitaṃ darśayati sarasmikam || 21.107{7} ||
tadarthaṃ samupādiśya sarvāllokān prabodhaya |
ityānaṃdavaca śrutvā bhagavān sa munīśvaraḥ |
tamānaṃdaṃ sabhāṃ cāpi samālokyaivamādiśat || 21.108{8} ||
evametattathānaṃda nāhetupratyayaṃ kvacit |
saṃbuddhāḥ sugatāḥ sarve pravikurvanti saṃsmitam || 21.109{9} ||
yadarthe'hamihānaṃda smitaṃ muṃcāmi sāṃpratam |
tadarthaṃ samupākhyāmi śṛṇuta yūyamādarāt || 21.110{10} ||
yadanena satānaṃda nāvikena prasādinā |
sasaṃgho'haṃ samabhyarcya vaṃdito bodhicetāsā || 21.111{11} ||
anena kuśalenāyaṃ nāviko'pi bhaviṣyati |
saṃsārottaraṇo nāma pratyekabuddha ātmavit || 21.112{12} ||
evaṃ matvā mahatpuṇyaṃ triratnaṃ satkṛtodbhavam |
satkṛtya śraddhayā nityaṃ triratnaṃ bhajatādarāt || 21.113{13} ||
triratneṣu kṛtaṃ puṇyaṃ na kṣiṇoti kadā cana |
vratapuṇyavipākena saṃbuddhapadamāpnuyāt || 21.114{14} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa śrutvānaṃdaḥ sasāṃghikaḥ |
tathetyabhyanumoditvā triratnaṃ sarvadābhajet || 21.115{15} ||
evaṃ śāstrā munīndreṇa vyākṛtamātmanastadā |
śrutvā sa nāviko buddhaḥ prābhyanaṃdatprasāditaḥ || 21.116{16} ||
tadārabhya sadā nityaṃ nāvikaḥ saṃpramoditaḥ |
triratnaśaraṇaṃ kṛtvā satkṛtya śraddhayābhajat || 21.117{17} ||
sarve lokāśca taddṛṣṭvā saddharmaguṇavāṃchinaḥ |
satkṛtya śraddhayā nityaṃ triratnaṃ prābhajanmudā || 21.118{18} ||
tataśca sa jagacchāstā bhagavān saha sāṃghikaiḥ |
tathānyatra śubhaṃ kartuṃ pratasthe sāṃprabhāsayan || 21.119{19} ||
iti me guruṇādiṣṭaṃ mayā pracakṣyate tava |
tathātra tvaṃ mahārāja triratnaṃ sarvadā bhaja || 21.120{20} ||
prajāścāpi tathā dharme bodhayitvā prasādayan |
(rm 265)
bodhimārge pratisthāpya pālayasva samāhitaḥ || 21.121{21} ||
tathā te maṃgalaṃ nityaṃ sarvatrāpi bhaveddhruvam |
kramādbodhiṃ samāsādya saṃbuddhapadamāpnuyāḥ || 21.122{22} ||
iti tenārhatādiṣṭaṃ śrutvāśokaḥ sa bhūpatiḥ |
tathetyabhyanumoditvā prābhyanaṃdatsapārṣadaḥ || 21.123{23} ||
yacchāstrākhyātaṃ tathedaṃ yativarakathitaṃ nāvikasyāvadānaṃ
śṛṇvanti śrāvayanti pramuditamanasaḥ śraddhayā ye prasannāḥ |
te sarve śuddhabhāvā jinaguṇarūcano bodhicaryā carante |
bhuktvā saukhyāni nityaṃ munivaranilaye saṃprayānti pramodam || 21.124{24} ||

++ ityavadānatatve nāvikāvadānaṃ samaptam ++


(rm 266)
xxii gandhamādanāvadāna
athāśoko mahīpālaḥ kṛtāṃjalipuṭo mudā |
upaguptaṃ guruṃ natvā prārthayaccaivamādarāt || 22.1{1} ||
bhadaṃta śrotumichāmi punaranyatsubhāṣitam |
tadyathā guruṇādiṣṭaṃ tathādeṣṭuṃ ca me'rhati || 22.2{2} ||
iti saṃprārthite rājñā śrutvā so'rhan sudhīryatiḥ |
tamaśokaṃ mahīpālaṃ samālokyaivamādiśat || 22.3{3} ||
sādhu śṛṇu mahārāja yathā me gurubhāṣitam |
tathāhaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi tava puṇyavivṛddhaye || 22.4{4} ||
puraikasmin dine tatra rājagṛhe sasāṃghikaḥ |
bhagavān sa jagannāthaḥ piṇḍārthaṃ samupāviśat || 22.5{5} ||
tadā rājagṛhe tatra gṛhapateḥ suto mahān |
tasyaikā dāsikā bhadrā satyadharmānucāriṇī || 22.6{6} ||
sā svāmyaṅgānulepārthaṃ lohitacaṃdanaṃ tadā |
surabhigaṇasaṃyuktaṃ pipeṣa prastare mudā || 22.7{7} ||
tasminneva kṣaṇe tatra sa saṃbuddhaḥ sasāṃghikaḥ |
bhāsayaṃ maṃgalaṃ kṛtvā tadgṛhaṃ nikaṣācarat || 22.8{8} ||
tathāyātaṃ munīndraṃ taṃ śrutvā sā dārikā mudā |
sahasā nirgatā gehāttatra draṣṭumupācaran || 22.9{9} ||
tatra sā samupāgatya prādrākṣīttaṃ munīśvaram |
dvātriṃśallakṣaṇāśītivyaṃjanaparibhūṣitam || 22.10{10} ||
śatasūryādhikābhāsaṃ śatacaṃdrāmṛtaprabham |
vyāmaprabhāsamujvālaṃ ratnāṃgamiva jaṃgamam || 22.11{11} ||
dṛṣṭvā sā dāsikā tatra muditā vismayānvitā |
suciraṃ taṃ samālokya tasthau niścalamānasī || 22.12{12} ||
tatra sā suciraṃ dṛṣṭvā dāsikābhipramoditā |
satkāraṃ karttumichantī śāsturevaṃ vyaciṃtayat || 22.13{13} ||
aho bhāgyaṃ munīndro'yaṃ dṛśyate yanmayādhunā |
tadasya kiṃ bhajiṣyāmi daridrā preṣikā hyaham || 22.14{14} ||
kutra vā drakṣyate bhūyo munīndro'yaṃ jagadguruḥ |
dhigme janmāpi yenātra kiṃ citpuṇyaṃ na sādhyate || 22.15{15} ||
kiṃ mamānena dehena yenedṛśaṃ munīśvaram |
prāpya kiṃ citpradatvāpi puṇyaṃ prāptuṃ na śakyate || 22.16{16} ||
anena jīvitenāpi kiṃ mamātra prayojanam |
yena duḥkhāni bhuktvaiva kiṃ citpuṇyaṃ na sādhyate || 22.17{17} ||
puṇyaṃ vinātra saṃsāre saukhyaṃ prāptuṃ kathaṃ kuha |
tatpuṇyaṃ prāpyate kena vinā dānavrataḥ kvacit || 22.18{18} ||
(rm 267)
kiṃ cātra dāsyate dānaṃ kiṃ ciddravyaṃ na me gṛhe |
vrataṃ vā kiṃ kariṣyāmi parādhīnā hi dāsikā || 22.19{19} ||
tadatra kiṃ sukhaṃ lapsye kiṃ ciddānaṃ vrataṃ vinā |
sarvathāhaṃ vinaṣṭā syāṃ ko māṃ rakṣedbhavāntare || 22.20{20} ||
puṇyena sadgatiṃ yāyāṃ pāpena durgatiṃ sadā |
iti hetoḥ prakarttavyaṃ dānaṃ kiṃ cidapi vratam || 22.21{21} ||
tarhyetadvidyate haste lohitacaṃdanaṃ mama |
etenāpi munīndrasya bhajeyaṃ caraṇāmbujau || 22.22{22} ||
iti dhyātvā viniścitya darikā sā prasāditā |
sahasā svagṛhaṃ gatvā taccaṃdanaṃ samāgrahīt || 22.23{23} ||
taccaṃdanaṃ samādāya dāsikā sā vyacintayat |
yadidaṃ svāmino dravyaṃ tadanujñāṃ vinā katham || 22.24{24} ||
yadi svāmi vijānāti tadā me kupito ruṣā |
nirbhatsya tāḍayitvā māṃ gṛhānniṣkāsayedapl || 22.25{25} ||
tadātra kiṃ cariṣyāmi dāsikāhaṃ nirāśrayā |
avaśyaṃ bhāvino bhāvā bhavanti mahatāmapi || 22.26{26} ||
yadi me kupitaḥ svāmī paratra kiṃ kariṣyati |
prasanno yadi vā svāmī kalyāṇaṃ syādihāpi me || 22.27{27} ||
kupito va prasanno vā bhavatvatraiva me prabhuḥ |
svāmī bharttānuśāstaiva paratra kiṃ kariṣyati || 22.28{28} ||
buddhastu jagatāṃ svāmī bharttā nātho'nukaṃpakaḥ |
prabhu dharmānuśāstā me paratrehāpyavetsadā || 22.29{29} ||
tadatra kiṃ mamānena svāmināpi kariṣyate |
tadetaccaṃdanenāpi bhajeyaṃ taṃ munīśvaram || 22.30{30} ||
iti dhyātvā viniścitya dāsikā sā pramoditā |
lohitacaṃdanenobhau pāṇau pralipya setvayā || 22.31{31} ||
gṛhādvahirvinirgatvā bhagavate'nta upāgatā |
pādayorubhayostena caṃdanena lilepa sā || 22.32{32} ||
tataḥ sā dārikā nārī kṛtāṃjalipuṭo mudā |
pādau tasya munernatvā purataḥ samupāśrayat || 22.33{33} ||
tatra sa bhagavān dṛṣṭvā tasyā bhaktirataṃ manaḥ |
taccaṃdanasaurabhyaistatpuraṃ samapūrayat || 22.34{34} ||
tatastadgaṃdhamāghrāya sarve paurāḥ savismayāḥ |
kasyedaṃ gaṃdhamāyātamiti proktvā pracerire || 22.35{35} ||
tatastadvṛttimājñāya sarve lokāḥ pramoditāḥ |
taddraṣṭuṃ sahasā tatra bhagavato'ntikamupāyayuḥ || 22.36{36} ||
tatra sarve'pi te lokā dṛṣṭvā taṃ śrīghanaṃ munim |
natvā pradakṣiṇīkṛtvā samupatasthurādarāt || 22.37{37} ||
tadā sa bhagavān dṛṣṭvā sarvānstān samupasthitān |
āryyasatyaṃ samārabhya dideśa dharmamuttamam || 22.38{38} ||
taddhārmadeśanāṃ śrutvā sarve lokāḥ prabodhitaḥ |
triratnaśaraṇaṃ kṛtvā sadā bhajitumīchire || 22.39{39} ||
tatsaddharmāmṛtaṃ pītvā dāsikā sā prabodhitā |
(rm 268)
sāṃjalistaṃ muniṃ natvā praṇidhānaṃ vyadhāttathā || 22.40{40} ||
etatpuṇyavipākena saṃsāre'tra punarbhave |
pratyekāṃ bodhimāsādya nirvāṇapadamāpnuyām || 22.41{41} ||
evaṃ buddhapadaṃ prāptyai praṇidhānaṃ kṛtaṃ tayā |
dṛṣṭvā sa bhagavāṃstatra smitaṃ prādurvyadhāttadā || 22.42{42} ||
tatastasya munervaktrānnānāvarṇāḥ surasmayaḥ |
vinirgatā diśaḥ sarvā bhāsayantaḥ praserire || 22.43{43} ||
yā adhastādgatābhāsāḥ sarvatra narakeṣvapi |
avabhāsya śubhaṃ kṛtvā sarvatra saṃprasaṃsire || 22.44{44} ||
tatra satvān parispṛśya kṛtvā sarvān sukhānvitān |
saṃbuddhadarśanaṃ datvā saṃpreṣayan surālāyam || 22.45{45} ||
yāścāpyurddhaṃ gatābhāsatāḥ sarvāṃśca surālayan |
avabhāsyāmarān sarvān samudghuṣyābhyacodayan || 22.46{46} ||
anityaṃ khalu saṃsāraṃ sarvaṃ śūnyaṃ nirātmakam |
iti matvātra karttavyaṃ saṃbuddhabhajanaṃ sadā || 22.47{47} ||
