niṣkrāmata rabhadhvaṃ tatsaddharmaṃ buddhaśāsane |
māracaryāṃ parityajya carata bhadracārikām || 22.48{48} ||
yo'pramattaścaratyatra saṃbuddhaśāsane vratam |
sa vihāya punarjātiṃ duḥkhasyāṃtaṃ kariṣyati || 22.49{49} ||
iti matvātra saṃsāre pariśuddhatrimaṇḍalāḥ |
triratnaṃ śaraṇaṃ kṛtvā bhajadhvaṃ śraddhayā sadā || 22.50{50} ||
etadghoṣaṃ samākarṇya sarve devāḥ pramoditāḥ |
triratnasmaraṇaṃ kṛtvā prācaran sarvadā śubhe || 22.51{51} ||
tataste raśmayaḥ sarve bhāsayantaḥ samaṃtataḥ |
pratyāgatā munīndrasya purataḥ samupāśrayan || 22.52{52} ||
tatra te raśmayaḥ sarve'pyekībhūtāḥ supiṇḍitāḥ |
śāstuḥ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya bhruvo'ntare tirodadhau || 22.53{53} ||
taddṛṣṭvā te sabhāsīnāḥ sarve lokāḥ savismayāḥ |
śāstā kimādiśeddharmamiti dhyātvā samāśrayan || 22.54{54} ||
tatrānaṃdaḥ samālokya sarvāṃllokān savismayān |
sāṃjalistaṃ jagannāthaṃ praṇatvaivaṃ samabravīt || 22.55{55} ||
bhagavan hetunā kena smitaṃ muṃcati sāṃpratam |
nāhetupratyayaṃ buddhāḥ smitaṃ muṃcaṃti sarvadā || 22.56{56} ||
yadbhavataḥ smite vaktrānnānāvarṇāḥ suraśmayaḥ |
nirgatāḥ sarvalokeṣu prasṛtā avabhāsyata || 22.57{57} ||
ime sarvatra bhadrāṇi kṛtvā satvāṃśca bhadritān |
pratyāgatā bhavadurṇāmadhye cāntardadhanti hi || 22.58{58} ||
etadadbhutamālokya sarve lokā ime'dhunā |
śāstā kimādiśeddharmamiti dhyātvā samāsthitāḥ || 22.59{59} ||
bhavaddharmāmṛtaṃ pātuṃ sarve saddharmavāṃchinaḥ |
bhavantameva saṃvīkṣya saṃtiṣṭhante samāhitāḥ || 22.60{60} ||
(rm 269)
bhagavaṃstadyadarthe tvaṃ smitaṃ muṃcasi sāṃpratam |
tadarthaṃ samupādiśya sarvāllokān prabodhaya || 22.61{61} ||
iti saṃprārthite tena bhagavān sa munīścaraḥ |
tamānaṃdaṃ sabhāṃ cāpi samālokyaivamādiśat || 22.62{62} ||
evametanmahānaṃda sarve'pi sugatā jināḥ |
nāhetupratyayaṃ kvāpi smitaṃ muṃcaṃti sarvadā || 22.63{63} ||
yadarthe'hamihānaṃda smitaṃ muṃcāmi sāṃpratam |
tadarthaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi sarve śṛṇvaṃtu sādaram || 22.64{64} ||
paśyānaṃdānayā nāryyā dārikayā mama padoḥ |
lohitacaṃdanenaivaṃ pralipya satkṛtiḥ kṛtā || 22.65{65} ||
etatpuṇyavipākena dārikeyaṃ bhaviṣyati |
pratyekasugato gandhamādano nāma sarvavit || 22.66{66} ||
yatheyaṃ mayi saṃbuddhacittaṃ prasādya moditā |
praṇidhānaṃ karotyevaṃ pratyekabodhimāpsyati || 22.67{67} ||
evaṃ matvā mahatpuṇyaṃ saṃbuddhabhajanodbhavam |
satkṛtya śraddhayā nityaṃ triratnaṃ bhajamādarāt || 22.68{68} ||
triratnabhajanodbhūtaṃ puṇyamahattaraṃ bahu |
yathechābodhisaṃbhārapūraṇaṃ nirvṛtiṃ pradam || 22.69{69} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa śrutvā sarve'numoditāḥ |
triratnabhajanaṃ kartuṃ prābhyanandan sadāpi te || 22.70{70} ||
tadā sā dāsikāpyevaṃ śāstrā vyākṛtamātmanaḥ |
śrutvābhinaṃditā bhūyo nanāma taṃ munīśvaram || 22.71{71} ||
tataḥ sā svāminā dattaṃ dravyamādāya satvarā |
tadarhabhojanaiḥ pūrṇaṃ pātraṃ kṛtvā mudācarat || 22.72{72} ||
tatra sā muditā tasmai munīndrāya pradāpayat |
sāṃjaliḥ praṇatiṃ kṛtvā svagehaṃ samupācarat || 22.73{73} ||
tataḥ sa bhagavān pṛṣṭe piṇḍapātraṃ tayārcitam |
sasaṃghaṃ svāśrame gatvā vyaharaddharmamādiśat || 22.74{74} ||
tatra sā dāsikā bhadrā triratnaṃ śaraṇaṃ gatā |
satkṛtya śraddhayā nityaṃ prābhajan sarvadā mudā || 22.75{75} ||
evaṃ me guruṇākhyātaṃ śrutaṃ mayātra kathyate |
tathā tvamapi rājeṃdra triratnaṃ sarvadā bhaja || 22.76{76} ||
tathā rājaṃ prajāścāpi bodhayitvā prayatnataḥ |
bodhimārge pratiṣṭhāpya pālayasva samāhitaḥ || 22.77{77} ||
tathā taṃ maṃgalaṃ nityaṃ sarvatrāpi bhaveddhruvam |
kramādbodhiṃ samāsādya saṃbuddhapadamāpnuyāḥ || 22.78{78} ||
iti tenārhatādiṣṭaṃ śrutvāśokaḥ sa bhūmipaḥ |
tathetyabhyanumoditvā prābhyanandatsapārṣadaḥ || 22.79{79} ||
idaṃ narā ye ci ca gaṃdhamādanāvadānamatra prathitaṃ prasāditāḥ |
śṛṇvanti ye cāpi niśāmayanti sarve sukhāḍhyāḥ pracaranti sadgatau |

(rm 270)
++ iti ratnāvadānatatve gaṃdhamādanāvadānaṃ samāpta ++


(rm 271)
xxiii pretikāyāḥ kathā
athāśoko mahārājaḥ sāṃjaliḥ saṃpramoditaḥ |
upaguptaṃ guruṃ natvā punarevamabhāṣata || 23.1{1} ||
bhadanta śrotumichāmi punaranyatsubhāṣitam |
tadyathā guruṇākhyātaṃ tathādeṣṭuṃ ca me'rhati || 23.2{2} ||
iti saṃprārthite rājñā śrutvā so'rhaṃ mahāmatiḥ |
upagupto nareṃdraṃ taiḥ samālokyaivamādiśat || 23.3{3} ||
sādhu śṛṇu mahārāja yathā me guruṇoditam |
tathāhaṃ te pravakṣyāmi śrutvā buddhānumodaya || 23.4{4} ||
purā sa bhagavān buddhaḥ śākyasiṃho munīśvaraḥ |
sarvajñaḥ sugataḥ śāstā dharmarājo vināyakaḥ || 23.5{5} ||
bhikṣubhiḥ śrāvakaiḥ sārddhaṃ bhikṣuṇībhiśca cailakaiḥ |
upāsakaiḥ śubhācārairupāsikāgaṇairapi || 23.6{6} ||
bodhisatvairmahāsatvaiḥ saṃbodhicārikodyamaiḥ |
tathānyaistīrthikaiścāpi saṃbuddhaguṇavāṃchibhiḥ || 23.7{7} ||
rājagṛhapuropānte veṇuvane manorame |
jināśrame mahodyāne karaṃdakanivāpake || 23.8{8} ||
sarvasatvahitārthena bodhicaryāṃ prakāśayan |
saddharmadeśanāṃ kurvan vijahāra virocayan || 23.9{9} ||
tatsaddharmadeśanāṃ śrotuṃ tatra sarve samāgatāḥ |
devā daityāśca nāgendrā yakṣagaṃdharvakinnarāḥ || 23.10{10} ||
grahā vidyādharāḥ siddhā lokapālagaṇā api |
ṛṣayo brāhmaṇāścāpi rājānaḥ kṣatriyātmajāḥ || 23.11{11} ||
vaiśyāśca maṃtriṇo'mātyā gṛhādhipā mahājanāḥ |
paurikāḥ sārthavāhāśca vaṇijaḥ śilpino'pi ca || 23.12{12} ||
grāmyā jānapadāścāpi tathā kārpaṭikādayaḥ |
sarve te samupāyātāstatrāśrame upāviśan || 23.13{13} ||
tatra sabhāsanāsīnaṃ śrīghanaṃ taṃ munīśvaram |
vyāmaprabhāvirocaṃ taṃ bhikṣusaṃghaiḥ puraskṛtam || 23.14{14} ||
dṛṣṭvā te muditāḥ sarve praṇatvā samupāgatāḥ |
kṛtvā pradakṣiṇānyenaṃ samānarcu yathākramam || 23.15{15} ||
tatassarve'pi te lokāḥ parivṛtya samaṃtataḥ |
tatsaddharmāmṛtaṃ pātumupatasthuḥ samāhitāḥ || 23.16{16} ||
tadā sa bhagavān dṛṣṭvā sarvānstān samupāśṛtān |
āryyasatyaṃ samārgāṃgaṃ saddharmaṃ samupādiśan || 23.17{17} ||
tātsaddharmāmṛtaṃ pītvā sarve te saṃprabodhitāḥ |
(rm 272)
dharmaviśeṣamājñāya bodhicaryāṃ samīchire || 23.18{18} ||
tadaikasmin dine bhikṣurāyuṣmānnāradābhidhaḥ |
bhikṣārthaṃ pātramādāya rājagṛhapure yayau || 23.19{19} ||
tatra sa nārado bhikṣuḥ piṃḍārthaṃ samupācaran |
gṛhasthena samālokya nītvā gehe mudāsane || 23.20{20} ||
pratisthāpya samabhyarcya supraṇītarasānvitaiḥ |
tīlodanaiḥ sudhākalpaiḥ pānaiśca pratipūjitaḥ || 23.21{21} ||
tadbhuktvā sa yatistuṣṭo datvā tasmai śubhāśiṣam |
samutthāya tato'raṇye viharttuṃ nirjane'carat || 23.22{22} ||
tatra sa nārado yogī gṛdhrakūṭanagāntike |
dadarśa pretikāmekāṃ rākṣasīmiva bhīṣaṇām || 23.23{23} ||
vṛkṣamūle sthitāṃ raktavinduvyāptāmvarāvṛtām |
asthiśakalasaṃkīrṇe pretālaye nivāsinīm || 23.24{24} ||
svātmajānapi bhakṣantīṃ kṣutpipāsāgnidāhinām |
kṛśāṅgīṃ vikṛtākārāṃ karvuramuktakeśinīm || 23.25{25} ||
dṛṣṭvā sa nāradastatra samupāsṛtya tatpuraḥ |
pretikāṃ tāṃ samālokya paprachaivaṃ purākṛtam || 23.26{26} ||
kiṃ tvayā prakṛtaṃ ghoraṃ pātakaṃ pūrvajanmasu |
yenaivaṃ duḥsahamanubhavasi sāṃpratam || 23.27{27} ||
iti tenārhatā pṛṣṭe pretikā sā rudantyapi |
nāradaṃ taṃ yatiṃ dṛṣṭvā śanairevamabhāṣata || 23.28{28} ||
bhadanta kiṃ mayā hyatra vakṣyate yatpurākṛtam |
divārātrau paṃcaputrān prasūtā me prabhakṣitā || 23.29{29} ||
tenāpi na saṃtuṣṭā bubhukṣitā pravarddhate |
tadbhavāṃstaṃ jagadvijñaṃ pṛchatā matpurākṛtam || 23.30{30} ||
sa munīndro jagacchāstādiśette'smatpurākṛtam |
āditye hi samudyāte dīpena kiṃ prayojanam || 23.31{31} ||
tathāsmatprakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ śrutvānyena narādayaḥ |
satvāḥ saṃtrasitā evaṃ kuryu na ke cana || 23.32{32} ||
sarve te'pi diśetpāpān viramya śīlasaṃvṛtāḥ |
triratnaśaraṇaṃ kṛtvā prabhajeyuḥ sadādritāḥ || 23.33{33} ||
iti tayoditaṃ śrutvā nāradaḥ sa prabodhitaḥ |
tatheti sahasā gatvā tatra veṇuvane'sarat || 23.34{34} ||
tadā sa bhagavāṃstatra dhyānālayātsamutthitaḥ |
sabhāmadhyāsanāsīnaḥ saddharmaṃ samupādiśat || 23.35{35} ||
tasminnavasare tatra nāradaḥ satvarānvitaḥ |
dūrāttaṃ śrīghanaṃ paśyan sahasā samupācaran || 23.36{36} ||
tameva samupāyātaṃ nāradaṃ sa munīśvaraḥ |
dṛṣṭvārātsamupāmaṃtrya punarevamabhāṣata || 23.37{37} ||
ehi te svāgataṃ bhikṣo kutastvaṃ samupāgataḥ |
kimarthamiha yenaivaṃ prāyāsi tadvadasva me || 23.38{38} ||
ityādiṣṭe munīndreṇa nāradaḥ sa kṛtāṃjaliḥ |
samupetya munīndraṃ taṃ praṇatvaivaṃ nyavedayat || 23.39{39} ||
(rm 273)
bhagavan bhavatāṃ pādau draṣṭumiha samācare |
adyāhaṃ nirjane'raṇye vihartuṃ samupācare || 23.40{40} ||
tatrāhaṃ pretikāmekāṃ rākṣasīmiva bhīṣaṇām |
kṛśāṅgīṃ vikṛtākārāṃ raktavindu'nvitāṃvarām || 23.41{41} ||
anekā asthiṣaṃkīrṇe pretālaye iva sthitām |
paśyāmi tāmupāsṛtya paripṛchāmi tatkṛtam || 23.42{42} ||
kiṃ tvayā prakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ pūrvajanmasu dāruṇam |
yenedṛgmahāduḥkhamanubhavasi sāṃpratam || 23.43{43} ||
iti pṛṣṭe mayāthāsau pretikā duḥkhatāpinī |
rudantī māṃ samālokya śanairevamabhāṣata || 23.44{44} ||
bhadanta kiṃ vadiṣyāmi yanmayā pātakaṃ kṛtam |
yenedṛśaṃ mahāduḥkhamanubhavāmi sāṃpratam || 23.45{45} ||
paṃcaputrānahaṃ rātrau divā paṃca tathāparān |
prasūya tāṃ prabhakṣyāmi nāsti tṛptistathāpi me || 23.46{46} ||
evaṃ kṣutpipāsāgnitīvratāpābhidāhitā |
divārātriṃ mahadduḥkhaṃ bhuktvā tiṣṭhāmi jaṃgale || 23.47{47} ||
tatpurā kiṃ kṛtaṃ ghoraṃ pātakamatidāruṇam |
mayāpi jñāyate nātra paripṛcha munīśvaram || 23.48{148} ||
sarvajñastrijagacchāstā bhagavān sa munīśvaraḥ |
yanmayā prakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ tatsarvaṃ samupāviśet || 23.49{49} ||
iti tayoditaṃ śrutvā tatheti paribodhitaḥ |
bhavantamatra saṃpraṣṭuṃ samāgachāmi sāṃpratam || 23.50{50} ||
kiṃ tayā prakṛtaṃ ghoraṃ pātakamatidāruṇam |
yenaivaṃ duḥsahaṃ duḥkhaṃ bhuktvā carati sādhunā || 23.51{51} ||
tatsarvaṃ samupādiśya prabodhayitumarhati || 23.52{52} ||
iti saṃprārthite tena bhagavān sa munīśvaraḥ |
nāradaṃ taṃ mahābhijñaṃ samālokyaivamādiśet || 23.53{53} ||
nārada pretikā sātitīvrapātakasādhinī |
yadīcchestatkṛtaṃ śroṭuṃ śṛṇu vakṣyāmi te'dhunā || 23.54{54} ||
tadyathābhūtpurā puryyāṃ vārāṇasyāṃ mahājanaḥ |
śreṣṭhī prāḍhyo mahacchrīmācchrīdopamo dhanādhipaḥ || 23.55{55} ||
sa kuladharmiṇīṃ bhāryāmupayeme yathāvidhim |
tataḥ sa kāṃtayā sārddhaṃ tayā reme pramodayan || 23.56{56} ||
tasyaivaṃ ramamāṇasya krīḍataḥ sucirādapi |
tasyāmeko'pi putro vā putrī vā nābhyajāyata || 23.57{57} ||
tataḥ sa gṛhabhṛcchreṣṭhī santatidarśanotsukaḥ |
tannirāśābhisaṃtaptamūrchito'bhūdyathāturaḥ || 23.58{58} ||
kapolaṃ svabhuje sthāpya śokāgāre vyavasthitaḥ |
saṃpaccintāviṣaṇṇāsyo manasaivaṃ vyaciṃtayat || 23.59{59} ||
hā daiva kiṃ mayā pāpaṃ sukṛtaṃ duḥkṛtaṃ purā |
yanmamaivaṃ vidhaṃ duḥkhaṃ sukhaṃ cātra prajāyate || 23.60{60} ||
anekadravyasaṃpattisamuditaṃ gṛhaṃ mama |
(rm 274)
tatra bhoktā tu naiko'pi putro'sti duhitāpi na || 23.61{61} ||
tatsarvaṃ svāpateyaṃ hi mamātyayānnarādhipaḥ |
aputrakamiti kṛtvā sarvasvaṃ sarvathā haret || 23.62{62} ||
yanmayānekayatnena sādhitaṃ rakṣitaṃ ca tat |
sarvameva nirarthena vinaṣṭaṃ me bhaviṣyati || 23.63{63} ||
tadatra kiṃ kariṣyāmi yasyopāyaṃ na manyate |
sarvathāhaṃ vinaṣṭo'smi ko māṃ rakṣedihādhunā || 23.64{64} ||
evaṃ cintāviṣaṇṇāsyaṃ svāminaṃ taṃ gṛhādhipam |
dṛṣṭvā sā ramaṇī bhāryā samālokyaivamabravīt || 23.65{65} ||
svāminnatra kimeva tvaṃ tiṣṭhase duḥkhacintayā |
kiṃ tava jāyate duḥkhaṃ tadvadasva mamāgrataḥ || 23.66{66} ||
iti bhāryoditaṃ śrutvā sa gṛhastho viniśvasan |
taṃ bhāryāṃ ramaṇīkāntāṃ samālokyaivamabravīt || 23.67{67} ||
priye'tra kiṃ vadiṣyāmi yasyopāyaṃ na manyate |
yanme'sti mahatī saṃpadasyā bhoktā na vidyate || 23.68{68} ||
nūnaṃ madatyayādrājā mamaitāḥ sarvasaṃpadaḥ |
aputrasya nṛpādhīnā iti kṛtvā hariṣyati || 23.69{69} ||
yanmamaitā mahāyatnaiḥ sādhitā vanditā api |
tatsarvaṃ hi nirarthena vinakṣyati niratyayāt || 23.70{70} ||
etanme jāyate duḥkhaṃ tadatra kiṃ vadeya hi |
ko mamaitanmahacchalyaṃ hṛdaye'tra samuddharet || 23.71{71} ||
iti cintāviṣaṇṇātra hṛdayaṃ dahyate mama |
tenaivamatra tiṣṭhāmi viṣadagdho yathāturaḥ || 23.72{72} ||
iti bhartroditaṃ śrutvā sā bhāryā ramaṇī priyā |
svāminaṃ taṃ samālokya bodhayituṃ samabravīt || 23.73{73} ||
etadarthe viṣādatvaṃ mā kṛthāḥ sarvathā priye |
upāyāṃ kuru tatprāptyai yadi saṃtatimichasi || 23.74{74} ||
upāyamapi siddhyeta devatānāṃ prasādataḥ |
taddevatāḥ samārādhya yācasva santatiṃ mudā || 23.75{75} ||
tadā devaprasādena jayennau santatiḥ khalu |
putro vā yadi vā putrī jātā syātsaphalaṃ bhave || 23.76{76} ||
iti me vacanaṃ śrutvā kulasthitiṃ yadīchasi |
devatārādhanaṃ kṛtvā bhajasva saṃtatīchayā || 23.77{77} ||
iti bhāryoditaṃ śrutvā sa gṛhasthaḥ prabodhitaḥ |
tathetyabhyanumoditvā tathā karttuṃ samaichata || 23.78{78} ||
tataḥ sa gṛhabhṛcchreṣṭhī bhāryayā saha moditaḥ |
svakuleśaṃ samabhyarcya santānaṃ samayāca tat || 23.79{79} ||
tathāpi śreṣṭhinastasya bhāryābhūnnaiva garbhiṇī |
tataḥ sa parikhinnātmā tasthāvevaṃ nirāśayā || 23.80{80} ||
tataḥ sā ramaṇī bhāryā nirāśākhinnamānasam |
svāminaṃ taṃ samālokya punarevamabhāṣata || 23.81{81} ||
svāminmātra viṣādatvaṃ kṛthā dhairyaṃ samāśraya |
viṣādo klīyate cittaṃ kṣīṇadtto na vīryabhāk || 23.82{82} ||
(rm 275)
vīryaṃ vinātra saṃsāre kiṃ citkāryaṃ na siddhyate |
tasmādvīryaṃ samālambya bhaja devān samāhitaḥ || 23.83{83} ||
yadi putro na jātāḥ syātputrī vāpi na jāyate |
puṇyaṃ tu varddhyate nūnaṃ devatārādhanādapi || 23.84{84} ||
iti matvātra saṃsāre sadā saukhyaṃ yadīchasi |
sarvān devān samabhyarcya bhajasvā śraddhayādarāt || 23.85{85} ||
iti bhāryoditaṃ śrutvā bharttā śreṣṭhī sa bodhitaḥ |
tatheti pratibhāṣitvā devānarcitumaichata || 23.86{86} ||
tathā santānamichen sa gṛhastho bharyyayā mudā |
vidhiṃ sraṣṭāramārādhya saṃtatiṃ samayācata || 23.87{87} ||
tathāpi tasya bhāryā sā garbhiṇī no'bhavacciram |
tataśca sa vibhagnāśastasthau śokaviṣāditaḥ || 23.88{88} ||
taṭo'pi bhāryayā bhūyaḥ preryyamāṇaḥ sa bodhitaḥ |
viśvaṃbharaṃ samabhyarcya yayāca saṃtatiṃ tathā || 23.89{89} ||
tathāpi sābhavannaiva dohadalakṣaṇāṅkitā |
tataścāsau gṛhastho'bhūnnirāśāparikheditaḥ || 23.90{90} ||
tathā sa bhāryayā bhūyaḥ preritaḥ paribodhitaḥ |
maheśvaraṃ samabhyarcya saṃtatiṃ samayācata || 23.91{91} ||
tathendraṃ ca samārādhya prārthayatsaṃtatiṃ mudā |
evaṃ lokādhipān sarvān grahān sarvānahūrapi |
samārādhya samabhyarcya prārthayatsantatiṃ tathā || 23.92{92} ||
tataścaivaṃ mahākālaṃ kumāraṃ ca gaṇādhipam |
bhairavaṃ mātṛkāścāpi prārthayadvaṃśamarcayan || 23.93{93} ||
evamanyānapi devān vihāramaṇḍapāśrayān |
maṭhasatrāśramārāmaśṛṃgāṭakapratiṣṭhitān || 23.94{94} ||
udyānaparvatāraṇyākṣatrasīmādisaṃsthitān |
sarvajalāśrayasthāṃśca sarvānnāgādhipānapi || 23.95{95} ||
rathyācatvaramārgādipratisthitān samutsukaḥ |
sarvān devān samārādhya yayācaivaṃ sa saṃtatim || 23.96{96} ||
tathāpi śreṣṭhinastasya bhāryāyāṃ sucīrādapi |
tasyāṃ naivābhavatkiṃ ciddohadotpattilakṣaṇam || 23.97{97} ||
tataḥ sa gṛhabhṛcchreṣṭhī dṛṣṭvā vaṃśanirantaram |
nirāśāpratikhinnātmā tasthāvevaṃ yathāturaḥ || 23.98{98} ||
etadduḥkhavibhinnāsyaṃ dṛṣṭvā sā ramaṇī priyā |
svāminaṃ taṃ samīkṣantī punarevamabhāṣata || 23.99{99} ||
svāminnatra viṣādena kiṃ citkāryaṃ na siddhyate |
iti matvā yathākāmaṃ saukhyaṃ bhuktvā samācara || 23.100{100} ||
yadāvābhyāṃ kṛtaṃ pāpaṃ tatphalaṃ bhujyate'dhunā |
tadatra kiṃ kariṣyāvo daivaṃ hi valavān bhave || 23.101{1} ||
yadabhāvi bhavennaiva bhāvi cennānyathā kvacit |
avaśyaṃ bhāvino bhāvā bhavanti sarvathā bhave || 23.102{2} ||
iti matvātra saṃsāre bhuktvā saukhyaṃ yathechayā |
triratnabhajanaṃ kṛtvā cara satyaṃ samāhitaḥ || 23.103{3} ||
(rm 276)
tehaiva sarvadā bhadramihāmutrāpi sarvataḥ |
kramādbodhipadaṃ prāpya nirvṛtiṃ samavāpsyati || 23.104{4} ||
iti bhāryāsamākhyātaṃ śrutvā śreṣṭhī sa bodhitaḥ |
triratnabhajanaṃ kṛtvā kañcitkālaṃ vyalaṃghayat || 23.105{5} ||
tatastasya suhṛnmitramupāgatya puraḥ sthitaḥ |
tatsaṃtānasamutpatyai tanmitramevamabravīt || 23.106{6} ||
bho mitra śṛṇu me vākyaṃ yatte hitaṃ mayocyate |
sarvathāsyāṃ priyāyāṃ te saṃtānaṃ vidyate na hi || 23.107{7} ||
tadupāyaṃ tu karttavyaṃ saṃtatiṃ pratipattaye |
nirvaṃśasya hi saṃsāre nirarthaṃ sarvasādhanam || 23.108{8} ||
tatsaṃtānasamutpatyai dvitīyaṃ putrabhāvinīm |
aparāṃ yuvatīṃ bhāryāṃ vivaha vidhinā punaḥ || 23.109{9} ||
kadā cittava bhāgyena sā putraṃ janayedapi |
tadā te saphalaṃ janmaḥ kulasthitirbhavedapi || 23.110{10} ||
iti mitroditaṃ śrutvā sa gṛhasthaḥ prabodhitaḥ |
tathā hīti pratijñāya taṃ mitramabhyamānayat || 23.111{11} ||
tataḥ sa gṛhabhṛcchreṣṭhī nirīkṣya putrabhāvinīm |
aparāṃ yuvatīṃ kāṃtāmupayeme yathāvidhiḥ || 23.112{12} ||
tataḥ sa kulikaḥ śreṣṭhī tayā sārddhaṃ pramoditaḥ |
yathākāmaṃ sukhaṃ bhuktvā rarāma paricārayan || 23.113{13} ||
tatastasya gṛhasthasya yathākāmaṃ prabhuṃjataḥ |
samaye sā priyā bhāryā vabhūva garbhiṇī'cirāt || 23.114{14} ||
tataḥ kramātpravṛddho'bhūdgarbhastasyā dine dine |
tadā sā garbhasaṃjātaṃ parijñāyābhyanandata || 23.115{15} ||
tataḥ sā bhadrikā kāntā svāminaṃ taṃ pramoditā |
rahasi samupāmaṃtrya samālokyaivamabravīt || 23.116{16} ||
diṣṭyāryyaputra vṛddho'si garbhe me jāyate śiśuḥ |
dakṣiṇapārśvasaṃsthāyattadayaṃ dārako bhavet || 23.117{17} ||
iti bhāryoditaṃ śrutvā sa gṛhastho'bhinaṃditaḥ |
savyavāhuṃ samutsārya mudaivaṃ samudāharat || 23.118{18} ||
hā me'bhilaṣitaṃ siddhyetpaśyeyaṃ saṃtatermukham |
sāṃprataṃ nāvajāto'smi kulasthitirbhavedapi || 23.119{19} ||
kṛtyāni me prakurvīta vibhṛyācca bhṛtaḥ prati |
dāyādyaṃ pratipadyeta vaṃśo pi syāccirasthitaḥ || 23.120{20} ||
yanmayā prakṛtaṃ dānaṃ yacca puṇyaṃ prasādhitam |
etadvipākakauśalyametayoranugachatu || 23.121{21} ||
evaṃ sa muditaḥ śreṣṭhī tāṃ bhāryāṃ bhadrikāṃ priyām |
supathyopacārasaṃyuktairāhāraiḥ samapoṣayat || 23.122{22} ||
dūṣyaiścīnāṃśukaiḥ paṭṭaiḥ komalaissaurabhānvitaiḥ |
nānāratnābhyalaṃkārairmaṇḍayitvābhyamodayat || 23.123{23} ||
evaṃ tāṃ bhadrikāṃ kāntāṃ pathyopacārabhūṣaṇaiḥ |
poṣitāṃ maṇḍitāṃ kṛtvā prāmāṇayatprasādayan || 23.124{24} ||
(rm 277)
evaṃ tāṃ garbhiṇīṃ bharturbahumānābhinaṃditām |
dṛṣṭvā sā prathamā bhāryā bhūya evaṃ vyaciṃtāyat || 23.125{25} ||
yadyeṣā yuvatī kāṃtā janayedatisaṃtatim |
tadetatsarvasaṃpattiradhīnāsyā bhavedapi || 23.126{26} ||
eṣaiva hi pradhānā syātsarvalokapramāṇikā |
ko'pi pramāṇayennaiva jaratīṃ māmaputritām || 23.127{27} ||
tadatrāsyāḥ sapatnyā me yathā syādgarbhaśātanam |
tathopāyaṃ prakarttavyaṃ suguptena mayādhunā || 23.128{28} ||
kāmarāgāgnitaptasya nāsti kiṃ ciddhi pātakam |
sarvamapi prakarttavyaṃ bhoktavyaṃ sarvato'pi ca || 23.129{29} ||
tataḥ sā pramadā duṣṭā tasyā garbhābhiśātanam |
drayaṃ pānābhisaṃyuktaṃ dadau viśrambhavādinī || 23.130{30} ||
tatsapatnyārpitaṃ pānaṃ dṛṣṭvā sā bhadrikārjavā |
hastābhyāṃ sahasādāya papau tṛṣṇārditā yathā || 23.131{31} ||
tadetatpītamātreṇa tasyā garbho vighūrṇitaḥ |
sahasābhidravībhūtaḥ prasaśrāvābhyaśoṣataḥ || 23.132{32} ||
tataḥ sā bhadrikā nārī bharttāraṃ taṃ sabāṃdhavam |
sahasā samupāmaṃtrya punarevaṃ nyavedayat || 23.133{33} ||
svāmiṃstavānayā patnyā priyayā garbhaśātanam |
pānamauṣadhasaṃyuktaṃ sādaraṃ me pradīyate || 23.134{34} ||
tattaddattaṃ saviśvāsaṃ gṛhītvā pīyate mayā |
tatpānapītamātreṇa garbho me srasyate'dhunā || 23.135{35} ||
tadatra kiṃ kariṣyāmi yato me naśyate śiśuḥ |
yadarthaṃ prakṛtaṃ dānaṃ tatsarvaṃ nisphalaṃ tava || 23.136{36} ||
ityākhyātaṃ tayā patnyā śrutvā śreṣṭhī sa roṣitaḥ |
tāṃ sahasāhūya paribhāṣyaivamālapat || 23.137{37} ||
hā re re durmate duṣṭe ghorapātakasādhinī |
evamapi mahāghoraṃ pātakaṃ prakṛtaṃ tvayā || 23.138{38} ||
yadarthe devatāḥ sarvāssamārādhyābhiyācitāḥ |
nānāvidhānayatnāni prakṛtāni mamāniśam || 23.139{39} ||
sarvāṇyetāni yatnāni tvayā vyarthīkṛtāni hi |
tadatra kiṃ tvayā patnyā pāpiṣṭhayā vrajādhunā || 23.140{40} ||
mayā tvaṃ pāpinī tyaktā mā tiṣṭheha gṛhe mama |
ityuktaṃ svāminā tena śrutvā sā duritāśayā |
sajñāteḥ svāminastasya purarevamabhāṣata || 23.141{41} ||
kimevaṃ vadase svāminna mayedaṃ kṛtaṃ khalu |
mayā na manyate nūnaṃ tadidaṃ tvaṃ vicāraya || 23.142{42} ||
yadi dattaṃ mayā dravyamasyā garbhābhiśātanam |
pretībhūtātmajān putrāñjātāṃ jātān divāniśam || 23.143{43} ||
prabhuktvaivaṃ mahadduḥkhe cireyu durgatau sadā |
ityevaṃ śapathaṃ kṛtvā svāminaṃ tamabodhayat || 23.144{44} ||
iti proktaṃ tayā patnyā śrutvā śreṣṭhī sa bodhitaḥ |
(rm 278)
cirāśāparimuktātmā mṛtā pretālaye yāyau || 23.145{45} ||
eṣaiva pretikā śreṣṭhibhāryā durāśayā |
svakṛtakarmabhogyāni bhuktvaivaṃ bhramate'dhunā || 23.146{46} ||
yadīrṣyayā tayā tasyāḥ prakṛtaṃ garbhaśātanam |
tena sā duṣkṛtākārā pretībhūtā caratyapi || 23.147{47} ||
yacca tayā mṛṣāvācā śapathaḥ prakṛtā yathā |
tathā tatkarmapākena durgatau bhramate sadā || 23.148{48} ||
paṃcaputrān divārātriṃ prasūya sā kṣudhānvitā |
sarvāṃstāṃ svātmajān vālānapi bhuktvā na tṛpyate || 23.149{49} ||
evaṃ sā pāpinī pretī jātāṃ jatāṃstathātmajān |
bhuktvā kṣudhāgnisaṃdagadhā bhrametpretālaye sadā || 23.150{50} ||
evaṃ nārada vijñāya viramya pātakātsadā |
mṛṣāvādācca saddharme caritavyaṃ śubhārthibhiḥ || 23.151{51} ||
saddharmasya vipāke hi sarvadā maṃgalaṃ bhave |
pātakasya sadā duḥkhaṃ viśrite sya tathobhayam || 23.152{52} ||
iti matvā sadā yūyaṃ triratnaṃ śaraṇaṃ gatāḥ |
satkṛtya śraddhayā nityaṃ prabhajadhvaṃ samāhitāḥ || 23.153{53} ||
ye buddhaśaraṇaṃ kṛtvā bhajanti śraddhayā mudā |
te sarve durgatiṃ hitvā saṃprayānti jinālaye || 23.154{54} ||
ye ca śṛṇvanti saddharmaṃ mānayanti ca sādaram |
te'pi kleśān vinirjitya saṃyāṃti saugatālaye || 23.155{55} ||
ye saṃghaṃ śaraṇaṃ kṛtvā satkṛtya prabhajanti te |
sarve'pi durgatermuktvā saṃprayānti sukhāvatīm || 23.156{56} ||
evaṃ matvātra saṃsāre sarvadā śubhavāṃchibhiḥ |
triratnabhajanaṃ kṛtvā caritavyaṃ śubhe sadā || 23.157{57} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ munīṃdreṇa śrutvā sa nāradaḥ sudhīḥ |
sasaṃghaḥ sarvadā lokāśca tatheti pratyapadyata || 23.158{58} ||
tataḥ sa nārado bhikṣuḥ kṛtāṃjaliḥ purogataḥ |
bhagavaṃtaṃ praṇatvā ca papracha tadviśodhanam || 23.159{59} ||
bhagavan sā pāpinī pretī tataḥ pretālayātkadā |
tadghorapātakānmuktā saṃyāyātsadgatau katham || 23.160{60} ||
tadupāyaṃ jagacchāsta samupādeṣṭumarhati |
yenāsau durgātermuktā sahasā sadgatiṃ vrajet || 23.161{61} ||
iti saṃprārthite tena nāradena sa sarvavit |
bhagavāṃstaṃ sabhāṃ cāpi samālokyaivamādiśat || 23.162{62} ||
śṛṇu nārada tasyāstatpātakapariśodhanam |
upāyaṃ samayaṃ cāpi pravakṣyāmyadhunā tathā || 23.163{63} ||
yadā lokeśvaro nāthaḥ sarvasatvānukaṃpakaḥ |
bodhisatvo mahāsatvastraidhātukādhipeśvaraḥ || 23.164{64} ||
sukhāvatyā vinirgatya sarvāllokān prabhāsayan |
sarvān satvān samuddhartuṃ pretaloke samācaret || 23.165{65} ||
tadā tatprabhayā spṛṣṭā sātisaukhyasamanvitā |
kimadya me mahatsaukhyamiti proktvābhyaciṃtayat || 23.166{66} ||
(rm 279)
aho saukhyaṃ samutpannaṃ prabhāveyaṃ prasāritā |
nūnamatra mahāsatvaḥ satvānuddhartumāgatāḥ || 23.167{67} ||
yasyedṛksatkṛpādṛṣṭiḥ satveṣvevaṃ prasaryyati |
tasyaiva śaraṇaṃ gatvā bhajeyaṃ sarvadādarāt || 23.168{68} ||
iti dhyātvā viniścitya sā pretī prativismitā |
tasyaivā smaraṇaṃ kṛtvā tiṣṭhe dṛṣṭaṃ samutsukā || 23.169{69} ||
tadā sa bhagavāṃstatra lokanāthaḥ kṛpāmayaḥ |
pretalokān samūddhartumupācaretprābhāsayaḥ || 23.170{70} ||
taṃ prabhāsaṃtamāyātaṃ dṛṣṭvā sarve'pi pretakāḥ |
sahasā samupetyaivaṃ prārthayeyuḥ samādarāt || 23.171{71} ||
svāgataṃ bhagavannātha prasīda parameśvara |
kṛpayāsmān samālokya pretalokān samuddhara || 23.172{72} ||
sarvadā bhavatāmeva śaraṇaṃ saṃvrajāmahe |
tatkṛtvā pariśuddhānnaḥ sarvā preraya sadgatau || 23.173{73} ||
iti tatprārthite sarve lokanāthaḥ kṛpānidhiḥ |
saddharmaṃ samupādiśya punarevamupādiśet || 23.174{74} ||
yadīchatha sadā saukhyaṃ yūyaṃ sarve samāhitāḥ |
triratnaṃ smaraṇaṃ kṛtvā bhajata śraddhayā mudā || 23.175{75} ||
tadaitatpātakairmuktāssarve yūyaṃ pavitritāḥ |
sahasā sadgatiṃ prāptā bhaveta bodhicāriṇaḥ || 23.176{76} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ jagacchāstrā lokanāthena tena te |
sarve'pi pretikāḥ śrutvā tathā kartuṃ samīchire || 23.177{77} ||
tadā te pretikāḥ sarve pariśuddhāśayā mudā |
triratnaṃ śaraṇaṃ kṛtvā smṛtvā bhajeyurānatāḥ || 23.178{78} ||
tadā tatsmṛtipuṇyena sarve te pretikāstataḥ |
pretalokātsamutthāya saṃyāsyanti sukhāvatīm || 23.179{79} ||
tatrāmitābhanāthasya sarve te śaraṇaṃ gatāḥ |
bhajanto dharmamākarṇya cariṣyaṃti śubhācarim || 23.180{80} ||
tataḥ sarve'pi te satvā bodhisatvāḥ śubhaṃkarāḥ |
kramādbodhiṃ samāsādya saṃbuddhapadamāpnuyuḥ || 23.181{81} ||
tadā sā pāpinī pretī sarvapāpavimocitā |
pretalokātsamutthāya sadgatiṃ samavāpsyati || 23.182{82} ||
evaṃ matvātra saṃsāre sadgatiṃ sukhavāṃchibhiḥ |
triratnabhajanaṃ kṛtvā caritavyaṃ sadā śubhe || 23.183{83} ||
triratnabhajanodbhūtaṃ puṇyaṃ na kṣiṇuyātkvacit |
sarvakleśān vinirdahya prāpayetsaugataṃ padam || 23.184{84} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa śrutvā sa nārado yatiḥ |
sasāṃghikasabhālokastatheti prābhyanaṃdata || 23.185{85} ||
iti me guruṇādiṣṭaṃ mayātra vakṣyate |
matvā rājaṃstvamapyevaṃ triratnaṃ bhaja sarvadā || 23.186{86} ||
prajāścāpi tathā dharmaṃ śrāvāyitvā prabodhayan |
bodhimārge pratiṣṭhāpya pālayasva samāhitaḥ || 23.187{87} ||
(rm 280)
tataste sarvadā bhadraṃ sarvatrāpi bhaveddhruvam |
kramādbodhiṃ śivāṃ prāpya saṃbuddhapadamāpnuyāḥ || 23.188{88} ||
iti tenārhatādiṣṭaṃ śrutvāśoko narādhipaḥ |
tathā hīti pratijñāya prābhyanandatsapārṣadaḥ || 23.189{89} ||
ye cedaṃ pretikāyāḥ sugatakathitamidaṃ śraddhayā ye'vadānaṃ śṛṇvaṃti śrāvayanti pramuditamanasaste narāḥ sarva evam |
kṛtvā ratnatrayasya prabhajanamaniśaṃ bodhicittaṃ samāpya kṛtvā lokeṣu bhadraṃ sakalaguṇabharā bauddhaloke prayānti |

++ iti ratnāvadānatatve pretikāyāḥ kathā samāptam ++


(rm 281)
xxivpretībhūtamaharddhikāvadāna
athāśoko mahīpālaḥ kṛtāṃjalipuṭo mudā |
upaguptaṃ yatiṃ natvā prārthayacca tathādarāt || 24.1{1} ||
bhadanta śrotumichāmi punaranyatsubhāṣitam |
tadyathā guruṇādiṣṭaṃ tathā me samupādiśa || 24.2{2} ||
iti saṃprārthite rājñā śrutvā so'rhaṃ sudhīryatiḥ |
nṛpatiṃ taṃ mahīpālaṃ saṃpaśyannevamādiśat || 24.3{3} ||
sādhu śṛṇu mahārāja yathā me guruṇoditam |
tathāhaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi tava dharmapravṛddhaye || 24.4{4} ||
tadyathā bhagavāñchāstā śrīghanaḥ sa munīśvaraḥ |
sarvajñaḥ sugato nātho dharmarāja vināyakaḥ || 24.5{5} ||
bhikṣubhiḥ śrāvakaiḥ sārddhaṃ bhikṣuṇībhiśca cailakaiḥ |
upāsakaiḥ śubhācārairupāsikāgaṇairapi || 24.6{6} ||
bodhisatvairmahāsatvaistathānyairbodhivāṃchibhiḥ |
brahmadevāsurendraiśca yakṣagaṃdharvakiṃnaraiḥ || 24.7{7} ||
siddhairvidyādharaiścāpi nāgendrai garuḍairapi |
sarvai lokādhipaiścāpi tathānyaiśca maharṣibhiḥ || 24.8{8} ||
brāhmaṇai rājabhi rājakumāraiśca śubhārthibhiḥ |
vaiśyai maṃtrigaṇaiścāpi tathāmātyagaṇairapi || 24.9{9} ||
śreṣṭhibhiḥ paurikaiścapi gṛhasthaiśca mahājanaiḥ |
vaṇigbhiḥ sārthavāhaiśca tathā śilpigaṇairapi || 24.10{10} ||
grāmyai jānapadaiścāpi kārpaṭikaiḥ satīrthikaiḥ |
tathānyaiḥ sujanai vijñaiḥ saddharmaguṇavāṃchibhiḥ || 24.11{11} ||
sevitābhyarcitastatra veṇuvane manorame |
karaṃdakanivāpākhye vihāre saugatāśrame || 24.12{12} ||
saddharmaṃ samupādiśya vijahāra prabhāsayan |
sarvasatvahitārthena ratnarāśimivojvalan || 24.13{13} ||
tadaiva samaye tatra rājagṛhe purottame |
āsīdgṛhapate śreṣṭhī mahājano mahādhanaḥ || 24.14{14} ||
śrimāṃ śrīdopamaḥ sarvadravyasaṃpatsamānvitaḥ |
sa sadṛśātkulā bhāryāṃ svakuladharmacāriṇīm || 24.15{15} ||
subhadrāṃgāṃ samānīya vidhinodavahanmudā |
tataḥ sa gṛhabhṛtkantā bhāryayā kāṃtayā tathā || 24.16{16} ||
yathākāmaṃ ratiṃ bhuktvā cakrīḍa paricārayan |
tatastasya gṛhasthasya yathākāmaṃ prabhuṃjataḥ || 24.17{17} ||
samaye sā priyā bhāryā babhūva garbhadhāriṇī || 24.18{18} ||
(rm 282)
tataḥ sā samaye'sūta dārakaṃ suṃdarāṃśikam |
tacchrutvā sa pitā śreṣṭhī mudā draṣṭumupācaran || 24.19{19} ||
tatra sa samupetyāśu dārakaṃ taṃ śubhāṃśikam |
dṛṣṭvā saṃmuditaḥ paśyanneva tasthāvatṛptitaḥ || 24.20{20} ||
tato jñātīn samāhūya kṛtvā jātimahaṃ śiśoḥ |
asya nāmaprasiddhena kriyatāmityabhāṣata || 24.21{21} ||
tacchrutvā jñātayaḥ sarve te kṛtvā samataṃ tathā |
taṃ śreṣṭhinaṃ samāhūya pura evaṃ samabruvan || 24.22{22} ||
yadayaṃ dārako jāto nakṣatra uttarābhidhe |
tenottara iti khyāto bhavatu bhavadātmajaḥ || 24.23{23} ||
iti tairjñātibhiḥ khyātaṃ śrutvā sa gṛhabhṛttathā |
tenaiva nāmadheyena prākhyāpayattamātmajam || 24.24{24} ||
tataḥ sa dārako'ṣṭābhi dhātribhiḥ pratipālitaḥ |
dine dine pravṛddho'bhū hradasthamambujaṃ yathā || 24.25{25} ||
tataḥ pravarddhamānaḥ sa gurubhiḥ śikṣitaḥ kramāt |
lipyādi sarvavidyānāṃ drutaṃ pāraṃ samāyayau || 24.26{26} ||
tadā sa uttaro vijñaḥ savayobhiḥ sahānvitaḥ |
caramānaḥ pure tatra lokamanoharo vabhau || 24.27{27} ||
yadā sa uttaraḥ prauḍhayauvanamadanāśrayaḥ |
tadā tasya pitā śreṣṭhī daivātkālaṃ samāyayau || 24.28{28} ||
tatastasya gṛhe tatra sa uttarastadātmajaḥ |
svāmī saṃpattisaṃprāpto gṛhi lokādhipo'bhavat || 24.29{29} ||
tataḥ sa kulavṛttistho vyavahāravicakṣaṇaḥ |
krayavikrayakāryāṇi kṛtvā dravyaṃ samarjayat || 24.30{30} ||
sa ekasmin dine tatra veṇuvane jināśrame |
saṃbuddhaṃ śrīghanaṃ draṣṭuṃ dharmārthī samupācarat || 24.31{31} ||
tatra sa samupāsṛtyādrākṣīttaṃ śrīghanaṃ munim |
dvātriṃśallakṣaṇāśītivyaṃjanapratimaṃḍitam || 24.32{32} ||
vyāmaprabhāvirocaṃtaṃ samaṃtabhadrarūpiṇam |
ratnarāśimivojvālaṃ ratnādrimiva jaṃgamam || 24.33{33} ||
sabhāmadhyāsanāsīnaṃ bhikṣusaṃghapuraskṛtam |
dharmāmṛtapravarṣantaṃ sarvalokairnamaskṛtam || 24.34{34} ||
dṛṣṭvā sa muditastatra sāṃjaliḥ purato vrajan |
pādau tasya munīndrasya praṇatvaikānta āśrayan || 24.35{35} ||
tatra tasyāśayaṃ śuddhaṃ dṛṣṭvā sa bhagavāṃstathā |
āryasatyaṃ samārabhya saddharmaṃ samupādiśat || 24.36{36} ||
tatsaddharmāmṛtaṃ pītvā sa uttaraḥ pramoditaḥ |
punaḥ pādau munernatvā pratyāyayau svamālayam || 24.37{37} ||
evaṃ sa uttaro vijñaḥ pratidinaṃ jagadguroḥ |
draṣṭuṃ dharmāmṛtaṃ vāpi pātuṃ nityamupācarat || 24.38{38} ||
tathā tasya munīndrasya pītvā dharmāmṛtaṃ tadā |
(rm 283)
saṃbuddhaśāsane tatra pravrajituṃ samaichata || 24.39{39} ||
tataḥ sa uttarastasya munīndrasya purogataḥ |
pādābjau sāṃjalirnatvā pravrajyāṃ samayācata || 24.40{40} ||
bhagavannātha sarvajña bhavatāṃ śaraṇaṃ vraje |
tatpravrajyāṃ vrataṃ mahyaṃ dātumarhati sāṃpratam || 24.41{41} ||
iti saṃprārthite tena bhagavānmunīśvaraḥ |
uttarākhyaṃ kumāraṃ taṃ samālokyaivamādiśat || 24.42{42} ||
kumāra śāsane bauddhe pravrajituṃ yadīchasi |
anujñāṃ māturāsādya prāgacha dāsyate tadā || 24.43{43} ||
ityādiṣṭe munīndreṇa sa uttaraḥ prabodhitaḥ |
tatheti caraṇau tasya munernatvā gṛhaṃ yayau || 24.44{44} ||
tatra gṛhe samāsādya sa uttaraḥ pramoditaḥ |
jananyāścaraṇau natvā sāṃjalirevamabravīt || 24.45{45} ||
mātaradya vihāre'haṃ gachāmi saugatāśrame |
tatra śāstu mukhābjotthamāryadharmāmṛtaṃ pibe || 24.46{46} ||
tatsaddharmāmṛtaṃ pītvā tṛṣṇā me varddhyate punaḥ |
tatsaddharmaṃ samālabdhumichāmi parituṣṭaye || 24.47{47} ||
dhanyāste hi śubhātmānaḥ pariśuddhatrimaṃḍalāḥ |
saṃbuddhaśāsane sthitvā sadā dharmaṃ pibanti ye || 24.48{48} ||
ahamapi sadā tatra saṃbuddhaśāsane caran |
tatsaddharmāmṛtaṃ labdhumichāmi jananī dhruvam || 24.49{49} ||
tatpravrajyāvrataṃ dhṛtvā carituṃ saugatāśrame |
ichāmi sāṃprataṃ mātastadanujñāṃ pradehi me || 24.50{50} ||
iti putrāditaṃ śrutvā matā sā prahatāśayā |
ciraṃ tamātmajaṃ dṛṣṭvā rudantyaivamabhāṣata || 24.51{51} ||
kimeva vadase putra maivaṃ me purato vadaḥ |
kimarthaṃ svagṛhaṃ tyaktvā pravrajitu tvamichasi || 24.52{52} ||
kiṃ te duḥkhaṃ kuto jātaṃ mayā kiṃ cinna dṛśyate |
tvayā tu dṛśyate yattadvaktavyaṃ purato mama || 24.53{53} ||
adyāpi tava tātasya śokādhiḥ śāmyate na me |
tathāpi māṃ kathaṃ tyaktumichasi sāṃprataṃ sute || 24.54{54} ||
iti mātroditaṃ śrutvā sa uttaraḥ kumārakaḥ |
mātaraṃ taṃ samālokya bodhayituṃ samabravīt || 24.55{55} ||
mātastvatkṛpayā me'tra kiṃ cidduḥkhaṃ na vidyate |
gṛhe'pi mahatī saṃpadasti bhogyaṃ yathechayā || 24.56{56} ||
anityaṃ khalu saṃsāraṃ kṣaṇadhvaṃsi śarīrakam |
anityaṃ jīvitaṃ vidyutsaṃpātamiva na dhruvam || 24.57{57} ||
saṃpaccāpi sthirā naivaṃ kasya śrī sarvadā sthirā |
sarveṣāmapi jaṃtūnāṃ sarvatra maraṇaṃ dhruvam || 24.58{58} ||
sarve'pi prāṇinaḥ kāle tyaktvā sarvān parigrahān |
jñātivaṃdhusuhṛtmitrānnapi yāṃti yamālayaiḥ || 24.59{59} ||
(rm 284)
eko'pi vidyate naiva mṛtyuprāpte sahānugaḥ |
dharma eva tadaikaḥ syāttrātānugo yamālaye || 24.60{60} ||
dharmeṇa rakṣyate tatra yamadūtairadhiṣṭhite |
tadatra sarvathā nityaṃ karttavyaṃ dharmameva hi || 24.61{61} ||
dharmavānnatra saṃsāre bhramannapi sadā sukhī |
sarvatra satsukhānyeva prabhuktvā yāti sadgatim || 24.62{62} ||
tatrāpi sarvadā saukhyaṃ bhuktvā kṛtvā ca maṃgalam |
sarvasatvahitārthena saddharmāṇyeva sādhayet || 24.63{63} ||
etatpuṇyavipākaiḥ sa sadguruṃ samupāśrayet |
sadgurostu prasādena bodhicittaṃ labhettadā || 24.64{64} ||
tato bodhicarīṃ prāpya bodhisatvo bhavetsudhīḥ |
tataḥ satvahitārthena bodhicaryāṃ samācaret || 24.65{65} ||
tataḥ pāramitāḥ sarvāḥ paripūrya yathākramam |
trividhāṃ bodhimāsādya saṃbuddhapadamāpnuyāt || 24.66{66} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa sarvajñena hitārthinā |
śrutaṃ mayā parijñāya tathā caritumichyate || 24.67{67} ||
etatpuṇyavibhāgatvamapi prāptuṃ yadīchasi |
tadanujñāṃ pradatvā me cittaṃ mātaḥ pratoṣaya || 24.68{68} ||
iti putroditaṃ śrutvā mātā sā vihatāśayā |
viyogaduḥkhasaṃkārttā vilapantyevamabravīt || 24.69{69} ||
hā putra kathamekānte māṃ vihātuṃ tvamichasi |
nānyo me vidyate putrastvamevaiko'sti naṃdanaḥ || 24.70{70} ||
pitāpi tava māṃ tyajya daivādyāto yamālayam |
tvamapi māṃ parityaktvā kutra gaṃtuṃ tathechasi || 24.71{71} ||
hā putra kathamajño'si kenātra tvaṃ pravaṃcyase |
kiṃ paśyan hetunā kena pravrajituṃ tvamichasi || 24.72{72} ||
hā putra kena mūḍho'si yadetā dravyasaṃpadaḥ |
tyaktvā paragṛhe bhikṣāṃ yācitvā bhoktumichasi || 24.73{73} ||
hā putra tvaṃ kumāro'si tatpravrajyāṃ careḥ katham |
pravrajito gṛhaṃ tyaktvā bahirdeśe sadāśrayeḥ || 24.74{74} ||
tatkathaṃ tyaktvā bahirdeśe samāśrayeḥ |
śāṇakaṃṭāvṛto muṃdo mṛtvātra dhareśvaraḥ || 24.75{75} ||
śītavātātapākrānte kathaṃ duḥkhaṃ saheḥ suta |
kṣutpipāsāgnisaṃtaptaḥ duḥkhaṃ kaste'bhiśāmayet || 24.76{76} ||
śmaśāneṣu śavānyasyan kathamekaścareḥ suta |
jaṃgale nirjane'raṇye ekākī nivaseḥ katham || 24.77{77} ||
tathā bhūtālaye śūnyageheva nivaseḥ katham |
tathāmitreṣu deśeṣu kathamekoriva ccare || 24.78{78} ||
jvarādi rogasaṃtapte kastava paricārayet |
ko vā te kṛpayā dadyātpathyamāhāramauṣadham || 24.79{79} ||
yadi daivādvipattiḥ syāttadā kaḥ pariśodhayet |
iti samīkṣya mā putra pravrajyāvrata utsaha |
(rm 285)
mamātra vacanaṃ śrutvā sukhaṃ bhuktvā gṛhe vasa || 24.80{80} ||
iti mātroditaṃ śrutvā sa putraścaivamabravīt |
daivabhāvā bhavantyeva sarvatrāpi jagatsvapi || 24.81{81} ||
eko'pi vidyate nātra suhṛnmitrasahānugaḥ |
dharma eva sahāyaḥ syātsarvatra maraṇe dhruvam || 24.82{82} ||
iti saddharmasaṃprāptyai pravrajyāvratamuttamam |
caritavyaṃ bhavedvijñairnirvṛtipadavāṃchibhiḥ || 24.83{83} ||
iti tena munīndreṇa samādiṣṭaṃ niśamyate |
māta tasmā tathātraitadvrataṃ caritumichyate || 24.84{84} ||
tadatra saugate dharme māṃ niyoktuṃ yadīchasi |
tadanujñāṃ pradānaṃ me kṛpayā dātumarhati || 24.85{85} ||
iti putrārthitaṃ śrutvā mātā sā gadgadasvarā |
snehaduḥkhāgnisaṃtaptā dṛṣṭvaivaṃ sutamabravīt || 24.86{86} ||
hā putra kathameva tvaṃ nirdayāsyatiniṣṭhuraḥ |
yadvṛddhāṃ jananīṃ dṛṣṭvā kāruṇyaṃ nāsti te hṛdi || 24.87{87} ||
tadatra yadi te putra dayāsti mayi mātari |
yāvajjīvāmyahaṃ tāvadgṛhe vasa mayā saha || 24.88{88} ||
yāvaccaitanmahatsaṃpatsvagṛhe sthirā tava |
tāvadyathepsitaṃ saukhyaṃ bhuktvā ramaṃ samācara || 24.89{89} ||
avaśyaṃ maraṇaṃ yāyāṃ kasya mṛtyurbhave na hi |
tadā mayi mṛtāyāṃ tu pravrajasva yathechayā || 24.90{90} ||
saṃpaccāpi bhavetkṣīṇā kasya saṃpatsadā sthirā |
tadā saṃpatparikṣīṇe bhikṣānnādo vrataṃ cara || 24.91{91} ||
yadyevaṃ prārthite'pi tvaṃ vilaṃghya vacanaṃ mama |
nirdayāniṣṭhurasvāntaḥ pravrajasi haṭhādapi || 24.92{92} ||
tadāhaṃ te purogatvā saugataśāsane'pi hi |
tyaktvāhāraṃ viṣaṃ bhuktvā yāsyāmi maraṇaṃ dhruvam || 24.93{93} ||
iti me vacanaṃ satyaṃ vijñāyeha tvamātmaja |
mayā saha gṛhe saukhyaṃ bhukṣva kutrāpi mā vraja || 24.94{94} ||
iti me vacanaṃ śrutvā yathākāmaṃ sukhaṃ gṛhe |
bhuktvā nityaṃ sadotsāhaiścaraṃ rama svamātmaja || 24.95{95} ||
iti mātroditaṃ śrutvā sa uttaro'bhiśaṃkitaḥ |
tatheti pratibuddhitvā tasthau gehe nirāśitaḥ || 24.96{96} ||
tataścaikadine prātaḥ samutthāya sa uttaraḥ |
saṃbuddhaṃ taṃ muniṃ draṣṭuṃ vihāre samupācarat || 24.97{97} ||
tatra taṃ śrīghanaṃ dṛṣṭvā sa uttaraḥ pramoditaḥ |
natvā pradakṣiṇīkṛtya sāṃjalissamupāśrayat || 24.98{98} ||
tataḥ sa bhagavāṃcchāstā dṛṣṭvā tasya manogatam |
bodhayituṃ samālokya tamevaṃ samupālapat || 24.99{99} ||
kumāra kiṃ viṣaṇṇo'si yanmano'ntargataṃ tava |
tanmamāgre samākhyāhi pūrayāmi yadīhitam || 24.100{100} ||
ityādiṣṭe munīndreṇa sa uttaraḥ samutthitaḥ |
pādau tasya munernatvā sāṃjalirevamabravīt || 24.101{1} ||
(rm 286)
sarvajña bhagavañchāstarvijānīyādbhavānmama |
yanmano'ntargataṃ sarvaṃ yathāpyahaṃ nivedaye || 24.102{2} ||
bhagavannātha sarvajña na me mātā prabodhitā |
tadanujñāṃ na me dātumichati prārthitāpi sā || 24.103{3} ||
tadbhavānme yathā yogyaṃ vrataṃ saṃbodhisādhanam |
anyathāpi pradatvātra prabodhayitumarhati || 24.104{4} ||
iti saṃprārthite tena bhagavān sa munīśvaraḥ |
taṃ viśuddhāśayaṃ dṛṣṭvā samāśvāsyaivamādiśat || 24.105{5} ||
mā kumāra viṣādatvaṃ kṛthā dhairyaṃ samāśraya |
avaśyaṃ samaye prāpte brahmacārī bhaviṣyasi || 24.106{6} ||
tāvadgṛhe sadā dānaṃ kṛtvārthibhyo yathepsitam |
triratnasmaraṇaṃ kṛtvā śubhe cara samāhitaḥ || 24.107{7} ||
etatpuṇyavipākena samaye samupāgate |
pravrajyāvratamāsādya nirvāṇapadamāpsyasi || 24.108{8} ||
iti matvā kumārātra yāvajjīvati sā prasūḥ |
tāvadgṛhe sadā dānaṃ kṛtvā śubhe caran vasa || 24.109{6} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa śrutvā sa uttaraḥ sudhīḥ |
tatheti prativijñapya prasasāda prabodhitaḥ || 24.110{10} ||
tataḥ sa uttaro natvā sāṃjalistaṃ munīśvaram |
suprasannamukhāmbhojastanmanāḥ svagṛhaṃ yayau || 24.111{11} ||
tatra sa svagṛhe sthitvā svakulavṛttisādhanaiḥ |
pratisāryāpaṇaṃ tatra cakāra krayavikrayam || 24.112{12} ||
tatra mulādhikaṃ lābhaṃ yallabdhaṃ gaṇavandhanam |
tatsarvaṃ sa sutastasyā māturhaste samarpayat || 24.113{13} ||
tataḥ sa uttaraḥ putro mātaraṃ tāṃ prasādayan |
tallabdhaṃ sakalaṃ dravyaṃ datvaivaṃ pratyabodhayat || 24.114{14} ||
śṛṇu mātarmamānena dravyena svajanāṃ janān |
arthinaḥ kṛpaṇān viprānmānayitvābhitoṣaya || 24.115{15} ||
iti putroditaṃ śrutvā mātā sā atikuṃcikā |
lubdhā matsarākrāṃtahṛdayaivamabhāṣata || 24.116{16} ||
putra pitrā prayatnena saṃpadaste samarjitāḥ |
tvametāḥ saṃpadaḥ sarvā datvārthibhyo vyayiṣyasi || 24.117{17} ||
yāvatpitrārjitaṃ dravyaṃ tāvanmayā na dāsyate |
tāvatmātraṃ tavādhīnaṃ yāvattvayā samarjitam || 24.118{18} ||
yāvajjivāmyahaṃ tāta na te dāsyāmi kiṃ cana |
mṛtāyāṃ mayi tatsarvaṃ tavādhīnaṃ bhavettadā || 24.119{19} ||
ityuktvā sā prasūstasya mātsaryyakaluṣāśayā |
sarvadravyaṃ prayatnena guptīkṛtyātyarakṣata || 24.120{20} ||
kiṃ cidarthibhyo sā kudhī dadau |
guptīkṛtya prayatnena taddravyaṃ samagopayat || 24.121{21} ||
dṛṣṭvā ca sārthinaḥ sarvān svagṛhaṃ samupāgatān |
sahasā samupāśritya paribhāṣyābhyanindayat || 24.122{22} ||
(rm 287)
are māyā na gehe'smiṃ yūyaṃ pretā ivāgatāḥ |
nityaṃ paragṛhe piṃḍaṃ bhoktuṃ bhramatha bhūtavat || 24.123{23} ||
evaṃ tānarthinaḥ sarvān sā nārī duritāśayā |
naikadhā vipralāpena paribhāṣyābhyaniṃdayat || 24.124{24} ||
evaṃ mātrā pratikruṣya vipralāpābhiniṃditān |
śrutvā sa uttaraḥ putro mātaraṃ tāmabhāṣata || 24.125{25} ||
are māta kimeva tvamarthinaḥ paribhāṣase |
dhiktvāṃ dharmaparibhraṣṭāṃ kathaṃ na narake pateḥ || 24.126{26} ||
yadi tvaṃ vāṃchase saukhyamihāmutrāpi sarvadā |
kṛpaṇānarthinaḥ sarvān saṃpradānaiḥ pramodaya || 24.127{27} ||
yadyevaṃ kriyate mātaḥ saṃgatiḥ sarvadāvayoḥ |
anyathā cettato nau syādvibhinnabhojanāśrayaḥ || 24.128{28} ||
yadyasti te mayā sārddhaṃ sarvadā bhojane ratiḥ |
yathepsitaṃ sadārthibhyo datvā cara śubhe vṛṣe || 24.129{29} ||
iti tenātmajenāpi pratyākhyataṃ niśamya sā |
mātā lajjāvibhinnāsyā tamātmajamabhāṣata || 24.130{30} ||
yadyevaṃ nandanārthibhyaḥ sarvasvamapi ditsasi |
tatsvadravyāṇi sarvāṇi dāsyāmi tadyathepsitam || 24.131{31} ||
ityuktvā sā prasū nārī tamātmajaṃ mṛṣāgirā |
bodhayitvā gṛhe tāni dravyāṇi samagopayet || 24.132{32} ||
tataḥ sā pramadā duṣṭā mithyāvācā tamātmajam |
adya yato'rthino bhikṣūnstoṣayāmītyabodhayat || 24.133{33} ||
evaṃ mithyayā vācā bodhayantī tamātmajam |
svayameva prabhuktvā ca taddravyaṃ sarvaṃ gopayat || 24.134{34} ||
kasmai cidarthine kiṃ cidapi sā na dadau kudhīḥ |
sā kebhyo'pi valiṃ dātuṃ naivotsehe kadā cana || 24.135{35} ||
evaṃ sā durmatirlubdhā matsarākrāntacetasā |
paribhāṣyārthinaḥ sarvānniṃdayantyaśubhe'carat || 24.136{36} ||
evaṃ kevalapāpāni prācitvā sā durāśayā |
kāle nirāśayā duḥkhārttā mṛtā pretālaye yayau || 24.137{37} ||
tatra pretagatiprāptā pretībhūtā durākṛtiḥ |
svakeśapratichannāṃgā śūcīmukhā kṛśāṃgikā || 24.138{38} ||
asthiyaṃtravaducchrāyā parvatasaṃnibhodarā |
kṣutpipāsāgnisaṃtaptā dagdhasthūṇāsamāṃgikā || 24.139{39} ||
ārttasvarā virāvantī kraṃdantī sā samaṃtataḥ |
annapāna hi mārgantī paryabhramaditastataḥ || 24.140{40} ||
tataḥ sa uttaro mātuḥ kṛtvāgnisaṃskṛtiṃ tadā |
snānaśaucādi karmāṃ kurvañchokaṃ vyanodayat || 24.141{41} ||
tataḥ sa uttaro vijo mātṛśokaṃ vyanodayat |
arthibhyo vāṃchitaṃ datvā bahupuṇyāṇyupārjayat || 24.142{42} ||
tataḥ kāmaviraktaḥ sa saṃsārabhoganispṛhaḥ |
(rm 288)
nirvṛtipadasaṃprātyai pravrajituṃ samaichata || 24.143{43} ||
tataḥ sa mudito gatvā veṇuvane jināśrame |
dṛṣṭvā taṃ śrīghanaṃ natvā sāṃjaliḥ samupācarat || 24.144{44} ||
tatra pradakṣiṇīkṛtvā sasaṃghaṃ taṃ munīśvaram |
praṇatvā purataḥ sthitvā sāṃjalirevamabravīt || 24.145{45} ||
bhagavannātha sarvajña vijānīyādbhavānapi |
yanme sā jananī vighnā sāṃprataṃ pralayaṃ gatā || 24.146{46} ||
tadatra bhagavacchāstarbhavatāṃ śaraṇaṃ vraje |
tanme'nugrahamādhāya pravrajyāṃ dātumarhati || 24.147{47} ||
iti saṃprarthite tena bhagavān sa munīśvaraḥ |
savyakareṇa tanmūrddhni saṃspṛśyaivaṃ samādiśat || 24.148{48} ||
ehi cara kumārātra brahmacaryaṃ samāhitaḥ |
ityuktvā bhagavāṃ chāstā sāṃghike taṃ samagrahīt || 24.149{49} ||
ehītyukte munīndreṇa muṃḍito'bhūtsa uttaraḥ |
khikkhirīpātrabhṛcchuddhakāṣāyacīvarāvṛtaḥ || 24.150{50} ||
tataḥ śāstuḥ prasādātsa pariśuddhastrimaṃḍalaḥ |
niḥkleśayaviśuddhātmā saṃsāraviratotsavaḥ || 24.151{51} ||
samādhidhāraṇīvidyāghaṭamāno vicakṣaṇaḥ |
hitvāvidyāgaṇaṃ sarvavidyāguṇāntamāptavān || 24.152{52} ||
viditvā sarvasaṃsāragatiṃ vidyutkṣaṇasthitim |
matvā ca sarvasaṃskāragatir'nekavighātinim || 24.153{53} ||
sarvakleśagaṇāṃ jitvā sarvānmāragaṇānapi |
sākṣādarhatvamāsādya śrāvakabodhimāptavān || 24.154{54} ||
tataḥ sa suviśuddhātmā vītarāgo jiteṃdriyaḥ |
ākāśanirmalasvāntaḥ sarvaloṣṭakakāṃcanaḥ || 24.155{55} ||
vāsīcaṃdanakalpāṃśo nirvikalpo niraṃjanaḥ |
sarvasatvahitādhānaḥ saṃbuddhaguṇasādhakaḥ || 24.156{56} ||
sadevāsuralokānāṃ sarveṣāṃ prāṇināmapi |
vaṃdyaḥ pūjyo'bhimānyaśca brahmacārī babhūva saḥ || 24.157{57} ||
tataḥ so'rhanmahābhijño gaṃgātire śubhasthale |
parṇakuṭyāṃ samāśritya tasthau dhyānasamāhitaḥ || 24.158{58} ||
tatraikasmin dine tasya mātā sā pretikā satī |
nagna svaromasaṃchannā daghasthūṇāvivarṇitā || 24.159{59} ||
sūcīmukhāsthiyaṃtreva parvatasannibhodarā |
kṣutpipāsāgnidagdhāṃgā pānāhāragaveṣinī || 24.160{60} ||
ārtasvaraṃ virāvantī kraṃdantī samupāgatā |
āyuṣmantaṃ tamarhantaṃ paśyantī samupāśrayat || 24.161{61} ||
evaṃ tāṃ samupāsīnāṃ dṛṣṭvā sa uttaro yatiḥ |
kā tvamevaṃvidhāyātā vadasvetyanvapṛchata || 24.162{62} ||
iti tenoditaṃ śrutvā sā pretī purataḥ sthitā |
uttaraṃ taṃ yatiṃ putraṃ samālokyaivamabravīt || 24.163{63} ||
ahaṃ te jananī snigdhā yayāsi janitaḥ sutaḥ |
annapānaviviktāsmi pretībhūtā'dhunā care || 24.164{64} ||
paṃcaviṃśati varsāṇi yāṃti kālagatā hyaham |
(rm 289)
nābhijānāmi pāṇīyaṃ kuto bhaktasya darśanam || 24.165{65} ||
saphalāṃ puṣpitāṃ vṛkṣaṃ dṛṣṭvā gachāmi dūrataḥ |
sarve'pi niṣphalā śuṣkā vipuṣpāśca bhavanti te || 24.166{66} ||
sarāṃsi cāmbupūrṇāṇi dṛṣṭvā gachāmi dūrataḥ |
tāni sarvāṇi śuṣkāni bhavanti tatkṣaṇādapi || 24.167{67} ||
bhedaṃ tasya mahāsaukhyaṃ vṛkṣamūlaśritasya te |
vidyate sarvadāpyatra kiṃ tvayā prakṛtaṃ śubham || 24.168{68} ||
tadatra kṛpayā paśyan kāruṇyaṃ janayan suta |
mātre tṛṣārditāyai me śītāmbu dātumarhasi || 24.169{69} ||
iti tayārthitaṃ śrutvā sa uttaraḥ savismayaḥ |
tāṃ pretīṃ suciraṃ dṛṣṭvā śaṃkitaścaivamabravīt || 24.170{70} ||
mātastarhi kṣaṇaṃ prāpte tvayā pāruṣyabhūtayā |
dānāni na kṛtānyeva puṇyāni vā citāni na || 24.171{71} ||
anyakṛtāni dānāni puṇyāni bhāṣitāni ca |
dṛṣṭvā śrutvāpi te citte krodhāgnirjvalitastadā || 24.172{72} ||
kiṃ cinnāpi śubhaṃ karma kṛtvāpuṇyaṃ tvayārjitam |
pareṇāpi kṛtaṃ dṛṣṭvā śrutvāpi nānumoditam || 24.173{73} ||
etatkarmavipākena jananī tvaṃ mṛtādhunā |
pretībhūtānnapānena viyuktā bhramase bhave || 24.174{74} ||
idāniṃ kiṃ kariṣyāmi tvaṃ cātra kiṃ kariṣyasi |
tadā sarvāhitaṃ proktaṃ tvayā tatra śrutaṃ na tat || 24.175{75} ||
iti tenātmajenoktaṃ śrutvā sā pratibodhitā |
vigaladaśrurukṣākṣā taṃ putramevamabravīt || 24.176{76} ||
na mayā hi kṛtaṃ dānaṃ puṇyaṃ na cābhisādhitam |
dṛṣṭvānumoditaṃ nāpi mātsaryaparibhūtayā || 24.177{77} ||
yadyaddravyaṃ tvayā putra mama haste samarpitam |
tattatsarvaṃ mayā vaṃdhikhadāyāṃ parigopitam || 24.178{78} ||
tadidāniṃ samuddhṛtya jñātihaste samarpaya |
tena taṃ sugataṃ buddhaṃ sasaṃghaṃ bhojayātmaja || 24.179{79} ||
dakṣiṇādeśanāṃ cāpi mama nāmnābhikāraya |
tadā pretagatermuktā yāyāmahaṃ surālayam || 24.180{80} ||
iti tayoditaṃ śrutvā sa uttaro'nubodhitaḥ |
evamastviti saṃśrutya punastāmevamabravīt || 24.181{81} ||
sasaṃghaṃ sugataṃ tena bhojayiṣyāmi sāṃpratam |
kiṃ tu tvayāmva tatrāpi sthātavyaṃ sugatāṃtike || 24.182{82} ||
iti tenoditaṃ śrutvā sā pretī lajjayānvitā |
tamarhantaṃ samālokya pura evamabhāṣata || 24.183{83} ||
yadahaṃ putra nagnāsmi pretī vibhransitākṛtī |
tatkathaṃ samupāśritya sthāsyāmi śrīghanāṃtike || 24.184{84} ||
iti tayoditaṃ śrutvā sa uttaro mahāmatiḥ |
tāṃ pretīṃ samupālokya punarevamabhāṣata || 24.185{85} ||
kriyamāne yadā pāpe lajjā nāsti tadā tava |
phalabhojanakāle'tra kimevaṃ lajjase'dhunā || 24.186{86} ||
iti tadvacanaṃ śrutvā sā pretī pratibodhitā |
(rm 290)
tathā gamiṣyāmīti proktva tvaritaṃ vijñālayaṃ yayau || 24.187{87} ||
tataḥ sa uttaro gatvā jñātīnāhūya tadvṛtim |
yathāmātroditaṃ khyāya sarvameva nyavedayat || 24.188{88} ||
tacchrutvā jñātivargāste sarva'tivismayānvitāḥ |
tathā taddravyamādāya sarvaṃ tasyānupāharan || 24.189{89} ||
taddravyaṃ sarvamālokya sa uttaraḥ pramoditaḥ |
sarveṣāmapi gotrāṇāṃ punarevaṃ samabravīt || 24.190{90} ||
bhavanto jñātayaḥ sarve dravyenaitena sāṃpratam |
sasāṃghikaṃ munīndraṃ taṃ bhojayiṣyāmi pūjayan || 24.191{91} ||
tadetatsarvamādāya yadā prakaṃpitāśayāḥ |
saṃghabhojyārhasāmagrīṃ sādhayitvā pradatta me || 24.192{92} ||
iti saṃprārthitaṃ śrutvā sarve te jñātayastathā |
saṃghabhojyārhasāmagrīṃ sarvadravyairasādhayat || 24.193{93} ||
taddṛṣṭvā muditaḥ so'rhanuttaraḥ saṃprasannadhīḥ |
śāsturnimaṃtraṇaṃ kartuṃ vihāre samupācarat || 24.194{94} ||
tatra sa samupāśritya taṃ munīndraṃ sasāṃghikaiḥ |
praṇatvā sāñjalistatra puraḥ sthitvā nyamaṃtrayat || 24.195{95} ||
bhagavannātha sarvajña bhagavaṃtaṃ sasāṃghikam |
pūjayituṃ samichāmi śvo'nugṛhītumarhati || 24.196{96} ||
iti saṃprārthite tena bhagavān sa munīśvaraḥ |
tamuttaraṃ samālokya tūṣṇībhūtvādhyuvāsa tat || 24.197{97} ||
tataḥ sa uttaro matvā bhagavatādhivāsitam |
taṃ munīndraṃ punarnatvā jñātīnāṃ purato yayau || 24.198{98} ||
tatra sa uttarasteṣāṃ ca nimaṃtraṇam |
kṛtvā tatra punasteṣāṃ pura evamabhāṣata || 24.199{99} ||
saṃghabhojye pravṛtte'tra yūyaṃ sarve samāgatāḥ |
pretībhūtāṃ mamāmbāṃ tāmavalokitumarhatha || 24.200{100} ||
ityuktvā svāśramaṃ gatvā sa uttaraḥ prasāditaḥ |
dhyānāgārasamāsīnastasthau dhyānasamāhitaḥ || 24.201{1} ||
tataḥ prātaḥ samutthāya jñātīnāṃ purato gataḥ |
pūjopacārasāmagrīṃ sahasā samasādhayat || 24.202{2} ||
tataḥ sa uttaraḥ prāhne vihāre samupāsaran |
bhagavaṃtaṃ munīndraṃ taṃ praṇatvaivamabhāṣata || 24.203{3} ||
bhagavan sarvavicchāstaḥ samayo varttate'dhunā |
tatsasaṃgho bhavānatra vijayituṃ samarhati || 24.204{4} ||
iti saṃprārthite tena gaṃḍīmākoṭayanmuniḥ |
tacchabdacoditāstatra sa ca saṃghā upāsaran || 24.205{5} ||
tatra te jñātayaḥ sarve lokā anye'pi harṣitāḥ |
tatra pretāṃ samāyātāṃ tāṃ draṣṭuṃ samupāgatāḥ || 24.206{6} ||
tadā sa bhagavāṃstatra sasāṃghikaḥ samāgatāḥ |
taddattaṃ pādyamādāya śuddhāsane samāśrayat || 24.207{7} ||
(rm 291)
tāṃ dṛṣṭvā jñātayaḥ sarve lokā anye'pi kheṭitāḥ |
hā pāpamiti bhāṣante tasthuḥ saddharmavāṃchayā || 24.208{8} ||
tadā te jñātayaḥ sarve bhagavaṃtaṃ sasāṃghikam |
yathākramaṃ samabhyarcya bhojanaiḥ samatoṣayat || 24.209{9} ||
tataste sāṃghikāḥ sarve saṃbuddhapramukhā api |
tatpraṇītarasaṃ bhojyaṃ bhuktvā tṛptiṃ samāyayuḥ || 24.210{10} ||
tato'panīya pātrāṇi śodhayitvā bhujādikam |
teṣāṃ pūgādi tāmbūlagaṇaṃ te pradadurmudā || 24.211{11} ||
tatra sa uttaro bhikṣurjñātibhistai sahānvitaḥ |
bhagavaṃtaṃ sasaṃghaṃ taṃ praṇatvā samupāśrayat || 24.212{12} ||
tatra sa bhagavān dṛṣṭvā tamuttaramupāśritam |
dākṣiṇādeśanāṃ tasyāḥ pretyā nāmnā samādiśat || 24.213{13} ||
ito dānāddhi yatpuṇyaṃ tatpretīmupagachatu |
uttiṣṭhatāmiyaṃ pretī pretalokāttato drutam || 24.214{14} ||
iti śubhāśiṣaṃ datvā bhagavān sa munīśvaraḥ |
āryyasatyaṃ samārabhya saddharmaṃ ca samādiśat || 24.215{15} ||
tadāryyadharmamākarṇya sarve lokāḥ prabodhitāḥ |
dharmaviśeṣamājñāya babhūvuḥ satyadarśinaḥ || 24.216{16} ||
sāpi pretī samānīya tatsaddharmāmṛtaṃ mudā |
suprasannāśayā smṛtvā triratnaṃ śaraṇaṃ yayau || 24.217{17} ||
tataḥ sā bhūtaloke'bhūjjātā bhūtamaharddhikā |
tatrāpi sābhavadduṣṭā mātsaryyarukṣitāśayā || 24.218{18} ||
tataḥ sa bhagavāṃstasmātsasāṃghikaḥ prabhāsayan |
gatvā svāśramamāśritya tasthau dharmamupādiśan || 24.219{19} ||
tataḥ sa buddhimān vijña āyuṣmān yatiruttaraḥ |
kutrāsau jāyate mātādhuneti samapaśyata || 24.220{20} ||
evaṃ sa uttaro vijñaḥ samabhīkṣya samaṃtataḥ |
dadarśa tāṃ saṃprajātāṃ bhūtamaharddhikālaye || 24.221{21} ||
dṛṣṭvā sa uttaraḥ putro mātaraṃ tāṃ ca bhautikām |
sahasā samupāśritya samāmaṃtryaivamabravīt || 24.222{22} ||
kimambāsti tvayā śaktirapi dāneṣu sāṃpratam |
yadīchasi sadā bhadraṃ kuru dānamihādhunā || 24.223{23} ||
iti tenoditaṃ śrutvā sā ca bhūtamaharddhikā |
uttaraṃ taṃ sutaṃ dṛṣṭvā punarevamabhāṣata || 24.224{24} ||
nādyāpi vidyate dāne'bhilāṣaṃ mama naṃdana |
tatkiṃ cidapi vā dravyaṃ pradātuṃ nāhamutsahe || 24.225{25} ||
iti tenoditaṃ śrutvāyuṣmān sa uttaro yatiḥ |
dṛṣṭvā tāṃ mātaraṃ bhūtamaharddhikāmabhāṣata || 24.226{26} ||
adyāpi te tiṣṭhati taccharīraṃ vivṛddhanirmānsatvagasthicarmā lobhāṃdhakārāvṛtalocanāyā nivartitaṃ yattava pretaloke |
dhiktvāṃ praduṣṭāmiha kiṃ vadeyamadyāpi yattvaṃ duritāśayāsi |
(rm 292)
kenātra te pāpanimagnacittaṃ saṃśodhitaṃ puṇyakaroddhṛtaṃ syāt || 24.227{27} ||
sadāpi caivaṃ narake nivāsā bhavetkadā tvaṃ parimokṣyase'taḥ |
aho hi durbuddhidurāśayānāṃ mano na kenāpi viśuddhyate vai || 24.228{28} ||
evaṃ bahuprakāreṇa paribhāṣyottaraḥ sa tām |
tadaṅgātyamalīmekāmākṛṣyāpaharadvalāt || 24.229{29} ||
tatastāṃ yamalīṃ so'rhaṃ saṃghāya samakalpayat |
tatra mūlyena tāṃ krītvā bhikṣumānavake nyaset || 24.230{30} ||
tatrāpi niśi sā gatvā yamalīṃ tāmupāharat |
tathāpahṛtāṃ bhikṣuruttarāya nyavedayat || 24.231{31} ||
tacchrutvā cottaro gatvā paribhāṣya valena tām |
apahṛtya punastasmai bhikṣave samakalpayat || 24.232{32} ||
bhūyo'pi niśi sā gatvā yamalīṃ tāmupāharat |
tathā sa uttaraścāpi tasyāśca tāmapāharat || 24.233{33} ||
evaṃ tridhā niśāyāṃ sā tatra gatvāharacca tām |
uttaraścāpahṛtyaiva tāṃ tasmai bhikṣave dadau || 24.234{34} ||
tenāpi bhikṣuṇādāya catudiksāṃghikāya sā |
pādyalepānikāyāṃ saṃsīvitvā pratipāditā || 24.235{35} ||
tatra tāṃ sīvitāṃ dṛṣṭvā sā ca bhūtamaharddhitā |
apahṛtavibhagnāśā rudantī svālayaṃ yayau || 24.236{36} ||
taccittāgniprataptā sā smṛtvā taṃ sutamuttaram |
kālaṃ gatā tato'nyatra yakṣaloke'labhajjanuḥ || 24.237{37} ||
evaṃ matvā mahatpāpamūlaṃ mātsaryyameva hi |
tatmātsaryaṃ parityajya karttavyaṃ dānamādarāt || 24.238{38} ||
dānena śuddhyate cittaḥ śuddhacittaḥ sudhīkṛtiḥ |
kramādbodhicariṃ dhṛtvā saṃbuddhapadamāpnuyāt || 24.239{39} ||
evaṃ vijñāya sarvatra yadīchanti sadā śubham |
mātsaryaṃ viṣavattyaktvā caritavyaṃ sadā śubhe || 24.240{40} ||
iti me guruṇākhyātaṃ śrutaṃ tathā mayocyate |
tvaṃ cāpyevaṃ parijñāya caritavyaṃ śubhe sadā || 24.241{41} ||
prajāścāpi mahārāja bodhayitvā prayatnataḥ |
bodhimārge pratiṣṭhāpya pālayituṃ sadārhasi || 24.242{42} ||
tathā te maṃgalaṃ nityaṃ sarvatrāpi bhaveddhruvam |
kramādbodhiṃ samāsādya saṃbuddhapadamāpnuyāḥ || 24.243{43} ||
iti tenārhatākhyātaṃ śrutvāśokaḥ sa bhūpatiḥ |
tatheti pratibhāṣitvā prābhyanaṃdatsapārṣadaḥ || 24.244{44} ||
idaṃ narā ye muditāśca bhūtamaharddhikāyānuvadānamatra |
mudā niśamya pratimodayante prayāṃti te śrīghanasaṃnivāse || 24.245{45} ||

++ iti ratnāvadānatve pretībhūtamaharddhikāvadānaṃ samāptam ++


(rm 293)
xxvdūtāvadāna
athāśoko mahīpālaḥ kṛtāṃjalipuṭo mudā |
upaguptaṃ guruṃ natvā punarevamavocat || 25.1{1} ||
bhadanta śrotumichāmi punaranyatsubhāṣitam |
tadyathā guruṇākhyātaṃ tathādeṣṭuṃ ca me'rhati || 25.2{2} ||
iti saṃprārthitaṃ rājñā śrutvā so'rhanmahāmatiḥ |
upagupto nareṃdraṃ taṃ samāmaṃtryaivamādiśat || 25.3{3} ||
śṛṇu rājanmahābhāga yathā me gurubhāṣitam |
tathāhaṃ te pravakṣyāmi śrutvā cātrānumodaya || 25.4{4} ||
tadyathā bhagavān buddhaḥ śrīghanaḥ sa munīśvaraḥ |
sarvajñaḥ sugataḥ śāstā dharmarājo vināyakaḥ || 25.5{5} ||
bhikṣubhiḥ śrāvakaiḥ sārddhaṃ bhikṣuṇībhiśca celakaiḥ |
upāsakagaṇaiścāpi tathā copāsikāgaṇaiḥ || 25.6{6} ||
rājagṛhapuropānte gṛddhakūṭe nagottame |
veṇuvane mahodyāne karaṃdakanivāpake || 25.7{7} ||
sarvasatvahitārthena bodhicaryāṃ prakāśayan |
saddharmaṃ samupādiśya vijahāra prabhāsayan || 25.8{8} ||
tatsaddharmāmṛtaṃ pātuṃ sarve lokā upāgatāḥ |
devā daityāśca nāgāśca yakṣagaṃdharvakinnarāḥ || 25.9{9} ||
grahā vidyādharāḥ siddhā rākṣasā garuḍā api |
lokapālagaṇāścāpi tīrthikāśca maharṣayaḥ || 25.10{10} ||
brāhmaṇāḥ kṣatriyā bhūpā vaiśyāśca māṃtriṇo'pi ca |
amātyāḥ śreṣṭhinaścāpi gṛhasthāśca mahājanāḥ || 25.11{11} ||
vaṇijaḥ sārthavāhāśca śilpinaḥ paurikāstathā |
grāmyā jānapadāścāpi tathā kārpaṭikā api || 25.12{12} ||
tathānye'pi janāścaivaṃ sarve tatra samāgatāḥ |
dṛṣṭvā taṃ śrīghanaṃ natvā samabhyarcya yathākramam || 25.13{13} ||
kṛtāṃjalipuṭāḥ sarve parivṛtyopatasthire |
tataḥ sa bhagavāṃ dṛṣṭvā sarvāṃstāṃ samupasthitān |
ādimadhyāṃtakalyāṇaṃ saddharmaṃ samupādiśat || 25.14{14} ||
tatsaddharmāmṛtaṃ pītvā sarve lokāḥ prabodhitāḥ |
dharmavaiśeṣamājñāya babhūvurbodhisādhinaḥ || 25.15{15} ||
evaṃ sa bhagavāṃstatra nityaṃ saddharmamādiśan |
varṣāsu vyaharallokahitaṃ kṛtvā sasāṃghikaḥ || 25.16{16} ||
tasmiṃstu samaye tatra śrāvastyāṃ puri sattamaḥ |
anāthapiṃḍadaḥ śreṣṭhī gṛhapatirmahājanaḥ || 25.17{17} ||
triratnabhaktimān dātā saṃbuddhadarśanotsukaḥ |
prasenajinmahīpālaṃ praṇatvā prārthayattathā || 25.18{18} ||
jaya deva mahāraja jānīyāttadbhavānapi |
(rm 294)
yadasmābhi munīndro'sau suciraṃ nābhidṛśyate || 25.19{19} ||
tattasmātra munīndrasya darśane tṛṣitā vayam |
bhagavaṃtaṃ munīndraṃ taṃ draṣṭumichāmahe'dhunā || 25.20{20} ||
tadbhavān samupāmaṃtrya bhagavaṃtaṃ jagadgurum |
atra lokahitārthāya vijayituṃ samarhati || 25.21{21} ||
iti saṃprārthitaṃ tena gṛhādhipatinā sadā |
śrutvā sa bhūpatī rājā taṃ gṛhapatimabravīt || 25.22{22} ||
kiṃ tvayā śrūyate sādho bhagavān sa munīśvaraḥ |
etarhi tiṣṭhate kutra varṣāsu saha sāṃghikaiḥ || 25.23{22!} ||
iti rājñoditaṃ śrutvā so'nāthapiṇḍado gṛhī |
taṃ nareṃdraṃ samālokya punarevamabhāṣata || 25.24{23} ||
śrutaṃ mayā mahārāja bhagavān sa sasāṃghikam |
rājagṛhe'dhunā prāvṛtsamaye'pyavatiṣṭhate || 25.25{24} ||
iti tena samākhyātaṃ śrutvā sa nṛpatistataḥ |
dūtamekaṃ samāhūya pura evamupālapat || 25.26{25} ||
ehi tvaṃ bho mahāsādho śrutvā me vacanaṃ drutam |
tatra rājagṛhe gatvā bhagavata upācara || 25.27{26} ||
asmākaṃ vacanaistasya śāstuḥ pādāmbuje namaḥ |
natvā dehādi sarvatra kauśalyaṃ paripṛcha ca || 25.28{27} ||
evaṃ cāpi munestasya puro vijñāpayādarāt |
kauśalye nṛpatī rājā bhagavaṃtaṃ draṣṭumichati || 25.29{28} ||
tathā sarve'pi lokāśca śrāvastyāyāśca paurikāḥ |
triratnadarśanaṃ kartumabhivāṃchanti sāṃpratam || 25.30{29} ||
bhavaddharmāmṛtaṃ cāpi pātumichaṃti sarvavit |
tatsasaṃgho bhavāṃstatra kṛpayāgaṃtumarhati || 25.31{30} ||
iti rajñoditaṃ śrutvā tatheti pratimoditaḥ |
taṃ nṛpaṃ sāṃjalirnatvā drutaṃ rājagṛhaṃ yayau || 25.32{31} ||
tatra rājagṛhe gatvā sa dūtaḥ pratinaṃditaḥ |
vilokya suprasannātmā gṛdhrakūṭe upācarat || 25.33{32} ||
tatra sa dūrato dṛṣṭvā bhagavaṃtaṃ pramoditaḥ |
upetya sāṃjalirnatvā prārthayadevamādarāt || 25.34{33} ||
bhagavannātha sarvajña vaṃde te caraṇāmbuje |
yadarthe'hamihāyāmi tajjānīyādbhavāṃ jinaḥ || 25.35{34} ||
tathāpyahaṃ jagacchāstuḥ puro vijñāpayāmi tat |
bhagava sarvavidrājā prasenajitsukauśalaḥ || 25.36{35} ||
bhavatāṃ vaṃdate pādau kuśalaṃ cāpi pṛchate |
tathā ca bhagavan sarve śrāvastyeyāśca paurikāḥ || 25.37{36} ||
vaṃdaṃte bhavatāṃ pādau pṛchaṃte cāpi kauśalam |
nṛpādi sarvalokāśca bhavatāṃ darśanotsukāḥ || 25.38{37} ||
triratnaṃ draṣṭumichāma ityevaṃ pravadanti te |
bhavaddharmāmṛtaṃ cāpi pātumichaṃti te'dhunā || 25.39{38} ||
nṛpatipramukhāḥ sarve tṛṣṇārditā ivāmṛtam |
tatsādhu bhagavāṃstatra śrāvastyāṃ puri sāṃpratam |
(rm 295)
anukampāmupādāya vijayatāṃ sasāṃghikaḥ || 25.40{39} ||
ityukte tena dūtena bhagavān sa munīśvaraḥ |
taṃ dūtaṃ puruṣaṃ dṛṣṭvā samāmaṃtryevamādiśat || 25.41{40} ||
bho puruṣa mahārājo bimbisāro nṛpādhipaḥ |
yadyanujñāṃ dadātyatra tadā yāsyāmyahaṃ dhruvam || 25.42{41} ||
tatsādhu nṛpatestasya bimbisārasya bhūbhṛtaḥ |
anujñāprārthanāṃ kṛtvā gachāyāsyāmyahaṃ tadā || 25.43{42} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa śrutvā sa dūta ādarāt |
tatheti taṃ muniṃ natvā rājño'ntikamupācarat || 25.44{43} ||
tatra sa samupāśritya bimbisāraṃ narādhipam |
sāṃjaliḥ purato gatvā praṇatvaivaṃ nyavedayat || 25.45{44} ||
jaya deva mahārāja prasīdānugrahaṃ kuru |
prasenajinmahībharturdūto'haṃ samupāgataḥ || 25.46{45} ||
mahārāja mahīpāla prasenajitsa kauśalaḥ |
bhagavato munīndrasya darśanamabhivāṃchati || 25.47{46} ||
tathā sarve'pi lokāśca śrāvastryeyāḥ sapaurikāḥ |
triratnasya jagadbhartuḥ praṣṭumichanti sāṃpratam || 25.48{47} ||
tadasya trijagacchāsturbhagavato bhavān prabho |
tatrānugamane'nujñāṃ kṛpayā dātumarhati || 25.49{48} ||
iti tenārthitaṃ śrutvā bimbisāraḥ sa bhūpatiḥ |
taṃ dūtaṃ samupālokya cireṇaivamabhāṣata || 25.50{49} ||
sadho sa bhagavānstatra yadi gaṃtuṃ samichati |
tadatra kiṃ vadiṣyāmi yatrechettatra gachatu || 25.51{50} ||
iti rājñā samādiṣṭaṃ śrutvā dūtaḥ sa moditaḥ |
bimbisāraṃ nareṃdraṃ taṃ natvā yayau jināntike || 25.52{51} ||
tatra sa sahasā gatvā śrīghanaṃ taṃ munīśvaram |
kṛtāṃjalipuṭo natvā prārthayadevamādarāt || 25.53{52} ||
bhagavaṃstena bhūpena hyanujñāto'si sāṃpratam |
tatsasaṃgho bhagavāṃstatra vijayituṃ samarhati || 25.54{53} ||
iti tenoditaṃ śrutvā bhagavān sa munīśvaraḥ |
enaṃ dūtaṃ samālokya tūṣṇībhūtvādhyuvāsa tat || 25.55{54} ||
tataḥ sa bhagavān varṣātrimāsānta sasāṃghikaḥ |
sarvatra maṃgalaṃ kṛtvā pratasthe saṃprabhāsayan || 25.56{55} ||
tadā sa puruṣo dūtastadvihārādvinirgataḥ |
sahasā rathamāruhya saṃpratasthe puraḥ pathi || 25.57{56} ||
tatra sa bhagavaṃtaṃ padbhyāṃ saṃprasthitaṃ pathi |
dṛṣṭvaiva sahasā tasmādrathātsvayamavātarat || 25.58{57} ||
tatra sa sahasopetya bhagavantaṃ munīśvaram |
sāṃjaliḥ purato gatvā praṇatvaivamavocata || 25.59{58} ||
prasīdatu jagannātha mamānukampayā bhavān |
idaṃ rathaṃ samāruhya vijayatāṃ sasāṃghikaḥ || 25.60{59} ||
iti tenoditaṃ śrutvā bhagavān sa jagadguruḥ |
taṃ dūtaṃ puruṣaṃ dṛṣṭvā tatraivaṃ samupādiśat || 25.61{60} ||
ṛddhipādarathenāhaṃ samyagvyāyāmavarttinā |
(rm 296)
pracarāmi mahīṃ kṛtsnāmakṣataḥ kleśakaṣṭakaiḥ || 25.62{61} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa sa dūtaḥ pratibodhitaḥ |
bhūyo'pi sāṃjalirnatvā prārthayattaṃ munīśvaram || 25.63{62} ||
yadyapi bhagavānṛddhiyānayāyī tathāpi tu |
mamānugrahamādhātuṃ gṛhyatāmanukampayā || 25.64{63} ||
iti tenārthitaṃ śrutvā bhagavān sasāṃghikaḥ |
ṛddhyā rathopari sthitvā saṃpratasthe prabhāsayan || 25.65{64} ||
tatra mārgeṣu sarvatra bhagavān sasāṃghikaḥ |
bhāsayanmaṃgalaṃ kṛtvā śrāvastīpuramāyayau || 25.66{65} ||
tatra sa puruṣo dūtaḥ sahasā pracaran puraḥ |
prasenajinnarendrasya gatvaivaṃ saṃnyavedayat || 25.67{66} ||
jaya deva mahārāja diṣṭyā varddhasva sāṃpratam |
yadasau bhagavāṃcchāstā sasaṃgho'tra samāgataḥ || 25.68{67} ||
iti tenoditaṃ śrutvā prasenajitsa bhūpatiḥ |
tatra sarvatra mārgeṣu śodhayitvābhyaśodhayat || 25.69{68} ||
chatradhvajavitānaiśca samaṃtato'bhyamaṃḍayat |
tataḥ sa nṛpati rājā tūryasaṃgītivādanaiḥ |
sajano bhagavantaṃ taṃ pratyudyayau mahotsavaiḥ || 25.70{69} ||
tatra samudito rājā samaṃtrijanapaurikaḥ |
dūrāttaṃ śrīghanaṃ dṛṣṭvā padbhyāṃ carannupācarat || 25.71{70} ||
tatropetya sa bhūpālaḥ sasaṃghan taṃ munīśvaram |
praṇatvā sāṃjaliḥ sthitvā puro dṛṣṭvaivamabravīt || 25.72{71} ||
bhagavannātha sārvajña kaccicca kuśalaṃ tanau |
bhavatāṃ viṣaye hyatra viharasva sasāṃghikaḥ || 25.73{72} ||
tathā sarve'pi lokāśca samaṃtrijanapaurikāḥ |
sāṃjalayaḥ praṇatvaivaṃ prārthayaṃstaṃ munīśvaram || 25.74{73} ||
svāgataṃ bhagavaṃcchāsta vijayasva kṛpānidhe |
sadātra svāśrame sthitvā viharasvānukampayā || 25.75{74} ||
ityuktvā sarvalokāste nṛpatipramukhā mudā |
pūjādi maṃgalotsāhaiḥ prāveśayaṃstataḥ pure || 25.76{75} ||
tataḥ sa bhagavān dṛṣṭvā sarvāṃstān saṃpramoditān |
nṛpatipramukhān datvā bhadrāśiṣaṃ samācarat || 25.77{76} ||
tatra sa bhagavāṃstasyāḥ śrāvastyā gopurāntike |
avatīrya yathā padbhyāṃ pracakrāma sasāṃghikaḥ || 25.78{77} ||
tatra sa śrīghano nāthaḥ prabhāsayan samaṃtataḥ |
sarveṣāṃ bhadratāṃ kurvan praviveśa sasāṃghikaḥ || 25.79{78} ||
praviṣṭe nagare tatra saṃbuddhe sugate jine |
tatpuṇyasuprabhāvyāpte sarvatrāpyabhavacchubham || 25.80{79} ||
aṃdhā rūpāṇi prādrākṣurvadhirāḥ śuśruvu ravam |
ye ca hīneṃdriyāste'pi sarve pūrṇendriyā vabhuḥ || 25.81{80} ||
kṣudhāgniparitaptāṃgāste'bhavan paritṛptitāḥ |
pipāsāparidagdhāṃgāste'pyāsan saṃpratarpitāḥ || 25.82{81} ||
kucelā durbhagāṃśā ye śuddhāṃśukāḥ śubhāṃśikāḥ |
(rm 297)
cirarogāturā yena nīrogā valino'bhavan || 25.83{82} ||
unmattāḥ smṛtimaṃtaśca daridrā dhanino'bhavan |
ciravairānuvaddhā ye te'pyāsanmaitrabhāvinaḥ || 25.84{83} ||
caṃḍālāśca dayāvantaḥ krodhinaḥ kṣāntibhāvinaḥ |
labdhāśca niḥspṛhā āsanīrṣyālavaśca sādhavaḥ || 25.85{84} ||
matsariṇaśca dātāraḥ kṛpaṇāḥ saṃtoṣitāśayāḥ |
adharmakāmasaṃraktāste dharmakāmasādhinaḥ || 25.86{85} ||
mṛṣāvādaratā ye ca te'pyāsan satyabhāṣiṇaḥ |
paiśunyābhiratā ye ca te'pi viśrambhabhojanāḥ || 25.87{86} ||
saṃbhinnalāpino duṣṭāste'pi saṃtaḥ samārjavāḥ |
pāruṣyaṃvādinaḥ krūrāste āsan priyavādinaḥ || 25.88{87} ||
ye'bhidhyālavaste'pi sajjanā kīrtimāninaḥ |
vyāpādaniratā ye ca te'pi satvahitārthinaḥ || 25.89{88} ||
mithyādṛṣṭicarā ye ca te dharmasatyadṛṣṭayaḥ |
bodhicaryāsamutsāhāḥ sarve puṇyārthino'bhavan || 25.90{89} ||
evamanye'pi ye satvā duḥkhinaḥ pāpasaṃratāḥ |
sarve te sukhinaḥ saṃtaḥ saddharmavāṃchino'bhavan || 25.91{90} ||
sugaṃdhinaḥ sukhasparśāḥ śītalā vāyavo vavuḥ |
prasedire diśaḥ sarvāḥ puṣpāṇi vavṛṣurghanāḥ || 25.92{91} ||
suraduṃdubhayo neduḥ prasasāra mahodadhiḥ |
pracacāra mahī ṣaḍdhā prajujvaluḥ śubhāgnayaḥ || 25.93{92} ||
babhūvuḥ pādapāḥ sarve phalapuṣpābharānattāḥ |
aṣṭāṃgaguṇayuktāmbuparipūrṇajalāśrayāḥ || 25.94{93} ||
anekadhāturatnādi nidhayaḥ pracakāsire |
nispannaśasyasaṃpannā bahhūva vasudhā tadā || 25.95{94} ||
bahukṣīrapradā gāva auṣadhayo guṇānvitāḥ |
rasāśca suguṇā āsan phalāni surasāni ca || 25.96{95} ||
evaṃ tatra subhadrāṇi sarvatrāpyabhavaṃstadā |
upadravāśca sarve'pi sarvatra vilayaṃ gatāḥ || 25.97{96} ||
sarve vighnagaṇā duṣṭā te'pyatra vilayaṃ yayuḥ |
sarve satvāḥ sukhāḍhyāśca pracerire praharṣitāḥ || 25.98{97} ||
evaṃ tadā munīndrasya prabhāvena samaṃtataḥ |
saddharmamaṃgalotsāhaṃ babhūva nirupadravam || 25.99{98} ||
evaṃ sa bhagavāṃstatra kṛtvā bhadraṃ prabhāsayan |
pradakṣiṇakrameṇaivaṃ pracakrāma nṛpālaye || 25.100{99} ||
tatra śodhāntare ramye prāsāde pariśodhite |
chatradhvajavitānaiśca maṇḍite visṛtāsane || 25.101{100} ||
praviśya pādyamādāya nṛpadattaṃ sasāṃghikaḥ |
bhagavān bhāsayaṃstatra śuddhāsane samāśrayan || 25.102{1} ||
tathā te sāṃghikāścāpi sarve dattaṃ mahībhujā |
svayaṃ samādāya svasvāsane samāśrayat || 25.103{2} ||
evaṃ tatra samāsīnaṃ śrīghanaṃ taṃ sasāṃghikam |
nṛpatipramukhāḥ sarve lokā dṛṣṭvā pramoditāḥ || 25.104{3} ||
(rm 298)
sarvapūjopahārāṃgaiḥ samabhyarcya yathāvidhiḥ |
yathārhabhojanaiścāpi praṇītaiḥ samatoṣayan || 25.105{4} ||
tatastaṃ sugataṃ tṛptaṃ sasamahaṃ sa narādhipaḥ |
dṛṣṭvāpanīya pātrāṇi taddhastādīnaśodhayat || 25.106{5} ||
tataḥ kramukatāmbūlamahauṣaddhirasāyanam |
datvā kṣamārthanāṃ kṛtvā nanāma taṃ sasāṃghikam || 25.107{6} ||
tatra samudito rājā tasya śāsturjagadguroḥ |
puro nīcāsanāsīno tasthau śrotuṃ vṛṣaṃ mudā || 25.108{7} ||
tathā maṃtrijanādyāśca sarve lokāḥ pramoditāḥ |
natvā taṃ śrīghanaṃ tatra dharmaṃ śrotumupāśrayan || 25.109{8} ||
tataḥ sa bhagavāṃ dṛṣṭvā sarvāṃstān samupāśritān |
nṛpatiṃ taṃ samāmaṃtrya saddharmaṃ samupādiśat || 25.110{9} ||
śṛṇu rājanmahābhāga sarvadārogyamastu vaḥ |
sarve satvāśca saddharmaniratā santu sarvadā || 25.111{10} ||
evaṃ tvaṃ bho mahārāja sādhayitvā prayatnataḥ |
sarvāṃ satvāṃ śubhe dharme yojayitvābhipālaya || 25.112{11} ||
evaṃ loke śubhaṃ kṛtvā bodhicaryāṃ samāhitaḥ |
triratnabhajanaṃ kṛtvā cinu dharmaṃ samādarāt || 25.113{12} ||
anityaṃ khalu saṃsāraṃ kṣaṇadhvaṃsi śarīrakam |
jīvitaṃ ca kṣaṇasthāyi dharma eva sahānugaḥ || 25.114{13} ||
iti matvā mahārāja kleśān durjayān valaiḥ |
jitvā satvāṃcchubhe sthāpya pālaya sadvṛṣaṃ cinuḥ || 25.115{14} ||
dharmeṇa sadgatiṃ yāyāḥ sadgatau sarvadā sukham |
bhuktvā bodhicariṃ dhṛtvā caraṃ bodhimavāpnuyāḥ || 25.116{15} ||
evaṃ vijñāya rājeṃdra svayaṃ dharme samāhitaḥ |
sarvasatvahitaṃ kṛtvā cara nityaṃ samāhitaḥ || 25.117{16} ||
ityādiśya nareṃdrāya salokāya śubhāśiṣam |
sasaṃgho bhagavān buddhaḥ svāśrame gaṃtumutthitaḥ || 25.118{17} ||
bhāsayaṃ sakalāṃllokāṃ bhadraṃ kṛtvā samaṃtataḥ |
svāśramābhimukhastasmātpratasthe kramataḥ purāt || 25.119{18} ||
tatra sarve'pi lokā nṛpatipramukhā api |
śāstustasya sasaṃghasya prānuyayuḥ sahotsavaiḥ || 25.120{19} ||
tathā sa bhagavān gatvā sasāṃghiko nṛpādibhiḥ |
lokaiḥ saha mahotsāhaiḥ svāśramaṃ samupāyayau || 25.121{20} ||
tatra jetavanodyāne vihāre samupāśritaḥ |
sasāṃghikaḥ sabhāsīnaḥ sa śāstā dharmamādiśat || 25.122{21} ||
taddharmadeśanāṃ śrutvā tatraiva te nṛpādayaḥ |
sarve lokāḥ pramodanto rātrau nyūṣuḥ sahotsavaiḥ || 25.123{22} ||
tasyāmeva niśāyāṃ sa dūto'lpāyūrjvarānvitaḥ |
triratnasmaraṇaṃ kurvan dehaṃ tyaktvā divaṃ yayau || 25.124{23} ||
(rm 299)
tatra svarge samutpannaḥ sa dūto vismayānvitaḥ |
kutaścyutaḥ kuhotpannaḥ kathamiti vyaciṃtayat || 25.125{24} ||
tadā sa manasādrākṣīnmanuṣyebhyaścyutāsmyaham |
iha svarge samutpannaḥ saṃbuddhabhajanāditi || 25.126{25} ||
atha sa dūtapūrvī sa devaputraḥ prasāditaḥ |
saṃbuddhasya guṇān smṛtvā punarevaṃ vyaciṃtayat || 25.127{26} ||
aho hyahaṃ munīndrasya prasādāddivi sāṃpratam |
jāto'smi tanmunīndrasya kuryāṃ saṃdarśanaṃ punaḥ || 25.128{27} ||
iti hi dūtapurvī sa devaputro viciṃtayan |
snātvā sugaṃdhiliptāṃgaṃ śuddhāmvarāvṛtaḥ sudhīḥ || 25.129{28} ||
maulikuṇḍalamālādi divyālaṃkārabhūṣitaḥ |
divyapūjopacārāṃ dhṛtvā devaṃ sahācarat || 25.130{29} ||
tasyāmeva niśāyāṃ sa devaputraḥ suraiḥ saha |
avabhāsya prabhāstasmijjetāraṇye upācarat || 25.131{30} ||
tatrodyāne vihāre taṃ bhagavaṃtaṃ munīśvaram |
ālokya sāṃjalirnatvā purataḥ samupācarat || 25.132{31} ||
tatastaṃ śrīghanaṃ nāthaṃ samabhyarcya yathāvidhiḥ |
natvā pradakṣiṇīkṛtya sāṃjaliḥ samupāśrayat || 25.133{32} ||
atha sa bhagavāṃstasya dṛṣṭvā cittaṃ viśodhitam |
āryyasatyaṃ samārabhya saddharmaṃ samupādiśat || 25.134{33} ||
tadāryyadharmamākarṇya devaputraḥ sa bodhitaḥ |
dharmaviśeṣamājñāya dṛṣṭasatyo'bhavaṃstataḥ || 25.135{34} ||
satkāyadṛṣṭibhūmīndhraṃ viṃśatiśikharodgatam |
prabhitvā jñānavajreṇa saddharmaṃ sādhanotsukaḥ || 25.136{35} ||
pariśuddhāśayaḥ śrotaāpattiphalamāptavān |
śāstustasya padau natvā sāṃjalirevamabravīt || 25.137{36} ||
bhagavannidamasmākaṃ gurubhirna kṛtaṃ khalu |
na mātrā nāpi pitrā vā na jñātimitravāṃdhavaiḥ || 25.138{37} ||
na ceṣṭairbrāhmaṇairvāpi na cāpi pūrvapretakaiḥ |
na rājñā śramaṇaiścānyairdevairapi kṛtaṃ khalu || 25.139{38} ||
yadasmākaṃ kṛtaṃ bhadraṃ bhavatāsthiśiroccayāḥ |
laṃghitā rudhirā cābdhiruchoṣitāśca sāṃpratam || 25.140{39} ||
pihito pāpamārgaśca svargamārgaśca vaivṛtaḥ |
darśito mokṣamārgo'pi tīrṇo duḥkhārṇavāpi ca || 25.141{40} ||
prasādādbhavatāmadya prāptaṃ śuddheṃdriyaṃ mayā |
śāntamāryapadaṃ kāṃtaṃ saṃbodhiguṇasādhanam || 25.142{41} ||
adya me saphalaṃ janma bhavatāṃ darśanādiha |
saddharmaśravaṇādadya buddhaputro'smi sāṃpratam || 25.143{42} ||
ityuktvā dūtapurvī sa devaputraḥ pramoditaḥ |
tasya śāsturjagadvaṃdhoḥ pādau natvā kṛtāṃjaliḥ || 25.144{43} ||
tridhā pradakṣiṇīkṛtya samabhinaṃditāśayaḥ |
tato'nujñāṃ samāsādya suraiḥ saha divaṃ yayau || 25.145{44} ||
(rm 300)
tatra svarge sa samyātaḥ suramye svālaye sthitaḥ |
triratnabhajanaṃ kṛtvāramaṃ devaiḥ sahāvasat || 25.146{45} ||
tatra dṛṣṭvāvabhāsaṃ taṃ prasenajitsa kauśalaḥ |
vismitaḥ prātarutthāya vihāre samupācarat || 25.147{56!} ||
tatra taṃ śrīghanaṃ dṛṣṭvā nṛpatiḥ sa pramoditaḥ |
natvā pradakṣiṇīkṛtya sāṃjaliḥ samupāśritaḥ || 25.148{57} ||
tataḥ sa nṛpa utthāya sūttarāsaṅgamudvahan |
jānubhyāṃ bhuvi saṃsthitvā prārthayattaṃ munīśvaram || 25.149{58} ||
bhagavannatra niśāyāṃ ko bhavatāṃ samupāgataḥ |
tadbhavatāṃ samupādiśyā māṃ bodhayitumarhati || 25.150{59} ||
iti saṃprārthite rājñā bhagavān sa munīśvaraḥ |
nṛpatiṃ taṃ mahīpālaṃ samālokyaivamādiśat || 25.151{60} ||
anyo na hi sa rājeṃdra yo'dya rātrāvihāgātaḥ |
api tu tāvako dūto devabhūtaḥ sa āgataḥ || 25.152{61} ||
ityādiṣṭe munīndreṇa nṛpatiḥ sa savismayaḥ |
bhagavaṃtaṃ punarnatvā tatkarma paryapṛchataḥ || 25.153{62} ||
bhavan kiṃ kadā tena dūtena sukṛtaṃ kṛtam |
yenāsau divi saṃjāto dṛṣṭasatyo bhavatyapi || 25.154{63} ||
tadbhavān bhagavaṃcchāsta yattena sukṛtaṃ kṛtam |
tatsarvaṃ samupākhyāya bodhayitumanāśayam || 25.155{64} ||
iti saṃprārthite rājñā bhagavān sa jagadguruḥ |
kauśalaṃ taṃ mahārājaṃ samālokyaivamādiśat || 25.156{65} ||
śṛṇu rājanmahābhāga tena devena yatkṛtam |
tadatra saṃpravakṣyāmi tava cittaprabodhane || 25.157{66} ||
yattvayā preṣito dūto mayi rājagṛhe sthite |
yena saṃprārthitaṃ śrutvā tathā hyahaṃ samāgataḥ || 25.158{67} ||
sa sādhustāvako dūto mayā sārddhamihāgataḥ |
saddharmadeśanāṃ śrutvā śraddhābhaktisamanvitaḥ || 25.159{68} ||
triratnabhajanaṃ kṛtvā prācaracchāsane mama |
ālpāyuṣko hyasau bhadrī vihāre'tra nivāsitaḥ || 25.160{69} ||
kāladharmābhisaṃyukte tyaktvā dehaṃ divaṃ yayau || 25.161{70} ||
sa hyasau tāvako dūto rātrāvadyeha bhāsayan |
mama saṃdarśanaṃ kartuṃ devalokaiḥ sahāgataḥ || 25.162{71} ||
mama pūjāṃ vidhāyāsau kṛtvā cāpi pradakṣiṇām |
natvā sādaramāśritya saddharmaṃ śraddhayāśṛṇot || 25.163{72} ||
matsaddharmāmṛtaṃ pītvā dṛṣṭasatyaḥ pramoditaḥ |
natvā māṃ sa punaḥ svarge devalokaiḥ sahācarat || 25.164{73} ||
tatra sa svālaye ramye sthitvā devaiḥ sahāraman |
triratnabhajanaṃ kurvan prācaraṃ vasate sukham || 25.165{74} ||
etatpuṇyavipākena dūtaḥ sa tāvakaḥ kṛtī |
svarge saukhyaṃ ciraṃ bhuktvā kramādbodhimavāpsyati || 25.166{75} ||
(rm 301)
evaṃ vijñāya rājeṃdra saṃsāre'tra mahatsukham |
yadīchasi sadā bhadre caritavyaṃ tvayādarāt || 25.167{76} ||
bhadraṃ tu jāyate śīghraṃ triratnabhajanodyamāt |
tattriratnamupāśritya bhaja nityaṃ samāhitaḥ || 25.168{77} ||
triratne yatkṛtaṃ karmaṃ tannaiva kṣiṇuyā kvacit |
yāvadbodhipadaṃ dadyā evaṃ mahattaraṃ vṛṣam || 25.169{78} ||
ye triratnaṃ bhajantyatra na te gachanti durgatim |
sadaiva sadgatiṃ yānti bodhiṃ ca samavāpnuyuḥ || 25.170{79} ||
tisro hīmāḥ samākhyātā agrāḥ prajñaptayo nṛpa |
yatra kiṃ citkṛtaṃ karma tadvipāko mahān smṛtāḥ || 25.171{80} ||
tadyathā katamāstisro agrāḥ prajñaptayaḥ smṛtāḥ |
triratne prakṛtā yāstā agrāḥ prajñaptayaḥ smṛtāḥ || 25.172{81} ||
tadyathā sarvasatvānāṃ traidhātukanivāsinām |
buddha eva mahānagro jyeṣṭhaḥ śreṣṭho varottamaḥ || 25.173{82} ||
ye buddhaśraddhayā bhaktyā prabhajaṃti prasāditāḥ |
sarve'tyagraprasannāste jyeṣṭhāḥ śreṣṭhā varottamāḥ || 25.174{83} ||
tadagrābhiprasannānāṃ vipāko'pyagra uttamaḥ |
tathā dharmāśca ye sarve saṃskṛtā vāpyasaṃkṛtāḥ || 25.175{84} ||
teṣāmapi ca sarveṣāṃ buddhadharmāgra ucyate |
tatra dharme prasannā ye te'gre hi saṃprasannitāḥ |
tadagrābhiprasannānāṃ vipāko'pyagra ucyate || 25.176{85} ||
ye ca saṃghā gaṇāsteṣāṃ buddhasaṃgho'gra ucyate |
tatra saṃghe prasannā ye te'gre'bhisaṃprasādinaḥ || 25.177{86} ||
teṣāmagraprasannānāṃ vipāko'pyagra ucyate |
etāḥ prajñaptayastisro hyagrāḥ khyāto munīśvaraiḥ || 25.178{87} ||
tadatra śraddhayā rāja triratne saṃprasannadhīḥ |
satkāraiḥ samupāśritya bhaja nityaṃ samādarāt || 25.179{88} ||
etatpuṇyamasaṃkhyeyamaprameyaṃ mahattaram |
sarvasatvamahāsaukhyaguṇasaṃpattidāyakam || 25.180{89} ||
evaṃ matvā mahatpuṇyaṃ sarvān satvān prabodhayat |
triratnabhajanotsāhaiścārayasva śubhe nṛpa || 25.181{90} ||
evaṃ te sarvadā bhadraṃ sarvatrāpi bhaveddhruvam |
kramādbodhiṃ samāsādya saṃbuddhapadamāpnuyāḥ || 25.182{91} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa śrutvā sa nṛpatirmudā |
tatheti hi pratijñāya prābhyanaṃdatprasāditaḥ || 25.183{92} ||
tataḥ sa nṛpa utthāya natvā taṃ jagadīśvaram |
mahānaṃdamahotsāhaiḥ svapuraṃ sajano yayau || 25.184{93} ||
tatra sa nṛpatī rājā prāsādatalamāśritaḥ |
sa maṃtripaurikān sarvān samāmaṃtryaivamādiśat || 25.185{94} ||
maṃtriṇaḥ paurikāḥ sarve śṛṇudhvaṃ vacanaṃ mama |
(rm 302)
yadi vāṃchāsti vo bhadre triratnaṃ bhajatādarat || 25.186{95} ||
yavadayaṃ jagacchāstā viharatyatra sāṃghikaḥ |
tāvadasya munīndrasya bhajata sarvadotsavaiḥ || 25.187{96} ||
vihāre sarvadā gatvā śraddhayā samupāśritāḥ |
tatsaddharmāmṛtaṃ pītvā mudā carata sadvṛtau || 25.188{97} ||
sarvāṃśca saugatān bhikṣūn sarvopakaraṇairapi |
satkṛtya śraddhayāmaṃtrya mānayadhvaṃ sadādarāt || 25.189{98} ||
etanme vacanaṃ śrutvā triratnaśaraṇaṃ gatāḥ |
saṃbodhipraṇidhānena carata bodhicārikām || 25.190{99} ||
tathā te maṃgalaṃ nityaṃ sarvatrāpi bhaveddhruvam |
kramādbodhiṃ samāsādya labhata saugatiṃ gatim || 25.191{100} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa śrutvā te maṃtriṇo janāḥ |
sarve vīrādayo lokāstatheti pratiśuśruvuḥ || 25.192{1} ||
tadā sarve'pi te lokāḥ saddharmasādhanodyatāḥ |
triratnabhajanaṃ kṛtvā pracerire samoditāḥ || 25.193{2} ||
tadārabhya sadā tatra maṃgalaṃ nirupadravam |
sarvatra sarvadāpyāsīttriratnasyānubhāvataḥ || 25.194{3} ||
iti me guruṇādiṣṭaṃ tathā te kathyate mayā |
tvamapyevaṃ sadā rājan triratnaṃ śaraṇaṃ gataḥ || 25.195{4} ||
saddharmaṃ samupāśritya cara nityaṃ sadā śubhe |
lokāścāpi mahārāja bodhayitvā prayatnataḥ |
triratnaśaraṇe sthāpya pālanīyāḥ sadā tvayā || 25.196{5} ||
evaṃ te maṃgalaṃ nityaṃ sarvatrāpi bhaveddhruvam |
kramādbodhiṃ samāsādya saṃbuddhapadamāpnuyāḥ || 25.197{6} ||
iti tenārhatādiṣṭaṃ śrutvāśokaḥ sa bhūmipaḥ |
tathetyabhyanumoditvā prābhyanaṃdatsapārṣadaḥ || 25.198{7} ||
dūtāvadānaṃ yadidaṃ mudā ye śṛṇvaṃti ye cāpi niśāmayanti |
te sarva evaṃ tridive vrajanti saṃbuddhaloke'pi saranti cānte || 25.199{8} ||

++ iti śriratnāvadānatatve dūtāvadānaṃ samāptam ++


(rm 303)
xxvi tairthikāvadāna
athāśoko mahīpālaḥ kṛtāṃjalipuṭo mudā |
upaguptaṃ tamarhantaṃ praṇatvaivamabravīt || 26.1{1} ||
bhadanta śrotumichāmi punaranyatsubhāṣitam |
tadyathā guruṇādiṣṭaṃ tathādeṣṭuṃ ca me'rhati || 26.2{2} ||
iti saṃprārthitaṃ rājñā śrutvā sa sugatātmajaḥ |
upagupto nareṃdraṃ taṃ samāmaṃtryaivamādiśat || 26.3{3} ||
sādhu śṛṇu mahārāja yathā me guruṇoditam |
tathātrāhaṃ pravakṣyāmi śrutvānumodanāṃ kuru || 26.4{4} ||
tadyathā bhagavāṃcchāstā śrīghano'rhanmunīśvaraḥ |
sarvajñaḥ sugato nātho dharmarājo vināyakaḥ || 26.5{5} ||
bhikṣubhiḥ śrāvakaiḥ sārddhaṃ tathā sarvaiśca sāṃghikaiḥ |
tatraiva jetakodyāne vihāre vyaharatpurā || 26.6{6} ||
taddharmadeśanāṃ śrotuṃ sarve lokaiḥ samāgataiḥ |
devadānavagaṃdharvairyakṣakinnararākṣasaiḥ || 26.7{7} ||
siddhairvidyādharaiścāpi nāgendrairgaruḍairapi |
lokapālaiḥ sasainyaiśca brāhmaṇaiśca maharṣibhiḥ || 26.8{8} ||
yogibhiryatibhiścāpi nirgranthibhiśca tāpasaiḥ |
rājabhiḥ kṣatriyaiścāpi tathā rājakumārakaiḥ || 26.9{9} ||
vaiśyaiśca maṃtribhiḥ śreṣṭhairgṛhasthaiśca mahājanaiḥ |
vaṇigbhiḥ sārthavāhaiśca śilpibhiḥ paurikairapi || 26.10{10} ||
grāmyairjānapadaiścāpi kārpaṭikaistathāparaiḥ |
triratnadarśanotsāhaiḥ saddharmaguṇavāṃchibhiḥ || 26.11{11} ||
satkṛto mānito nityaṃ pūjyamāno'bhivaṃditaḥ |
sarvasatvahitārthena varṣaṃ dharmāmṛtaṃ sadā || 26.12{12} ||
tadā tatra pure rājā prasenajinnṛpādhipaḥ |
saṃbuddhaśāsanaṃ dhṛtvā triratnamabhajanmudā || 26.13{13} ||
taddṛṣṭvā tīrthikāḥ sarve īrṣyāroṣāhatāśayāḥ |
saṃmīlya saugataṃ dharmaṃ pratikṣipyaivamabruvan || 26.14{14} ||
bhavanto yadayaṃ buddhaḥ sarvajña ṛddhimān kṛtī |
tatsarvānimāṃ lokān svaṃ dharme saṃniyojayet || 26.15{15} ||
tadāsmadvacanaṃ śrotumapīchennātra kaścana |
sarve naḥ śrāvakāścāpi bhajeyuḥ sugataṃ jinam || 26.16{16} ||
tadā sarve vayaṃ lokai niṃdyamānaḥ samaṃtataḥ |
kathamatra cariṣyāmastadanyatra caremahi || 26.17{17} ||
iti sarvairapi prokte tīrthikopāsako dvijaḥ |
sarvānstānstīrthikaṃ dṛṣṭvā punarevamabhāṣata || 26.18{18} ||
(rm 304)
bhavaṃto ma viṣīdatvaṃ kartumarhati sarvathā |
tadupāyaṃ kariṣyāmi yenānyatra carejjinaḥ || 26.19{19} ||
iti tānstīrthikān sarvān bodhayitvā sa durmatiḥ |
nṛpaterantike gatvā sādaramevamabravīt || 26.20{20} ||
śṛṇu rājanmahābhāga yaddhitaṃ te taducyate |
tathā tvayā samādhāya caritavyaṃ sadādarāt || 26.21{21} ||
yadayaṃ muṇḍito bhikṣuḥ kuladharmavināśakṛt |
jātidharmaparibhraṣṭaḥ saṃsāravṛttiniṃdakaḥ || 26.22{22} ||
māyāvī kutsitācāro duḥśīlo lokavaṃcakaḥ |
svayaṃ naṣṭaḥ parāṃścāpi nāśayitumihāgataḥ || 26.23{23} ||
tadasya darśanaṃ rājaṃ karttavyaṃ na kadā cana |
grahītavyaṃ na nāmāpi śrotavyaṃ nāpi tadvacaḥ || 26.24{24} ||
satkāraṃ na ca karttavyaṃ dātavyaṃ nāpi kiṃ cana |
āśrame'pi na gantavyaṃ sthātavyaṃ na tadantike || 26.25{25} ||
darśanaṃ ye prakurvanti tasya bhikṣordurātmanaḥ |
te sarve narake yāyuḥ sadgatiṃ na kadā cana || 26.26{26} ||
ye ca śṛṇvanti tadvākyaṃ sarve te nilayaṃ gatāḥ |
tīvraduḥkhābhisaṃtaptā vaseyuranutāpitāḥ || 26.27{27} ||
satkāraṃ ye ca kurvaṃti sarve te vilayaṃ gatāḥ |
daridrā durgatiṃ gatvā bhrameyurnihatāśayāḥ || 26.28{28} ||
ye ca dānaṃ prakurvanti tatrāśirluhitāśayāḥ |
te'pi ca narake yātā duḥkhānyevamavāpnuyuḥ || 26.29{29} ||
tadāśrame ca gachaṃti sarve te duritāṅkitāḥ |
kuṣṭhādi rogasaṃtaptā yāyuśca niraye'cirāt || 26.30{30} ||
ye ca taiḥ saha tiṣṭhanti te sarve pāpasaṃgitāḥ |
tīvraduḥkhāgnisaṃdagdhā careyu niraye sadā || 26.31{31} ||
gṛhnanti ye ca mannāma teṣāṃ geheṣu sarvadā |
lakṣmīrmaiva śrayettatra nairṛtireva saṃśrayet || 26.32{32} ||
ye buddhaṃ bhajantyatra te te sarve'pyadharmiṇaḥ |
kadāpi sadgatiṃ naiva yāṃti dūragatimeva hi || 26.33{33} ||
iti matvā mahārāja sadā bhadraṃ yadīchasi |
satkāraṃ darśanaṃ tasya karttavyaṃ naiva ke cana || 26.34{34} ||
kiṃ dharmaṃ saugataṃ dharmaṃ yanmithyā parikalpitam |
tattasya duryaterdharmaṃ śrotavyaṃ naiva kena cit || 26.35{35} ||
yasya dharmo hi sarvatra vedaśāstre na gaṇyate |
sa kiṃ dharmātmako buddhastattatra maṃgalaṃ na hi || 26.36{36} ||
iti tenoditaṃ śrutvā sa paribodhitaḥ |
triratnabhajanotsāhaśaithilyakṣubhito'bhavat || 26.37{37} ||
tatastasya vacaḥ śrutvā tīrthikopāsakasya saḥ |
nṛpatistairthike dharme'bhilalāṣa prasāditaḥ || 26.38{38} ||
(rm 305)
tatraiko gṛhabhṛcchreṣṭhī saṃbuddhopāsakaḥ sudhīḥ |
taṃ tīrthyopāsakaṃ dṛṣṭvā bodhayitumabhāṣata || 26.39{39} ||
kimevaṃ durmate buddho dharmarājo'pi niṃdyate |
mā maivaṃ vadatāmatra tvameva narakaṃ vrajeḥ || 26.40{40} ||
yo loke samutpannaḥ sarvakleśān vinirjayan |
jitvā māragaṇān sarvān saṃbodhijñānamāptavān || 26.41{41} ||
sarvai brahmādibhirlokaiḥ saṃstuto vaṃdito'rcitaḥ |
mānitaḥ satkṛto buddhastvayaiva niṃdyate katham || 26.42{42} ||
yenaiva trijagallokāṃ putravatpratipālitam |
pūjanīyaḥ kathaṃ nāthaṃ dharmarājo munīśvaraḥ || 26.43{43} ||
buddha eva jagannātho dayāluḥ sadguṇākaraḥ |
sarvasatvahitādhāno'dvayavādī vināyakaḥ || 26.44{44} ||
sarvajñaḥ sugato bhadrarūpaḥ sarvahitārthabhṛt |
sarvavidyādhipaḥ śāstā saṃbodhimārgadeśakaḥ || 26.45{45} ||
nirjitakleśa ātmajñaḥ ṣaḍabhijño jagadguruḥ |
saṃbuddho'rhañjinastrāyī dharmarājo munīśvaraḥ || 26.46{46} ||
tataḥ sa bhagavānneva sarvatraidhātukeṣvapi |
agraḥ śreṣṭho mahāñjeṣṭho nānyaḥ kaścidato'dhikaḥ || 26.47{47} ||
tasmādasya munīndrasya satkāraṃ śraddhayā sadā |
śaraṇagamanaṃ kṛtvā śrotavyaṃ dharmamādarāt || 26.48{48} ||
ye buddhe śaraṇaṃ yānti na te gachaṃti durgatim |
sarvadā sadgatau saukhyaṃ bhuktvā yānti jinālaye || 26.49{49} ||
śṛṇvanti ye ca taddharmaṃ te'pi no yānti durgatim |
sadā svarge sukhaṃ bhuktvā yāyuścānte sunirvṛtim || 26.50{50} ||
bhajanti ye ca saṃgheṣu te'pi na yānti durgatim |
sadā dharmaratāḥ saukhyaṃ bhuktvā yānti sukhāvatim || 26.51{51} ||
evaṃ ye ye triratnānāṃ sarvadā śaraṇaṃ gatāḥ |
satkṛtya śraddhayā nityaṃ bhajanti bodhimānasāḥ || 26.52{52} ||
sarve te sadguṇādhārāḥ sarvasatvahitārthinaḥ |
kramādbodhicarīḥ pūrya saṃbuddhapadamāpnuyuḥ || 26.53{53} ||
tenāyaṃ bhagavānneva jagannātho jagadguruḥ |
dharmarājo jagadbharttā jagattrāyī vināyakaḥ || 26.54{54} ||
tenāsya sadguroḥ śāstuḥ śraddhayā śaraṇaṃ gatāḥ |
saddharmaṃ sarvadā śrutvā kartumarhati satkṛtim || 26.55{55} ||
ye cāpyasya munīndrasya pratikṣipanti śāsanam |
sadgatiṃ naiva te yānti durgatimeva sarvadā || 26.56{56} ||
iti matvā na karttavyā buddhe niṃdā kathaṃ cana |
triratnabhajanānyeva karttavyāni śubhārthibhiḥ || 26.57{57} ||
ye triratnaṃ bhajantyatra sarve te bodhibhāginaḥ |
bodhisatvā mahāsatvā bhaveyuḥ sugatātmajāḥ || 26.58{58} ||
tena te'pi mahāsatvāstriratnopāsakā api |
śraddhayā praṇatiṃ tesāṃ karttumarhanti sarvadā || 26.59{59} ||
iti tenoditaṃ śrutvā tīrthikopāsakaśca saḥ |
(rm 306)
