taṃ buddhopāsakaṃ dṛṣṭvā punarevamabhāṣata || 26.60{60} ||
ā kimevaṃ tvayā proktaṃ kiṃ buddho'tāpasā'yatiḥ |
pūraṇo brahmavijñastapasvī saṃyamī sudhīḥ || 26.61{61} ||
tattasya pūraṇasyāgre buddhaḥ kiṃ vaktumutsahet |
bālakāneva vañcitvā hyasaddharmme yunakti saḥ || 26.62{62} ||
pūraṇa eva sarveṣāṃ yatīnāṃ brahmacāriṇām |
ṛddhimatāṃ mahānagro mātraikaścittato'dhikaḥ || 26.63{63} ||
iti tenoditaṃ śrutvā sa buddhopāsakastataḥ |
tīrthikopāsikaṃ dṛṣṭvā punareva tamabravīt || 26.64{64} ||
are kiṃ jñāyate tasya saṃbuddhasya guṇastvayā |
yenaiva kṛpayā dṛṣṭvā pālitaṃ putravajjagat || 26.65{65} ||
yasya loke dayā nāsti tena kiṃ pālyate jagat |
kevalalobhasatkāraṃ hetunādombhiko hi saḥ || 26.66{66} ||
tapasvī yogavitprājña ṛddhimān siddhimānapi |
yasya nāsti dayā satve sa kiṃ śāstā jagadguruḥ || 26.67{67} ||
yo dṛṣṭvā sakalān satvān kṛpayā saṃprabodhayan |
saddharme yojayatyatra sa eva jagatāṃ guruḥ || 26.68{68} ||
tasmādatra jagacchāstā sarvasatvahitārthabhṛt |
buddha eva jagannātho jagattrāyī jagadguruḥ || 26.69{69} ||
nāsti buddhasamaḥ kaścitsarvasatvahitaṃkaraḥ |
tena brahmādidevaiḥ sa vaṃdanīyo munīśvaraḥ || 26.70{70} ||
yato'yaṃ śrīghanaḥ trāyī ṣaḍabhijño vināyakaḥ |
munīndro'rhaṃ subhadrāṃśastena tasyādhiko hi kaḥ || 26.71{71} ||
tenāyaṃ sugato buddho dharmarājastathāgataḥ |
satkṛtya śraddhayā sarve bhajanīyaḥ śubhechubhiḥ || 26.72{72} ||
iti tenoditaṃ śrutvā tīrthikopāsako'tha saḥ |
roṣāgniparidagdhāsyastaṃ bauddhamevamabravīt || 26.73{73} ||
ā evamucyate ko'tra pūraṇātpara uttamaḥ |
yatīrvijño mahābhijñaḥ sa eva trijagadguruḥ || 26.74{74} ||
iti tenoditaṃ śrutvā saṃbuddhopāsako'pi saḥ |
sāṃjaliḥ praṇatiṃ kṛtvā bhūmāvevaṃ tamabravīt || 26.75{75} ||
buddhādanyo viśiṣṭaḥ kastraidhātubhuvaneṣvapi |
bahudhā kiṃ pravādena buddha eva jagatprabhuḥ || 26.76{76} ||
tenetyukte sa ruṣṭātmā tīrthikopāsakastadā |
kopāgniparidagdhāṃgastaṃ buddhamevamabravīt || 26.77{77} ||
yadi buddho viśiṣṭaḥ syātpūraṇādarhato yateḥ |
sarvasvaṃ me tavādhīnaṃ dāsaścāpi bhavāmyaham || 26.78{78} ||
yadi buddhādviśiṣṭo hi pūraṇo'rhaṃ mahāmatiḥ |
sarvasvaṃ te mamādhīnaṃ dāsaścāpi bhaveḥ khalu || 26.79{79} ||
iti tenodite tatra saṃbuddhopāsako'pi saḥ |
tatheti vaṃdhanikṣepaṃ kṛtvā svīkaraṇaṃ vyadhāt || 26.80{80} ||
tayoretatpravādatvaṃ prasenajinnarādhipaḥ |
(rm 307)
matvāmātyān samāhūya purarevamabhāṣata || 26.81{81} ||
gachadhvaṃ maṃtriṇaḥ sarve buddhatairthikayorapi |
nirṇayitvā vivādatve mimāṃsā kriyatāmiha || 26.82{82} ||
iti rājñā samādiṣṭaṃ śrutvā te maṃtriṇo janāḥ |
sahasā tajjanān sarvān samāhūyaivamādiśat || 26.83{83} ||
bhavanto nṛpaterevamājñāṃ śrutvā samāhitāḥ |
sarvatra vijite ghaṇṭānādaiścodaya taddrutam || 26.84{84} ||
iti tairmaṃtribhiḥ proktaiḥ śrutvā te sajanāstathā |
ghaṇṭāvaghoṣanaṃ kṛtvā sarvatraivaṃ ghoṣayan || 26.85{85} ||
śṛṇvantu paurikāḥ sarve ito'hni saptame'hani |
bauddhatairthikasaṃvāde mimāṃsātra bhaviṣyati || 26.86{86} ||
ye ye'tra draṣṭumichanti sarve te samupāgatāḥ |
buddhasya tīrthikasyāpi prabhāvaṃ draṣṭumarhata || 26.87{87} ||
evaṃ te vijite tatra sarvatra viṣayeṣvapi |
ghaṇṭāvaghoṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā maṃtriṇāṃ pura āyayuḥ || 26.88{88} ||
tataste sajanāḥ sarve rājño'ntikamupāgatāḥ |
praṇatvaitatpravṛttāṃtaṃ purataḥ saṃnyavedayan || 26.89{89} ||
tato'hni saptame prāpte sarve lokāḥ samāgatāḥ |
vistīrṇe bhūtale ramye taddraṣṭumupatasthire || 26.90{90} ||
tathā devā aneke ca taddarśanakutūhalāḥ |
antarīkṣe samāśritya saṃtasthuḥ pratimoditāḥ || 26.91{91} ||
evaṃ sarveṣu lokeṣu nṛpamaṃtrijanādiṣu |
naikaśatasahasreṣu sannipāteṣu sarvataḥ || 26.92{92} ||
tīrthikopāsakastatra sa gomaye praleṣite |
maṃḍale gaṃdhapuṣpādipūjāṃgaiḥ samapūjayat || 26.93{93} ||
tataḥ sa sāṃjalirnatvā jānubhyāṃ bhuvi saṃsthitaḥ |
udvahannuttarāsaṃgaṃ prārthayadevamādarāt || 26.94{94} ||
yena satyena ṣaḍvijñāḥ śāstāraḥ pūraṇādayaḥ |
bhagavaṃto mahābhijñā arhanto brahmacāriṇaḥ || 26.95{95} ||
anena satyabhāvena sarvānetān yatīśvarān |
etatpūjopahārāṇi prānugaṃchaṃtu saṃmukham || 26.96{96} ||
evaṃ saṃprārthanāṃ kṛtvā tīrthikopāsako mudā |
tāni paṃcopahārāṇi prādāya gagaṇe'kṣipat || 26.97{97} ||
tena kṣiprakṣiptamātrāṇi tāni sarvāṇi bhūtale |
patitānyastaṃgatānyeva sahasādarśane yayuḥ || 26.98{98} ||
taddṛṣṭvā sasurā lokāḥ sarve te bhūmipādayaḥ |
uccairnādairhasantastaṃ tairthikameva menire || 26.99{99} ||
taddṛṣṭvā dhikpravādaṃ ca śrutvā sa tairthiko'patat |
tataścirātsamutthāya lajjābhinnamukhāśayaḥ || 26.100{100} ||
trasito'dhomukhodraṣṭo niṣprabhaḥ parikaṃpitaḥ |
bhrastaskaṃdhaḥ kare kaṇḍaṃ sthāpya tasthau vimurchitaḥ || 26.101{1} ||
taddṛṣṭvā sa prasannātmā buddhopāsako mudā |
prakṛtvā maṇḍalaṃ tatra gomayapratilepite || 26.102{2} ||
(rm 308)
paṃcopahārapūjaṃgaiḥ samabhyarcya yathāvidhiḥ |
udvahannuttarāsaṅgaṃ jānubhyāṃ bhuvi saṃsthitaḥ || 26.103{3} ||
sāṃjaliḥ purataḥ sthitvā saṃbuddhaṃ taṃ munīśvaram |
smṛtvā natvā tathā satyaṃ prārthayadevamādarāt || 26.104{4} ||
yato'yaṃ bhagavān buddhaḥ sarvalokavināyakaḥ |
tena satyena sarvāṇi puṣpāṇīmāni sūdakam || 26.105{5} ||
idaṃ saurabhyadhūpaṃ ca bhagavantaṃ munīśvaram |
saṃbuddhaṃ śrīghanaṃ nāthaṃ prābhiyāntu vihāyasā || 26.106{6} ||
iti saṃprārthanāṃ kṛtvā sa buddhopāsako mudā |
tāni sarvopahārāṇi prādāya gagaṇe'kṣipat || 26.107{7} ||
tāni tena prakṣiptāni sarvāṇi gagaṇe'saran |
tāni sarvāṇi puṣpāṇi chatrībhūtvācaran kramāt || 26.108{8} ||
dhūpo meghavadambho'pi vaiḍūryyavattadācarat |
taddṛṣṭvā janatā sarvā mahāścaryyaṃ pralehire || 26.109{9} ||
krameṇa tāni sarvāṇi pracaranti vihāyasā |
jhaṭiti jetakāraṇye vihāre saṃmukhaṃ yayuḥ || 26.110{10} ||
dṛṣṭvā sajanakāyo'pi pratihāryaṃ tadadbhutam |
jayaśabdaṃ prakurvantaḥ paśyanto'nuyayurmudā || 26.111{11} ||
tatra sarvāṇi puṣpāṇi chatrībhūtvā jagadguroḥ |
saṃbuddhasya jagacchāsturupari samupāśrayan || 26.112{12} ||
taddhūpamagratastasya tasthau kṛtvā pradakṣiṇām |
padāmbujaṃ tadambho'pi papāta viyataḥ sarat || 26.113{13} ||
tatsamālokya sarve te dvijādi paurikā janāḥ |
prasāditā munīndrasya sabhāyāṃ samupācaran || 26.114{14} ||
sarve pradakṣiṇīkṛtya kṛtāṃjalipuṭā mudā |
bhagāvantaṃ muniṃ natvā sarve te samupāśrayan || 26.115{15} ||
sa buddhopāsakaścāpi natvā taṃ śrīghanaṃ mudā |
tatsaddharmāmṛtaṃ pātumupatasthau kṛtāṃjaliḥ || 26.116{16} ||
tīrthikopāsakaḥ so'pi taddṛṣṭvā vismayānvitāḥ |
mahadbuddhānubhāvatvaṃ matvā draṣṭumupāśrayat || 26.117{17} ||
tataḥ sa bhagavān dṛṣṭvā sarvānstān samupasthitān |
āryasatyamupādiśya punarevamupādiśat || 26.118{18} ||
śṛṇudhvaṃ sādhavaḥ sarve āryadharmaṃ samucyate |
yattriratnaṃ samākhyātaṃ tattrilokye mahattaram || 26.119{19} ||
tadyathā sarvalokānāṃ traidhātukanivāsinām |
sarveṣāmapi bhadrārthaṃ saddharmaṃ saṃprakāśaye || 26.120{20} ||
sa saṃbuddho jagacchāstā sarvasatvahitaṃkaraḥ |
jagannātho jagattrātā buddharatnamidaṃ varam || 26.121{21} ||
atra yaiḥ śraddhayā karmaṃ dānādi sukṛtaṃ kṛtam |
teṣāmagraprasannānāṃ vipākaiḥ phalamuttamam || 26.122{22} ||
aprameyamasaṃkhyeyaṃ saṃbodhipadadāyakam |
(rm 309)
evaṃ matvātra saṃbuddhaṃ bhajata sarvadā mudā || 26.123{23} ||
yāvataścātra saṃsāre saṃskṛtā vāpyasaṃskṛtāḥ |
dharmāsteṣāṃ mahānagro virāgo dharmamuttamaḥ || 26.124{24} ||
dharmaratnamidaṃ ye'tra prabhajanti prasāditāḥ |
teṣāmagraprasannānāṃ vipāke phalamuttamam || 26.125{12} ||
aprameyamasaṃkhyeyaṃ saṃbodhijñānadāyakam |
tadetatsaugataṃ dharmaṃ śrutvā bhajaṃti saṃvaram || 26.126{13} ||
yāvantaścātra trailokye saṃghāḥ pūgā gaṇāḥ smṛtāḥ |
teṣāmagro mahān khyāto buddhasaṃgho jagadvaraḥ || 26.127{14} ||
saṃgharatnamidaṃ ye'tra bhajanti śraddhayā mudā |
teṣāmagraprasannānāṃ vipāke phalamuttamam || 26.128{15} ||
aprameyamasaṃkhyeyaṃ saṃbodhimārgacālakam |
tadatra saugate saṃghe bhajata śraddhayādarāt || 26.129{16} ||
idaṃ ratnatrayaṃ khyātaṃ sarvasatvaśubhaṃkaram |
vāñchitārthapradaṃ siddhaṃ saṃbodhijñānadāyakam || 26.130{17} ||
tadetattritayaṃ ratnaṃ saṃbodhipadavāṃchibhiḥ |
satkṛtya śraddhayā nityaṃ pūjanīyaṃ samādarāt || 26.131{18} ||
ye triratnaṃ sadā nityaṃ pūjayanti prasāditāḥ |
na te māravaśaṃ yātā bhavaṃti bodhilābhinaḥ || 26.132{19} ||
iti matvā triratnaṃ taddṛṣṭvā kṛtvānumodanām |
sāñjalipraṇatiṃ kṛtvā dūrādvanditumarhatha || 26.133{20} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa śrutvā te brāhmaṇādayaḥ |
sarve lokāḥ prasīdantastriratnaśaraṇaṃ yayuḥ || 26.134{21} ||
tatra ke cijjanā bhadrāḥ saṃbuddhaśaraṇaṃ gatāḥ |
śikṣāpadaṃ samādāya prācaratsaugataṃ vratam || 26.135{22} ||
ke cidbhadrāśayā vijñā saṃbuddhaśaraṇaṃ gatāḥ |
pravrajyāvratamādāya prācaradbrahmacārikām || 26.136{23} ||
tataḥ kleśān vinirjitya saṃsārabhogyanispṛhāḥ |
sākṣādarhatpadaṃ prāpya babhūva brahmacāriṇaḥ || 26.137{24} ||
etatsarvaṃ samālokya tīrthikopāsako'pi saḥ |
vismitaḥ suprasannātmā triratnaṃ śaraṇaṃ yayau || 26.138{25} ||
tataḥ sa tairthikastasya munīndrasya padāmbuje |
sāñjalipraṇataścaivaṃ praṇidhānaṃ vyadhānmudā || 26.139{26} ||
adyārabhya sadāpyasya munīndrasya jagadguroḥ |
saddharmaṃ samupāśritya triratnopāsako bhave || 26.140{27} ||
etatpuṇyavipākena loke'ndhe'parināyake |
saṃbodhijñānamāsādya bhaveyaṃ sugato jinaḥ || 26.141{28} ||
atīrṇāṃ tārayiṣyāmi mocayiṣyāmyamocitān |
sarvānāśvāsayiṣyāmi hyanāśvastān suduḥkhinaḥ || 26.142{29} ||
satvānnirvāpayiṣyāmi bhavālayādanirvṛtān |
kariṣyāmi sadā bhadraṃ traidhātubhuvaneṣvapi || 26.143{30} ||
(rm 310)
iti tena kṛtaṃ bodhipraṇidhānaṃ svacetasā |
matvā sa bhagavān smitaṃ visasarja saraśmikam || 26.144{31} ||
tadā tā raśmayaḥ paṃcavarṇāḥ sarvāḥ samaṃtataḥ |
lokeṣu prasṛtā bhadraṃ saukhyaṃ kṛtvā praserire || 26.145{32} ||
yā adhastādgatābhāso sarvatra narakeṣu tāḥ |
prasṛtā avabhāsyaivaṃ bhadrasaukhyaṃ pracakrire || 26.146{33} ||
tatprabhābhiḥ parispṛṣṭā sarve te narakāśritāḥ |
nirduḥkhā saukhyamāsādya vismayaṃ samupāyayuḥ || 26.147{34} ||
tataste nārakāḥ satvāḥ sarve te narake sthitāḥ |
ekaikanarakeṣvevaṃ saṃmīlya saṃvabhāṣire || 26.148{35} ||
aho citraṃ kimadyaivaṃ mahatsaukhyaṃ prajāyate |
itaścyutāḥ kimanyatra samutpannā vayaṃ khalu || 26.149{36} ||
iti saṃvādināṃ tesāṃ sarveṣāṃ saṃprabodhane |
bhagavānnirmitaṃ buddhaṃ prerayannarake prati || 26.150{37} ||
taṃ dṛṣṭvā nārakāḥ sarve vismitāḥ saṃprasāditāḥ |
bhūya ekatra saṃmīlya mitha evaṃ vabhāṣire || 26.151{38} ||
aho vayamitaścyutvā naivānyatra gatāḥ khalu |
kiṃ tvasyāpūrvarūpasya prabhāvānnaḥ śubhāyate || 26.152{39} ||
nūnamayaṃ jagannātho buddho'smān duḥkhapīḍitān |
samīkṣya kṛpayā dṛṣṭyā samuddharttumihāgataḥ || 26.153{40} ||
tadasyātra vayaṃ sarve satkṛtya śraddhayādarāt |
sarvadā śaraṇaṃ gatvā bhaktumarhāmahe'dhunā || 26.154{41} ||
iti saṃbhāṣaṇāṃ kṛtvā sarve te saṃprasāditāḥ |
asyaiva śaraṇaṃ gatvā praṇatvā saṃprabhejire || 26.155{41!} ||
tataste nārakāḥ sarve tatpāpaparimocitāḥ |
pariśuddhāḥ śubhātmānaḥ sadgatiṃ sahasā yayuḥ || 26.156{42} ||
evaṃ tānnārakān sarvāḥ samuddhṛtya prabhāśca tāḥ |
sarvāḥ pratyāgatāstatra saṃbuddhasya puro'saran || 26.157{43} ||
evamūrddhaṃ gatāyāśca raśmayastāḥ samaṃtataḥ |
sarvān devālayāṃ lokānavabhāsya praserire || 26.158{44} ||
yāvadbhavāgraparyantamavabhāsya samaṃtataḥ |
prasāritāḥ tato gāthā nisṛtāḥ samacodayan || 26.159{45} ||
anityaṃ khalu saṃsāraṃ duḥkhaṃ śūnyamanātmakam |
puṇyameva jagatsāraṃ tatpuṇyaṃ cinutādarāt || 26.160{46} ||
niṣkrāmatārabhadhvaṃ tadyujyadhvaṃ buddhaśāsane |
dhunīta mṛtyusainyāni carata saugataṃ vratam || 26.161{47} ||
yo hyasmin saugate dharme cariṣyati samāhitaḥ |
sa hitvā janma saṃsāre duḥkhasyāntaṃ kariṣyati || 26.162{48} ||
tattriratnaṃ sadā yūyaṃ saṃsmṛtvā śaraṇaṃ gatāḥ |
satkṛtya śraddhayā nityaṃ bhajata bodhiprāptaye || 26.163{49} ||
evaṃ tadghoṣamākarṇya sarvadevāḥ prabodhitāḥ |
triratnaṃ śaraṇaṃ kṛtvā prabhejire sadādarāt || 26.164{50} ||
(rm 311)
evaṃ tā raśmayo devāñcodayitvā samaṃtataḥ |
punaḥ pratyāgatāstatra buddhāntikamupāyayuḥ || 26.165{51} ||
tatra tā raśmayaḥ sarvā ekībhūtvābhipiṇḍitāḥ |
tridhā pradakṣiṇīkṛtya sūṣṇīṣe'ntardadhurmuneḥ || 26.166{52} ||
taddṛṣṭvā sasabhālokāḥ sarve te vismayānvitāḥ |
śāstā kimādiśeddharmamiti dhyātvā niṣedire || 26.167{53} ||
athāyuṣmānmahānando matvā tallokaciṃtitam |
utthāya sāṃjālirnatvā papracha taṃ munīśvaram || 26.168{54} ||
vigatoddhavā dainyamadaprahīṇā buddhā jagatyuttamahetubhūtāḥ |
nākāraṇe śaṃkhamṛṇālagauraṃ smitamupadeśayanti jinā jitārayaḥ || 26.169{55} ||
tatkālaṃ svayamevamadhigamya dhīra buddhyā śrotṝṇāṃ śramaṇa jineṃdra kāṅkṣitānāṃ dhīrabhir |
munivṛṣa vāgbhiruttamābhirutpannaṃ vyapanaya saṃśayaṃ śubhābhiḥ || 26.170{56} ||
nākasmāllavaṇajalādrirājadhairyāḥ saṃbuddhāḥ smitamupadarśayanti nāthāḥ |
yasyārthe smitamupadarśayaṃti dhīrāstaṃ śrotuṃ samabhilaṣanti janoghāḥ || 26.171{57} ||
tadbhavān sarvasatvānāṃ cittābhiparibodhane |
yadabhilaṣitaṃ kāryaṃ tadupadeṣṭumarhati || 26.172{58} ||
iti saṃprārthite tena bhagavān sa munīśvaraḥ |
āyuṣmantaṃ tamānaṃdaṃ samālokyaivamādiśat || 26.173{59} ||
evametanmahānaṃda nāhetupratyayaṃ jināḥ |
saṃbuddhāḥ sugatāḥ sarve darśayanti smitaṃ kvacit || 26.174{60} ||
śṛṇvānaṃda yadarthe'haṃ smitaṃ muñcāmi sāṃpratam |
tadarthaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi sarvasatvaprabodhane || 26.175{61} ||
paśyasi yadayaṃ śreṣṭhī tīrthikopāsako mudā |
mamātra śaraṇaṃ gatvā praṇidhānaṃ karoti hi || 26.176{62} ||
etatpuṇyavipākena trikalpāntamanātmavit |
kramādbodhicārīḥ sarvāḥ paripūrya bhavetsudhīḥ || 26.177{63} ||
tataḥ kleśagaṇāñjitvā sarvānmāragaṇānapi |
bodhiṃ prāpyācalo nāma tathāgato bhaviṣyati || 26.178{64} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa śrutvānaṃdādi bhikṣavaḥ |
sarve te suprasannāsyāḥ prābhyanandan savismayāḥ || 26.179{65} ||
tathā tena munīndreṇa vyākhyātaṃ sa tairthikaḥ |
śrutvānumodanāṃ kṛtvā prābhyanandan savismayaḥ || 26.180{66} ||
tataḥ sa sarvadā nityaṃ satkṛtya śraddhayā mudā |
triratnabhajānaṃ kṛtvā prācaratsaugataṃ vratam || 26.181{67} ||
iti me guruṇākhyātaṃ śrutaṃ mayā tathocyate |
tvamapyevaṃ mahārāja triratnasevako bhava || 26.182{68} ||
prajāścāpi tathā rājaṃ bodhayitvā prayatnataḥ |
triratnabhajanotsāhe saṃcārayitumarhasi || 26.183{69} ||
tathā te sarvadā nityaṃ sarvatrāpi śubhaṃ bhavet |
(rm 312)
kramādbodhiṃ samāsādya saṃbuddhapadamāpnuyāt || 26.184{70} ||
iti tenārhatādiṣṭaṃ śrutvāśokaḥ sa bhūpatiḥ |
satyamiti pratijñāya prābhyanāndatsa pārthikaḥ || 26.185{71} ||
śṛṇvantīdaṃ narā ye munivarakathitaṃ tairthikasyāvadānaṃ ye cāpi śrāvayanti pramuditamānasaḥ śraddhayā bhaktiyuktāḥ |
te sarve bodhisatvāḥ sakalaguṇabharā bhadracaryā caranto bhuktvā satsaukhyabhadraṃ jinavaranilaye saṃprayānti pramodāḥ || 26.186{72} ||

++ iti śrīratnāvadānatatve tairthikāvadānaṃ samāptam ++


(rm 313)
xxvii mālikāvadāna
athāśoko mahīnāthaḥ kṛtāṃjalipuṭo mudā |
upaguptaṃ tamarhantaṃ praṇatvā prārthayatpunaḥ || 27.1{1} ||
bhadanta śrotumichāmi punaranyatsubhāṣitam |
tadyathā guruṇādiṣṭaṃ tathādeṣṭuṃ tvamarhati || 27.2{2} ||
iti tena narendreṇa prārthitaṃ sa yatīśvaraḥ |
upagupto narendran taṃ samālokyaivamādiśat || 27.3{3} ||
śṛṇu sādhu mahārāja yathā me guruṇoditam |
tathāhaṃ tatpravakṣyāmi tava puṇyavivṛddhaye || 27.4{4} ||
tadyathā bhagavān buddhaḥ śākyasiṃho jagadguruḥ |
sarvajñaḥ sugataḥ śāstā dharmarājo munīśvaraḥ || 27.5{5} ||
ekasmin samaye tatra śrāvastyā upakaṇṭhike |
jetāraṇye mahodyāne vihāre śrāvakaiḥ saha || 27.6{6} ||
bhikṣubhiścailakaiścāpi bhikṣuṇībhirupāsakaiḥ |
upāsikābhiranyaiśca bodhisatvagaṇairapi || 27.7{7} ||
sarvasatvahitārthena bodhicaryaṃ prakāśayan |
saddharmaṃ samupādiśya vijahāra prabhāsayan || 27.8{8} ||
tatsaddharmāmṛtaṃ pātuṃ sarve lokāḥ samāgatāḥ |
śakrādayo'pi devendrā brahmādilokapālakāḥ || 27.9{9} ||
daityeṃdrā garuḍā nāgā yakṣagaṃdharvakiṃnarāḥ |
siddhā vidyādharāścāpi rākṣasāśca maharddhikāḥ || 27.10{10} ||
ṛṣayo brāhmaṇā cāpi kṣatriyāśca narādhipāḥ |
vaiśyā rājakumārāśca maṃtriṇo'mātyakā janāḥ || 27.11{11} ||
gṛhasthāḥ sārthavāhāśca vaṇijaḥ śilpino'pi ca |
paurā jānapadā grāmyā anye kārpaṭikā api || 27.12{12} ||
sarve te samupāgatya tatra taṃ śrīghanaṃ munim |
dṛṣṭvā pradakṣiṇīkṛtya praṇatvā samupācaran || 27.13{13} ||
yathākramaṃ samabhyarcya natvā sāṃjalayo mudā |
parivṛtya puraskṛtya sarve te samupāśrayan || 27.14{14} ||
tān sarvān samupāsīnāṃ dṛṣṭvā sa bhagavānmuniḥ |
āryasatyaṃ samārabhya dideśa dharmamuttamam || 27.15{15} ||
taddharmaṃ samupākarṇya sarve te saṃprabodhitāḥ |
dharmavaiśeṣamājñāya babhuvurbodhivāṃchitāḥ || 27.16{16} ||
tadā tatra pure rājā prasenajitkauśalaḥ |
yadā na sugatastatra tadā tīrthikabhāgabhūt || 27.17{17} ||
yadā tu sugatastatra sasāṃghikaḥ samāgataḥ |
tadā sa nṛpatirnityaṃ śrīghanaṃ taṃ sadābhajat || 27.18{18} ||
tasmiṃśca samaye tatra kaścidārāmiko vanāt |
(rm 314)
navaṃ padmaiḥ samādāya rājñe dātuṃ pure'viśat || 27.19{19} ||
tatraikapathi taṃ dṛṣṭvā tīrthikopāsiko mudā |
sahasā tasya puro gatvā prārthayattaṃ saroruham || 27.20{20} ||
bho mālinnidaṃ padmaṃ yadi vikretumichasi |
ucitaṃ mūlyamādāya mahyaṃ dātuṃ tvamarhasi || 27.21{21} ||
iti tenārthitaṃ śrutvā mālikaḥ lubhitāśayaḥ |
yadīchati bhavān dadyāmiti taṃ dātumaichata || 27.22{22} ||
tasminneva kṣaṇe tatra gṛhastho'nāthapiṇḍadaḥ |
puṣpārthī samupāyāto mālikaṃ tamapṛchata || 27.23{22} ||
bho mālinnidaṃ padmaṃ yadi vikretumichati |
dviguṇaṃ mūlyamādāya bhavānme dātumarhati || 27.24{23} ||
iti tenoditaṃ śrutvā tīrthikopāsako'pi saḥ |
taddviguṇena mūlyena tadpadmaṃ samayācata || 27.25{24} ||
iti tenārthitaṃ śrutvā sa cāpyanāthapiṇḍadaḥ |
taddvaiguṇyena mūlyena kamalaṃ tamayācata || 27.26{25} ||
tacchrutvā tairthikaḥ so'pi madadarpābhimāninaḥ |
tattriguṇamūlyena gṛhītuṃ taṃ samaichata || 27.27{26} ||
taddṛṣṭvā sa gṛhastheṇānāthapiṇḍadotsahī |
caturguṇena mūlyena padmaṃ tamabhyayācata || 27.28{27} ||
evaṃ tau gṛhapatī so tha yāvacchatasaharsrakaiḥ |
mūlya taṃ padmamādāya mālinaṃ taṃ yayācatuḥ || 27.29{28} ||
taddṛṣṭvārāmikaḥ sa'pi pravarddhayan parasparam |
tayordvayoradatvaiva manasaivaṃ vyaciṃtayat || 27.30{29} ||
ayaṃ gṛhapatiḥ śreṣṭho hyanāthapiṇḍadaḥ sudhīḥ |
acapalo mahādhīraḥ sthirasatvo vicakṣaṇaḥ || 27.31{30} ||
kasyārthe nvidaṃ padmamiyanmūlyena yācate |
nūnamatra mahaddheturbhaviṣyati na saṃśayaḥ || 27.32{31} ||
tadetatparipṛchāva dāsyāmi sadguṇārthine || 27.33{32} ||
iti dhyātvā svacittena sa ārāmika utsukaḥ |
tairthikaṃ taṃ samālokya paprachaivaṃ samādarāt || 27.34{33} ||
bho puruṣa bhavān kasya kāryasyārthe idaṃ kajam |
iyanmūlyena vikretumichati tadvadasva me || 27.35{34} ||
iti tenoditaṃ śrutvā sa tairthikaḥ pramoditaḥ |
ārāmikaṃ tamālokya prabodhayitumabravīt || 27.36{35} ||
yaḥ śrīpatirjagadbharttā nārāyano janārdanaḥ |
tasya pūjāṃ kariṣyāmi tadidaṃ dehi me'mbujam || 27.37{36} ||
iti tenoditaṃ śrutvārāmiko sānubodhitaḥ |
anāthapiṇḍadaṃ taṃ ca dṛṣṭvā kāryamapṛchata || 27.38{37} ||
bho sādho bhavaṃścāpi kimarthamidamambujam |
bahumūlyaiva samāharttumichati tadvadasva me || 27.39{38} ||
iti tadvacanaṃ śrutvā so'nāthapiṇḍadaḥ sudhīḥ |
ārāmikaṃ tamālokya prabodhayitumabravīt || 27.40{39} ||
sādhu sādhu mahābhāga yadidaṃ te saroruham |
bahumūlyena vāṃchāmi tadarthaṃ kathyate śṛṇu || 27.41{40} ||
(rm 315)
yo'tra śauddhodanī rājā cakravarttī nṛpādhipaḥ |
sarvasatvahitārthena saṃbodhisādhanodyataḥ || 27.42{41} ||
sarvatra bhuvane loke kleśākulāsamāhite |
bodhicaryāvihīne'tra mlechadharmapravarttate || 27.43{42} ||
bodhicaryāṃ svayaṃ dhṛtvā prakāśayitumudyataḥ |
sarvarājyaṃ parityajya sarvāṃścāpi parigrahān || 27.44{43} ||
vairāgyadharmamāśritya saṃsārabhoganispṛhaḥ |
gayāśīrṣe nage gatvā kṛtvā tapaḥ suduṣkaram || 27.45{44} ||
tato loke hitaṃ kartuṃ samutthāya samāhitaḥ |
bodhivṛkṣatalāsīnastasthau dhyānasamāhitaḥ || 27.46{45} ||
tatra māragaṇān sarvāñjitvā kleśagaṇānapi |
saṃbodhijñānamāsādya buddho bhavati sāṃpratam || 27.47{46} ||
so'yaṃ buddho jagacchāstā jagannātho jagadguruḥ |
jagadbharttā jagattrāyī dharmarājā munīśvaraḥ || 27.48{47} ||
sarvajñaḥ sugataḥ śrimān ṣaḍabhijño vināyakaḥ |
satyavādī mahādhīraḥ satyadharmaśubhārthabhṛt || 27.49{48} ||
samantabhadrarūpāṃśaḥ saumyarūpo jiteṃdriyaḥ |
dvātriṃśallakṣaṇāśītivyaṃjanapratimaṃḍitaḥ || 27.50{49} ||
bhagavāñchrīghano brahmavihāriko guṇākaraḥ |
sarvatraidhātukādhīśaḥsarvaduṣṭapraśāntakṛt || 27.51{50} ||
sarvamāravijetārhaṃ saṃbodhimārgadeśakaḥ |
vāñchitārthapradātāraściṃtāmaṇiguṇādhikaḥ || 27.52{51} ||
śuddhaśīlaḥ śubhācāraḥ kṣāntivratadharaḥ sudhīḥ |
sarvasatvahitodyogī niḥkleśaḥ sarvajitkṛtī || 27.53{52} ||
samādhidhyānasaṃraktaḥ saṃbodhidharmabhāskaraḥ |
prajñānidhirmahāvijñaḥ sarvavidyāviśāradaḥ || 27.54{53} ||
sarvopāyavidhānajñaḥ praṇidhipāratārakaḥ |
trailokavijayī vīraḥ saṃbodhikāryapūrakaḥ || 27.55{54} ||
vijñānormisamādhisthaḥ saṃbodhijñānaratnabhṛt |
sarvasatvaśubhaṃkārī sarvākārasvarūpikaḥ || 27.56{55} ||
śākyamunirmahābuddho jineśvarastathāgataḥ |
sarvasatvahitārthena sarvatra samupācaran || 27.57{56} ||
bhāsayan sakalān lokān bhadraṃ kṛtvā samaṃtataḥ |
ihāpi sarvasatvānāṃ bhadraṃ kartuṃ samāgataḥ || 27.58{57} ||
sāṃprataṃ jetakārāme vihāre saha sāṃghikaiḥ |
saddharmamādiśannityaṃ viharati prabhāsayan || 27.59{58} ||
tasya pūjāmahaṃ kartumichāmi sāṃprataṃ khalu |
tadarthe bahumūlyena yācāmīdaṃ saroruham || 27.60{59} ||
tadbhavānmūlyamādāya dehi me idamambujam |
yathābhilaṣitaṃ dravyaṃ dāsyāmi te prasāṇayan || 27.61{60} ||
śraddhayedaṃ pradattaṃ cettvayāhaṃ saṃpramoditaḥ |
etatpuṇyavipākena bodhicittamavāpsyasi || 27.62{61} ||
sarvadā sadgatiṃ yāyāddurgatiṃ na kadā cana |
kramādbodhicarīḥ pūrya saṃbodhimapi prāpnuyāḥ || 27.63{62} ||
(rm 316)
evaṃ matvā bhavān sādho yadi saṃbodhimichati |
yathābhilaṣitaṃ dravyamādāyedaṃ pradehi me || 27.64{63} ||
iti tenārthitaṃ śrutvā sa mālikaḥ pramoditaḥ |
taṃ gṛhasthamanāthānāṃ bharttāramevamabravīt || 27.65{64} ||
aho buddho mahābhijño dṛśyate na kadā cana |
īdṛgmahadguṇaṃ cāpi śruyate na kvacinmayā || 27.66{65} ||
tadahaṃ taṃ jagannāthaṃ draṣṭumichāmi sāṃpratam |
tatsaddharmāmṛtaṃ pātuṃ vāṃchā me jāyate khalu || 27.67{66} ||
tasmāttatra svayaṃ gatvā saṃbuddhaṃ taṃ munīśvaram |
anena puṇḍarīkena pūjayiṣye'hamādarāt || 27.68{67} ||
tadbhavān kṛpayā sādho saṃbuddhasya jagadguroḥ |
nītvā māmāśrame tatra saddharme yoktumarhati || 27.69{68} ||
iti tenārthitaṃ śrutvā so'nāthapiṇḍado mudā |
mālikaṃ taṃ samālokya punarevamabhāṣata || 27.70{67} ||
sādhu sādhu mahābhāga kuruṣvevaṃ yadīchasi |
prāgacha tvaṃ mayā sārddhaṃ pūjaye taṃ munīśvaram || 27.71{68} ||
ityuktvā mālikaṃ taṃ sa gṛhapatiḥ prasādayan |
ādāya jetakārāme vihāre samudācarat || 27.72{69} ||
tatra tena sahāyebhya so'nāthapiṇḍado gṛhī |
sāṃjalistaṃ jagannāthaṃ natvaikānte samāśrayat || 27.73{70} ||
tataḥ sa māliko drākṣīcchrīghanaṃ taṃ munīśvaraiḥ |
sabhāmadhyāsanāsīnaṃ bhikṣusaṃghapuraskṛtam || 27.74{71} ||
dvātriṃśallakṣaṇāśītivyāṃjanapratimaṇḍitam |
ratnarāśimivojvālaṃ vyomaprabhābhyalaṃkṛtam || 27.75{72} ||
samaṃtabhadrarūpāṃśaṃ saumyarūpaṃ śubhendriyam |
dṛṣṭvā sa mudito natvā kṛtvā tridhā pradakṣiṇam || 27.76{73} ||
tatpadmaṃ purataḥ kṣiptvā kṛtāṃjaliḥ puro mudā |
natvā pādau munestasya tatraikānte samāśrayat || 27.77{74} ||
kṣiptamātraṃ ca tatpadmaṃ sahasākāśaṃ saṃsaran |
chatrībhūtvā mune mūrddhnaruparisthaṃ vyarājata || 27.78{75} ||
taddṛṣṭvā te sabhālokaḥ sarve te vismayānvitāḥ |
aho buddhasya māhātmyamiti proktvā prasedire || 27.79{76} ||
taddṛṣṭvā mālikaḥ sa'tivismayānaṃditāśayaḥ |
utthāya sahasā śastuḥ pādayo nyapatannataḥ || 27.80{77} ||
tataḥ sa sāṃjalirnatvā pādābjavorjagadguroḥ |
saṃbuddhapadalābhāya praṇidhānaṃ vyadhāddhṛdo || 27.81{78} ||
etatpuṇyavipākena loke'ndhe'parināyake |
kleśākule vinaṣṭe'haṃ bhūyāsaṃ sugato jinaḥ || 27.82{79} ||
svayaṃ tarttumaśaktānaṃ tārayitā bhavodadhim |
tathā kleśābdhimagnānāṃ samuddharttā vimocākāḥ || 27.83{80} ||
dharme'nāśvasitānaṃ ca sādāśvāsayitāpi vai |
adṛṣṭabodhimārgānāṃ darśayitā bhaveya hi || 27.84{81} ||
(rm 317)
iti tena kṛtaṃ matvā praṇidhānaṃ sa sarvavit |
bhagavānmuditaḥ prāvirakārṣītsa smitaṃ tadā || 27.85{82} ||
tatra smite munīndrasya mukhapadmādvinirgatāḥ |
suraśmayaḥ paṃcavarṇāḥ prasesrustrijagatsvapi || 27.86{83} ||
yā adhastādgatābhāsastā sarvanarakeṣvapi |
avabhāsya śubhaṃ kṛtvā pracakru duḥkhanāśaṇam || 27.87{84} ||
tatprabhābhiḥ parispṛṣṭvā sarve te narakāśritāḥ |
duḥkhamuktā sukhaspṛṣṭā vismitā evamabruvan || 27.88{85} ||
aho kiṃ nu mahāsaukhyamasmākaṃ jāyate'dhunā |
itaścyutāḥ kuhānyatra samutpannā vayaṃ nu kim || 27.89{86} ||
iti cintāparitānāṃ teṣāṃ cittaprabodhane |
bhagavānnirmitaṃ buddhaṃ preṣayatprati nairayam || 27.90{87} ||
taddṛṣṭvā narakasthāste sarve'tivismayānvitāḥ |
parasparaṃ samālokya punarevaṃ vabhāṣire || 27.91{88} ||
bhavanto no itaścyutvā nānyatra saṃgatā vayam |
ihaiva narake sthāḥ sma kutrāpi calitā na hi || 27.92{89} ||
kiṃ tvayaṃ samupāyātā hyapūrvadarśanaḥ pumān |
nūnamasyānubhāvena jāyate śubhatā'dhunā || 27.93{90} ||
nūnamayaṃ mahāsatvo dṛṣṭvāsmān duḥkhapīḍitān |
kṛpayā sarvathoddharttuṃ narake'tra samāgataḥ || 27.94{91} ||
tadasya samupāśritya prakṛtvā śaraṇaṃ sadā |
praṇatvā śraddhayā nityaṃ bhajemahi samādarāt || 27.95{92} ||
iti saṃbhāṣya te sarve nārakīyāḥ prabodhitāḥ |
taṃ buddhaṃ śaraṇaṃ gatvā praṇatvā prābhajanmudā || 27.96{93} ||
tadbhajanodbhavaiḥ puṇyaiḥ sarve te narakotthitāḥ |
sahasā sadgatiṃ yātā babhūvurdharmabhājanāḥ || 27.97{94} ||
evaṃ tā raśmayaḥ sarvāḥ sarvāṃstānnarakāśritān |
samuddhṛtya punastatra jagacchāsturupāyayuḥ || 27.98{95} ||
yā cāpyūrddhvaṃ gatābhāsastāvabhāsya diśo'ṣṭa ca |
yāvadbhavāgraparyantamavabhāsyābhiprāsaran || 27.99{96} ||
gāthābhiśca surān sarvān divyakāyasukhāratān |
samantato mahacchabdaṃ ruccairevamacodayan || 27.100{97} ||
anityaṃ sarvasaṃsāraṃ duḥkhaṃ śūnyamanātmakam |
iti matvā śubhe dharmaṃ sarvadā caratādarāt || 27.101{98} ||
niṣkrāmatārabhadhvaṃ tadyujyadhvaṃ buddhaśāsane |
triratnaśaraṇaṃ kṛtvā bhajadhvaṃ sarvadādarāt || 27.102{99} ||
yo hyasmiṃ saugate dharme cariṣyati samāhitaḥ |
sa hitvā janma saṃsāre duḥkhasyāntaṃ kariṣyati || 27.103{100} ||
etacchabdaṃ samākarṇya sarve devāḥ prabodhitāḥ |
triratnaśaraṇaṃ kṛtvā prabhejire samāhitāḥ || 27.104{1} ||
evaṃ tāḥ raśmayaḥ paṃcaḥ sarvāṃllokān surānapi |
triratnaśaraṇe sthāpya punarmunerupāyayuḥ || 27.105{2} ||
tatra tā raśmayaḥ sarvā ekībhūtā ca piṇḍitāḥ |
(rm 318)
jinaṃ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya taduṣṇīṣe viśetpunaḥ || 27.106{3} ||
taddṛṣṭvā te sabhālokāḥ sarve te vismayānvitāḥ |
kimādeśenmunīndro'yamiti dhyātvā niṣedire || 27.107{2} ||
athā yuṣmān sa ānando matvā teṣāṃ vitarkitam |
utthāya sāṃjalirnatvā bhagavantaṃ tamabravīt || 27.108{5} ||
bhagavan bhavatā kena hetunā sṛjyate smitam |
nāhetu sugatāḥ sarve vihasanti kadā cana || 27.109{6} ||
yadarthe hetunā smitaṃ bhavānmuṃcati sāṃpratam |
tadarthaṃ śrotumichanti sarve lokā ime khalu || 27.110{7} ||
yadarthena bhavān smitaṃ prakaroti jagadguro |
tadarthaṃ samupādiśya sarvān lokān prabodhaya || 27.111{8} ||
ityānandoditaṃ śrutvā bhagavān sa munīśvaraḥ |
sarvān lokān samālokya tamānaṃdaṃ cābravīt || 27.112{9} ||
evametanmahānaṃda nāhetupratyayaṃ kvacit |
sarvabuddhāḥ pramuṃcanti smitaṃ kiṃcitkadā cana || 27.113{10} ||
paśya tvaṃ yadayaṃ sādhu ārāmikaḥ prasāditaḥ |
mamaivaṃ śraddhayā pūjāṃ karoti śraddhayā mudā || 27.114{11} ||
etatpuṇyavipākena māliko'yaṃ subuddhimān |
bodhicittaṃ samāsādya bodhicaryāṃ caran kramāt || 27.115{12} ||
sarvāḥ pāramitāḥ pūryya sarvānmāragaṇān vinijayan |
samyakṣaṃbodhimāsādya niḥkleśo'rhanmunīśvaraḥ || 27.116{13} ||
daśabalo jagannāthaḥ sarvasatvahitārthabhṛt |
padmottamo'bhidho buddhastathāgato bhaviṣyati || 27.117{14} ||
yadayaṃ mama saddharmasādhane'bhiprasāditaḥ |
saṃbodhipraṇidhiṃ dhṛtvā karoti śraddhayārcanām || 27.118{15} ||
evaṃ ye śraddhayā nityaṃ triratnaṃ śaraṇaṃ gatāḥ |
bhajanti te janāḥ sarve bhaveyuḥ sugatātmajāḥ || 27.119{16} ||
tataste sarvasatvānāṃ hitārtheṣu samudyatāḥ |
bodhisatvā mahāsatvā bhaviṣyaṃti guṇākarāḥ || 27.120{17} ||
tataḥ pāramitāḥ sarvāḥ paripūrya yathākramam |
tataḥ saṃbodhimāsādya bhaveyuḥ sugatā api || 27.121{18} ||
evaṃ matvātra saṃsāre saṃbodhijñānavāṃchibhiḥ |
triratnabhajanaṃ kṛtvā caritavyaṃ sadā śubhe || 27.122{19} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa śrutvānaṃdādayo'pi te |
bhikṣavaḥ sāṃghikāḥ sarve prābhyanandan prabodhitāḥ || 27.123{20} ||
sarve lokāśca te sarve śrutvaivaṃ śrīghanoditam |
triratnaśaraṇaṃ kṛtvā prabhejire'numoditāḥ || 27.124{21} ||
evaṃ śāstrā munīndreṇa vyākṛtaṃ svayamātmanaḥ |
śrutvā sabhāntikaścāpi prābhyanandatpramoditaḥ || 27.125{22} ||
tadārabhya sadā nityaṃ mālikaḥ so'numoditaḥ |
triratnabhajanaṃ kṛtvā prācaradbodhicārikām || 27.126{23} ||
tataḥ sānāthapiṇḍo'pi tena saha pramoditaḥ |
sāṃjalistaṃ jagannāthaṃ natvā sve nilaye yayau || 27.127{24} ||
(rm 319)
tatra sa māliko gehe bhāryayā saha moditaḥ |
triratnaśaraṇaṃ kṛtvā prācaratsarvadā śubham || 27.128{25} ||
tatastaṃ saugate dharme'nucarantaṃ niśamya saḥ |
tīrthikopāsiko ruṣṭo upetya paryabhāṣata || 27.129{26} ||
are re durmate pāpin kimevaṃ carase'śubhe |
sarvathā tvaṃ paribhraṣṭo narake duḥkhamāpsyasi || 27.130{27} ||
yadapātre durācāre mithyādharmābhibhāṣinī |
upetya bhajanaṃ kṛtvā vinoṣi kilviṣaṃ mahat || 27.131{28} ||
bhajante ye narā buddhaṃ na te gachanti sadgatim |
durgatimeva te yātāścarantyaśubhe sadā || 27.132{29} ||
kadā cidapi tadgehe bhadraṃ na jāyate kvacit |
lakṣmīścāpi vasennaiva yato dharmo vinaśyate || 27.133{30} ||
kiṃ cinnāsti śubhācāraṃ tasya bhikṣāśino'yateḥ |
kimevaṃ śraddhayā nityaṃ bhajase sadguroriva || 27.134{31} ||
kṛpaṇo yaḥ svayaṃ bhraṣṭaḥ parāṃśca bhraṃśayediha |
adharme ye'pi saddharmamityākhyāyābhivaṃcate || 27.135{32} ||
sa kiṃ śāstā sudhīrvijño dharmarājo munīśvaraḥ |
sadbhirna pūjanīyo'sau niṃdanīyo hi durgatiḥ || 27.136{33} ||
iti satyaṃ mayākhyātaṃ hitārthaṃ te śubhārthinaḥ |
yadi dharme'sti te vāṃchā śrutvāsmadvacanaṃ cara || 27.137{34} ||
tadbuddhasaṃgatiṃ tyaktvā tīrthe snātvā samāhitaḥ |
brahmaṇaṃ viṣṇumīśānaṃ bhaja nityaṃ yathāvidhim || 27.138{35} ||
brāhmaṇaṃ ye bhajaṃtyatra sarve te brahmacāriṇaḥ |
pariśuddhāśayā bhadrā saṃprayānti surālayam || 27.139{31} ||
ye bhajanti sadā viṣṇuṃ na te gachaṃti durgatim |
sadgatiṃ sarvadā yātā bhavanti manujādhipāḥ || 27.140{32} ||
ye bhajanti mahārudraṃ na te gachanti nārake |
sarvasaukhyāni saṃbhuktvā yānti cānte śivālayam || 27.141{33} ||
evaṃ matvātra saṃsāre yadi saukhyaṃ sadechasi |
tadetāṇtrijagannāthān bhaja nityaṃ prapūjayan || 27.142{34} ||
etanme vacanaiḥ śrutvā bhaja tān sadgurūn yatīn |
teṣāṃ saddharmamākarṇya cara nityaṃ śubhe vrate || 27.143{35} ||
tathā te maṃgalaṃ nityaṃ sarvadātra bhaveddhruvam |
ante pāpavinirmuktāḥ prayānti tridaśālayam || 27.144{36} ||
iti tenoditaṃ śrutvā mālikaḥ sa viroṣitaḥ |
tairthikaṃ taṃ samārabhya paribhāṣyaivamabravīt || 27.145{37} ||
ā kimevaṃ vadasyatra mā maivaṃ vadathāḥ punaḥ |
tvameva narakaṃ yāyā nindase yanmunīśvaram || 27.146{38} ||
śṛṇvatrāhaṃ pravakṣyāmi saṃbuddhe guṇamalpakam |
vistareṇa kathaṃ vaktuṃ śakyate'lpaśiyā mayā || 27.147{39} ||
tathāpi kathyate'lpatvāttavābhibodhane mayā |
(rm 320)
śrutvā tvayā vicāryaiva sadasacca parīkṣyatām || 27.148{40} ||
saṃbuddha eva bhagavān suguṇī na viṣṇuḥ saṃśasyate na hiraṇyagarbhaḥ |
teṣāṃ subhadracaritātiśayaprabhāvāñchrutvā vicārayata ko guṇavānna veti || 27.149{41} ||
viṣṇuḥ samudyatataho vighṛṇaiḥ pramāyī
rudro vibhūtyajakayoladharaḥ pramattaḥ |
ekāntaśāntacaritāśayastu buddhaḥ
kaṃ pūjayemahi suśāntamaśāntarūpam || 27.150{36!} ||
duryodhanādi nṛpanāśakaraḥ sa cakrī harastripūranāśakaḥ pinākī |
krauṃcaṃ guho'pi dṛḍhaśakti hate cakāra buddhastokavalamayastu jagatāṃ hiteṣī || 27.151{67} ||
pīḍyāmamāyamayameva tu rakṣaṇīyo vadhyo yamityapi surottamanītireṣā |
niḥśreyasātyudayasaukhyahitaikabuddhirbuddhasya naivaridavonahivam || 27.152{68} ||
rāgādi doṣajanitāni vacānsi viṣṇorunmattaceṣṭitakarāṇi ca yāni śambhoḥ |
niḥśeṣadoṣaśamakāritatathāgatasya vaṃdyatvamarhati ca ko'tra vicārayadhvam || 27.153{69} ||
yaścodyataḥ parivadhāya ghṛṇāṃ vihāya trāṇāya yaśca jagataḥ kṛpayā pravṛttaḥ |
rāgī ca yo bhavati yaśca vimuktarāgaḥ pūjyaṃstayoḥ ka iha taṃ vadatānucintya || 27.154{70} ||
śakraṃ vajradharaṃ balaṃ haladharaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ ca cakrāyudhaṃ skaṃdaṃ śaktidharaṃ śmaśānavilayaṃ rudraṃ triśūlāyudham |
etān duḥkhabhayāṅkitān gataghṛṇān bālān vicitrāyudhānnityaṃ prāṇivadhodyute praharaṇān kastānnamasyedbudhaḥ || 27.155{71} ||
na yaḥ śūlaṃ dhatte na ca suratimaṅke suvadanāṃ na cakraṃ śaktiṃ vā na ca kuliśamugraṃ rava halaṃ vinirmuktaṃ kleśaiḥ parahitavidhānādyatadhiyam |
śaraṇyaṃ lokānaṃ tamṛṣimupayātāsmi śaraṇam || 27.156{72} ||
rudro rāgavaśātstriyaṃ vahati yo hiṃsyā hriyā varjitā viṣṇuḥ krūrataraḥ kṛtaghnacaritaḥ skaṃdhaḥ svayaṃjñāti ha |
krūrāsyā mahiṣāntakṛnnaravaśā mānsāśinī pārvatī pānepsī ca vināyako daśabalo srasto hyadoṣaḥ suhṛt || 27.157{73} ||
vaṃdhurnabhe sa bhagavānna ripurna cānyaḥ śāstā trilokagururekatamo'pi dhīraḥ śrutvā vacaḥsucaritātiśayaprabhāvaṃ buddhaṃ guṇātiśayalobhatayāśritāḥ smaḥ || 27.158{74} ||
nāsmākaṃ sugataḥ pitā na ripavastīrthyāṃdhanaṃ naiva te |
dattaṃ tena tathāgatena ca hṛtaṃ kiṃcitkaṇādādibhiḥ |
kiṃ tvekāṃtajagaddhitaḥ sa bhagavān buddho yataścāmalaṃ vākyaṃ sarvamalāpahārica yatastadbhiktavaṃto vayam || 27.159{75} ||
hiteṣī yo nityaṃ satatamupakārīva jagataḥ kṛtaṃ yena svāsthyaṃ bahuvidharujārttasya jagataḥ |
(rm 321)
gūḍhaṃ yaśca jñe bhayaṃ karatalamivāvaiti sakalaṃ prapadyadhvaṃ santastemṛṣivaradaraṃ bhaktimanasaḥ || 27.160{76} ||
asarvabhāvena yadṛcchayā vā parānuvṛtya vicikitsayā vā |
ye taṃ namasyanti munīndracaṃdraṃ te'pyāmarīsaṃpadamāpnuvaṃti || 27.161{77} ||
paurāṇīśrutineṣa lokamahito buddhaḥ kilāyaṃ harirdṛṣṭvā janmajarāvipattivaśagaṃ lokaṃ kṛpābhyudyataḥ |
jātaḥ śākyakulenduracyutamatistrātā nṛṇāṃ gautamaḥ śāstāraṃ hitameva kastamadhunā nāvaiti mūḍho janaḥ || 27.162{78} ||
yadā rāgadveṣādasurasuradārāpaharaṇaṃ kṛtaṃ māyādvitvaṃ dharaṇiharaṇāśaktamatinā |
tadā pūjyo vandyo hariraparimukto'budhatayā vinirmuktaṃ buddhaṃ na namati jano māhavahulaḥ || 27.163{79} ||
caturjaladhimekhalākuratambabhārālasāṃ mahīṃ mahatīṃ visṛjya harayevāpakaṣṭāṃ valiḥ |
pradāya munaye tu pāṃśulavamapyaśoko nṛpaḥ kṣitiṃ sakalacaṃdramaṇḍalatarāñca saṃprāptavān || 27.164{80} ||
yakṣapāto na me buddhena dveṣaṭakapilādiṣu |
muktimacca vaco yasya kāryastasya parigrahaḥ || 27.165{81} ||
avaśyameṣāṃ katamo'pi sarvavitjagarddhitaikāntaviśālasāraḥ |
sa eva mṛgyo hyatisūkṣmacetasā janena śeṣaiḥ kimanarthapaṇḍitaiḥ || 27.166{82} ||
yasyāpi kiñcinna hi doṣaleśaṃ śaradguṇāḥ santi jagaddhitārthe |
brahmāpi viṣṇutiriṇo harirvā samehi śāstā śubhakārimitram || 27.167{83} ||
yasya na vidyate doṣo vidyante sakalā guṇāḥ |
sarvajñaḥ sa jagacchāstā tamahaṃ śaraṇaṃ vraje || 27.168{84} ||
yo'smin sudṛṣṭyātsaṃrakṣyati jagacchubhe |
tasyāhaṃ śaraṇaṃ gatvā bhajāmi satataṃ mudā || 27.169{85} ||
yaṃ dṛṣṭvā suprasannāste bhavanti nirmalāśayāḥ |
tameva sugataṃ nāthaṃ bhajāmi śaraṇaṃ gataḥ || 27.170{86} ||
yenaiva trijagallokaṃ putravatpratipālitam |
tameva trijagannāthaṃ bhajāmi śaraṇaṃ tataḥ || 27.171{87} ||
yasmai suśraddhayā dattaṃ tatphalaṃ bodhisādhanam |
tameva śrīghanaṃ bhaktyā bhajāmi śaraṇaṃ gataḥ || 27.172{88} ||
yasmāddharmāmṛtaṃ pītvā sarve satvāḥ pramodinaḥ |
tameva saṃti sarve'pi duḥkhachedanasadguṇāḥ |
tameva śrīpradātāraṃ bhajāmi śraddhayādarāt || 27.173{89} ||
buddha eva jagacchāstā buddha eva jagadguruḥ |
buddha eva jagannātho buddha eva munīśvaraḥ || 27.174{90} ||
tadahaṃ sarvabhāvena buddhasya śaraṇaṃ gataḥ |
śraddhayā dharmamāsādya bhajāmi bodhiprāptaye || 27.175{91} ||
ye buddhaṃ śaraṇaṃ yānti na te gachanti durgatim |
nirmuktapāpakāḥ saukhyaṃ bhuktvā yāṃti jinālayam || 27.176{92} ||
(rm 322)
śṛṇvanti saugataṃ vākyaṃ ye te na yānti durgatim |
samantabhadrasaukhyāni bhuktvā yāṃti sukhāvatim || 27.177{93} ||
bhajanti lokanāthaṃ ye te'pi na yānti durgatim |
pāpamuktāḥ sukhānyeva bhuktvā yāṃti surālayam || 27.178{94} ||
etadratnatrayaṃ loke utpannaṃ sadguṇākaram |
tadahaṃ śaraṇaṃ gatvā bhajāmi samupāsikaḥ || 27.179{95} ||
etatpuṇyavipākena sarvasatvahitārthakaḥ |
kramādbodhicarāḥ pūrya saṃbuddhapadamāpnuyāt || 27.180{96} ||
tadāhaṃ sugato bhūtvā sarvasatvaśubhārthabhṛt |
satvān bodhau pratiṣṭhāpya nirvṛtiṃ samavāpnuyāt || 27.181{97} ||
evaṃ vijñāya saddharmaṃ yadīchati bhavānapi |
triratnaśaraṇaṃ gatvā bhaja nityaṃ samādarāt || 27.182{98} ||
etatpuṇyavipākena sadā tvaṃ sadgatiṃ tataḥ |
bodhiṃ cāpi samāsādya saṃbuddhapadamāpnuyāḥ || 27.183{99} ||
iti tena samākhyātaṃ śrutvā sa paribodhitaḥ |
tairthyadharmaṃ pratikṣipya saṃbuddhaśrāvako'bhavat || 27.184{100} ||
tadārabhya sadā nityaṃ saddharmaśravaṇotsukaḥ |
vihāre samupāśritya prābhajattaṃ munīśvaram || 27.185{1} ||
iti me guruṇādiṣṭaṃ śrutaṃ mayā tathocyate |
tvaṃ cāpyevaṃ mahārāja triratnaṃ sarvadā bhaja || 27.186{2} ||
prajāścāpi mahārāja bodhayitvā prayatnataḥ |
bodhimārge pratiṣṭhāpya pālayasva samāhitaḥ || 27.187{3} ||
etatpuṇyavipākena sadā te maṃgalaṃ bhavet |
kramādbodhicarīḥ pūrya saṃbuddhapadamāpnuyāḥ || 27.188{4} ||
iti tenārhatādiṣṭaṃ śrutvāśokaḥ sa bhūmipaḥ |
tatheti prativijñapya prābhyanandatsapārṣadaḥ || 27.189{5} ||
śṛṇvantīdaṃ mudā ye jinavarakathitaṃ mālikasyāvadānaṃ ye cāpi śrāvayanti pramuditamanasaḥ śraddhayā bhadrakāmāḥ |
te sarve lokanāthāstrimalakalijitā bodhisatvā maheśā bhuktvā saukhyaṃ sadānte jinavaranilaye saṃprayānti prasannāḥ |

++ iti ratnāvadānatatve mālikāvadānaṃ samāptam ++


(rm 323)
xxviii pāñcālarājāvadāna
athāśoko mahīpālaḥ kṛtāṃjalipuṭo mudā |
upaguptaṃ guruṃ bhikṣuṃ natvaivaṃ punarabravīt || 28.1{1} ||
bhadanta śrotumichāmi punaranyatsubhāṣitam |
tadyathā guruṇākhyātaṃ tathādeṣṭuṃ ca me'rhati || 28.2{2} ||
iti saṃprārthite rājñā sa upagupta ātmavit |
tamaśokaṃ mahīpālaṃ samāmaṃtryaivamabravīt || 28.3{3} ||
sādhu śṛṇu mahārāja yathā me guruṇoditam |
tathāhaṃ te pravakṣyāmi śrutvānumodanāṃ kuru || 28.4{4} ||
tadyathāsau jagacchāstā śākyasiṃhastathāgataḥ |
sarvajñaḥ sugato nātho dharmarājā munīśvaraḥ || 28.5{5} ||
ekasmin samaye tatra śrāvastyā bahirāśrame |
vihāre jetakodyāne vijahāra sasāṃghikaḥ || 28.6{6} ||
tadā sa bhagavāṃstatra bhikṣusaṃghaiḥ puraskṛtaḥ |
sabhāmadhyāsanāsīno dharmamādeṣṭumārabhat || 28.7{7} ||
taddharmadeśanāṃ śrotuṃ sarve lokāḥ samāgatāḥ |
devā daityāśca nāgāśca yakṣagaṃdharvakinnarāḥ || 28.8{8} ||
siddhā vidyādharāścāpi garuḍā rākṣasā api |
ṛṣayo brāhmaṇā vijñā rājānaḥ kṣatriyā api || 28.9{9} ||
vaiśyā rājakumārāśca maṃtriṇaśca mahājanāḥ |
vaṇijaḥ sārthavāhāśca gṛhasthāḥ śilpino'pi ca || 28.10{10} ||
grāmyā jānapadāścāpi kārpaṭikādayo'pi ca |
tīrthikā api te sarve jetārāme upāgatāḥ || 28.11{11} ||
vihāre taṃ jagannāthaṃ dṛṣṭvā sāṃjalayo mudā |
natvā pradakṣiṇīkṛtya samabhyarcya yathākramam || 28.12{12} ||
tataste muditāḥ sarve parivṛtya samaṃtataḥ |
tatsaddharmāmṛtaṃ patumupatasthuḥ samāhitāḥ || 28.13{13} ||
tadā sa bhagavān dṛṣṭvā sarvānstān samupasthitān |
ādimadhyāntakalyāṇaṃ dideśa dharmamuttamam || 28.14{14} ||
tatsaddharmāmṛtaṃ pītvā sarve lokāḥ prabodhitāḥ |
saṃbuddhaṃ śaraṇaṃ kṛtvā pracerurbodhisaṃvaram || 28.15{15} ||
tasminnavasare tatra paṃcāla uttarādhipaḥ |
dakṣiṇādhipapaṃcālaṃ parājetumupākramat || 28.16{16} ||
tayoḥ paṃcālayo rājño viruddhāsyabhimāninoḥ |
mahāyuddhamabhūnnityaṃ parasparavighātanam || 28.17{17} ||
pravarttite sadā yuddhe tayo rājño hi māninoḥ |
durbhikṣamapi tatrābhūdbahulokavighātanam || 28.18{18} ||
(rm 324)
tadā tau kṣatriyau krūrau parasparavighātinau |
viruddhamānasau vīrau vairaśāmyaṃ na jagmataḥ || 28.19{19} ||
taddṛṣṭvā nṛpatī rājā prasenajin sa kauśalaḥ |
tadvairaśamanopāyaṃ ciraṃ dhyātvā vyaciṃtayat || 28.20{20} ||
yadetau nṛpatī vīrau nityaṃ yuddhābhikāṃkṣiṇau |
saṃbodhisādhanaṃ dharmaṃ śrotuṃ naivābhivāṃchata || 28.21{21} ||
sarve'pi maṃtriṇo'mātyāḥ sarve lokāśca yodhinaḥ |
saṃbuddhadarśane cāpi nātrāgachanti ke cana || 28.22{22} ||
evaṃ teṣāṃ sadā nityaṃ hiṃsākarmābhirāgiṇām |
saddharme śravaṇe vāpi gocaraṃ naiva vidyate || 28.23{23} ||
dharmaṃ vinātra saṃsāre kiṃ sāraṃ janma mānuṣe |
parasparaṃ nihatyaiva yāsyaṃti narake dhruvam || 28.24{24} ||
dhigjanma mānuṣe teṣāṃ kevalaṃ pāpasādhanam |
ye kleśamānadarpyāndhā na paśyaṃti munīśvaram || 28.25{25} ||
ye cātra saugataṃ dharmaṃ na śṛṇvanti kadā cana |
kathaṃ te bhadracaryāyāṃ gocaraṃ samavāpnuyuḥ || 28.26{26} ||
sadā te māracaryāyāṃ sthitvā kleśābhimāninaḥ |
bodhicaryāṃ pratikṣipya careyuḥ kāmacāriṇaḥ || 28.27{27} ||
tataḥ svayaṃ paribhraṣṭā bhraṃśayantaḥ parānapi |
yathechayā carantaste cinuyurbahupātakam || 28.28{28} ||
tataste kleśasaṃtaptāḥ svaparārthābhighātinaḥ |
tīvraduḥkhāgnisaṃtaptāḥ pateyurnarake dhruvam || 28.29{29} ||
narakeṣu bhramantaste sadā duḥkhābhibhojinaḥ |
paścāttāpāgnisaṃtaptāścareyurābhavaṃ tathā || 28.30{30} ||
tadeṣāṃ sarvalokānāṃ bhadrārthaṃ hitakāraṇam |
sarvathopāyamādhātumarhāmi sāṃprataṃ drutam || 28.31{31} ||
yāvadetau nṛpau vīrau viruddhau nopaśāmyataḥ |
tāvadatra bhuvi kvāpi maṃgalaṃ na bhavetsadā || 28.32{32} ||
tadetau nṛpatī tāvadbodhayitvā prayatnataḥ |
maitrasnehasaṃbaddhau kuryāṃ nirvairabhāvinau || 28.33{33} ||
etau hi kṣatriyau vīrau pāṃcālau mānagarvitau |
anayormitravaṃdhaṃ ko bodhayitvā kariṣyati || 28.34{34} ||
buddha eva jagacchāstā dharmarājaḥ prabodhayan |
anayościravairyāgniṃ śamayituṃ praśaknuyāt || 28.35{35} ||
tadatra sahasā gatvā prārthayeyaṃ munīśvaram |
nūnaṃ buddhānubhāvāttau nṛpau mitratvamāpsyataḥ || 28.36{36} ||
iti niścitya rājā sa prasenajitsamutthitaḥ |
vihāre sahasā gatvā nanāma taṃ munīśvaram || 28.37{37} ||
tatra sa purato gatvā sāṃjaliḥ samupāśrayan |
etatsarvapravṛttāṃtaṃ nivedyaivamabhāṣata || 28.38{38} ||
bhagavannātha sarvajña vijānīyādbhavānapi |
yattau pāṃcālabhūpālau viruddhau bhavato'dhunā || 28.39{39} ||
yadetayorviruddhe ta sarve lokā virodhitāḥ |
tadatra sarvadā yuddhaṃ pravarttate divāniśam || 28.40{40} ||
(rm 325)
tattesāṃ sarvalokānāṃ saddharme gocaraṃ kadā |
sadā yuddhābhisaktāste sādhayeyuḥ śubhaṃ katham || 28.41{41} ||
tadatra bhagavāñcchāstā bodhayaṃstau narādhipau |
saṃbodhimārgamādiśya śubhe cārayitumarhati || 28.42{42} ||
iti saṃprārthite tena rājñā sa bhagavānmuniḥ |
tasya rājñe nareṃdrasya tūṣṇībhūtvādhyuvāsa tat || 28.43{43} ||
tataḥ sa nṛpatī rājā matvā śāstrādhivāsitam |
sāṃjalistaṃ muniṃ natvā muditaḥ svālayaṃ yayau || 28.44{44} ||
tataḥ sa bhagavān pātramādāya cīvarāvṛtaḥ |
bhāsayan bhadratāṃ kurvan pratasthe saha sāṃghikaiḥ || 28.45{45} ||
evaṃ sarvatra mārgeṣu bhāsayaṃ sa munīśvaraḥ |
kṛtvā bhadraṃ krameṇaivaṃ vārāṇasīmupācarat || 28.46{46} ||
tatra sa bhagavāṃ prāpto bhābhiḥ saṃbhāsayan kramāt |
caraṃ kṛtvā subhadratvaṃ mṛgadāve upācarat || 28.47{47} ||
tatra sa bhagavān prātaḥ sasaṃghaḥ saugatāśrame |
sabhāmadhyāsanāsīnaḥ saddharmaṃ samupādiśat || 28.48{48} ||
tatsaddharmāmṛtaṃ pītvā sarve lokāḥ pramoditāḥ |
triratnaṃ śaraṇaṃ kṛtvā prabhejire samādarāt || 28.49{49} ||
tadā dakṣiṇapāṃcālanṛpatiḥ sa pramoditaḥ |
saṃbuddhadarśanaṃ kartuṃ mṛgadāve upācarat || 28.50{50} ||
tatra taṃ śrīghanaṃ dṛṣṭvā nṛpatiḥ sa pramoditaḥ |
sāṃjaliḥ purato gatvā natvaikānte upāśrayat || 28.51{51} ||
tasminnavasare tatra mṛgadāve samāśritaḥ |
bhagavānniti śuśrāva pāṃcālauttarādhipaḥ || 28.52{52} ||
sa uttarapāṃcālo nṛpatiḥ pratiroṣitaḥ |
taṃ nṛpaṃ yāmyapāṃcālaṃ parājetumupācarat || 28.53{53} ||
tatra sa nṛpatirvīraścaturaṃgavalaiḥ saha |
jayavādyamahotsāhaiḥ sahasā samupācarat || 28.54{54} ||
tatra sa yāmyapāṃcālarājā taṃ ripumāgatam |
niśamya sahasotthāya natvā ha taṃ munīśvaram || 28.55{55} ||
bhagavannātha sarvajña sa me vairā ihāgataḥ |
tadatra kiṃ kariṣyāmi tadanujñāṃ dadātu me || 28.56{56} ||
iti tena narendreṇa prārthite sa jineśvaraḥ |
dṛṣṭvā taṃ yāmyapāṃcālaṃ samāśvāsyaivamādiśat || 28.57{57} ||
nṛpate mā vibheṣī tvaṃ dhairyyamālambya tiṣṭhata |
tadupāyaṃ kariṣyāmi yenāsau te vaśe caret || 28.58{58} ||
ityādiśya sa sarvajñaścaturaṅgabalānvitam |
mahatsainyādhipaṃ vīraṃ nirmāya'preṣayattataḥ || 28.59{59} ||
sa sainyādhipatirvīraścaturaṅgabalaiḥ saha |
carannuttarapāṃcālamabhiyoddhumupācarat || 28.60{60} ||
tanmahatsainyamāyātaṃ samālokyābhyupadrutam |
sarva uttarapāṃcālasainyā bhītāḥ parāyayuḥ || 28.61{61} ||
taddṛṣṭvottarapāṃcālanṛpatiścāpi kheṭitaḥ |
ekākī rathamāruhya bhagavatsaṃmukhe'carat || 28.62{62} ||
(rm 326)
tatra sa dūrato dṛṣṭvā śrīghanaṃ taṃ sabhāśritam |
avatīrya rathāttatra mṛgadāve upācarat || 28.63{63} ||
tatra sa nṛpati rājā samupetya kṛtāṃjaliḥ |
tridhā pradakṣiṇīkṛtya nanāma śaraṇaṃ gataḥ || 28.64{64} ||
tataścirātsamutthāpya sa pāṃcalo narādhipaḥ |
tatsaddharmāmṛtaṃ pātuṃ tatraikānte samāśrayat || 28.65{65} ||
tadā sa bhagavān dṛṣṭvā nṛpatiṃ taṃ samāśritam |
āryyasatyaṃ samārabhya dideśa dharmamuttamam || 28.66{66} ||
tadāryasatyamākarṇya nṛpatiḥ so'bhibodhitaḥ |
saṃsārasādhanaṃ dharmaṃ punaḥ śrotuṃ samaichata || 28.67{67} ||
tataḥ sa nṛpatī rājā samutthāya kṛtāṃjaliḥ |
praṇatvā bhagavaṃtaṃ taṃ prārthayadevamādarāt || 28.68{68} ||
bhagavannātha sarvajña bhavatāṃ śaraṇaṃ vraje |
tanme'trānugrahaṃ kṛtvā samyagādeṣṭumarhati || 28.69{69} ||
yadatra bhagavan satvāḥ pravarttanto bhavodadhau |
sukhaduḥkhābhibhuṃjāno bhramaṃte ṣaḍgatau katham || 28.70{70} ||
karmaṇā kena devāḥ syurmānuṣāḥ kena karmaṇā |
daityāśca karmaṇā kena tatsamyaksamupādiśa || 28.71{71} ||
tiryañcaḥ karmaṇā kena pretāśca kena karmaṇā |
nārakāḥ karmaṇā kena tatsarvaṃ samupādiśa || 28.72{72} ||
tatrāpyanekarūpāśca sarve bhinnāśayā api |
nihīnamadhyamotkṛṣṭāḥ sukhaduḥkhānvitā api || 28.73{73} ||
etatsarvaṃ samākhyāya bhavāñchāstā jagadguruḥ |
saṃsāragatisaṃcāraṃ prabodhayitumarhati || 28.74{74} ||
iti saṃprārthite tena rājñā sa bhagavān sudhīḥ |
taṃ viśuddhāśayaṃ bhūpaṃ dṛṣṭvaivaṃ samupādiśat || 28.75{75} ||
sādhu śṛṇu mahārāja saṃsāragaticāraṇam |
samyagdharmaṃ pravakṣyāmi yuṣmākaṃ paribodhane || 28.76{76} ||
kāyavāgmānasaṃ karmakṛtaṃ yacca śubhāśubham |
lokastasya phalaṃ bhukte karttā nānyo'sti kasya cit || 28.77{77} ||
iti sarve kṛpāviṣṭāstrailokye guravo jināḥ |
uktavantastathā taddhi karmaṇo yasya yatphalam || 28.78{78} ||
karmaṃ kartuṃ vihātuṃ ca sadasadgatihetukam |
lobhamohabhayakrodhā ye narā naraghātinaḥ || 28.79{79} ||
saṃvaddhyānyāñca hi santi saṃjīvaṃ yānti te dhruvam |
saṃvatsarasahasrāṇi bahūnyapi hatāhatāḥ || 28.80{80} ||
saṃjīvanti yatastatra tena saṃjīva ucyate || 28.81{81!} ||
mātāpitṛsuhṛdvaṃdhumitradrohakarāśca ye |
paiśunyānṛtavattāraḥ kālasūtrābhigāminaḥ || 28.82{82} ||
kālasūtreṇa saṃsūḍya pāṭyante dāruvadyataḥ |
jvaladbhiḥ krakacaistatra kālasūtrastataḥ smṛtaḥ || 28.83{83} ||
dāvādau dahanairdāhaṃ dehināṃ vidadhāti ye |
sa tīvrajvalanairjantustapyate tapane raṭan || 28.84{84} ||
(rm 327)
tīvraṃ tapanasaṃtāpaṃ tanotyeva niraṃtaram |
yattato'nvarthamāloke khyātastapanasaṃjñayā || 28.85{85} ||
dharmādharmaviparyāsaṃ nāstiko yaḥ prakāśayan |
saṃtāpayati cānyāṃśca tapyate sa pratāpane || 28.86{86} ||
pratāpayati tatrasthān satvānstīvreṇa vahninā |
tapanātiśayano'sau proktastasmātpratāpanaḥ || 28.87{87} ||
ajaiḍakaśṛgālāṃśca śaśākhumṛgāsukarān |
anyāṃśca prāṇino ghnaṃti saṃghātaṃ yānti te narāḥ || 28.88{88} ||
saṃhatāstatra ghātyaṃte samyagvāhaiva vaṃyayaḥ |
tasmātsaṃghāta ityevaṃ vikhyāto'nvarthasaṃjñayā || 28.89{89} ||
kāyavāgmānasaṃ tāpaṃ ye kurvantīha dehinām |
kaṭukyapaṭikā ye ca rauravaṃ yānti te nārāḥ || 28.90{90} ||
tīvreṇa vahninā tatra dahyamānā niraṃtaram |
raudraṃ ravaṃ vimuṃcanti yatastasmātsa rauravam || 28.91{91} ||
devadvijagurordravyaṃ hṛtaṃ yairduḥkhināmiha |
te mahārauravaṃ yānti ye cānyasvāpahāriṇaḥ || 28.92{92} ||
raudratvādvahnidāhasya ravasya ca mahattayā |
rauravo hi mahāṃstasya mantva rauravādapi || 28.93{93} ||
kṛtvā guṇādhike tīvramapakāraṃ nihatya ca |
mātāpitṛgurun kalpamavīcau pacyate dhruvam || 28.94{94} ||
asthīnyapi viśīryyante raudrāgnau tatra dehinām |
yato na vīciḥ saukhyasya nāvīcirudāhṛtaḥ || 28.95{95} ||
mithodroharatā ye traṇe ghnantīva dehinaḥ |
pāpādasinakhāste tu jāyante duḥkhabhāginaḥ || 28.96{96} ||
nakhā evāsayasteṣāmāyasā jvalitāḥ kharāḥ |
tairanyonyaṃ nikṛntanti yattenāsinakhāḥ smṛtāḥ || 28.97{97} ||
lohajvalitatīkṣṇāgrāḥ ṣoḍaśāṅgulakaṇṭakāḥ |
balādārohyata hraṃdan śālmalīḥ pāladārikaḥ || 28.98{98} ||
lohadamṣṭrā mahākāyā jvalitāstīvrabhairavāḥ |
aśliṣya bhakṣayantyenaṃ paradāropahāriṇam || 28.99{99} ||
āraṭanto'pi khādyante śvagṛdhrolūkavāyasaiḥ |
aśivaṃ kurvate chinnā viśvāsaghāṭino narāḥ || 28.100{100} ||
ayoguḍāni bhujyante prataptāni punaḥ punaḥ |
yāpyante kvathitaṃ tāmraṃ ye parasvāpahāriṇaḥ || 28.101{1} ||
krūraiḥ śvabhirayodraṃṣṭraiḥ kṛtyante vivaśā bhṛśam |
varṣakoṭi raṭanto'pi ye narāḥ kheṭake ratāḥ || 28.102{2} ||
matsyādīñjalajān hatvā jvalantyamradravodakām |
yāti vaitaraṇīṃ śaśvadvahninā dahyate naraḥ || 28.103{3} ||
ye svārthalavasaṃmūḍho vyavahāramadhārmikam |
karoti narake kradan sa cakreṇābhipīḍyate || 28.104{4} ||
pīḍā vahubhirākāraiḥ kṛtā yairduḥkhināmiha |
pīḍyante te ciraṃ taptairyatra mudgaraparvataiḥ || 28.105{5} ||
bhedakā dharmasetūnāṃ ye cāsanmārgavāhinaḥ |
(rm 328)
kṣuradhārācitaṃ mārgaṃ gatvā krāmanti te narāḥ || 28.106{6} ||
nakhaiḥ saṃcūrṇya yūkādīñcūrṇyante meṣaparvatai |
bhūyo bhūyo mahākāyāḥ krandantaste śaracchatam || 28.107{7} ||
vrataṃ yastu samāśritya saṃpanno parirakṣati |
śīryamānamānsāsthiḥ kukuṃle pacyate dhruvam || 28.108{8} ||
aṇunāpi hi yaḥ kaścinmithyājīvena jīvati |
bhakṣyate krimibhiścaṇḍaiḥ saṃmagno gūthamṛttike || 28.109{9} ||
dṛṣṭvāpi vrīhimadhyasthān prāṇinaścūrṇayanti ye |
āyasai mukhalaistaptaiḥ kṣobhyante te punaḥ punaḥ || 28.110{10} ||
atyantakrodhanāḥ krūrāḥ śaṭhāḥ pāpābhikāṃkṣiṇaḥ |
paravyasanakṛṣṭvā ca jāyante yamarākṣasāḥ || 28.111{11} ||
sarveṣāmeva duḥkhānāṃ vījaṃ mṛdvābhibhedataḥ |
kāyavāgmānasaṃ karma pāpaṃ yattadaṇvapi varttayet |

++ narakakāṇḍaṃ samāptam ++

haṃsapārāvatādīnaṃ kharāṇāmapi rāgiṇām |
yena rāgeṇa jāyante mūḍhāḥ kīṭādiyoniṣu || 28.112{1} ||
sarppāḥ krodhopanāhābhyāṃ mārastabdhā mṛgādhipāḥ |
abhimānena jāyante gadāmāśvādiyoniṣu || 28.113{2} ||
mātsaryerṣyādi doṣeṇa vānarāḥ pretadehinaḥ |
jāyante mukharā dhṛṣṭāścapalātmānaśca vāyasāḥ || 28.114{3} ||
vadhavaṃdhanamātsaryairgavāśvādiṣu dehinaḥ |
jāyante krūrakarmāṇo lūtāḥ kharjjūravṛścikaḥ || 28.115{4} ||
vyāghramārjāragomāyuṛkṣagṛdhravṛgādāyaḥ |
jāyante pretamansādoḥ krodhanā matsarā narāḥ || 28.116{5} ||
dātāraḥ krodhanāḥ krūrā narā nāgamaharddhikāḥ |
bhavāntatyāgino darppātkrodhācca garuḍeśvarāḥ || 28.117{6} ||
kṛtaṃ yatpāpakaṃ karma svayaṃ vākkāyamānasam |
tiryañcastena jāyante tanmanogatimākṛthāḥ || 28.118{7} ||

++ iti tiryakkāṇḍaṃ samāptam ++

bhakṣyabhojyāpahartāro ye kṣudrā dānavarji |
bhavanti kuṇapāhārāḥ pretāste kaṭapūtanāḥ || 28.119{1} ||
viheṭhayanti ye bālā vaṃcayantyapi tṛṣṇayā |
te'pi garbhamalāhārā jāyante kaṭapūtanāḥ || 28.120{2} ||
hīnācārātidīnāśca matsarā nityakāṃkṣiṇaḥ |
jāyaṃte ye narāḥ pretā'ṣṭāste galagaṇḍakāḥ || 28.121{3} ||
dānaṃ nivārayatyeva na hi kiṃ tiddadāti yaḥ |
kṣutkṣāmāsau mahākukṣiḥ pretaḥ śūcimukho bhavet || 28.122{4} ||
dhanaṃ rakṣati vaṃśārthe na bhuṃkte na dadāti yaḥ |
dattādāyī tataḥ pretaḥ śrāddhabhoktā sa jāyate || 28.123{5} ||
yaḥ parasvāpahārepsurdatvā caivānutapyate |
bhoktā viṭśleṣmavāntānāṃ pretya pretaḥ sa jāyate || 28.124{6} ||
apriyaṃ vakti yaḥ krodhādvākyaṃ marmavibhedatam |
bbavantyulkāmukhaḥ pretaḥ suciraṃ tena karmaṇā || 28.125{7} ||
(rm 329)
kurute kalahaṃ yastu niṣkṛpaḥ krūramānasaḥ |
krimikīṭapataṅgādeḥ prete'sau jyotiko bhavet || 28.126{8} ||
grāmakūṭau dadātyeva yo janaṃ pīḍayantyapi |
kumbhāṇḍo vikṛtākāraḥ pūyāhāraśca jāyate || 28.127{9} ||
nirdayāḥ prāṇino ghnanti bhakṣyārthaṃ ye dadantyapi |
māṃsāhārāśca te'vaśyaṃ bhavanti pretarākṣasāḥ || 28.128{10} ||
gaṃdhamālāratā nityaṃ maṃdakrodhāḥ pradāyakāḥ |
gaṃdharvāḥ pretya jāyante devānāṃ ratihetavaḥ || 28.129{11} ||
krodhanāḥ piśunaḥ kiñcidarthārthaṃ yaḥ prayachati |
sa piśācaṃ praduṣṭātmā jāyate vikṛtānanaḥ || 28.130{12} ||
nityapraduṣṭāścapalāḥ parapīḍākarā narāḥ |
saṃpradānaratā nityaṃ bhūtāḥ pretya bhavanti te || 28.131{13} ||
krūrāḥ krūddhāḥ pradātāraḥ priyā sarvasurā ca ye |
jāyante pretya yakṣāstu krūrātmānaḥ surāpriyāḥ || 28.132{14} ||
mātāpitṛgurūn yānairye nayanti yathepsitam |
vimānacāriṇo yakṣā jāyaṃte susukhānvitāḥ || 28.133{15} ||
tṛṣṇāmātsaryadoṣeṇa pretā bhavantyamī sadā |
yakṣādayaḥ śubhaiḥ kliṣṭairatastān parivarjayet || 28.134{16} ||

++ iti pretakāṇḍaṃ samāptam ++

devāsuramanuṣyeṣu dīrghamāyurahiṃsayā |
jāyate hinsayā svalpāmato hiṃsāṃ vivarjayet || 28.135{1} ||
kuṣṭharogajvaronmādā ye cānye vyādhayo nṛṇām |
bhavanti bhūtaprayukteste vadhavaṃdhanatāḍanaiḥ || 28.136{2} ||
yo na harttā parasvānāṃ na vañcavitprayachati |
mahatāpi prayatnena sa dravyaṃ nādhigachati || 28.137{3} ||
adattaṃ vittamādāya dānāni ca dadāti yaḥ |
sa pretya dravyavān bhūtvā bhūyo bhavati nirdhanaḥ || 28.138{4} ||
yo na harttā na dātā ca na cātikṛpaṇo naraḥ |
kṛtsnena mahatā dravyaṃ sthiraṃ sa labhate dhruvam || 28.139{5} ||
na harttā yaḥ parasvānāṃ tyāgavān vītamatsaraḥ |
ahāryaṃ vipulaṃ vittamiṣṭaṃ ca labhate naraḥ || 28.140{6} ||
āyurvarṇavalopetaḥ śrīmān rogavivarjitaḥ |
sukhī ca sa labhennityaṃ yo dadātīha bhojanam || 28.141{7} ||
salajjo rūpavān bhogī suchāyaḥ prāṇināṃ priyaḥ |
sa bhavedvastralābhī ca yo vāsānsi prayachati || 28.142{8} ||
āvāsaṃ yo dadātīha suprasannena cetasā |
prāsādāḥ sarvakāmāśca jāyante tasya dehinaḥ || 28.143{9} ||
upānatsaṃkramādīni ye prayachanti mānavāḥ |
bhavanti sukhino nityaṃ yānāni ca labhanti te || 28.144{10} ||
vāpīkūpataḍāgādīn kārayitvā jalāśrayān |
sukhinastyaktasaṃtāpā niḥpipāsā bhavanti te || 28.145{11} ||
puṣpairabhyarcitaḥ śrīmān śaraṇyaḥ sarvadehinām |
samṛddhitaḥ sa syādārāmaṃ yaḥ prayachati || 28.146{12} ||
vidyādānena paṇḍityaṃ prajñābhyāsena cāpyate |
bhaiṣajyābhayadānena rogamuktastu jāyate || 28.147{13} ||
(rm 330)
dīpadānena cakṣuṣmān vādyadānena susvaraḥ |
śayanāsanadānena sukhī bhavati mānavaḥ || 28.148{14} ||
gavādīnn yo dadātīha bhojyaṃ rasasamanvitam |
balavān varṇavān bhogī dīrghāyuḥ sa bhavennaraḥ || 28.149{15} ||
kanyādānena samānyāṃ lābhī syātparivāravān |
dhanadhānyasamṛddhastu bhūmidānena jāyate || 28.150{16} ||
patraṃ puṣpaṃ phalaṃ toyamabhayaṃ vacanaṃ priyam |
yadyadevepsitaṃ śaktyā dātavyaṃ tattadarthinaḥ || 28.151{17} ||
kleśayitvā dadātīha svakārthaṃ vā ha yena vā |
yaśaḥsaukhyābhilākhādvā sa kliṣṭaṃ labhate phalam || 28.152{18} ||
svakāryaṃ nirapekṣeṇa kṛpāviṣṭena cetasā |
parārthaṃ yo dadātīha so'kliṣṭaṃ phalamaśnute || 28.153{19} ||
yatkiñciddīyate'nyasmai yathākālaṃ yathāvidhim |
tena tena prakāreṇa tatsarvamupatiṣṭhate || 28.154{20} ||
parānanvapahatyevaṃ sarvakālaṃ yathepsitam |
akleśayitvā dātavyaṃ hitaṃ dharmāvirodhiyat || 28.155{21} ||
evaṃ hi dīyamānasya dānasyaiva phalādayaḥ |
dānaṃ hi sarvasaukhyānāmananyatkāraṇaṃ matam || 28.156{22} ||
virajaḥ paradārebhyo dārāniṣṭānavāpnuyāt |
svebhyo'pyadeśakālau ca varjayetpuṃstvamarchati || 28.157{23} ||
paradāreṣu saṃsaktaṃ cittaṃ yo na niyachati |
anaṃgeṣu cakṣyeta sa pumāṃ strītvamarchati || 28.158{24} ||
strītvaṃ jugupsate yā tu suśīlamandarāgiṇī |
puṃstvamākāṃkṣate nityaṃ sā nārī naratāṃ vrajet || 28.159{25} ||
yastu samyagnirātaṅkaṃ brahmacaryaṃ niṣevate |
tejasvī sadguṇaḥ śrīmān devairapi sa lakṣyate || 28.160{26} ||
dṛḍhasmṛtirasaṃmūḍho maghavāno niṣevanāt |
jāyate satyavādī ca yaśaḥsaukhyānvitaḥ pumān || 28.161{27} ||
bhinnānāmapi satvānāṃ bhedaṃ naiva karoti yaḥ |
abhedyaparivāro'sau jāyate sthiramānasaḥ || 28.162{28} ||
yastvājñāṃ kurute nityaṃ gurūṇāṃ hṛṣṭamānasaḥ |
hitāhitābhidhāyī ca sa syādādeyavākkṛtī || 28.163{29} ||
nīcāparāyamānena viparyāsena codgataḥ |
bhavanti sukhinaḥ saukhyaṃ duḥkhaṃ datvā ca duḥkhinaḥ || 28.164{30} ||
paraprapañcābhiratāḥ śaṭhāścānṛtavādinaḥ |
kubjavāmanatāṃ yānti ye ca rūpābhimāninaḥ || 28.165{31} ||
jaḍo vijñānamātsaryādbhavenmūkaḥ priyāpriyaḥ |
vādhiryaṃ yāti mūḍhātmā hitavākyābhigūḍhakaḥ || 28.166{32} ||
duḥkhaṃ pāpasya puṇyasya sukhaṃ miśrasya miśritam |
sarvaṃ sādṛśyaniḥsyandamabhyuhyaṃ karmaṇaḥ phalam || 28.167{33} ||
++iti manuṣya kāṃdaṃ samāptam ++

śaṭhyena māyayā nityaṃ caratyakṛtakilbiṣaḥ |
kalipriyaḥ pradātā ca sa bhavedasureśvaraḥ || 28.168{1} ||
(rm 331)

++ ityasurakāṇḍam ++

nātmanā yaḥ sukhākāṃkṣī na ca kṛvyeta parigraham |
grahāṇāmagraṇī cāsau mahārājikatāṃ vrajet || 28.169{1} ||
mātāpitṛkulajyeṣṭhapūjakasyāgavān kṛtī |
hṛṣyame yo na kalahaistridaśeṣu sa jāyate || 28.170{2} ||
na vigrahe ratā naiva kalahe hṛṣṭamānasāḥ |
ekāntakuśalodyuktā ye ca moyāpagāstu te || 28.171{3} ||
bahuśrutā dharmadharāḥ suprajñā mokṣakāṃkṣiṇaḥ guṇair |
ye parihṛṣṇāśca narāste tuṣitopagāḥ || 28.172{4} ||
śīlapradānavinaye pravṛttā ye svayaṃ narāḥ |
mahotsāhāśca te'vaśyaṃ nirmāṇaratigāminaḥ || 28.173{5} ||
adīnasatvā me śreṣṭhāḥ pradāne damasaṃyamaiḥ |
guṇādhikāśca ye yānti paranirmitatāṃ dhruvam || 28.174{6} ||
śīlena svargamāpnoti dhyānena ca viśeṣataḥ |
yathābhūtaparijñānātparyante vo punarbhavaḥ || 28.175{7} ||
śubhāśubhaphalaṃ karma yadetatkathitaṃ sphuṭam |
śubhena labhyate saukhyaṃ duḥkhaṃ tvaśubhasaṃbhavam || 28.176{8} ||
mṛtyurvyādhirjarā caiva cintanīyamidaṃ trayam |
viprayogā priyaiḥ sārddhaṃ karmaṇāṃ ca svakaṃ phalam || 28.177{9} ||
evaṃ virāgamāpnoti viviktaḥ puṇyamarchati |
pāpaṃ ca nārjayedevaṃ tacca saṃkṣepataḥ śṛṇu || 28.178{10} ||
samyakparārthakaraṇaṃ parānarthavivarjanam |
puṇyaṃ viparyayātpāpabhuktameva munīśvaraiḥ || 28.179{11} ||

++ iti devakāṇḍaṃ samāptam ++

evaṃ matvā mahārāja ṣaḍgatikārikāṃ bhave |
saṃbodhidharmamādhāya caritavyaṃ sadā śubhe || 28.180{1} ||
ye saṃbodhivrataṃ dhṛtvā pracaranti samāhitāḥ |
sarvasatvahitārthena bodhisatvā bhavanti te || 28.181{2} ||
evaṃ vijñāya rājendra sadā bhadraṃ yadīchasi |
triratnaṃ śaraṇaṃ kṛtvā bhaja nityaṃ samādarāt || 28.182{3} ||
etatpuṇyavipākena sarvakleśan vinirjayan |
kramādbodhiṃ samāsādya saṃbuddhapadamāpnuyāḥ || 28.183{4} ||
iti tena munīndreṇa samākhyātaṃ niśamya saḥ |
rājāpyuttarapāṃcālaḥ pravrajituṃ samaichata || 28.184{5} ||
tataḥ sa nṛpatī rājā samutthāya kṛtāṃjaliḥ |
bhagavantamānatvā prārthayadevamādarāt || 28.185{6} ||
bhagavannātha sarvajña bhavatāṃ śaraṇaṃ gataḥ |
saṃbodhivratamādhāya prechāmi carituṃ sadā || 28.186{7} ||
yadbhavāñjagatāṃ śāstā saṃbodhidharmadeśikaḥ |
tanme'nugrahatāṃ kṛtvā pravrajyāṃ dātumarhati || 28.187{8} ||
iti saṃprārthite tena rājñā sa bhagavān sudhīḥ |
tasya rājño manaḥśuddhaṃ vijñāyaivamupādiśat || 28.188{9} ||
sādhu rājanmahābhāga yadīchasi śubhāṃ gatim |
ehi cara samādhāya cara me śāsane vratam || 28.189{10} ||
ityādiśya munīndro'sau pāṇinā tacchiraḥ spṛśan |
(rm 332)
saṃbuddhe śāsane dharme taṃ nareṃdraṃ samagrahīt || 28.190{11} ||
ehīti tena śāstrokte nṛpatiḥ sa prabodhitaḥ |
khikkhirīpātradharo bhikṣu'bhūccīvarāvṛtaḥ || 28.191{12} ||
tataḥ sa bhūpatiścāpi virakto bhogyanispṛhaḥ |
niḥkleśaḥ pariśuddhātmā babhūva vijiteṃdriyaḥ || 28.192{13} ||
sākṣādarhatvamāsādya brahmacārī niraṃjanaḥ |
saṃbodhipadamāsādya saṃbuddhaḥ śrāvako'bhavat || 28.193{14} ||
tataḥ sa brahmavidyogī traidhātuvāsināmapi |
sadevāsuralokānāṃ vandyo mānyo'bhavadguruḥ || 28.194{15} ||
tato dakṣiṇapāṃcālarājā sa pratimoditaḥ |
utthāya sāṃjalirnatvā prārthyattaṃ munīśvaram || 28.195{16} ||
bhagavannātha sarvajña kṛpayā me prasīdatu |
traimāsyaṃ bhaktumichāmi tatkurutāmanugraham || 28.196{17} ||
iti saṃprārthite rājñā bhagavān sa munīśvaraḥ |
nṛpatiṃ taṃ samālokya tūṣṇībhūtvādhyuvāsa tat || 28.197{18} ||
tataḥ sa nṛpatī rājā bhagavatādhyuvāsitam |
viditvā sarvasāmagrīṃ sahasā samasādhayat || 28.198{19} ||
tataḥ sa rājā bhagavaṃtaṃ sasāṃghikam |
yathārhabhojanairnityaṃ samabhyarcya'bhyatoṣayat || 28.199{20} ||
vicitracīvaraiścāpi śrīghanaṃ taṃ sasāṃghikam |
āchādya sāṃjalirnatvā praṇidhānaṃ mudā vyadhāt || 28.200{21} ||
etatpuṇyavipākena loke'ṃdhe'parināyake |
saddharmabhāskaraḥ śāstā saṃbuddho'rhaṃ bhaveya hi || 28.201{22} ||
evaṃ tena narendreṇa praṇidhānaṃ kṛtaṃ mudā |
matvā sa bhagavān smitaṃ prāmuñcacchubharociṣam || 28.202{23} ||
tatsmitasamutpannāḥ paṃcavarṇāḥ suraśmayaḥ |
prasāritāstrilokeṣu bhāsayantyaḥ pracerire || 28.203{24} ||
tadrasmisaṃparispṛṣṭāḥ sarve'pi nārakāśritāḥ |
nirmuktavedanākhedā mahatsaukhyaṃ pralebhire || 28.204{25} ||
tadā te vismitāḥ sarve nārakīyāḥ pramoditāḥ |
kimevaṃ jāyate saukhyamiti dhyātvā niṣedire || 28.205{26} ||
tataḥ sa bhagavāṃsteṣāṃ vismayākrāṃtacetasām |
manāṃsi paribodhārthaṃ praiṣayattatra nairmitam || 28.206{27} ||
tadā te nārakāḥ sarve dṛṣṭvā taṃ sugataṃ mudā |
upetya praṇatiṃ kṛtvā prābhajañcharaṇaṃ gatāḥ || 28.207{28} ||
tatastatpuṇyaliptāste sarve vimuktapāpakāḥ |
pariśuddhāḥ śubhātmānaḥ sadgatiṃ samupāyayuḥ || 28.208{29} ||
tatastā raśmayaḥ sarvā avabhāsya samaṃtataḥ |
akaniṣṭhālayaṃ yāvatprasṛtāḥ samabhāsayan || 28.209{30} ||
gāthāghoṣaiśca sarvatra sarvān devān pramodinaḥ |
bodhayitvā śubhe dharme pratiṣṭhāpya pacerire || 28.210{31} ||
tatastā raśmayaḥ sarvā piṇḍībhūtā muneḥ puraḥ |
tridhā pradakṣiṇīkṛtvā mahoṣṇīṣe samāviśat || 28.211{32} ||
taddṛṣṭvā te sabhālokāḥ sarve'tivismayoddhatāḥ |
(rm 333)
śāstā kimādiśeddharmamiti dhyātvā niṣedire || 28.212{33} ||
iti teṣāṃ manastarkaṃ matvānaṃdaḥ samutthitaḥ |
upetya sāṃjalirnatvā prārthayettaṃ munīśvaram || 28.213{34} ||
bhagavan hetunā kena smitaṃ muṃcati sāṃpratam |
nāhetvapratyayaṃ smitaṃ na muṃcaṃti munīśvarāḥ || 28.214{35} ||
tadyadarthe bhavān smitaṃ muṃcatīme sabhājanāḥ |
śrotumichaṃti sarve tadarthamādeṣṭumarhati || 28.215{36} ||
ityānaṃdoditaṃ śrutvā bhagavān sa jagadguruḥ |
sarvāṃllokān samālokya tamānaṃdaṃ samabravīt || 28.216{37} ||
evameva sadānaṃda sarve buddhā munīśvarāḥ |
nāhetupratyayaṃ smitaṃ vimuṃcanti kadā cana || 28.217{38} ||
paśyatāmayamānaṃda rājātisaṃprasāditaḥ |
satkṛtya śraddhayāsmākaṃ traimāsyaṃ bhajate mudā || 28.218{39} ||
etatpuṇyavipākena rājāyaṃ sadguṇākaraḥ |
kramātpāramitāḥ sarvāḥ paripūrya balānvitaḥ || 28.219{40} ||
sarvakleśagaṇāñjitvā mārāñcāpi vinirjayan |
bodhimāsādya sarvajñastathāgato munīśvaraḥ || 28.220{41} ||
sarvavidyādhipaḥ śāstā dharmarājo vināyakaḥ |
vijayo nāma saṃbuddho bhaviṣyati bhavāntare || 28.221{42} ||
evamānaṃda vijñāya buddhakṣetraśubhaṃkṛtam |
tadvipāke mahatsaukhyaṃ bhadraṃ saṃbodhisādhanam || 28.222{43} ||
triratnaśaraṇaṃ kṛtvā satkāraiḥ śraddhayā sadā |
saṃbodhivāñchibhirlokaiścaritavyaṃ śubhe mudā || 28.223{44} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa śrutvānaṃdādayo'pi te |
sarve lokāstathetyuktvā prābhyanandan prasāditāḥ || 28.224{45} ||
so'pi dakṣiṇapāṃcālo rājā śrutvā pramoditaḥ |
triratnabhajanaṃ kṛtvā pracacāra śubhe sadā || 28.225{46} ||
tadārabhya sadā tatra maṃgalaṃ nirupadravam |
samaṃtato mahotsāhaṃ kṛtayuga ivābhavat || 28.226{47} ||
iti me guruṇākhyātaṃ śrutaṃ mayā tathā'dhunā |
kathyate'tra tvayāpyevaṃ caritavyaṃ śubhe sadā || 28.227{48} ||
prajāścāpi mahārāja bodhayitvā prayatnataḥ |
bodhimārge pratiṣṭhāpya pālanīyāḥ sadā tvayā || 28.228{49} ||
tathā te sarvadā bhadraṃ sarvatrāpi bhaveddhruvam |
kramādbodhiṃ samāsādya saṃbuddhapadamāpnuyāḥ || 28.229{50} ||
iti tenārhatādiṣṭaṃ śrutvāśokaḥ sa bhūmipaḥ |
tathetyabhyanumoditvā prābhyanandatsapārṣadaḥ || 28.230{51} ||
rājño pāñcālayoryanmunikathitamidaṃ tatprasiddhāvadānam |
śṛṇvanti śrāvayanti pramuditamanasaḥ śraddhayā ye prasannāḥ |
sarve te bodhisatvāḥ sakalaguṇadharāḥ sarvasaṃpatsukhāḍhyāḥ |
kṛtvā lokeṣu bhaddraṃ munivaranilaye saṃprayānti pramodāḥ || 28.231{52} ||

(rm 334)
++ iti ratnāvadānatatve pāṃcālarājāvadanaṃ samāptam ++


(rm 335)
xxix upapādukāvadāna
athāśoko mahīpālaḥ samupetya kṛtāṃjaliḥ |
upaguptaṃ yatiṃ natvā prārthayadevamādarāt || 29.1{1} ||
bhadanta śrotumichāmi punaranyatsubhāṣitam |
tadyathā guruṇādiṣṭaṃ tathākhyātuṃ ca me'rhati || 29.2{2} ||
iti saṃprarthitaṃ rājñā śrutvā so'rhanmahāmatiḥ |
upagupto nareṃdraṃ taṃ samālokyaivamādiśat || 29.3{3} ||
sādhu śṛṇu maharāja yathā me guruṇoditam |
tathāhaṃ te pravakṣyāmi śrutvā cāpyanumodaya || 29.4{4} ||
tadyathā bhagavān buddhā śākyasiṃho munīśvaraḥ |
sarvajñaḥ sugataḥ śāstā dharmarājo vināyakaḥ || 29.5{5} ||
ekasmin samaye māturmāyādevyā hitechayā |
saddharmaṃ samupādeṣṭuṃ prācarattridaśālaye || 29.6{6} ||
tatra sa bhagavān gatvā suramye naṃdane vane |
pārijātatale pāṇḍusacchilāyāmupāśrayat || 29.7{7} ||
tatra śakrādayo devāḥ sarve taṃ sadguṇākaram |
śākyamuniṃ samālokya muditāḥ samupācaran || 29.8{8} ||
tatra taṃ śrīghanaṃ dṛṣṭvā sarve devāḥ pramoditāḥ |
tridhā pradakṣiṇīkṛtya sāñjalayaḥ praṇemire || 29.9{9} ||
tato devādhipaḥ śakro divyaratnamayāsanam |
saṃbuddhasya jagacchāstuḥ prājñāpayacchubhojjvalam || 29.10{10} ||
tatra sa bhagavān dṛṣṭvā divyaratnamahojvale |
āruhya samupāśritya samātasthau prabhāsayan || 29.11{11} ||
tadā sā jananī mātā māyādevī samāgatā |
tamātmajaṃ jagannāthaṃ saṃdṛṣṭvā samupācarat || 29.12{12} ||
tatra sa bhagavān dṛṣṭvā mātaraṃ tāṃ samāgatām |
utthāya samupāsṛtya praṇatvaivamabhāṣata || 29.13{13} ||
svāgataṃ kuśalaṃ mātarārogyamastu te sadā |
tvatmukhaṃ draṣṭumāyāmi tatprasāditumarhasi || 29.14{14} ||
atrāsane samāśritya niṣīdaikāgramānasā |
upadikṣyāmi saddharmaṃ tacchṛṇuṣva samāhitā || 29.15{15} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa śrutvā sā jananī satī |
māyādevī samālokya tamātmajaṃ samavrāvīt || 29.16{16} ||
prāyāmi te mukhaṃ draṣṭuṃ kuśalaṃ te sadātmaja |
tanmamānugrahārthena dharmamādeṣṭumarhasi || 29.17{17} ||
ityuktvā sā satī māyādevī mātā jagadguroḥ |
upāsanaṃ samāśritya nisasāda samāhitā || 29.18{18} ||
tataḥ śakrādayo devāḥ sarve te saṃpraharṣitāḥ |
(rm 336)
pūjāṃgaistaṃ jagannāthaṃ māyādevīṃ ca prārcayan || 29.19{19} ||
tataḥ pādānstayornatvā kṛtvā ca tripradakṣiṇam |
parivṛtya puraskṛtvā dharmaṃ śrotumupāśrayan || 29.20{20} ||
tatasta bhagavān dṛṣṭvā tān devān samupasthitān |
āryasatyaṃ samārabhya saddharmaṃ samupādiśat || 29.21{21} ||
tataśca bhagavāṃsteṣāṃ devānāṃ sukhamāninam |
saṃbodhisādhanaṃ punarevamupādiśat || 29.22{22} ||
anityaṃ khalu saṃsāre duḥkhaṃ śūnyaṃ hyanātmakam |
kāme kiṃcitsukhaṃ nātra kleśasaṃghe samākule || 29.23{23} ||
iti matvātra saṃsāre sadā saukhyaṃ yadīchatha |
bodhicaryāvrataṃ dhṛtvā saṃcaradhvaṃ sadā śubham || 29.24{24} ||
niṣkrāmatārabhadhvaṃ ca yujyadhvaṃ buddhaśāsane |
māracaryyāṃ parityajya caradhvaṃ bodhicārikām || 29.25{25} ||
yaḥ satvahitārthena bodhicaryāṃ cariṣyati |
sa samyagbodhimāsādya saṃbuddhapadamāpsyati || 29.26{25} ||
evaṃ matvātra saṃsāre sarve yūyaṃ samāhitāḥ |
triratnabhajanaṃ kṛtvā caradhvaṃ bodhisaṃvaram || 29.27{26} ||
ye triratnaṃ bhajantyatra na te gachaṃti durgatim |
sadā sadgatisaṃyātāḥ kramādbodhimavāpnuyuḥ || 29.28{27} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa śrutvā sarve'pi te'marāḥ |
satyameva parijñāya prābhyanandan prabodhitāḥ || 29.29{28} ||
tataḥ śakraḥ samutthāya kṛtāṃjaliḥ |
bhagavaṃtaṃ tamānamya prārthayadevamādarāt || 29.30{29} ||
bhagavannātha sarvajña sarvadeha surālaye |
saddharmaṃ naḥ samādiśya bhavan vihartumarhati || 29.31{30} ||
vayaṃ sarve surā nityaṃ bhavatāṃ śaraṇaṃ gatāḥ |
saddharmaṃ sarvadākarṇya cariṣyāmaḥ śubhāṃ carim || 29.32{31} ||
tataḥ sāpi mahāmāyādevi mātā samutthitā |
upetya sāṃjalirnatvā bhagavantaṃ tamabravīt || 29.33{32} ||
dhanyo'si yattvayā putra pratijñātaṃ yathāpurā |
tathā tvaṃ pūryate sarvaṃ sarvārthasiddhirastu te || 29.34{33} ||
sadātra devalokānāṃ hitārthe ca prabodhayan |
saddharmaṃ samupādiśya vihara saṃprabhāsayan || 29.35{34} ||
iti mātrārthitaṃ śrutvā bhagavān sa jagadguruḥ |
jananīṃ tāṃ sabhāṃ cāpi samālokyaivamādiśat || 29.36{35} ||
mātarnaikahitārthenabodhicaryāṃ dadhāmi hi |
sarveṣāmapi satvānāṃ hitārthe'haṃ jino bhave || 29.37{36} ||
tadatra sarvadā naivaṃ tiṣṭheyaṃ tridaśaiḥ saha |
varṣā māsatrayaṃ sthātumichāmi dharmamādiśan || 29.38{37} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa śrutvā sā jananī mudā |
tathāstviti prabhāṣitvā prābhyanaṃdatprabodhitā || 29.39{38} ||
tathā sa bhagavānstatra bodhicaryāṃ prakāśayan |
saddharmaṃ samupādiśya tasthau māsatrayaṃ divi || 29.40{39} ||
(rm 337)
sarvaśakrādayo devāstriratnaśaraṇaṃ gatāḥ |
satkṛtya śraddhayā nityaṃ dharmaṃ śrutvā prabhejire || 29.41{40} ||
tadā tatra sadā svarge maṃgalaṃ nirupadravam |
saddharmasādhanotsāhaṃ prāvarttate samaṃtataḥ || 29.42{41} ||
tasmiṃścā samaye tatra srāvastyā vahirāśrame |
jetodyāne vihāre sa maudgalyāyana āśrayat || 29.43{42} ||
tadā te sāṃghikāḥ sarve taṃ maudgalyāyanaṃ yatim |
vihāre saṃsthitaṃ dṛṣṭvā dharmaṃ śrotuṃ samīchire || 29.44{43} ||
tataste sāṃghikāḥ sarve lokāścānye'pi sajjanāḥ |
tatsaddharmadeśanāṃ śrotuṃ muditāḥ samupācaran || 29.45{44} ||
tatra sarve'pi te lokāstaṃ maudgalyāyanaṃ gurum |
samabhyarcya samānamya dharmaṃ śrotu]mupāśrayan || 29.46{45} ||
tān dṛṣṭvā samupāsīnān sa maudgalyo jinātmajaḥ |
ādimadhyāṃtakalyāṇaṃ dideśa dharmamuttamam || 29.47{46} ||
tatsaddharmāmṛtaṃ pītvā sarve lokāśca sāṃghikāḥ |
saddharmmasādhanodyuktā vabhūvuḥ paribodhitāḥ || 29.48{47} ||
atha te bhikṣavaḥ sarve kṛtāṃjaliḥ puṭo mudā |
taṃ maudgalyāyanaṃ natvā paprachurevamādarāt || 29.49{48} ||
bhadanta bhagavaṃ chāstā kutredānīṃ samāśritaḥ |
saddharmaṃ samupādiśya viharati na manyate || 29.50{49} ||
tadasmākaṃ manānsyatra bhagavāñchāstā yataḥ sthitaḥ |
tatsatyaṃ samupādiśya prabodhayitumarhati || 29.51{50} ||
iti taiḥ prārthitaṃ śrutvā sa maudgalyāyanaḥ sudhīḥ |
sarvānstān saṃghikān bhikṣūn samālokyaivamabravīt || 29.52{51} ||
śrutaṃ mayā bhavanto'sau bhagavāñchāstā'dhunā divi |
tato mātuḥ surāṇāṃ cā tiṣṭhate dharmamādiśat || 29.53{52} ||
tato varṣātrimāsānte bhagavān sa jagadguruḥ |
atra lokahitaṃ kartumavataretsvayaṃ khalu || 29.54{53} ||
iti tenārhatākhyātaṃ śrutvā te sāṃghikā janāḥ |
sarve'py'bhyanumodantaḥ svasvālayaṃ samācaran || 29.55{54} ||
sadā te sāṃghikāstasya maudgalyasya mahāmateḥ |
saddharmaṃ sarvadākarṇya bhajantaḥ samupāśrayan || 29.56{55} ||
tato varṣātrimāsānte sarve te sāṃghikā janāḥ |
api taṃ maudgalyamānatvā prārthayadevamādarāt || 29.57{56} ||
bhavantātra bhavāñchāstā vijānīyājjagadguroḥ |
draṣṭumichāmahe śāstuḥ saddharmaṃ tṛṣitā vayam || 29.58{57} ||
ciradṛṣṭo yadasmābhirbhagavāṃ sa munīśvaraḥ |
tadasyāgramunīndrasya darśanaṃ dātumarhati || 29.59{58} ||
tadasmadanukampārthaṃ bhavanṛddhiprabhāvataḥ |
gatvā svarge munīndrasya sahasā samupakramet || 29.60{59} ||
tatra gatvā bhavācchāsturmunīndrasya jagadguroḥ |
asmākaṃ vacanaiścāpi vaṃdatāṃ caraṇāmbujau || 29.61{60} ||
(rm 338)
dehe ca kauśalaṃ saukhyaṃ pṛchatāṃ vacanaiśca naḥ |
evaṃ cāpi puraḥ śāsturnivedituṃ samarhati || 29.62{61} ||
bhagavaṃ nātha sarvajña te jāmbūdvīpikā narāḥ |
bhavantaṃ draṣṭumichanti saddharmāmṛtalālasāḥ || 29.63{62} ||
yadbhavān sarvasatvānāṃ hitaṃ kartuṃ samudyataḥ |
tadbhagavānmanuṣyāṇāṃ dharmamādeṣṭumarhati || 29.64{63} ||
narāḥ svarge samāgaṃtuṃ śaknuvanti na ke cana |
devāstu sarvalokeṣu pracaranti yathechayā || 29.65{64} ||
tadbhagavānmanuṣyāṇāṃ hitārthāyānukampayā |
saddharmaṃ samupādeṣṭuṃ jambudvīpamavātaret || 29.66{65} ||
vayaṃ sarve sadā nityaṃ bhavatāṃ śaraṇaṃ gatāḥ |
satkṛtya śraddhayā dharmmaṃ śrutvā saṃprabhajemahi || 29.67{66} ||
evaṃ śāstarmahāvijña gatvā tatra surālaye |
bhagavaṃtaṃ jagannāthaṃ saṃprārthyeha samānaya || 29.68{67} ||
iti taiḥ prārthitaiḥ śrutvā sa maudgalyo mahārddhimān |
evamiti pratijñāya tūṣṇībhūtvādhyuvāsa tat || 29.69{68} ||
atha te sāṃghikāḥ sarve lokāścāpi mahātmanā |
tenādhivāsitaṃ matvā muditāḥ svasvālayaṃ yayuḥ || 29.70{69} ||
atha so'rhaṃ mahābhijño maudgalyana ātmavit |
svarge gaṃtuṃ samādhāya samādhiṃ vidadhe kṣaṇam || 29.71{70} ||
tataḥ sa ṛddhivīryeṇa suparṇa iva khe'gamat |
bhāsayan sahasā gatvā divi śāsturupācarat || 29.72{71} ||
tatra taṃ samupāyātaṃ dṛṣṭvā sa bhagavānmudā |
svāgataṃ prehi maudgalya niṣīdeti samabravīt || 29.73{72} ||
ityādiṣṭe munīndreṇa sa maudgalyaḥ pramoditaḥ |
sāñjaliḥ pādayoḥ śāstuḥ praṇatvaivaṃ samabravīt || 29.74{73} ||
bhagavannātha sarvajña prāgato'haṃ mudā divi |
bhavatāṃ darśanaṃ kartuṃ tatprasīda dayānidhe || 29.75{74} ||
kaccitte kuśalaṃ śāstaḥ kiñcidvādhāpi naiva hi |
yātrā sukhaṃ valaṃ vīryaṃ kaccitkāye na cānyathā || 29.76{75} ||
bhavānapi vijānīyādyadarthe'hamihāgataḥ |
tathāpi bhagavannatra vijñāpayeya sarvathā || 29.77{76} ||
bhavatāṃ sāṃghikaiḥ sarvairlokaiścāpi mahājanaiḥ |
bhavantaṃ draṣṭumichadbhiḥ preṣito'hamihāgataḥ || 29.78{77} ||
te sarve sāṃghikā lokā bhavatāṃ caraṇāmbuje |
vandanti kauśalaṃ kāye paripṛchanti cādarāt || 29.79{78} ||
evaṃ cāpi vadante te sarve lokāśca sāṃghikāḥ |
saddharmaśravaṇotsāharahitā vikalāśayāḥ || 29.80{79} ||
bhagavān sarvasatvānāṃ hitārthe bodhimāptavān |
tattathātra manuṣyāṇāṃ dharmamādeṣṭumarhati || 29.81{80} ||
tataḥ svarge samāgaṃtuṃ śaknuvanti na ke cana |
devāstu sarvalokeṣu pracaranti yathechayā || 29.82{81} ||
tadbhagavānmanuṣyānāṃ hitārthāyānukampayā |
(rm 339)
saddharmaṃ samupādeṣṭuṃ jambūdvīpamavātaret || 29.83{82} ||
vayaṃ sarve sadā nityaṃ bhavatāṃ śaraṇaṃ gatāḥ |
satkṛtya śraddhayā dharmaṃ śrutvā saṃprabhajemahi || 29.84{83} ||
evamasmadvacobhistvaṃ gatvā tatra surālaye |
bhagavaṃtaṃ jagannāthaṃ saṃprārthyeha samānaya || 29.85{84} ||
iti taiḥ prārthyamāne'haṃ tatheti pratibodhitaḥ |
bhavatāṃ vijñāpanāṃ kartuṃ sahasā samupācare || 29.86{85} ||
tadbhagavanmanuṣyāṇāṃ hitānukampayādhunā |
saddharmaṃ samupādeṣṭuṃ bhuvyeva tarttumarhati || 29.87{86} ||
iti tenārthitaṃ śrutvā bhagavān sa munīśvaraḥ |
taṃ maudgalyāyanaṃ dṛṣṭvā tūṣṇībhūtvādhyuvāsa tat || 29.88{87} ||
tasmiṃśca samaye tatra sarve śakrādayo'marāḥ |
saddharmaṃ śravaṇotsāhāḥ samāgātāḥ samaṃtataḥ || 29.89{88} ||
kṛtvā pūjāṃ munīndrasya praṇatvā caraṇāmbuje |
parivṛtya puraskṛtya dharmaṃ śrotumupāśrayan || 29.90{89} ||
tadā sa bhagavān dṛṣṭvā tān sarvan samupāśritān |
āryasatyaṃ samārabhya saddharmaṃ samupādiśat || 29.91{90} ||
tatsaddharmāmṛtaṃ pītvā sarve śakrādayo'pi te |
devāḥ saṃharṣitā bodhicaryābhivāñchito'bhavan || 29.92{91} ||
tatsamīkṣya sa maudgalyaḥ kiñcidvihasito mudā |
bhagavaṃtaṃ munīndraṃ taṃ dṛṣṭvaivaṃ samaciṃtayat || 29.93{92} ||
ihāpi bhagavānnevaṃ samākīrṇasabhāśritaḥ |
saddharmaṃ samupādiśya viharati yathābhuvi || 29.94{93} ||
atha sa bhagavāṃstasya maudgalyasya mahāmateḥ |
manaścintitamājñāya taṃ maudgalyaṃ samabravīt || 29.95{94} ||
na me maudgalya satkāravaṃdhitaṃ bhavate manaḥ |
api tvahaṃ yathechāmi tathā sarvaṃ samṛddhyati || 29.96{95} ||
āyāntvihāmarāḥ sarve iti mayā yadecchyate |
tadā sarva ime devā āgachaṃti samaṃtataḥ || 29.97{96} ||
gachantu ca surāḥ sarve iti mayā yadehyate |
tadā sarva ime devā gachaṃti ca svamālayam || 29.98{97} ||
evaṃ me sarvakāryeṣu mano'bhilaṣitaṃ yathā |
tathātra sarvakāryāṇi siddhyaṃte hi sadā tadā || 29.99{98} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa śrutvā maudgalya ātmavit |
śāstuḥ pādāmbujau natvā sāṃjaliḥ samupāśrayat || 29.100{99} ||
tataḥ sabhāṃ surāṃ kīrṇāṃ sa maudgalyāyano yatiḥ |
tāra sarvāṃ saṃnirīkṣyaivaṃ bhagavantamabhāṣata || 29.101{100} ||
vicitrā bhagavan devāparṣadiyaṃ virājate |
nūnamime surāḥ sarve saṃbuddhapadalābhinaḥ || 29.102{1} ||
santi hyasyāṃ sabhāyāṃ ye saṃbuddhe saṃprasāditāḥ |
kāyabhedādihotpannāste saṃbodhilābhinaḥ || 29.103{2} ||
santi dharme prasannā ye āryaśīlaiḥ samanvitāḥ |
kāyabhedādihotpannāste'pi saṃbodhilābhinaḥ || 29.104{3} ||
(rm 340)
santi saṃghe prasannā ye āryadharmānucāriṇaḥ |
kāyabhedādihotpannāste'pi saṃbodhilābhinaḥ || 29.105{4} ||
iti maudgalyasaṃproktaṃ śrutvā sa bhagavāṃstathā |
taṃ maudgalyāyanaṃ dṛṣṭvānuvarṇayan sāmādiśat || 29.106{5} ||
evametaddhi maudgalya vicitreyaṃ mahāsabhā |
surendrapramukhā devā yatra santi pramoditāḥ || 29.107{6} ||
santi buddhe prasannā ye saṃbodhisādhanotsahāḥ |
kāyabhedādihotpannāste saṃbodhipadonmukhāḥ || 29.108{7} ||
santi dharme prasannā ye āryaśīlasamācarāḥ |
kāyabhedādihotpannāste'pi saṃbodhigāminaḥ || 29.109{8} ||
saṃti saṃghe prasannā ye āryasatyasamanvitāḥ |
kāyabhedādihotpannāste'pi saṃbodhilābhinaḥ || 29.110{9} ||
evaṃ śāstrā samākhyātaṃ śrutvā devādhipo'pi saḥ |
bhagavaṃtaṃ samālokya maudgalyaṃ ca tathābravīt || 29.111{10} ||
vicitrā bhagavan devaparṣadiyaṃ virājate |
yatra santi mahāsatvāḥ saṃbodhisādhanodyatāḥ || 29.112{11} ||
santi buddhe prasannā ye śraddhayā śaraṇaṃ gatāḥ |
kāyabhedādihotpannāste saṃbuddhapadalābhinaḥ || 29.113{12} ||
saṃti dharme prasannā ye śraddhayā śaraṇaṃ gatāḥ |
kāyabhedādihotpannāste saṃbuddhapadalābhinaḥ || 29.114{13} ||
santi dharme prasannā ye śraddhayā śaraṇaṃ gatāḥ |
kāyabhedādihotpannāste'pi saṃbodhilābhinaḥ || 29.115{14} ||
saṃti saṃghe prasannā ye śraddhayā śaraṇaṃ gatāḥ |
kāyabhedādihotpannāste'pi saṃbodhilābhinaḥ || 29.116{15} ||
iti devādhipākhyātaṃ śrutvānyāpi surātmajaḥ |
maudgalyaṃ śrīghanaṃ cāpi śakraṃ caikṣyaivamabravīt || 29.117{16} ||
vicitreyaṃ mahāsatva devasabhā virājate |
santi yatra mahātmānaḥ saṃbodhisādhodyatāḥ || 29.118{17} ||
santi saṃghe prasannā ye satkṛtya prabhajanti te |
dehabhedādihotpannā bhavaṃti bodhilābhinaḥ || 29.119{18} ||
santi dharme prasannā ye bhajanti śraddhayā mudā |
dehabhedādihotpannāste'pi saṃbodhilābhinaḥ || 29.120{19} ||
santi buddhe prasannā ye bhajaṃti śaraṇaṃ gatāḥ |
dehabhedādihotpannāste'pi saṃbodhilābhinaḥ || 29.121{20} ||
evametatsamākhyātaṃ maudgalyena mahātmanā |
munīndraśakradevaiśca śrutvā sarve surā mudā || 29.122{21} ||
prabodhitāḥ parijñāya saṃbuddhaśaraṇaṃ yayuḥ |
tatra sarve'pi te devāstriratnabhajanodyatāḥ || 29.123{22} ||
tatpuṇyaiḥ śrotaāpattiphalaṃ sākṣātsamāyayuḥ || 29.124{23!} ||
tatastāṃ śrīghanaṃ nātvā maudgalyaṃ ca prasāditāḥ |
te śakrādayo devāḥ sarve svasvālayāṃ yayuḥ || 29.125{24} ||
atha so'rhanmahābhijño maudgalyāyana ātmavit |
bhagavantaṃ tamānamya sāṃjalirevamabravīt || 29.126{25} ||
bhagavannātha sarvajña bhavatkāryyaṃ prasiddhyati |
(rm 341)
atra sarve surā bhadraiścaranti bodhimānasāḥ || 29.127{26} ||
tattatrāpi manuṣyāṇāṃ hitāya bhagavānapi |
jambudvīpe'dhunā śāstaḥ samavatarttumarhati || 29.128{27} ||
iti saṃprārthite tena maudgalyena mahātmanā |
bhagavāṃstaṃ mahāsatvaṃ maudgalyamevamādiśat || 29.129{28} ||
sādhu gachādhunā tatra jambudvīpe nijāśrame |
gatvā lokasabhāmadhye sthitvaivaṃ saṃprabodhaya || 29.130{29} ||
avatarejjagadvandhurito'hni saptame divaḥ |
sāṃkāśe nagare hyāpajjure vana udumvare || 29.131{30} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa śrutvā maudgalya ātmavit |
tatheti prativijñapya tathā kartuṃ samaichata || 29.132{31} ||
atha sa ṛddhimān yogī maudgalyāyana ātmavit |
sāṃjalistaṃ muniṃ natvā samādhiṃ vidadhe tadā || 29.133{32} ||
tataḥ so'rhaṃ mahābhijño maudgalyo'ntarhito divi |
kṣaṇātpakṣī yathākāśājjambudvīpamavātarat || 29.134{33} ||
tatra jetāśrame so'rhannavatārya nijāśrame |
śubhāsanasamāsīnaḥ prabhāsayannyaṣīdata || 29.135{34} ||
taṃ dṛṣṭvāsana āsīnaṃ maudgalyaṃ samupāgatam |
matvā te sāṃghikāḥ sarve muditāḥ samupācaran || 29.136{35} ||
tacchrutvā muditāḥ sarve lokāścāpi nṛpādayaḥ |
vihāre jetakārāme sahasā samupācaran || 29.137{36} ||
tatrāsanasamāsīnaṃ maudgalyaṃ taṃ maharddhikam |
dṛṣṭvā sarve'pi te natvā parivṛtyopatasthire || 29.138{37} ||
tataste bhikṣavaḥ sarve maudgalyaṃ taṃ maharddhikam |
kṛtāṃjalipuṭā natvā paprachurevamādarāt || 29.139{38} ||
bhadanta kuśalaṃ kaccidbhavato'pi jagadguroḥ |
kutra sa bhagavān dharmamādiśaṃstiṣṭhate divi || 29.140{39} ||
kadā sa bhagavāṃcchāstā svargādiha samācaret |
kathaṃ cāpi samākhyāti tatsarvaṃ samupādiśa || 29.141{40} ||
iti taiḥ prārthitaṃ śrutvā sa maudgalyāyano yatiḥ |
tāṃ sarvān saṃghikāllokāṃścāpi dṛṣṭvaivamabravīt || 29.142{41} ||
bhavantaḥ kuśalaṃ śāstu munīndrasya mamāpi ca |
yuṣmākamapi nāmno'hamavandaṃ taṃ munīśvaram || 29.143{42} ||
parijātamupāśritya sendradevaiḥ samarcitaḥ |
mātuḥ sa bhagavān dharmamādiśaṃstiṣṭhate divi || 29.144{43} ||
yathā yuṣmābhirākhyātaṃ tathā niveditaṃ mayā |
śrutvā sa bhagavāṃcchāstā mamaivaṃ samupādiśat || 29.145{44} ||
ito'hni saptame svargādavatareya bhūtale |
sāṃkāśanagaropāpajjure vane udumvare || 29.146{45} ||
ityādiśya munīndreṇa preṣito'hamihācare |
etadyūyaṃ parijñāya prābhyanandata sāṃpratam || 29.147{46} ||
iti tenārhatākhyātaṃ śrutvā te saṃghikā mudā |
sevaṃ nṛpādi lokāśca prābhyanandan pramoditāḥ || 29.148{47} ||
(rm 342)
tataste sāṃghikāḥ sarve lokā bhūpādayo'pi ca |
sāṃkāśanagaropāpajjure vane upācaran || 29.149{48} ||
tadā sa bhagavāṃstatra svarge sendrān surānapi |
mātaraṃ samupāmaṃtrya pura evamupādiśat || 29.150{49} ||
mātaḥ sendrādi devāśca yūyaṃ sarve samādarāt |
triratnabhajanaṃ kṛtvā pracaradhvaṃ sadā śubhe || 29.151{50} ||
gacheyaṃ sāṃprataṃ dharmamupadeṣṭuṃ mahītale |
nṛṇāmapi hitārthena gantumichāmi nānyathā || 29.152{51} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa sarve sendrāḥ surā api |
mātāpi sāṃjalirnatvā prārthayattaṃ munīśvaram || 29.153{52} ||
bhagavannātha sarvajña ko'smākaṃ dharmamādiśet |
tadatra kṛpayā dharmamādeṣṭuṃ sthātumarhati || 29.154{53} ||
iti taiḥ prārthite bhūyo bhagavān sa munīśvaraḥ |
mātaraṃ tān surān sarvān dṛṣṭvaivaṃ ca samādiśat || 29.155{54} ||
mātarnāhaṃ sadaikatra tiṣṭheyaṃ dharmamādiśan |
sarvasatvahitārthena saṃbuddho'smi jagadguruḥ || 29.156{55} ||
tanmātarahamālokya martyānāṃ hitakāmpayā |
saddharmāmṛtasaṃdānaṃ dātuṃ gachāmi sāṃpratam || 29.157{56} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa śrutvā mātā surā api |
sendrāḥ sarve'pi te śāstuḥ pādāmbuje praṇemire || 29.158{57} ||
tataste tridaśāḥ sarve mātāpi tasya tāyinaḥ |
tridhā pradakṣiṇīkṛtya natvā svasvālayaṃ yayuḥ || 29.159{58} ||
tataḥ sa bhagavāṃstatra devālaye samāhitaḥ |
tatsamādhiṃ samādhāya kṣaṇādantarhito'carat || 29.160{59} ||
tataḥ sa bhagavāṃstatra jambudvīpe prabhāsayan |
sāṃkāśanagaropāpajjure dāve udumvare || 29.161{60} ||
avatīrya kṣaṇāttatra śuddhāsane surāstṛte |
samādhāya samāsīnastasthau dhyānasamāhitaḥ || 29.162{61} ||
taṃ dṛṣṭvā svāsanāsīnaṃ śrīghanaṃ samupāgatam |
sarve te sāṃghikā lokā bhūpādayo'pyupācaran || 29.163{62} ||
tatra taṃ śrīghanaṃ natvā sarve te saṃpramoditāḥ |
tridhā pradakṣiṇīkṛtya samabhyarcyopatasthire || 29.164{63} ||
brahmaśakrādi devāśca yakṣagaṃdharvakinnarāḥ |
daityā vidyādharā nāgā garuḍāḥ siddhādayo'pi ca || 29.165{64} ||
śrīghanaṃ samāyātaṃ draṣṭuṃ sarve samāgatāḥ |
natvā pradakṣiṇīkṛtya samabhyarcyopatasthire || 29.166{65} ||
tatropapāduko bhikṣurbhagavantaṃ sasāṃghikam |
devādi sarvalokāṃśca bhojayituṃ nyamaṃtrayat || 29.167{66} ||
bhagavānapi devādi lokāḥ sarve prasāditāḥ |
tasyānugrahamādhātuṃ tatheti pratyamodayan || 29.168{67} ||
atha sa bhagavāṃstatra bhojanasamayāgate |
saṃghamelāpane gaṇḍīmākoṭayan pranādinīm || 29.169{68} ||
