tataḥ sa svagṛhaprāptaḥ suprasannamukhāmbujaḥ |
tāṃ śāṃtamatāṃ bhāryāṃ samāmaṃtryaivamabravīt || 37.112{12} ||
priye dhanyāsi kalyāṇī yattvayāhaṃ prabodhitaḥ |
saṃbuddhaśāsanaṃ ratnamāptavānahamāgataḥ || 37.113{13} ||
tadyathā samupādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇānuśāsinā |
tathāvāṃ tadvrataṃ devyāścarevahi samādarāt || 37.114{14} ||
iti bhartrā samuddiṣṭe sāpi śāntamatī mudā |
bharttāraṃ taṃ samālokya sudṛṣṭyaivamabhāṣata || 37.115{15} ||
ayi svāmin kathaṃ śāstra samādiṣṭaṃ vadasva me |
yathādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa tatha carevahi dhruvam || 37.116{16} ||
iti bhāryoditaṃ śrutvā bharttā dharmadhvajo'tha saḥ |
mudā śāṃtamatīṃ bhāryāṃ tāṃ samīkṣyaivamabravīt || 37.117{17} ||
śṛṇu bhadre yathādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa tathā khalu |
yadvrataṃ vasudhārāyāstadvidhiṃ te pravakṣyate || 37.118{18} ||
ityukte svāminā tena sā ca śāntamatī priyā |
bharttāraṃ taṃ samālokya muditaivamabhāṣata || 37.119{19} ||
svāminstena munīndreṇa yathādiṣṭaṃ tathā bhavān |
suvistaraṃ samākhyāya mano me saṃvinodata || 37.120{20} ||
ityukte bhāryayā sātha dharmadhvajaḥ suvistaram |
yathādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa tathā sarvaṃ nyavedayat || 37.121{21} ||
tacchrutvā sā prabodhantī śāṃtamatyanumoditā |
tathā tasyā mahādevyā vrataṃ caritumaichata || 37.122{22} ||
tatastaṃ svāminaṃ natvā sā ca śāntamatī satī |
tadvratārambhasāmagrīṃ sādhitumevamabravīt || 37.123{23} ||
svāmin vrataṃ cariṣyāvo vasulakṣmyā yathāvidhi |
sāmagrīṃ sādhayeyāsya tadanujñāṃ pradehi me || 37.124{24} ||
iti bhāryoditaṃ śrutvā dharmadhvajaḥ sa moditaḥ |
sādhyatāṃ sarvasāmāgrīmiti tāṃ sāṃvyanodayat || 37.125{25} ||
(rm 416)
tataḥ sā muditāḥ sarve sāmagrīṃ sādhayantyapi |
bharttāraṃ tamupāmaṃtrya darśayantyevamabravīt || 37.126{26} ||
sarvaṃ me sādhitaṃ siddhaṃ svāminnatra prasīdatu |
tatsadguruṃ samāmaṃtrya vratamālabdhumarhati || 37.127{27} ||
iti bhāryoditaṃ śrutvā sa dharmadhvaja ādarāt |
sadguruṃ samupārādhya natvevaṃ prārthayanmudā || 37.128{28} ||
bhadanta śrīvasudhārādevyā vrataṃ carevahi |
tasmātdevīṃ samārādhya vratopadeśamādiśa || 37.129{29} ||
iti saṃprārthite tena tatheti hi sa sadguruḥ |
pratiśrutya yathādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa tathācarat || 37.130{30} ||
tathā bhādrapade māse kṛṣṇapakṣe sa sadguruḥ |
dvitīyāyaṃ tithau tīrthe snātvā suvāsasā sudhīḥ || 37.131{31} ||
yathāvidhiṃ samārabhya maṃḍalaṃ samavarttayat |
tatra tāṃ sagaṇāṃ devīṃ pratiṣṭhāpya samarcayat || 37.132{32} ||
tatparedyustritīyāyāṃ susnātaṃ śubhāmbaram |
dharmadhvajaṃ sadāraṃ taṃ paṃcagavyairvyaśodhayat || 37.133{33} ||
tatastau daṃpatī śāstrā yathādiṣṭaṃ samāhitau |
śrutvā tathā samādhāya pracaratu vrataṃ mudā || 37.134{34} ||
yathā śāstrā samādiṣṭaṃ tathā kṛtvā vrataṃ mudā |
gaṇacakrādikaṃ sarvaṃ samāpya tau nanandatuḥ || 37.135{35} ||
tatastasya gṛhe devīṃ vasudhārā samāśritā |
sarvadravyaṃ samāpūryya tasthau sadā prasāditā || 37.136{36} ||
tatastasya gṛhe saṃpaddine pravarddhitā |
saratnadhātusadvastraśasyādi dravyajātayaḥ || 37.137{37} ||
tā evaṃ varddhitā dṛṣṭvā sadāraḥ sa gṛhādhipaḥ |
sa vismayo muditotphullavaktrāmbujo'bhyanaṃdata || 37.138{38} ||
tataḥ sa sarvadārthibhyo datvā dānaṃ yathepsitam |
triratnabhajanaṃ kṛtvā sukhaṃ bhuktvā śubhe'carat || 37.139{39} ||
taddṛṣṭvā tasya gehasya samīpastho dvijottamaḥ |
viṣṇudāsābhidho vijño vismayoddhatamānasaḥ || 37.140{40} ||
dhīmatīṃ ramaṇīṃ bhāryāṃ suviśuddhāśayāṃ satīm |
niḥśvasanstāṃ samāmaṃtrya punarevamabhāṣata || 37.141{41} ||
paśya bhadrasya mitrasya gṛhe saṃpatsamṛddhitā |
kimanena kṛtaṃ dharmaṃ yenaivaṃ śrīḥ pravarddhitā || 37.142{42} ||
kimāvābhyāṃ kṛtaṃ pāpaṃ yenaivaṃ no vipatsadā |
kimatrāvāṃ kariṣyāvo yena saṃpatpravarddhitā || 37.143{43} ||
tadupāyaṃ na jānāmi ko me upadiśeddhitam |
dhanyāste dhanavaṃtā ye yathākāma sukhāśinaḥ || 37.144{44} ||
kiṃ te sadguṇavaṃto'pi kṛpaṇā ye daridritā |
varaṃ prāṇaparityāgaṃ na tvevaṃ jīvitaṃ bhave || 37.145{45} ||
kiṃ teṣāṃ sadguṇaiścāpi yeṣāṃ saṃpadgṛhe na hi |
dhanavān puruṣo dhīraḥ kulīno nirguṇo'pi ca |
(rm 417)
sarve hi dhanināṃ vaśyā paṃḍitāḥ suguṇā api || 37.146{46} ||
dhanavānnirguṇaścāpi mānyate guruvajjanaiḥ |
dhanahīnā na pūjyaṃte paṃḍitā brahmaṇā api || 37.147{47} ||
dhigme jīvitamevaṃ hi brāhmaṇasyāpi duḥkhinaḥ |
varamevādyetanmṛtyurna tveva cirajīvitum || 37.148{48} ||
kimanenāpi jīvena kevaladuḥkhabhoginā |
tadatra marttumichāmi naiva jīveya duḥkhabhāk || 37.149{49} ||
sarveṣāmapi jaṃtūnāmavaśyaṃ maraṇaṃ bhave |
tadatra vidyamāno'haṃ kathaṃ loke careya hi || 37.150{50} ||
ityevaṃ vilapaṃ vipro viṣṇudāsaḥ sa mohitaḥ |
galadaśruvirukṣākṣastasthau muhurviniḥśvasan || 37.151{51} ||
ityevaṃ vilapantan taṃ dṛṣṭvā sā dhīmatī priyā |
bodhayituṃ samāśvāsya bharttāramevamabravīt || 37.152{52} ||
mā viṣādaṃ kṛthāḥ svāmin dhairyyamālambya tiṣṭhatu |
kiṃ kariṣyāvahe hyatra daivātsaṃpanna tau gṛhe || 37.153{53} ||
tathāpi dhairyyamālambya yatnaṃ kuru samāhitaḥ |
yatnena siddhyate sarvaṃ guṇadravyādi sādhanam || 37.154{54} ||
tadatrāhaṃ vadiṣyāmi hitārthaṃ te śṛṇu prabho |
bhāryā hi svāmino bhartturhitārtham'bhyanudeśinī || 37.155{55} ||
yadayaṃ gṛhabhṛdbharttā dharmadhvajastava priyaḥ |
suhṛnmitraṃ mahāsādhustadādarātsa pṛchyatām || 37.156{56} ||
yathā tena samādiṣṭaṃ tathaiva krīyatāṃ prabho |
tatastasya yathā saṃpattathāsmākaṃ bhavedapi || 37.157{57} ||
iti bhāryoditaṃ śrutvā viṣṇudāsaḥ sa tairthikaḥ |
bauddhavākye viruddhatvādanichannevamabravīt || 37.158{58} ||
vayaṃ hi brāhmaṇā bhadre buddhadevī vasuṃdharā |
tadvidhiṃ vedaśāstreṣu kathitaṃ na kvacidapi || 37.159{59} ||
tatkathaṃ svakulācāraṃ tyaktvānyacharaṇaṃ gatāḥ |
bauddhopadeśamāsādya lakṣmyā vrataṃ caremahi || 37.160{60} ||
ye tyaktvā svakulācāramanyatra śaraṇaṃ gatāḥ |
vrataṃ caraṃti saṃpattilābhilābhākulāśayāḥ || 37.161{61} ||
te svadharmaparibhraṣṭā ihāpi kliṣṭabhoginaḥ |
rogiṇo duhitātmāno mṛtā yāsyaṃti durgatīm || 37.162{62} ||
iti bhadre na me vāṃchā bauddhadevyā vrate'śubhe |
varaṃ prāṇaparityāgaṃ nānyadvrataṃ caremahi || 37.163{63} ||
asmākamapi yā devī mahālakṣmī kuleśvarī |
tasyā vrataṃ samādhāya caremahi sadādarāt || 37.164{64} ||
tataḥ sā śrīmahādevī gṛhe'śmākaṃ samāśritā |
yathābhivāṃchitaṃ dravyaṃ sarvaṃ dadyātprasāditā || 37.165{65} ||
iti tasyā mahālakṣmyā vrataṃ carāvahe vayam |
tattadvratasya sāmagrīṃ sādhaya sarvamādarāt || 37.166{66} ||
iti bhartroditaṃ śrutvā dhīmatī sā pramoditā |
(rm 418)
tathetyabhyanumodantī tatsāmagrīmasādhayat || 37.167{67} ||
tatra sā dhīmatī tasyāḥ śāṃtamatyāḥ puro gatā |
tadvṛttāntaṃ sāmākhyāya sāmagrīṃ samayācata || 37.168{68} ||
sāpi śāṃtamatī tasyai dhīmatyai saṃpramoditā |
yadyatsaṃprārthitaṃ dravyaṃ tattatsarvaṃ dadau mudā || 37.169{67} ||
taddravyaṃ sā samādāya bhartturagre samāgatā |
tatsarvaṃ samupasthāpya vinodyaivamabhāṣata || 37.170{70} ||
svāmin tvayā yathādiṣṭaṃ tatsarvaṃ sādhitaṃ mayā |
tathā tasyā mahālakṣmyā vratamārabha sāṃpratam || 37.171{71} ||
iti bhāryoditaṃ śrutvā viṣṇudāsaḥ sa moditaḥ |
yathāvidhi mahālakṣmīvrataṃ caritumārabhat || 37.172{72} ||
māse bhādrapade kṛṣṇapakṣe'ṣṭamyāṃ yathāvidhi |
maṇḍalādīn pratisthāpya śuddhaśīlaḥ samāhitaḥ || 37.173{73} ||
sadārastāṃ mahālakṣmīṃ samāvāhya samarcayan |
vidhinā tadvrataṃ kṛtvā saṃpattiṃ prārthayanmudā || 37.174{74} ||
tataḥ sa brāhmaṇaḥ kṛtvā tadvrataṃ susamāptitam |
sabhāryaḥ suprasannātmā prācarattāṃ śriyaṃ smaran || 37.175{75} ||
tathā tasya gṛhe saṃpatsamudbhūtābhyajāyata |
dṛṣṭvā tau daṃpatī tatra mahānaṃdamavāpatuḥ || 37.176{76} ||
tataḥ sa brāhmaṇo lubdho dṛṣṭvā tān saṃpadaṃ mudā |
sarvān gupte nidhāyaiva na kiṃ cittadvyayaṃ vyadhāt || 37.177{77} ||
bhūyastṛṣṇābhisaṃtapto mātsaryābhihatāśayaḥ |
kasmai cidapi taddravyaṃ kiṃ ciddānaṃ dadau kvacit || 37.178{78} ||
tathā saṃrakṣya taddravyaṃ nidhāya tu viyatnataḥ |
gopayitvā svayaṃ bhoktumapi naivābhyavāṃchata || 37.179{79} ||
tathāpi sa pralubdhātmā nityaṃ kṛpaṇo'rthivat |
dhanināṃ samupāśritya yācitvā dhanamārjayat || 37.180{80} ||
tathārjanaḥ kṛtastasya gṛhe dravyaṃ na varddhitam |
dine dine nihīnatvaṃ gatvā kṣayaṃ kramādyayau || 37.181{81} ||
tataḥ sa brāhmaṇo lubdho dhanināmarthino gṛhe |
dṛṣṭvātiruṣitaḥ sarvān paribhāṣyābhyaniṃdayat || 37.182{82} ||
tatpāpatāvipākena gṛhe yadgopitaṃ dhanam |
tatsarvaṃ nihṛtaṃ caurairnidagdhaṃ cāpi vahninā || 37.183{83} ||
tataḥ sa brāhmaṇastīvrakleśāgniparitāpitaḥ |
daridritāvibhagnāśaḥ sabhāryo nyavasadgṛhe || 37.184{34!} ||
tasminnavasare tasyā dhīmatyāḥ sa pitā dvijaḥ |
hariśarmābhidho gehe yajñaṃ karttuṃ sadārabhat || 37.185{35} ||
tadā sa vāṃdhavān sārvāñjñātīnmitrasuhṛjjanān |
bhaginībhāgineyāṃśca jāmātrīṃśtatsutānapi || 37.186{36} ||
duhitṝrapi sarvāśca nyamaṃtrayatsamādarāt || 37.187{37} ||
(rm 419)
tatra te vāṃdhavā sarve jñātimitrasuhṛjjanāḥ |
bhaginyā bhāgineyāśca jāmātaro'pi tatsutāḥ || 37.188{38} ||
duhitarastathā pautrāḥ pautryādayo'pi cāpare |
sarve te samupāgatya samupatasthurādritāḥ || 37.189{39} ||
tatra sā kṛpaṇībhūtā durbhageti pitāpi saḥ |
tatra yajñamahotsāhe tāmekāṃ na nyamaṃtrayat || 37.190{40} ||
tadā sā dhīmatī śrutvā pituryajñamahotsavam |
kathaṃ me vismṛtaṃ pitrā mātrāpi vāṃdhavairapi || 37.191{41} ||
iti ciṃtāviṣāghrātahṛdayā parimūrchitā |
bhojane'pi nirutsāhā tasthau lajjānvitā gṛhe || 37.192{42} ||
tathā sa viṣṇudāso'pi sasurasya gṛhe tadā |
śrutvā yajñamahotsāhaṃ tāṃ bhāryāmevamabravīt || 37.193{43} ||
kathaṃ te janakenāvāṃ vismṛtau kena hetunā |
naiva nimaṃtritau bhadre yathā sarve nimaṃtritā || 37.194{44} ||
iti bhartroditaṃ śrutvā dhīmatī sātilajjitā |
bharttāraṃ viṣṇudāsaṃ taṃ bodhayantyevamabravīt || 37.195{45} ||
kimatrāhaṃ vadiṣyāmi yannau saṃpadgṛhe'sti na |
tena nau lajjayāmbāpi nimaṃtrituṃ na vāṃchate || 37.196{46} ||
iti sā dhīmatī lajjāsaṃkuṃcitāśayānanā |
bhartrā saha vilapyaiva tasthau gṛhe nirāśitā || 37.197{47} ||
tadā tajjanaṃ smṛtvā dhīmatīṃ tāmanāgatām |
kāñcitpaṭhigatāṃ ceṭīṃ samāhūyaivamabravīt || 37.198{48} ||
ayi sakhi sutā me sā dhīmatā yadi dṛśyate |
madvācasā sametyāsau vaktavyaivaṃ laghu tvayā || 37.199{49} ||
pituste mandire yajñamahotsāhaṃ pravarttate |
tatkathaṃ na samāyāsi sabharttāgantumarhasi || 37.200{50} ||
iti saṃdiśya te mātrā preṣitāhaṃ tvadantike |
tanmāturvacanaṃ śrutvāgacheti tatpuro vada || 37.201{51} ||
tatsaṃdiṣṭaṃ tayā śrutvā tatheti pratibhāṣya sā |
ceṭī tato'bhigachantī dhīmatyā bhavane yayau || 37.202{52} ||
tatra tāṃ dhīmatīṃ dṛṣṭvā sā ceṭī samupasthitā |
yathā mātrā samādiṣṭaṃ tathā sarvaṃ nyavedayat || 37.203{53} ||
tattayā kathitaṃ śrutvā dhīmatī sāśrumocinī |
viniḥśvasya prasūṃ smṛtvā vilapantyaivamabravīt || 37.204{54} ||
hā mātaḥ kathamadyāhaṃ saṃsmṛtā sāṃprataṃ tvayā |
kimāgacheya te putrī pāpinī duḥkhabhāginī || 37.205{55} ||
tasminneva kṣaṇe bharttā viṣṇudāsa upācarat |
tāṃ ceṭīṃ samupālokya kimarthamityapṛchata || 37.206{56} ||
iti tenābhisaṃpṛṣṭe sā ceṭī racitāṃjaliḥ |
brāhmaṇaṃ taṃ praṇamyaitatsarvaṃ vṛtāṃtamabravīt || 37.207{57} ||
tathā tatkathitaṃ śrutvā viṣṇudāso'pi sa dvijaḥ |
galadaśruvirukṣākṣastāṃ bhāryāmevamabravīt || 37.208{58} ||
kiṃ vadiṣyāvahe bhārye daivānnau jāyate vipat |
(rm 420)
tathāpi dhairyyamālamvya gacha draṣṭuṃ piturmanaḥ || 37.209{59} ||
iti bhartroditaṃ śrutvā dhīmatī sā kuvāsinī |
nirmaṇḍanātsutān putrīḥ samādāya śanairyayau || 37.210{60} ||
tato māturgṛhe prāptā dṛṣṭvā tau pitarau puraḥ |
gatvā sā dhīmatī natvā tasthau lajjāhatāśayā || 37.211{61} ||
pitarau tāṃ sutāṃ dṛṣṭvā vichandāṃ lajjitāśayam |
kiṃ ciddṛṣṭvāgatāsīti kathitvaiva niṣedatuḥ || 37.212{62} ||
tataḥ sā dhīmatī sarvāñjñātīn bhrātrīṃśca vāṃdhavān |
bhaginīḥ suhṛdo jeṣṭhān praṇanāma yathākramam || 37.213{63} ||
tāṃ dṛṣṭvā jñātayaḥ sarve bhrātaro vāṃdhavā api |
bhaginyāpi suhṛdaścāpi dṛṣṭvopahasya jalpire || 37.214{64} ||
tathā prahasitaṃ sarve sā dṛṣṭvātitrapānvitā |
svadaivasmṛtimādhāya niḥśvasyaikānta āśrayat || 37.215{65} ||
tataḥ sa hariśarmā sa purohito yathāvidhi |
yajñakarmasamārabhya yathākāmaṃ samāpayat || 37.216{66} ||
tatastān vāṃdhavāñjñātīn suhṛnmitrajanānapi |
satkṛtyātyabhisaṃtuṣṭān sādareṇa vyasarjayat || 37.217{67} ||
tataste jñātayaḥ sarve vaṃdhumitrasuhṛjjanāḥ |
naṃditāstaṃ praśaṃsitvā svasvālayaṃ mudā yayuḥ || 37.218{68} ||
tataste susthitāḥ sarve jñātivaṃdhusuhṛjjanāḥ |
nānākathābhisaṃlāpaṃ kurvantaḥ saṃniṣedire || 37.219{69} ||
tataḥ sā dhīmatī paścādbhuktvaikānte nirādritā |
putraputrī samādāya śivasya bhavane'carat || 37.220{70} ||
tatra sā stṛṇamāstīrya putraputrīsamanvitā |
dāridryaduḥkhacintānuniḥśvasantī nyaṣīdata || 37.221{71} ||
tatra sa viṣṇudāso'pi paścādbhuktvā nirādritaḥ |
śivālaye sthitāṃ śrutvā saha sthātumupācarat || 37.222{72} ||
bharttāraṃ tamupāyātaṃ dṛṣṭvā sā dhīmatī tadā |
galadaśruvirukṣākṣā vilapantyevamabravīt || 37.223{73} ||
dhigme jīvaṃ daridrāyāḥ kimevaṃ jīvitena hi |
pitrorapi dayāsnehadṛṣṭirna vidyate mayi || 37.224{74} ||
yatpitroḥ samāḥ sarvā bhaginyaḥ svātmajā api |
tathāhaṃ mānitā naiva mānitāstā yathādarāt || 37.225{75} ||
dhigjīvitaṃ daridrāyā janmāpi dhikca mānuṣe |
kiṃ rūpaiḥ suguṇaiḥ vāpi vidyābhiḥ kiṃ dhanaṃ vinā || 37.226{76} ||
hā daiva kiṃ mayā pāpaṃ dāruṇaṃ prakṛtaṃ purā |
yanme na vidyate saṃpatpitro dadhāpi dayā mayi || 37.227{77} ||
tadatra kiṃ kariṣyāmi yāsyāmi śaraṇaṃ kuha |
kā māṃ rakṣetsvadaivotthadaridryaduḥkhatāpinīm || 37.228{78} ||
kimevaṃ jīvitenāpi varaṃ mṛtyurhi sāṃpratām |
avaśyameva marttavyaṃ sarveṣāmapi janminām || 37.229{79} ||
evaṃ nānāpralāpena vilapantī sutaṃ sutām |
(rm 421)
bharttāraṃ ca muhurdṛṣṭvā kāruṇyārttā ruroda sā || 37.230{80} ||
evaṃ vilāpaṃ kurvantīṃ rudantīṃ tāṃ vilokya saḥ |
viṣṇudāso viniḥśvasya samāśvāsyaivamabravīt || 37.231{81} ||
priye'tra mā rudihi tvaṃ kiṃ kariṣyāvahe'dhunā |
yadāvābhyāṃ kṛtaṃ pāpaṃ bhoktavyameva tatphalam || 37.232{82} ||
tadatra dhairyyamālamvya tiṣṭha kiṃ vakṣyate bahu |
avaśyaṃ bhāvino bhāvā bhavaṃti mahatāmapi || 37.233{83} ||
śivo'pi sa virūpākṣo nīlakaṇṭho digaṃvaraḥ |
unmatto bhasmaliptāṃgaḥ kapālayo'pi rasthidhṛk || 37.234{84} ||
viṣṇurvīro'pi saṃsāre bhramitaḥ sa janārdanaḥ |
so'pi dāso validvāsthaścauro vyādheṣuṇāhataḥ || 37.235{85} ||
brahmā lokādhipaḥ so'pi kāmārtto'gamyakarmakṛt |
niyujyo nighnito vairisevakaśchinnamastaka || 37.236{86} ||
cicheda brahmaṇaḥ pūrvaṃ rudraḥ krodhātta paṃcamam |
tacchiro dustyajaṃ gṛhṇan brahmāṇḍaṃ parivabhrame || 37.237{87} ||
śaṃkhatīrthe gato rudrastacchiraḥ parimuktavān |
kapālamocanastīrtho dvitīyāvarttasaṃsthitaḥ || 37.238{88} ||
iti utkalakhaṇḍakṣetramāhātmye uktamāste |
evamanye'pi lokāśca munayastāpasā api || 37.239{89} ||
kopāgnidahitātmāno vrajanti narakeṣvapi |
evaṃ sarve'pi lokāśca svasvadaivānubhāvinaḥ || 37.240{90} ||
sukhino duḥkhino bhūtā bhramaṃti bhavasāgare |
kṣaṇāllabhanti saṃpattiṃ bhavanti ca daridritāḥ || 37.241{91} ||
sarve devādayo lokāḥ svakṛtakarmabhoginaḥ |
evaṃ matvātra saṃsāre sukhaṃ duḥkhaṃ svadaivajam || 37.242{92} ||
mā kṛthāstadviṣāda tvaṃ dhairyaṃ dhṛtvā samācara |
svakuleśamanusmṛtvā samabhyarcya yathāvidhiḥ |
śraddhayā śaraṇaṃ kṛtvā bhaja nityaṃ samāhitā || 37.243{93} ||
tatastatpuṇyapākena pāpaṃ no vilayaṃ vrajet |
tataḥ saṃpatsamṛddhiḥ syāttadā lapsyāvahe sukham || 37.244{94} ||
iti me vacanaṃ śrutvā dhairyyamālamvya sāṃpratam |
putraputrīṃ samādāya praihi gṛhe vrajāmahe || 37.245{95} ||
iti bhartroditaṃ śrutvā dhīmatī sābhibodhitā |
putraṃ putrīṃ purodhāya śanairbhartrā sahācarat || 37.246{96} ||
tatra sā svagṛhe gatvā kṣaṇaṃ sthitvā nirutsahā |
tataḥ śāntamatīṃ draṣṭuṃ samutthāya śanairyayau || 37.247{97} ||
tatra sā dhīmatī tasyāḥ śāṃtamatyāḥ purogatā |
galadaśruviliptāsyā niḥśvasaṃtyevamabravīt || 37.248{98} ||
śāṃtamate pituryajñamahotsāhapravarttitam |
tatsaṃdraṣṭu gatā tatra mahālajjānvitā caram || 37.249{99} ||
yanmāṃ dṛṣṭvā bhaginyo'pi jñātivaṃdhusuhṛjjanāḥ |
api sarve mitho dṛṣṭvā prajalpitābhyupāhasan || 37.250{100} ||
(rm 422)
bhāgineyāśca sarve'pi māṃ dṛṣṭvā ruṣitāśayāḥ |
putraputrīṃ ca me dṛṣṭvā phutkṛtya pratyātāḍayan || 37.251{1} ||
pitṛbhyāṃ mānitāḥ sarvāḥ bhaginyastā yathādarāt |
tathāhaṃ mānitā naiva draṣṭumapi na caiṣyate || 37.252{2} ||
rātrāvapi gṛhe vāsaṃ na me dattaṃ nirādarāt |
śivālaye niṣaṇṇā tanniśāṃ kṛcchrādvyalaṃghayam || 37.253{3} ||
evamahaṃ mahallajjāduḥkhāgnipatitāpitā |
marttumapi tadicchāmi kimevaṃ jīvitenapi || 37.254{4} ||
yadi te'sti kṛpā bhadre duḥkhinyāṃ mayi vidyate |
taddravyopārjanopāyaṃ samupadeṣṭumarhati || 37.255{5} ||
iti tayoditaṃ śrutvā śāntamatīrdayārditaḥ |
dhīmatīṃ tāṃ samālokya samāśvāsyaivamabravīt || 37.256{6} ||
dhīmatī śrīmahāsaṃpatsādhanopāyamasti me |
yadyatrāpyasti te vāṃchā pravakṣyāmi śṛṇuṣva tat || 37.257{7} ||
yadi śraddhāsti te devyā vasulakṣmyā vrataṃ cara |
tataste śrīvasundhārā mahādevī vasedgṛhe || 37.258{8} ||
tatastasyā mahādevyāḥ kṛpādṛṣṭiprasādataḥ |
saṃpattiste samudbhūtā pravarddhitā bhavedgṛhe || 37.259{9} ||
tadā tvaṃ śraddhayārthibhyo dānaṃ datvā yathepsitam |
triratnabhajanaṃ kṛtvā śubhe cara sukhāśinī || 37.260{10} ||
etatpuṇyavipākena sarvadā sadgatiṃ gatā |
kramādbodhiṃ samāsādya saṃbuddhapadamāpnuyāḥ || 37.261{11} ||
evaṃ matvāsti te vāṃchā saṃbuddhapada sādhane |
śrīdevyāḥ śaraṇaṃ gatvā samādhāya vrataṃ cara || 37.262{12} ||
iti tayā samādiṣṭaṃ śrutva sā dhīmatī mudā |
tāṃ śāṃtamatīmānamya sāṃjalīrevamabravīt || 37.263{13} ||
sādhu bhadre vrataṃ dhṛtvā śrīdevyāḥ śaraṇaṃ gatā |
triratnabhajanaṃ kṛtvā cariṣyāmi śubhe sadā || 37.264{14} ||
tadbhavaṃtī kṛpādṛṣṭyā tadupadeśamādiśa |
yāvajjīvaṃ cariṣyāmi śrīdevyā vratamādarāt || 37.265{15} ||
iti tayoditaṃ śrutvā śāntamatiḥ samādarāt |
dhīmatīṃ tāṃ samālokya punarevamabhāṣata || 37.266{16} ||
yadi bhadre'sti te satyaṃ snātvā śuddhāmvarāvṛtā |
prehi taṃ śrīghanaṃ nāthaṃ saṃsmṛtvā prārthayāvahe || 37.267{17} ||
tayeti samupādiṣṭaṃ śrutvā sā dhīmatī mudā |
sahasā svagṛhe gatvā snātvā śuddhāmvarāvṛtā || 37.268{18} ||
sa lājākṣatapuṣpāṇi gṛhitvā sahasā mudā |
tasyāḥ śāṃtamateragre prayātevamabhāṣata || 37.269{19} ||
bhadre tvayā yathādiṣṭaṃ tathāhaṃ samupāgatā |
taṃ munīndramanusmṛtvā prārthayeha samāgamam || 37.270{20} ||
iti tayoditaṃ śrutvā sā śāntamatirādarāt |
tāṃ dhīmatīṃ samāmaṃtrya prāsādāntaṃ yayau mudā || 37.271{21} ||
tatra śāntamatiḥ sāṭṭe dhīmatyā saha saṃsthitā |
(rm 423)
jānubhyāṃ bhuvi saṃsthitvā bhagavatsthitadigmukhā || 37.272{22} ||
saṃbhavantyuttarāsaṅgaṃ saha lājākṣatādibhiḥ |
sāmbupuṣpāñjaliṃ dhṛtvā natvaivaṃ prārthayanmunim || 37.273{23} ||
bhagavannātha sarvajña vijānīte bhavānapi |
yadiyaṃ dhīmatī sādhvī bhagavaccharaṇamāgatā || 37.274{24} ||
yadiyaṃ sāṃprataṃ śāsturupadeśādyathāvidhi |
śrīvasudhāmahādevyā vrataṃ caritumichati || 37.275{25} ||
tadatra samupāgatya kṛpayāsmai dayānidhe |
śrīsādhanaṃ vrataṃ śreṣṭhaṃ samupadeṣṭumarhati || 37.276{26} ||
iti saṃprārthya sā śāntamatistaṃ trijagadgurum |
anusmṛtvā tridhā nātvā prākṣipattajjalādikam || 37.277{27} ||
prakṣiptāni tayā tāni sāmbulājākṣatāṇyapi |
sadūrvākuṇḍapuṣpāṇi sarvāṇi gagaṇe śaran || 37.278{28} ||
tatastāni pragacchanti vihāyasā vihaṅgavat |
kośāmvyāṃ ghoṣirārāme vihāre samupāyayuḥ || 37.279{29} ||
tatrasthasya munīndrasya murddhā pari vihāyasi |
saṃsthitāni prabhāsanti virejustāni chatravat || 37.280{30} ||
taddṛṣṭvā bhikṣavaḥ sarve sāṃghikāste savismayāḥ |
kuto nimaṃtraṇamāyātamiti dhyātvābhitasthire || 37.281{31} ||
tatrānandaḥ samutthāya sāṃjaliḥ purato gataḥ |
taṃ munīndraṃ praṇatvaivaṃ papracha vismayānvitaḥ || 37.282{32} ||
bhagavan kuta etāni sāmbulājākṣatānyapi |
puṣpāṇi samupāyānti tatsamādeṣṭumarhati || 37.283{33} ||
iti tenoditaṃ śrutvā bhagavāṃ sa munīśvaraḥ |
tamānaṃdaṃ samāmaṃtrya samālokyaivamabravīt || 37.284{34} ||
yaḥ kāśīpure vipro viṣṇudāso daridritaḥ |
dharmadhvajo dhanī śrīmān gṛhastho dhanadopamaḥ || 37.285{35} ||
tayorbhārya ubhe sādhvau śāntamatī ramopamā |
dhīmatī brāhmaṇī bhadrā daridritā suduḥkhinī || 37.286{36} ||
tābhyāṃ śrīvasudhārāyā vrataṃ caritumichyate |
tadidaṃ nimaṃtraṇārthaṃ me upahāraṃ samāgatam || 37.287{37} ||
tasmācchrīvasudhārāyā vratavidhiṃ tvamātmanā |
upadeṣṭuṃ tayorgehe kāśyāṃ gaṃtuṃ samarhasi || 37.288{38} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa śrutvānaṃdastatheti saḥ |
śāsturājñāṃ śirodhṛtvā kāśyāṃ vihāyasā yayau || 37.289{39} ||
tatrākāśān sa ānaṃdaḥ śāṃtamatergṛhe puraḥ |
avatīrya sudhāraśmiriva tasthau prabhāsayan || 37.290{40} ||
tatrasthaṃ taṃ samālokya śāntamatiḥ sa dhīmatī |
muditā sahasā tasya praṇaṃtuṃ samupācarat || 37.291{41} ||
tatra sā samupāsṛtya śāṃtamatiḥ sadhīmatī|
tamānaṃdaṃ samālokya natvaivaṃ prārthayanmudā || 37.292{42} ||
bhadanta caraṇau vaṃde bhavatāṃ śaraṇaṃ gatā |
tadatra kṛpayāsmākamanugṛhya prasīdatu || 37.293{43} ||
yadasmākaṃ bhavāñchāstā tadgṛhe samupāśrayan |
(rm 424)
vasudhārāmahādevyā vratavidhiṃ samādiśa || 37.294{44} ||
yadiyaṃ dhīmatī bhadrā bhavatāmupadeśataḥ |
vasulakṣmyā mahādevyā vrataṃ caritumichati || 37.295{45} ||
tadasyai śrīmahādevyā vratavidhiṃ yathākramam |
vistaraṃ samupādeṣṭumarhati kṛpayā bhavān || 37.296{46} ||
iti saṃprārthitaṃ śāntamatyā tayā niśamya saḥ |
ānaṃdaste ubhe dṛṣṭvā tatheti pratyabhāṣata || 37.297{47} ||
tataḥ sā dhīmatī natvā sāṃjalistaṃ jinātmajam |
ānaṃdaṃ suprasannāsyā dṛṣṭvaivaṃ prārthayanmudā || 37.298{48} ||
bhadaṃto'haṃ svadaivena pāpinī duḥkhabhāginī |
tadbhavaccharaṇaṃ gatvā samiche vasudhāvratam || 37.299{49} ||
tadbhavān kṛpayā śāstargṛhe me samupāgataḥ |
yathāvidhi samādiśya vrataṃ me dātumarhati || 37.300{50} ||
tayaivaṃ prārthitaṃ śrutvā sa ānaṃdo jinātmajaḥ |
tatra utthāya dhīmatyāstasyā gṛhe upācarat || 37.301{51} ||
tatra sā dhīmatī sādhvī śāntamatyā samanvitā |
sahasā svagṛhe gatvā prastārayattadāsanam || 37.302{52} ||
tataḥ sā dhīmatī tasya śāstuḥ śuddhāmbunā mudā |
pādaprakṣālanaṃ kṛtvā praṇatvaiva ca prārthayat || 37.303{53} ||
bhadaṃtārhanmahābhijña mamānugrahakāraṇam |
atrāsane samāśritya dharmamādeṣṭumarhati || 37.304{54} ||
tayeti prārthitaṃ śrutvā sa ānaṃdaḥ prasannadhīḥ |
taddattāsana āśritya tasthau dhyātvā prabhāsayan || 37.305{55} ||
taṃ dṛṣṭvā brāhmaṇaḥ so'pi viṣṇudāsaḥ prasāditaḥ |
sahasā sāṃjalirnatvā tatraikāṃta upāśrayan || 37.306{56} ||
tataḥ sā dhīmatī sādhvī sāṃtamatīsamanvitā |
tamānaṃdaṃ samabhyarcya bhojanenābhyatoṣayat || 37.307{57} ||
tataḥ sā praṇatiṃ kṛtvā nīcāsanasamāśritā |
tamānaṃdaṃ mahābhijñaṃ sāṃjalirevamabravīt || 37.308{58} ||
bhadanta bhavatāmājñāṃ dhṛtvāhaṃ śirasā mudā |
śrīdevyā vasudhārāyāścariṣyāmi vrataṃ dhruvam || 37.309{59} ||
tadbhadanta bhavāñchāstā tadvidhiṃ me yathākramam |
kṛpānugrahamādhāya samupādeṣṭumarhati || 37.310{60} ||
iti saṃprārthite tena viṣṇudāsena saddhiyā |
śrutvānaṃdaḥ sa saṃpaśyanstaṃ dvijamevamabravīt || 37.311{61} ||
śṛṇu vipra munīndreṇa yāthādiṣṭaṃ mayā śrutam |
tathāhaṃ te pravakṣyāmi śrīvratasya vidhiṃ kramāt || 37.312{62} ||
bhādrapade'śite pakṣe tṛtīyāyāṃ tithau tathā |
māghe vāpi śite pakṣe śuklapakṣe'pi vā sadā || 37.313{63} ||
tīrthe devālaye śuddhabhūpradeśe gṛheṣu vā |
samyagbhūśodhanaṃ kuryādgomayena mṛdāmbunā || 37.314{64} ||
prātarutthāya tīrtheṣu snātvā śucisamācaraḥ |
tridhācamyāmṛtaṃ kāyaṃ paṃcagavyena śodhayet || 37.315{65} ||
śuddhaśīlasamādhānaḥ pariśuddhāmvarāvṛtaḥ |
(rm 425)
saṃbodhicittamādhāya sadgurorupadeśa dhṛk || 37.316{66} ||
subhūmau maṇḍalaṃ samyagvarttayitvā yathāvidhi |
dhvajachatrapatākābhi vitānaiścābhimaṃḍayet || 37.317{67} ||
maṃḍalasya vahistasya jvālāvalyā vahiḥ punaḥ |
paṃcavalyāvalībhiśca sa lājākṣatasaṃyutaiḥ || 37.318{68} ||
veṣṭayitvā tato vāhye kramān saṃsthāpayedghaṭān |
tīrthāmbupūritān pītavastrāvṛtān saciṃhnitān || 37.319{69} ||
tātra śrīvasudhāṃ devīṃ sagaṇāṃ vidhinā kramāt |
samāvāhya samabhyarcya pratiṣṭhāpyādhivāsayet || 37.320{70} ||
tṛtīyāyāṃ tathā prātaḥ snātvā śuddhāmvarāvṛtaḥ |
madhyāhne svāsanāsīna uttarādigmukhasthitaḥ || 37.321{71} ||
ādau guruṃ samabhyarcya ratnatrayaṃ samarcayet |
tato dhyātvā mahādevīṃ vasudhārāṃ śubhakarīm || 37.322{72} ||
sagaṇāṃ tāṃ samāvāhya datvā pādārghamādarāt |
pītamayopahāreṇa samabhyarcya yathāvidhi || 37.323{73} ||
sarvopakaraṇānyagre nivesya samaḍhokayat |
tatastaddhāraṇīṃ samyakpaṭhenmaṃtrāṇi ca kramāt || 37.324{74} ||
tataḥ kṛtāṃjaliḥ stutvā kuryāśca pāpadeśanām |
puṇyānumodanāṃ cāpi kuryācca praṇatiṃ smaran || 37.325{75} ||
kuryātpradakṣiṇānyevamaṣṭāṅgaiḥ praṇametpunaḥ |
bodhicittaṃ samādhāya saṃprārthayedabhīpsitam || 37.326{76} ||
tato valiṃ pradatvā ca sa bhūtān paritoṣayet |
tataḥ kṣamārthanāṃ kṛtvā vratasūtraṃ ca prārthayet || 37.327{77} ||
ṣoḍaśabheditaṃ sūtraṃ ṣoḍaśagraṃthisaṃyutam |
vratasūtraṃ samādāya rakṣārthaṃ vaṃdhayetkare || 37.328{78} ||
tato guruṃ samārādhya deyāddānaṃ samichitam |
sadakṣiṇaṃ susatkāraiḥ satkṛtya samatoṣayet || 37.329{79} ||
tatastṛtīyā yāme'hneḥ prāśnuyādyavapūrakān |
paṃcāmṛtaphalaskaṃdamūlapatrādikānapi || 37.330{80} ||
pālanānte punaḥ snātvā tāṃ śrīdevīmanusmaran |
taddhāraṇīṃ paṭhan rātrau pūjayecca samāhitaḥ || 37.331{81} ||
evaṃ nityaṃ catuḥ saṃdhyaṃ pūjayecchrīvasuṃdharām |
tatparedyustathābhyarcya tanmaṃḍalaṃ visarjayet || 37.332{82} ||
tadrajāṃsi samādāya saṃnidhāya śubhe ghaṭe |
bhaṃḍāgāre pratisthāpya sadārān bhajennamet || 37.333{83} ||
śeṣarajaḥ sa nirmālyaṃ nadyāṃ nāgān yathāvidhi |
samabhyarcya samarppitvā sarvaṃ jale pravāhayet || 37.334{84} ||
tatastajjalamādāya gṛhe gatvā samantataḥ |
prokṣya tanmaṃḍalasthāne kumārīṃ vidhinārcayet || 37.335{85} ||
tatastan sagaṇaṃ cakraṃ samabhyarcya yathāvidhi |
bhojanaiḥ saṃparitoṣya prārthayedyatsamīchitam || 37.336{86} ||
(rm 426)
tathā tatsagaṇaṃ cakraṃ kṛtvā kṣamārthanāṃ tataḥ |
sāṃjaliśca kramānnatvā visarjayetprasādayan || 37.337{87} ||
tadā śrīvasudhā devī svayamāgatya tadgṛhe |
samāśritya sadā sarvadravyaiḥ saṃpūrayeddhruvam || 37.338{88} ||
tatastatra gṛhe nityaṃ maṃgalaṃ nirupadravam |
dānapuṇyamahotsāhapravarttitaṃ bhavetsadā || 37.339{89} ||
evaṃ tasyā mahādevyā vasulakṣmyā vratodbhavam |
puṇyaṃ mahadasaṃkhyeyamaprameyaṃ vidurjināḥ || 37.340{90} ||
evaṃ mahattaraṃ puṇyaṃ vijñāya dvija tattathā |
śraddhaye tadvrataṃ kuryā yadi saṃpattimichasi || 37.341{91} ||
yaśca tāṃ śrīmahādevīṃ vasudhārāmanusmaran |
yatkāryyamārabhettasya tatsarvaṃ hi samṛddhyati || 37.342{92} ||
ratnārthī labhate ratnaṃ dhanārthī labhate dhanam |
bhogyārthī labhate bhogyaṃ yaso'rthī labhate yaśaḥ || 37.343{93} ||
dravyārthī labhate dravyān guṇārthī labhate guṇān |
vidyārthī labhate vidyāṃ śubhārthī labhate śubham || 37.344{94} ||
mānyārthī labhate mānyaṃ jayārthī labhate jayam |
dharmārthī labhate dharmān sukhārthī labhate sukham || 37.345{95} ||
rājyārthī labhate rājyaṃ gṛhārthī labhate gṛham |
kṣetrārthī labhate kṣetrān rasārthī labhate rasān || 37.346{96} ||
bhāryārthī labhate bhāryāṃ sutārthī labhate sutām |
putrārthī labhate putrān pautrārthī pautramāpnuyāt || 37.347{97} ||
jñānārthī labhate jñānaṃ mokṣārthī mokṣamāpnuyāt |
evamanyāni vastūni sarvopakaraṇānyapi || 37.348{98} ||
sarvāṇi dravyajātāni vastrādi bhūṣaṇānyapi |
samāpnuyātsamṛddhāni vasulakṣmyāḥ prasādataḥ || 37.349{99} ||
tato dānāni dadyātsa śuddhaśīlaḥ samācaret |
kṣāntiṃ ca bhāvayennityaṃ dharmavīryyaṃ samārabhet || 37.350{100} ||
tathā dhyānaṃ samādhāya prajñābdhiṃ saṃtareddrutam |
sarvopāyavidhānajño bodhipraṇidhimānasaḥ || 37.351{1} ||
sadbuddhibalavān dhīraḥ sa bodhijñānamāpnuyāt |
sarvamāragaṇāñjitvā niḥkleśo'rhañjitendriyaḥ |
saṃbuddhapadamāsādya nirvṛtapadamāpnuyāt || 37.352{2} ||
evaṃ brāhmaṇa vijñāya saddharmmaṃ yadi vāṃchasi |
tāṃ śrīdevīṃ mahālakṣmīṃ vasudhārāṃ sadā bhaja || 37.353{3} ||
tathā te sarvadā gehe maṃgalaṃ nirupadravam |
sarvatreha paratrāpi bhavennūnaṃ na saṃśayaḥ || 37.354{4} ||
tathā dānāni datvā tvaṃ svayaṃ tadvrataṃ saṃcaran |
sarvasatvahitaṃ kṛtvā yāvajjīvaṃ sukhaṃ cara || 37.355{5} ||
tataḥ kleśavimuktātmā saṃbodhipraṇidhānadhṛk |
bodhicaryāvratāraktā bodhisatvo bhaveddhruvam || 37.356{6} ||
sarve'pi sugatā bodhisatvāścāpi jinātmajāḥ |
(rm 427)
vasudhārāvrataṃ dhṛtvā saṃvibhrati jagattrayam || 37.357{7} ||
iti tenārhatādiṣṭaṃ śrutvā sa brāhmaṇo mudā |
tathā śrīvasudhārāyā vrataṃ caritumaichata || 37.358{8} ||
tataḥ sa viṣṇudāso'pi bhāryāṃ tāṃ dhīmatīṃ mudā |
suprasannamukhāmbhojaḥ samālokyaivamabravīt || 37.359{9} ||
priye dhanyāsi kalyāṇī yadahaṃ tvatprayatnataḥ |
śrīdevyā vrataṃ ratnaṃ prāpnomi sāṃprataṃ khalu || 37.360{10} ||
tadyathānena śāstrātra samādiṣṭaṃ tathā khalu |
śrīdevyāḥ śaraṇaṃ gatvā vāṃchāmi carituṃ vratam || 37.361{11} ||
tattvamapi samādhāya mayā sārddhaṃ tathādarāt |
śrīdevyāḥ śaraṇaṃ kṛtvā vrataṃ caritumarhati || 37.362{12} ||
iti bhartroditaṃ śrutvā dhīmatī sānumoditā |
bharttāraṃ taṃ samālokya punarevamabhāṣata || 37.363{13} ||
tathā satyaṃ samādhāya śraddhayā śaraṇaṃ gatau |
śrīdevyā vratamatrāvāṃ samādarāccarāvahe || 37.364{14} ||
ityuktvā sā satī bhadrā dhīmatī sahasādarāt |
vratopacārasāmagrīṃ muditā samasādhayat || 37.365{15} ||
tatastaṃ gurumānaṃdaṃ natvā sā sāṃjalirmudā |
tadvratārambhamādhātuṃ prārthayedevamādarāt || 37.366{16} ||
bhadanta śrīmahādevyā vratadina upāsthitaḥ |
tadyathāvidhimādiśya saṃcārayatāṃ vratam || 37.367{17} ||
tayeti prārthitaṃ śrutvā sa ānaṃdo jinātmajaḥ |
sarvavidhiṃ samādiśya vratāraṃbhaṃ samākramat || 37.368{18} ||
tatra bhūśodhanaṃ kṛtvā sa ānaṃdo yathāvidhi |
maṃḍalaṃ varttayitvā tatpratisthāpyādhyavāsayat || 37.369{19} ||
tathā tau daṃpatī tīrthe snātvā śuddhāmvarāvṛtau |
yathā śāstrā samādiṣṭaṃ sarvaṃ kṛtvopatasthatuḥ || 37.370{20} ||
tatastau daṃpatī tasya śāsturādeśataḥ kramāt |
śuddhāsanasamāsīnāvuttarābhimukhasthitau || 37.371{21} ||
gavyaiḥ kāmādi vastūni sarvāṇi śuddhitānyapi |
kṛtvā śāstrā yathādiṣṭaṃ tathāraṃbhaṃ pracakratuḥ || 37.372{22} ||
ādau guruṃ samabhyarcya tato ratnatrayaṃ mudā |
abhyarcya śaraṇaṃ kṛtvā praṇatvā vidhinādarāt || 37.373{23} ||
tatastanmaṇḍale devīṃ sagaṇāṃ śrīvasuṃdharām |
dhyātvā tathā samāvāhya datvā pādārghamādarāt || 37.374{24} ||
tatastāṃ sagaṇāṃ devīṃ samabhyarcya yathākramam |
sarvapūjopacāraiśca paṭhitvā dhāraṇīṃ tataḥ || 37.375{25} ||
sahṛdvījādimaṃtrāṇi yathākramaṃ jajalpatuḥ |
tato'ṣṭāṅgapraṇāmaiśca natvā kṛtvā pradakṣiṇām || 37.376{26} ||
guṇābhivādanai stotrai stutvā natau vavaṃdatuḥ || 37.377{27!} ||
tatastau sāṃjalī sthitvā prakṣitvā pāpadeśanām |
(rm 428)
puṇyānumodanāṃ cāpi saṃprārthya śrīsamṛddhyatām || 37.378{28} ||
salokapālabhūtebhyo baliṃ paṃcāmṛtānvitam |
yathāvidhi praḍhaukitvā prārthayatāṃ susaṃpadam || 37.379{29} ||
tato dānaṃ pradatvaivaṃ gurave'pi sudakṣiṇām |
tataḥ kṣamārthanāṃ kṛtvā samāptaṃ cakraturvratam || 37.380{30} ||
tato gurustayoḥ śāstā vratasūtraṃ samarppayat |
saṃpattisasamṛddhāśīrvādaṃ dadau prasāditaḥ || 37.381{31} ||
tato guruṃ puraskṛtya yathoktaṃ vratapālanam |
kṛtvā tataḥ punaḥ snātvā devīṃ dhyātvā niṣedituḥ || 37.382{32} ||
tato rātrau samādhāya sagaṇāṃ vasuṃdharām |
devīṃ dhyātvā samabhyarcya dharmmaṃ śrutvā niṣedatuḥ || 37.383{33} ||
tataḥ prātaḥ samutthāya snātvā śuddhāmvarāvṛtau |
tathā tāṃ sagaṇāṃ devīmāvāhyānarcatuḥ kramāt || 37.384{34} ||
tatastadvratasaṃpūrṇaṃ kṛtvā dānaṃ sadakṣiṇam |
pradatvā gurave natvā tanmaṇḍalaṃ vyasarjatām || 37.385{35} ||
tatastadrajamādāya bhaṃḍāgāre yathoditam |
nidhāya tatsa nirmālyaṃ śeṣaṃ nadyāṃ yathāvidhim || 37.386{36} ||
gururnāgān samabhyarcya samarpya prākṣipajjale |
tatastajjalamādāya mahotsāhairgṛhaṃ yayau || 37.387{37} ||
tata gṛhe tadambhobhirabhiṣiṃcya samantataḥ |
kumārīṃ vidhinārādhya gaṇacakraṃ samarcayat || 37.388{38} ||
tatastau daṃpatī natvā sāṃjali sādaraṃ mudā |
saṃpattiprārthanāṃ kṛtvā kṣamāpya tadvyasarjatām || 37.389{39} ||
tatastasyā gṛhe devī vasundhārā svayaṃ sthitā |
sarvadravyasamāpūrṇaṃ kṛtvā tasthau prasāditā || 37.390{40} ||
sarvadravyābhisaṃpūrṇaṃ dṛṣṭvā tau daṃpatī gṛham |
vismayānaṃdasaṃpannau mitha evaṃ samūcatuḥ || 37.391{41} ||
aho satyaṃ jagacchāsturyathādiṣṭaṃ tathā khalu |
tathāvāṃ śaraṇaṃ gatvā devyā vrataṃ carevahi || 37.392{42} ||
ityuktvā tau prasīdaṃtau śaraṇaṃ gatvā samāhitau |
tasyāḥ śrīmahādevyāḥ sadā vrataṃ praceratuḥ || 37.393{43} ||
tadārabhyaḥ sadārthibhyo datvā dānaṃ yathārthitam |
triratnabhajanaṃ kṛtvā praceratuḥ śubhe sadā || 37.394{44} ||
tato'sau yatirānaṃdastau samāmaṃtrya daṃpatī |
pakṣivatsahasākāśātkauśāṃvyāṃ samupācarat || 37.395{45} ||
tatra sa taṃ jagannāthaṃ praṇatvā sāṃjaliḥ punaḥ |
etatsarvaṃ pravṛttāṃtaṃ nivedyaikāntamāśrayat || 37.396{46} ||
tataḥ sa bhagavān dṛṣṭvā tamānaṃdaṃ sabhāṃ ca tām |
dhīmatyāḥ śāṃtamatyāśca prasāditvaivamādiśat || 37.397{47} ||
dhanyā sā dhīmatī śāntamatī cāpi subhāvinī |
yatte śrīvasudhārāyā bhavetāṃ vratadhāriṇī || 37.398{48} ||
tanme ubhe mahatpuṇyalābhinyau śrīsamanvite |
(rm 429)
sarvasatvahitādhānabodhicaryāsamudyate || 37.399{49} ||
kramādbodhiṃ samāsādya buddhapadamavāpsyata || 37.400{50!} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ munīndreṇa śrutvā te sarvasāṃghikāḥ |
tāṃ śrīvasuṃdharāṃ devīṃ smṛtvā nityaṃ prabhejire || 37.401{51} ||
tadā tasyāḥ pitā viprā dhīmatyā duhiturgṛhe |
samudbhūtā mahāsaṃpadityaśroṣījjanoditam || 37.402{52} ||
tataḥ sa brāhmaṇo bhāryāṃ dhīmatyā mātaraṃ mudā |
sahasā samupāmaṃtrya pura evamabhāṣata || 37.403{53} ||
kiṃ tvayānuśrutaṃ bhārye dhīmatyā yadgṛhe'dhunā |
mahāsaṃpatsamudbhūtā mayeti śrūyate khalu || 37.404{54} ||
iti bhartrā samākhyātaṃ śrutvā sā vismayānvitā |
kathametadbhavetsatyamiti taṃ patimabravīt || 37.405{55} ||
iti bhāryoditaṃ śrutvā hariśarmāpi sa dvijaḥ |
savismayenākhyātaṃ śrutvā caivamabhāṣata || 37.406{56} ||
priye śrutaṃ mayā satyaṃ dhīmatyā yadgṛhe'dhunā |
mahāsaṃpatsamudbhūtā vasundhārāprasādataḥ || 37.407{57} ||
yadi naitatpratīto'pi janaiḥ sā dhīmatī sutā |
svagṛhe'tra samāhūya tvamevaṃ paripṛchyatām || 37.408{58} ||
kathaṃ saṃjāyate saṃpanmahatī te gṛhe'dhunā |
etatsatyaṃ tvayā putrī puro me kathyatāmiti || 37.409{59} ||
evaṃ bhartrā samākhyātaṃ śrutva sā dhīmatīprasūḥ |
tatheti prativijñāya svasakhīmevamabravīt || 37.410{60} ||
śṛṇu sakhi sutāyā me dhīmatyā yadgṛhe'dhunā |
saṃbhūtā mahatī saṃpadvasuṃdhārā prasādataḥ || 37.411{61} ||
iti śrutaṃ mayā satyaṃ draṣṭumichāmi tatkhalu |
tattāṃ sutāṃ samāhūya samādarādihānaya || 37.412{62} ||
iti tayā samākhyātaṃ śrutvā sā supriyā sakhī |
tatheti prativijñapya dhīmatyā bhavane yayau || 37.413{63} ||
tatra sā samupāsṛtya dhīmatīṃ tāṃ subhadrikām |
dṛṣṭvā savismayānaṃdayuktā natvaivamabravīt || 37.414{64} ||
bhadre'haṃ preṣitā mātrā tvadāhvānaṃ pratīha tat |
sahasā tvaṃ samāgaṃtumarhasi svātmajānvitā || 37.415{65} ||
iti tayā samākhyātaṃ śrutvā sā dhīmatī mudā |
hasantī tāṃ sakhīṃ mātuḥ samālokyaivamabravīt || 37.416{66} ||
ayi māṃ smarate mātā kimarthaṃ sāṃprataṃ katham |
tadāhaṃ na smṛtā mātrā dāridriyaduḥkhinī yadā || 37.417{67} ||
tathāpyahaṃ mukhaṃ māturdraṣṭumichāmi sāṃpratam |
tathāhamāgamiṣyāmi yathā mātuḥ samīhitam || 37.418{68} ||
ityuktvā dhīmatī sātha putraṃ ca bhūṣaṇam |
bhūṣayitvā suvastraiśca prāvārya samamaṇḍayat || 37.419{69} ||
svayaṃ ca sā prasādhitvā duṣyapaṭṭāmvarāvṛtā |
śrīmadratnāmvalaṃkārairmaṇḍitāṃgābhiśobhitā || 37.420{70} ||
(rm 430)
tataḥ sakhyā tayā sārddhaṃ putraputrīsamanvitā |
saṃprasthitā janaiścāpi saha māturgṛhe yayau || 37.421{71} ||
tatra sā jananī dṛṣṭvā dhīmatīṃ tāṃ samāgatām |
taṃ suputraṃ suputrīṃ ca paśyantī samupācaran || 37.422{72} ||
tatra tāṃ jananīṃ dṛṣṭvā dhīmatī sātmajānvitā |
sahasā samupāgatya natvāpṛchatsukauśalam || 37.423{73} ||
jananī sāpi tāṃ putrīṃ putraputrīsamanvitām |
dhīmatīṃ sahasāliṃgya papracha kauśalaṃ mudā || 37.424{74} ||
tataḥ sā dhīmatī putrī putraputrīsamanvitā |
mātrā sahābhigachaṃtī piturgehamupāsarat || 37.425{75} ||
tatra sādhīmatīṃ putrīṃ putraputrīsahāgatām |
dṛṣṭvā sa janako'pyevaṃ paśyan pratyudyayau mudā || 37.426{76} ||
janakaṃ taṃ samālokya dhīmatī sātmajānvitā |
saṃsmitā samupāsṛtya natvāpṛchatsukauśalam || 37.427{77} ||
pitāpi sa samālokya dhīmatīṃ tāṃ śubhāṃśikām |
putraputryau ca saṃbhāṣya papracha kauśalaṃ mudā || 37.428{78} ||
pitā sa jananī sāpi dhīmatīṃ tāṃ subhāvinīm |
pautraṃ pautrīṃ ca saṃdhṛtvā svagṛhe jagmaturmudā || 37.429{79} ||
tatra sā jananī tasyāḥ pautryāḥ pautrasya cādarāt |
pādān prakṣālayitvāśu svakamānayat || 37.430{80} ||
tadāsanaṃ samāstīrya tāṃ putrīṃ svayamādarāt |
pautraṃ pautrīṃ ca tān sarvān saṃdhṛtvā saṃnyaveśayat || 37.431{81} ||
tatrāsane samāśritya pārśvayorvāmadakṣayoḥ |
putrīṃ putramupasthāpya tasthau sā dhīmatī smitā || 37.432{82} ||
tāmāgatāṃ samākarṇya sarve'pi jātayo mudā |
sahasā draṣṭumāgatya sā daraṃ samupāyayuḥ || 37.433{83} ||
tathā te vāṃdhavāḥ sarve suhṛtminrajanā api |
tāṃ tathā svagṛhāyātāṃ kuśalaṃ praṣṭumāyayuḥ || 37.434{84} ||
tathā tāśca bhaginyo'pi tāsāṃ sarve'pi cātmajāḥ |
tāṃ dhīmatīṃ tathāyātāṃ śrutvā draṣṭu samā yayuḥ || 37.435{85} ||
evamanye'pi lokāśca dhīmatīṃ tāṃ subhūṣaṇām |
svamāturgṛhamāyātāṃ śrutvā draṣṭumupāyayuḥ || 37.436{86} ||
tatretaṃ dhīmatīṃ kāṃtāṃ suvastrabhūṣaṇāvṛtām |
te sajñātyādayaḥ sarve dṛṣṭvā vismayamāyayuḥ || 37.437{87} ||
tato'nujā bhaginyādi jñātivaṃdhusuhṛjjanāḥ |
sarve tāṃ sādaraṃ natvā paprachuḥ kuśalaṃ mudā || 37.438{88} ||
tathā sā dhīmatī cāpi jñātivaṃdhusuhṛjjanān |
vandanīyānstānnatvāpṛchatsukauśalam || 37.439{89} ||
tataḥ sā jananī tasyai dhīmatyai sādarānmudā |
varṇagaṃdharasopetaṃ bhojanaṃ samupāharat || 37.440{90} ||
tathā sautāya sautāyai bhojanaṃ surasānvitam |
sādaramupaḍhokitvā pura evamabhāṣata || 37.441{91} ||
(rm 431)
gṛhṇāṇa surasaṃ bhogyaṃ bhukṣva hṛddhīmati priye |
yo sauta saute ca saudārdda procyutaṃ yadabhīpsitam || 37.442{92} ||
janako vitathya sarvo bhaginyo bhrātaro'pi ca |
bhātṛjāyāṃ sukhāścāpi tasyāścakruḥ samādaram || 37.443{93} ||
tathādarāṃ kṛtaṃ sarve śrutvā sā dhīmatī tadā |
pūrvavṛttimanusmṛtvā manasaivaṃ vyaciṃtayat || 37.444{94} ||
naivāhaṃ mānitā hyebhirnāyaṃ putro'pi mānitaḥ |
na ca yaṃ mānitā putri dhanamevātra manyate || 37.445{95} ||
yadā na me gṛhe saṃpaddaridro'haṃ ca duḥkhinī |
tademe sarva ete'pi nāpi'paśyanmukhaṃ mama || 37.446{96} ||
idānīṃ me gṛhe saṃpajjātā tatsukhānvitām |
evaṃ mate'pi sarve māṃ mānayaṃti samādarāt || 37.447{97} ||
yadahaṃ mānitā naiva dhanamevābhimānyate |
tadyasya taiḥ kriyate mānyaṃ sa bhuṃjyādidamādaram || 37.448{98} ||
iti sā manasā dhyātvā sarvāṇi bhūṣaṇānyapi |
svāni putrasya putryāśca saṃnirīkṣya samādade || 37.449{99} ||
tataḥ sā dhīmatī kāṃśasthālyāṃ sarvāṇi tānyapi |
bhūṣaṇāni prasāryaivaṃ sādaraṃ pratyabhāṣata || 37.450{100} ||
bho bho bhavanti sarvāṇi bhūṣaṇāni priyāṇi me |
idaṃ saurasikaṃ bhojyaṃ prāśnuta svechayā sukham || 37.451{1} ||
saṃsāre mānanīyaṃ hi dhanameva sadādarāt |
dhanānurūpeṇa yalloke mānyate niṃdyate naraḥ || 37.452{2} ||
dhanavān puruṣo loke nīco'pi mānyate'guṇaḥ |
nirdhanā guṇavāṃścāpi sujāto'pi na mānyate || 37.453{3} ||
dhanānurūpeṇa sarvatra vicāramānyabhojanam |
dhanaprasaṃgato mūrkho nīco'pi dhanyato bhavet || 37.454{4} ||
yāvatsaṃpadgṛhe tāvalloke samādrito naraḥ |
yadā saṃpadvihīne tu tadā sa bhūtavaccaret || 37.455{5} ||
iti sābhilapantyeva sarvāṇi bhūṣaṇānyapi |
tāni punaḥ samādāya yathā sthānaṃ nyayojayat || 37.456{6} ||
tatastadbhojanaṃ kiṃcidbhaktvā sā svātmajānvitā |
natvaiva pitarau bhartturgṛhe yayau prasāditā || 37.457{7} ||
tatra sā dhīmatī bhartturviṣṇudāsasya pādayoḥ |
natvā sarvapravṛttāṃtaṃ nyavedayatpuraḥ sthitā || 37.458{8} ||
etadbhāryāsamākhyātaṃ śrutvā sa brāhmaṇaḥ patiḥ |
muditastāṃ sukalyāṇīṃ bhāryaṃ dṛṣṭvaivamabravīt || 37.459{9} ||
dhanyo'si tvaṃ sukalyāṇī yatte samupādeśataḥ |
śrīdevīvratamādhāya samāpnomi susaṃpadam || 37.460{10} ||
(rm 432)
priye tvāmatra tadratnasaṃbhavakulasaṃbhavām |
śrīvasudhāmahādevīsamāṃ manye'hamātmanā || 37.461{11} ||
ityuktvā brāhmaṇo bharttā viṣṇudāsaḥ kṛtāṃjaliḥ |
dhīmatīṃ taṃ sukalyāṇīṃ natvaivaṃ punarabravīt || 37.462{12} ||
priye tava prasādena sāphalyaṃ janma me'dhunā |
yacchrīvasuṃdharādevyā upāsako vratī bhave || 37.463{13} ||
tanmayā prakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ tatsarvaṃ kṣīyate'dhunā |
saṃpattiḥ saṃprajātā ca naiva kṣīṇā bhave dhi naḥ || 37.464{14} ||
tathā vayaṃ sadāpyasyāḥ śrīdevyāḥ śaraṇaṃ gatāḥ |
yathāvidhi vrataṃ dhṛtvā caremahi śubhe'dhunā || 37.465{15} ||
triratnabhajanaṃ kṛtvā datvārthibhyo yathepsitam |
yāvajjīvaṃ śubhaṃ kṛtvā bhuktvā saukhyaṃ caremahi || 37.466{16} ||
etatpuṇyavipākena sarvadā sadgatīṃ gatāḥ |
kramātsaṃbodhimāsādya buddhapadaṃ labhemahi || 37.467{17} ||
iti bhartroditaṃ śrutvā dhīmatī sānumoditā |
tathā sadā vasuṃdhārāvrataṃ dhṛtvā śubhe'carat || 37.468{18} ||
tataḥ sā dhīmatī tasyāḥ śāṃtamateḥ purogatā |
sarvametatpravṛttāṃtaṃ nivedyaivamabhāṣata || 37.469{19} ||
priye sakhi mahābhāge bhavatkṛpāprasādataḥ |
pāpaṃ me kṣīyate sarvaṃ tajjanma saphalaṃ bhave || 37.470{20} ||
saṃpattiḥ saṃprajātā me naiva kṣīṇā bhavedapi |
tadyāvajjīvitaṃ devyāḥ śaraṇastho bhajāni hi || 37.471{21} ||
tvameva śāstā na cānyā hi saddharmopadeśinī |
tanme puṇyānubhāvena saṃbuddhapadamāpnuhi || 37.472{22} ||
ityudite tamāśrutvā śāṃtamatiśca sā mudā |
dhīmatīṃ tāṃ samālokya punarevamabhāṣāta || 37.473{23} ||
bhadre dhīmati saṃbuddhaśaraṇasthā sadādarāt |
śrīdevyāvratamādhāya śubhe cara samāhitā || 37.474{24} ||
etatpuṇyavipākena sarvadā sadgatīṃ gatā |
kramātsaṃbodhimāsādya saṃbuddhapadamāpnuyāḥ || 37.475{25} ||
iti matvātra saṃsāre śubhaṃ dharmārthasādhanam |
dharmo lokahitārthāya tallokārthe dharmamācareḥ || 37.476{26} ||
iti tayoditaṃ śrutvā dhīmatī sā prabodhitā |
tatheti pratinaṃditvā muditā svagṛhe yayau || 37.477{27} ||
tataḥ prabhṛti sā sādhvī dhīmatī sarvadā gṛhe |
śrīdevyā bhajanaṃ kṛtvā datvā dānaṃ śubhe'carat || 37.478{28} ||
tathā tasyā gṛhe saṃpaddine dine pravarddhitā |
sarvasatvopabhogyārthaṃ babhūva dharmasādhinī || 37.479{29} ||
tathā sā dhīmatī dṛṣṭvā sarvasaṃpatpravarddhitām |
mudā lokahitaṃ kṛtvā pracacāra śubhe sadā || 37.480{30} ||
iti me guruṇādiṣṭaṃ śrutaṃ mayā tathocyate |
tathā tvamapi rājendra vasudhārāvrataṃ cara || 37.481{31} ||
prajāścāpi mahārāja bodhayitvā prayatnataḥ |
(rm 433)
śrīdevyāḥ śaraṇe sthāpya vrataṃ cāraya sarvadā || 37.482{32} ||
tathā te sarvadā kṣemaṃ sarvatrāpi bhaveddhruvam |
kramādbodhiṃ samāsādya saṃbuddhapadamāpnuyāḥ || 37.483{33} ||
iti tenārhatādiṣṭaṃ śrutvāśokaḥ sa bhūpatiḥ |
tathetyabhyanumoditvā prābhyanandatsapārṣadaḥ || 37.484{34} ||
dhīmatyā ye'vadānaṃ śubhaphaladamidaṃ suprasannāḥ śṛṇvanti |
śrāvayanti pramuditamanasā bodhicaryānurāgāḥ |
sarve te bodhisatvāḥ sakalaguṇadharāḥ sarvasaṃpatsamṛddhāḥ |
śrīmanto lokaśreṣṭhā jinanilayagatāḥ saṃvasaṃti sukhāḍhyāḥ || 37.485{35} ||

++ iti śrīratnāvadānatatve dhīmatyāvadānaṃ samāptam ++


(rm 434)
xxxviii vasundharāvadāna
athāśoko maharājaḥ kṛtāñjalipuṭo mudā |
upaguptaṃ yatiṃ natvā punarevamabhāṣata || 38.1{1} ||
bhadanta śrotumichāmi vasudhārāvrataṃ kadā |
kena kuha kathaṃ martyaloke pracāritaṃ purā || 38.2{2} ||
tadatra no bhavāñcchāstaryathā pravarttitaṃ purā |
tathā sarvaṃ samākhyātuṃ vistareṇa samarhati || 38.3{3} ||
iti saṃprārthitaṃ rājñā śrutvā so'rhanmahāmatiḥ |
aśokaṃ taṃ mahīpālaṃ samālokyaivamabravīt || 38.4{4} ||
śṛṇu rājan yathākhyātaṃ guruṇā me srutaṃ mayā |
tathāhaṃ te pravakṣyāmi vasudhārāvratodbhavam || 38.5{5} ||
tadyathā bhagavān buddhaḥ śākyamunistathāgataḥ |
sarvajño'rhaṃ jagacchāstā dharmarājo vināyakaḥ || 38.6{6} ||
sa ekasamaye tatra kauśānvyā upakaṇṭhike |ms: ekaḥ samaye
vihāre ghoṣirārāme vijahāra sasāṃghikaḥ || 38.7{7} ||
tadā śakrādayo devā brahmādi brāhmaṇā api |
daityendrā lokapālāśca saddharmaśravaṇotsukāḥ || 38.8{8} ||
rākṣasā garuḍā nāgā yakṣagaṃdharvakinnarāḥ |
siddhā vidyādharāścāpi tathā cāpyapsarogaṇāḥ || 38.9{9} ||
yogino yatayastīrthā ṛṣayo brahmacāriṇaḥ |
rājāno rājaputrāśca vaiśyāśca maṃtriṇo janāḥ || 38.10{10} ||
amātyāḥ sainikāścāpi gṛhasthāśca mahājanāḥ |
vaṇijaḥ sārthavāhāśca śilpinaḥ paurikā api || 38.11{11} ||
grāmyā jānapadāścāpi tathānyadeśavāsinaḥ |
evamanye'pi lokāśca saddharmāmṛtavāṃchinaḥ || 38.12{12} ||
sarve te tatra kauśāṃvyā ghoṣilārāma āgatāḥ |
sabhāsanasamāsīnaṃ prādrākṣustaṃ munīśvaram || 38.13{13} ||
dṛṣṭvā te muditāḥ sarve praṇatvā samupāsṛtāḥ |
yathākramaṃ samabhyarcya kṛtvā tritvaḥ pradakṣiṇam || 38.14{14} ||
tatsaddharmāmṛtaṃ pātumupatasthuḥ samāhitāḥ || 38.15{15} ||
tatra te tatsabhāsīnā bhikṣavaḥ śrāvakā api |
bhikṣuṇyaścailakāścaivamupāsakā upāsikāḥ || 38.16{16} ||
bodhisatvā mahāsatvāḥ saṃbodhivratacāriṇaḥ |
vasuṃdharāvratotpattiṃ śrotuṃ sarve samīchire || 38.17{17} ||
tatrānaṃdaḥ parijñāya teṣāṃ manaḥsamīhitam |
sāṃjaliḥ purataḥ sthitvā natvaivaṃ prārthayanmunim || 38.18{18} ||
bhagavan sarvavicchāstānime sarve sabhājanāḥ |
vasulakṣmīvratotpattiṃ śrotumichanti āsṛtāḥ || 38.19{19} ||
(rm 435)
tadbhavāṃstrijagacchāstā sarvasatvābhibodhane |
vasulakṣmīvratotpattiṃ samupādeṣṭumarhati || 38.20{20} ||
ityānaṃdena bhadreṇa prārthite sa munīśvaraḥ |
tān sabhāsaṃsthitān sarvān samālokyaivamādiśat || 38.21{21} ||
śṛṇvānanda pravakṣyāmi munīndreṇa yathoditam |
vajradharābdhigambhīranirghoṣeṇa śrutaṃ mayā || 38.22{22} ||
tadyathābhūtpurā rājā sūryodayābhidho nṛpaḥ |
śrīpure nagare ramye cakravarttī nṛpādhipaḥ || 38.23{23} ||
daśākuśalasaṃraktaḥ pramadājanavaśyagaḥ |
tadā tatra narāḥ sarve'pyasanmitropadeśataḥ |
daśākusalamūleṣu karmasu saṃpracerire || 38.24{24} ||
tadā tatpāpadoṣeṇa mahetayaḥ pravarttitāḥ |
tadītisamupākrāntā saṃkṣobhitābhavanmahī || 38.25{25} ||
tataḥ sarvatra deśeṣu durbhikṣamabhavadbhuvi |
tadā te manujāḥ sarve daridrā abhavan kramāt || 38.26{26} ||
tataste nirdhanāḥ sarve kṣutpipāsāhatāturāḥ |
lobhitā nirdayāḥ krūrāḥ praduṣṭā duritāratāḥ |
svakuladharmamaryādaṃ vilaṃghyāpyaśubhe'caran || 38.27{27} ||
tataḥ sarve'pi te lokā andhā iva kumārgagāḥ |
asatsaṃgānusaṃraktāḥ pracerurnarakāmukhāḥ || 38.28{28} ||
tadā tatpāpasaṃghṛṣṭaduḥkhāgniparitāpitā |
śoṣitevābhavadbhūmī nirguṇā nīrasā kharā || 38.29{29} ||
tadā sarve'pi satvāśca kṣuttṛṣṇāgnyahidāhitāḥ |
nirāśā mṛtyumichanto niṣeduḥ parimohitāḥ || 38.30{30} ||
tadaikasmin dine tatra tuṣitabhuvane sthitāḥ |
śrīdevīpramukhā devyo vasundharyyāḥ sahāyikāḥ || 38.31{31} ||
sarvāstā militā martyaloke gaṃtuṃ samipsavaḥ |
kimidānīṃ manuṣyāṇāmavastheti samīkṣire || 38.32{32} ||
tatastānmanujān sarvān daridrān pāpacāriṇaḥ |
dṛṣṭvā tāḥ sakalā devyā dayārttā evamabruvan || 38.33{33} ||
aho duḥkhaṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ yatpāpe niratā narāḥ |
daridrā nirdayāḥ krūrā bhavanti narakāmukhāḥ || 38.34{34} ||
tattatra martyaloke'pi daridryaduḥkhaśāntaye |
yatnācchrīvasudhārāyā vrataṃ cārayemahi || 38.35{35} ||
tataste manujāḥ sarve śrutvā dṛṣṭvānumoditāḥ |
śrīdevyāḥ śaraṇaṃ gatvā careyurvratamādarāt || 38.36{36} ||
tadā tatpuṇyasaṃpūtāḥ sarve te mānavā api |
śrīmantaḥ sukhino bhadre careyuḥ sarvadādarāt || 38.37{37} ||
tataste manujāḥ sarve daśakuśalasaṃratāḥ |
parasparahitaṃ kṛtvā careyurbodhisaṃvaram || 38.38{38} ||
tataste bhadritātmānaḥ sarvasatvahitodyatāḥ |
bodhisatvā mahāsatvā bhaveyurbodhicāriṇaḥ || 38.39{39} ||
tataste sadguṇādhānāstriratnabhajanāratāḥ |
kramātsaṃbodhimāsādya saṃbuddhapadamāpnuyuḥ || 38.40{40} ||
(rm 436)
iti hetorvayaṃ sarvāḥ śrīdevyāḥ purato gatāḥ |
etatsarvapravṛttāṃtaṃ vijñāpayema sāṃpratam || 38.41{41} ||
ityevaṃ saṃmataṃ kṛtvā sarvā devvyo'pi tāstataḥ |
śrīvasudhāmahādevyā ālaye samupācaran || 38.42{42} ||
tatra tāḥ sakalā devyaḥ sāṃjalaya upāsṛtāḥ |
tāṃ śrīvasuṃdharāṃ devīṃ praṇatvaivaṃ nyavedayan || 38.43{43} ||
bhagavati mahādevi vijānīyādbhavaṃtyapi |
yanmanuṣyā daridrāste bhavanti pāpacāriṇaḥ || 38.44{44} ||
tadbhavatī prayatnena manujānstān suduḥkhitān |
kṛpayā sahasālokya sarvānnuddharttumarhati || 38.45{45} ||
iti saṃprārthite tābhirdevībhiḥ sā vasundharā |
tā devī sakalā dṛṣṭvā prasannāsyaivamabravīt || 38.46{46} ||
aho duḥkhaṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ yatra śāstā na sadguruḥ |
tena te'kuśalācārāḥ sarve narā daridratāḥ || 38.47{47} ||
pāpābhissaṃratā duṣṭā kṣuttṛṣṇāgnyabhidāhitāḥ |
durbhagā nirdayāḥ krūrāḥ pateyurnarake dhruvam || 38.48{48} ||
iti hetorahaṃ tesāṃ martyānāṃ duḥkhaśāṃtaye |
saṃvare tānnupasthāpya bodhau niyojayemahi || 38.49{49} ||
iti niścitya sā devī vasulakṣmī kṛpārditā |
tatra samādhimādhāya trimurttiṃ sā samādadhau || 38.50{50} ||
tadyathaikā vasuṃdharyyā mahālakṣmī dvitīyakā |
tṛtīyikā kumāryyāśca trimūrttiriti sā dadhe || 38.51{51} ||
tataḥ sā śrīmahādevī gaṃtuṃ martyālaye svayam |
naṃdīmukhāśvaghoṣau dvau samāmaṃtryaivamādiśat || 38.52{52} ||
naṃdīmukhāśvaghoṣau yanmayā saṃprārthitaṃ yathā |
tathā tanme mahatkāryaṃ harttumarhatha sarvathā || 38.53{53} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ tayā devyā niśamya tau kṛtāṃjaliḥ |
nandimukhāśvaghoṣau tāṃ devīṃ natvaivamūcatuḥ || 38.54{54} ||
devi kiṃ te mahatkāryaṃ tadā jñāpayatu dhruvam |
yathādiṣṭaṃ bhavatyāśu kariṣyāvastathā khalu || 38.55{55} ||
iti tābhyāṃ samākhyātaṃ śrutvā sā śrīvasuṃdharā |
mahādevī prasannāsyā tau dṛṣṭvaivamabhāṣata || 38.56{56} ||
śṛṇuta bhadramukhau yanme kāryaṃ vāṃ tanmayocyate |
tathā yuvāṃ samādhāya karttumarhatha sāṃpratam || 38.57{57} ||
yannarā bhūtale sarve daridrā atiduḥkhitā |
pāpābhiniratā duṣṭā bhavanti narakāmukhāḥ || 38.58{58} ||
tattān sarvān samuddharttumichāmi sarvathā'dhunā |
tridhā mūrttimahaṃ dhṛtvā gacheyaṃ tanmahītale || 38.59{59} ||
tayo rājā mahīpālaḥ sarvalokādhipaḥ prabhuḥ |
tamevaṃ prathamaṃ dharme vinīya yojayemahi || 38.60{60} ||
yatra rājā svayaṃ dharme sthitvā caretsamāhitaḥ |
tatra sarve'nugā rājño dharme careyurādarāt || 38.61{61} ||
yatrābhinirato rājā carate'pyaśubhe śubhe |
(rm 437)
tatra sarve'pi lokāste pracareyurhi sādaram || 38.62{62} ||
ityādau nṛpati dharme yojanīyaḥ prayatnataḥ |
tataḥ sa nṛpatiḥ sārvāllokān dharme niyojayet || 38.63{63} ||
tataḥ sarve'pi lokāste daśākuśalavaimukhāḥ |
saddharme hi ratā bhadre pracareyuḥ samāhitāḥ || 38.64{64} ||
tataḥ sarve'pi te lokāḥ parasparahitonmukhāḥ |
triratnabhajanaṃ kṛtvā careyuḥ saṃvare sadā || 38.65{65} ||
tataste sarvadā śreyaḥ kṛtvā yāyuḥ sukhāvatīm |
tatrāpi saṃvaraṃ dhṛtvā pracareyuḥ sukhānvitāḥ || 38.66{66} ||
tataḥ kramātsamādhāya sarvapāramitāsvapi |
carantaḥ sarvasatvānāṃ hitaṃ kuryuḥ sadādarāt || 38.67{67} ||
tataste bodhisatvāḥ syuḥ saddharmaguṇaratninaḥ |
śuddhātmāno mahāsatvā bhaviṣyanti jinātmajāḥ || 38.68{68} ||
tataḥ sarve'pi te dhīrāḥ pariśuddha trimaṇḍalāḥ |
triyānabodhimāsādya saṃbuddhapadamāpnuyuḥ || 38.69{69} ||
iti hetorahaṃ teṣāṃ satvānāṃ vinayārthataḥ |
trimūrttirūpamādhāya gachāmi bhūtale'dhunā || 38.70{70} ||
tadyuvāṃ me vaco dhṛtvā gantumarhatha bhūtale |
tatra gatvā prayatnena sarvathāhara taṃ nṛpam || 38.71{71} ||
tato'haṃ taṃ vaśīkṛtya bodhayitvā prayatnataḥ |
saddharmacāriṇaṃ kṛtvā cārayiṣyāmi me vratam || 38.72{72} ||
iti tayā mahādevyā samādiṣṭaṃ niśamya tau |
nandimukhāśvaghoṣau tāṃ devīṃ natvaivamūcatuḥ || 38.73{73} ||
bhadre devi tavādeśaṃ dhṛtvāvāṃ śirasā tathā |
gatvā nṛpaṃ samāhṛtya prāṇayiṣyāvahe puraḥ || 38.74{74} ||
ityuktvā tau prasannāsyau kṛtāṃjalipuṭo mudā |
pādau devyāḥ praṇatvaivaṃ prajagmatustato bhuvi || 38.75{75} ||
trimūrttirūpamādhāya vasulakṣmīrapi svayam |
śrīdevyādisakhīyuktā gaṃgātīramupāgatāḥ || 38.76{76} ||
tatra tau bhūtale gatvā prāpaśyataṃ samaṃtataḥ |
ramaṇīyaṃ mahodyāne puṣkariṇīpraśobhitam || 38.77{77} ||
dṛṣṭvā tāvubhau tatra kṣaṇaṃ viśramya saṃsthitau |
tato mithasamāsīnau dṛṣṭvaivaṃ samabhāṣatām || 38.78{78} ||
aho ramyaṃ mahīlokaṃ taḍāgodyānamaṇḍitam |
tatkathamiha rājānamapaharttuṃ carevahi || 38.79{79} ||
tatra nandīmukhaḥ prāha divyastrīsuṃdaraṃ vapuḥ |
dhṛtvāvāṃ surasaṃgītaṃ gāyantau prācarevahi || 38.80{80} ||
nūnaṃ sa nṛpatī rājā dṛṣṭvā nau divyasuṃdaram |
vapuḥ kāmāgnisaṃtaptaḥ samāharttumupācaret || 38.81{81} ||
tadāvāṃ sahasāhṛtya nṛpatiṃ taṃ prayatnataḥ |
mohayitvā vaśīkṛtya devyā upānayevahi || 38.82{82} ||
iti saṃbhāṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā vidhāya ca susaṃmatam |
nandimukhāśvaghoṣau tau strīrūpaṃ dadhatustathā || 38.83{83} ||
tataste divyarāmāṃśe manābhirāmaniḥsvanaiḥ |
(rm 438)
gāyaṃtyau surasaṃgītaṃ durgāsthane niṣedatuḥ || 38.84{84} ||
tayostatsurasaṃgītaṃ śrutvā tatpuravāsinaḥ |
janāḥ kautūhalākrāntacittā draṣṭumupāsaran || 38.85{85} ||
dṛṣṭvā te divyarūpāṃśe sarve te vismayānvitāḥ |
devakanye ime nūnamiti proktvā samīkṣire || 38.86{86} ||
divyāṃśe te samālokya sarve te vismayoddhatāḥ |
aho citramiti proktvā pratyāyayuścirātpuram || 38.87{87} ||
tatra te surakanyāṃśe naikasthāne niṣedatuḥ |
pratidinaṃ caturddikṣu pragāyaṃtyau praceratuḥ || 38.88{88} ||
tathā te caritaṃ dṛṣṭvā sarve te paurikā api |
atyadbhutasamākrāṃtacittā ceruḥ praśaṃkitāḥ || 38.89{89} ||
tataste paurikā lokāḥ sādbhutaśaṃkitāśayāḥ |
nṛpateḥ purato gatvā praṇatvaivaṃ nyavedayet || 38.90{90} ||
jaya deva mahārāja vijānīyā bhavānnapi |
tathāpyatra mahaccitraṃ vijñaptuṃ vayamāgatāḥ || 38.91{91} ||
tadatra surarāmāṃśe ubhe striyau sulakṣaṇau |
gāyantyau surasaṃgītaṃ durgāsthāne samāśrite || 38.92{92} ||
pratidinaṃ caturdikṣu tathā te devakanyake |
gāyaṃtyo surasaṃgītaṃ bhramitvā carato mudā || 38.93{93} ||
tannimittaṃ kathaṃ rājaṃ cchubhaṃ vā syādathāśubham |
iti praṣṭuṃ samāyāmo vayaṃ sarve'pi nānyathā || 38.94{94} ||
iti taiḥ paurikaiḥ sarvairniveditaṃ niśamya saḥ |
mahīpatiḥ kṣaṇaṃ dhyātvā tato dṛṣṭvaivamabravīt || 38.95{95} ||
strībhirālāpitaṃ gītaṃ śubhāya na bhavetkvacit |
ityuktvā sa viṣaṇṇātmā manasaivaṃ vyacintayat || 38.96{96} ||
kṣemaṃ neme bhavennūnaṃ yatte striyau na mānave |
strīrūpā vahinirmāya preṣite kena māyayā || 38.97{97} ||
tadatra kiṃ bhavennūnaṃ tannimittaṃ śubhaṃ na hi |
kimupāyaṃ kariṣyāmi na manye ko hitaṃ diśet || 38.98{98} ||
iti ciṃttā viṣaṇṇātmā nṛpatiḥ sa viniśvasan |
śokālaye samāsīno vahi naivācaretkvacit || 38.99{99} ||
tathā sthitaṃ narendraṃ taṃ matvā vahiranirgatam |
nandīmukho'svaghoṣaṃ taṃ samālokyaivamabravīt || 38.100{100} ||
aśvaghoṣa narendro'sau tathāpi na vahiścaret |
tadatra kiṃ kariṣyāvo yannṛpo na vahiścaret || 38.101{1} ||
iti tenoditaṃ śrutva so'śvaghoṣo vicintayan |
taṃ nandimukhamālokya bodhayannevamabravīt || 38.102{2} ||
śṛṇu naṃdimukhopāyaṃ yena nṛpaḥ sa nicaret |
tadahaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi tannṛpāharaṇaṃ prati || 38.103{3} ||
yattatra nṛpatergehe māyāgninābhidhakṣyate |
tadā sa nṛpatistrastaḥ kopito niḥsaredvahi || 38.104{4} ||
tadā taṃ nṛpatiṃ dūrātsamāhṛtya prayatnataḥ |
sahasā purato devyā upaneṣyāvahe drutam || 38.105{5} ||
(rm 439)
iti tenoditaṃ śrutvā nandīmukhaḥ sa bodhitaḥ |
tathā hīti pratiśrutya tathā kartuṃ samaichata || 38.106{6} ||
tatastau saṃmataṃ kṛtvā tato'pyantarhitau drutam |
gatvā māyāgninā tābhyāṃ pradāhitaṃ nṛpālayam || 38.107{7} ||
taddṛṣṭvā nṛpatistrastaḥ sahasā niścaranstataḥ |
upāyaistajjvaladvahniṃ śamayāmāsa satvaram || 38.108{8} ||
tataḥ sa bhūpatī rājā taccintāparikheṭitaḥ |
śokāgare viṣaṇṇātmā sthitaścaiva vyacintayat || 38.109{9} ||
aho sadyaphalaṃ hyetadyatstrīgītanimittajam |
tadatra kiṃ kariṣyāmi yenaitacchāmyate'śubham || 38.110{10} ||
bhūyo'pyatra bhavedanyanmahotpātamamaṃgalam |
kathamatra pratīkāraṃ kariṣyāmi na manyate || 38.111{11} ||
iti ciṃtāviṣaṇṇātmā nṛpatiḥ sa vimohitaḥ |
śokāgāre samāsīno naiva vahirviniryayau || 38.112{12} ||
tathāpi svālayāsīnaṃ dṛṣṭvā vahirayāyinam |
nandīmukhāśvaghoṣau tau punarevaṃ samūcatuḥ || 38.113{13} ||
tathāpi sa nṛpo rājā naiva vahirviniḥsaret |
tadatra kiṃ kariṣyāvo yenāśu niścarennṛpaḥ || 38.114{14} ||
tataścāpi samālokya so'śvaghoṣo vicintayan |
naṃdīmukhaṃ sahāyaṃ taṃ samāmaṃtryaivamabravīt || 38.115{15} ||
bho sakhe sa mahārājastathāpi nātra niścaret |
tadatra kiṃ vilambena yatnena taṃ harevahi || 38.116{16} ||
varāharūpamādhāya tasyodyāne manorame |
sarvatrotpātanaṃ kastuṃ pracārevo'dhunā drutam || 38.117{17} ||
iti tenoditaṃ śrutvā naṃdīmukhastatheti sā |
pratiśrutya sakhāyaṃ taṃ samālokyaivamabravīt || 38.118{18} ||
evamapi kariṣyāvo devīkāryābhisiddhaye |
iti tau saṃmataṃ kṛtvā tatrodyāne prajagmatuḥ || 38.119{19} ||
tatra tau samupālokya puṣkariṇīṃ manoramām |
nānāpuṣpasamākīrṇāṃ paśyaṃtau tasthatuściram || 38.120{20} ||
tato nandīmukho dṛṣṭvā tamudyānaṃ manoramam |
puskariṇīṃ ca prāmodya gaṃdhāḍhyāmevamabravīt || 38.121{21} ||
ahodyānaṃ manoramyaṃ suramyaṃ ca saro varam |
nedaṃ vidhvaṃsanīyaṃ hi kathaṃ vidhvaṃsayevahi || 38.122{21!} ||
iti tenoditaṃ śrutvā so'śvaghoṣo viciṃtayan |
udyānaṃ tatsaro dṛṣṭvācirādevamabhāṣata || 38.123{22} ||
yathābhilaṣitaṃ devyāḥ sādhayeva tathā drutam |
tannṛpatimapāharttuṃ tadidaṃ dhvaṃsayāvahe || 38.124{23} ||
ityuktvā saumataṃ kṛtvā tāvubhau mahadāśrayam |
varāharūpamāsthāya prajagmatussarastaṭe || 38.125{24} ||
raktākṣau hṛṣṭaromāṇau nādāpuritadiṅmukhau |
siṃhaśārdūlasadṛśau damṣṭravibhīṣaṇānanau || 38.126{25} ||
tatra tau prasarantau tadudyānaṃ tatsaro'pi ca |
vindhvaṃsitumupākramya praceratuḥ samaṃtataḥ || 38.127{26} ||
(rm 440)
tau varāhau samālokya traste udyānapālakaḥ |
sahasā sa vinirgatya tato rājakule yayau || 38.128{27} ||
tatra sa samupāsṛtya rājñaḥ pādau kṛtāṃjaliḥ |
natvā trāsavibhinnāsyaḥ pura evaṃ nyavedayat || 38.129{28} ||
jaya deva vijānīyā yadudyāne sarovaram |
dvau varāhāvupākramya dhvaṃsituṃ caratā'dhunā || 38.130{29} ||
tadatra sahasā deva sarvasainyajanānnapi |
samupāhūya tau haṃtuṃ preṣayasva drutaṃ vane || 38.131{30} ||
iti tenoditaṃ śrutvā sa rājā parikopitaḥ |
sahasā sainyamāhūya svayaṃ tau hantumudyayau || 38.132{31} ||
tataḥ sa nṛpatī rājā samaṃtrīsainyapaurikāḥ |
sahasā nādayan vādyānstatrodyāne upācarat || 38.133{32} ||
tatra sa nṛpatirdṛṣṭvā samaṃtrisainyapaurikān |
tān sarvān samupāmaṃtrya punarevamupādiśat || 38.134{33} ||
bho bhavanto janāḥ sarve sajjīkṛtya samāhitāḥ |
atra dṛṣṭvopatiṣṭhantu parivṛtya samaṃtataḥ || 38.135{34} ||
ahaṃ prāgdiśi tiṣṭhāmi yāmye'mātyāḥ samaṃtriṇaḥ |
paścimādiśi sainānyaḥ paurāstiṣṭhantu cottare || 38.136{35} ||
evaṃ sarve'pyamātyādyāḥ sarvadikṣu samāhitāḥ |
sajjīkṛtyābhitiṣṭhantu parivṛtya samaṃtataḥ || 38.137{36} ||
yatra sthāne varāhau tau boddhuṃ naivābhiśaknutha |
yataścāsau parāyetāṃ taddiksthān daṇḍayeya hi || 38.138{37} ||
iti me vacanaṃ śrutvā sarve yūyaṃ samāhitāḥ |
sajjīkṛtya samālokya parivṛtyānutiṣṭhata || 38.139{38} ||
iti rājñā samādiṣṭaṃ śrutvā sarve'pi te janāḥ |
tatheti prativijñapya tathā sthātuṃ pracerire || 38.140{39} ||
tathā rājā sthitaḥ prācyāṃ yāmye'mātyagaṇāstathā |
senānyo paścimāyāṃ ca paurāstasthustathottare || 38.141{40} ||
evaṃ te sanṛpā lokāḥ sāmātyasainyapaurikāḥ |
sajjīkṛtya samālokya sarve tasthuḥ samāhitāḥ || 38.142{41} ||
evaṃ te sarvato dikṣu nirudhya samavasthitāḥ |
sarvavādyābhinirghoṣaṃ mahārāvairakheṭayan || 38.143{42} ||
tatsavādyamahārāvaṃ śrutvā tau tānupasthitān |
samaṃtataḥ samālokya mahāśabdaṃ vyamuṃcatām || 38.144{43} ||
tatastau sahasotpātamahāvāte pravarttataiḥ |
kṛtvāndhakāraṇairlokya rājānamadhyadhāvatām || 38.145{44} ||
tatra sa nṛpatistābhyāṃ spṛśyamāno vimūrchitaḥ |
saṃtrāsaparibhūyātmā kaṃpito nyapatadbhuvi || 38.146{45} ||
tataścirātsamutthāya kutremāviti lokya saḥ |
sahasāśvaṃ samāruhya tadanuprāsaran yayau || 38.147{46} ||
tathā te maṃtriṇo'mātyāḥ sainyalokāśca paurikāḥ |
sarve te'pyanudhāvantaḥ pradudruvurnṛpānugāḥ || 38.148{47} ||
sa nṛpāśvasamārūḍhastadvarāhānugaḥ śaram |
maurvīyuktaiḥ samādhāya vegādvanamupāviśat || 38.149{48} ||
(rm 441)
tatra tau māyikau tyaktvā vārāhadehamāśu tau |
niviśyāśvaṃ samāhṛtya prerayatāṃ vanāṃtare || 38.150{49} ||
tatra so'śvo mahāvegādvāyunā riva mahājavi |
vilaṃghya nimnagāṃ chailāṃ pradudrāva mahāvane || 38.151{50} ||
tataste maṃtriṇo'mātyāḥ sainānyaḥ sainikāḥ prajāḥ |
nṛpaṃ dūravanāviṣṭaṃ dṛṣṭvā pratyāyayuḥ śramāt || 38.152{51} ||
sena eko mahāvīraḥ svāmyamārgānusaṃcaran |
asakteḥ asahastūrṇaṃ kamaṇḍalukarastadā || 38.153{52} ||
chāyeva sahago vegādvanāṃtare tathānvagāt || 38.154{53} ||
tatrātivegato rājā vāyuhato samāhitaḥ |
aśvavṛkṣatale so'śvāddhāvatā nyapatadbhuvi || 38.155{54} ||
tatra sa mūrchito rājā kṣaṇaṃ tasthāvacetanaḥ |
tataḥ senaḥ prayatnena niḥśvasanrudatiṣṭhata || 38.156{55} ||
tataḥ sena samāśvāsya dhṛtvā taṃ nṛpamātmanā |
saha tatrāsane sthāpya vyaśrāmayecchramānvitam || 38.157{56} ||
tataḥ sa nṛpa ālokya tamaśvaṃ samupasthitam |
tṛṣārtto niḥśvasan senaṃ muhurdṛṣṭvaivamabravīt || 38.158{57} ||
aho sena mahatkaṣṭaṃ jāyate nāvihā'dhunā |
yatsudūre mahāraṇye majjāvahe sudustare || 38.159{58} ||
tadatra kiṃ kariṣyāvo yatsarve nirvṛto janāḥ |
sudūre nirjane'raṇye ko'tra mārgaṃ hi nirdiśet || 38.160{59} ||
yadatrātitṛṣārtto'haṃ tāpavātāhatāturaḥ |
śramakleśāgnisaṃdagdho dhairyeṇāpi vimocitaḥ || 38.161{60} ||
tadatra sahasā sena mama jīvitarakṣaṇam |
yatastato'pi vānviṣya śītalāmṛtamānaya || 38.162{61} ||
iti rājñoditaṃ śrutvā sa seneḥ sahasotthitaḥ |
svāminaṃ taṃ samāśvāsya paśyannevamabhāṣata || 38.163{62} ||
mā viṣīdāta rājendra yatkṣatriyo'si dhīravān |
tadbhavān dhairyamālambya muhurttaṃ sthātumarhati || 38.164{63} ||
yatastato'pi vānviṣya pāṇīyamamṛtopamam |
sahasā samupānīya dāsyāmi te narādhipa || 38.165{64} ||
tadyāvannātra pāṇīyaṃ dhṛtvāgachāmi sarvathā |
tāvaddhairyaṃ samālambya tiṣṭhāśvena saha prabho || 38.166{65} ||
iti vijñapya seno'sau svāminaṃ taṃ narādhipam |
samāśvāsya supāṇīyamanveṣituṃ drutaṃ yayau || 38.167{66} ||
tatra sa samupālokya sarvatrāpyabhilokayan |
supāṇīyamanasādya paśyan dūratare yayān || 38.168{67} ||
tatra suranadīśabdaṃ śrutvā senaḥ sa satvaraḥ |
tacchabdaṃ samanuśrutvā tatrābhyanusaran yayau || 38.169{68} ||
tatra tīraṃ samāsādya suranadyāḥ samāditaḥ |
sahasā samupāśritya pānīyaṃ surasaṃ papau || 38.170{69} ||
tatra sa parituṣṭātmā samaṃtato vilokayan |
tatpāre śuddhite dvīpe prādrākṣīdapsarogaṇān || 38.171{70} ||
(rm 442)
tānapsarogaṇān dṛṣṭvā sa seno vismayānvitaḥ |
mudā vilokayanneva tasthau citrārppitā yathā || 38.172{71} ||
tatrasthaṃtaṃ samālokya sarvā apsaraso'pi tāḥ |
kathamatrāgataḥ kastvaṃ prehīti samakandayan || 38.173{72} ||
iti tābhiḥ samāhūtaṃ śrutvā sa sena ātmanā |
suranadīṃ vilaṃghyāśu tatprabhāvādupāsarat || 38.174{73} ||
tatra sa samupāśritya sarvānstānapsarogaṇān |
sāṃjaliḥ suprasannāsyaḥ praṇatvaivaṃ nyavedayat || 38.175{74} ||
mahādevyā vijānīdhvaṃ yattrailokyeṣu viśrutaḥ |
sūryodayo mahārājastasyāhaṃ sevako'nugaḥ || 38.176{75} ||
yannṛpasya varāhābhyāmudyānaṃ dhvaṃsitaṃ tataḥ |
tau varāhau nihaṃtuṃ sa nṛpo'nudhāvati drutam || 38.177{76} ||
aśvenāpahṛto rājā sa mayaikena naujjhitaḥ |
atyabhidhāvato'raṇye patito'śvādihā'dhunā || 38.178{77} ||
aśokavṛkṣa āśritya gharmavātā hato'turaḥ |
śramatṛsārditastatra sthito'mbu pātumichati || 38.179{78} ||
tadambu sarvato'nviṣya kutrāpi labhyate na hi |
nadīśabdamihākarṇya sahasāhamupāsare || 38.180{79} ||
atra me bhāgyayogena preṣito'hamihāgataḥ |
labhāmi darśanaṃ devyo bhavāṃtīnamaho'dhunā || 38.181{80} ||
ityuktvā sena ālokya sāṃjaliḥ purato gataḥ |
tāḥ sarvā apsarā devīnatvaitaṃ samapṛchata || 38.182{81} ||
bho devyo bhavantyo'pi kimarthamiha tiṣṭhatha |
vaktavyaṃ cettadarthaṃ me samupādeṣṭumarhatha || 38.183{82} ||
iti saṃprārthitaṃ tena niśamyāyāpsaro'dhipāḥ |
sā taṃ senaṃ samāmaṃtrya purarevamupādiśat || 38.184{83} ||
mānava tvaṃ na jānāsi yadidaṃ vratamuttamam |
śrīvasudhāmahādevyā vayaṃ sarvāścarāmahe || 38.185{84} ||
iti devyā tayākhyātaṃ śrutvā senaḥ sa moditaḥ |
tadvrataṃ dharttumichaṃstāṃ devīṃ natvaivamabravīt || 38.186{85} ||
prasīda me mahādevi yacchrīdevyā idaṃ vratam |
ahamapi samichāmi dhartuṃ tanme upādiśa || 38.187{86} ||
iti tenārthitaṃ śrutvā sā devī paritoṣitā |
taṃ senaṃ sudhiyaṃ dhīraṃ matvaivamabravītpunaḥ || 38.188{87} ||
mānava vadi te vāṃchāpyasti śrīvasudhāvrate |
tattāvadiha saṃpaśya samāpyetā'dhunā vratam || 38.189{88} ||
paścātte'haṃ samākhyāya śrīdevyā vratasādhanam |
vidhiṃ samupadekṣyāmi saṃbuddhena yathoditam || 38.190{89} ||
ityuktvā sāpsaro devī sarvāstā apsarogaṇāḥ |
prāhūya tannadītīre vratārambhaṃ tathākarot || 38.191{90} ||
tatra tāḥ samupāśritya sarvā devyā yathākramam |
yathāvidhi samārabhya vrataṃ pūrṇaṃ pracakrire || 38.192{91} ||
(rm 443)
tadvrataṃ samupālokya sa seno'bhyanumoditaḥ |
aṣṭāṃgapraṇatiṃ kṛtvā sāṃjalirevamabravīt || 38.193{92} ||
aho bhāgyena saṃprāptaṃ śridevīvratadarśanam |
tanmetiḥ kṣīyate pāpamātmāri śuddhyate'dhunā || 38.194{93} ||
prasīdata mahādevyaḥ sarvā me kṛpayā dṛśā |
paśyantyo'nugrahaṃ kṛtvā vrate pradātumarhatam || 38.195{94} ||
sarvadāhaṃ cariṣyāmi śrīdevyāḥ śaraṇaṃ gatāḥ |
tadvratavidhi saṃpūrṇaṃ samupādeṣṭumarhatha || 38.196{95} ||
iti saṃprārthitaṃ tena śrutvā devī pradhānikā |
sā taṃ senaṃ viśuddhāṃśaṃ dṛṣṭvaivaṃ samupādiśat || 38.197{96} ||
śṛṇu sādho samādhāya vratavidhiṃ samucyate |
vajradharābdhigaṃbhīranirghoṣeṇa yathoditam || 38.198{97} ||
bhādrapade'site pakṣe śuklapakṣe'pi vā tathā |
māghe māse ca tadvacca sarveṣvapi ca māsu vā || 38.199{98} ||
dvitīyāyāmupātīrthe snātvā śuddhāmvarāvṛtaḥ |
śuddhabhūmī pradeśeṣu saṃśodhayetsamaṃtataḥ || 38.200{99} ||
mṛdgomayaśubhāmbhobhiḥ pralipya pariśodhayan |
tatra ratnamayaraṅgai dhātucūrṇaiśca paṃcabhiḥ || 38.201{100} ||
pauṣpikairvrīhicūrṇairvā tathā pāṣāṇacūrṇakaiḥ |
yathāvidhi samārabhya sūtrayitvā yathākramam || 38.202{1} ||
guruḥ śiṣyaiḥ sahālakṣya varttayenmaṇḍalaṃ kramāt |
tatra devyādi cihnāni likhedyathāvidhi kramāt || 38.203{2} ||
saptavrīhyāvalībhiśca saṃveṣṭayetsamaṃtataḥ |
tatastīrthāmbusaṃpūrṇān pītavastrāvṛtān ghaṭān |
ṣoḍaśadevatācihna lakṣitān pariśuddhitān || 38.204{3} ||
paṃcaratnāmṛtauṣadhyapallavapratisaṃyutān |
suvarṇatilakachatradhvajapatākikānvitān || 38.205{4} ||
bahiḥ pītāmbujāvalyāṃ saṃsthāpya veṣṭayetkramāt |
dhvajacāmarasaṃyuktavitānaiḥ parimaṃḍayet || 38.206{5} ||
vicitrapuṣpamālābhiḥ sugaṃdhibhiḥ samaṃtataḥ |
saurabhyadhūpanai dīpai paṃktibhiścābhiśobhayet || 38.207{6} ||
tatastanmaṇḍale devīṃ sagaṇāṃ śrīvasuṃdharām |
dhyātvāvāhya samabhyarcya pratiṣṭhāpyādhivāsayet || 38.208{7} ||
tato rātrau trisaṃdhyaṃ ca samabhyarcya yathāvidhiḥ |
baliṃ paṃcāmṛtairyuktaṃ datvā devīṃ smaraṃścaret || 38.209{8} ||
tataḥ prātastṛtīyāyāṃ tīrthe dravyaiḥ sugaṃdhibhiḥ |
saha śuddhāmbubhiḥ snātvā śuddhapītāmvarāvṛtaḥ || 38.210{9} ||
paṃcagavyaiḥ svamātmānaṃ pariṣiṃcya viśodhayet |
maṇḍale sagaṇāṃ devīṃ dhyātvāvāhya samarcayet || 38.211{10} ||
vratīnāpi tathā snātvā śuddhapītāmvarāvṛtāḥ |
paṃcagavyaistathā sarvārabhisiṃcya viśodhayet || 38.212{11} ||
tataste vratinaḥ sarve ācāryyapramukhāḥ kramāt |
madhyāhne yakṣarājasya digmukhāḥ svāsanāḥ śritāḥ || 38.213{12} ||
(rm 444)
yathācāryasamādiṣṭaṃ tathādhārya samāhitāḥ |
ādau vajradharaṃ natvā triratnaṃ śaraṇaṃ gatāḥ || 38.214{13} ||
maṃtrale sagaṇāṃ devīṃ dhyātvārcaye yathākramam || 38.215{14!} ||
tadyathā gururācāryaḥ śūnye drīkārasaṃbhavam |
pītavarṇaṃ mahāpadmaṃ tatrasthaṃ candramaṇḍalam |
tadutthaṃ śrībhavaṃ ratnaparvataṃ tasya copari |
tvaṃja bhadraghaṭāsīnāṃ bhāvayecchrīvasuṃdharām || 38.216{15} ||
pītāmekamukhāṃ kāṃtāṃ ṣaḍbhujāṃ lalitāsanām |
savye varadasaṃbuddhaṃ praṇāmaratnamaṃjalīm || 38.217{16} ||
vāme bhadraghaṭaprajñā pustakadhānyamaṃjalim |
dadhānaṃ ratnasaṃbuddhamakuṭinīṃ susuṃdarīm || 38.218{17} ||
pītapaṭṭāvṛtāṃ divyaratnālaṃkārabhūṣitam |
taddvitīyapuṭe pṛṣṭhe pīto vajrāsano jināḥ || 38.219{18} ||
vajradharābdhinirghoṣo varābhayapradānabhṛt || 38.220{19} ||
dakṣiṇe dvibhujārakto varadavajrahastakaḥ |
lokeśvaro rājallīlāvajraparyaṃkasaṃsthitaḥ || 38.221{20} ||
vajrapāṇistathā vāme varadavajrabhṛtkaraḥ |
haritā rājasaṃlīlā vajraparyaṃkasaṃsthitaḥ || 38.222{21} ||
līlā devī puraḥ pītā satvaparyaṃkasaṃsthitā |
bhujadvayena sarvopakaraṇādhikṛtiṃ tadā || 38.223{22} ||
tadvāme varuṇaḥ śvetaḥ satvaparyaṃkasaṃsthitaḥ |
vījapūrakamutsaṅgasthitiṃ karadvayena bhṛt || 38.224{23} ||
nīlobhe dakṣiṇe bhadraḥ satvaparyaṃkasaṃsthitaḥ |
bījapūrakamekena vibhratkroḍaṃ tathāparam || 38.225{24} ||
civikuṇḍalirāgneye iṣadraktastathāsthitaḥ |
bījapūrakamutsaṅge sthitiṃ dorbhyāṃ samā tathā || 38.226{25} ||
nairṛtye kelimālī ca śyāmavarṇastathā sthitaḥ |
bījapūrakamutsaṃgasthitaṃ dadhatkaradvayaḥ || 38.227{26} ||
vāyavye ca mukhendrākhyaḥ pītavarṇastathā sthitaḥ |
bījapūrakamutsaṃgasthitiṃ dadhatkaradvayam || 38.228{27} ||
carendro gaurariśāne satvaparyaṃkasaṃsthitaḥ |
bījapūrakamutsaṃgasthitiṃ dadhadbhujadvayaḥ || 38.229{28} ||
tattṛtīyapuṭe pūrve guptā devī tathā sthitā |
haridvarṇā bhujābhyāṃ sa cāmaraṃ vibhratī dharā || 38.230{29} ||
nairṛte vasuguptākhyā devī raktāstathā sthitā |
bhujābhyāṃ vibhratī chatraṃ sarvaratnasamujvalam || 38.231{30} ||
sarasvatī ca vāyavye devī pītā tathā sthitā |
dhvajahastā subhadrāṃgī sarvavidyārthadāyinī || 38.232{31} ||
iśāne caṃdrakāṃtākhyā devī śvetā tathā sthitā |
patākā vibhratī sarvaratnālaṃkārabhūṣitā || 38.233{32} ||
pūrvadvāre mahāyakṣo māṇibhadrastathā sthitaḥ |
kuṃkumābhā dadhaddorbhyāṃ nakulībījapūrakau || 38.234{33} ||
dakṣiṇe pūrṇabhadrākhyo nīlavarṇastathāsthitaḥ |
karābhyāṃ vidadhadratnastavakaṃ bījapūrakam || 38.235{34} ||
(rm 445)
paścime dhanado raktaḥ satvaparyaṃkasaṃsthitaḥ |
bhujadvayena vibhrāṇaḥ sughaṭabījapūrakau || 38.236{35} ||
uttare pītavarṇaśca vaiśravaṇastathā sthitaḥ |
bhujābhyāṃ saṃdadhadratnaprasavabījapūrakauḥ || 38.237{36} ||
ityetā devatā sarvāḥ śrīdevīpramukhān kramāt |
dhyātvāvāhya tato datvā pādyācamanaprokṣaṇam || 38.238{37} ||
tatastā maṇḍale sarvāḥ pratiṣṭhāpya yathā kramam |
ṣoḍaśavidhipūjāṃgaiḥ samarcayedyathā vidhi || 38.239{38} ||
gaṃdhaṃ puṣpaṃ tathā dhūpaṃ dīpaṃ naivedyameva ca |
ete paṃcopacārāstu kaivalyaphaladāyinaḥ || 38.240{39} ||

iti paṃcopacārāḥ

pādyamarghyaṃ tathā snānaṃ madhuparkācanaṃ tathā |
gaṃdhaṃ puṣpaṃ ca dhūpaṃ ca dīpaṃ naivedyameva ca |
pūtanācamanīyaṃ ca daśaitān saṃpracakṣyate || 38.241{40} ||

iti daśopacārā

āsanaṃ svāgataṃ pādyamarghyamācamanīyakam |
madhuparkācamanasnānaṃ vasanābharaṇāni ca || 38.242{41} ||
sugaṃdhasumano dhūpadīpanaivedyacaṃdanam |
prayojayedarcanāyāmupacārāstu ṣoḍaśaḥ || 38.243{42} ||
tato devyāḥ svahṛtmaṃtraṃ tathā upahṛdayaṃ japet |
sarvāsāmapi maṃtrāṇi yāvacchatasahasradhā || 38.244{43} ||
tataḥ stotrādibhi stutvā kṛtāṃjalipuṭo mudā |
kṛtvā pradakṣiṇānyevamaṣṭāṃgaiḥ praṇayetpunaḥ || 38.245{44} ||
tataśca sāṃjaliḥ sthitvā prakuryātpāpadeśanām |
puṇyānumodanāṃ capi saṃsthitiyācanāmapi || 38.246{45} ||
tataḥ kṣīrodanaiḥ śuddharasaiḥ paṃcāmṛtairyutam |
baliṃ gāthāḥ paṭhan dadyātsarvabhūtā hi tuṣṭaye || 38.247{46} ||
tathā te vratinaḥ sarve sarvaṃ dhṛtvā samāhitāḥ |
yathā śāstrā samādiṣṭamanukuryu vrataṃ kramāt || 38.248{47} ||
tataste vratinaḥ sarve dānaṃ dadyuḥ sa dakṣiṇam |
gurave devatābhyo'pi praṇameyuśca sādaram || 38.249{48} ||
tataḥ kṣamārthanaṃ kṛtvā sūtraṃ ṣoḍaśabheditam |
ṣoḍaśagraṃthitaṃ pītaṃ vratibhyo gururarpayet || 38.250{49} ||
tatsūtraṃ te samādāya sarve'pi vratino mudā |
rakṣārthaṃ svakare vadhvā dadhyurvratasamāptitaḥ || 38.251{50} ||
tataste vratinaḥ sarve dhṛtvā saṃpattiprārthanām |
natvā kṣamārthanāṃ cāpi kṛtvā careyurutthitāḥ || 38.252{51} ||
tato dinatriyāmāgre yavagodhūmapiṣṭakam |
saha paṃcāmṛtaiḥ sarvaiḥ kuryustadvratapālanām || 38.253{52} ||
tatastevratinaḥ sarve snātvā cāpi samāhitāḥ |
dīpamālāḥ samujvālya tiṣṭheyu niśi jāgritāḥ || 38.254{53} ||
tāṃ devīṃ sagaṇāṃ smṛtvā paṭhanto dhāraṇīmapi |
japastotrādikaṃ kṛtvā toṣayeyurupasthitāḥ || 38.255{54} ||
evaṃ te tanniśāṃ sarve vyatilaṃghya punastathā |
(rm 446)
prātaḥ sugaṃdhibhistīrthe snātva śuddhāmvarāvṛtāḥ || 38.256{55} ||
tāṃ devīṃ sagaṇāṃ dhyātvā samāvāhya yathākramam |
sarvapūjopahāraiśca prārcayeyustathādarāt || 38.257{56} ||
japastotrādikaṃ kṛtvā ratvā sāṃjalayo mudā |
kṛtvā pradakṣiṇāṃ cāpi kuryuraṣṭāṅgaprāṇatim || 38.258{57} ||
tataśca purataḥ sthitvā prakuryuḥ pāpadeśanām |
puṇyānumodanāṃ cāpi prārthayeyuḥ susaṃsthitim || 38.259{58} ||
tataḥ saṃbodhicaryārthaṃ sarvasatvahitārthataḥ |
sarvadravyasusaṃpattiḥ prārthayeyuryathechayā || 38.260{59} ||
tato guruṃ samabhyarcya datvā dānaṃ sadakṣiṇam |
śraddhayā praṇatiṃ kṛtvā toṣayeyuḥ samādarāt || 38.261{60} ||
tato bhūtavaliṃ datvā prārthayitvā yathepsitam |
kṛtvā kṣamārthanāṃ cāpi visarjayedyathāvidhi || 38.262{61} ||
tataḥ śāstā sa ācāryaḥ kṛtvā cāpi kṣamārthanām |
saṃpūrṇaṃ tadvrataṃ kṛtvā vratasūtraṃ samāharet || 38.263{62} ||
tato'bhiṣekamādāya vratināpyabhiṣiṃcayet |
śubhāśiṣaṃ pradatvā ca tanmaṇḍalaṃ visarjayet || 38.264{63} ||
tatastadrajamādāya koṣṭhāgāre suguptite |
nikṣipya pratisaṃsthāpya nityaṃ nametsadādarāt || 38.265{64} ||
aparaṃ puṣpādikaṃ sarvaṃ nirmālyaṃ savidambhasi |
nāgānabhyarcya tatsarvaṃ samarppitvā pravāhayet || 38.266{65} ||
tannadījalamādāya gṛhe tataḥ samācaret |
tatra sarvatra geheṣu tajjalairabhiṣiṃcayet || 38.267{66} ||
tatastanmaṇḍale sthāne samāvāhya yathāvidhi |
śrīkumārīṃ mahādevīṃ samabhyarcya hi toṣayet || 38.268{67} ||
tatprasādaṃ samādāya gaṇacakraṃ yathāvidhi |
samabhyarcya yathārheṇa bhojanenapi toṣayet || 38.269{68} ||
evaṃ tadvratasaṃpūrṇaṃ kṛtvā sarve pramoditāḥ |
śrivasudhāṃ mahādevīṃ smṛtvā careyurābhavam || 38.270{69} ||
evametadvidhānena śrīdevyāḥ śaraṇaṃ gataḥ |
yathāvidhi samādhāya vrataṃ caretsamādarāt || 38.271{70} ||
tasya gṛhe tadārabhya śrīdevī śrīvasundharā |
svayamāgatya sarvatra kṣemaṃ kṛtvā vasetsadā || 38.272{71} ||
tadārābhya sadā tasya gṛhe sarvatra maṃgalam |
nirupadravamānaṃdaṃ sarvadāpi bhaveddhruvam || 38.273{72} ||
tatastasya gṛhe saṃpaddine dine pravarddhitā |
sarvadravyābhisaṃpūrṇā śrīdasyeva samudbhavet || 38.274{73} ||
atha cāpi tathā śaktau yaḥ śrāddhaḥ puruṣaḥ sudhīḥ |
snātvā śuddhāmvaro gehe śodhayitvā samaṃtataḥ || 38.275{74} ||
śrīdevyāḥ pratimāyaivaṃ paṭaṃ vāpi prasārya ca |
yathāśakti samabhyarcya dhāraṇīṃ sarvadā paṭhet || 38.276{75} ||
tasyāpi ca gṛhe tiṣṭhecchrīdevī sagaṇānvitā |
tathā tatra bhavedevaṃ maṃgalaṃ nirupadravam || 38.277{76} ||
(rm 447)
tadā tasya gṛhe saṃpaddine dine hi varddhate |
sarvadravyābhisaṃpūrṇā bhavenna kṣiṇuyātkvacit || 38.278{77} ||
atha vā prāktane pītarajasā maṃḍalaṃ tathā |
varttayitvātra tadrātrīṃ niyamenātināmayet || 38.279{78} ||
prātaḥ snātvā sugaṃdhena śuddhapītāmvarāvṛtaḥ |
brahmacārī viśuddhātmā triratnaśaraṇaṃ gataḥ || 38.280{79} ||
tāṃ śrīvasuṃdharāṃ devīṃ dhyātvāhya samāhitaḥ |
yathāśakti samabhyarcya dhāraṇīṃ śraddhayā paṭhet || 38.281{80} ||
tasyāpi ca gṛhe bhadraṃ kṛtvā devī vasetsadā |
tatastathā kramātsaṃpatparivṛddhā bhaveddhruvam || 38.282{81} ||
atha prātastathā snātvā śuddhavastrāvṛtaḥ sudhīḥ |
brahmacārī samādhāya triratnaśaraṇaṃ gataḥ || 38.283{82} ||
śubhe sthāne paṭaṃ cāpi devyā mūrttiṃ prasārya ca |
yāthāśakti samabhyarcya trivārān dhāraṇīṃ paṭhet || 38.284{83} ||
tasyāpi ca gṛhe bhadraṃ kṛtvā devī sadā vaset |
tathā tatra mahāsaṃpadabhivṛddhā bhaveddhruvam || 38.285{84} ||
atha vā ca tathā snātvā śuddhavastrāvṛtaḥ sudhīḥ |
brahmacārī viśuddhātmā triratnaśaraṇaṃ gataḥ || 38.286{85} ||
śubhe sthāne gṛhe devyāścandanenāpi maṃḍalam |
varttayitvā tathā devīṃ dhyatvāvāhya samarcayet || 38.287{86} ||
saṃpaṭheddhāraṇīṃ caivaṃ kuryājjapastavādikam |
kṛtvā pradakṣiṇānyevaṃ natvā saṃprārthayennidhim || 38.288{87} ||
tasyāpi ca gṛhe devī maṃgalaṃ nirupadravam |
kṛtvā svayaṃ samāśritya nivasetsarvadā dhruvam || 38.289{88} ||
tatastatra gṛhe saṃpatsarvadravyasamanvitā |
abhivṛddhya bhavennūnaṃ nidhiścāpi samudbhavet || 38.290{89} ||
atha vā svagṛhe śuddhe kośakoṣṭhālaye'pi ca |
tathā paragṛhe vāpi manoramye'bhiśodhite || 38.291{90} ||
atha tathāgatasyāpi lokeśasyāpi cāgrataḥ |
tathānyabodhisatvānāṃ devatānāṃ tathāgrataḥ || 38.292{91} ||
candanenāpi kṛtvaivaṃ śrīdevyā maṇḍalaṃ tathā |
vidhivanmanasā dhyātvā samāvāhya samarcayet || 38.293{92} ||
tathā tathāgatānaṃ ca trailokādhipaterapi |
tathānyabodhisatvānāṃ yathāśakti samarcayet || 38.294{93} ||
tatastāṃ sagaṇāṃ devīṃ dhyātvā japetsamāhitaḥ |
saṃpaṭheddhāraṇīṃ cāpi śraddhayā bhaktimānasaḥ || 38.295{94} ||
tatha caikamahorātramavichinnaṃ paṃthenmudā |
yāvattripaṃcasaptāhorātrāṇi ca paṭhettathā || 38.296{95} ||
ācāryaśca tathā rātrau striḥ kṛtvā divasasya ca |
sa lokapālabhūtebhyo baliṃ dadyādyathāvidhi || 38.297{96} ||
tato dānapatiḥ śāstre sarvopakaraṇādikam |
dāne sadakṣiṇaṃ dadyādanyebhyo'pi tathādarāt || 38.298{97} ||
(rm 448)
tataśca sāṃjalirnatvā prārthayitvā kṣamāmapi |
saṃbodhisādhanīṃ dravyasaṃpattiṃ prārthayenmudā || 38.299{98} ||
tataḥ sarvaṃ samāpyaivaiḥ kṛtvāpi tadvisarjanam |
yathārhabhojanaiḥ sarvāñchāstrādīn samatoṣayet || 38.300{99} ||
tanmaṇḍale rajaḥ sarvaṃ samādāya sapuṣpakam |
koṣṭhakoṣṭhālayāgāre nikṣipya sthāpayetsadā || 38.301{100} ||
tadrajo yatra nikṣiptaṃ tatra saṃpatpravarddhitaḥ |
sarvadravyābhisaṃpūrṇā bhavenna kṣiṇuyātkvacit || 38.302{1} ||
nirmālyamaparaṃ sarvaṃ sanaivedyadhvajādikam |
nadyāṃ nāgān samabhyarya saṃprakṣipya pravāhayet || 38.303{2} ||
tatastaddhāraṇīvidyā bhavetpaṭhitasiddhitā |
yatrāpi paṭhyate tatra saṃpatpravarddhitā bhavet || 38.304{3} ||
evaṃ satyaṃ samākhyātaṃ sarvairapi munīśvaram |
śrīdevyāḥ sādhanaṃ kalyaṃ matvaivaṃ cara tadvratam || 38.305{4} ||
ya evaṃ śrīmahādevyāḥ śraddhayā śaraṇaṃ gataḥ |
yathāvidhi samādhāya carate vratamādarāt || 38.306{5} ||
tasya māragaṇāḥ sarve satvopadravakārakāḥ |
viheṭhanāṃ sadā kvāpi karttu maivābhiśaknuyuḥ || 38.307{6} ||
sarvaśakrādayo devāḥ sarvadaityādhipā api |
grahāstārāśca siddhāśca sādhyā vidyādharā api || 38.308{7} ||
lokapālāśca sarve'pi gaṃdharvāḥ kiṃnarā api |
yakṣāśca rākṣasāḥ sarve nāgāśca garuḍā api || 38.309{8} ||
mātṛkā bhairavāḥ sarve sakumāravināyakāḥ |
mahākālāśca ḍākinyaḥ śākinyo'pi tathāparāḥ || 38.310{9} ||
yoginyo'pi tathā sarve vīrā yamāntakādayaḥ |
rakṣanti sarvadā nityaṃ śrīdevyāḥ śaraṇāśritam || 38.311{10} ||
dhāraṇīpāṭhakaṃ yo'pi sādhakaṃ vratacāriṇam |
tathā bhūtagaṇāḥ sarve piśācāśca durāśayāḥ || 38.312{11} ||
sarve jvarādayo rogā apasmārādayo'pi ca || 38.313{12} ||
unmādā piṭakāścāpi sarve kuṣṭhāśca kachavaḥ |
śrīdevīvratino dehe na spṛśaṃti kadācana || 38.314{13} ||
ītayaśca tathā sarvā mahopadravakārakāḥ |
tasya deśe gṛhe dehe na viśanti kadā cana || 38.315{14} ||
vasuṃdharyā vrate yatra dhāraṇī ca pravarttate |
tatra māragaṇā duṣṭā upasargā viśanti na || 38.316{15} ||
nṛpāgnyudakā caurādi duṣṭāḥ śaṭhāśca vaṃcakāḥ |
śrīdevīvratinaḥ kiṃcidapakartuṃ na śaknuyuḥ || 38.317{16} ||
yaśca śrīvasudhārāyā bhaktimāñcharaṇaṃ gataḥ |
sarvadā smaraṇaṃ kṛtvā bhajate saṃcaradvratam || 38.318{17} ||
yaśca tasyā mahādevyā dhāraṇīṃ muditaḥ smaran |
paṭhate sarvadāpyevaṃ parebhyo'pi samādiśet || 38.319{18} ||
yaścaitaddhāraṇīṃ samyag'likhellikhāyayedapi |
likhitāṃ samādāya saṃsthāpya bhajate gṛhe || 38.320{19} ||
yaścāpi hṛdayaṃ devyā likhitvā bhūrjapatrake |
(rm 449)
mūrddhni kaṇṭhe bhuje vāhau dadhāti nityamādarāt || 38.321{20} ||
teṣāmapi ca sarveṣāṃ māravighnopasargikāḥ |
sarve'pi sarvadā kvāpi hyapakarttuṃ na śaknuyuḥ || 38.322{21} ||
sarve'pi bodhisatvāste traidhātukanivāsinaḥ |
etadvidyānubhāvena dānapāramitāśritāḥ || 38.323{22} ||
sarvopakaraṇaiḥ sarvān satvān saṃtarppayantyapi |
tathā dānaṃ sadā kṛtvā bodhimārge caranti ye || 38.324{23} ||
te sarve krameṇaivaṃ pūrya pāramitā daśaḥ |
sarvānmāragāṇāṃ jitvā prāpnuyu bodhisaṃpadam || 38.325{24} ||
sarvabhūpāśca rājānaścakravarttyādayo nṛpāḥ |
pālayanti prajā lokān dharmairlakṣmīvratodbhavaiḥ || 38.326{25} ||
evamanye'pi lokāśca ye ye saṃpatsamṛddhitāḥ |
mahādhanā mahābhogāḥ sarvadravyasamanvitāḥ || 38.327{26} ||
datvā dānaṃ sukhaṃ bhuktvā yāvajjīvaṃ śubhe sadā |
pracarantyapi sarve te jñeyā lakṣmīprasādataḥ || 38.328{27} ||
ye śrīdevyā vrataṃ dhṛtvā sarvārthibhyo yathepsitam |
dadāti te mahāsatvāḥ śrīmantaḥ sadguṇānvitāḥ || 38.329{28} ||
yaśaḥsaṃpatsamāpannāḥ sarvavidyākalāspadāḥ |
dharmātmāno maheśākhyaḥ sudhiyaḥ satyavādinaḥ || 38.330{29} ||
suśīlāḥ subhagā bhadrāḥ kṣāntisaurabhyavāsinaḥ |
saumyendriyā viśuddhāṃgāḥ karuṇārdraśubhāśayāḥ || 38.331{30} ||
balavīryamahotsāhā niḥkleśāḥ susamāhitāḥ |
jitendriyā mahādhīrāḥ prajñāvanto vicakṣaṇāḥ || 38.332{31} ||
upāyavidhivijñāśca sarvasatvahitārthinaḥ |
supraṇidhisamādhānā balino brahmacāriṇaḥ || 38.333{32} ||
jñānavijñānavidvānsaḥ sarvasatvaśubhaṃkarāḥ |
bodhisatvā jagannāthā buddhātmajā bhavaṃtyapi || 38.334{33} ||
yatibhirnā atītāste varttamānā anāgatāḥ |
te'pi sarve mahādevyā vratapuṇyānubhāvataḥ || 38.335{34} ||
kramātpāramitāḥ sarvāḥ paripūrya samāhitāḥ |
bodhilabdhā bhavaṃtyevaṃ lapsyaṃti nānyathā khalu || 38.336{35} ||
evaṃ matvā mahāvidyaṃ śrīdevīṃ dhāraṇīṃ parām |
samādhāya paṭhannityaṃ vrataṃ cara samādarāt || 38.337{36} ||
eṣā mahaiśvarī devī bodhilakṣmīrjineśvaśrī |
jananī sarvabuddhānāṃ dhātrī mātānupālinī || 38.338{37} ||
adhṛṣyā sarvaduṣṭānāṃ sarvavighnavināśinī |
mahāvidyeśvarī bhadrā sarvāriṣṭhanisūdanī || 38.339{38} ||
yasyāḥ śaraṇamātreṇa vrataṃ caraṃti mānavāḥ |
dāridryaduḥkhaśokāgnau na pataṃti kadāpi te || 38.340{39} ||
acintitāni ratnādi dhanadravyaguṇāni ca |
yaśodharmārthakāmādi sukhāni samavāpnuyuḥ || 38.341{40} ||
etatpuṇyamasaṃkhyeyamaprameyaṃ mahattaram |
sarvairapi munīndrairhi saṃkhyātuṃ nāpi śakyate || 38.342{41} ||
sarve vratajapuṇyānāni saṃkhyātumabhiśakyate |
tadetadvratapuṇyānāṃ saṃkhyātuṃ kena śakyate || 38.343{42} ||
(rm 450)
yadetadvratasādṛśyaṃ kutrāpi no hi vidyate |
tadadhikaṃ kuto loke sarvatrāpi na dṛśyate || 38.344{43} ||
īdṛkpuṇyamahāratnaṃ trailokyeṣvapi durllabham |
triratnabhajanādeva labhyate bhuvi mānavam || 38.345{44} ||
tadevaṃ tvaṃ parijñāya triratnaśaraṇaṃ gataḥ |
śrīdevīṃ vidhināvāhya vrataṃ cara samāhitaḥ || 38.346{45} ||
etatpuṇyānubhāvena sarvadā sadgatīṃ gataḥ |
śrīsaṃpattisamāpannaḥ pradātā sukhabhṛdbhavet || 38.347{46} ||
trividhāṃ bodhimāsādya saṃbuddhapadamāpnuyuḥ || 38.348{47!} ||
iti hetormahādevyāḥ svayaṃ tvaṃ śaraṇaṃ gataḥ |
vrataṃ caraṃstathā loke pracāraya samaṃtataḥ || 38.349{48} ||
iti tayā mahādevyāḥ samādiṣṭaṃ niśamya saḥ |
senaḥ kṛtāṃjalirnatvā tāṃ devīmevamabravīt || 38.350{49} ||
evaṃ devi kariṣyāmi bhavatyājñāṃ śiro vahan |
tatprasīda mahādevyā dhāraṇīṃ saṃpradehi me || 38.351{50} ||
iti saṃprārthitaṃ tena śrutvā sā saṃprasāditā |
devī taṃ senamālokya samāmaṃtryaivamādiśat || 38.352{51} ||
sādhu sena gṛhāṇa tvaṃ devyāyā dhāraṇīṃ varām |
svayaṃ paṭhaṃ parebhyo'pi deśayasva yathāvidhi || 38.353{52} ||
ityādiśyāpsarorūpā sā śrīdevī vasuṃdharā |
tasmai senāsu tāṃ devyā dhāraṇīṃ saṃpaṭhan dadau || 38.354{53} ||
sa senastāṃ mahādevīṃ praṇatvā sāṃjalirmudā |
śrīdevyā dhāraṇīṃ vidyāṃ praṇatvā śraddhayādade || 38.355{54} ||
tataḥ sā cāpsaro devī tasmai senāya sādaram |
sa paṃcāmṛtagodhūmai piṣṭakaṃ pālane dadau || 38.356{55} ||
taddattaṃ piṣṭataṃ taṃ sa seno dṛṣṭvā pramoditaḥ |
praṇatvādāya taṃ bhūpaṃ smṛtvā na bubhuje svayam || 38.357{56} ||
tadā divyapsarorūpaṃ tyaktvā sākṣāttrimūrttidhṛk |
