caṃdramaṇḍalamadhyasthabhadraghaṭoparisthitā || 38.358{57} ||
darśayāmāsa rūpaṃ svaṃ prabhāmaṇḍalavarttitam |
anyā devyo'pi rūpaṃ svaṃ dhṛtvā devīṃ vasuṃdharām || 38.359{58} ||
parivṛtya puro dhāya patākādhvajacāmaraiḥ |
vījayaṃtyaḥ sarvadikṣu tasthu saṃproktarūpavat || 38.360{59} ||
tataḥ sa sena utthāya tāṃ devīṃ sagaṇāṃ mudā |
muhurmuhurvaddhagala aṣṭāṅgaiḥ praṇatiṃ vyadhāt || 38.361{60} ||
tataḥ sa sena ādhāya sapaṃcāmṛtapiṣṭakam |
taṃ jalaṃ ca samādhāya sahasā nṛpāṃtike yayau || 38.362{61} ||
tatra sa sena ālokya nṛpaṃ durādupāgatam |
tadamṛtādikaṃ sarvamupasthāpyāpyadhaukayat || 38.363{62} ||
tataḥ sa sena ālokya taṃ nṛpaṃ sāṃjalirmudā |
praṇatvā suprasannāsyaḥ purataḥ samupāśrayat || 38.364{63} ||
tamamṛtādikaṃ sarvaṃ dṛṣṭvā sa vismito nṛpaḥ |
kiṃ vilaṃvaṃ kṛtaścaitadityevaṃ tamapṛchata || 38.365{67!} ||
(rm 451)
iti pṛṣṭe narendreṇa sa senaḥ samupāśritaḥ |
taṃ narendraṃ samālokya vinayannevamabravīt || 38.366{68} ||
kṣamasva bho mahārāja yadarthe me vilaṃvitam |
tatsarvaṃ samupākhyāya bhavantaṃ bodhayānyapi || 38.367{69} ||
idaṃ tāvanmahārāja suranadyamṛtaṃ pivā |
idaṃ paṃcāmṛtaṃ cāpi bhukṣvedaṃ ca supiṣṭakam || 38.368{70} ||
ityuktvā sa samādāya kamaṇḍaluṃ samarppayat |
bhogyaṃ paṃcāmṛtaṃ cāpi piṣṭakaṃ cāgrataḥ prabhoḥ || 38.369{71} ||
tadamṛtādikaṃ sarvaṃ dṛṣṭvā sa mudito nṛpaḥ |
kamaṇḍaluṃ samādāya papau tatsurasāmṛtam || 38.370{72} ||
tataḥ sa nṛpatistuṣṭo nistṛṣṇāhlāditāśayaḥ |
taṃ ca paṃcāmṛtaṃ bhogyaṃ bubhuje piṣṭakānyapi || 38.371{73} ||
tataḥ sa nṛpatistuṣṭo mahānaṃdapramoditaḥ |
taṃ senaṃ sumatiṃ dhīraṃ samālokyaivamabravīt || 38.372{73!} ||
tvamapīdaṃ sudhākalpamamṛtaṃ piva sanmate |
bhukṣva pacāmṛtaṃ bhogyaṃ piṣṭakaṃ cedamuttamam || 38.373{74} ||
iti rājñā samādiṣṭe sa senaḥ pratimaṃḍitaḥ |
tadamṛtādikaṃ sarvamādāya bubhuje svayam || 38.374{75} ||
tatastaccheṣamādāya sa senaḥ paritoṣitaḥ |
aśvāya samupasthāpya sarvaṃ dadau vinodayan || 38.375{76} ||
so'pi cāśvaḥ samālokya tatsarvamamṛtādikam |
āśvādya paribhuṃjāno mahānaṃdaṃ samāyayau || 38.376{77} ||
tataḥ sa nṛpati rājā pariśuddhāśayo mudā |
taṃ senaṃ tamupāmaṃtrya punarevamapṛchata || 38.377{78} ||
aho sena tvamānītaṃ yadetadamṛtādikam |
kutaḥ kathaṃ samāsādya tatsatyaṃ vaktumarhasi || 38.378{79} ||
ityevaṃ prārthite rājñā sa senaḥ sāṃjalirmudā |
svāminaṃ taṃ nṛpaṃ natvā samālokyaivamabravīt || 38.379{80} ||
śṛṇu rājan samādhāya yadetadamṛtādikam |
mayāsādya samānītaṃ tatsaṃnivedayāni te || 38.380{81} ||
yadatra tvaṃ tṛṣārtto'si tadarthe'haṃ sasaṃbhramaḥ |
jalamanveṣituṃ tatra vane paśyan bhramāmyaham || 38.381{82} ||
kutrāpi dṛśyate nātra pānīyaṃ nirjane vane |
tato dūrataraṃ gatvā jalamanveṣituṃ bhrame || 38.382{83} ||
tatrāpi dṛśyate naiva jalaṃ kvāpi samaṃtataḥ |
tato'tidūrato nadyāḥ śabdaṃ śṛṇvannanuvraje || 38.383{84} ||
tacchabdānusaran gatvā paśyāmi suranimnagām |
tatra devīgaṇāścāpi paśyāmi vratacārikāḥ || 38.384{85} ||
tatra tāsāṃ samālokya samākraṃdaṃti sādaram |
tato'haṃ tatprabhāvena tāṃ nadīṃ laṃghayan vraje || 38.385{86} ||
tatra māṃ samupāhūya sarvā tā apsarogaṇāḥ |
kutaḥ kathamihāyāsi tvaṃ vadeti babhāṣire || 38.386{87} ||
tato'haṃ samupāsṛtya tāḥ sarvā apsarogaṇāḥ |
sāṃjaliḥ sādaraṃ natvā pura evaṃ nivedaya || 38.387{88} ||
(rm 452)
sūryodayo mahārājo mahīpālo narādhipaḥ |
tasyodyāne varāhau dvau prakurvāta upadravam || 38.388{89} ||
tanniśamya sa bhūpālaḥ samaṃtrisainyapaurikaḥ |
saha bhogyaṃ samāruhya varāhau haṃtumudyayau || 38.389{90} ||
tatrodyāne caturdikṣu parivṛtya samaṃtataḥ |
hāhākāraṃ prakurvantastiṣṭhanti te nṛpādayaḥ || 38.390{91} ||
tatastau dvau mahāghorau varāhau saha sāgataḥ |
udyānānnirgatau vegānnṛpāntikātparāgatau || 38.391{92} ||
tatra sa nṛpatiḥ kopāttau varāhāvanudrutaḥ |
aśvamāruhya vegena praviṣṭo bhramate vane || 38.392{93} ||
tatra sa nṛpatiraśvādvegātpradhāvato'patat |
śramatāpapipāsārtto tarutale niṣīdati || 38.393{94} ||
tasya rājñastṛṣārttasya jalamanveṣituṃ bhraman |
kutrāpi nirjane'raṇye jalaṃ paśyāmyahaṃ na hi || 38.394{95} ||
tato dūrataraṃ gatvā jalamanviṣya sarvataḥ |
bhraman dūrānnadīśabdaṃ śrutvāhaṃ samupāsare || 38.395{96} ||
etadarthamahaṃ devyo dūrataḥ sahasāgataḥ |
tadbhavantyaḥ prasīdaṃtu dṛśyaṃte bhāgyato mayā || 38.396{97} ||
bhavantyo'pi kimarthe'tra viharanti samāśritāḥ |
iti pṛṣṭe mayā tāśca devyaḥ sarvāḥ prasāditāḥ || 38.397{93} ||
ādarānmāṃ samāmaṃtrya pura evaṃ babhāṣire |
mānava tvaṃ na jānīṣe yadiheva mamāśritā |
tadarthaṃ samupākhyāyastatsaṃśṛṇu samāhitaḥ || 38.398{99} ||
yā śrībhagavatī devī māheśvarī vasuṃdharā |
tasyā vrataṃ vayaṃ sarvā caritumiha saṃsthitāḥ || 38.399{100} ||
tadatra no vrataṃ paśyaṃ kṣaṇaṃ tiṣṭha samāhitaḥ |
etaddṛṣṭe'pi te puṇyaṃ saṃprajāyenmahattaram || 38.400{1} ||
iti tābhiḥ samādiṣṭaṃ śrutvāhaṃ saṃpramoditaḥ |
tatheti prativijñapya tadvrataṃ draṣṭumāśrame || 38.401{2} ||
tatastā apsarodevyaḥ sarvāstatra yathākramam |
svasvāsane samāśritya prārabhanti yathākramāt || 38.402{3} ||
tatra tā maṃḍale devīṃ sagaṇāṃ śrīvasuṃdharāṇ |
yathāvidhiṃ samāvāhya pūjayaṃti samādarāt || 38.403{4} ||
japastotrādikaṃ kṛtvā sarvāḥ sāṃjalayaśca tāḥ |
natvā pradakṣiṇīkṛtya prārthayaṃti susaṃpadam || 38.404{5} ||
tatastā apsarodevyaḥ sarvāḥ samāptite vrate |
sa paṃcāmṛtaiḥ pūpaiḥ kurvaṃti vratapālanām || 38.405{6} ||
mahyamapi tathā dattaṃ tābhiḥ sarvābhirādarāt |
idaṃ paṃcāṃṛtaṃ bhojyaṃ godhūmapiṣṭakaṃ varam || 38.406{7} ||
smṛtvā bhavaṃtamādāya tābhirdattaṃ samādarāt |
tathā pātre pratiṣṭhāpya gopayāmi tavārthataḥ || 38.407{8} ||
tato'haṃ sāṃjalirnatvā tā devīḥ sakalā api |
tathā caritumālocya prārthayāmi vidhiṃ vrate || 38.408{9} ||
mayā saṃprārthitaṃ śrutvā yā taddevī gaṇādhipā |
(rm 453)
sā tadvratavidhiṃ samyaksamādiśati me kramāt || 38.409{10} ||
tatsarvaṃ samupādiṣṭaṃ śrutvāhaṃ saṃpramoditaḥ |
tathā tadvratamārādhya prechāmi carituṃ sadā || 38.410{11} ||
pratyakṣadarśanaṃ labdhvā aṣṭāṃgaiḥ praṇato'smyaham || 38.411{12} ||
tatastāḥ sakalā devīḥ prārthayitvā samādārāt |
idaṃ divyāmṛtaṃ bhojyaṃ prādāya sahasā care || 38.412{13} ||
iti heto mahārāja vilamvo jāyate tathā |
tatkṣamasva prabho mātra raukṣyaṃ kṛthāḥ prasīda me || 38.413{14} ||
iti tenoditaṃ śrutvā sa rājā vismayānvitaḥ |
taddarśanotsuko dṛṣṭvā taṃ senametadabravīt || 38.414{15} ||
aho āścaryyamatra yadvasudhārā vrataṃ śrutam |
pratyakṣadarśanamapi labdhaṃ seneha nirjane || 38.415{16} ||
mayā na śrūyate kvāpi dṛśyate na kadā cana |
taddraṣṭuṃ sena me cittaṃ samabhilaṣati dhruvam || 38.416{17} ||
tasmāttvaṃ tatra māṃ nītvā tā devī darśaya drutam |
iti rājñoditaṃ śrutvā sa senaḥ sacivaḥ sudhīḥ |
tatheti prativijñapya tatra rājñā saha caran || 38.417{18} ||
sahasā tau pragachaṃtau taddevīdarśanotsukau |
daivayogātkṣaṇātprāptau tatra sthāne samaikṣatām || 38.418{19} ||
tadā tatra na tā devyaḥ sthitāḥ sarvā divaṃ gatāḥ |
vratanirmālyapuṣpādi tatrādrāṣṭāṃ na tāḥ kvacit || 38.419{20} ||
tatra sa nṛpatistāsāṃ devīnāṃ darśanaṃ kvacit |
alabhyamāne udvignaparikhinnāśayo'bhavat || 38.420{21} ||
tatra sa nṛpatirnatvā vratasthāne kṛtāṃjaliḥ |
sarvatrāpi samālokya khaṃ paśyannevamabravīt || 38.421{22} ||
hā mayā labhyate nātra devīnāṃ darśanaṃ kvacit |
kiṃ mayā prakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ maṃdabhāgyo'smyahaṃ yataḥ || 38.422{23} ||
vrataṃ caritumichāmi tatkathaṃ jñāsyate vidhim |
aho me daivayogena śrutameva na darśanam || 38.423{24} ||
tadatra kiṃ kariṣyāmi ko'tra me vidhimādiśet |
yadichāmi na tatsiddhaṃ vṛthaivetye pariśramam || 38.424{25} ||
iti proktvā viṣaṇṇātmā nṛpatiḥ sa vimohitaḥ |
nirāśayā vibhinnāsyo nirjale mīnajātivat || 38.425{26} ||
hā heti ninditātmānaṃ hā me bhāgyamapīha dhik || 38.426{27!} ||
ityevaṃ vilapannaśrupūrṇāsyo gadgadaḥ svaraḥ |
hṛdi lalāṭe vāhubhyāṃ tāḍayanmūrchito muhuḥ || 38.427{28} ||
smṛtimāṃśca svayamapi tasthau tatra viniḥsvasan || 38.428{28!} ||
ityevaṃ taṃ sthitaṃ dṛṣṭvā sā śrīdevī vasuṃdharā |
ākāśānmahadāghoṣaṃ tatraivaṃ niracārayat || 38.429{29} ||
rājanmātra viṣīda tvaṃ setsyate te'bhilāṣitam |
sarvaṃ seno vijānāti tatsenaṃ pṛcha tadvidhim || 38.430{30} ||
ityākāśātsamuccāraṃ ghoṣaṃ śrutvā sa bhūpatiḥ |
sāṃjaliḥ praṇato'ṣṭauṣīdūrddhvāsyaḥ śrīvasuṃdharām || 38.431{31} ||
(rm 454)
yā susmṛtā suruciraṃ suṣṭhutaraṃ pravṛddhaṃ dāridryaduḥkhaduritaṃ śamate narāṇām |
tāṃ kalpavṛkṣasadṛśīṃ vasudhārasaṃjñāṃ bhaktyā namāmi śirasā jagato hitāya || 38.432{31!} ||
praṇamāmi sadā vasudhāṃ jananīṃ karuṇārdrahṛdaṃ parivīkṣya janam |
bahudheti bhayānvitaduḥkhataraṃ praṇamāmi sadā vasudhāṃ jananīm || 38.433{2!} ||
pāpācārasya durbuddherdurbhagasya sadā mama |
kṣaṃtavyo bhūyaśaścāgaḥ śrīvasudhe namo'stu te || 38.434{3!} ||
namaste bho mahādevi prasīda me kṛpāṃ kuru |
bhavaṃtyatra yathādiṣṭaṃ kariṣyāmi tathā khalu || 38.435{32} ||
ityuktvā sa nṛpo bhūyastatra natvā kṛtāṃjaliḥ |
taṃ senaṃ purataḥ kṛtvā tato'caratpramoditaḥ || 38.436{33} ||
tataḥ sa nṛpatirgachan sahasā muditāśayaḥ |
tatrāśokatalāsīnamaśvaṃ paśyannupācaran || 38.437{34} ||
tatra prātaḥ sa bhūyastamaśvaṃ dṛṣṭvā pramoditaḥ |
rājā taṃ senamānatvā dṛṣṭvā evamabhāṣata || 38.438{35} ||
sena tamaśvamāruhya gacha tvaṃ puramāśrame |
mama tavānugachāmi sahasā gachatu dhruvam || 38.439{36} ||
iti rājñoditaṃ śrutvā sa senaḥ sāṃjalirnaman |
nṛpatiṃ taṃ mahīpālaṃ samālokyaivamabravīt || 38.440{37} ||
naivamatra mahārāja yanme svāmi bhavān prabhuḥ |
tadbhavadvāhanaṃ hīnaṃ kathamārohayeya vai || 38.441{38} ||
iti tenoditaṃ śrutvā sūryodaya ātmavit |
rājā taṃ senamānatvā punarevamabhāṣata || 38.442{39} ||
mā tvayaivaṃ pravaktavyaṃ yadadyārabhya bhavān guruḥ |
yathākāśasamādiṣṭaṃ tattathā karttumarhasi || 38.443{40} ||
iti me vacanaṃ śrutvā mamānukaṃpayā'dhunā |
śāstaragre'śvamāruhya pravrajatāmayānvitaḥ || 38.444{41} ||
iti rājñoditaṃ śrutvā tatheti sa prabodhitaḥ |
svayamagre'śvamāruhya pṛṣṭhe prārohayennṛpam || 38.445{42} ||
tataḥ sa'śvastadārūḍho vāyuriva samuccaran |
mahāvegātpuropāṃtaṃ tatkṣaṇātsamupāyayau || 38.446{43} ||
tatra taṃ nṛpamāyātaṃ samaṃtrijanapaurikāḥ |
sarve te samupālokya pratyujjagmuḥ pramoditāḥ || 38.447{44} ||
tatrāśvapṛṣṭhamārūḍhaṃ nṛpaṃ senaṃ puraḥ sthitam |
dṛṣṭvā te maṃtriṇaḥ sarve paurāśca vismayaṃ yayuḥ || 38.448{45} ||
tataste maṃtriṇo'mātyā paurāḥ sarve pramoditāḥ |
praṇatvā taṃ nṛpaṃ rājakule'nayanmahotsavam || 38.449{46} ||
tatrāśvapṛṣṭhato rājā so'varuhya svayaṃ tataḥ |
senaṃ puraḥ sthitaṃ paścādavārohayadādarāt || 38.450{47} ||
tato rājakule gatvā nṛpaḥ pṛṣṭhataścaran |
pādaprakṣālanasthāne taṃ senamevamabravīt || 38.451{48} ||
he sene sumateratra pādaprakṣālayāgrataḥ |
(rm 455)
tvattaḥ paścādahaṃ pādau prakṣālayāmi sāṃpratam || 38.452{49} ||
iti rājñoditaṃ śrutvā sa senaḥ sāṃjalirnaman |
nṛpatiṃ taṃ mahīpālaṃ samālokyaivamabravīt || 38.453{50} ||
vibhramo'si kathaṃ rājan bhavān svāmi narādhipaḥ |
tatprathamaṃ tvamevātra pādau prakṣālaya prabho || 38.454{51} ||
iti tenoditaṃ śrutvā nṛpatiḥ sa kṛtāṃjaliḥ |
namaṃstaṃ senamālokya tatraivaṃ saṃnyavedayat || 38.455{52} ||
bho sena yadbhavāṃ chāstā mama sanmārgadeśakaḥ |
tadatra prathamaṃ pādau prakṣālayitumarhati || 38.456{53} ||
iti saṃprārthite rājñā sa senaḥ paribodhitaḥ |
rājña ājñā hyalaṃghyeti pādau prakṣālayetpuraḥ || 38.457{54} ||
tataḥ sa nṛpatiḥ pādau prakṣālyānusaranmudā |
taṃ senaṃ purataḥ kṛtvā prāsāde samupācarat || 38.458{55} ||
tatrāsane purodhāya taṃ senaṃ gurumādarāt |
nṛpatiḥ sa sabhāmadhye tasthau lokān vilokayan || 38.459{56} ||
tataḥ sa nṛpatirdṛṣṭvā sarvāllokān sabhāśritān |
sarvavṛttāntamākhyāya vyanodayadvratotsave || 38.460{57} ||
tannṛpādeśitaṃ śrutvā sarve lokāḥ pramoditāḥ |
śrīdevyāstadvrataṃ dharttuṃ samaichanta dhanārthinaḥ || 38.461{58} ||
tatassarve'pi te lokā natvā taṃ senamādarāt |
nṛpatiṃ ca samālokya svasvālayaṃ yayurmudā || 38.462{59} ||
tataḥ sa nṛpatistena senena guruṇā saha |
sānaṃdaṃ prerayāmāsa dināni katicinmudā || 38.463{60} ||
tataḥ sa bhūpatī rājā tadvratasamayāgate |
sāmātyānmaṃtriṇān sarvān samāmaṃtryaivamādiśat || 38.464{16} ||
bho maṃtriṇo janāḥ sarve vratasamayamāgatam |
tasmātsarve mayā sārddhaṃ vrataṃ caritumarhatha || 38.465{62} ||
tadenaṃ senamevātra pravrajyāsadguruṃ kuru |
iti maṃtribhirākhyātaṃ śrutvā nṛpastatheti saḥ |
taṃ senaṃ śāsane bauddhe prāvrājayadyathāvidhi || 38.466{63} ||
sātha pravrajito nāmnā śrīsenagupta ityabhūt |
khikkhirīpātrabhṛnmuṇḍaḥ kāṣāyacīvarāvṛtaḥ || 38.467{64} ||
tatastadvratamutsṛjya vajradharapadāptaye |
vajrābhiṣekamāsādya vajrācāryo'bhavatsa ca || 38.468{65} ||
tataḥ sa nṛpatī rājā tadvrataṃ karttumādarāt |
śāstāraṃ taṃ samārādhya saṃprārthayedyathāvidhim || 38.469{66} ||
tataḥ śrīsenaguptaḥ sa vajrācāryo yathāvidhiḥ |
śrīdevīṃ tāṃ samārādhya pūrvasevāṃ samādadhat || 38.470{67} ||
tato bhādrapade māse dvitīyāyāṃ site tare |
prātastīrthe śubhe snātvā śuddhavastrāvṛtaḥ sudhīḥ || 38.471{68} ||
(rm 456)
prāsādopari mṛdgavyaparilipte subhūtale |
vidhivanmaṃḍalaṃ kṛtvā pratiṣṭhāpyādhivāsayet || 38.472{69} ||
tadrātrau ca samārādhya śrīdevīṃ vidhinārcayet |
dīpadhūpādibhiścāpi baliṃ datvābhyatoṣayat || 38.473{70} ||
tataḥ prātaḥ sa ācāryyastīrthe snātvā suvastrabhṛt |
madhyāhne svāsanāsīna uttarādiṅmukhasthitaḥ || 38.474{71} ||
tataḥ ca gururācāryastrisamādhisamāhitaḥ |
śrīdevīṃ sagaṇāṃ samyagāvāhya vidhinārcayet || 38.475{72} ||
tataḥ snānaviśuddhāṅgān pariśuddhāmvarāvṛtān |
vratinaḥ sanṛpān sarvān paṃcagavyairvyaśodhayat || 38.476{73} ||
tataste vratinaḥ sarve guruṃ natvā yathākramam |
nṛpatipramukhā evaṃ vrataṃ caritumāśrayat || 38.477{74} ||
tataste vratinaḥ sarve śridevīsaṃmukhasthitāḥ |
yathācārya samādiṣṭaṃ tathācaran vrataṃ mudā || 38.478{75} ||
tatra tāṃ śrīmahādevīṃ sagaṇāṃ vidhinā kramāt |
samāvāhya samabhyarcya pūjāṅgaiḥ samatoṣayan || 38.479{76} ||
japastotrādikaṃ kṛtvā kṛtvā ca pāpadeśanām |
puṇyānumodanāṃ cāpi kṛtvā ca sthitiyācanām || 38.480{77} ||
kṛtāṃjalipuṭāḥ sarve te ca kṛtvā pradakṣiṇām |
aṣṭāṅgaiḥ praṇatiṃ kṛtvā saṃprārthayan susaṃpadam || 38.481{78} ||
piṣṭakaṃ pāyasaṃ cāpi paṃcāmṛta samanvitam |
phalamūlādikaṃ cāpi samupāḍhokayanmudā || 38.482{79} ||
tato lokādhipebhyo'pi sa bhūtebhyo mahāvalim |
yathāvidhi pradatvā saṃprārthayacchrī samṛddhitām || 38.483{80} ||
tataḥ śāstā sa ācāryaḥ prārthayitvā kṣamāṃ mudā |
sarvebhyo vratilokebhyaḥ pratyekaṃ sūtramārppayet || 38.484{81} ||
tatsūtraṃ vratinaḥ sarve ādāya guruṇārppitam |
svasvahaste'bhivaṃdhitvā rakṣārthamādarāddadhuḥ || 38.485{82} ||
yā rājño mahiṣī bhāryā cūḍadevīti viśrutā |
saikā tatsūtramādāya ca vandhanevamādarāt || 38.486{83} ||
tato vratasamāpte sā svālayaṃ samupāśritā |
tadvratasūtramālokya karavaddhaṃ vyaciṃtayat || 38.487{84} ||
kiṃ mamānena sūtreṇa sarvaratnādibhūṣaṇaiḥ |
maṇḍitā yā kare vaddhamiti tyaktuṃ samaihata || 38.488{85} ||
tataḥ sā durmatī rājñī rakṣāsūtramapi svayam |
karavaddhamapi chitvā gavākṣātsahasākṣipat || 38.489{86} ||
tasminnavasare tatra nimvadevyāḥ sakhī vanāt |
svāminyai bhojanaṃ rājñai yācituṃ samupācarat || 38.490{87} ||
tatra sā tadvratotsāhaśabdaṃ śrutvā sa kautukā |
tacchabdanihitasvāntā tasthāvekānta āśritā || 38.491{88} ||
cūḍadevyā yadutkṣiptaṃ sūtraṃ vātāyanādbahiḥ |
niścaranmūrddhani tasyāḥ sakhāḥ saṃnyapataddrutam || 38.492{89} ||
sā tatsūtraṃ samādāya paśyantī kimidaṃ nviti |
vratasūtramiti jñātvā praṇatvā muditāgrahīt || 38.493{90} ||
(rm 457)
tataḥ sa preṣikā devyā bhogyamaprārthayaṃtyapi |
tatsūtrameva dhṛtvāśu nimvavanaṃ mudācaran || 38.494{91} ||
tatra sā sahasā gatvā bhartṛkāyāḥ purogatāḥ |
tatsūtraṃ samupasthāpya praṇatvaivaṃ nyavedayat || 38.495{92} ||
jayo'stu te mahādevi yadahaṃ rājamaṃdire |
tataḥ śrīvasudhārāyā vrataṃ śṛṇomi cāritam || 38.496{93} ||
cūḍadevyā svayaṃ chitvā vratasūtramidaṃ bahiḥ |
kṣiptaṃ gavākṣaraṃdhrānme mūrddhni sajati niścarat || 38.497{94} ||
dṛṣṭvā tadahamādāya vratasūtramidaṃ khalu |
iti matvātra te bhogyamayācitvā vraje drutam || 38.498{95} ||
tathāvāmapi he devi dhṛtvedaṃ vratasūtrakam |
śrīvasudhāṃ mahādevīṃ smṛtvā vrataṃ carāvahe || 38.499{96} ||
sā śrīdevī vasuṃdhārā dṛṣṭvā nau bhaktisādhanam |
svayamatra samāśritya saṃdadyādapi saṃpadam || 38.500{97} ||
yadi nau daivayogena saṃpattirnātra vidyate |
puṇyaṃ tu khalu vidyeta tataḥ syājjanma sāphalam || 38.501{98} ||
iti matvātra bho devi snātvā śuddhāmvarāvṛtau |
śrīdevīṃ manasā dhyātvā smṛtvā vrataṃ carāvahe || 38.502{99} ||
iti sakhyā samākhyātaṃ nimvadevī niśamya sā |
tathetyabhyanumoditvā tathā caritumaichata || 38.503{100} ||
tatastatheha evaṃ te snātvā śuddhāmvarāvṛte |
sanmṛdgomaya saṃlipte svagṛhe samupāśrite || 38.504{1} ||
manasā śrīmahādevīṃ vasundharāṃ samādarāt |
dhyātvā smṛtvā samādhāya saṃcarate vrataṃ mudā || 38.505{2} ||
tasminnavasare tatra sā śrīdevī vasuṃdharā |
vṛddhīrūpadharā rājakuladvāramupācarat || 38.506{3} ||
tatra sā jyāyasī vṛddhā prakīrṇaśuklamūrddhajā |
upasṛtya sakhīmekāṃ samāmaṃtryaivamabravīt || 38.507{4} ||
bhadrike tvamihāgacha cūḍadevyāḥ puro gatā |
tava mātāmahī dvare tiṣṭhatīti nivedaya || 38.508{5} ||
iti tayā samādiṣṭaṃ śrutvā ceṭī tatheti sā |
cūḍadevyāḥ purogatvā samāmaṃtryaivamabravīt || 38.509{6} ||
devī mātāmahī vṛddhā jyāyasī te ihāgatā |
bhavaṃtyo darśanaṃ kartumichantī dvāri tiṣṭhati || 38.510{7} ||
tadbhavaṃtī samāgacha tasyā darśanamādarāt |
datvā saṃbhāṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā vinodayitumarhati || 38.511{8} ||
iti tayā samākhyātaṃ cūḍadevī niśamya sā |
na me mātāmahī kā sā dhyātvetyevaṃ ca prāvadat || 38.512{9} ||
mama mātāmahī nāsti kāsāviha samāgatā |
iti proktvā tvayā dvārātsahasā preṣyatāṃ balāt || 38.513{10} ||
iti devyoditaṃ śrutvā tatheti sā sakhī gatā |
vṛddhāṃtikamupāśritya dṛṣṭvā tāmevamabravīt || 38.514{11} ||
mātāmahī na me kā ciditi devī vadatyapi |
ataḥ sā te mukhaṃ draṣṭuṃ naiveha samupāvrajet || 38.515{12} ||
(rm 458)
atastvamiha mā tiṣṭha gachātaḥ sahasā vahiḥ |
na vrajeryadi sā devī praiṣayettvāṃ balādapi || 38.516{13} ||
iti tayoditaṃ śrutvā sā vṛddhā tata utthitā |
na tiṣṭheyamiti proktvā tataḥ śanairyayau vahiḥ || 38.517{14} ||
tataḥ sā śrīmahādevī vṛddhī rūpadharā tathā |
nimvavane sthitāṃ rājñīṃ samuddharttumupācarat || 38.518{15} ||
tatra sā jyāyasī vṛddhā śanairdvāramupāśritā |
dṛṣṭvā ceṭīṃ samāhūya mṛdugiraivamabravīt || 38.519{16} ||
dārike tvamihāgacha kiṃ cinme vacanaṃ śṛṇu |
rājñā vicāraṇārthe'haṃ śanairiha samāvraje || 38.520{17} ||
etanme vacanaṃ śrutvā nimvadevyā purogatā |
vṛddhekā te mukhaṃ draṣṭumāgateti nivedaya || 38.521{18} ||
iti tayoditaṃ śrutvā sā ceṭī sādaraṃ mudā |
paśyaṃti sāṃjalirnatvā tāṃ vṛddhāmevamabravīt || 38.522{19} ||
mātāmahi samāgacha bhavaṃtyā gaditaṃ yathā |
tathā devyā niveditvā sahasātrāvrajāmyaham || 38.523{20} ||
ityuktvā sā sakhī tasyā nimvadevyāḥ puro gatā |
muditā suprasannāsyā samālokyaivamabravīt || 38.524{21} ||
devi tava vicārārthe vṛddhaikeha samāgatā |
tadbhavaṃtī samāmaṃtrya saṃmānayitumarhati || 38.525{22} ||
iti sakhyoditaṃ śrutvā nimvadevī samutthitā |
sahasā samupāgatya tāṃ vṛddhāmevamabravīt || 38.526{23} ||
svāgataṃ bhadrike jeṣṭhe mātāmahi samāvraja |
śanai dhairyaṃ samālamvya samādhāya samāsara || 38.527{24} ||
ityuktvā sā subhadrāṃśā nimvadevī samādarāt |
tāṃ vṛddhāṃ samupāmaṃtrya svāsane samupāśrayat || 38.528{25} ||
tatra sā jyāyasī vṛddhā svāsane samupāśṛtā |
tāṃ rājñīṃ sudṛśālokya suprasannaivamabravīt || 38.529{26} ||
kiṃ ve'tra carase vatse tanme vaktuṃ tvamarhasi |
kasminnarthe'bhilāṣaṃ te tavāpi me puro vada || 38.530{27} ||
iti tayā samākhyātaṃ nimvadevī niśamya sā |
tāṃ vṛddhāṃ suprasannāsyā samālokyaivamabravīt || 38.531{28} ||
kimatrāhaṃ vadiṣyāmi mandabhāgyāsmi duḥkhitā |
daridritā na me saṃpadvidyate'tra gṛhe kvacit || 38.532{29} ||
vratopahārasāmagrimapi kiṃ cinna vidyate |
tadidaṃ kadarīpatrakṛtamarghādi bhājanam || 38.533{30} ||
tanmayā śraddhayā bhaktimātreṇa śrīvasuṃdharām |
dhyātvā smṛtvā samārādhya saṃcaritamidaṃ vratam || 38.534{31} ||
vratasādhanasāmagrīsaṃpūrṇaṃ vidyate na me |
tadvidhinā vrataiḥ karttuṃ śakyate na mayā tathā || 38.535{32} ||
tanmanobhāvanāmātrapūjāṅgaiḥ śrivasuṃdharām |
mahādevīṃ samārādhya vratamevaṃ carāvahe || 38.536{33} ||
etattayā samākhyātaṃ śrutvā sā jyāyasī mudā |
taṃ rājñīṃ sasakhīṃ bhadrāṃ samālokyaivamadiśat || 38.537{34} ||
(rm 459)
siddhyatu te vrataṃ pūrṇaṃ carasvevaṃ samāhitā |
śraddhayā bhaktibhāvena smṛtvā devīṃ sadā bhaja || 38.538{35} ||
tathā sā śrīmahādevī vasulakṣmīḥ prasāditā |
parituṣṭā gṛhe te'tra sadāpi sagaṇāśnayet || 38.539{36} ||
tatastasyā mahādevyāḥ prabhāvena sadā tava |
sarvatrāpi bhavennūnaṃ maṃgalaṃ nirupadravam || 38.540{37} ||
tataste sarvasaṃpattiparipūrṇaṃ bhavedgṛhe |
tatastvaṃ svechayā dānaṃ datvārthibhyaḥ śubhe cara || 38.541{38} ||
etatpuṇyaparītā tvaṃ sarvadā sadgatīṃ gatāḥ |
pariśuddhāśayā dhīrā suśīlā śubhabhāvinī || 38.542{39} ||
sākṣādarhatpadaṃ prāptā saṃbuddhālayamāpnuyāḥ |
evaṃ vijñāya bho vatse saṃbuddhapadalabdhaye |
triratnaśaraṇaṃ kṛtvā śrīdevīṃ bhaja sarvadā || 38.543{40} ||
ityuktvā sā mahādevī tyaktvā vṛddhākṛtiṃ tathā |
dhṛtvā rūpaṃ vasuṃdharyyā tasthau sarvagaṇairvṛtā || 38.544{41} ||
tāṃ devīṃ śrīprabhāsvaṃtīṃ sagaṇān samavasthitān |
ālokya nimvadevī sā tasthau kṣaṇaṃ suvismitā || 38.545{42} ||
tataḥ svapnavibuddheva sākṣāttāṃ śrīvasuṃ dharām |
dṛṣṭvā sā sahasotthāya sāṃjali nyapatatpuraḥ || 38.546{43} ||
tataḥ sā śrīmahādevī tāṃ rajñīṃ purato natām |
dhṛtvā hastena he vatsa uttiṣṭhetyevamabravīt || 38.547{44} ||
vatse dāridryaduḥkhaṃ te naśyate sāṃprataṃ khalu |
sarvadātra gṛhe saṃpatparivṛddhā bhavedapi || 38.548{45} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ tayā devyā nimvadevī niśamya sā |
utthāya sāṃjali devīṃ prādrākṣīttāṃ vasuṃdharām || 38.549{46} ||
gṛhe cāpi mahāsaṃpatparipūrṇaṃ samaṃtataḥ |
paśyantī kimidaṃ svapnamityuktvā samalokayat || 38.550{47} ||
tataḥ sā śrivasuṃdhārā mahādevī gaṇaiḥ saha |
bhadre'nubhūyatāṃ saukhyamityuktvānudadhau tataḥ || 38.551{48} ||
tatra sā nimvadevī tāṃ śrīdevīṃ sagaṇāṃ tathā |
aṃtardhānagatāṃ dṛṣṭvā kṣaṇaṃ tasthau vimūrchitā || 38.552{49} ||
tataścaitanyamāṣādya sā rājñī paribodhitā |
tāṃ devīṃ sagaṇāṃ dhātvā smṛtvā tasthau samāhitam || 38.553{50} ||
tatastasyā nimvadevyā udyāneṣu samaṃtataḥ |
sarvapuṣpadrumā jātāstathā sarvaphaladrumāḥ || 38.554{51} ||
aṣṭāṅgaguṇasaṃpanna jalapūrṇā manoramāḥ |
padmotpalādi puṣpāḍhyāḥ puṣkariṇyaḥ samudbhavāḥ || 38.555{52} ||
gṛhaṃ cāpi manoramyaṃ prāspādaṃ divyasaṃnibham |
sarvasaṃpatti saṃpūrṇaṃ śrīdapuramivābhavat || 38.556{53} ||
tadīdṛgbhavanaṃ dṛṣṭvā tathodyānaṃ manoramam |
naṃditā nimvadevī sā tasthau divyasukhānvitā || 38.557{54} ||
tadā sa nṛpatiścūḍadevyā vrataṃ na dhāritam |
vratasūtraṃ visṛṣṭaṃ ca śrutvātiruṣitābhavat || 38.558{55} ||
(rm 460)
tataḥ so nṛpatī rājā maṃtribhiḥ paribodhitaḥ |
mahiṣīṃ tāṃ priyāṃ bhāryāṃ sametyevamabhāṣata || 38.559{56} ||
are re hetunā kena tvayā na dhāritaṃ vratam |
vratasūtraṃ kathaṃ chitvā visṛṣṭaṃ tadvadasva me || 38.560{57} ||
iti rājñoditaṃ śrutvā cūḍadevī pragarvitā |
svāminaṃsapi taṃ bhūpamanādṛtyyaivamabravīt || 38.561{58} ||
mamaitacchrīsamṛddhāsti gṛhe ratnādi saṃyutā |
kiṃ tena vratasūtreṇa vratenāpi prayojanam || 38.562{59} ||
iti tayoditaṃ śrutvā nṛpatiḥ sa prakopitaḥ |
prayatnādapi tāṃ bhāryāṃ bodhayituṃ śaśāka na || 38.563{60} ||
tataḥ sa nṛpati rājā tadvratadhāraṇotsukaḥ |
sakhīmekāṃ samāhūya pura evamabhāṣata || 38.564{61} ||
sakhī tvaṃ me vacaḥ śrutvā gacha nimvavane'dhunā |
nimvadevyāḥ puro gatvā vadasvaivaṃ samādarāt || 38.565{62} ||
svāmī sa nṛpatī rājā vrataṃ caritumichati |
vasulakṣmyāstvayā sārddhaṃ tadāśu gamyatāmiti || 38.566{63} ||
sā tatheti pratiśrutya drutaṃ nimvavane gatā |
dṛṣṭvodyānaṃ manoramyaṃ kṣaṇaṃ tasthau savismayā || 38.567{64} ||
tataḥ sā kautukākrāṃtahṛdayā samupāviśat |
tatra tadbhavanaṃ ramyaṃ dṛṣṭvā tasthau sakautukā || 38.568{65} ||
tataḥ sā suciraṃ dṛṣṭvā muditā vismayānvitā |
nimvadevyāḥ puro gatvā pādau natvaivamabravīt || 38.569{66} ||
devi tvadantike rājñā preṣitāhaṃ samāgatā |
tadbhavaṃtī prabhorājñāṃ śrotumarhati sāṃpratam || 38.570{67} ||
sa svāmī śrīvasuṃdharyyā vrataṃ carati sapriyaḥ |
cūḍadevyā vrataṃ samyakparitaṃ na pramādataḥ || 38.571{68} ||
tattvayā saha sa svāmī vrataṃ caritumichati |
tattvadāhūtaye rājñā preṣitāhaṃ tathātra hi || 38.572{69} ||
tadbhavaṃtī prabhorājñāṃ śiro dhṛtvā samādarāt |
tadvrataṃ carituṃ bharttrā sahāśvāgaṃtumarhati || 38.573{70} ||
iti tayā samākhyātaṃ nimvadevī niśamya sā |
tāṃ ceṭīṃ svāmino dūtīṃ samālokyaivamabravīt || 38.574{71} ||
sakhyatra śrīvasuṃdhārāvrataṃ mayā vidhāryate |
paśya me saṃpado jātāḥ śrīdevīsaṃprasādataḥ || 38.575{72} ||
tadahaṃ sāṃprataṃ tatra nechāmi carituṃ vratam |
tadarthāgamanenāpi prayojanaṃ na me'dhunā || 38.576{73} ||
tadahaṃ nāgamiṣyāmi tacchaitatkathitaṃ mayā |
nṛpateḥ purato gatvā vaktavyaṃ nānyathā tvayā || 38.577{74} ||
iti tayā samākhyātaṃ śrutvā ceṭī tatheti sā |
tāṃ rājñīṃ sāṃjalī natvā pratyāyayau nṛpālayam || 38.578{75} ||
tatra sā nṛpateḥ pādau praṇamya sāṃjaliḥ puraḥ |
uvācaitatpravṛttāṃtaṃ nimvadevyā yathoditam || 38.579{76} ||
tattayā kathitaṃ śrutvā sa rājā vismayāhataḥ |
(rm 461)
bhūyastāṃ ceṭikāṃ dūtīṃ samāmaṃtryaivamādiśat || 38.580{77} ||
bhūyo'pi tatra gacha tvaṃ bodhayitvā prayatnataḥ |
nimvadevīṃ samāhūya sahasānetumarhati || 38.581{78} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ narendreṇa śrutvā sā ceṭikāpi ca |
nṛpatiṃ taṃ mahārājaṃ praṇatvaivaṃ nyavedayat || 38.582{79} ||
kiṃ tatrāhaṃ gamisyāmi nimvadevīha māvrajet |
yatsā tatra mahāsaukhyaṃ bhuktvā tiṣṭhati sāṃpratam || 38.583{80} ||
yattasyā bhavanaṃ ramyaṃ prāsādaṃ bhavate'dhunā |
sarvasaṃpattisaṃpūrṇaṃ śrīdasyeva kimucyate || 38.584{81} ||
udyāne'pi manoramyāḥ puṣkariṇyaḥ samudbhavāḥ |
nānāvṛkṣāśca saṃjātāḥ phalapuṣpasamanvitāḥ || 38.585{82} ||
kimatra vahimoktena divyaśrī saṃpadānvitam |
yadi tadichasi draṣṭuṃ tatra gatvābhilokaya || 38.586{83} ||
evaṃ tayoditaṃ śrutvā sa rājā tṛṣitānvitaḥ |
tathā tadbhavanaṃ draṣṭuṃ sahasā gaṃtumaichata || 38.587{84} ||
tataḥ sa nṛpatī rājā sakhimaṃtrijanaiḥ saha |
yānārūḍho vrajanstatra nimvavane upāsarat || 38.588{85} ||
tatrodyānaṃ manoramyaṃ sarvavṛkṣāḥ samanvitam |
puṣkariṇīśca dūrātsa rājādrākṣīnmanoharāḥ || 38.589{86} ||
bhavanaṃ ca manoramyaṃ prāsādaṃ divyakalpitam |
dṛṣṭvā sa mudito rājā sa śīghraṃ dvārāntike yayau || 38.590{87} ||
tannṛpaṃ samupāyātaṃ dṛṣṭvā devyāḥ sakhī drutam |
purataḥ sahasopetya samāmaṃtryaivamabravīt || 38.591{88} ||
devī prasīda yadbharttā svāmīha svayamāgataḥ |
tadbhavaṃtī prasannāsyā darśanaṃ dātumarhati || 38.592{89} ||
iti sakhyoditaṃ śrutvā nimvadevī praharṣitā |
sahasotthāya paryaṅkaṃ prājñapayatsukomalam || 38.593{90} ||
tatra sa bhūpatī rājā praviśya maṃdire caran |
tāṃ devīṃ samupāmaṃtrya prayayau sahite janaiḥ || 38.594{91} ||
tatra taṃ prabhumāyātaṃ nimvadevī samīkṣya sā |
sahasotthāya paśyaṃtī nanāma caraṇe prabhoḥ || 38.595{92} ||
tatra sa prabhurālokya tāṃ bhāryāṃ śrīsamāṃśikām |
sarvatra kuśalaṃ praśnaṃ kṛtvāsane samāśrayat || 38.596{93} ||
tatra tāṃ śrīsamākāraṃ nimvadevīṃ nirīkṣya saḥ |
rājā prabhuḥ svayaṃ dhṛtvā sahāsane nyaveśayat || 38.597{94} ||
tatastau daṃpatī tatra sahāsane samāśritau |
parasparaṃ samāśritya niṣedatuḥ pramoditau || 38.598{95} ||
tataḥ sa nṛpatī svāmī sakautukapramoditaḥ |
tāṃ bhāryāṃ śrīsamānāṃśaṃ dṛṣṭvaivaṃ paryapṛchata || 38.599{96} ||
kathaṃ te bhavanaṃ ramyaṃ prāsādaṃ bhavane'dhunā |
udyānaṃ ca manoramyaṃ jāyate tadvadasva me || 38.600{97} ||
iti pṛṣṭe nareṃdreṇa nimvadevī vinoditā |
taṃ prabhuṃ sāṃjalirnatvā samālokyaivamabravīt || 38.601{98} ||
(rm 462)
śṛṇu svāminmahārāja yadi śrotuṃ tvamichasi |
tathāhaṃ kathayiṣyāmi yanme saṃpatprajāyate || 38.602{99} ||
pratyahaṃ yattvayā dattaṃ tadbhogyaṃ yācituṃ sakhī |
amuṣmin divase tatra mayeyaṃ preṣitācarat || 38.603{100} ||
tatreyaṃ sahasā gatvā prāsādaikāṃtamāśritā |
tadā vātāyanātkṣiptaṃ vratasūtramadho'patat || 38.604{1} ||
taddṛṣṭvaivaṃ sakhī gṛhya kimidamiti vīkṣya sā |
vismitā sahasāgatvā mamāgre samadarśayat || 38.605{2} ||
taddṛṣṭvāyaṃ mayā pṛṣṭā tyaktvāhāramidaṃ kutaḥ |
sūtramādāya praiṣi tvaṃ tatkathamucyatāmiti || 38.606{3} ||
tatheyaṃ paripṛṣṭā me vistareṇaivamabravīt |
svāmini yadi te śrotuṃ vāṃchāsti śrūyatāmiti || 38.607{4} ||
pratyahaṃ svāmināhāraṃ dattaṃ tadyācituṃ tadā |
tatra rājakule devyā vratotsāhaṃ śṛṇomyaham || 38.608{5} ||
tadvrataśabdamākarṇya tatraikāṃte samāśritā |
tadā vātāyanātkṣiptaṃ sūtraṃ patati me mama || 38.609{6} ||
tadidaṃ sūtramālokyamiti vismayānvitā |ālokya kimiti?
tyaktvāhāramidaṃ sūtramādāya sahasā vraje || 38.610{7} ||
ityuktveyaṃ sakhī me'gre upasthāyāpyadarśayat |
vratasūtraṃ tadālokya natvāgṛhnānmudādarāt || 38.611{8} ||
tataśceyaṃ sakhi dṛṣṭvā premnā hitārthavāṃchinī |
mātevānunayaṃ kṛtvā mamāgre evamabravīt || 38.612{9} ||
bhavaṃtyapi tathā śraddhābhaktimātreṇa sarvadā |
śrīdevīṃ manasā dhyātvā smṛtvārādhya vrataṃ cara || 38.613{10} ||
ityasyā vacanaṃ śrutvā tathāhaṃ bhaktimātrataḥ |
śrīdevīṃ manasā dhyātvā smṛtvārādhyācaraṃ vratam || 38.614{11} ||
tadaikā jyāyasī vṛddhā mātāmahī tavāgatā |
ityākhyāya śanairatra mamāgre samupāgatā || 38.615{12} ||
mayāpi sādareṇātra natvāsane niveśitā |
tataśca māṃ samālokya kuśalaṃ samapṛchata || 38.616{13} ||
tataścāsau parijñāya mayātra vratasādhanam |
kiṃ vrataṃ sadhyate vatsa iti māṃ paryapṛchata || 38.617{14} ||
tatastāṃ sāṃjalirnatvā nyavedayan samādarāt |
mātāmahi nu me kiṃ cidapi dravyaṃ na vidyate || 38.618{15} ||
tadahaṃ śraddhayā bhaktibhāvamātreṇa sādaram |
śrīdevīṃ manasā dhyātvā smṛtvārādhya vrataṃ care || 38.619{16} ||
iti mayoditaṃ śrutvā sā vṛddhā parimoditā |
tadvratavidhipuṇyāni yathā kramamupādiśat || 38.620{17} ||
tadupādiṣṭamākarṇya muditā saṃpramoditā |
sāṃjalistanpadābje'haṃ natvā tiṣṭhamadhomukhaḥ || 38.621{18} ||
tadā sā tajjyāyasī rūpaṃ tyaktvātra ca kṣaṇādapi |
śrīdevīmūrttimādhāya sagaṇāṃ samadarśayat || 38.622{19} ||
(rm 463)
ato'haṃ sahasotthāya dṛṣṭvā tāṃ śrīvasuṃdharām |
gaṇavṛtāṃ samālokya yathāśakti samarcyam || 38.623{20} ||
tataḥ sā śrīmahadevī madbhaktiparitoṣitā |
sagaṇā me varaṃ datvā vyavadhānamito'vrajat || 38.624{21} ||
tatastasyā mahālakṣmyā kṛpādṛṣṭiprasādataḥ |
idaṃ me bhavanaṃ ramyaṃ prāsādaṃ bhavate kṣaṇāt || 38.625{22} ||
udyānaṃ ca manoramyaṃ nānāpuṣpaphaladrumaiḥ |
padmotpalādi puṣpāḍhyāḥ puṣkariṇyo codbhavāḥ || 38.626{23} ||
sadhāturatnajātādi sarvadravyābhipūritam |
koṣṭhāgāraṃ samudbhūtaṃ savastrālaṃkārapūritam || 38.627{24} ||
sarvavrīhisamāpūrṇaṃ ṣaḍraradravyapūritam |
ghṛtadadhimadhukṣīramodakādiprapūritam || 38.628{25} ||
vividhapānabhojyāni paṭṭapuṣpāsanāni ca |
phalapuṣpādi bhogyāni saṃjāyaṃte kṣaṇādiha || 38.629{26} ||
īdṛśī mahatī saṃpatsamudbhūtā mama kṣaṇāt |
tatsarvaṃ śrīmahādevyāḥ prasādādapi nānyathā || 38.630{27} ||
iti vijñāya rājendra tasyā devyāḥ sadā mudā |
śraddhayā śaraṇaṃ gatvā vrataṃ caritumarhati || 38.631{28} ||
etatpuṇyānubhāvena sarvadā sadgatīṃ vrajeḥ |
yāvadbhavaṃ vrajernaiva durgatīṃ ca kadā cana || 38.632{29} ||
sadgatau satsukhānyeva labdho sadā śubhe caran |
triratnabhajanaṃ kṛtvā bodhicaryāṃ caretkramāt || 38.633{30} ||
tataḥ pāramitāḥ sarvāḥ pūrayitvā yathākramāt |
svayaṃ caraṃ parāṃ cāpi bodhimārge niyojayet || 38.634{31} ||
tato māragaṇān sarvāṃ jitvā śuddhatrimaṃḍalaḥ |
arhan saṃbodhimāsādya saṃbuddhapadamāpnuyāt || 38.635{32} ||
iti tayā mahādevyāḥ samupādiṣṭamatra me |
tacchrutvā sarvadā svāmi mano'tirocate vratam || 38.636{33} ||
tadahaṃ sarvadā tasyāḥ śrīdevyāḥ śaraṇaṃ gatā |
śraddhayā bhaktibhāvena cariṣyāmi vrataṃ prabho || 38.637{34} ||
tathā bhavānapi svamiñchrīdevyāḥ śaraṇaṃ gataḥ |
śraddhābhaktiprasannātmā vrataṃ caritumarhati || 38.638{35} ||
iti bhāryoditaṃ śrutvā sa rājābhyanumoditaḥ |
tathā sadā mahādevyā vrataṃ caritumaichata || 38.639{36} ||
tatraivaṃ sa mahīpālo nimvadevyā tayā saha |
yathākāmaṃ sukhaṃ bhuktvā tasthau krīḍan yathechayā || 38.640{37} ||
evaṃ krīḍan sa rājendro nimvadevyā sahārataḥ |
tatrānaṃdasukhotsāhairnyuvāsa rājyanispṛtaḥ || 38.641{38} ||
tatraivaṃ taṃ mahīpālaṃ nimvadevyā saha sthitam |
cūḍadevī samākarṇya cukopātipraroṣitā || 38.642{39} ||
tataḥ sā cūḍadevī taṃ svāminaṃ tatra saṃsthitam |
svayaṃ gatvā samākramya saṃmānayitumaichata || 38.643{40} ||
tataḥ sā krodhitā caṇḍī cūḍadevī pradhāvitā |
vrajaṃtī sahasā tatra nimvavana upāsarat || 38.644{41} ||
(rm 464)
tatra tadvratanirmālyaprakṣiptaḥ puṣpasaṃkulam |
sthānaṃ sātiprakopāṃdhā laṃghitvā sahasācarat || 38.645{42} ||
tallaṃghitātipāpena sā devī tatkṣaṇādapi |
kolamukhī mahāghorarūpāvichaṃditā bhavat || 38.646{43} ||
tatra tāṃ sahasāyātāṃ lokān dṛṣṭvā bhayotthitāḥ |
kolamukhī praviṣṭeti śabdaṃ kṛtvā vicerire || 38.647{44} ||
tadvirāvaṃ janāḥ sarve śrutvā tatra samutthitāḥ |
kolāhalamahāśabdaṃ prakurvanta upādruvan || 38.648{45} ||
tatkolāhalaṃ śabdaṃ śrutvā saṃtrasitāśayā |
parāyitvā tato'nyatra pradudrāva vanāṃtare || 38.649{46} ||
tatraikā vibhramaṃtī sā daivasaṃpreritā vane |
nirjane durgame'raṇye parikhinnāpyadhāvata || 38.650{47} ||
tathā sā dūrato gatvā prādrākṣītsarasī dvayam |
dṛṣṭvā sā tatra tṛṣṇārttā jalaṃ pātumupācarat || 38.651{48} ||
tadā te sarasī yuddhaṃ parasparaṃ pracakratuḥ |
dṛṣṭvā sā vismitā'pītvā jalaṃ tasthau savismayā || 38.652{49} ||
tatra te sarasī dṛṣṭvā tāṃ kolavadanāṃ striyam |
kutra gaṃtumihaiṣi tvamityevaṃ paryapṛchatām || 38.653{50} ||
tatpṛṣṭaṃ sā samākarṇya sā śaṃkavismayāhatā |
kimetaditi saṃciṃtya kṣaṇaṃ tasthau vimohitā || 38.654{51} ||
tatra sā daivayogena pratilabhya sucetanām |
kiṃ mayātrāpi vaktavyamiti dhyātvā vyaciṃtayat || 38.655{52} ||
hā kathamiha prāptāhaṃ bhramāmi nirjane vane |
kiṃ mayā prakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ yena bhraṣṭā carāmyaham || 38.656{53} ||
hā kiṃ kuryāṃ kva gacheyaṃ sahāyaikāpi me'sti na |
sarvathātra vinaṣṭāsmi ko māṃ rakṣedihā'dhunā || 38.657{54} ||
iti ciṃtāviṣaṇṇā sā tasthau tatra nirāśitā |
tataḥ sā daivayogena sasmāra śrīvasuṃdharām || 38.658{55} ||
taddevīsmṛtipuṇyena sā karmabhāvinī sudhīḥ |
yatsvayaṃ prakṛtaṃ karma tatsarvaṃ paryyabudhyatām || 38.659{56} ||
hā mayā durdhiyā tatra samyanna dhāritaṃ vratam |
etatpāpena nūnaṃ me jāte'yamīdṛśī vipat || 38.660{57} ||
tadatra kiṃ kariṣyāmi kolamukhī yato'smyaham |
tattatra kathamevaṃ hi pratiyāyaṃ nṛpālayam || 38.661{58} ||
yaccāhaṃ durmatirduṣṭā svakṛtapāpabhāginī |
taddaivapreṣitā hyatra bhramāmi nirjane vane || 38.662{59} ||
tadatra kiṃ careyāhaṃ jīvite'pi na me ruciḥ |
sarveṣāmapi jaṃtūnāṃ sarvatra maraṇaṃ dhruvam || 38.663{60} ||
iti matvāhamatrāpi nirapekṣaḥ svajīvite |
śrīdevyā darśanaṃ karttuṃ smṛtvā gacheya sāṃpratam || 38.664{61} ||
iti niścitya sā rājñī cūḍadevī samudyatā |
śrīdevyā darśanaṃ kartuṃ smṛtvā caritumaichata || 38.665{62} ||
tataḥ sā cūḍadevī te sarasī dve samīkṣya ca |
(rm 465)
upetya tajjalaṃ pītvā tīre sthitvaivamabravīt || 38.666{63} ||
sarasī yadahaṃ mūḍhā durmatī maṃdabhāginī |
tacchrīvasuṃdharāṃ devīṃ draṣṭumihāvrajāmi hi || 38.667{64} ||
iti tayoditaṃ śrutvā puṣkariṇyāvubhe'pi te |
tāṃ rājñīṃ samupāmaṃtrya nṛgiraivamabhāṣatām || 38.668{65} ||
sādhu sādhu mahābhāge vrajāstu maṃgalaṃ tava |
puna nau vacasā devīmevaṃ pṛcha tvamādarāt || 38.669{7} ||
kenāvāṃ hetunā yuddhaṃ kṛtvā tiṣṭhāvahe sadā || 38.670{8} ||
ityetatkathitaṃ tābhyāṃ śrutvā rājñī tatheti sā |
pratijñāya tatrānyatra svadaivapreṣitācarat || 38.671{9} ||
tatrāraṇye bhramaṃtī sā kiṃ ciddūraṃ gatā tataḥ |
mahāṃtaṃ śūkaraṃ ghoraṃ dadarśa bhīṣaṇākṛtim || 38.672{10} ||
tatra sā dūrataḥ sthitvā paśyaṃstaṃ bhīmaśūkaram |
mātrā tiṣṭha vrajānyatra sahaseti tamabravīt || 38.673{11} ||
iti tenoditaṃ śrutvā sa śūkaraḥ samutthitaḥ |
sahasopetya tāṃ rājñīṃ samālokyaivamabravīt || 38.674{12} ||
kā tvaṃ kuta ihāyāsi kutra gaṃtuṃ pradhāvasi |
ekākinī kathaṃ bhrāṃto vana etadvadasva me || 38.675{13} ||
iti tenoditaṃ śrutvā sā devī praticitritā |
śanairupetya taṃ pretaṃ saṃpaśyannevamabravīt || 38.676{14} ||
sūryodayanarendrasya bhāryāhaṃ mahiṣī priyā |
śrīdevyā darśanaṃ kartuṃ vāṃchaṃtīha samāvraje || 38.677{15} ||
iti tadgaditaṃ śrutvā śūkaraḥ so'tivismitaḥ |
tāṃ devīṃ samupāśritya paśyannevamabhāṣata || 38.678{16} ||
sādhu sādhvi mahābhāge vrajāstu te sumaṃgalam |
tāṃ śrībhagavatīdevīṃ mannāmnā praṇamādarāt || 38.679{17} ||
bhūyo me vacasā sādhvī tāṃ devīṃ paripṛchatām |
kenāhaṃ śūkarībhūtastiṣṭhāmīti bhavaṃtyapi || 38.680{18} ||
etattatkathitaṃ śrutvā cūḍadevī tatheti sā |
pratijñāya tato'nyatra vrajaṃtī sahasācarat || 38.681{19} ||
tatra sūcīmukhapretamasthikāyaṃ mahodaram |
svakeśaromasaṃchannamapaśyaddūrato vane || 38.682{20} ||
tatra sa preta ālokya tāṃ devīṃ samupāgatām |
sahasopetya saṃpaśyan purastha evamabravīt || 38.683{21} ||
ko tvaṃ kuta ihāyāsi kutra gaṃtumihāgatā |
niḥsahāyā kathaṃ bhrāṃtā carate tadvadasva me || 38.684{22} ||
iti tatkathitaṃ śrutvā sā devī vismayānvitā |
śanairupetya taṃ pretaṃ paśyaṃtyevamavocata || 38.685{23} ||
sūryodayamahībharttu bhāryāhaṃ mahiṣī satī |
śrīdevyā darśane gaṃtuṃ pathānenāvrajāmi hi || 38.686{24} ||
iti taduditaṃ śrutvā sa preto'tivicitritaḥ |
tāṃ devīṃ sāṃjalirnatvā pura evamavocata || 38.687{25} ||
sādhu sādhvi mahābhāge carāstu te subhadrakam |
tāṃ śrībhagavatīlakṣmīṃ mannāmnā praṇamādarāt || 38.688{26} ||
bhūyo'pi vacasā me tāṃ śrīdevīṃ paripṛchatu |
(rm 466)
kenāhaṃ pāpakenaivaṃ tiṣṭhāmi duḥkhabhāginī || 38.689{27} ||
etattatkathitaṃ śrutvā cūḍadevī tatheti sā |
pratiśrutya gatānyatra prādrākṣīddaśapāpinaḥ || 38.690{28} ||
te'pi tāṃ samupāyātāṃ dṛṣṭvopetyevamabruvan |
kā tvaṃ kuta ihāyāsi kutra gaṃtuṃ ca tadvada || 38.691{29} ||
iti tairgaditaṃ śrutvā sā devī samupāsthitā |
tān sarvān pāpino dṛṣṭvā pura evamabhāṣata || 38.692{30} ||
sūryodayanarendrasya bhāryāhaṃ mahiṣī priyā |
śrīdevyā darśane gaṃtuṃ pathānenāvrajāmyaham || 38.693{31} ||
iti tadgaditaṃ śrutvā sarve te daśapāpinaḥ |
kṛtāṃjalipuṭā natvā tāṃ devīmevamabruvan || 38.694{32} ||
sādhu devī mahābhāge pravrajāstu jayaṃ tava |
asmākaṃ prakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ tāṃ devīṃ paripṛchatu || 38.695{33} ||
iti taddeśitaṃ śrutvā cūḍadevī tatheti sā |
tato'nyatra vrajaṃtyevaṃ dadarśa kṛṣṇapaṃnagam || 38.696{34} ||
tatra sa kṛṣṇasarpastāmālokya samupāgatām |
sahasā samupākramya viṣāgninātyatāpayat || 38.697{35} ||
tataḥ sā mūrchitā devī cirātsvayaṃ samutthitā |
tāṃ śrīvasuṃdharāṃ smṛtvā tatra tasthau kṣaṇaṃ tathā || 38.698{36} ||
tataḥ sā suṃdarīrūpā tyaktakolamukhābhavat |
kālasarpaḥ sa tāmāha kā tvaṃ kutaḥ samāgatā || 38.699{37} ||
cūḍadevī tataḥ prāha sūryodayapriyāsmyaham |
śrīdevīdarśanārthaṃ tu āgatā mānyatha phaṇi || 38.700{38} ||
tataḥ pātakamuktā sā pracaraṃtī bubhukṣitā |
bījapūrakamālokya tadādātumupācarat || 38.701{39} ||
tatra tatphalamālokya hastābhyāṃ sahasāgrahīt |
tasya hi daivayogena tatphalaṃ na vyamuṃcyata || 38.702{40} ||
tatra sā tatphale lagnahastā tasthau tathā kṣaṇam |
tataḥ sā trasitā dṛṣṭvā na gṛhnāmīti cāvadat || 38.703{41} ||
etadukte bhujau tasyāstatphalaṃ sahasātyajat |
tataḥ sā vismitānyra bhramaṃtī dūrato'vrajat || 38.704{42} ||
tataḥ sā dūrato nadyāḥ śabdaṃ śrutvā pradhāvitā |
svadaivapreṣitā tatra tīramavāpatatkṣaṇāt || 38.705{43} ||
tatra sā tajjalaṃ pātuṃ sahasā samupācarat |
tatra sā tajjalaṃ pītvā snātvā caiva samāśrayat || 38.706{44} ||
tatra sā dūrataḥ pūrṇahemaghaṭaśirovahāḥ |
surāṃgaṇāḥ subhadrāṃgā dadarśa samupāgatāḥ || 38.707{45} ||
tatra sā tāḥ samālokya sahasopetya sāṃjaliḥ |
praṇatvā samupāsīnā samīkṣyaivamapṛchata || 38.708{46} ||
aho bhāgyaṃ mayā labdhaṃ yadbhavatyātra darśitā |
taddṛṣṭvā kṛpayā sarvā snātumarhanti sarvathā || 38.709{47} ||
bhavaṃtyo'mṛtasaṃpūrṇahaimaghaṭaśirovahāḥ |
kutra gaṃtuṃ caraṃtyatra satyametadvadaṃtu me || 38.710{48} ||
iti tayoditaṃ śrutvā tāsāmekā pradhānikā |
sālokya cūḍadevīṃ tāṃ samāmaṃtryaivamabravīt || 38.711{49} ||
(rm 467)
bhadre tvaṃ na jānāsi yā śrīdevī vasuṃdharāḥ |
tasyā ime'mṛtā pūrṇā nīyaṃte snapane ghaṭāḥ || 38.712{50} ||
devīsnānodakaiḥ sarvaireṣā nadī vahatyapi |
etannadyamṛtenātra netre prakṣālya dṛśyatām || 38.713{51} ||
evaṃ devyā tayādiṣṭaṃ cūḍadevī niśamya sā |
tathetyabhyanumodaṃtī tathā kartuṃ samaichata || 38.714{52} ||
tataḥ sā tannadītīre sthitvāmṛtena cakṣuṣī |
saṃprakṣālya tataḥ sasne papau tadamṛtaṃ mudā || 38.715{53} ||
tato nirmuktapāpā sā cūḍadevī pavitritā |
sudṛṣṭirabhavattatra samaṃtāpratihatekṣaṇaḥ || 38.716{54} ||
tatrasthā cūḍadevī sā prādrākṣīttatkṣaṇādapi |
śrīvasudhāṃ mahādevīṃ sagaṇāṃ maṇḍalāsthitām || 38.717{55} ||
tatra tāṃ śrīmahādevīṃ dṛṣṭvā sā pratimaṃḍitā |
sahasā sāṃjalirnatvā paśyaṃtī samupācarat || 38.718{56} ||
tatra sā samupetyāśu kṛtvā pradakṣiṇāṃ mudā |
sagaṇāṃ sa mahādevīṃ praṇanāma kṛtāṃjaliḥ || 38.719{57} ||
tataḥ sā śrīmahādevī cūḍadevīṃ samīksya tām |
svāgatāsi samehi tvamityevaṃ samabhāṣata || 38.720{58} ||
evaṃ devyā tayādiṣṭaṃ cūḍadevī niśamya sā |
sāṃjaliraśru mocaṃtī devyāḥ pādāmbuje'patat || 38.721{59} ||
tataḥ sā śrīvasuṃdhārā mahādevī vilokya tām |
pādābje patitāṃ dhṛtvā samāśvāsyaivamādiśat || 38.722{60} ||
bhāgini bhadra uttiṣṭha dhairyamālaṃvya paśyatām |
mā viṣīda prasīdātra pūrayāmi tavaihitam || 38.723{61} ||
etattayā mahādevyā samādiṣṭaṃ niśamya sā |
cūḍadevī prasannāsyā praṇatvaivaṃ nyavedayat || 38.724{62} ||
bhagavati jagadbhartti vasudhāre maheśvari |
bhavatyāḥ śaraṇaṃ yāmi kṣamasva me'parādhatām || 38.725{63} ||
prasīda me mahādevi kṛpānugrahatāṃ kuru |
yanmayā prakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ tatsarvaṃ kṣaṃtumarhati || 38.726{64} ||
yadetaddūrato cāhaṃ samāyātāsmyavighnataḥ |
tadbhavatyāḥ prasādena nānyatheti hi manyate || 38.727{65} ||
tadiha te mahādevyā śraddhayā śaraṇāsthitā |
vrataṃ caritumichāmi tatprasīda kṛpānidhe || 38.728{66} ||
iti tayārthitaṃ śrutvā sā śrīdevī vasuṃdharā |
tāṃ śraddhābhisaṃśuddhāṃ samālokyaivamādiśat || 38.729{67} ||
sādhu vatse punargatvā svapure svāminā saha |
ācāryeṇa yathādiṣṭaṃ vrataṃ kuru samāhita || 38.730{68} ||
tadā tatra gṛhe te'haṃ svayamāgatya saṃsthitā |
kariṣyāmi sadā bhadraṃ sarvatra nirupadravam || 38.731{69} ||
tadā te sarvadā gehe dehe ca maṃgalaṃ bhavet |
tataḥ sarvārthasaṃpattiḥ parivṛddhāḥ susaṃsthitā || 38.732{70} ||
tatastadarthamādāya dehe'rthibhyo yathepsitam |
triratnabhajanaṃ kṛtvā carasva sarvadā śubhe || 38.733{71} ||
(rm 468)
tathā saṃpārapūrya tvaṃ sarvāḥ pāramitāḥ kramāt |
trividhāṃ bodhimāsādya saṃbuddhapadamāpsyasi || 38.734{72} ||
etaddevyā samādiṣṭaṃ cūḍadevī niśamya sā |
tathetyabhyanumodaṃtī pratyaśroṣītpraṇāmitā || 38.735{73} ||
tataḥ sā cūḍadevī ca tāṃ śrīdevīṃ vasuṃdharām |
suprasannā samānamya samīkṣyaivaṃ nyavedayat || 38.736{74} ||
yanmārgasthitairlokaiḥ saṃdarśitaṃ vaco'sti me |
dadehaṃ praṣṭumichāmi bhagavati samādiśa || 38.737{75} ||
mārge'haṃ sarasī dṛṣṭvā jalaṃ pātumupācaram |
tadā te sarasī yuddhaṃ prākurvatāṃ parasparam || 38.738{76} ||
tataste vigrahāṃte māṃ dṛṣṭvaivaṃ paryapṛchatām |
kutra gaṃtumihāyāsi tatsatyaṃ kathyatāmiti || 38.739{77} ||
tacchrutvā vismayākrāṃtahṛdayāhaṃ vilokya te |
śrīdevīdarśanaṃ gaṃtumihaimītyavadamapi || 38.740{78} ||
evaṃ mayoditaṃ śrutvā sarasī ta ubhe'pi ca |
māṃ sa kṛtāṃjalirnatvā samīkṣyaivamabhāṣatām || 38.741{79} ||
yadāvāṃ vigrahaṃ nityaṃ kṛtvā tiṣṭhāvahe mithaḥ |
tanno pāpena keneti śrīdevīṃ paripṛchatu || 38.742{80} ||
kiṃ tābhyāṃ prakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ yadevaṃ vigrahaṃ mithaḥ |
kṛtvā te tiṣṭhato devi tatsamādeṣṭumarhati || 38.743{81} ||
iti tayoditaṃ śrutvā sā śrīdevī vasuṃdharā |
samīkṣya cūḍadevīṃ tāṃ samāmaṃtryaivamādiśat || 38.744{82} ||
bhaginyau tau purābhūtaṃ bhojane samaye sadā |
ākruṣya vigrahaṃ kṛtvā pratikṣepaṃ pracakratuḥ || 38.745{83} ||
tena pāpena tau dve'pi sarasī bhūtvā parasparam |
sarvadā vigrahaṃ kṛtvā tiṣṭhataḥ sāṃprataṃ tathā || 38.746{84} ||
evaṃ tayoḥ puraḥ proktvā citte tvaṃ paribodhaya |
etacchrutvaiva te vākyaṃ pāpānmuktvai cyato divam || 38.747{85} ||
tato'sau cūḍadevī ca natvā tāṃ śrīvasuṃdharām |
sāṃjaliḥ suprasannātmā samīkṣyevaṃ nyavedayat || 38.748{86} ||
bhūyo'pi śrīmahādevi śūkaro dṛśyate pathi |
so'pi māṃ samupāyātaṃ dṛṣṭvaivaṃ paryapṛchata || 38.749{87} ||
kutra gaṃtumihāyāsi vadasveti niśamya tat |
śrīdevīdarśane gaṃtumihaimītyavadamaham || 38.750{88} ||
tataḥ sa śūkaraścāhaṃ prarudatpurāto mama |
kiṃ mayā prakṛtaṃ pāpamiti devyabhipṛchyatām || 38.751{89} ||
kiṃ tena prakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ purā kutra kadā katham |
tatsarvaṃ samupādiśya prabodhayatu me manaḥ || 38.752{90} ||
iti tatpṛchitaṃ śrutvā sā śrīdevī vasuṃdharā |
samīkṣya cūḍadevīṃ tāṃ samāmaṃtryaivamabravīt || 38.753{91} ||
bhaṇḍāgārī purābhūtsa viśvāsaghātakaḥ kudhīḥ |
janebhyo bhojanaṃ kiṃ ciddatvā sarvaṃ gṛhe'nayat || 38.754{92} ||
(rm 469)
etatpāpavipākena bhavati śūkaro'dhunā |
etatmayā yathākhyātaṃ tathā tasya puro vada || 38.755{93} ||
sa tvatkathitamākarṇya pāpamukto divaṃ vrajet || 38.756{94} ||
iti devyā samādiṣṭaṃ cūḍadevī niśamya sā |
tatheti prativijñapya punarevaṃ nyavedayat || 38.757{95} ||
punarmārge mayā dṛṣṭaḥ pretaḥ sūcīmukho mahān |
kiṃ tena prakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ tava devi samādiśa || 38.758{96} ||
iti tatkathitaṃ śrutvā sā śrīdevī maheśvarī |
samīkṣya cūḍadevīṃ tāṃ samāmaṃtryaivamabravīt || 38.759{97} ||
purā sa brāhmaṇo vidvān vedavidvādasaṃrataḥ |
siddhāṃtavādino bauddhān pratikṣipyābhiniṃdayan || 38.760{98} ||
lobherṣyāmadamātsaryaparītātmā hi mānikaḥ |
āgṛhītapariṣkāraḥ svārthātilālasaḥ kudhīḥ || 38.761{99} ||
parebhyo dattamālokya lobhātikaluṣāśayaḥ |
sa chidravacasākruṣya bauddhe dānaṃ nyavārayat || 38.762{100} ||
saddharmaśrāvakān sarvān dṛṣṭvā sa duritāśayaḥ |
praduṣṭavacasākṣipya niṃdayan pratyavārayat || 38.763{1} ||
kiṃ tena na kṛtaṃ pāpaṃ saddharmavighnakāriṇā |
triratnabhajanotsāhavighātābhyanurāginā || 38.764{2} ||
etatpāpavipākena brāhmaṇaḥ sa durāśayaḥ |
sūcīmukho mahākāyaḥ preto bhavati sāṃpratam || 38.765{3} ||
etanmayā yathākhyātaṃ tathā tasya puro vada |
etatte kathitaṃ śrutvā smṛtimān sa sudhīrbhavet || 38.766{4} ||
tataḥ sa svakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ sarvaṃ smṛtvādiśan svayam |
triratnasmaraṇaṃ kṛtvā samicheccarituṃ śubhe || 38.767{5} ||
tato vimuktapāpaḥ sa pariśuddhāśayaḥ sudhīḥ |
namo buddhāya dharmāya saṃghāyeti vadan bhajet || 38.768{6} ||
tatastatpretalokātsa samutthāya vikalpakaḥ |
pretāśayaṃ parityajya tato'pi hi divaṃ vrajet || 38.769{7} ||
iti tasyā mahādevyā samādiṣṭaṃ niśamya sā |
cūḍadevī prasannāsyā punarevaṃ nyavedayat || 38.770{8} ||
bhūyo'pi pathi bho devi dṛśyate duḥkhitā mayā |
kiṃ kiṃ taiḥ prakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ tatsarvaṃ samupādiśa || 38.771{9} ||
iti saṃprārthite rājñyā sā śrīdevī vasuṃdharā |
niśamya cūḍadevīṃ tāṃ samīkṣyaivamupādiśat || 38.772{10} ||
te sarve pāpino duṣṭā daśākuśalacāriṇaḥ |
adyāpi duḥkhasaṃtaptā bhramaṃti bhavasāgare || 38.773{11} ||
yo hatvā prāṇino bhuṃkta so'lpāyuṣko bhave bhraman |
adyāpi tadvipākena tiṣṭhatyevaṃ suduḥkhitaḥ || 38.774{12} ||
yo'dattaṃ dravyamādāya prābhuṃkta mithyayāharan |
sa daridro mahāduṣṭo bhavatyadyāpi duḥkhabhāk || 38.775{13} ||
(rm 470)
yo mithyākāmarāgāṃdho'gamyā api prabhuktavān |
sa sveṣṭasujanaistyakto bhavatyarivaśe sthitaḥ || 38.776{14} ||
mṛṣāvādena yo lokañjayannabhyavaṃcayat |
sa kuṣṭharogavān pūtigaṃdhavaho bhavatyapi || 38.777{15} ||
piśunavacanai yo sanmitrabhedaṃ bhave'karot |
so puṇyakalahonmādī sarvaśatrurbhavatyapi || 38.778{16} ||
yo'sau vibhratsito lokairdurākṛtirdurāśayaḥ |
sa pāruṣyavacasā lokātpurākruyyatyabhaṃdayat || 38.779{17} ||
sarvabhinneva vā yo'sau nighnito nādritaḥ kudhīḥ |
sa purā bhinnavādena lokaṃ bhinnāśayaṃ vyadhāt || 38.780{18} ||
yo'sau vimohito mūrkhā vipannabuddhirunmanāḥ |
sābhidhyāyatasā loke saṃpattīśchadmanāhatat || 38.781{19} ||
vidveṣī durmatā mohī yo'sau sajjanagarhitaḥ |
sa vyāpādakhilacittena satvāpakāra udyataḥ || 38.782{20} ||
yo'sau nīcā viṣaṇṇātmā hīneṃdriyo durāśayaḥ |
mithyādṛṣṭi sa saddharmamadhikṣiptvābhyaniṃdayat || 38.783{21} ||
iti ye daśā pāpāni prakurvanto carenmudā |
sarve te pāpino duṣṭā bhavaṃti duḥkhino'dhunā || 38.784{22} ||
evaṃ mayā yathākhyātaṃ tathā teṣāṃ puro vada |
etattvaduktamākarṇya smareyuste purākṛtam || 38.785{23} ||
tataste durgate bhītāḥ sadgatītamanotsukāḥ |
saddharmasādhanaṃ karttuṃ samicheyuḥ samādarāt || 38.786{24} ||
tadā tāṃ pura āmaṃtrya sarvānevaṃ vadādarāt |
yadi cāsti śubhe vāṃchā triratnaṃ sevyatāmiti || 38.787{25} ||
tataḥ sarve ime yūyaṃ nirmuktapātakā dhruvam |
sahasā sadgatau saukhyaṃ labhedhvamiti cābruhi || 38.788{26} ||
tataste te vacaḥ śrutvā sarve satsaukhya ālasāḥ |
triratnaśaraṇaṃ kṛtvā saṃprayāyuḥ surālayam || 38.789{27} ||
ye buddhe śaraṇaṃ yāṃti na te gaṃchaṃti durgatīm |
kramātpāramitāḥ pūrya saṃbuddhapadamāpnuyuḥ || 38.790{28} ||
śṛṇvaṃti ye ca saddharmaṃ na te gachaṃti durgatīm |
sarvadā sadgatiṃ yātāḥ pracareyuḥ śubhe mudā || 38.791{29} ||
ye bhajaṃti mudā saṃghe te'pi na yāṃti durgatīm |
sadā lokaśubhaṃ kṛtvā saṃprayāyuḥ sukhāvatīm || 38.792{30} ||
kālasarppo mayā dṛṣṭo bhūyo mārge maheśvari |
kiṃ tena prakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ tadvadasva vasuṃdhare || 38.793{31} ||
devyuvāca
purā āḍhyo gṛhastho'bhūdvistīrṇaparivārakaḥ |
saṃpadāmudayairnityaṃ dānadasyeva saṃyutaḥ || 38.794{32} ||
tena kākavaliṃ dātuṃ na prasehe kadā cana |
dvijān vinīpakān dṛṣṭvā cittaiḥ prāduṣāyetsadā || 38.795{32*} ||
kasmai cidapi kiṃcidvā hyadatvā bubhuje svayam |
sarvadhanasvakodyāne sthāpayāmāsa durmatiḥ || 38.796{33} ||
(rm 471)
pātakenā'dhunā tena kṛṣṇasarppo bhavatyasau |
tasyāpi purataḥ sarvaṃ madādiṣṭaṃ tathā vada || 38.797{34} ||
so'pi nirmuktapāpaśca mṛtaḥ svargaṃ vrajeddhruvam || 38.798{35} ||
yenaiva yatkṛtaṃ karma tenaiva paribhujyate |
abhuktaṃ kṣīyate naiva karma kvāpi kadā cana || 38.799{36} ||
ityetadevamākhyataṃ śrutvā tvaṃ ca samādarāt |
triratnabhajanaṃ kṛtvā cara me vratamuttamam || 38.800{37} ||
tathā lokeṣu sarvatra pracāraya mama vratam |
etatpuṇyavipākena sarvadā te bhavecchubham || 38.801{38} ||
tato bodhimatiṃ prāptaḥ purāyitvā yathākramam |
sarvāḥ pāramitā hodhiṃ prāpya buddhapadaṃ vrajeḥ || 38.802{39} ||
iti vijñāya kalyāṇi gatvā taṃ svapure punaḥ |
śraddhayā svāminā sārddhaṃ vrataṃ cara samādarāt || 38.803{40} ||
ityuktvā sā mahādevī sagaṇā śrīvasuṃdharā |
tataścāṃtarhitā svarge svālayamāgamaddrutam || 38.804{41} ||
tatra sā cūḍadevī tāṃ sagaṇāṃ śrīvasuṃdharām |
aṃtardhānagatāṃ dṛṣṭvā kṣaṇaṃ tasthau savismayā || 38.805{42} ||
tataḥ sā cūḍadevī tāṃ sagaṇāṃ śrīvasuṃdharām |
smṛtvā kṛtāṃjalirnatvā tataḥ pratyāyayau mudā || 38.806{43} ||
tataḥ sā cūḍadevī tāṃ sagaṇāṃ śrīvasuṃdharām |
manasānusmarantyeva pracaraṃti nyavarttataḥ || 38.807{44} ||
taṃ kṛṣṇasarpamālokya cūḍadevī bravīti sā |
devyā yathā copadiṣṭaṃ tathā tena purākṛtam || 38.808{45} ||
tannāmasmaraṇātso hityaktvā dehaṃ divaṃ yayau |
tatra mārge sametyāsau cūḍadevī yathākramam |
tāṃ daśapāpinaḥ sarvān samīkṣya samupācarat || 38.809{46} ||
tatra sā cūḍadevī tān daśākuśalacāriṇaḥ |
pṛṣṭvā devyā yathādiṣṭaṃ tathā sarvamupādiśat || 38.810{47} ||
tacchrutvānusmṛtiṃ prāptāḥ sarve te vismayānvitāḥ |
satyamiti prabhāṣaṃtaḥ śrīdevīśaraṇaṃ yayuḥ || 38.811{48} ||
tataste pāpinaḥ sarve śrīdevīdṛkprabhānvitāḥ |
trimaṇḍalaviśuddhāśca śubhe caritumīchire || 38.812{49} ||
tataste śraddhayā nityaṃ triratnaśaraṇaṃ gatāḥ |
tāṃ śrīvasuṃdharāṃ devīṃ dhyātva vrataṃ samācaran || 38.813{50} ||
tataste śuddhitātmānaḥ sarve'pi nirmalāśayāḥ |
tatraivaṃ tāṃ mahādevīṃ dhyātvā mṛtā divaṃ yayuḥ || 38.814{51} ||
tataśca cūḍadevī sā pracaraṃtī samāgatā |
sūcīmukhaṃ tamālokya dūrataḥ samupācarat || 38.815{52} ||
tatra sā sahasā tasya pretasya purato gatā |
śrīdevyāḥ samupādiṣṭaṃ tathā sarvamabhāṣata || 38.816{53} ||
tacchrutvā sa purāvṛttamanusmṛtvā niciṃtayat |
paścāttāpāgnisaṃtaptaḥ kṣaṇaṃ tasthau viniḥśvasan || 38.817{54} ||
tataḥ sa manasā dhyātvā tāṃ śrīdevīṃ vasuṃdharām |
(rm 472)
smarantī sāṃjalirnatvā dideśa sarvapātakam || 38.818{55} ||
tataḥ sa pariśuddhātmā nimuktapātakaḥ sudhīḥ |
śrīdevīśaraṇaṃ gatvā dehaṃ tyaktvā divaṃ yayau || 38.819{56} ||
tataśca cūḍadevī sā pracaramṭī samāgatā |
śūkaraṃ taṃ samālokya dūrataḥ samupācarat || 38.820{57} ||
tataḥ sopasthitā tasya śūkarasya purākṛtam |
yathādiṣṭaṃ mahādivyā tathā sarvamupādiśat || 38.821{58} ||
tacchrutvā śūkaraḥ so'pi samanusmṛtimāptavān |
tatrānutāpasaṃtaptaḥ kṣaṇaṃ tasthau viciṃtayan || 38.822{59} ||
tataḥ sa manasā dhyātvā tāṃ śrīdevīṃ vasuṃdharām |
saṃsmṛtvā sāṃjalirnatvā dideśa sarvapātakam || 38.823{60} ||
tato nirmuktapāpo'sau pariśuddhāśayaḥ sudhīḥ |
śrīdevīśaraṇaṃ gatvā dehaṃ tyaktvā divaṃ yayau || 38.824{61} ||
tataśca cūḍadevī sā pracaraṃtī samāgatā |
durātte sarasī dṛṣṭvā sahasā samupāsarat || 38.825{62} ||
tatra sā samupāsṛtya puṣkariṇyostayoḥ puraḥ |
mahādevyā yathādiṣṭaṃ tathā sarvamupādiśat || 38.826{63} ||
tacchrutvā puṣkariṇyau tau samanusmṛtimāptuḥ |
tatrānutāpataptā tau nidhāya taṣṭhatuḥ kṣaṇam || 38.827{64} ||
tataste manasā dhyātvā taṃ śrīdevīvasuṃdharām |
saṃsmṛtvā sāṃjalirnatvā dideśatuḥ svapāpakam || 38.828{65} ||
tato vimuktapāpau tau pariśuddhāśayo drutam |
puṣkariṇyāśrayaṃ tyaktvā prayayatuḥ surālayam || 38.829{66} ||
tatra sarve'pi te svarge gatāstatsmṛtibhāvinaḥ |
dhyātvā tāṃ śrīmahādevīṃ smṛtvā nityaṃ prabhejire || 38.830{67} ||
tatra teṣāṃ mahādevyāḥ prasādācchrī pravarddhitāḥ |
tacchrīsaṃpanmahatsaukhyaṃ bhuktvā sarve te'raman || 38.831{68} ||
tataḥ sā cūḍadevī tān sarvān kṛtvā vimuktitān |
svadeśyābhimukhāyātā sva purāṃtikamāyayau || 38.832{69} ||
tatra tāṃ samupāyātāṃ dṛṣṭvā lokāḥ pramoditāḥ |
sahasā nṛpateragre gatvaivaṃ saṃnyavedayat || 38.833{70} ||
jaya deva mahārāja cūḍadevī nivarttitā |
tadbhavān sahasopetya tāṃ samānetumarhati || 38.834{71} ||
iti niveditaṃ taiḥ sa rājā śrutvā pramoditaḥ |
sahasā maṃtriṇo'mātyān samāmaṃtryaivamādiśat || 38.835{72} ||
cūḍadevī samāyātā tadyūyaṃ sahasādarāt |
pratyudgamya mahotsāhairihānayata tāṃ priyām || 38.836{73} ||
iti rājñā samādiṣṭaṃ śrutvā te maṃtriṇo janāḥ |
amātyāḥ sahasā sarve tathetyuktvā mudācaran || 38.837{74} ||
tataste maṃtriṇo'mātyāḥ sajanāḥ paurikā api |
sarve pratyudgatā tatra cūḍadevī samāyayuḥ || 38.838{75} ||
tatra sarve'pi te lokā dṛṣṭvā tāṃ sahasādarāt |
praṇatvā yāta āropya mahotsāhaiḥ pure'nayat || 38.839{76} ||
tatra sā mahiṣī devī mahotsāhapramoditā |
(rm 473)
praviśya maṃdire rājñaḥ svāminaḥ samupācarat || 38.840{77} ||
tatra sā svāminaṃ dṛṣṭvā sahasopetya sāṃjaliḥ |
praṇatvā svāminā tena sahāsane samāśrayat || 38.841{78} ||
tatra sa nṛpatiḥ svāmi tāṃ devīṃ supriyāṃ mudā |
saṃpaśyan kauśalaṃ pṛṣṭvā puna evamapṛchata || 38.842{79} ||
kena tvaṃ hetunā kutra gatā kuto'dhunāgatā |
etatsarvaṃ samākhyāya mano me paribodhaya || 38.843{80} ||
ityetatsvāminā tenādiṣṭaṃ cūḍadevī niśamya sā |
svapravṛttāṃtamākhyāya mārgavṛttiṃ nyavedayat || 38.844{81} ||
tato'haṃ sahasā yātā draṣṭuṃ devīṃ vasuṃdharām |
tatra dūrānnadīśabdaṃ śrutvāhaṃ samupācaram || 38.845{82} ||
tatra tīre mayā dṛṣṭā devyā jalaghaṭāvahāḥ |
bhavaṃtyaḥ kuta āyātā iti pṛṣṭe'vadaṃ ca tāḥ || 38.846{83} ||
vasudhārāmahādevyāḥ snānārthamime ghaṭāḥ |
nīyaṃte'mṛtasaṃpūrṇā asmābhiriti manyatām || 38.847{84} ||
eṣā nadī mahādevīsnānāmṛtapravāhitā |
tadetadamṛte nātra mukhaṃ prakṣālya dṛśyatām || 38.848{85} ||
iti tābhiḥ samādiṣṭaṃ śrutvā tatheti bodhitā |
tannadyā amṛtenāsyaṃ prakṣālya kṣamahaṃ tathā || 38.849{86} ||
tadā tatra sabhāsīnāṃ sagaṇāṃ śrivasuṃdharām |
apaśyaṃ tāṃ nadīmāśu laṃghitvā samupācaram || 38.850{87} ||
tatrāhaṃ samupāśritya tān devīṃ śrīvasuṃdharām |
sagaṇāṃ sāṃjalirnatvā tatraikāṃte upāśrayam || 38.851{88} ||
tadā sā śrīmahādevī māṃ dṛṣṭvaivamapṛchata |
kutaḥ kimarthamāyāsi tadiha kathyatāmiti || 38.852{89} ||
tacchrutvā sāṃjalirnatvā kṛtvā cāpi pradakṣiṇām |
sagaṇāṃ tāṃ mahādevīmupāśrityaivamabruvam || 38.853{90} ||
sūryodayanarendrasya bhāryāhaṃ mahiṣī priyā |
bhavatyā darśanaṃ karttuṃ mudātra samupācare || 38.854{91} ||
yanmayā maṃdabhāvinyā vrataṃ samyagna dhāritam |
tanmahadaparādhaṃ me bhavatā kṣaṃtumarhati || 38.855{92} ||
sarvadāhaṃ mahādevī bhavatyāḥ śaraṇe sthitā |
samyagvrataṃ punardharttumichāmi sāṃprataṃ khalu || 38.856{93} ||
tadbhavaṃtī mahādevī kṛtvā me'nugrahaṃ punaḥ |
kṛpayā tadvratālaṃbhaṃ kārayituṃ samarhati || 38.857{94} ||
iti mayārthitaṃ śrutvā sā śrīdevī vasuṃdharā |
kṛpādṛṣṭyā samālokya māmevaṃ samupādiśat || 38.858{95} ||
sādhu vatse'sti te vāṃcā yadi me vratadhāraṇe |
gatvā tvaṃ svagṛhe bhartrā saha vrataṃ carādarāt || 38.859{96} ||
tadāhaṃ svayamāgatya drakṣyāmi maṃdiraṃ tava |
tatrāhaṃ samupāśritya dāsyāmi prārthitaṃ varam || 38.860{97} ||
ityādiṣṭaṃ mahādevyā śrutvāhaṃ paribodhitā |
tāṃ śrīvasuṃdharāṃ devīmapṛchaṃ punarādarāt || 38.861{98} ||
(rm 474)
devi mārgasthitairlokaiḥ saṃdarśitaṃ mayocyate |
tadbhavaṃtī samākhyāya māṃ bodhayitumarhati || 38.862{99} ||
tacchrutvā sā mahādevī taiḥ sarvaiḥ prakṛtaṃ yathā |
tattatsarvaṃ tathā teṣāṃ karma me samupādiśat || 38.863{100} ||
tato'haṃ śraddhayā bhaktimātreṇa taṃ vasuṃdharām |
śrīdevīṃ sagaṇāṃ cāpi samārcayaṃ samādarāt || 38.864{1} ||
tataḥ pradakṣiṇāṃ kṛtvā kṛtāṃjalipuṭo mudā |
aṣṭāṃgaiḥ śrīmahādevyāḥ pādābje praṇatāpatam || 38.865{2} ||
tataḥ sā sagaṇā devī kṣaṇādaṃtarhitābhavat |
tata utthāya tāṃ devīṃ naivāpaśyatkuhāpi ca || 38.866{3} ||
tato'haṃ vismayākrāṃtahṛdayā tatra kṛtāṃjaliḥ |
smṛtvā sagaṇāṃ devīṃ natvā śanaistato'caram || 38.867{4} ||
tatra mārge samāsādya sarvānstān pāpinaḥ kramāt |
mahādevyā yadādiṣṭaṃ tathākhyāya vyanodayam || 38.868{5} ||
tataste pāpinaḥ sarve vacasā me'nutāpitāḥ |
śrīdevīśaraṇaṃ kṛtvā dehaṃ tyaktvācaran divam || 38.869{6} ||
etān satvān samuddhṛtya śrīdevyāḥ saṃprasādataḥ |
kṣemeṇeha samāyātā bhavatāṃ darśanaṃ labhe || 38.870{7} ||
ityevaṃ sarvavṛttāṃtaṃ cūḍādevyā niveditam |
śrutvā sa nṛpatiḥ svāmī vismitaścaivamabravīt || 38.871{8} ||
aho bhāgyena saṃprāptā śrīdevyā saṃprasādataḥ |
bhāryā tvaṃ mahiṣī cāpi vrataṃ śrīsaṃpado'pi me || 38.872{9} ||
tathā bhūyo'pyahaṃ devyā yathādiṣṭaṃ samādarāt |
saha tvayā mahiṣyātra carāṇi sarvadā vratam || 38.873{10} ||
kimetadrājyabhogyena vinā tvāṃ mahiṣīṃ mama |
etadeva hi saṃsāraṃ yatsabhāryā vrataṃ caret || 38.874{11} ||
tadāvāṃ sarvadāpīha śrīdevyāḥ śaraṇaṃ gatau |
śraddhayā vratamārādhya prabhajeva samādarāt || 38.875{12} ||
ityevaṃ samupādiśya nṛpatiḥ saṃpramoditaḥ |
tābhyāṃ yuto mahotsāhaiḥ śrīnagaraṃ samāyayau || 38.876{13} ||
tayā devyā subhāvinyā sahābhyānaṃdito'ramat |
dinaikasmiṃścūḍadevī tato garbhan dadhau kramāt || 38.877{14} ||
prāsūta saptame māsi rājalakṣaṇamaṃḍitam |
prāsādikaṃ supīnāṃgaṃ putraṃ brāhme muhurttake || 38.878{15} ||
tataḥ pitā samāhūya sarvānamātyamaṃtriṇaḥ |
śrīsenaguptamācāryaṃ papracha janmakāraṇam || 38.879{16} ||
senagupto vicāryaivaṃ bodhayannṛpamabravīt || 38.880{17} ||
mā viṣīdātra rājendra yuto yadrājalakṣaṇaiḥ |
yogo'yaṃ sārvabhaumaḥ syātsaptamāsaphalaṃ śṛṇu || 38.881{18} ||
prathame kalalaṃ vidyāddvitīye ghanameva ca |
tṛtīyamāsyaṃ krūraṃ ca caturthe'sthisamuccayaḥ || 38.882{19} ||
paṃcame carmasaṃchannaḥ ṣaṣṭhe'ṃgajasamudbhavaḥ |
saptame cetanāvāptiriti garbhasthalakṣaṇam || 38.883{20} ||
(rm 475)
yo bālaḥ saptame māsi jāyate sa tu bhāgyabhāk |
sārvabhauma nṛpo bhūtvā mahīṃ śāsti sunītitaḥ || 38.884{21} ||
tasmānnṛpeṃdra taṃ bālaṃ rakṣaṇīyaḥ prayatnataḥ |
ityācāryavacaḥ śrutvā sa rājā parimoditaḥ |
jātakarmādikaṃ karma mahotsavairakārayat || 38.885{22} ||
tadācāryaḥ senagupto vratamaṃgalakārakam |
iti matvā nāma cakre maṃgalodaya ityapi || 38.886{23} ||
tato bālaḥ śuklapakṣe caṃdra iva dine dine |
pupoṣa vṛddhiṃ sahasā tejobalasamanvitaḥ || 38.887{24} ||
tato rājakule rājā bhāryāputrasamanvitaḥ |
vasuṃdharāvratotsāhī muditaḥ samupasthitaḥ || 38.888{25} ||
tato vratadinaprāpte bhāryayā saha saṃmatam |
kṛtvā sa bhūpatī rājā vrataṃ caritumaichata || 38.889{26} ||
tatastadvratasāmagrīṃ sādhayitvā yathāvidhi |
ācāryeṇa yathādiṣṭaṃ tathā sarvaṃ samācarat || 38.890{27} ||
śrīdevyā vratamārādhya samahiṣī sa bhūpatiḥ |
yathāvidhi susaṃpūrṇa pracacāra samādarāt || 38.891{28} ||
tasminnavasare tatra sā śrīdevī vasuṃdharā |
sagaṇā svayamāgatya bhāsayaṃtī samāśrayat || 38.892{29} ||
tāṃ śrīvasuṃdharāṃ devīṃ sagaṇāṃ samupāśritām |
sākṣādālokya rājā sa sabhāryaḥ samupasthitaḥ || 38.893{30} ||
pādārghaṃ sahasā datvā pūjāṃgaiśca samarcayat |
kṛtvā pradakṣiṇāṃ natvā sāṃjalirnyapatatpuraḥ || 38.894{31} ||
tataḥ sā śrīmahādevī vasulakṣmī vilokya tam |
sabhāryaṃ nṛpamutthāya samālokyaivamādiśat || 38.895{32} ||
śṛṇu rājanmahābhāga yathā te yatsamīpsitam |
sarvaṃ te'haṃ pravakṣyāmi satyametadvaco mama || 38.896{33} ||
ityādiṣṭe mahālakṣmyā sabhāryaḥ sa nṛpo mudā |
tāṃ devīṃ sāṃjalirnatvā prārthayadevamādarāt || 38.897{34} ||
namaste śrīmahādevī prasīda parameśvaśrī |
yathā te īhitaṃ kāryaṃ tadeva dehi me varam || 38.898{35} ||
etatsaṃprārthitaṃ rājñā śrutvā sā śrīmaheśvarī |
sabhāryaṃ taṃ mahīpālaṃ samālokyaivamādiśat || 38.899{36} ||
sādhu rājan svayaṃ dhṛtvā vrataṃ sarvatra cāraya |
kṛtvaitanme mahatkāryaṃ tataḥ praihi mamālayam || 38.900{37} ||
ityādiśya tataḥ sā śrīmahādevī vasuṃdharā |
sagaṇā tatkṣaṇādaṃtarhitā svaṃ bhuvanaṃ yayau || 38.901{38} ||
tataḥ sa sapriyo rājā tāṃ devīṃ sagaṇāmapi |
sarvāmantarhitāṃ dṛṣṭvā kṣaṇaṃ tasthau savismayaḥ || 38.902{39} ||
tatastathā sa bhūmīndro bhāryayā saha sarvadā |
śrīdevyāḥ śaraṇaṃ gatvā vrataṃ dhṛtvā samācarat || 38.903{40} ||
tathā sarvatra lokeṣu śrīdevyā vratamādiśat |
bodhayitvā prayatnena prācārayatsamaṃtataḥ || 38.904{41} ||
tathā sa nṛpatirdevyā vrataṃ sarvatra cārayan |
(rm 476)
sarveṣāmapi satvānāṃ maṃgalaṃ sarvadākarot || 38.905{42} ||
etatpuṇyavipākena sa rājā śrīsamanvitaḥ |
divyātiriktasaukhyāni labdhvā sadā śubhe'carat || 38.906{43} ||
tataḥ sa nṛpatī rājā sarvārthibhyo yathepsitam |
saṃbodhipraṇidhānena datvā vrataṃ sadācarat || 38.907{44} ||
tathā sa bhūpatī rājā bhāryayā saha moditaḥ |
śrīdevyāḥ pradadhe bhaktyā vrataṃ sāṣṭottaraṃ śatam || 38.908{45} ||
tataḥ sa bhāryayā sārddhamabhiṣiṃcyātmajaṃ sutam |
svapade lokadharmārthaṃ kṛtvānte tuṣite yayau || 38.909{46} ||
tataḥ sa sapriyastatra śrīdevyāḥ samupasthitaḥ |
sarvadā bhajanaṃ kṛtvā śrīmānstasthau śubhe caran || 38.910{47} ||
tatra sa sarvadā satvahitaṃ kṛtvā sukhānvitaḥ |
sarvāḥ pāramitāḥ pūrya saṃbuddhapadamāpsyati || 38.911{48} ||
tadārabhya sadā sarvalokeṣu satataṃ śubham |
triratnabhajanotsāhaṃ pravarttate kṛtāviva || 38.912{49} ||
tadā bhuvi tathā devyā svayaṃ vrataṃ pracāritam |
ityetanmunibhiḥ sarvaiḥ khyātamiti śrutaṃ mayā || 38.913{50} ||
etanme guruṇākhyātaṃ śrutaṃ maya tathocyate |
tvaṃ cāpyevaṃ mahārāja śrīdevyāścara tadvratam || 38.914{51} ||
tathā te sarvadā kṣemaṃ sarvatrāpi bhaveddhruvam |
śrīdasyeva mahāsaṃpadakṣīṇā saṃbhavetkhalu || 38.915{52} ||
tatastvaṃ śrīsamādhārā datvārthibhyo yathepsitam |
triratnabhajanaṃ kṛtvā saṃbuddhapadamāpnuyāḥ || 38.916{53} ||
evaṃ matvā mahārāja prajāścāpi prabodhayan |
śrīdevyāḥ śaraṇe sthāpya sadā vrataṃ pracāraya || 38.917{54} ||
tathā satvahitaṃ kartuṃ saṃbodhiratnalabdhaye |
śrīdevyā dhāraṇīvidyā pradhātavyā sadādarāt || 38.918{55} ||
etatpuṇyānubhāvena sarvatra sarvadā bhave |
bhavetaṃ maṃgalotsāhaṃ satataṃ nirupadravam || 38.919{56} ||
atītairapi saṃbuddhaiḥ sarvairapi munīśvaram |
śrīdevyā dhāraṇīvidyāratnairlokahitaṃ kṛtam || 38.920{57} ||
tathādhunāpi sarvaiśca varttamānairmunīśvaram |
tridevyā dhāraṇīvidyāratnaiḥ satvaśubhaṃ kṛtam || 38.921{58} ||
tathā cānāgataiḥ sarvai munīndrairapi sarvataḥ |
śrīdevyā dhāraṇīvidyāratnaiḥ kariṣyate śivam || 38.922{59} ||
sarve'pi sugatā nāthā bodhisatvāḥ kṛpālavaḥ |
śrīdevīsaṃprasādena caranto bhadracārikām || 38.923{60} ||
āryyasaddharmamādiśya prakurvate jagaddhitam |
evaṃ jagaddhitaṃ karttuṃ saṃbodhiratnamīpsubhiḥ |
śrīdevyā dhāraṇīṃ dhṛtvā caritavyaṃ jagaddhite || 38.924{61} ||
ye cāpi śrīmahādevyā dhāraṇīṃ śraddhayādarāt |
bhaktyā paṭhaṃti śṛṇvaṃti pāṭhayantyapi sarvadā || 38.925{62} ||
likhitvā pustake vāpi gṛhe sthāpya sadādarāt |
(rm 477)
satkṛtya śraddhayābhyarcya dhṛtvāṃjaliṃ namaṃti ye || 38.926{63} ||
tathā bhurje likhitvā ca vāhau kaṇṭhe ca mastake |
śrīdevyā dhāraṇīvidyāratnaṃ dadhati ye sadā || 38.927{64} ||
eteṣāmapi sarveṣāṃ gṛheṣu viṣayeṣvapi |
sarvatra maṃgalaṃ nityaṃ nirvighnaṃ sarvadā bhavet || 38.928{65} ||
ītaya upasargāśca mahotpātā upadravāḥ |
na teṣāṃ viṣaye kvāpi pracareyuḥ kadā cana || 38.929{66} ||
jvarāśca vividhā rogāḥ sarvasaṃhārakārakāḥ |
tesāṃ kāyeṣu sarvatra na spṛśeyuḥ kadā cana || 38.930{67} ||
apathyamapi pathyaṃ syādviṣo'pi cāmṛtaṃ bhavet |
teṣāṃ deheṣu sarvatra hitāyate'hito'pi ca || 38.931{68} ||
brahmaśakrādayo devā ye duṣṭā vahnayo'pi ca |
yamasya kiṃkarāścāpi rākṣasā garuḍā api || 38.932{69} ||
nāgāśca vāyavaścāpi yakṣagaṃdharvakiṃnarāḥ |
bhūtapretapiśācāśca kumbhāṇḍāḥ kaṭapūtanāḥ || 38.933{70} ||
evamanye'pi ye duṣṭāḥ sarvasatvabhayaṃkarāḥ |
sarve te viṣaye teṣāṃ vighnaṃ karttuṃ na śaknuyuḥ || 38.934{71} ||
sarve devādayo'pyete tridhātubhuvanasthitāḥ |
teṣāṃ devīprabhāvena rakṣeyuśca prasāditāḥ || 38.935{72} ||
teṣāṃ kāryāṇi sarvāṇi saṃsiddhyeyuravighnataḥ |
teṣāṃ sarvatra kāyeṣu kiṃcidvighnaṃ bhavenna hi || 38.936{73} ||
sarvatra gachatāṃ teṣāṃ salābhasiddhimaṃgalam |
bhavedeva sadā naiva vighnatā syātkadā cana || 38.937{74} ||
śrīdevīkṛpādṛṣṭiprabhāvena samaṃtataḥ |
sarvārthasādhane siddhirbhavenna tu vighātatā || 38.938{75} ||
sarve'pi lokapālāśca devādayo maharddhikāḥ |
yatayo yoginaḥ siddhā ṛṣayo brahmacāriṇaḥ || 38.939{76} ||
grahāstārāgaṇāḥ sarvā yoginyo bhairavā api |
ḍākinyo mātṛkāṣṭāśca mahākālagaṇā api || 38.940{77} ||
evamanye'pi vīreśāḥ sarvaduṣṭapramardakāḥ |
sṛṣṭisaṃsthitisaṃhārakārakā bhīmarūpiṇaḥ || 38.941{78} ||
te'pi sarve samālokya śrīdevīsevakānmudā |
kṛpārddāḥ samupāśritya rakṣeyuḥ sarvadānugāḥ || 38.942{79} ||
sarve'pi bhikṣavo buddhā arhanto brahmacāriṇaḥ |
śrīdevī bhāvino dṛṣṭvā saṃrakṣeyuḥ prasāditāḥ || 38.943{80} ||
sarve pratyekabuddhāśca tāñchrīdevyā upāśrikān |
dūrataḥ sudṛśālokya saṃrakṣeyuḥ prasāditāḥ || 38.944{81} ||
bodhisatvāśca sarve'pi tāṃcchrīdevyā upāsakān |
yathepsitaṃ varaṃ datvā pālayeyuḥ kṛpānvitāḥ || 38.945{82} ||
sarve'pi sugatā buddhāḥ śrīdevyāḥ śaraṇe sthitān |
sudṛśā sarvadālokya sa bhaveyurdayānvitāḥ || 38.946{83} ||
evaṃ sarve'pi lokeśā brahmā viṣṇuśivādayaḥ |
śrīdevīdhāraṇīvidyādharāṃ rakṣeyurādarāt || 38.947{84} ||
evaṃ sarvairmahābhijñairmunīndrairgaditaṃ khalu |
(rm 478)
iti me guruṇādiṣṭaṃ śrutaṃ mayā tathocyate || 38.948{85} ||
śrutvā rājaṃstvamapyevaṃ tacchrīdevīvrataṃ cara |
tataste śrīmahādevīvratapuṇyānubhāvataḥ || 38.949{86} ||
mahāsaṃpatpravṛddhā syādakṣīṇā sarvadā sthirā || 38.950{87} ||
tatastvaṃ taddravyamādāya sarvārthibhyo yathepsitam |
datvā caracchubhe saukhyaṃ bhuṃkṣva saṃpālayan prajāḥ || 38.951{88} ||
etatpuṇyavipākena durgatīṃ na vrajeḥ kvacit |
sarvadā sadgatīṃ yātā dharme caran sukhaṃ vaseḥ || 38.952{89} ||
iti matvā mahārāja śrīdevyāḥ śaraṇaṃ gataḥ |
saddharmaṃ svayamākarṇya lokāśca śrāvaya prabho || 38.953{90} ||
sarveṣāmapi saṃsāre dharma eva suhṛtsakhā |
tasmādatra prayatnena dharma eva samarjyām |
dharmaṃ tu sarvadharmāṇāṃ praśreṣṭhaṃ saugataṃ varam || 38.954{91} ||
yataḥ sarvatra lokeṣu saṃsāre bhadratā sadā |
tasmātsarvatra saṃsāre sadā bhadraṃ samīpsubhiḥ |
satkṛtya saugataṃ dharmaṃ śrotavyaṃ śraddhayādarāt || 38.955{92} ||
ye'pyatra saugataṃ dharmaṃ śṛṇvaṃti śraddhayādarāt |
durgatīṃ te na gachaṃti saṃyāṃti sadgatīṃ sadā || 38.956{93} ||
ye cāpi saugataṃ dharmaṃ śrāvayaṃti parānapi |
te'pi na durgatīṃ yāṃti yāṃti sadgatimeva hi || 38.957{94} ||
yaścāpi saugataṃ dharmaṃ prabhāṣati prabedhayan |
so'pi na durgatiṃ yāti saṃprayāyātsukhāvatīm || 38.958{95} ||
yasyāpi syātkalau paṃcakaṣāyakleśasaṃkule |
durlabhaṃ saugataṃ dharmaṃ vaktā śrotā ca durlabhaḥ || 38.959{96} ||
tadā tān kleśitāllokān dṛṣṭvā ghṛṇādayāhataḥ |
svakāyajīvitasnehaṃ tyaktvā yo dharmamādiśet || 38.960{97} ||
sa eva hi mahābhijño dhīraḥ sudhīrvicakṣaṇaḥ |
sarvakleśavijetā ca mārajitsyājjinātmajaḥ || 38.961{98} ||
yaścāpi ca tadā kleśasaṃghasamākule tathā |
tyaktaḥ svaprāṇakāyo yaḥ saddharmaṃ śṛṇuyānmudā || 38.962{99} ||
sāpi dhīro mahāvīraḥ sarvakleśābhimardakaḥ |
saṃbodhimārgasaṃprāpto jñātavyo hi jinapriyaḥ || 38.963{100} ||
yaścāpyeva kalau kāle saddharmāntarhite tathā |
nisnehasvāsukāyo yaḥ saddharmaṃ śrāvayetparān || 38.964{1} ||
sa'pi syātsugataputro mahādhīro viśāradaḥ |
sarvasarvahitākāṃkṣī bodhisatvaḥ sudhīḥ kṛtī || 38.965{2} ||
yaścāpi parataḥ śrutvā saddharmaṃ saugatoditam |
anumodetprasannātmā so'pi syātsugatātmajaḥ || 38.966{3} ||
sarvatīrthābhiṣeke yatpuṇyaṃ tatsarvamāpnuyuḥ |
sarvatra sajjanā nūnaṃ karttavyaṃ nātra saṃśayam || 38.967{4} ||
yāvaṃtaḥ prāṇinaḥ satvāstān sarvān yo hi pālayet |
tasya yāvanmahatpuṇyaṃ tatsarvaṃ te samāpnuyuḥ || 38.968{5} ||
(rm 479)
yāvaṃto bhikṣavo'rhantastān sarvān yaḥ samarcayet |
tasya puṇyaphalaṃ yāvattatsarvaṃ te hyavāpuyuḥ || 38.969{6} ||
yāvatpratyekabuddhāśca tān sarvān yo bhajenmudā |
yāvatpuṇyaphalaṃ tasya labheyuste tato'rthikam || 38.970{7} ||
yāvaṃtaḥ sugatāścāpi tān sarvān yaḥ sadā bhajet |
yāvatpuṇyaṃ mahattasya labheyuste tato'dhikam || 38.971{8} ||
yāvaṃto bhūtale gavastān sarvāñchraddhayā dadet |
tasya yāvanmahatpuṇyaṃ prāpnuyuste tato'dhikam || 38.972{9} ||
merupramāṇasauvarṇaratnarāśiṃ ca yo dadet |
tasya puṇyamasaṃkhyeyaṃ prayāyuste tato'dhikam || 38.973{10} ||
sābdhiśailaṃ mahīṃ sarvāṃ śraddhayā yaḥ samarppayet |
tasya bahutaraṃ puṇyaṃ samyāyuste tato'dhikam || 38.974{11} ||
sauṣadhīdravyabhogyādīn pradadyācchraddhayāpi ca |
tasya puṇyamasaṃkhyeyaṃ labheyuste tato'dhikam || 38.975{12} ||
yasya kalpasahasrāṇi tṛṇāmbuprāśanastayet |
yāvaṃtasya mahatpuṇyaṃ labheyuste tato'dhikam || 38.976{13} ||
yajñakoṭisahasrāṇi kuryādyaḥ śraddhayā mudā |
tāvattasya mahatpuṇyaṃ prāpnuyuste tato'dhikam || 38.977{14} ||
evaṃ yāvaṃti puṇyāni dānavratodbhavānyapi |
saṃti tato'dhikaṃ puṇyaṃ te sarve samavāpnuyuḥ || 38.978{15} ||
iti satyaṃ samākhyātaṃ sarvairapi munīśvaraiḥ |
etanme guruṇādiṣṭaṃ śrutaṃ mayā ca vakṣyate || 38.979{16} ||
etatsatyaṃ parijñāya yadi saṃbodhimichasi |
sadā saddharmamākarṇya triratnabhajanaṃ kuru || 38.980{17} ||
prajāścāpi mahārāja bodhayitvā prayatnataḥ |
bodhimārge pratiṣṭhāpya pālayasva samāhitaḥ || 38.981{18} ||
tataste maṃgalaṃ nityaṃ sarvatrāpi bhaveddhruvam |
kramādbodhiṃ samāsādya saṃbuddhapadamāpnuyāḥ || 38.982{19} ||
etattenārhatādiṣṭaṃ śrutvāśokaḥ sa bhūpatiḥ |
tathetyabhyanumoditvā prābhyanaṃdatsapārṣadaḥ || 38.983{20} ||
tataḥ sa upagupto'rhan vihāre samupāśrayet |
so'śoko'pi mahārājastathā dharmaṃ sadācarat || 38.984{21} ||
etadevaṃ samākhyāya jayaśrī sa jinātmajaḥ |
sarvāsāṃ śiṣyasaṃghāṃśca samāmaṃtryaivamādiśat || 38.985{22} ||
subhāṣitamahāratnamālāmimāṃ prabhāṣati |
yo yatra meva śṛṇvaṃti śrāvayaṃtyapi ye mudā || 38.986{23} ||
śrutvānumodaṃti ye ca dṛṣṭvā namaṃti ye mudā |
satkṛtya śraddhayā nityaṃ samabhyarcya bhajaṃti ye || 38.987{24} ||
eteṣāṃ tatra sarveṣāṃ bhavaṃtu maṃgalaṃ sadā |
jayaśrī parivṛddhāstu sarvatra sarvadāpi ca || 38.988{25} ||
ītaya upasargāśca sarve vighnagaṇā api |
duṣṭā māragaṇāṃścāpi vilayaṃ yāṃtu sarvadā || 38.989{26} ||
gṛheṣu sarvadā teṣaṃ lakṣmīstiṣṭhatu susthirāḥ |
(rm 480)
dharmārthakāmabhogyāśca saṃsiddhyaṃtu sa medhitāḥ || 38.990{27} ||
nīrogā sucirāyuṣkā bhavaṃtu te sukhānvitāḥ |
viratapāpakarmāṇaḥ pracaraṃtu śubhe sadā |
triratnabhajanaṃ kṛtvā bhavaṃtu brahmacāriṇaḥ || 38.991{28} ||
sarvasatvahitaiḥ kṛtvā bhuktvā bhadrasukhaṃ sadā |
kramātpāramitāḥ pūrya vrajaṃtu saugataṃ padam || 38.992{29} ||
etatpuṇyānubhāvena tatra sarvatra sarvadā |
bhavaṃtu maṃgalaṃ nityaṃ subhikṣaṃ nirupadravam || 38.993{30} ||
sarve satvāśca saddharmaṃ sādhayaṃtu samudyatāḥ |
triratnabhajanotsāhaiḥ sarve vrajaṃtu saugatim || 38.994{31} ||
kāle varṣaṃtu meghāśca bhūyācchasyavatī mahī |
bahukṣīrapradā gāvo bhavaṃtu vatsalānvitāḥ || 38.995{32} ||
sarvadā suphalaiḥ puṣpaṃ vṛkṣāḥ saṃtu samanvitāḥ |
rājā bhavaṃtu dhārmiṣṭho nayasthāḥ saṃtu maṃtriṇaḥ || 38.996{33} ||
sarve sainyāśca yodhāro jayaṃtu dūrjanānnarīn |
sarvetayaḥ praśāmyaṃtu saṃtu saukhyānvitāḥ prajāḥ || 38.997{34} ||
nīrujāḥ sukhasaṃpannāḥ śrīsaṃpattisamanvitāḥ |
brahmādi lokapālāśca daśadikpā maharddhikāḥ |
vighnāñjitvā śubhaṃ kṛtvā pālayaṃtu prajāḥ sadā || 38.998{35} ||
sarve traidhātulokāśca kṛtvā maitrīṃ parasparam |
triratnabhajanaṃ kṛtvā caraṃtu bodhicārikām || 38.999{36} ||
sarve bhadrāṇi paśyaṃtu mā kaścitpāpamācarat |
sarvatra śāsanaṃ bauddhaṃ dhṛtvā yāṃtu jinālayam || 38.1000{37} ||
evaṃ jayaśrīrmunirājakalpaḥ saṃbodhicaryāpravikāśahetoḥ |
sarvān svaśiṣyān paribodhayan saḥ samādideśa śryavadānamālām |
śrutvāpi te sarva udāracittāḥ śrutvā salokā abhinaṃdyamānāḥ |
saddharmamāśritya sadā triratnaṃ bhaktyā bhajaṃta vyaharan pramādā || 38.1001{38} ||
yatredaṃ sūtrarājeṃdraṃ prāvarttayetkalāvapi bhāṣedyaḥ śṛṇuyādyaśca śrāvayedyaśca cārayet |
eteṣāṃ tatra sarveṣāṃ saṃbuddhāḥ sakalā sadā kṛpādṛṣṭyā samālokya kurvaṃtu bhadramābhavaṃ sarvāḥ pāramitā devyastesāṃ tatra sadā śivam |
kurvantyo bodhisaṃbhāraṃ pūrayaṃtu jagaddhite |
anena saddharmarasāmṛtena sarvajñabhāsvadvadanodbhavena |
kleśānalaprahvarirāturāsu prajāsu duḥkhaṃ praśamo'stu nityam || 38.1002{39} ||


++ iti subhāṣitaratnāvadānatatve vasudhārā vratakathā samāptā ++


śreyo'stu samvat1028 māghamāse kṛṣṇapakṣe dvitīyāyāṃ tithau pūrvaphālguṇīnakṣatre suddhayoge budhavāsare kuṃbharāśigate savitari siṃharāśigate caṃdramasi etaddine saṃpūrṇamidaiḥ pustakaṃ (rm 481) mānadevasaṃskāritacakramahāvihāre nivāsitaḥ vajrācāryyaśrīdharmmarājena likhitamidaṃ pustakametatpuṇyena dharmmārthakāmamokṣān prāptimastu śvapustakasunānaṃ lobhayāyamadu ||
